sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from bt_b12 import load_b12, CFG

frac, N = float(sys.argv[1]), int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 96
rt, st = load_b12(frac)
import ctypes
import mlx.core as mx
from mtplx import generation as G
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig


class _RU(ctypes.Structure):                                          # rusage_info_v4 (as bt_b12's CLI)
    _fields_ = [("uuid", ctypes.c_uint8 * 16)] + [(f"u{i}", ctypes.c_uint64) for i in range(40)]


def footprint_peak():
    ru = _RU()
    return ru.u28 / 1e9 if ctypes.CDLL(None).proc_pid_rusage(os.getpid(), 4, ctypes.byref(ru)) == 0 else float("nan")


tok = rt.tokenizer
EOS = {248044, 248046}                                                # the suite's EOS set
greedy = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)
PROMPTS = {"code": open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
           "prose": ("Explain, in plain prose for a curious 14-year-old, how a CPU cache works and why it makes "
                     "programs faster. Use one everyday analogy and keep it to about 300 words."),
           "ifeval": ("Write a short note to a neighbour asking them to water your plants while you are away. "
                      "Your entire response must be in lowercase letters, contain exactly 3 paragraphs separated by "
                      "the markdown divider ***, and end with the phrase 'thanks again'.")}
tag = (f"b12 C_m={CFG['cm']} C_l={CFG['cl']} late3={CFG['l3']} w={CFG['w']} chunk "
       f"{os.environ.get('MTPLX_PREFILL_CHUNK_SIZE', '2048')} pf1={os.environ.get('BT_AR_PF', '0')}")
for name, text in PROMPTS.items():
    ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
    first = {}
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    res = G.generate_ar(rt, ids, max_tokens=N, sampler=greedy, seed=0,
                        token_callback=lambda _t: first.setdefault("t", time.perf_counter()))
    t1 = time.perf_counter()
    toks = [int(x) for x in (getattr(res, "tokens", None) or [])]
    cut = next((j + 1 for j, x in enumerate(toks) if x in EOS), None)
    toks, finish = (toks[:cut], "eos") if cut else (toks, "length")
    tf = first.get("t", t1)
    print(f"[{name}] generate_ar {tag}: {len(toks)} tokens {finish}; prompt {len(ids)} tokens, first token after "
          f"{tf - t0:.2f} s, decode {(len(toks) - 1) / max(t1 - tf, 1e-9):.1f} tok/s; PEAK FOOTPRINT "
          f"{footprint_peak():.1f} GB; sha {hashlib.sha1(str(toks).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]} | "
          f"{tok.decode([x for x in toks if x not in EOS])[:150]!r}", flush=True)


### scripts/bt_bild.py
"""Breakthrough D1c (2026-10-21): confidence-gated coverage (BiLD-style fallback) on the fast path, eager decoding.
Each step runs frugal (wait for ranks 0-1, drop missing ranks 2-9 without renormalising: the night probes' best cheap
rule); if the step's next-token entropy exceeds tau, the step is rolled back (MTPLX snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy +
rollback_after_verify, verified_tokens=1) and recomputed with every top-10 expert waited through the native gate.
Engine: ~/Breakthrough/fork125bt (BigMoE ffc859b + selective waiting + protect-what-fits). Spin gate (daytime only:
a SIGSTOP mid-pass invalidates it; the night probes use tf_frugal_var.py's CPU-synchronous residency instead).
usage: bt_bild.py FRAC TAU TASK      TASK = speed (PROMPT env code|prose|edit, BT_N tokens, 128) | gsmN (OFFSET env)
   TAU < 0 = plain frugal (never re-run); TAU >= 99 = exact (every step exact).
Prints tok/s, re-run share, SSD loads per token (ext misses), peak memory; GSM8K accuracy and answers equal to BigMoE's
exact eager run (MoEPrefetch/data/w125_gsm_eager.json, problems 0-149). Checkpoints in data/bild/."""
import hashlib, json, os, re, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, "0")
os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = "0"
os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
for _k, _v in (("BIGMOE_COMPILE_HC", "1"), ("BIGMOE_HC_VREAD", "1"), ("BIGMOE_ASYNC_EVERY", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bt"))
sys.path.insert(1, os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/scripts"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe.gated import ext
from fused_route import topk_probs

OUT = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/bild"); os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
frac, TAU, TASK = float(sys.argv[1]), float(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(MD, "config.json"))); cfg = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
E, K = cfg["num_experts"], cfg["num_experts_per_tok"]
C = int(os.environ.get("BT_C", "0")) or max(16, round(frac * E))
t0 = time.perf_counter()
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, C, mtp=False)
print(f"bt_bild: C={C} of {E} ({100 * C / E:.0f} %), tau {TAU}, loaded in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp
from mtplx.attention_math import attention_gate
from mtplx.cache_state import rollback_after_verify, snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy
orig_call = qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
MODE = {"exact": TAU >= 99}


def block(self, x):
    sw = self.switch_mlp
    bm = getattr(sw, "_bm", None)
    if bm is None or x.shape[1] != 1 or bm["full"]:
        return orig_call(self, x)
    if bm["L"] == bm["first"]:
        bm["gate"].new_pass()
    ae = bm.get("async_every", 0)
    if ae and bm["L"] % ae == ae - 1:
        mx.async_eval(x)
    ids, pk = topk_probs(self.gate(x)[0], K)                            # [1, K] top-10, descending
    nw = K if MODE["exact"] else 2
    slots = bm["gate"].route_and_gate(bm["L"], ids[0], nw=nw, nk=K).reshape(1, K).astype(mx.int32)
    res = slots >= 0
    w = pk * res / pk.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)                      # no renormalisation over the computed ones
    safe = mx.where(res, slots, slots[:, :1]).astype(mx.uint32)
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
    y = (base.__call__(sw, x, safe[None]) * w[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))


qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = block
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
EOS = set()
for _a in ("eos_token_ids", "eos_token_id"):
    _v = getattr(tok, _a, None)
    if _v is not None:
        EOS |= set(_v) if isinstance(_v, (list, set, tuple)) else {_v}
for _t in ("<|im_end|>", "<|endoftext|>"):
    try:
        _i = tok.convert_tokens_to_ids(_t)
        if isinstance(_i, int) and _i >= 0: EOS.add(_i)
    except Exception:
        pass


def chat_ids(text):
    ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    return list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])


def generate(prompt_ids, n, stop_eos=False):
    """greedy BiLD decode -> tokens, stats"""
    from bigmoe.fuse import reset_generation_state
    reset_generation_state(model)
    cache = model.make_cache()
    ids = mx.array([list(prompt_ids)[:-1]])
    for i in range(0, ids.shape[1], 2048):
        lg = model(ids[:, i:i + 2048], cache=cache)
        mx.eval(lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)
    y, out = int(prompt_ids[-1]), []
    s = dict(steps=0, reruns=0, t=0.0)
    ext.reset_stats()
    m0, t0 = ext.stats()["misses"], time.perf_counter()
    while len(out) < n:
        snap = snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy(cache) if 0 <= TAU < 99 else None
        lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
        lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)
        lp = lg - mx.logsumexp(lg)
        ent, nxt = -(mx.exp(lp) * lp).sum(), mx.argmax(lg)
        mx.eval(ent, nxt)
        if snap is not None and float(ent) > TAU:
            rollback_after_verify(cache, snap, verified_tokens=1)
            MODE["exact"] = True
            try:
                lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
                nxt = mx.argmax((lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1])
                mx.eval(nxt)
            finally:
                MODE["exact"] = False
            s["reruns"] += 1
        s["steps"] += 1
        y = int(nxt.item())
        if stop_eos and y in EOS:
            break
        out.append(y)
    st.gate.check()
    s["t"] = time.perf_counter() - t0
    s["loads"] = ext.stats()["misses"] - m0
    return out, s


sha = lambda t: hashlib.sha1(str(list(t)).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
if TASK == "speed":
    P = os.environ.get("PROMPT", "code")
    text = {"code": lambda: open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
            "prose": lambda: ("Explain, in plain prose for a curious 14-year-old, how a CPU cache works and why it makes "

                             + "```\n\nRewrite the whole file with total_value renamed to total everywhere. Output only the full file in one code block.")}[P]()
    ids = chat_ids(text)

          f"to exact eager {same}/{sum(1 for k in keys if int(k) < len(ref))}, re-ran {rr / max(1, steps):.1%} of steps, "
          f"{ld / max(1, steps):.1f} loads/token, {steps / max(tt, 1e-9):.1f} tok/s decode; per-problem "
          f"{''.join(str(ok[k]) for k in keys)}", flush=True)


### scripts/bt_defer.py
"""Breakthrough I1 speed prototype (2026-10-21): half-layer deferred experts on the fast path. fork125bd = fork125bf +
BIGMOE_DEFER=M: in flag mode the loader reads the flagged (waited) experts, releases the GPU with the waited + resident
slots (phase A, late ones -1), then reads the late ones and publishes up to M (position, slot) pairs (phase B).
Semantics = NewIdeas' tf_defer125.py dh_wK: top-10 by score, ranks < K waited, resident ranks combined now, every other
top-10 rank read and its gate-weighted output (on layer L's MLP input) written into the hyper-connection streams with
layer L's inject weights after layer L+1's attention, before its MLP read (phase B's spin is ordered after that
attention); weights p / sum(top-10 p) (no renormalisation); the last layer waits for all. Eager 1-row calls only.
  BT_K=4 waited ranks | BT_M=6 phase-B pairs (more late ids than M: the first ones are waited) | BT_SAME=1: add the late
  experts at the end of layer L instead (within-layer overlap only, for comparison) | BT_K=10: exact (no late)
  BT_THETA=0.5: only ranks before 50 % of the top-10 mass are used at all (tf_defer125's _m50)
  BT_FRUGAL=1: the qualifying frugal (w2f): top-20 candidates, ranks 0-1 waited, the first 10 resident in rank order,
  renormalised; BT_LATE1=1 waits for rank 0 only and adds a missing rank 1 half a layer late (same set and weights).
The GPU marks waited ids with bit 20 and late-eligible ids with bit 21 (fork125bd's loader reads both).
usage: bt_defer.py FRAC TASK   TASK = speed (PROMPT env code|prose, BT_N) | gsmN (OFFSET env) | check (BT_K=10 vs 4)
Prints tok/s, SSD reads per token (waited / late / overflow), peak memory."""
import hashlib, json, os, re, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
for _k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, "0")
KW = int(os.environ.get("BT_K", "4"))
M = int(os.environ.get("BT_M", "6"))
SAME = os.environ.get("BT_SAME") == "1"
THETA = float(os.environ.get("BT_THETA", "0"))
FRUGAL = os.environ.get("BT_FRUGAL") == "1"
LATE1 = FRUGAL and os.environ.get("BT_LATE1") == "1"
os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = "0"
os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
os.environ["BIGMOE_SPLIT"] = str(M)        # allocates the phase-B region buffer (flag-mode rows never take the split path)
os.environ["BIGMOE_DEFER"] = str(M)
for _k, _v in (("BIGMOE_MAXK", "80"), ("BIGMOE_COMPILE_HC", "1"), ("BIGMOE_HC_VREAD", "1"), ("BIGMOE_ASYNC_EVERY", "1"),
               ("BIGMOE_FRSPEC", "1"), ("BIGMOE_MTP_STREAM", "0")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bd"))
sys.path.insert(1, os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/scripts"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe.gated import ext
from fused_route import topk_probs

OUT = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/defer"); os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
frac, TASK = float(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(MD, "config.json"))); cfg = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
E, K, NL = cfg["num_experts"], cfg["num_experts_per_tok"], cfg["num_hidden_layers"]
C = int(os.environ.get("BT_C", "0")) or max(16, round(frac * E))
t0 = time.perf_counter()
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, C, mtp=False)
print(f"bt_defer: C={C} of {E} ({100 * C / E:.0f} %), " + (f"frugal, rank 1 {'late' if LATE1 else 'waited'}" if FRUGAL else f"wait ranks < {KW}" + (f", mass < {THETA}" if THETA else "")) + f", {M} phase-B pairs, "
      f"{'same layer' if SAME else 'half a layer late'}, loaded in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp
from mtplx.models.qwen4_exp import _hyper_residual_write
from mtplx.attention_math import attention_gate
orig_call = qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
STASH, PENDH = {}, {}
KC = 20 if FRUGAL else K
R = mx.arange(KC)
if FRUGAL:                                   # rank 0 waited; rank 1 waited, or late-eligible (bit 21) with BT_LATE1
    FLAGS = mx.where(R == 0, 1 << 20, mx.where(R == 1, (1 << 21) if LATE1 else (1 << 20), 0)).astype(mx.int32)[None]
else:                                        # ranks < K waited, the rest of the top-10 late-eligible
    FLAGS = mx.where(R < KW, 1 << 20, 1 << 21).astype(mx.int32)[None]
WALL = mx.where(R < (2 if FRUGAL else K), 1 << 20, 0).astype(mx.int32)[None]   # last layer: nothing late


def late_fn(xL, wv, dep, seq, n, sw, L):
    """phase B of layer L as a function of a later array (`after`): the spin is ordered after it"""
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]

    def f(after):
        o = mx.isnan(after.reshape(-1)[:1]).astype(mx.int32) * 0
        depB = mx.concatenate([dep[:1] + o, mx.array([n, 0] * M, dtype=mx.int32)])
        lb = ext.spin_wait(depB, L, seq, 2 * M, 1)
        pos, slb = lb[0::2], lb[1::2]
        xf = xL.reshape(-1, xL.shape[-1])
        xpad = mx.concatenate([xf, mx.zeros((1, xf.shape[-1]), dtype=xf.dtype)])
        yB = base.__call__(sw, xpad[pos // n][None], slb[:, None][None].astype(mx.uint32))[0, :, 0, :]   # [M, D]
        wpad = mx.concatenate([wv.reshape(-1), mx.zeros((1,), wv.dtype)])                               # pads: weight 0
        return (yB * wpad[pos][:, None].astype(yB.dtype)).sum(axis=0).reshape(1, 1, -1)
    return f


def block(self, x):
    sw = self.switch_mlp
    bm = getattr(sw, "_bm", None)
    if bm is None or bm["full"] or x.shape[1] != 1:
        return orig_call(self, x)
    L = bm["L"]
    if L == bm["first"]:
        bm["gate"].new_pass()
    ae = bm.get("async_every", 0)
    if ae and L % ae == ae - 1:
        mx.async_eval(x)
    ids, pk = topk_probs(self.gate(x)[0], KC)                          # [1, KC] by score
    last = L == NL - 1
    fl = WALL if last else FLAGS
    use = None
    if THETA > 0 and not FRUGAL:                                       # mass rule: ranks past theta are not used at all
        p10 = pk[:, :K]
        use = ((mx.cumsum(p10, axis=-1) - p10) / p10.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)) < THETA
        fl = mx.where(use | (R < KW), fl, 0)
    flat = (ids.astype(mx.int32) | fl).reshape(-1)
    slots = bm["gate"].route_and_gate(L, flat, nw=255, nk=K).reshape(1, KC).astype(mx.int32)
    dep, seq, n = bm["gate"]._last
    res = slots >= 0
    if FRUGAL:                                                         # rank 0, rank 1 (now or late), then the first
        r1 = (R == 1)[None]                                            # resident ranks 2-19 up to 10 in all
        pool = res & ~r1
        sel = (pool & (mx.cumsum(pool.astype(mx.int32), axis=-1) <= K - 1)) | r1
        w = pk * sel
        w = w / w.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)                          # renormalised over the 10 (as select_resident)
    else:
        w = pk / pk.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)                        # no renormalisation beyond the top-10 sum
        if use is not None:
            w = w * use
    now = res                                                          # late ids are -1 here: phase B adds them
    safe = mx.where(res, slots, slots[:, :1]).astype(mx.uint32)        # rank 0 is always waited -> resident
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
    y = (base.__call__(sw, x, safe[None]) * (w * now)[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
    if not last and (LATE1 or (not FRUGAL and KW < K)):
        STASH[L] = late_fn(x, w, dep, seq, n, sw, L)
    return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))


def layer_call(self, hidden, *, input_ids, ssm_mask, cache):          # MTPLX DecoderLayer.__call__ + the late writes
    if "ple" in self:
        hidden = hidden + self.ple(hidden, input_ids, cache)
    mixed, hyper, inject = self.attn_hyper_connection(hidden)
    if self.is_linear:
        block_out = self.linear_attn(mixed, ssm_mask, cache)
    else:
        block_out = self.self_attn(mixed, cache)
    L = self._bt_L
    for f, inj in PENDH.pop(L, []):                                   # layer L-1's late experts, before this MLP read
        hyper = _hyper_residual_write(hyper, f(block_out), inj)
    mixed, hyper, inject = self.mlp_hyper_connection(hyper, pending=(block_out, inject))
    block_out = self.mlp(mixed)
    hidden = _hyper_residual_write(hyper, block_out, inject)
    f = STASH.pop(L, None)
    if f is not None:
        if SAME:
            hidden = _hyper_residual_write(hidden, f(block_out), inject)
        else:
            PENDH.setdefault(L + 1, []).append((f, inject))
    return hidden


TM = rt.model.language_model.model if hasattr(rt.model, "language_model") else rt.model.model
for i, l in enumerate(TM.layers):
    l._bt_L = i
qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = block
qwen4_exp.DecoderLayer.__call__ = layer_call
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
EOS = set()
for _a in ("eos_token_ids", "eos_token_id"):
    _v = getattr(tok, _a, None)
    if _v is not None:
        EOS |= set(_v) if isinstance(_v, (list, set, tuple)) else {_v}
for _t in ("<|im_end|>", "<|endoftext|>"):
    try:
        _i = tok.convert_tokens_to_ids(_t)
        if isinstance(_i, int) and _i >= 0: EOS.add(_i)
    except Exception:
        pass


def chat_ids(text):
    ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    return list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])


def generate(prompt_ids, n, stop_eos=False):
    """-> tokens, stats (seconds of decode, SSD reads, waited misses, late reads, overflow)"""
    from bigmoe.fuse import reset_generation_state
    reset_generation_state(model)
    STASH.clear(); PENDH.clear()
    out = []
    cache = model.make_cache()
    ids = mx.array([list(prompt_ids)[:-1]])
    for i in range(0, ids.shape[1], 2048):
        lg = model(ids[:, i:i + 2048], cache=cache)
        mx.eval(lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)
    y = int(prompt_ids[-1])
    ext.reset_stats(); ext.wt_reset(); ext.defer_reset()
    m0, t0 = ext.stats()["misses"], time.perf_counter()
    while len(out) < n:
        lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
        y = int(mx.argmax((lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1]).item())
        if stop_eos and y in EOS:
            break
        out.append(y)
    t = time.perf_counter() - t0
    st.gate.check()
    wm = ext.wt_stats()[0]; rows, late, over = ext.defer_stats()
    return out, dict(t=t, n=len(out), reads=ext.stats()["misses"] - m0, waited=wm, late=late, over=over, rows=rows)


sha = lambda t: hashlib.sha1(str(list(t)).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]

          f"{s['reads'] / nt:.1f} (waited misses {s['waited'] / nt:.1f}, late {s['late'] / nt:.1f}, overflow-waited "
          f"{s['over'] / nt:.2f}), peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB, sha {sha(out)} | {tok.decode(out)[:70]!r}", flush=True)
elif TASK.startswith("gsm"):
    N = int(TASK[3:]); OFF = int(os.environ.get("OFFSET", "0"))
    G8 = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))][OFF:OFF + N]
    INSTR = "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n"

        gold = p["answer"].split("####")[-1].strip().replace(",", "")
        a = m[-1].replace(",", "").rstrip(".") if m else None

### scripts/bt_frugal.py
"""Frugal wait-top-2 runtime hook for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 125B (Breakthrough D1, 2026-10-20), for the Eval suite.

    import os, sys; sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/scripts"))
    from bt_frugal import load_frugal
    rt, st = load_frugal(0.10)      # 10 % of each layer's 512 experts resident (C = 51); 0.05 -> C = 26
    # then use rt as an ordinary MTPLX runtime: mtplx.generation.generate_ar(rt, ids, ...), rt.model(...), rt.tokenizer
    # after a run: st.gate.check() raises if any gate timed out (that pass would be invalid)

What it does (the setup measured in data/expA: GSM8K-150 146/150 at 10 %, 143/150 at 5 %, vs 147 exact):
- MoEPrefetch's fork125 streamer (read-only import from ~/MoEPrefetch/fork125) with BIGMOE_SPARE=0, so its background
  prefetch has zero capacity: a missing expert at rank >= 2 is never read from the SSD.
- Every decode step (one token per call): the router's top-20 are computed; ranks 0-1 are waited for (read if missing);
  the K = 10 experts actually used are the first 10 RESIDENT ones in rank order (so missing ranks 2-9 are replaced by
  resident ranks 10-19, else dropped), gates renormalised over the ones used. Resident ranks 2-9 are LRU-touched.
- IMPORTANT: multi-token calls (prefill, or teacher-forcing a sequence in one call) run the EXACT streamed MoE. Frugal
  routing depends on the expert cache, which evolves token by token, so a prefill-only / teacher-forced test of this
  variant must feed the reference tokens one at a time (decode-style calls) to measure frugal behaviour. Prompts can be
  prefilled normally (exact), as in the GSM8K runs.
- Decode is eager (MTP off), on the fork's older BigMoE base (no MTP, no PF_JIT): speed numbers are not the main track's.
"""
import json, os, sys


def load_frugal(frac, nwait=2, mtp=False):
    os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
    for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
        os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
    os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = "0"          # frugal: no background reads of missing low ranks
    os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
    root = os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch")
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(root, "fork125"))
    sys.path.insert(1, os.path.join(root, "scripts"))
    import mlx.core as mx
    from bigmoe_wt.loader import load_streamed
    from fused_route import topk_probs, select_resident
    md = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
    cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(md, "config.json")))
    cfg = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
    E, K = cfg["num_experts"], cfg["num_experts_per_tok"]
    C = max(16, round(frac * E))
    rt, st = load_streamed(md, C, mtp=mtp)
    from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp
    from mtplx.attention_math import attention_gate
    orig_call = qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
    KC = 20

    def block(self, x):
        sw = self.switch_mlp
        bm = getattr(sw, "_bm", None)
        if bm is None or x.shape[1] != 1 or bm["full"]:
            return orig_call(self, x)
        if bm["L"] == bm["first"]:
            bm["gate"].new_pass()
        ids, pk = topk_probs(self.gate(x)[0], KC)
        slots = bm["gate"].route_and_gate(bm["L"], ids[0], nw=nwait, nk=K).reshape(1, KC).astype(mx.int32)
        sl, w = select_resident(slots, pk, topk=K)
        base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
        y = (base.__call__(sw, x, sl[None].astype(mx.uint32)) * w[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
        return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))

    qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = block
    print(f"bt_frugal: C={C} of {E} experts per layer ({100 * C / E:.0f} %), wait for top-{nwait}, BIGMOE_SPARE=0", flush=True)
    return rt, st


### scripts/bt_frugal_spec.py
"""Breakthrough D1 x speculation (2026-10-20): frugal wait-top-2 routing on the streamed 125B with MTP / prompt-lookup
speculative decoding (SpecDecode's spec125.generate, read-only import).

Engine: ~/Breakthrough/fork125bt = MoEPrefetch's fork125 (BigMoE ffc859b + selective waiting) with one change in the
native loader: ensure_wait protects only what fits in the pool (waited ids, then resident candidates in list order), so
S verify rows x KC candidate ids can exceed C at 5-10 % residency without "no free slot" aborts. Eviction is unchanged
whenever all candidates fit (every eager frugal run so far).

Frugal block (every streamed MoE call with S <= 8 rows; longer calls = prefill run the exact streamed path):
  router top-KC per row (KC = min(20, MAXK // S)); the routing row is rank-major (all rows' rank 0, then all rank 1,
  ...), so the loader waits only for the first 2*S ids (each row's ranks 0-1), LRU-touches resident ranks 2-9 and never
  reads a missing one (BIGMOE_SPARE=0); per row the K = 10 experts used are the first 10 resident in rank order,
  gates renormalised over them. S = 1 is exactly the eager frugal setup of data/expA (GSM8K-150 146 at 10 %).
usage: bt_frugal_spec.py FRAC TASK [CONFIGS]
  TASK  smoke : 24 tokens eager + 24 tokens with the first config (mechanics only)
        speed : PROMPT env (code | prose | edit), BT_N tokens (128): eager (ar2, pipelined) then each config
        gsmN  : GSM8K problems OFFSET..OFFSET+N with the first config ('ar2' = eager), 600-token cap, EOS stop;
                checkpoint per problem in data/expS/bt_gsm_<tag>.json; compared with BigMoE's exact eager answers
  CONFIGS: comma list of k_mtp:max_draft:lookup (spec125.generate), default 3:3:1
Env: BT_TAG (checkpoint tag), BT_SMAX (8), BIGMOE_MTP_C (64). Run with BigMoE's venv, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1."""
import hashlib, json, os, re, sys, time

os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
for _k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, "0")
PF = os.environ.get("BT_PF") == "1"        # frugal + look-ahead prefetch of predicted experts into spare slots

if PF:
    os.environ.setdefault("BIGMOE_PREFETCH", "12")
    os.environ.setdefault("BIGMOE_PF_JIT", "1")
    os.environ.setdefault("BIGMOE_PF_QD", "4")

    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
ENG = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bt")

import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed

def block(self, x):
    sw = self.switch_mlp
    bm = getattr(sw, "_bm", None)
    S = x.shape[1]
    kc = min(20, MAXK // max(1, S))
    if bm is None or bm["full"] or S > SMAX or kc < K:
        CALLS["exact"] += 1
        return orig_call(self, x)
    CALLS["frugal"] += 1; CALLS["rows"] += S
    if bm["L"] == bm["first"]:
        bm["gate"].new_pass()
    ae = bm.get("async_every", 0)
    if ae and bm["L"] % ae == ae - 1:   # as the streamed SwitchGLU: hand the graph so far to the GPU
        mx.async_eval(x)
    ids, pk = topk_probs(self.gate(x)[0], kc)                     # [S, kc] router top-kc, softmax probs (descending)
    flat = ids.T.reshape(-1) if S > 1 else ids.reshape(-1)        # rank-major: every row's rank 0 first, then rank 1 ...
    preds, counts = None, (0, 0)
    nx = bm.get("next") if PF else None
    if nx is not None:   # BT_PF: look-ahead predictions for layers L+1.. (stream.py's unranked path) -> spare-slot prefetch
        rows = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1])
        parts, cnt, left = [], [], 30 + 31 * PRED_LINES
        for router, ratio, B in nx:
            b = min(B, left // rows.shape[0])
            if b <= 0:
                break
            lg = router(rows * ratio if ratio is not None else rows)
            if _CORR is not None and router is nx[0][0] and bm["L"] < len(_CORR):   # MoEPrefetch's corrected d=1
                _c = _CORR[bm["L"]]                                               # (BIGMOE_PF_CORR, as stream.py)
                lg = lg.astype(mx.float32) + ((rows.astype(mx.float32) - _c[2]) @ _c[0]) @ _c[1] + _c[3]
            parts.append(mx.argpartition(-lg, kth=b - 1, axis=-1)[:, :b].reshape(-1)); cnt.append(b * rows.shape[0])
            left -= cnt[-1]
        if parts:
            preds = mx.concatenate(parts) if len(parts) > 1 else parts[0]
            counts = tuple(cnt + [0] * (3 - len(cnt))) + (0,)
    slots = bm["gate"].route_and_gate(bm["L"], flat, preds=preds, counts=counts, nw=NW * S, nk=K * S)
    slots = (slots.reshape(kc, S).T if S > 1 else slots.reshape(1, kc)).astype(mx.int32)
    sl, w = select_resident(slots, pk, topk=K)                    # first K resident per row, renormalised
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
    y = (base.__call__(sw, x, sl[None].astype(mx.uint32)) * w[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))


qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = block
tok = rt.tokenizer


def chat_ids(text):
    ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    return list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])


EOS = set()
for _a in ("eos_token_ids", "eos_token_id"):
    _v = getattr(tok, _a, None)
    if _v is not None:
        EOS |= set(_v) if isinstance(_v, (list, set, tuple)) else {_v}
for _t in ("<|im_end|>", "<|endoftext|>"):
    try:
        _i = tok.convert_tokens_to_ids(_t)
        if isinstance(_i, int) and _i >= 0:
            EOS.add(_i)
    except Exception:
        pass
EOS = {int(e) for e in EOS if e is not None}


class _Stop(Exception):
    pass


def gen(ids, n, conf, stop_eos=False):
    """-> tokens, tok/s, stats dict (passes, tok/pass, waited misses / token, ...)"""
    out, stamps = [], []

    def cb(t):
        if stamps == []:
            ext.reset_stats()
            if hasattr(ext, "wt_reset"): ext.wt_reset()
        stamps.append(time.perf_counter())
        if stop_eos and int(t) in EOS:
            raise _Stop
        out.append(int(t))
    s = {}
    try:
        if conf == "ar2":
            D.greedy(rt.model, ids, n, on_token=cb)
        else:
            k, md, lk = (int(v) for v in conf.split(":")[:3])
            spec125.generate(rt.model, ids, n, k_mtp=k, max_draft=md, lookup=lk, stats=s, on_token=cb)
    except _Stop:
        pass
    st.gate.check()
    nd = max(1, len(stamps) - 1)
    tps = nd / (stamps[-1] - stamps[0]) if len(stamps) > 1 else float("nan")
    wm = ext.wt_stats()[0] if hasattr(ext, "wt_stats") else 0
    s.update(waited_per_tok=wm / nd, n=len(out))
    return out, tps, s


sha = lambda t: hashlib.sha1(str(list(t)).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
PROMPTS = {"code": lambda: open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
           "prose": lambda: ("Explain, in plain prose for a curious 14-year-old, how a CPU cache works and why it makes "
                             "programs faster. Use one everyday analogy and keep it to about 300 words."),
           "edit": lambda: ("Here is a file:\n\n```python\n" + open(os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode/scripts/inventory_sample.py")).read()
                            + "```\n\nRewrite the whole file with total_value renamed to total everywhere. Output only the full file in one code block.")}

if TASK in ("smoke", "speed"):

        div = next((i for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(out, ref)) if a != b), None)
        print(f"[{P}] spec {conf} C={C}: {tps:6.1f} tok/s, {s.get('tok_per_pass', float('nan')):.2f} tok/pass, "
              f"lookup {s.get('lookup_used', 0)}/{s.get('passes', 0)}, waited misses/tok {s['waited_per_tok']:.1f}, "
              f"draft {s.get('t_draft', 0):.2f}s verify {s.get('t_verify', 0):.2f}s, refused {s.get('commit_refused', 0)}, "
              f"frugal calls {CALLS['frugal']} ({CALLS['rows'] / max(1, CALLS['frugal']):.2f} rows) exact {CALLS['exact']}, "
              f"sha {sha(out)} {'= ar2' if out == ref else 'differs from ar2 at ' + str(div)} | {tok.decode(out)[:70]!r}", flush=True)

elif TASK.startswith("gsm"):

    probs = G8[OFF:OFF + N]

        m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\**\s*\$?\s*(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", text) or re.findall(r"(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", text)
        gold = p["answer"].split("####")[-1].strip().replace(",", "")

    mean_tps = tot_tok / max(1e-9, sum(n_l[k] / tps_l[k] for k in keys if tps_l[k] == tps_l[k] and tps_l[k] > 0))
    print(f"125B C={C} frugal spec {conf} [{tag}]: GSM8K {sum(ok[k] for k in keys)}/{len(keys)} (offset {OFF}), "
          f"answers equal to exact eager {same}/{nref}, decode {mean_tps:.1f} tok/s (token-weighted, {tot_tok} tokens), "
          f"{time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s, peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB; per-problem "
          f"{''.join(str(ok[k]) for k in keys)}", flush=True)


### scripts/bt_mass.py
"""Breakthrough D1 quality policies on the fast path (2026-10-21): wait-by-gate-mass routing with the wait decision made
on the GPU and carried to the loader as a flag bit (bit 20) on each routing-row id (~/Breakthrough/fork125bf: fork125bt
+ flag mode: post kernel masks the flag, the loader waits for exactly the flagged ids, ensure_wait_list). No CPU sync per
layer, works for multi-row verify (each row waits for its own prefix).
Rule per row: rank r of the top-10 is waited if the gate mass of ranks < r is below theta (so ranks up to the one that
crosses theta); missing non-waited ranks take the first resident ranks 10-19 at their own weight; no renormalisation
(weights = p / sum of the top-10), as tf_frugal_var.py's mNN_nr probes.
  BT_THETA=0.85 (default) | BT_ADA=lo,hi,tau (eager only: theta = lo if the previous step's entropy <= tau else hi)
usage: bt_mass.py FRAC TASK [CONFIG]   TASK = speed (PROMPT env code|prose|edit, BT_N) | gsmN (OFFSET env)
  CONFIG = ar (eager, default) | k:md:lookup (spec125.generate, fixed theta)
Prints tok/s, SSD reads per token (ext misses), peak; GSM8K accuracy + answers equal to BigMoE's exact eager run."""
import hashlib, json, os, re, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
for _k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, "0")
PF = os.environ.get("BT_PF") == "1"        # look-ahead prefetch of predicted experts into spare slots (part of C)
os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = os.environ.get("BT_SPARE", "12") if PF else "0"
if PF:
    os.environ.setdefault("BIGMOE_PREFETCH", "12")
    os.environ.setdefault("BIGMOE_PF_JIT", "1")
    os.environ.setdefault("BIGMOE_PF_QD", "4")
os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
for _k, _v in (("BIGMOE_MAXK", "80"), ("BIGMOE_COMPILE_HC", "1"), ("BIGMOE_HC_VREAD", "1"), ("BIGMOE_ASYNC_EVERY", "1"),
               ("BIGMOE_FRSPEC", "1"), ("BIGMOE_MTP_STREAM", "1"), ("BIGMOE_MTP_C", "64")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
if (sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else "ar") == "ar":
    os.environ["BIGMOE_MTP_STREAM"] = "0"   # no MTP head in eager runs: a streamed MTP gate layer would be a phantom
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bf"))
sys.path.insert(1, os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/scripts"))
sys.path.insert(2, os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe.gated import ext, MAXK, PRED_LINES
from fused_route import topk_probs

OUT = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/mass"); os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
frac, TASK = float(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
CONF = sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else "ar"
cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(MD, "config.json"))); cfg = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
E, K = cfg["num_experts"], cfg["num_experts_per_tok"]
C = int(os.environ.get("BT_C", "0")) or max(16, round(frac * E))
THETA = float(os.environ.get("BT_THETA", "0.85"))
ADA = [float(v) for v in os.environ["BT_ADA"].split(",")] if os.environ.get("BT_ADA") else None
assert ADA is None or CONF == "ar", "adaptive theta is eager-only"
t0 = time.perf_counter()
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, C, mtp=CONF != "ar")
print(f"bt_mass: C={C} of {E} ({100 * C / E:.0f} %), theta {THETA if ADA is None else 'ada %s' % ADA}, {CONF}, "
      f"loaded in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp
from mtplx.attention_math import attention_gate
orig_call = qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
PREV_ENT = {"v": mx.array(99.0)}         # previous step's next-token entropy (lazy, on the GPU)


def block(self, x):
    sw = self.switch_mlp
    bm = getattr(sw, "_bm", None)
    S = x.shape[1]
    kc = min(20, MAXK // max(1, S))
    if bm is None or bm["full"] or S > 8 or kc < K:
        return orig_call(self, x)
    if bm["L"] == bm["first"]:
        bm["gate"].new_pass()
    ae = bm.get("async_every", 0)
    if ae and bm["L"] % ae == ae - 1:
        mx.async_eval(x)
    ids, pk = topk_probs(self.gate(x)[0], kc)                           # [S, kc]
    p10 = pk[:, :K]; tot = p10.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    before = (mx.cumsum(p10, axis=-1) - p10) / tot                       # mass of the ranks before r
    th = mx.array(THETA) if ADA is None else mx.where(PREV_ENT["v"] <= ADA[2], ADA[0], ADA[1])
    wait = before < th                                                   # rank 0 always waited
    if S > 1 and S * K > C - 4:   # IDEAS D1d: a pass may not wait for more distinct experts than the pool holds
        M = max(0, C - 4 - S)                                            # every row's rank 0, then M more by priority
        prio = mx.where(wait, p10 / tot * (0.8 ** mx.arange(S, dtype=mx.float32))[:, None], -1.0)
        prio = mx.concatenate([mx.full((S, 1), -1.0), prio[:, 1:]], axis=-1)   # rank 0 is handled separately
        kth = mx.sort(prio.reshape(-1))[-M] if M > 0 else mx.array(9.0)
        wait = mx.concatenate([mx.ones((S, 1), mx.bool_), (prio[:, 1:] >= kth) & (prio[:, 1:] >= 0)], axis=-1)
    wait = wait.astype(mx.int32)
    flags = mx.concatenate([wait << 20, mx.zeros((S, kc - K), mx.int32)], axis=-1) if kc > K else wait << 20
    idf = ids.astype(mx.int32) | flags
    flat = idf.T.reshape(-1) if S > 1 else idf.reshape(-1)               # rank-major
    preds, counts = None, (0, 0)
    nx = bm.get("next") if PF else None
    if nx is not None:   # BT_PF: look-ahead predictions for layers L+1.. (stream.py's unranked path) -> spare-slot prefetch
        rows = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1])
        parts, cnt, left = [], [], 30 + 31 * PRED_LINES
        for router, ratio, B in nx:
            b = min(B, left // rows.shape[0])
            if b <= 0:
                break
            lg = router(rows * ratio if ratio is not None else rows)
            parts.append(mx.argpartition(-lg, kth=b - 1, axis=-1)[:, :b].reshape(-1)); cnt.append(b * rows.shape[0])
            left -= cnt[-1]
        if parts:
            preds = mx.concatenate(parts) if len(parts) > 1 else parts[0]
            counts = tuple(cnt + [0] * (3 - len(cnt))) + (0,)
    slots = bm["gate"].route_and_gate(bm["L"], flat, preds=preds, counts=counts, nw=255, nk=K * S)
    slots = (slots.reshape(kc, S).T if S > 1 else slots.reshape(1, kc)).astype(mx.int32)
    res = slots >= 0
    sel = res & (mx.cumsum(res.astype(mx.int32), axis=-1) <= K)         # first K resident in rank order
    w = pk * sel / tot                                                   # no renormalisation
    safe = mx.where(sel, slots, slots[:, :1]).astype(mx.uint32)
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
    y = (base.__call__(sw, x, safe[None]) * w[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))


qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = block
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
EOS = set()
for _a in ("eos_token_ids", "eos_token_id"):
    _v = getattr(tok, _a, None)
    if _v is not None:
        EOS |= set(_v) if isinstance(_v, (list, set, tuple)) else {_v}
for _t in ("<|im_end|>", "<|endoftext|>"):
    try:
        _i = tok.convert_tokens_to_ids(_t)
        if isinstance(_i, int) and _i >= 0: EOS.add(_i)
    except Exception:
        pass


def chat_ids(text):
    ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    return list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])


class _Stop(Exception):
    pass


def generate(prompt_ids, n, stop_eos=False):
    """-> tokens, stats (steps, seconds of decode, SSD reads)"""
    from bigmoe.fuse import reset_generation_state
    reset_generation_state(model)
    out, s = [], {}
    if CONF == "ar":
        cache = model.make_cache()
        ids = mx.array([list(prompt_ids)[:-1]])
        for i in range(0, ids.shape[1], 2048):
            lg = model(ids[:, i:i + 2048], cache=cache)
            mx.eval(lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)
        y = int(prompt_ids[-1]); PREV_ENT["v"] = mx.array(99.0)
        ext.reset_stats(); m0, t0 = ext.stats()["misses"], time.perf_counter()
        while len(out) < n:
            lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
            lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)
            nxt = mx.argmax(lg)
            if ADA is not None:
                lp = lg - mx.logsumexp(lg); PREV_ENT["v"] = -(mx.exp(lp) * lp).sum()
            y = int(nxt.item())
            if stop_eos and y in EOS:
                break
            out.append(y)
    else:
        import spec125
        k, md, lk = (int(v) for v in CONF.split(":")[:3])
        first = {}                                  # clock and read counter start at the first generated token
        m0, t0 = None, None                         # (spec125.generate runs the prompt prefill itself)

        def cb(t):
            if not first:
                first.update(t=time.perf_counter(), m=ext.stats()["misses"])
            if stop_eos and int(t) in EOS:
                raise _Stop
            out.append(int(t))
        try:
            spec125.generate(model, prompt_ids, n, k_mtp=k, max_draft=md, lookup=lk, stats=s, on_token=cb)
        except _Stop:
            pass
    st.gate.check()
    if CONF != "ar":                                # decode only: from the first token on (one token of the count fewer)
        t0, m0 = first.get("t", time.perf_counter()), first.get("m", ext.stats()["misses"])
        s.update(t=time.perf_counter() - t0, reads=ext.stats()["misses"] - m0, n=max(1, len(out) - 1))
        return out, s
    s.update(t=time.perf_counter() - t0, reads=ext.stats()["misses"] - m0, n=len(out))
    return out, s


sha = lambda t: hashlib.sha1(str(list(t)).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
if TASK == "speed":
    P = os.environ.get("PROMPT", "code")
    text = {"code": lambda: open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),

                             + "```\n\nRewrite the whole file with total_value renamed to total everywhere. Output only the full file in one code block.")}[P]()
    ids = chat_ids(text)
    N = int(os.environ.get("BT_N", "128"))

    out, s = generate(ids, N)
    print(f"[{P}] mass C={C} theta {THETA if ADA is None else ADA} {CONF}: {s['n'] / s['t']:.1f} tok/s (incl. the first step), "
          f"{s['reads'] / max(1, s['n']):.1f} SSD reads/token ({s['reads'] / max(1, s['n']) * 2.765:.0f} MB), "
          f"{s.get('tok_per_pass', float('nan')):.2f} tok/pass, peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB, sha {sha(out)} | "

elif TASK.startswith("gsm"):

    G8 = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))][OFF:OFF + N]
    INSTR = "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n"

        m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\**\s*\$?\s*(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", text) or re.findall(r"(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", text)
        gold = p["answer"].split("####")[-1].strip().replace(",", "")
        a = m[-1].replace(",", "").rstrip(".") if m else None
        try:
            good = int(a is not None and abs(float(a) - float(gold)) < 1e-6)
        except ValueError:
            good = 0
        ans[key], ok[key], st_l[key] = a, good, {k2: s[k2] for k2 in ("t", "reads", "n")}
        json.dump({"answers": ans, "ok": ok, "stats": st_l, "C": C, "theta": THETA, "ada": ADA, "conf": CONF}, open(ck, "w"))
    keys = [str(OFF + j) for j in range(len(G8))]
    ref = json.load(open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/w125_gsm_eager.json")))["answers"]
    same = sum(1 for k in keys if int(k) < len(ref) and ref[int(k)] == ans.get(k))
    nt = sum(st_l[k]["n"] for k in keys); tt = sum(st_l[k]["t"] for k in keys); rd = sum(st_l[k]["reads"] for k in keys)
    print(f"125B C={C} mass {THETA if ADA is None else ADA} {CONF} [{tag}]: GSM8K {sum(ok[k] for k in keys)}/{len(keys)} "
          f"(offset {OFF}), answers equal to exact eager {same}/{sum(1 for k in keys if int(k) < len(ref))}, "
          f"{nt / max(tt, 1e-9):.1f} tok/s decode, {rd / max(1, nt):.1f} reads/token; per-problem "
          f"{''.join(str(ok[k]) for k in keys)}", flush=True)


### scripts/bt_waittop125.py
"""Breakthrough copy of MoEPrefetch scripts/waittop125.py (2026-10-19): identical logic; reads MoEPrefetch's fork125
and GSM8K data read-only, writes checkpoints only to ~/Breakthrough/data/expA. Original docstring:
Selective waiting on Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 125B (BigMoE's model, MTPLX runtime), forked gate in fork125/
(upstream BigMoE 1fde1b8 + fork/apply_patch.py). Decode only (MTP off: BigMoE measured no MTP gain here).
  waittop125.py FRAC MODE TASK
    MODE  exact  : BigMoE's unmodified streamed block (reference)
          w10    : our block, wait for all top-10 (should reproduce exact)
          w2     : wait for missing top-2 of 10, substitute ranks 3-10 from resident ranks 11-20
          wl     : layer-aware: early layers (0-11 of 48) wait only for top-1, later layers top-2
                   (the gate cannot express "wait for none" without a C++ change; top-1 is the conservative stand-in)
    TASK  speed  : BigMoE's code prompt, 128 tokens -> tok/s, stalls, sha
          trace  : (MODE exact) log each decode token's router top-20 per layer on code/chat/math prompts
                   -> data/traces125/<name>.npz  (125B routing traces for residency policy work)
          gsmN   : first N GSM8K problems (env OFFSET), greedy, 600-token cap -> accuracy + per-problem string
Run with BigMoE's venv. Model ~/BigMoE/models/bare is read-only. Summary only."""
import hashlib, json, os, re, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
os.environ.setdefault("BIGMOE_SPARE", "8")
os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
ROOT = os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch")          # read-only: fork125, data/gsm8k
OUT = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/expA")  # all writes go here
UPSTREAM = os.environ.get("W125_UPSTREAM") == "1"     # exact mode via BigMoE's current package (its new flags)
import numpy as np

if UPSTREAM:
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
    from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
    from bigmoe.gated import ext
else:
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "fork125"))
    from bigmoe_wt.loader import load_streamed
    from bigmoe_wt.gated import ext
sys.path.insert(1, os.path.join(ROOT, "scripts"))
from fused_route import topk_probs, select_resident

MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
frac, MODE, TASK = float(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(MD, "config.json"))); cfg = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
E, K = cfg["num_experts"], cfg["num_experts_per_tok"]
C = max(16, round(frac * E))
if MODE == "exact":
    os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = "0"
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, C, mtp=False)

if MODE != "exact":
    from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp
    from mtplx.attention_math import attention_gate
    orig_call = qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
    KC = 20
    NK = int(os.environ.get("BT_NK", K))   # Breakthrough: ranks nw..NK-1 are LRU-touched / loaded in the background;
                                           # BT_NK=2 with w2 = frugal mode: missing ranks >= 2 are never read from the SSD

    def block(self, x):
        sw = self.switch_mlp
        bm = getattr(sw, "_bm", None)
        if bm is None or x.shape[1] != 1 or bm["full"]:
            return orig_call(self, x)
        if bm["L"] == bm["first"]:
            bm["gate"].new_pass()
        ids, pk = topk_probs(self.gate(x)[0], KC)        # fused: softmax over 512 + bitonic top-20
        if MODE == "w10":
            sl = bm["gate"].route_and_gate(bm["L"], ids[0, :K]).reshape(1, 1, K)
            w = pk[None, :, :K]
            if self.norm_topk_prob:
                w = w / mx.maximum(w.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True), 1e-6)
        else:
            nw = 1 if (MODE == "wl" and bm["L"] < 12) else 2
            slots = bm["gate"].route_and_gate(bm["L"], ids[0], nw=nw, nk=NK).reshape(1, KC).astype(mx.int32)
            sl, w = select_resident(slots, pk, topk=K)     # fused: first K resident, renormalised
            sl, w = sl[None], w[None]
        base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
        y = (base.__call__(sw, x, sl.astype(mx.uint32)) * w[..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
        return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))

    qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = block

TRACE = {}
if TASK == "trace":
    from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp as _q
    _orig_tr = _q.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
    def _traced(self, x):
        bm = getattr(self.switch_mlp, "_bm", None)
        if bm is not None and x.shape[1] == 1:
            g = self.gate(x)[0, 0].astype(mx.float32)
            top = mx.argsort(-g, axis=-1)[:20]
            TRACE.setdefault(bm["L"], []).append(top.astype(mx.int16))
            TRACE.setdefault(f"G{bm['L']}", []).append(mx.softmax(g)[top].astype(mx.float16))   # Breakthrough: router probs
            for d, (router, ratio, _B) in enumerate(bm.get("next") or [], 1):   # BigMoE's look-ahead predictor:
                xin = x[0, 0] * ratio if ratio is not None else x[0, 0]           # router_{L+d}(MoE input of L), top-32
                TRACE.setdefault(f"P{d}_{bm['L']}", []).append(mx.argsort(-router(xin), axis=-1)[:32].astype(mx.int16))
        return _orig_tr(self, x)
    _q.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = _traced

from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx import generation as G
greedy = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)


def chat_ids(text):
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    return list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])


import threading
_last = [None]                       # Breakthrough watchdog: time of the last generated token (None = not started)


def _watchdog(limit=float(os.environ.get("BT_WATCHDOG_S", "180"))):
    while True:
        time.sleep(10)
        if _last[0] is not None and time.time() - _last[0] > limit:
            print(f"HANG watchdog: no token for {time.time() - _last[0]:.0f}s, exiting", flush=True)
            os._exit(3)


threading.Thread(target=_watchdog, daemon=True).start()


def run(ids, n):
    stamps = []
    _last[0] = time.time()           # a new generation: prefill + first tokens must arrive within the limit
    def cb(toks):
        _last[0] = time.time()
        if not stamps:
            ext.reset_stats()
            if hasattr(ext, 'wt_reset'): ext.wt_reset()
        stamps.append((time.perf_counter(), len(toks) if isinstance(toks, list) else 1))
    res = G.generate_ar(rt, ids, max_tokens=n, sampler=greedy, token_callback=cb)
    toks = getattr(res, "tokens", None) or getattr(res, "token_ids", None) or (res.get("tokens") if isinstance(res, dict) else None)
    if toks is None and isinstance(res, tuple): toks = res[0]
    toks = list(toks or [])
    n_dec = max(1, sum(k for _, k in stamps) - stamps[0][1]) if stamps else 1
    dec = stamps[-1][0] - stamps[0][0] if len(stamps) > 1 else float("nan")
    return toks, n_dec / dec if dec == dec and dec > 0 else float("nan"), n_dec

### scripts/combo_sim.py
"""How the levers multiply (Breakthrough, 2026-10-19). 125B routing traces (MoEPrefetch traces125, top-20 ids per
token/layer). A pass covers K consecutive tokens (K=1 eager, K=4 ~ a depth-3 MTP verify pass when all drafts are
accepted). Per pass and layer: ranks < r of every row are exact (fetched if not resident); a row's rank >= r uses its
expert if resident, else the best-ranked resident expert among that row's ranks 10-19 not already used by the row, else
the rank is dropped. Strict LRU capacity C per layer (experts used in the pass become MRU). r=10 = exact routing.
Cache sizes in GB depend on bits per weight: an expert is 4.9152M weights x b/8 bytes (4.5 bits today, 2.5 if the
bits direction works). Prints SSD MB per token = fetches/token x expert MB.
"""
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict
T = "/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/"; SETS = ["code", "chat", "bulgarian", "math"]; NL = 48
trs = {s: np.stack([np.load(T + f"{s}_800.npz")[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for s in SETS}
def run(tr, C, r, K):
    fetch = subs = drops = 0
    for l in range(NL):
        c = OrderedDict()
        for a in range(0, tr.shape[0], K):
            used_all = []
            for t in range(a, min(a + K, tr.shape[0])):
                row = [int(x) for x in tr[t, l]]
                alt = [e for e in row[10:20] if e in c]
                used = []
                for rank, e in enumerate(row[:10]):
                    if rank < r or e in c or e in used_all:
                        if e not in c and e not in used_all: fetch += 1
                        used.append(e)
                    else:
                        x = next((y for y in alt if y not in used), None)
                        if x is None: drops += 1
                        else: subs += 1; used.append(x); alt.remove(x)
                used_all += [e for e in used if e not in used_all]
            for e in used_all:
                if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                else: c[e] = 1
            while len(c) > C: c.popitem(last=False)
    n = tr.shape[0]; return fetch / n, subs / n, drops / n
import sys
cases = [(c.split(":")[0], float(c.split(":")[1]), float(c.split(":")[2])) for c in sys.argv[1:]] or [  # (label, GB of expert cache, bits/weight)
    ("12 GB total today (5.4 GB, 4.5 b)", 5.4, 4.5), ("6.9 GB cache, 4.5 b", 6.9, 4.5),
    ("12 GB total + bits/dense diet (7.6 GB, 2.5 b)", 7.6, 2.5), ("6 GB total + diet (1.6 GB, 2.5 b)", 1.6, 2.5),
    ("17 GB cache, 4.5 b (today's 24 GB)", 17.0, 4.5)]
print("SSD MB per token (fetches/token) [substituted/dropped ranks per token], mean of 4 prompts")
for label, gb, b in cases:
    mb = 4.9152 * b / 8; C = int(gb * 1000 / mb / NL)
    print(f"\n{label}: expert {mb:.2f} MB, C={C} per layer ({100*C/512:.1f} %)")
    for K in (1, 4, 16):
        cells = []
        for r in (10, 3, 2, 1):
            f, s_, d = np.mean([run(trs[s], C, r, K) for s in SETS], 0)
            cells.append(f"r={r:<2d} {f*mb:5.0f} MB ({f:5.1f}) [{s_:4.0f}/{d:4.0f}]")
        print(f"  K={K:<3d} " + "   ".join(cells))


### scripts/d3_mustfix.py
"""D3 first experiment: the must-fix gap F (Breakthrough, 2026-10-20). How many tokens can a small in-RAM drafter write
before the 125B would have to correct it, under exact and lossy acceptance rules? (research/sparse_consult.md: chunked
verification only beats eager decoding on the SSD if F >= ~100-250, and it is the key number for one-stream speed.)
  gen125 C  : the 125B (BigMoE's streamed runtime, exact eager greedy, C = fraction of experts resident) answers 40 prompts
              (20 GSM8K, 8 code, 8 knowledge, 4 Bulgarian; <= 320 tokens), then is teacher-forced over each prompt +
              answer to log its top-20 log-probs at every answer position -> data/d3/gen125.npz
  draft NAME: a same-vocabulary drafter (q2b / q4b = Qwen3.5-2B / 4B MLX 4-bit) teacher-forced over the same tokens:
              its top-1, top-5, entropy and its log-prob of the 125B's token -> data/d3/draft_NAME.npz
  analyze   : (CPU) per drafter and acceptance rule, must-fix rate and F = answer tokens / (must-fix tokens + 1) per
              answer, pooled and by prompt type; AUROC of the drafter's entropy as a stop predictor. Teacher-forced: the
              drafter always continues from the 125B's own text (the usual approximation; real drafts diverge after a
              lossy accept, so end-to-end answer identity is the later check).
"""
import glob, json, os, sys, time
import numpy as np

OUT = os.path.expanduser(os.environ.get("BT_D3_OUT", "~/Breakthrough/data/d3"))
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
DRAFT = {"q2b": "models--mlx-community--Qwen3.5-2B-MLX-4bit", "q4b": "models--mlx-community--Qwen3.5-4B-MLX-4bit"}
CODE = ["Write a Python function that returns the n-th Fibonacci number iteratively, with a docstring and two tests.",
        "Write a Python function that parses an ISO 8601 date string (YYYY-MM-DD) and returns the day of the week.",
        "Write a shell one-liner that counts the lines of every .py file under the current directory, largest first.",
        "Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put in O(1), using collections.OrderedDict.",
        "Explain and fix the bug: def avg(xs): return sum(xs) / len(xs) - it crashes on an empty list.",
        "Write a SQL query that returns the top 3 customers by total order amount from tables customers and orders.",
        "Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list without using sort().",
        "Write a JavaScript function debounce(fn, ms) and show how to use it on a window resize handler."]
KNOW = ["Why is the sky blue? Explain briefly.", "What causes the seasons on Earth?",
        "Explain how a hash table works and what a collision is.", "What is the difference between TCP and UDP?",
        "Summarize the causes of the First World War in a short paragraph.", "How does a vaccine train the immune system?",
        "What is the time complexity of binary search, and why?", "Explain what inflation is and one way central banks fight it."]
BG = ["Разкажи ми накратко историята на Рилския манастир.", "Обясни какво е фотосинтеза с прости думи.",
      "Какви са предимствата и недостатъците на електрическите коли?", "Напиши кратко писмо до приятел, в което го каниш на рожден ден."]


def prompts():
    gsm = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))][300:320]
    ps = [("gsm", "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + p["question"])
          for p in gsm]
    return ps + [("code", t) for t in CODE] + [("know", t) for t in KNOW] + [("bg", t) for t in BG]


def gen125(frac):
    import mlx.core as mx
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
    os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
    from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
    from bigmoe import decode as D
    from bigmoe.fuse import reset_generation_state
    cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(MD, "config.json")))
    E = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)["num_experts"]
    rt, st = load_streamed(MD, max(16, round(frac * E)), mtp=False)
    tok, model = rt.tokenizer, rt.model
    rec = {"kind": [], "plen": [], "seq": [], "top": [], "tlp": [], "match": []}
    t0 = time.time()
    for kind, text in prompts():
        ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
        ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
        out = D.greedy(model, ids, 320)
        eos = getattr(tok, "eos_token_id", None)
        if eos is not None and eos in out:
            out = out[:out.index(eos) + 1]
        seq = ids + out
        reset_generation_state(model)
        cache = model.make_cache()
        lg = model(mx.array([seq[:-1]]), cache=cache)
        lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, len(ids) - 1:].astype(mx.float32)
        lp = lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        top = mx.argsort(-lp, axis=-1)[:, :20]
        tlp = mx.take_along_axis(lp, top, axis=-1)
        mx.eval(top, tlp)
        top, tlp = np.array(top), np.array(tlp)
        rec["kind"].append(kind); rec["plen"].append(len(ids)); rec["seq"].append(np.array(seq, np.int32))
        rec["top"].append(top.astype(np.int32)); rec["tlp"].append(tlp.astype(np.float16))
        rec["match"].append(float((top[:, 0] == np.array(out)).mean()))
        reset_generation_state(model)
        print(f"{kind:4s} answer {len(out):3d} tok, teacher-forced top-1 = decoded token {rec['match'][-1]:.3f}, "
              f"{time.time() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
    np.savez(os.path.join(OUT, "gen125.npz"), kind=np.array(rec["kind"]), plen=np.array(rec["plen"]),
             **{f"seq{i}": s for i, s in enumerate(rec["seq"])}, **{f"top{i}": s for i, s in enumerate(rec["top"])},
             **{f"tlp{i}": s for i, s in enumerate(rec["tlp"])}, match=np.array(rec["match"]))
    st.gate.check()
    print(f"gen125: {len(rec['seq'])} answers, mean decode/teacher-forced agreement {np.mean(rec['match']):.4f}, "
          f"{time.time() - t0:.0f}s, peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB", flush=True)


def draft(name):
    import mlx.core as mx
    from mlx_lm import load
    g = np.load(os.path.join(OUT, "gen125.npz"))
    model, _ = load(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser(f"~/.cache/huggingface/hub/{DRAFT[name]}/snapshots/*/"))[0])
    rec = {}
    for i in range(len(g["kind"])):
        seq, plen = g[f"seq{i}"], int(g["plen"][i])
        lg = model(mx.array(seq[None, :-1]))
        lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, plen - 1:].astype(mx.float32)
        lp = lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        top5 = mx.argsort(-lp, axis=-1)[:, :5]
        ent = -(mx.exp(lp) * lp).sum(-1)
        own = mx.take_along_axis(lp, mx.array(seq[plen:, None].astype(np.int32)), axis=-1)[:, 0]
        mx.eval(top5, ent, own)
        rec[f"top5_{i}"], rec[f"ent{i}"], rec[f"own{i}"] = np.array(top5), np.array(ent), np.array(own)
    np.savez(os.path.join(OUT, f"draft_{name}.npz"), **rec)
    print(f"draft {name}: {len(g['kind'])} answers, peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB", flush=True)


def auroc(score, label):
    order = np.argsort(score); r = np.empty(len(score)); r[order] = np.arange(1, len(score) + 1)
    n1 = label.sum(); n0 = len(label) - n1
    return (r[label].sum() - n1 * (n1 + 1) / 2) / max(n1 * n0, 1)


def analyze():
    g = np.load(os.path.join(OUT, "gen125.npz"))
    kinds = g["kind"]
    print(f"125B decode vs teacher-forced top-1 agreement {g['match'].mean():.4f} over {len(kinds)} answers")
    rules = {"exact top-1": lambda d, top, tlp: d != top[:, 0],
             "in 125B top-3": lambda d, top, tlp: ~(top[:, :3] == d[:, None]).any(1),
             "margin <= 1 nat": lambda d, top, tlp: margin(d, top, tlp) > 1.0,
             "margin <= 2 nats": lambda d, top, tlp: margin(d, top, tlp) > 2.0,
             "margin <= 3 nats": lambda d, top, tlp: margin(d, top, tlp) > 3.0}
    for name in DRAFT:
        f = os.path.join(OUT, f"draft_{name}.npz")
        if not os.path.exists(f):
            continue
        dr = np.load(f)
        print(f"\ndrafter {name}: must-fix share / F (answer tokens per must-fix) pooled and gsm/code/know/bg; "
              f"AUROC of drafter entropy for must-fix")
        for rname, rule in rules.items():
            mf, n, per = [], [], {}
            labels, ents = [], []
            for i in range(len(kinds)):
                top, tlp = g[f"top{i}"].astype(np.int64), g[f"tlp{i}"].astype(np.float32)
                d = dr[f"top5_{i}"][:, 0].astype(np.int64)
                m = rule(d, top, tlp)
                mf.append(m.sum()); n.append(len(m))
                per.setdefault(str(kinds[i]), [0, 0]); per[str(kinds[i])][0] += m.sum(); per[str(kinds[i])][1] += len(m)
                labels.append(m); ents.append(dr[f"ent{i}"])
            F = sum(n) / (sum(mf) + len(n))
            lab, en = np.concatenate(labels), np.concatenate(ents)
            pk = "  ".join(f"{k} {v[1] / (v[0] + 1):6.1f}" for k, v in per.items())
            print(f"  {rname:18s} must-fix {sum(mf) / sum(n):6.3f}  F {F:6.1f}   [{pk}]  AUROC {auroc(en, lab):.3f}")


def margin(d, top, tlp):
    hit = top == d[:, None]
    m = np.full(len(d), 99.0)
    rows = hit.any(1)
    m[rows] = tlp[rows, 0] - tlp[rows][hit[rows]]
    return m


if __name__ == "__main__":
    {"gen125": lambda: gen125(float(sys.argv[2])), "draft": lambda: draft(sys.argv[2]), "analyze": analyze}[sys.argv[1]]()


### scripts/energy_sim.py
"""Missed gate ENERGY under frugal policies (Breakthrough, 2026-10-21 night). The 125B's routed experts give mutually
orthogonal outputs (sim_table.py: best |cos| median 0.024) with similar norms within a layer (CV 0.15), so the error a
missing expert leaves is ~ w_r^2 ||y||^2 and errors of different experts add: the natural predictor of KLD is the missed
gate energy E = sum over missing ranks of w_r^2 (top-10 renormalised weights), and for an expert read at b bits instead
of dropped, NMSE_b * w_r^2. This replays the 125B traces (data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz) with an LRU cache of C
experts per layer for the probe policies and prints mean missed mass, missed energy and loads/token, plus per 8-layer
band. Fit KLD ~ a * energy on the probes afterwards (fit_energy in this file's tail).   usage: energy_sim.py [C]"""
import glob, os, sys
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict

NL = 48
sets = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
        for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz")))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in sets.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in sets.items()}
w = {k: g[:, :, :10] / g[:, :, :10].sum(-1, keepdims=True) for k, g in gp.items()}


def run(C, nw_rule, band=None):
    """nw_rule(wt_row) -> ranks waited; band=(a, b): policy only there, exact elsewhere. Returns per-token means."""
    mass = np.zeros(NL); en = np.zeros(NL); loads = 0; ntok = 0
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt = ids[k], w[k]
        ntok += tr.shape[0]
        for l in range(NL):
            c = OrderedDict()
            inband = band is None or band[0] <= l < band[1]
            for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
                row, ww = tr[t, l], wt[t, l]
                nw = nw_rule(ww) if inband else 10
                for r in range(10):
                    e = int(row[r])
                    if e in c:
                        c.move_to_end(e)
                    elif r < nw:
                        c[e] = 1; loads += 1
                    else:
                        mass[l] += ww[r]; en[l] += ww[r] ** 2
                while len(c) > C:
                    c.popitem(last=False)
    return mass / ntok, en / ntok, loads / ntok


def theta(th):
    return lambda ww: int(np.argmax(np.cumsum(ww) >= th - 1e-6)) + 1


C = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 51
pol = {"r2 (base/noren)": lambda ww: 2, "w4": lambda ww: 4, "m50": theta(0.5), "m70": theta(0.7), "m85": theta(0.85)}
print(f"C={C}: per token (48 layers): missed mass / layer-mean, missed energy sum w^2 / layer-mean, loads")
for name, f in pol.items():
    m, e, ld = run(C, f)
    print(f"  {name:16s} mass {m.mean():.3f}  energy {e.mean():.4f}  loads {ld:6.1f}   per band energy: "
          + " ".join(f"{e[a:a + 8].mean():.4f}" for a in range(0, 48, 8)), flush=True)


### scripts/evict_sim.py
"""Can the cache recover frugal's missed gate mass for free? (Breakthrough, 2026-10-20 22:10.) Frugal wait-top-2 replay
on the 125B traces with router probabilities (data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz), per layer capacity C, only waited
experts are loaded; missing ranks 2-9 are skipped (no substitution here: the mass that is NOT computed is the metric).
Eviction policies (victim = a resident expert not used by the current token):
  lru      : least recently used (uses = computed experts)
  lfu-m    : lowest decayed routed mass: score = d*score + p_r for every expert in the token's top-20 (resident or not)
  belady   : oracle, evict the expert whose next appearance in a top-10 is farthest (upper bound for prediction)
  belady-m : oracle, evict the lowest future gate mass over the next H tokens (mass-aware bound)
Prints missed top-10 mass per token-layer (mean, P99) and loads/token.  usage: evict_sim.py [C ...]"""
import bisect, glob, sys
import numpy as np

NL = 48
sets = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
        for f in sorted(glob.glob("/Users/kamen/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz"))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in sets.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in sets.items()}
w = {k: g[:, :, :10] / g[:, :, :10].sum(-1, keepdims=True) for k, g in gp.items()}


def run(C, pol, decay=0.97, H=64):
    loads = ntok = 0
    missed = []
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt, g20 = ids[k], w[k], gp[k]
        T = tr.shape[0]; ntok += T
        for l in range(NL):
            occ = {}
            for t in range(T):
                for r in range(10):
                    occ.setdefault(int(tr[t, l, r]), []).append(t)
            res, score, last = set(), {}, {}
            for t in range(T):
                row = [int(e) for e in tr[t, l, :10]]
                if pol == "lfu-m":
                    for e, p in zip(tr[t, l], g20[t, l]):
                        e = int(e); score[e] = score.get(e, 0.0) * 1.0 + float(p)   # decay applied lazily below
                used, miss = set(), 0.0
                for r, e in enumerate(row):
                    if r < 2:
                        if e not in res:
                            loads += 1
                            if len(res) >= C:
                                cand = [x for x in res if x not in used and x not in row[:2]]
                                if pol == "lru":
                                    v = min(cand, key=lambda x: last.get(x, -1))
                                elif pol == "lfu-m":
                                    v = min(cand, key=lambda x: score.get(x, 0.0))
                                elif pol == "belady":
                                    def nxt(x):
                                        o = occ.get(x, []); i = bisect.bisect_right(o, t)
                                        return o[i] if i < len(o) else 10 ** 9
                                    v = max(cand, key=nxt)
                                else:   # belady-m: future routed mass in the next H tokens
                                    def fm(x):
                                        o = occ.get(x, []); i = bisect.bisect_right(o, t); j = bisect.bisect_right(o, t + H)
                                        return sum(float(wt[s, l][list(tr[s, l, :10]).index(x)]) for s in o[i:j])
                                    v = min(cand, key=fm)
                                res.discard(v)
                            res.add(e)
                        used.add(e)
                    elif e in res:
                        used.add(e)
                    else:
                        miss += float(wt[t, l, r])
                for e in used:
                    last[e] = t
                if pol == "lfu-m":
                    for e in list(score):
                        score[e] *= decay
                missed.append(miss)
    m = np.array(missed)
    return loads / ntok, m.mean(), np.percentile(m, 99)


for C in [int(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or [51, 26]:
    print(f"C={C} ({100 * C / 512:.0f} %)", flush=True)
    for pol in ("lru", "lfu-m", "belady", "belady-m"):
        ld, mm, p99 = run(C, pol)
        print(f"  {pol:9s} loads {ld:5.1f}/token ({ld * 2.77:4.0f} MB)  missed mass {mm:.3f} / P99 {p99:.3f}", flush=True)


### scripts/floor_diet.py
"""RAM floor of the streamed 125B with no experts (TASKS.md dense diet, Breakthrough 2026-10-21): load at the minimum
cache (C = 16), apply dense changes, decode 32 tokens (fills the n-gram hot cache and the KV), then report MLX active and
peak memory, the process's resident set (ps rss) and the expert pools; floor = total - pools.
usage: floor_diet.py [MODS]   MODS: comma list of hc8 | hc4 | lm3 (env MTPLX_NGRAM_HOT_MB sets the n-gram hot cache)"""
import json, os, subprocess, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = "0"
os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bt"))
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx.utils import tree_flatten
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe import decode as D

MODS = [m for m in (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "").split(",") if m]
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, 16, mtp=False)
if "hc8" in MODS or "hc4" in MODS:
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/patches/hc8"))
    import hc8
    print("hc", hc8.install(rt.model, bits=8 if "hc8" in MODS else 4), flush=True)
if "lm3" in MODS:
    TM = getattr(rt.model, "language_model", rt.model)
    for name, mod in TM.named_modules():
        if name.endswith("lm_head") and isinstance(mod, nn.QuantizedLinear):
            W = mx.dequantize(mod.weight, mod.scales, mod.biases, mod.group_size, mod.bits)
            lin = nn.Linear(W.shape[1], W.shape[0], bias=False); lin.weight = W
            q = nn.QuantizedLinear.from_linear(lin, 64, 3)
            parent = TM
            for part in name.split(".")[:-1]:
                parent = getattr(parent, part)
            setattr(parent, name.split(".")[-1], q)
    mx.eval(TM.parameters())
mx.clear_cache()
ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": "Explain briefly how a CPU cache works."}],
                                       add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
D.greedy(rt.model, ids, 32)
mx.eval()
rss = int(subprocess.run(["ps", "-o", "rss=", "-p", str(os.getpid())], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout) / 1e6
act, peak, pools = mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9, mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9, st.resident_gb()
print(f"floor [{','.join(MODS) or 'base'} hot={os.environ.get('MTPLX_NGRAM_HOT_MB', '1024')} MB]: MLX active {act:.2f} GB, "
      f"peak {peak:.2f} GB, process RSS {rss:.2f} GB, expert pools {pools:.2f} GB -> floor active {act - pools:.2f} / "
      f"RSS {rss - pools:.2f} GB", flush=True)


### scripts/gate_mass.py
"""D1 with real router probabilities (Breakthrough, 2026-10-20). 125B re-trace (data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz:
L<l> = router top-20 ids, G<l> = their softmax probabilities over 512) of code/chat/Bulgarian/math decode tokens.
(1) gate-mass shape: share of the top-10 renormalized mass in rank 0, ranks 0-1, ranks 0-3.
(2) frugal policies per layer (LRU, strict capacity C; only waited experts are read from the SSD; a missing unwaited
    rank uses the best-ranked resident expert among ranks 10-19, else is dropped): loads/token and the "missed mass" =
    renormalized top-10 gate mass whose expert was not computed (substituted or dropped), mean and P99 per token-layer.
    Policies: wait-top-r (r = 1, 2, 3) and wait-until-mass >= theta (theta = 0.5 .. 0.9).
CPU only (one core, ~1-2 min); run it when nobody holds a quiet lock.
"""
import glob
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict

NL = 48
sets = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
        for f in sorted(glob.glob("/Users/kamen/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz"))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in sets.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in sets.items()}
w = {k: g[:, :, :10] / g[:, :, :10].sum(-1, keepdims=True) for k, g in gp.items()}


def nwait(wt, rule):
    if rule[0] == "r":
        return int(rule[1:])
    return int(np.argmax(np.cumsum(wt) >= float(rule[1:]) - 1e-6)) + 1


def run(C, rule):
    loads, missed, ntok = 0, [], 0
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt = ids[k], w[k]
        ntok += tr.shape[0]
        for l in range(NL):
            c = OrderedDict()
            for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
                row, nw = tr[t, l], nwait(wt[t, l], rule)
                alt = [int(e) for e in row[10:20] if int(e) in c]
                used, miss = [], 0.0
                for r in range(10):
                    e = int(row[r])
                    if r < nw or e in c:
                        if e not in c:
                            loads += 1
                        used.append(e)
                    else:
                        miss += wt[t, l, r]
                        a = next((x for x in alt if x not in used), None)
                        if a is not None:
                            used.append(a)
                            alt.remove(a)
                missed.append(miss)
                for e in used:
                    if e in c:
                        c.move_to_end(e)
                    else:
                        c[e] = 1
                while len(c) > C:
                    c.popitem(last=False)
    m = np.array(missed)
    return loads / ntok, m.mean(), np.percentile(m, 99)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    allw = np.concatenate([x.reshape(-1, 10) for x in w.values()])
    cum = np.cumsum(allw, -1)
    print(f"(1) {len(allw)} token-layers: mass in rank 0 {cum[:, 0].mean():.3f}, ranks 0-1 {cum[:, 1].mean():.3f}, "
          f"0-3 {cum[:, 3].mean():.3f}; ranks needed for 50/70/90 % of the mass: "
          + " / ".join(f"{(np.argmax(cum >= t, -1) + 1).mean():.2f}" for t in (0.5, 0.7, 0.9)))
    print("(2) loads/token (MB at 2.77 MB/expert) | missed mass mean / P99 per token-layer")
    for C in (51, 102):
        print(f"C={C} ({100 * C / 512:.0f} %)")
        for rule in ("r1", "r2", "r3", "m0.5", "m0.6", "m0.7", "m0.8", "m0.9"):
            L_, mm, p99 = run(C, rule)
            print(f"  {rule:5s} loads {L_:6.1f} ({L_ * 2.77:5.0f} MB)  missed mass {mm:.3f} / P99 {p99:.3f}", flush=True)


### scripts/gate_table.py
