"""Per-part gate screen for teacher-forced KLD probes (Breakthrough 2026-10-21): every data/tfvar/<VAR>[_Sgc]_e8.npz
(tf_frugal_var.py, NewIdeas' tf_defer125.py), mean KLD per corpus part, a one-sided 90 % upper bound (bootstrap over
positions: optimistic, positions within a sequence are correlated), top-1 agreement, and PASS/FAIL against the official
per-part targets (1.10 x ar4's teacher-forced KLD: code 0.0025, gsm 0.0045, ifeval 0.0204, multi 0.0206; the subsets are
every 8th sequence, so this is a screen, not the Eval suite's verdict). For deferral variants (d1_* / dh_*) it also
prints f = (KLD - exact) / (drop - exact) per part for W = 2, drop = noren (wait ranks 0-1, use resident ranks of the
top-10, drop the rest, no renorm: exactly what dh_w2 / d1_w2 add late); other W need their own drop row (w4_nr ...).
usage: gate_table.py [VAR ...]   (default: every variant with at least one subset)"""
import glob, os, sys
import numpy as np

D = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/tfvar")
TARGET = {"tf_code": 0.0025, "tf_gsm": 0.0045, "tf_ifeval": 0.0204, "tf_multi": 0.0206}
PARTS = ["tf_gsm", "tf_code", "tf_ifeval", "tf_multi"]
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
runs = {}
for f in sorted(glob.glob(f"{D}/*_e8.npz")):
    v = os.path.basename(f)[:-len("_e8.npz")]
    base = v[:-4] if v.endswith("_Sgc") else v
    z = np.load(f)
    for k in z.files:
        if k.startswith("kld_"):
            runs.setdefault(base, {})[k[4:]] = (z[k], z["agree_" + k[4:]])
want = sys.argv[1:] or sorted(runs)
ex = runs.get("exact", {})


def ub90(a):
    return np.percentile([a[rng.integers(0, len(a), len(a))].mean() for _ in range(1000)], 90)


print(f"{'variant':22s} " + " ".join(f"{p[3:]:>24s}" for p in PARTS) + "   verdict")
for v in want:
    r = runs.get(v)
    if r is None:
        continue
    cells, ok, have = [], True, 0
    for p in PARTS:
        if p not in r:
            cells.append(f"{'-':>24s}"); continue
        k, a = r[p]
        have += 1
        u = ub90(k)
        good = u <= TARGET[p]
        ok &= good
        cells.append(f"{k.mean():.4f} ub {u:.4f} {'ok' if good else 'X'} t{a.mean():.3f}".rjust(24))
    verdict = ("PASS" if ok else "FAIL") + ("" if have == 4 else f" ({have}/4 parts)")
    print(f"{v:22s} " + " ".join(cells) + f"   {verdict}")
    if (v.startswith("d1_w2") or v.startswith("dh_w2")) and "noren" in runs:
        fs = []
        for p in PARTS:
            if p in r and p in runs["noren"] and p in ex:
                fs.append(f"{p[3:]} {(r[p][0].mean() - ex[p][0].mean()) / (runs['noren'][p][0].mean() - ex[p][0].mean()):.2f}")
        if fs:
            print(f"{'':22s} f (share of noren's excess left): " + ", ".join(fs))


### scripts/kv8_long.py
"""Queue L2-06 task check (Breakthrough, 2026-10-25): does 8-bit QSA KV (b12_kv8) change long-context retrieval?
One long document per length (the first 32K / 60K tokens of data/prompts/ctx64k.txt: real Python source) with planted
facts, then three questions as successive user turns on the same cache (one long prefill per length and arm):
  mq   : 8 needles "The special magic number for {word} is: {7 digits}." at depths 10..95 %; 4 asked, the other 4 are
         distractors of the same form (RULER multi-key style); one point per asked number in the answer
  vt   : a 5-step variable chain (VAR A = 48213. VAR B = VAR A. ...) over depths 5..93 % plus a 3-step distractor chain
         with another value (RULER vt); one point per chain name in the answer
  uuid : 40 UUID key -> value pairs as one JSON block at depth 30 %; one key asked; one point for its value
Model path: B12LL (B12_LATE3=2,10, mass), decoupled prefill (512 / 4096) of the first turn; later turns are fed one row
at a time (decode semantics, so B12LL's late writes stay in order); greedy one-row decode with bt_b12's repetition guard
as b12_generate runs it, EOS stop. Arms bf16 then kv:64 per length in one process, on the same token ids (memory is
measured separately, one arm per process, by kv8_tf.py).
usage: kv8_long.py build | run    (env KV8L_LENS=32000,60000 KV8L_ARMS=bf16,kv:64)
       build -> data/kv8/long_tasks.json (CPU, tokenizer only); run -> data/kv8/long_<L>.json + a line per arm in
       data/kv8/results.txt
KV8L_SET=_dx (10-26, NewIdeas: more distractor items before any 8-bit KV adoption): per length one document with 6
interleaved 4-step variable chains (distinct values, names unique in the document), each chain asked as its own turn:
6 vt items per prefill, and every other chain's names are an item's distractors. Files long_tasks_dx.json /
long_<L>_dx<TAG>.json.
Re-run (NewIdeas 12:30, after the INVALID 09:44 run): every item, the first included, starts from fresh copies of the
post-document buffers (snapshot / restore below; the first run handed back the snapshot's own arrays, which later writes
changed); a 2K self-test per arm first (prefill, answer, restore, other item, restore, answer again: same ids, same
first-step logits and the same cache fingerprint after every restore, else the run stops); cap KV8L_CAP (160) tokens;
a run whose re-asked item 0 differs is INVALID. The 14:00 self-test failed with the cache restored bit-exactly (the
single-row passes' bits depend on the decode pools), so since NewIdeas' 14:08 GO the pools are also emptied before every
item, as PFR leaves them after a long prefill (run 3, tag _g64_lane_r3)."""
import hashlib, json, os, random, sys, time

HOME = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough")
CTX = os.path.join(HOME, "data/prompts/ctx64k.txt")
SET = os.environ.get("KV8L_SET", "")                   # "" (the 3-question set) or "_dx"
TASKS = os.environ.get("KV8L_TASKS", os.path.join(HOME, f"data/kv8/long_tasks{SET}.json"))
LENS = [int(x) for x in os.environ.get("KV8L_LENS", "32000,60000").split(",")]
ARMS = os.environ.get("KV8L_ARMS", "bf16,kv:64").split(",")
LANE = os.environ.get("KV8L_LANE", "0") == "1"         # L2-10: the first-turn prefill inside mtplx attention_phase("prefill")
TAG = os.environ.get("KV8L_TAG", "")                   # suffix of the record files / result lines (e.g. "_g64_lane")
CAP = int(os.environ.get("KV8L_CAP", "160"))           # _dx answer cap (48 cut every 60K answer in the 09:44 run)
ST_N = int(os.environ.get("KV8L_ST_N", "2048"))        # _dx self-test: prefix tokens (0 = no self-test)
WORDS = ["harbor", "violet", "glacier", "lantern", "meadow", "copper", "falcon", "orchid", "summit", "willow", "ember",
         "quartz"]
TURN = "<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>user\n{}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n"


def build():
    import uuid
    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
    TK = Tokenizer.from_file(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare/tokenizer.json"))
    enc = lambda s: TK.encode(s, add_special_tokens=False).ids
    text = open(CTX).read()
    e = TK.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)
    rng = random.Random(2510)
    out = {"eos": [TK.token_to_id("<|im_end|>"), TK.token_to_id("<|endoftext|>")], "tasks": {}}
    if SET == "_dx":
        return build_dx(TK, enc, text, e, out)
    for L in LENS:
        hay = text[:text.rfind("\n", 0, e.offsets[L][0])]
        words = rng.sample(WORDS, 8)
        nums = [str(rng.randint(1_000_000, 9_999_999)) for _ in words]
        depths = [0.10, 0.22, 0.35, 0.47, 0.60, 0.72, 0.85, 0.95]
        asked = [0, 2, 4, 6]                                  # depths 10 / 35 / 60 / 85 %; 22 / 47 / 72 / 95 % distract
        facts = [(d, f"The special magic number for {w} is: {n}.") for d, w, n in zip(depths, words, nums)]
        names = rng.sample(["".join(rng.choice("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ") for _ in range(5)) for _ in range(20)], 8)
        val, val2 = str(rng.randint(10000, 99999)), str(rng.randint(10000, 99999))
        chain = [f"VAR {names[0]} = {val}."] + [f"VAR {names[i]} = VAR {names[i - 1]}." for i in range(1, 5)]
        dchain = [f"VAR {names[5]} = {val2}."] + [f"VAR {names[i]} = VAR {names[i - 1]}." for i in range(6, 8)]
        facts += list(zip([0.05, 0.27, 0.50, 0.73, 0.93], chain)) + list(zip([0.15, 0.55, 0.80], dchain))
        pairs = [(str(uuid.UUID(int=rng.getrandbits(128))), str(uuid.UUID(int=rng.getrandbits(128)))) for _ in range(40)]
        kq = rng.randrange(len(pairs))
        facts.append((0.30, "UUID pairs (key -> value):\n" + json.dumps(dict(pairs), indent=0)))
        doc = hay
        for d, f in sorted(facts, key=lambda x: -x[0]):       # deepest first: earlier positions do not move
            p = doc.rfind("\n", 0, int(len(hay) * d)) + 1
            doc = doc[:p] + f + "\n" + doc[p:]
        qs = [f"What are the special magic numbers for {', '.join(words[i] for i in asked[:3])} and {words[asked[3]]} "
              f"mentioned in the text above? Answer with the four numbers only, in that order.",
              f"Find all variables that are assigned the value {val} in the text above, directly or through other "
              f"variables. Answer with the variable names only.",
              f"In the JSON object of UUID pairs in the text above, what is the value for the key \"{pairs[kq][0]}\"? "
              f"Answer with the value only."]
        first = ("<|im_start|>user\nBelow is the source of several files from one repository, with some facts mixed in. "
                 "Read it, then answer my questions about it.\n\n" + doc + "\n\n" + qs[0] +
                 "<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n")
        out["tasks"][str(L)] = {"first": enc(first), "turns": [enc(TURN.format(q)) for q in qs[1:]], "questions": qs,
                                "gold": [[nums[i] for i in asked], names[:5], [pairs[kq][1]]], "max_tokens": [48, 48, 64]}
        print(f"kv8_long build L={L}: first turn {len(out['tasks'][str(L)]['first'])} tokens, asked {[words[i] for i in asked]}, "
              f"chain {names[:5]} = {val}, uuid key #{kq}", flush=True)
    json.dump(out, open(TASKS, "w"))


def build_dx(TK, enc, text, e, out, nc=6, steps=4):
    rng = random.Random(2610)
    for L in LENS:
        hay = text[:text.rfind("\n", 0, e.offsets[L][0])]
        pool = set()
        while len(pool) < nc * steps:
            pool.add("".join(rng.choice("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ") for _ in range(5)))
        names = rng.sample(sorted(pool), nc * steps)
        vals = [str(v) for v in rng.sample(range(10000, 100000), nc)]
        chains = [names[c * steps:(c + 1) * steps] for c in range(nc)]
        facts = []
        for nm, val in zip(chains, vals):                     # root first, links deeper: each chain reads in order
            ds = sorted(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.97) for _ in range(steps))
            facts += list(zip(ds, [f"VAR {nm[0]} = {val}."] + [f"VAR {nm[i]} = VAR {nm[i - 1]}." for i in range(1, steps)]))
        doc = hay
        for d, f in sorted(facts, key=lambda x: -x[0]):       # deepest first: earlier positions do not move
            p = doc.rfind("\n", 0, int(len(hay) * d)) + 1
            doc = doc[:p] + f + "\n" + doc[p:]
        qs = [f"Find all variables that are assigned the value {v} in the text above, directly or through other "
              f"variables. Answer with the variable names only." for v in vals]
        prefix = ("<|im_start|>user\nBelow is the source of several files from one repository, with some facts mixed in. "
                  "Read it, then answer my question about it.\n\n" + doc)
        # NewIdeas 03:10: every item starts from the same state right after the document (run() rolls the cache back
        # to the prefix before each question), so the 6 items are independent; each question ends the same user turn
        out["tasks"][str(L)] = {"prefix": enc(prefix), "suffixes": [enc("\n\n" + q + "<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n"
                                                                        "<think>\n\n</think>\n\n") for q in qs],
                                "questions": qs, "gold": chains, "kinds": ["vt"] * nc, "max_tokens": [CAP] * nc}
        print(f"kv8_long build_dx L={L}: prefix {len(out['tasks'][str(L)]['prefix'])} tokens, {nc} chains x {steps}: "
              + " ".join(f"{v}={'/'.join(c)}" for v, c in zip(vals, chains)), flush=True)
    json.dump(out, open(TASKS, "w"))


def run():
    os.environ.setdefault("B12_LATE3", "2,10")
    os.environ.setdefault("B12_NORM", "mass")
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    import bt_b12 as B
    rt, st = B.load_b12(0.10)
    import mlx.core as mx
    import numpy as np
    import b12_kv8 as K
    K.install()
    from bigmoe_v3f.fuse import reset_generation_state
    from bigmoe_v3f import repguard as RG
    from contextlib import nullcontext
    from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
    model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
    T = json.load(open(TASKS))
    EOS, IM_END = set(T["eos"]), T["eos"][0]
    import ctypes, threading

    class _RU(ctypes.Structure):                             # rusage_info_v4 (as kv8_tf): u7 = physical footprint now
        _fields_ = [("uuid", ctypes.c_uint8 * 16)] + [(f"u{i}", ctypes.c_uint64) for i in range(40)]
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None)

    def fp_now():
        ru = _RU()
        return ru.u7 / 1e9 if libc.proc_pid_rusage(os.getpid(), 4, ctypes.byref(ru)) == 0 else 0.0

    PK = {}                                                  # (footprint, MLX active, b12_prefill PHASE) at the max
                                                             # footprint and at the max footprint - active, per phase
    def pk_reset():
        PK.update(fp=(0.0, 0.0, None), gap=(0.0, 0.0, None))

    def sampler():                                           # where the long prefill's footprint peak sits (20 Hz)
        while True:
            try:
                f, a, p = fp_now(), mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9, B.PHASE.get("p")
                if f > PK["fp"][0]:
                    PK["fp"] = (f, a, p)
                if f - a > PK["gap"][0] - PK["gap"][1]:
                    PK["gap"] = (f, a, p)
            except Exception:
                pass
            time.sleep(0.05)
    pk_reset()
    threading.Thread(target=sampler, daemon=True).start()
    fmt = lambda r: f"{r[0]:.2f} GB (MLX active {r[1]:.2f}) at {r[2]}"

    def step(y, cache):
        lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
        return (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1]

    def answer(y, cache, n, lgh=None):                       # b12_generate's loop (lgh: gets the first-step logits sha)
        guard = RG.make()
        out = []
        while len(out) < n:
            lg = step(y, cache)
            if lgh is not None and not out:
                lgh.append(hashlib.sha1(np.array(lg.astype(mx.float32)).tobytes()).hexdigest()[:10])
            y = int(mx.argmax(RG.steer(guard, out, lg) if guard is not None and guard.armed else lg).item())
            if out:
                RG.observe(guard, out)
            out.append(y)
            if y in EOS:
                break
        return out

    def _leaves(v):
        if isinstance(v, mx.array):
            return [v]
        if isinstance(v, (tuple, list)):
            return [a for x in v for a in _leaves(x)]
        if isinstance(v, dict):
            return [a for x in v.values() for a in _leaves(x)]
        return []

    def _clone(v):
        """a fresh buffer with the same bits. Never the snapshot's own array objects: a later setitem or a donated write
        would change them (MTPLX cache_state._clone_tree's rule, its issue #247; the 09:44 run kept them and its fork
        check failed). Floats go through an integer view, so -0.0 stays -0.0 (x + 0 would not)."""
        if isinstance(v, mx.array):
            u = {2: mx.uint16, 4: mx.uint32, 8: mx.uint64}.get(v.dtype.size) if mx.issubdtype(v.dtype, mx.floating) else None
            return mx.view(mx.view(v, u) + 0, v.dtype) if u is not None else v + mx.zeros((), v.dtype)
        if isinstance(v, (tuple, list)):
            return type(v)(_clone(x) for x in v)
        if isinstance(v, dict):
            return {k: _clone(x) for k, x in v.items()}
        return v

    QF = ("raw_keys", "pooled", "pooled_f32_t", "pooled_len", "_reserved_raw_capacity", "_reserved_pooled_capacity")
    KF = ("keys", "values", "_k", "_v", "offset")            # stock QSAKVCache keys / values, b12_kv8's 8-bit _k / _v

    def _fields(c):                                          # (kind, the cache's own fields, its KV's fields)
        if hasattr(c, "kv"):
            d, kv = vars(c), vars(c.kv)
            extra = [k for k, v in list(d.items()) + list(kv.items()) if _leaves(v) and k not in QF + KF]
            assert not extra, f"QSA cache arrays the snapshot does not cover: {extra}"
            return "qsa", {k: d[k] for k in QF if k in d}, {k: kv[k] for k in KF if k in kv}
        assert isinstance(getattr(c, "cache", None), list), type(c)
        assert getattr(c, "left_padding", None) is None and getattr(c, "lengths", None) is None, "batched ArraysCache"
        return "arr", list(c.cache), None

    def snapshot(cache):
        """every layer's buffers right after the prefix, copied whole (full capacity; the 8-bit KV as stored, no
        dequantize / requantize): QSA = KV + offset, the indexer's raw and pooled keys, the fp32 mirror and lengths;
        linear layers = their ArraysCache list (conv / recurrent state, the PLE n-gram history)"""
        assert not B.STASH and not B.PENDH, "late writes pending after the prefill"
        snap = [_clone(_fields(c)) for c in cache]
        mx.eval(_leaves(snap))
        return snap

    def empty_pools():
        """NewIdeas 14:08 (dx fix GO): every item gets the post-document pool state. The feed and the answer are
        single-row passes, which add cold MSB-3 ranks 2-9 late (B12_LATE3), so their bits depend on what the decode
        pools hold (the 14:00 self-test: cache restored bit-exactly, item 0's answer still changed). A long prefill
        leaves the pools empty (PFR re-creates them empty; without PFR the prefill reads into its own scratch), so they
        are emptied before every item: bt_b12's PFR release + re-create (ext.reset_layer, every table invalidated)"""
        B.PFR_LOG.append(("reset",))
        with B._PFR():
            pass

    def restore(cache, snap):
        """fresh copies of the snapshot into every layer and empty pools, before every item (the first included)"""
        for c, (kind, a, b) in zip(cache, snap):
            if kind == "qsa":
                for k, v in a.items():
                    object.__setattr__(c, k, _clone(v))
                for k, v in b.items():
                    object.__setattr__(c.kv, k, _clone(v))
            else:
                c.cache[:] = _clone(a)
        mx.eval(_leaves([_fields(c) for c in cache]))
        B.STASH.clear(); B.PENDH.clear()
        reset_generation_state(model)                        # LC1: no indexer-core state carried between items
        empty_pools()

    def fingerprint(cache):                                  # sha of every snapshotted buffer, bit for bit
        h = hashlib.sha1()
        for a in _leaves([_fields(c) for c in cache]):
            u = {2: mx.uint16, 4: mx.uint32, 8: mx.uint64}.get(a.dtype.size) if mx.issubdtype(a.dtype, mx.floating) else None
            h.update(np.array(mx.view(a, u) if u is not None else a).tobytes())
        for c in cache:                                      # and the plain ints (offsets, lengths)
            _, a, b = _fields(c)
            h.update(repr(sorted((k, v) for k, v in (a.items() if isinstance(a, dict) else ()) if isinstance(v, int))).encode())
            h.update(repr(sorted((k, v) for k, v in (b or {}).items() if isinstance(v, int))).encode())
        return h.hexdigest()[:10]

    def set_arm(arm):
        K.ON[0] = arm != "bf16"
        if K.ON[0]:
            part, gs = arm.split(":")
            K.CONF.update(part=part, gs=int(gs))
        reset_generation_state(model)
        B.STASH.clear(); B.PENDH.clear()
        mx.clear_cache()

    def selftest(t, arm):
        """NewIdeas 12:30 (2): prefill ST_N tokens of the document, item 0, restore, item 1, restore, item 0 again: the
        two item-0 answers must match (ids + first-step logits) and every restore must give the post-prefill fingerprint"""
        set_arm(arm)
        cache = model.make_cache()
        with (attention_phase("prefill") if LANE else nullcontext()):
            B.b12_prefill(model, mx.array([t["prefix"][:ST_N]]), cache, 512, 4096)
        snap = snapshot(cache)
        h0, got = fingerprint(cache), []
        for q in (0, 1, 0):
            restore(cache, snap)
            h, lgh = fingerprint(cache), []
            for f in t["suffixes"][q][:-1]:
                mx.eval(step(f, cache))
            got.append((h, answer(t["suffixes"][q][-1], cache, CAP, lgh), lgh[0]))
        ok = all(g[0] == h0 for g in got) and got[0][1:] == got[2][1:]
        print(f"kv8_long selftest {arm} ({ST_N} tokens, pools emptied before every item, last reset "
              f"{B.PFR_LOG[-1][1:]} GB / release s / re-create s): {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} | fingerprint after prefill {h0}, after "
              f"each restore {' '.join(g[0] for g in got)} | item 0 logits {got[0][2]} / again {got[2][2]}, ids equal "
              f"{got[0][1] == got[2][1]} ({len(got[0][1])} / {len(got[2][1])} tokens) | item 1 logits {got[1][2]} | "
              f"{tok.decode([v for v in got[0][1] if v not in EOS])[:60]!r}", flush=True)
        del cache, snap
        return ok

    t0_ = T["tasks"][str(LENS[0])]
    if "prefix" in t0_ and ST_N:                             # NewIdeas 12:30: the restore is proven first, per arm
        if not all([selftest(t0_, arm) for arm in ARMS]):
            print("kv8_long selftest FAILED: no long runs", flush=True)
            sys.exit(3)
    for L in LENS:
        t = T["tasks"][str(L)]
        fork = "prefix" in t                                 # the _dx set: one prefix, each question from its end
        first = t["prefix"] if fork else t["first"]
        rec = {"L": L, "n_first": len(first), "questions": t["questions"], "gold": t["gold"], "fork": fork, "arms": {}}
        for arm in ARMS:
            set_arm(arm)
            cache = model.make_cache()
            B.PHASE.clear(); pk_reset(); mx.reset_peak_memory()
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            with (attention_phase("prefill") if LANE else nullcontext()):
                B.b12_prefill(model, mx.array([first if fork else first[:-1]]), cache, 512, 4096)
            tp = time.perf_counter() - t0
            mem = {"prefill_fp_peak": PK["fp"], "prefill_gap_peak": PK["gap"], "prefill_mlx_peak": mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9}
            B.PHASE["p"] = "qa"; pk_reset()
            snap = snapshot(cache) if fork else None
            h0 = fingerprint(cache) if fork else None
            y, prev, res = first[-1], None, []
            for qi in range(len(t["questions"]) + (1 if fork else 0)):   # fork: item 0 again at the end (fork check)
                q = qi % len(t["questions"])
                if fork:
                    restore(cache, snap)
                    hn = fingerprint(cache) if qi == len(t["questions"]) else None   # outside the timings
                t1 = time.perf_counter()
                if fork:
                    feed = t["suffixes"][q]
                elif qi:                                     # close the last answer, then the next user turn
                    feed = ([prev[-1]] if prev and prev[-1] != IM_END else []) + t["turns"][qi - 1]
                else:
                    feed = [y]
                for f in feed[:-1]:
                    mx.eval(step(f, cache))
                t2 = time.perf_counter()
                prev = answer(feed[-1], cache, t["max_tokens"][q])
                text = tok.decode([v for v in prev if v not in EOS])
                if qi == len(t["questions"]):                # a differing re-ask makes the run INVALID (NewIdeas 12:30)
                    rec.setdefault("fork_check", {})[arm] = {"ids_equal": prev == res[0]["ids"], "text": text,
                                                             "fingerprint": [h0, hn]}
                    break
                hits = [str(g).lower() in text.lower() for g in t["gold"][q]]
                res.append({"text": text, "hits": hits, "s": round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 1), "ids": prev,
                            "feed": [len(feed) - 1, round(t2 - t1, 2)], "gen": [len(prev), round(time.perf_counter() - t2, 2)]})
            st.gate.check()
            mem["qa_fp_peak"] = PK["fp"]
            gen_n, gen_s = sum(r["gen"][0] for r in res), sum(r["gen"][1] for r in res)
            feed_n, feed_s = sum(r["feed"][0] for r in res), sum(r["feed"][1] for r in res)
            rec["arms"][arm] = {"prefill_s": round(tp, 1), "res": res, "mem": mem,
                                "decode_tok_s": round(gen_n / max(gen_s, 1e-9), 2), "feed_rows_s": round(feed_n / max(feed_s, 1e-9), 2)}
            line = (f"long L={L}{SET}{TAG} ({len(first)} tok) {arm}: " + " ".join(
                f"{q} {sum(r['hits'])}/{len(r['hits'])}" for q, r in zip(t.get("kinds", ("mq", "vt", "uuid")), res)) +
                f" | prefill {tp:.0f} s | decode {rec['arms'][arm]['decode_tok_s']} tok/s ({gen_n} tokens), feed "
                f"{rec['arms'][arm]['feed_rows_s']} rows/s" +
                (f" | fork check item 0 again: {'identical' if rec['fork_check'][arm]['ids_equal'] else 'DIFFERS -> INVALID'}"
                 f" (fingerprint {'/'.join(rec['fork_check'][arm]['fingerprint'])})" if fork else "") +
                " | " + " || ".join(repr(r["text"][:70]) for r in res))
            print(line, flush=True)
            print(f"long L={L} {arm} memory: prefill footprint peak {fmt(mem['prefill_fp_peak'])}; widest footprint - "
                  f"active {fmt(mem['prefill_gap_peak'])}; prefill MLX peak {mem['prefill_mlx_peak']:.2f} GB; Q&A footprint "
                  f"peak {fmt(mem['qa_fp_peak'])}", flush=True)
            if "KV8L_TASKS" not in os.environ:                # smoke runs on other task files leave no record
                with open(os.path.join(HOME, "data/kv8/results.txt"), "a") as f:
                    f.write(time.strftime("%m-%d %H:%M ") + line + "\n")
                json.dump(rec, open(os.path.join(HOME, f"data/kv8/long_{L}{SET}{TAG}.json"), "w"), indent=1)
            del cache


def score():
    """precision beside the hits: distractors named in each answer (vt: the names of every VAR line in the document that
    is not in the gold chain, i.e. the distractor chain; mq: the 7-digit numbers of the unasked needles). CPU, tokenizer."""
    import re
    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
    TK = Tokenizer.from_file(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare/tokenizer.json"))
    T = json.load(open(TASKS))
    for L in LENS:
        p = os.path.join(HOME, f"data/kv8/long_{L}{SET}{TAG}.json")
        if not os.path.exists(p):
            continue
        rec, doc = json.load(open(p)), TK.decode(T["tasks"][str(L)].get("first") or T["tasks"][str(L)]["prefix"])
        gold = rec["gold"]
        if SET == "_dx":                                      # each item: its chain's names found, other chains' named
            for arm, a in rec["arms"].items():
                per = []
                for qi, r in enumerate(a["res"]):
                    others = [(c, [v for v in g if v in r["text"]]) for c, g in enumerate(gold) if c != qi]
                    per.append((sum(r["hits"]), sum(len(n) for _, n in others),
                                [c for c, n in others if len(n) >= len(gold[c]) - 1]))
                print(f"long L={L}{SET} {arm}: gold names {sum(x[0] for x in per)}/{sum(len(g) for g in gold)}, "
                      f"distractor names {sum(x[1] for x in per)}, items with a (near-)whole other chain "
                      f"{sum(1 for x in per if x[2])} {[i for i, x in enumerate(per) if x[2]]} | per item hits/distractors " +
                      " ".join(f"{x[0]}/{x[1]}" for x in per) + f" | decode {a.get('decode_tok_s')} tok/s | fork check " +
                      ("same" if rec.get("fork_check", {}).get(arm, {}).get("ids_equal") else "DIFFERS -> INVALID RUN"),
                      flush=True)
            continue
        dvars = sorted(set(re.findall(r"VAR ([A-Z]{5}) =", doc)) - set(gold[1]))
        dnums = sorted(set(re.findall(r"special magic number for \w+ is: (\d{7})", doc)) - set(gold[0]))
        for arm, a in rec["arms"].items():
            mq, vt = a["res"][0]["text"], a["res"][1]["text"]
            print(f"long L={L} {arm}: mq {sum(a['res'][0]['hits'])}/4 + {sum(n in mq for n in dnums)} distractor numbers | "
                  f"vt {sum(a['res'][1]['hits'])}/5 + {sum(v in vt for v in dvars)} distractor names {[v for v in dvars if v in vt]}"
                  f" | uuid {sum(a['res'][2]['hits'])}/1", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    {"build": build, "run": run, "score": score}[sys.argv[1]]()


### scripts/kv8_tf.py
"""Queue L2-06 screen (Breakthrough, 2026-10-25): 8-bit KV (b12_kv8) vs bf16 KV at long context, on the B12LL decode path
(B12_LATE3=2,10, mass: every top-10 expert computed, so residency cannot move the logits). Per context N: the decoupled
prefill of the first N tokens of data/prompts/ctx64k.txt, then the next M tokens teacher-forced in S-row chunks (verify
width). Arms run in one process (bf16 first, then 8-bit) on the same tokens; per position KL(bf16 || 8-bit) over the
full vocabulary, top-1 agreement, ms per chunk, QSA KV bytes and MLX active memory after the prefill.
usage: kv8_tf.py   (env KV8_N=16384,32768 KV8_M=256 KV8_S=4)   -> a line per N in data/kv8/results.txt"""
import os, sys, time

os.environ.setdefault("B12_LATE3", "2,10")
os.environ.setdefault("B12_NORM", "mass")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
NS = [int(x) for x in os.environ.get("KV8_N", "16384,32768").split(",")]
M, S = int(os.environ.get("KV8_M", "256")), int(os.environ.get("KV8_S", "4"))
ARMS = os.environ.get("KV8_ARMS", "bf16,kv:64").split(",")      # arm 0 = the reference; "part:gs" = b12_kv8 CONF
import bt_b12 as B
rt, st = B.load_b12(0.10)
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
import b12_kv8 as K
K.install()
from bigmoe_v3f.fuse import reset_generation_state
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
ids_all = tok.encode(open(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/prompts/ctx64k.txt")).read())
print(f"kv8_tf: {len(ids_all)} tokens available; N {NS}, M {M}, S {S}, 8-bit group {K.GS}", flush=True)
assert max(NS) + M <= len(ids_all)


import ctypes


class _RU(ctypes.Structure):                              # rusage_info_v4: uuid, then 64-bit counters (as bt_b12)
    _fields_ = [("uuid", ctypes.c_uint8 * 16)] + [(f"u{i}", ctypes.c_uint64) for i in range(40)]


def footprint():
    """(physical footprint now, lifetime max) in GB: Activity Monitor's Memory"""
    ru = _RU()
    if ctypes.CDLL(None).proc_pid_rusage(os.getpid(), 4, ctypes.byref(ru)) != 0:
        return float("nan"), float("nan")
    return ru.u7 / 1e9, ru.u28 / 1e9


def kv_bytes(cache):
    return sum(c.kv.nbytes for c in cache if hasattr(c, "kv"))


def run(N, arm):
    arm, _, sa = arm.partition("@")                     # "@256": the decoupled prefill's attention chunk (a benign
    sa = int(sa or 512)                                 # runtime perturbation: the noise floor of this metric)
    K.ON[0] = arm != "bf16"
    if K.ON[0]:
        part, gs = arm.split(":")
        K.CONF.update(part=part, gs=int(gs))
    reset_generation_state(model)
    B.STASH.clear(); B.PENDH.clear()
    mx.clear_cache()
    cache = model.make_cache()
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    B.b12_prefill(model, mx.array([ids_all[:N]]), cache, s_attn=sa)
    mx.eval([c.state for c in cache if hasattr(c, "state")])
    tp = time.perf_counter() - t0
    act, kvb = mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9, kv_bytes(cache) / 1e9
    print(f"kv8_tf {arm}@{sa} N={N}: after prefill active {act:.2f} GB, QSA KV {kvb:.3f} GB, MLX peak "
          f"{mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.2f} GB, footprint now / lifetime max {footprint()[0]:.2f} / {footprint()[1]:.2f} GB",
          flush=True)
    lps, tc = [], []
    for i in range(0, M, S):
        t1 = time.perf_counter()
        lg = model(mx.array([ids_all[N + i:N + i + S]]), cache=cache)
        lg = lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg
        lp = lg[0].astype(mx.float32)
        lp = lp - mx.logsumexp(lp, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        mx.eval(lp)
        tc.append(time.perf_counter() - t1)
        lps.append(np.array(lp))
    st.gate.check()
    print(f"kv8_tf {arm}@{sa} N={N}: after {M} teacher tokens active {mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9:.2f} GB, footprint now / "
          f"lifetime max {footprint()[0]:.2f} / {footprint()[1]:.2f} GB, ms/chunk {1e3 * float(np.median(tc[2:])):.0f}", flush=True)
    del cache
    return np.concatenate(lps), dict(prefill_s=tp, active_gb=act, kv_gb=kvb, ms_chunk=1e3 * float(np.median(tc[2:])))


os.makedirs(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/kv8"), exist_ok=True)
if len(ARMS) == 1:                                      # memory arm alone, in its own process
    for N in NS:
        run(N, ARMS[0])
    sys.exit(0)
for N in NS:
    a, ia = run(N, ARMS[0])
    p = np.exp(a)
    for arm in ARMS[1:]:
        b, ib = run(N, arm)
        kld = (p * (a - b)).sum(-1)
        agree = (a.argmax(-1) == b.argmax(-1)).mean()
        line = (f"N={N} M={len(kld)} S={S} {ARMS[0]} vs {arm}: KLD mean {kld.mean():.6f} p50 {np.median(kld):.6f} "
                f"p99 {np.quantile(kld, 0.99):.6f} max {kld.max():.6f} | top-1 agree {agree:.4f} | QSA KV {ia['kv_gb']:.3f} -> "
                f"{ib['kv_gb']:.3f} GB | active {ia['active_gb']:.2f} -> {ib['active_gb']:.2f} GB | ms/chunk {ia['ms_chunk']:.0f} "
                f"-> {ib['ms_chunk']:.0f} | prefill {ia['prefill_s']:.0f} -> {ib['prefill_s']:.0f} s")
        print(line, flush=True)
        with open(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/kv8/results.txt"), "a") as f:
            f.write(time.strftime("%m-%d %H:%M ") + line + "\n")


### scripts/la_advise_test.py
"""D1k mechanism check (CPU + SSD only, ~15 s): can the OS read-ahead (fcntl F_RDADVISE on a cached fd) stage records3
experts that v3's tier prefill then reads through its F_NOCACHE fd? If an advised set reads ~as fast as RAM, layer L+1's
predicted union can be advised while layer L computes, with the prefill path (and its outputs) unchanged.
Reads 16 threads x one pread per expert (as read_rebuilt / the v3 stream do), 2.93 MB records.
Usage: la_advise_test.py [LAYER] [N_EXPERTS] [WAIT_MS ...]"""
import fcntl, os, random, struct, sys, time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

F_RDADVISE = 44                                     # <sys/fcntl.h>; struct radvisory {off_t ra_offset; int ra_count;}
D = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare/records3")
L = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 30
N = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 200
WAITS = [int(v) for v in sys.argv[3:]] or [0, 30, 60, 250]
RS, E = 2932736, 512
path = os.path.join(D, f"L{L:02d}.bin")
fd_nc = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY)
fcntl.fcntl(fd_nc, fcntl.F_NOCACHE, 1)               # as the ext's read_rebuilt and the v3 stream open records3
fd_ad = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY)                    # cached fd for the advisories
pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=16)
bufs = [bytearray(RS) for _ in range(16)]


def read(ids):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    futs = [pool.submit(os.preadv, fd_nc, [memoryview(bufs[k % 16])], e * RS) for k, e in enumerate(ids)]
    for f in futs:
        assert f.result() == RS
    dt = time.perf_counter() - t0
    return dt, len(ids) * RS / dt / 1e9


def advise(ids):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for e in ids:
        fcntl.fcntl(fd_ad, F_RDADVISE, struct.pack("qi4x", e * RS, RS))
    return time.perf_counter() - t0


rng = random.Random(L)
order = list(range(E)); rng.shuffle(order)
sets = [order[i * N:(i + 1) * N] for i in range(E // N)]
need = len(WAITS) + 1
if len(sets) < need:                                  # fewer disjoint sets than rows: smaller sets
    n = E // need; sets = [order[i * n:(i + 1) * n] for i in range(need)]
dt, gbs = read(sets[0])
print(f"L{L:02d} cold, {len(sets[0])} experts: {dt * 1e3:.0f} ms ({gbs:.1f} GB/s)", flush=True)
dt, gbs = read(sets[0])
print(f"L{L:02d} same set again through F_NOCACHE: {dt * 1e3:.0f} ms ({gbs:.1f} GB/s)", flush=True)
for w, s in zip(WAITS, sets[1:]):
    ta = advise(s)
    time.sleep(w / 1e3)
    dt, gbs = read(s)
    print(f"L{L:02d} advised ({ta * 1e3:.1f} ms to advise {len(s)}), read after {w} ms: {dt * 1e3:.0f} ms "
          f"({gbs:.1f} GB/s)", flush=True)


### scripts/lane_ref.py
"""Queue L2-10 (Breakthrough, 2026-10-25; NewIdeas' question): is the prefill lane the REFERENCE behaviour?
MTPLX's generate_ar (the Eval suite's reference path, chunk 512) runs its prefill inside attention_phase("prefill"),
so past the 32K crossover the reference itself takes the sparse lane (metal selector + NAX flash); b12_prefill never
entered the phase, so its dense path may be the deviation. Cheap test at a ~4K prompt with both crossovers lowered to
2049 (MTPLX_QSA_PREFILL_MIN_CONTEXT, MTPLX_QSA_PREFILL_FLASH_MIN_CONTEXT), B12LL (every top-10 expert computed, so
residency cannot move the logits), one process, the same prompt (first PK_N tokens of ctx64k.txt):
  A0 generate_ar as the Eval suite runs it on B12: no MTPLX_SUSTAINED_PREFILL, so ONE forward over the whole prompt
     from history 0 (the lane never engages; dense [24, T, T] plane)
  A  generate_ar, MTPLX_SUSTAINED_PREFILL=1 + MTPLX_PREFILL_CHUNK_SIZE 512 (MTPLX's profile reference: lane past 2049)
  B  b12_prefill(512, 512) inside attention_phase("prefill"), greedy decode (MoE per 512 rows = A's MoE rows/groups)
  C  b12_prefill(512, 512) outside it (b12_prefill as it ships: dense)
  D  generate_ar with MTPLX_QSA_PREFILL=0 (the reference with the lane off)
  E  B with the opt-in flash-skip DECODE lane (MTPLX_QSA_FLASH=1; the suite never sets it, so dense-mask decode is the
     reference and flash-skip the change to verify)
Compared: the greedy tokens and the first token's top-20 log-probabilities (mtplx first_token_logprobs, same function
for all arms). A == B and C == D, A != D -> b12_prefill + lane reproduces the reference; the dense path is the deviation.
B vs E: is flash-skip decode bitwise equal to the dense-mask decode?
env: PK_N (4200), PK_TOK (16) -> data/pk64/lane_ref.txt"""
import hashlib, json, os, sys, time

os.environ.setdefault("B12_LATE3", "2,10")
os.environ.setdefault("B12_NORM", "mass")
os.environ["MTPLX_QSA_PREFILL_MIN_CONTEXT"] = "2049"
os.environ["MTPLX_QSA_PREFILL_FLASH_MIN_CONTEXT"] = "2049"
os.environ["MTPLX_PREFILL_CHUNK_SIZE"] = "512"
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
HOME = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough")
N, NT = int(os.environ.get("LR_N", "4200")), int(os.environ.get("LR_TOK", "16"))   # (not PK_N: day_pk64b exports 65000)
import bt_b12 as B
rt, st = B.load_b12(0.10)
import mlx.core as mx
import mtplx.models.qwen4_exp as Q
from mtplx import generation as G
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
from bigmoe_v3f.fuse import reset_generation_state
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
ids = tok.encode(open(os.path.join(HOME, "data/prompts/ctx64k.txt")).read())[:N]
GREEDY = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)


def fresh():
    reset_generation_state(model)
    B.STASH.clear(); B.PENDH.clear()
    mx.clear_cache()
    Q._QSA_PREFILL_COUNTS.clear()


def arm_ar(lane_off, sustained=True):
    """generate_ar chunks its prefill only with MTPLX_SUSTAINED_PREFILL=1 (MTPLX's profiles set it; load_b12 and the
    Eval suite don't): without it the whole prompt is ONE forward from history 0, so the lane never engages and the
    dense plane is [24, T, T] (the 10-25 14:04 crash: 203 GB at T = 64,999)."""
    fresh()
    if lane_off:
        os.environ["MTPLX_QSA_PREFILL"] = "0"
    if sustained:
        os.environ["MTPLX_SUSTAINED_PREFILL"] = "1"
    before = G._runtime_counter_snapshot(rt)
    try:
        res = G.generate_ar(rt, list(ids), max_tokens=NT, sampler=GREEDY, first_token_logprobs_top_k=20)
    finally:
        os.environ.pop("MTPLX_QSA_PREFILL", None)
        os.environ.pop("MTPLX_SUSTAINED_PREFILL", None)
    tiers = dict(Q._QSA_PREFILL_COUNTS)
    tiers["prefill_chunks"] = G._runtime_counter_delta(rt, before).get("prefill_chunks")
    return [int(x) for x in res.tokens], res.first_token_logprobs, tiers


def arm_b12(lane, flash=False):
    fresh()
    cache = model.make_cache()
    with (attention_phase("prefill") if lane else __import__("contextlib").nullcontext()):
        B.b12_prefill(model, mx.array([ids[:-1]]), cache, 512, 512)
    if flash:                                                # E: the opt-in flash-skip decode lane (the suite never
        os.environ["MTPLX_QSA_FLASH"] = "1"                  # sets it: dense-mask decode is the reference)
    y, out, ftl = ids[-1], [], None
    try:
        for i in range(NT):
            lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
            lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1]
            y = int(mx.argmax(lg).item())
            if i == 0:
                ftl = G.first_token_logprobs(lg, token_id=y, top_k=20)
            out.append(y)
    finally:
        os.environ.pop("MTPLX_QSA_FLASH", None)
    return out, ftl, dict(Q._QSA_PREFILL_COUNTS)


res = {}
for name, f in (("A0_ar_onespan", lambda: arm_ar(False, sustained=False)),
                ("A_ar", lambda: arm_ar(False)), ("B_b12_lane", lambda: arm_b12(True)),
                ("C_b12_dense", lambda: arm_b12(False)), ("D_ar_laneoff", lambda: arm_ar(True)),
                ("E_b12_lane_flashdec", lambda: arm_b12(True, flash=True))):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    toks, ftl, tiers = f()
    res[name] = dict(toks=toks, top=[(t, v) for t, v in (ftl.top if ftl else ())], tiers=tiers,
                     s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 1))
    print(f"lane_ref {name}: {res[name]['s']} s, tiers {tiers}, sha {hashlib.sha1(json.dumps(toks).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]}"
          f" | {tok.decode(toks)[:60]!r}", flush=True)
st.gate.check()


def cmp(a, b):
    ta, tb = res[a]["toks"], res[b]["toks"]
    n = min(len(ta), len(tb))
    same = next((i for i in range(n) if ta[i] != tb[i]), n)
    pa, pb = dict(res[a]["top"]), dict(res[b]["top"])
    common = set(pa) & set(pb)
    dmax = max((abs(pa[t] - pb[t]) for t in common), default=float("nan"))
    return f"{a} vs {b}: tokens equal for {same}/{n}, top-20 ids {'same' if set(pa) == set(pb) else 'DIFFER'}, max |dlogprob| {dmax:.2e}"


lines = [cmp("A_ar", "B_b12_lane"), cmp("C_b12_dense", "D_ar_laneoff"), cmp("A_ar", "D_ar_laneoff"), cmp("B_b12_lane", "C_b12_dense"),
         cmp("B_b12_lane", "E_b12_lane_flashdec"), cmp("A0_ar_onespan", "C_b12_dense"), cmp("A0_ar_onespan", "D_ar_laneoff")]
for l in lines:
    print("lane_ref " + l, flush=True)
with open(os.path.join(HOME, "data/pk64/lane_ref.txt"), "a") as f:
    f.write(time.strftime("%m-%d %H:%M ") + f"N {N}, crossovers 2049, B12LL, env TIER_PF_GROUP "
            f"{os.environ.get('BIGMOE_TIER_PF_GROUP', '-')}: " + " | ".join(lines) + "\n")
json.dump(res, open(os.path.join(HOME, "data/pk64/lane_ref.json"), "w"))


### scripts/mincore.py
"""How much of a set of files sits in the OS page cache (mincore over a read-only mmap). usage: mincore.py GLOB
(Breakthrough, 2026-10-20: checks whether F_NOCACHE expert reads could have been served from cached pages.)"""
import ctypes, ctypes.util, glob, mmap, os, sys
import numpy as np
libc = ctypes.CDLL(ctypes.util.find_library("c"), use_errno=True)
libc.mincore.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_size_t, ctypes.c_char_p]
PG = os.sysconf("SC_PAGE_SIZE")
tot = res = 0
for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser(sys.argv[1]))):
    sz = os.path.getsize(f)
    if sz == 0:
        continue
    fd = os.open(f, os.O_RDONLY)
    m = mmap.mmap(fd, sz, prot=mmap.PROT_READ)
    a = np.frombuffer(m, dtype=np.uint8)
    n = (sz + PG - 1) // PG
    vec = ctypes.create_string_buffer(n)
    if libc.mincore(ctypes.c_void_p(a.ctypes.data), ctypes.c_size_t(sz), vec) == 0:
        r = int((np.frombuffer(vec.raw, dtype=np.uint8) & 1).sum())
        tot += n; res += r
    del a
    m.close(); os.close(fd)
print(f"{sys.argv[1]}: {res * PG / 1e9:.1f} GB of {tot * PG / 1e9:.1f} GB in the page cache ({100 * res / max(1, tot):.1f} %)")


### scripts/noise35.py
"""D2 first experiment: noise-injection sweep on the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B proxy (Breakthrough, 2026-10-19).
How much weight error (NMSE) can each tensor class take before it costs more than today's Q4 (MLX affine 4-bit g64)?
Rationale (research/bits_limits.md): HIGGS' linearity theorem (equal weighted error -> equal quality), and an ideal
trellis code at R bits has NMSE ~1.1 x 2^-2R: 3.5 / 3.25 / 3.0 / 2.75 / 2.5 bits -> 0.0086 / 0.012 / 0.017 / 0.024 / 0.034.
Base: the 8-bit unsloth 35B (near-bf16), which is the reference. For ONE tensor class at a time, every matching quantized
module is
  q4  : requantized to affine 4-bit g64 (the anchor: what Q4 costs in that class), or
  none: nothing modified (the floor: run-to-run differences against the separate reference pass)
  nX  : given white Gaussian noise with per-row variance X * mean(w_row^2) on its dequantized weights, then requantized to
        8-bit g64 (which adds ~1e-5 NMSE).
Measured on 3 teacher-forced segments (technical prose, GSM8K solutions, Python code; BT_SEG tokens each): mean and P99
KLD(ref || variant) over the reference's top-64 plus the tail mass, top-1 agreement with the reference, mean NLL delta,
and the class's measured NMSE. The model is reloaded for every arm.
usage: noise35.py ref                  -> data/noise35/ref.npz
       noise35.py CLASS ARM [ARM ...]  -> one line per arm appended to data/noise35/results.txt
CLASS: experts_gu, experts_down, experts, shared, gdn, attn, lm_head, all (every quantized linear but routers/embeddings)
"""
import glob, json, os, sys, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm import load

MODEL = os.environ.get("BT_MODEL") or glob.glob(os.path.expanduser(
    "~/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-8bit/snapshots/*/"))[0]
OUT = os.path.expanduser(os.environ.get("BT_OUT", "~/Breakthrough/data/noise35"))
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
SEG = int(os.environ.get("BT_SEG", "1365"))
TOPK = 64
CLASSES = {
    "experts_gu": lambda n: n.endswith("switch_mlp.gate_proj") or n.endswith("switch_mlp.up_proj"),
    "experts_down": lambda n: n.endswith("switch_mlp.down_proj"),
    "experts": lambda n: ".switch_mlp." in n,
    "shared": lambda n: ".shared_expert." in n,
    "gdn": lambda n: ".linear_attn." in n,
    "attn": lambda n: ".self_attn." in n,
    "lm_head": lambda n: n.endswith("lm_head"),
    "all": lambda n: not (n.endswith("mlp.gate") or n.endswith("shared_expert_gate") or "embed" in n),
}


def segments(tok):
    srcs = [[os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/FINDINGS.md")], None,
            sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/scripts/*.py")))]
    gsm = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))][200:400]
    out = []
    for k, files in enumerate(srcs):
        text = ("\n\n".join("Question: " + p["question"] + "\nAnswer: " + p["answer"] for p in gsm) if files is None
                else "\n\n".join(open(f).read() for f in files[:40]))
        ids = tok.encode(text)[:SEG + 1]
        assert len(ids) == SEG + 1, (k, len(ids))
        out.append(mx.array(ids)[None])
    return out


def logprobs(model, seg):
    out = model(seg[:, :-1])
    lg = (out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out)[0].astype(mx.float32)
    return lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True)


def ref_pass():
    model, tok = load(MODEL)
    d = {}
    for i, s in enumerate(segments(tok)):
        lp = logprobs(model, s)
        top = mx.argpartition(-lp, TOPK - 1, axis=-1)[:, :TOPK]
        tlp = mx.take_along_axis(lp, top, axis=-1)
        nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lp, s[0, 1:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0]
        a1 = mx.argmax(lp, axis=-1)
        mx.eval(top, tlp, nll, a1)
        d[f"top{i}"], d[f"tlp{i}"], d[f"nll{i}"], d[f"a1{i}"] = (np.array(x) for x in (top, tlp, nll, a1))
    np.savez(os.path.join(OUT, "ref.npz"), **d)
    print(f"ref: {len(d) // 4} segments x {SEG} tokens, mean NLL {np.mean([d[f'nll{i}'].mean() for i in range(3)]):.4f}, "
          f"peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB", flush=True)


def modify(model, cls, arm, seed):
    if arm == "none":                      # null arm: reload only, nothing modified (measures the floor vs the ref pass)
        return 0, 0.0
    match = CLASSES[cls]
    mx.random.seed(seed)
    se = sw = 0.0
    n = 0
    for name, m in model.named_modules():
        if "language_model" not in name or not hasattr(m, "scales") or not hasattr(m, "bits") or not match(name):
            continue
        gs, bits, dt = m.group_size, m.bits, m.scales.dtype
        w = mx.dequantize(m.weight, m.scales, m.biases, group_size=gs, bits=bits).astype(mx.float32)
        if arm == "q4":
            ngs, nb, wn = 64, 4, w
        elif arm.startswith("t"):            # QTIP-style trellis fake-quant at k bits (scripts/trellis.py, L=12, CPU Viterbi)
            from trellis import quantize as tq
            ngs, nb = gs, bits
            wn = tq(w, int(arm[1:]), L=int(os.environ.get("BT_TL", "12")))
        else:
            ngs, nb = gs, bits
            wn = w + mx.random.normal(w.shape) * mx.sqrt(float(arm[1:]) * mx.mean(w * w, axis=-1, keepdims=True))
        wq, s, b = mx.quantize(wn.astype(dt), group_size=ngs, bits=nb)
        wd = mx.dequantize(wq, s, b, group_size=ngs, bits=nb).astype(mx.float32)
        e, t = mx.sum((wd - w) ** 2), mx.sum(w * w)
        mx.eval(wq, s, b, e, t)
        se += e.item()
        sw += t.item()
        n += 1
        m.weight, m.scales, m.biases, m.group_size, m.bits = wq, s, b, ngs, nb
        del w, wn, wd
        mx.clear_cache()
    return n, se / max(sw, 1e-30)


def compare(model, segs, ref):
    kl, agree, dnll, seg_kl = [], [], [], []
    for i, s in enumerate(segs):
        lp = logprobs(model, s)
        top, p_lp = mx.array(ref[f"top{i}"]), mx.array(ref[f"tlp{i}"])
        q_lp = mx.take_along_axis(lp, top, axis=-1)
        p, q = mx.exp(p_lp), mx.exp(q_lp)
        pt, qt = mx.maximum(1 - p.sum(-1), 1e-12), mx.maximum(1 - q.sum(-1), 1e-12)
        k = (p * (p_lp - q_lp)).sum(-1) + pt * (mx.log(pt) - mx.log(qt))
        nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lp, s[0, 1:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0]
        a1 = mx.argmax(lp, axis=-1)
        mx.eval(k, nll, a1)
        kl.append(np.array(k))
        seg_kl.append(float(kl[-1].mean()))
        agree.append(np.array(a1) == ref[f"a1{i}"])
        dnll.append(np.array(nll) - ref[f"nll{i}"])
    kl, agree, dnll = (np.concatenate(x) for x in (kl, agree, dnll))
    return kl.mean(), np.percentile(kl, 99), agree.mean(), dnll.mean(), seg_kl


def main():
    if sys.argv[1] == "ref":
        return ref_pass()
    cls, arms = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2:]
    ref = np.load(os.path.join(OUT, "ref.npz"))
    for j, arm in enumerate(arms):
        t0 = time.time()
        model, tok = load(MODEL)
        segs = segments(tok)
        n, nmse = modify(model, cls, arm, seed=1000 + j)
        kl, kl99, agree, dnll, seg_kl = compare(model, segs, ref)
        line = (f"{cls:12s} {arm:8s} modules {n:4d}  NMSE {nmse:.5f}  KLD {kl:.5f} (P99 {kl99:.4f}; prose/gsm/code "
                f"{seg_kl[0]:.4f}/{seg_kl[1]:.4f}/{seg_kl[2]:.4f})  top-1 agree {agree:.4f}  dNLL {dnll:+.4f}  "
                f"{time.time() - t0:.0f}s peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB")
        print(line, flush=True)
        open(os.path.join(OUT, "results.txt"), "a").write(line + "\n")
        del model, segs
        mx.clear_cache()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### scripts/pass_budget_sim.py
"""Pass-level expert budget (Breakthrough, 2026-10-19): a pass of K consecutive tokens may use at most U experts per
layer, chosen by rank-weighted votes of the rows' router top-10 (weight 1/(rank+1)). Reports the share of each row's
rank-0, rank-0/1 and top-10 choices inside the budget, and RAM bytes per token for that pass (U experts x 48 layers x
expert MB, plus dense) if all K tokens were accepted. 125B traces (MoEPrefetch traces125). Quality is NOT modelled.
"""
import numpy as np
T = "/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/"; SETS = ["code", "chat", "bulgarian", "math"]; NL = 48
trs = [np.stack([np.load(T + f"{s}_800.npz")[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32)[:, :, :10] for s in SETS]
w = 1.0 / (np.arange(10) + 1)
def run(K, U):
    r0 = r01 = r10 = n = 0; uni = []
    for tr in trs:
        for a in range(0, tr.shape[0] - K + 1, K):
            blk = tr[a:a + K]
            for l in range(NL):
                ids = blk[:, l]; uni.append(len(np.unique(ids)))
                votes = {}
                for row in ids:
                    for r, e in enumerate(row): votes[e] = votes.get(e, 0) + w[r]
                keep = set(sorted(votes, key=votes.get, reverse=True)[:U])
                for row in ids:
                    r0 += row[0] in keep; r01 += (row[0] in keep) + (row[1] in keep); r10 += sum(e in keep for e in row); n += 1
    return r0 / n, r01 / (2 * n), r10 / (10 * n), np.mean(uni)
print("K rows, U budget per layer: kept share of rank-0 / ranks 0-1 / top-10 uses; natural union; GB/token at 2.5 b (1.54 MB/expert, dense 2.0 GB) if all K accepted")
for K in (4, 16, 32):
    for U in (10, 16, 26, 40, 64):
        a, b, c, u = run(K, U)
        gb = (2.0 + min(U, u) * 48 * 1.536e-3) / K
        print(f"K={K:<3d} U={U:<3d} kept {a:.3f} / {b:.3f} / {c:.3f}   union {u:5.1f}   {gb:5.2f} GB/token -> {250/gb:5.0f} tok/s at 250 GB/s")


### scripts/pass_mass_sim.py
"""Wait-by-gate-mass under speculative verify passes (Breakthrough, 2026-10-20 22:45). 125B traces with router probs.
A pass = K consecutive tokens (verify rows; ~3 accepted per pass at MTP 3:3). Per layer and pass, each row needs the
ranks covering theta of its top-10 mass (at least rank 0); the pass reads the union of needed experts not resident
(LRU capacity C, whole 4-bit experts, 2.77 MB). Rows' other ranks are computed if resident (frugal), else skipped.
Prints per token: loads, MB, missed mass; per layer and pass: mean / P90 loads (the parallel read depth of one wait).
usage: pass_mass_sim.py [C ...]"""
import glob, sys
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict

NL = 48
sets = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
        for f in sorted(glob.glob("/Users/kamen/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz"))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in sets.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in sets.items()}
w = {k: g[:, :, :10] / g[:, :, :10].sum(-1, keepdims=True) for k, g in gp.items()}


def run(C, theta, K):
    loads = ntok = 0
    per, missed = [], []
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt = ids[k], w[k]
        T = tr.shape[0]; ntok += T
        cw = np.cumsum(wt, -1)
        for l in range(NL):
            c = OrderedDict()
            for a in range(0, T, K):
                need, rows = [], range(a, min(a + K, T))
                for t in rows:
                    nw = int(np.argmax(cw[t, l] >= theta - 1e-6)) + 1
                    for r in range(nw):
                        e = int(tr[t, l, r])
                        if e not in need: need.append(e)
                miss = [e for e in need if e not in c]
                loads += len(miss); per.append(len(miss))
                for e in miss: c[e] = 1
                for t in rows:
                    m = 0.0
                    for r in range(10):
                        e = int(tr[t, l, r])
                        if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                        else: m += float(wt[t, l, r])
                    missed.append(m)
                while len(c) > C:
                    c.popitem(last=False)
    p = np.array(per)
    return loads / ntok, np.mean(missed), p.mean(), np.percentile(p, 90)


for C in [int(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or [51, 102]:
    print(f"C={C} ({100 * C / 512:.0f} %)", flush=True)
    for theta in (0.36, 0.70, 0.85):
        for K in (1, 4):
            ld, mm, pm, p90 = run(C, theta, K)
            print(f"  theta {theta:.2f} K={K}: loads {ld:6.1f}/token ({ld * 2.77:4.0f} MB)  missed {mm:.3f}  per layer-pass {pm:4.1f} (P90 {p90:.0f})", flush=True)


### scripts/pfr_ab.py
"""Breakthrough L2-11 (NewIdeas PORT GO 12:30): the A/B before bt_b12's B12_PFR becomes the default. One arm per process,
on the v3l engine, AR path, shipped env (B12LL, no lane): the first PA_N tokens of data/prompts/ctx64k.txt prefilled by
B.b12_prefill(model, ids[:-1], cache, 512, 4096) (B12_PFR=1: bt_b12 releases the trunk pools and picks the 512-aligned
chunks itself), then 32 greedy one-row decode steps. Exact = the same KV hash after the prefill, first-step logits hash
and 32-token sha as the B12_PFR=0 arm. Harness = Fit12's scripts/fit_pfr64.py (same KV hash, phase sampler and RESULT
line, so the hashes compare with Fit12's r8 / r64 on v3p), with the release / restore inside bt_b12 instead.
Usage: [BigMoE venv] B12_ENGINE=v3l B12_PFR=0|1 PA_N=8474|65000 pfr_ab.py TAG"""
import ctypes, hashlib, json, os, sys, threading, time

ENV = {"B12_CACHE_MB": "64", "BIGMOE_TIER_PF_GROUP": "64", "BIGMOE_PF_GROUP": "64", "BIGMOE_MTP_C": "32",
       "BIGMOE_ASYNC_EVERY": "1", "B12_LATE3": "2,10", "B12_NORM": "mass"}          # pk64.py's shipped env
for k, v in ENV.items():
    os.environ.setdefault(k, v)
TAG = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "pfr_ab"
N, DEC = int(os.environ.get("PA_N", "65000")), int(os.environ.get("PA_DEC", "32"))


class _RU(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("uuid", ctypes.c_uint8 * 16)] + [(f"u{i}", ctypes.c_uint64) for i in range(40)]


_LIBC = ctypes.CDLL(None)


def fp():                                            # (now, lifetime peak) physical footprint, GB
    ru = _RU()
    return (ru.u7 / 1e9, ru.u28 / 1e9) if _LIBC.proc_pid_rusage(os.getpid(), 4, ctypes.byref(ru)) == 0 else (float("nan"),) * 2


import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
PH = {"name": "load"}
PK = {}                                              # phase -> [max footprint, max MLX active, samples]


def _sampler():
    while True:
        f, a = fp()[0], mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9
        d = PK.setdefault(PH["name"], [0.0, 0.0, 0])
        d[0], d[1], d[2] = max(d[0], f), max(d[1], a), d[2] + 1
        time.sleep(0.02)


threading.Thread(target=_sampler, daemon=True).start()
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import bt_b12 as B
rt, st = B.load_b12(0.10, mtp=False)
from bigmoe_v3f.fuse import reset_generation_state
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
ids = tok.encode(open(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/prompts/ctx64k.txt")).read())[:N + 1]
assert len(ids) == N + 1, len(ids)
time.sleep(0.1)
PH["name"] = "idle"


def kv_hash(cache):
    """(sha of the whole cache, per-layer prefixes): every array of each layer's state, bf16 hashed as exact float32"""
    full, per = hashlib.sha1(), []
    for c in cache:
        h = hashlib.sha1()
        for a in (c.state if hasattr(c, "state") else []):
            if isinstance(a, mx.array):
                h.update(np.array(a.astype(mx.float32) if a.dtype == mx.bfloat16 else a).tobytes())
        full.update(h.digest())
        per.append(h.hexdigest()[:6])
    return full.hexdigest()[:10], per


n = len(ids) - 1
n0, p0 = fp()
print(f"[{TAG}] engine {B.ENGINE}, AR path, N {n}, B12_PFR {os.environ.get('B12_PFR', '0')}, C_m {B.CFG['cm']} C_l "
      f"{B.CFG['cl']}, pools {st.resident_gb():.2f} GB | after load: active {mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9:.2f} GB, "
      f"footprint now {n0:.2f}, lifetime peak {p0:.2f} GB", flush=True)
reset_generation_state(model); B.STASH.clear(); B.PENDH.clear()
mx.clear_cache()
mx.reset_peak_memory()
PH["name"] = "prefill"
tw = t0 = time.perf_counter()
cache = model.make_cache()
B.b12_prefill(model, mx.array([ids[:-1]]), cache, 512, 4096)
mx.eval([a for c in cache for a in (c.state if hasattr(c, "state") else []) if isinstance(a, mx.array)])
tp = time.perf_counter() - t0
pk = mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9
print(f"[{TAG}] prefill {n} tokens in {tp:.1f} s = {n / tp:.0f} tok/s | PFR {B.PFR_LOG or '-'} (tokens, s_moe, GB, release s, "
      f"restore s) | MLX peak {pk:.2f} GB | after prefill footprint {fp()[0]:.2f} GB, MLX active "
      f"{mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9:.2f} GB", flush=True)
PH["name"] = "kvhash"
t0 = time.perf_counter()
kvh, kvl = kv_hash(cache)
th = time.perf_counter() - t0
print(f"[{TAG}] KV after prefill: sha {kvh} ({th:.2f} s, not in the timings) | per layer {' '.join(kvl)}", flush=True)
PH["name"] = "decode"
y, out, lhash, TT = ids[-1], [], None, []
td = time.perf_counter()
for i in range(DEC):
    lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
    lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1]
    if i == 0:
        lhash = hashlib.sha1(np.array(lg.astype(mx.float32)).tobytes()).hexdigest()[:10]
    y = int(mx.argmax(lg).item())
    out.append(y)
    TT.append(time.perf_counter())
st.gate.check()
f2 = fp()
sha8 = hashlib.sha1(json.dumps(out[:8]).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
sha32 = hashlib.sha1(json.dumps(out).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
print(f"[{TAG}] decode {DEC}: time to first token {TT[0] - tw - th:.2f} s (first step {TT[0] - td:.2f} s), steps 2-{DEC} "
      f"{(DEC - 1) / (TT[-1] - TT[0]):.2f} tok/s | after decode footprint {f2[0]:.2f} GB | process lifetime peak "
      f"{f2[1]:.2f} GB | sha {sha8} (first 8; logits {lhash}), {sha32} ({DEC})", flush=True)
print(f"[{TAG}] RESULT kv {kvh} logits {lhash} sha32 {sha32} sha8 {sha8}", flush=True)
PH["name"] = "end"
time.sleep(0.1)
for ph, (f, a, n_) in PK.items():
    print(f"[{TAG}] phase {ph}: peak footprint {f:.2f} GB, peak MLX active {a:.2f} GB ({n_} samples)", flush=True)
print(f"[{TAG}] done", flush=True)


### scripts/pk64.py
"""Queue L2-10 (Breakthrough, 2026-10-25): where the 64K prefill's footprint peak sits, and what cuts it.
One variant per process (footprint peaks are per process): B12LL, the decoupled prefill of the first PK_N tokens of
data/prompts/ctx64k.txt, then 8 greedy one-row decode steps (token sha + a hash of the first step's logits: exactness
across variants). Two samplers: (1) 20 Hz physical footprint, MLX active and bt_b12.PHASE; (2) every 3 s `footprint -p`
categories: 'IOAccelerator (graphics)' (mapped GPU buffers) and 'Owned physical footprint (unmapped) (graphics)' (GPU
memory already freed but not yet reclaimed; it still counts in the footprint: 10-25 10:35, kv8_long at 32K, it swung
0.07 -> 2.2 -> 0.07 GB within seconds of large frees).
Suspect (10-25): every s_attn chunk grows each QSA layer's stock KV by concatenation (a new history + 512 rows buffer, the
old one freed: at 64K ~1.5 GB freed per chunk over 12 layers), so freed-not-reclaimed memory piles up late in the prompt.
env: PK_N (65000), PK_ATTN (512), PK_MOE (4096), PK_TARGET (0 = stock growth; 1 = mtplx qsa_rows_target(N + PK_HEAD) around
     the prefill: every QSA buffer sized once, attention reads the same rows), PK_HEAD (1024), PK_MTP (1 = the MTP head's
     history appended per MoE chunk, as b12_prefill_state), PK_KV8 (1 = b12_kv8 8-bit KV), PK_LANE (1 = the prefill inside
     mtplx attention_phase("prefill"), as MTPLX's own generation runs it: chunks with >= 32K history then take the sparse
     selector + NAX flash consumer instead of the dense [S, T] mask (b12_prefill never set it: every chunk was dense;
     same visible set, not bit-exact)), B12_CACHE_MB, PK_TAG
     -> a line in data/pk64/results.txt, the series in data/pk64/<tag>.json"""
import hashlib, json, os, subprocess, sys, threading, time

os.environ.setdefault("B12_LATE3", "2,10")
os.environ.setdefault("B12_NORM", "mass")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
HOME = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough")
OUT = os.path.join(HOME, "data/pk64")
N, SA, SM = int(os.environ.get("PK_N", "65000")), int(os.environ.get("PK_ATTN", "512")), int(os.environ.get("PK_MOE", "4096"))
TARGET, HEAD = os.environ.get("PK_TARGET", "0") == "1", int(os.environ.get("PK_HEAD", "1024"))
MTP, KV8 = os.environ.get("PK_MTP", "0") == "1", os.environ.get("PK_KV8", "0") == "1"
LANE = os.environ.get("PK_LANE", "0") == "1"
TAG = os.environ.get("PK_TAG") or (f"n{N}_a{SA}_m{SM}" + ("_tgt" if TARGET else "") + ("_lane" if LANE else "") +
                                   ("_mtp" if MTP else "") +
                                   ("_kv8" if KV8 else "") + f"_c{os.environ.get('B12_CACHE_MB', '-')}")
import bt_b12 as B
rt, st = B.load_b12(0.10, mtp=MTP)
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
import mtplx.models.qwen4_exp as Q
from bigmoe_v3f.fuse import reset_generation_state
from bigmoe_v3f import tier as _T                             # PF counters + the L2-10r scatter flag, for the result line
if KV8:
    import b12_kv8 as K
    K.install(); K.ON[0] = True
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
ids = tok.encode(open(os.path.join(HOME, "data/prompts/ctx64k.txt")).read())[:N + 1]
assert len(ids) == N + 1, len(ids)
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)

import ctypes


class _RU(ctypes.Structure):                                 # rusage_info_v4 (as kv8_tf): u7 = physical footprint now
    _fields_ = [("uuid", ctypes.c_uint8 * 16)] + [(f"u{i}", ctypes.c_uint64) for i in range(40)]


libc = ctypes.CDLL(None)


def fp_now():
    ru = _RU()
    return ru.u7 / 1e9 if libc.proc_pid_rusage(os.getpid(), 4, ctypes.byref(ru)) == 0 else 0.0


SER, CAT, STOP, T0 = [], [], threading.Event(), time.perf_counter()
CATS = {"IOAccelerator (graphics)": "ioacc", "Owned physical footprint (unmapped) (graphics)": "unmapped",
        "MALLOC_LARGE": "malloc_large", "untagged (VM_ALLOCATE)": "untagged"}


def sampler():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        try:
            p = B.PHASE.get("p")
            SER.append((round(time.perf_counter() - T0, 2), round(fp_now(), 3), round(mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9, 3),
                        list(p) if isinstance(p, tuple) else p))
        except Exception:
            pass
        STOP.wait(0.05)


def categories():                                            # footprint -p: which kind of memory makes the gap
    while not STOP.is_set():
        try:
            t, p = round(time.perf_counter() - T0, 2), B.PHASE.get("p")
            o = subprocess.run(["footprint", "-p", str(os.getpid()), "-f", "bytes"], capture_output=True, text=True,
                               timeout=20).stdout
            row = {"t": t, "p": list(p) if isinstance(p, tuple) else p}
            for ln in o.splitlines():
                for k, v in CATS.items():
                    if ln.rstrip().endswith(k):
                        row[v] = round(int(ln.split()[0]) / 1e9, 3)
                if "phys_footprint:" in ln:
                    row["fp"] = round(int(ln.split()[1]) / 1e9, 3)
            CAT.append(row)
        except Exception as e:
            CAT.append({"err": repr(e)[:80]})
        STOP.wait(3.0)


reset_generation_state(model)
B.STASH.clear(); B.PENDH.clear()
mx.clear_cache()
B.PHASE.clear()
th = [threading.Thread(target=f, daemon=True) for f in (sampler, categories)]
for t_ in th:
    t_.start()
time.sleep(3.5)                                              # a baseline sample of each kind before the prefill
base = (fp_now(), mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9)
mx.reset_peak_memory()
t0 = time.perf_counter()
R = ((N + HEAD + 255) // 256) * 256
cache = model.make_cache()
head = None
pids = mx.array([ids[:-1]])
from contextlib import nullcontext
from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
with (Q.qsa_rows_target(R) if TARGET else nullcontext()), (attention_phase("prefill") if LANE else nullcontext()):
    if MTP:                                                  # b12_prefill_state's path (D1I_HEAD_EACH=1), the head's
        if os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode") not in sys.path:   # appends tagged in PHASE
            sys.path.insert(1, os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode"))
        import spec125
        head = spec125._MTPHead(model)

        def feed(pairs):
            for (a, b), h in pairs:
                B.PHASE["p"] = (a, "mtp")
                head.append(h, [int(v) for v in ids[a + 1:b + 1]])
                mx.eval(head.logits)
        B.b12_prefill(model, pids, cache, SA, SM, on_chunk=feed)
    else:
        B.b12_prefill(model, pids, cache, SA, SM)
    mx.eval([a for c in cache for a in (c.state if hasattr(c, "state") else []) if isinstance(a, mx.array)])
tp = time.perf_counter() - t0
mlx_peak = mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9
after_pf = (fp_now(), mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9)
B.PHASE["p"] = "decode"
y, out, lhash = ids[-1], [], None
for i in range(8):
    lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
    lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1]
    if i == 0:
        lhash = hashlib.sha1(np.array(lg.astype(mx.float32)).tobytes()).hexdigest()[:10]
    y = int(mx.argmax(lg).item())
    out.append(y)
st.gate.check()
after_dec = (fp_now(), mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9)
DEC = {}
if os.environ.get("PK_DECAB", "0") == "1":                  # paired decode A/B on the same 64K cache: MTPLX_QSA_FLASH
    B.PHASE["p"] = "decab"                                   # (opt-in flash-skip decode lane; off = the dense [1, T]
    for r in range(3):                                       # mask over the whole KV in all 12 QSA layers), interleaved
        for fl in ("0", "1"):                                # rounds of 16 greedy steps, median ms per step per round
            os.environ["MTPLX_QSA_FLASH"] = fl
            ts = []
            for i in range(16):
                t1 = time.perf_counter()
                lg = model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache)
                lg = (lg[0] if isinstance(lg, tuple) else lg)[0, -1]
                y = int(mx.argmax(lg).item())
                ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t1)
            DEC.setdefault(fl, []).append(round(1e3 * float(np.median(ts[2:])), 1))
    os.environ.pop("MTPLX_QSA_FLASH", None)
    st.gate.check()
time.sleep(3.5)
STOP.set()
for t_ in th:
    t_.join(timeout=25)
pf = [r for r in SER if r[3] not in (None, "decode", "decab")]
top = max(pf, key=lambda r: r[1])
gap = max(pf, key=lambda r: r[1] - r[2])
act = max(pf, key=lambda r: r[2])
kinds = {}
for r in pf:
    k = r[3][-1] if isinstance(r[3], list) else str(r[3])
    if r[1] > kinds.get(k, (0,))[0]:
        kinds[k] = (r[1], r[2], r[3])
cpf = [c for c in CAT if "unmapped" in c and c["p"] not in (None, "decode", "decab")]
um = max(cpf, key=lambda c: c["unmapped"]) if cpf else {}
kvb = sum(c.kv.nbytes for c in cache if hasattr(c, "kv")) / 1e9
sha = hashlib.sha1(json.dumps(out).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
f2 = lambda r: f"{r[1]:.2f} GB (act {r[2]:.2f}) at {tuple(r[3]) if isinstance(r[3], list) else r[3]}"
line = (f"pk64 {TAG}: N {N} attn/moe {SA}/{SM} target {R if TARGET else 0} lane {int(LANE)} mtp {int(MTP)} kv8 {int(KV8)} cache_mb "
        f"{os.environ.get('B12_CACHE_MB', '-')} | prefill {tp:.0f} s | before {base[0]:.2f}/{base[1]:.2f} | PEAK fp {f2(top)} | "
        f"widest fp-act {gap[1] - gap[2]:.2f} at {tuple(gap[3]) if isinstance(gap[3], list) else gap[3]} | max act {act[2]:.2f} | "
        f"MLX peak {mlx_peak:.2f} | after prefill fp/act {after_pf[0]:.2f}/{after_pf[1]:.2f}, after 8 decode "
        f"{after_dec[0]:.2f}/{after_dec[1]:.2f} | unmapped max {um.get('unmapped', float('nan')):.2f} GB at {um.get('p')} "
        f"(ioacc {um.get('ioacc', float('nan')):.2f}) | QSA KV {kvb:.3f} GB | per part " +
        " ".join(f"{k} {v[0]:.2f}" for k, v in sorted(kinds.items())) + f" | tiers {dict(getattr(Q, '_QSA_PREFILL_COUNTS', {}))}"
        f" | sha {sha} logits {lhash} | {tok.decode(out)!r}" +
        (f" | decode ms/step dense {DEC['0']} flash {DEC['1']}" if DEC else "") +
        f" | env TIER_PF_GROUP {os.environ.get('BIGMOE_TIER_PF_GROUP', '-')} PF_GROUP {os.environ.get('BIGMOE_PF_GROUP', '-')}"
        f" ASYNC_EVERY {os.environ.get('BIGMOE_ASYNC_EVERY', '-')}"
        f" | tier scatter {getattr(_T, '_SCATTER', '-')} grouped/single/fallback {_T.PF['grouped']}/{_T.PF['single']}/{_T.PF['fallback']}")
print(line, flush=True)
with open(os.path.join(OUT, "results.txt"), "a") as f:
    f.write(time.strftime("%m-%d %H:%M ") + line + "\n")
json.dump({"tag": TAG, "line": line, "series": SER, "categories": CAT}, open(os.path.join(OUT, TAG + ".json"), "w"))


### scripts/plane_sim.py
"""Frugal routing x bit-plane expert cache (Breakthrough D1 x planes, 2026-10-20): trace replay on the 125B routing
traces (MoEPrefetch traces125: router top-20 ids per decode token and layer; code / chat / bulgarian / math, 800 tokens).

Expert = 4.9152M weights: 4 code planes of 0.614 MB + scales/biases 0.307 MB (2.765 MB at 4 bits). A plane cache keeps
per layer an H tier of MSB-k parts (k planes + scales: k=2 1.536 MB, k=3 2.150 MB) for C_H experts and an F tier of
the remaining 4-k planes (LSB parts) for C_F experts whose MSB part is resident; both LRU, fixed byte budgets.
Frugal rule per token (eager, one row) with plan bits b(r) per router rank (e.g. 4433332222):
  ranks 0-1: full 4 bits always: missing MSB part -> read MSB + LSB (waited); MSB resident, LSB not -> read LSB (waited)
  ranks 2-9: resident MSB -> use at min(b(r), planes resident) bits (b(r) = 4 needs the LSB part too, else MSB-k);
             missing -> the best-ranked resident expert among the row's ranks 10-19 (at min(b(r), resident) bits),
             else the rank is dropped. Nothing is read for ranks >= 2.
Baseline 'full': today's frugal (one tier of whole 4-bit experts, C = budget / 2.765 MB).
Prints per budget and layout: waited SSD MB/token, reads/token, dropped / substituted ranks per token, mean bits of the
used ranks 2-9, and the slot counts (the GPU emulation runs frugal with C = C_H slots + the plan's bits by position).
usage: plane_sim.py [FRAC ...]   (default 0.05 0.10; budget = FRAC x 512 x 2.765 MB per layer)"""
import sys
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np

T = "/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/"; SETS = ["code", "chat", "bulgarian", "math"]; NL = 48; E = 512
PL, SC = 0.6144, 0.3072                       # MB per code plane, MB of scales + biases per expert
FULL = 4 * PL + SC
trs = {s: np.stack([np.load(T + f"{s}_800.npz")[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for s in SETS}


def run(tr, k, CH, CF, plan):
    """one prompt's trace -> per-token reads MB, reads, drops, subs, mean bits of used ranks 2-9"""
    mb = reads = drops = subs = 0.0
    bits_sum = bits_n = 0
    sM, sL = k * PL + SC, (4 - k) * PL
    for l in range(NL):
        H, F = OrderedDict(), OrderedDict()
        for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
            row = [int(x) for x in tr[t, l]]
            used = set()
            for r in range(2):                                  # waited ranks: full 4 bits
                e = row[r]
                if e not in H:
                    mb += sM; reads += 1
                    H[e] = 1
                if k < 4 and e not in F:
                    mb += sL; reads += 1
                    F[e] = 1
                H.move_to_end(e)
                if k < 4: F.move_to_end(e)
                used.add(e)
            alt = [e for e in row[10:20] if e in H]
            for r in range(2, 10):
                e, b = row[r], int(plan[r])
                if e in H and e not in used:
                    x = e
                else:
                    x = next((y for y in alt if y not in used), None)
                    if x is None:
                        drops += 1
                        continue
                    subs += 1
                used.add(x)
                have = 4 if (k == 4 or x in F) else k
                bits_sum += min(b, have); bits_n += 1
                H.move_to_end(x)
                if b > k and x in F:
                    F.move_to_end(x)
            while len(H) > CH:
                v, _ = H.popitem(last=False)
                F.pop(v, None)
            while len(F) > CF:
                F.popitem(last=False)
    n = tr.shape[0]
    return mb / n, reads / n, drops / n, subs / n, bits_sum / max(1, bits_n)


fracs = [float(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or [0.05, 0.10]
print("waited SSD MB/token (reads/token) | dropped / substituted ranks 2-9 per token | mean bits of used ranks 2-9; mean of 4 prompts")
for frac in fracs:
    B = frac * E * FULL                                          # MB per layer
    print(f"\nbudget {frac:.0%} of the 4-bit experts = {B:.0f} MB per layer, {B * NL / 1000:.1f} GB of expert cache")
    rows = [("full 4-bit (today's frugal)", 4, int(B / FULL), 0, "4444444444")]
    for k, plan in ((3, "4433333333"), (3, "4433332222"), (2, "4422222222"), (2, "4444222222")):
        sM, sL = k * PL + SC, (4 - k) * PL
        for fshare in (0.0, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5):               # share of the budget given to the LSB tier
            CF = int(B * fshare / sL)
            CH = int((B - CF * sL) / sM)
            if CF > CH: continue
            rows.append((f"MSB-{k} H={CH} F={CF} plan {plan}", k, CH, CF, plan))
    for label, k, CH, CF, plan in rows:
        mb, rd, dr, su, bt = np.mean([run(trs[s], k, CH, CF, plan) for s in SETS], 0)
        print(f"  {label:44s} {mb:6.1f} MB ({rd:5.1f}) | drop {dr:5.1f} sub {su:5.1f} | bits {bt:.2f}", flush=True)


### scripts/rd_sim.py
"""Rate-distortion-optimal coverage for frugal routing (Breakthrough, 2026-10-21 06:15). 125B traces with router probs,
LRU cache of C whole 4-bit experts per layer. Per token-layer, ranks 0-1 are always computed at 4 bits (read if
missing); each missing rank r >= 2 (top-10 weight w) takes the option minimising  err + lam * MB:
    drop        err w^2              0 MB
    MSB-2 read  err NMSE2 * w^2      1.536 MB   (not cached)
    MSB-3 read  err NMSE3 * w^2      2.150 MB   (not cached)
    4-bit read  err 0                2.765 MB   (enters the cache)
    [optional]  trellis 2-bit / 1-bit copies (NMSE 0.071 / 0.27 at ~1.30 / ~0.70 MB incl. scales), --trellis
Missed energy E = mean over token-layers of the summed err; the probes fit KLD_excess ~ 0.0051 * (E / 0.0032)^1.5 on
the hard subset (ifeval+multi; exact floor 0.0043), validated by m85_nr_t2 (predicted 0.0152, measured 0.0151).
Prints the frontier (MB/token vs predicted KLD) for each lam, plus the simple theta rules for comparison.
usage: rd_sim.py [C] [--trellis]"""
import glob, os, sys
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict

NL = 48
NMSE2, NMSE3 = 0.10, 0.02
OPTS = [("drop", 1.0, 0.0), ("msb2", NMSE2, 1.536), ("msb3", NMSE3, 2.150), ("full", 0.0, 2.765)]
if "--trellis" in sys.argv:
    OPTS += [("tr2", 0.071, 1.30), ("tr1", 0.27, 0.70)]
args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith("--")]
C = int(args[0]) if args else 51
sets = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
        for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz")))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in sets.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in sets.items()}
w = {k: g[:, :, :10] / g[:, :, :10].sum(-1, keepdims=True) for k, g in gp.items()}


def kld(E):
    return 0.0043 + 0.0051 * (E / 0.0032) ** 1.5


def run(choose):
    """choose(r, w) -> option index; returns E per token-layer, MB per token, option counts per token"""
    E = mb = 0.0; ntl = ntok = 0; cnt = np.zeros(len(OPTS))
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt = ids[k], w[k]
        ntok += tr.shape[0]
        for l in range(NL):
            c = OrderedDict()
            for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
                ntl += 1
                for r in range(10):
                    e, ww = int(tr[t, l, r]), float(wt[t, l, r])
                    if e in c:
