                        c.move_to_end(e); continue
                    o = 3 if r < 2 else choose(r, ww)
                    name, err, size = OPTS[o]
                    E += err * ww * ww; mb += size; cnt[o] += 1
                    if name == "full":
                        c[e] = 1
                while len(c) > C:
                    c.popitem(last=False)
    return E / ntl, mb / ntok, cnt / ntok


print(f"C={C}: options {[o[0] for o in OPTS]}; predicted KLD on the hard subset from E")
for lam in (1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 2e-3, 4e-3, 8e-3):
    def choose(r, ww, lam=lam):
        return int(np.argmin([err * ww * ww + lam * size for _, err, size in OPTS]))
    E, mb, cnt = run(choose)
    print(f"  RD lam {lam:.0e}: {mb:6.1f} MB/token, E {E:.4f} -> KLD ~{kld(E):.4f} | per token " +
          " ".join(f"{OPTS[i][0]} {cnt[i]:.1f}" for i in range(len(OPTS))), flush=True)
for th in (0.5, 0.7, 0.85):            # the theta rule with full reads, for comparison (cumulative mass needs the row)
    E = mb = 0.0; ntl = ntok = 0
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt = ids[k], w[k]; ntok += tr.shape[0]
        cw = np.cumsum(wt, -1)
        for l in range(NL):
            c = OrderedDict()
            for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
                ntl += 1
                nw = int(np.argmax(cw[t, l] >= th - 1e-6)) + 1
                for r in range(10):
                    e, ww = int(tr[t, l, r]), float(wt[t, l, r])
                    if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                    elif r < nw: c[e] = 1; mb += 2.765
                    else: E += ww * ww
                while len(c) > C: c.popitem(last=False)
    print(f"  theta {th:.2f} (4-bit reads): {mb / ntok:6.1f} MB/token, E {E / ntl:.4f} -> KLD ~{kld(E / ntl):.4f}", flush=True)


### scripts/reroute_energy.py
"""Gate-energy prediction of near-tie re-routing (NewIdeas' bNN: +delta on resident experts' router logits before the
top-10, true ranks 0-1 pinned, weights from the original probabilities over the true top-10 sum, every chosen expert read)
before its GPU probe (Breakthrough 2026-10-21 15:55). Experts are orthogonal with similar norms (energy_sim.py), so the
error of a swap is the displaced expert's w^2 plus the added expert's w^2. Replays the 125B traces (top-20 ids and gate
probabilities, data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz) with an LRU cache of C per layer, prints reads/token, swapped mass and
energy, and predicts teacher-forced KLD with the energy law fitted per kind of text on the measured mass-rule probes
(ifeval+multi: frugal/m50/m70/m85 rows; gsm+code: the _Sgc rows).   usage: reroute_energy.py [C ...]"""
import glob, os, sys
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict

NL = 48
T = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
     for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz")))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in T.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float64) for k, d in T.items()}


def replay(C, sets, delta=0.0, nw_rule=None):
    """delta >= 0: re-routing policy (all chosen read). nw_rule: mass-rule policy instead (wait ranks < nw, others used
    only if resident, no renorm). Returns reads/token, swapped-or-missed mass and energy (per layer, mean)."""
    reads = mass = en = 0.0; ntok = 0
    for k in sets:
        tr, g = ids[k], gp[k]
        ntok += tr.shape[0]
        for l in range(NL):
            c = OrderedDict()
            for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
                row, p = tr[t, l], g[t, l]
                w = p / p[:10].sum()
                res = np.array([int(e) in c for e in row])
                if nw_rule is not None:
                    nw = nw_rule(w[:10])
                    use = [r for r in range(10) if r < nw or res[r]]
                    miss = [r for r in range(10) if r >= nw and not res[r]]
                    mass += w[miss].sum(); en += (w[miss] ** 2).sum()
                else:
                    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-30)) + delta * res
                    z[:2] = np.inf                                  # true ranks 0-1 pinned
                    use = list(np.argsort(-z, kind="stable")[:10])
                    disp = [r for r in range(10) if r not in use]
                    add = [r for r in use if r >= 10]
                    mass += w[disp].sum(); en += (w[disp] ** 2).sum() + (w[add] ** 2).sum()
                for r in use:
                    e = int(row[r])
                    if e in c:
                        c.move_to_end(e)
                    else:
                        c[e] = 1; reads += 1
                while len(c) > C:
                    c.popitem(last=False)
    n = ntok
    return reads / n, mass / (n * NL), en / (n * NL)


def theta(th):
    return lambda ww: int(np.argmax(np.cumsum(ww) >= th - 1e-6)) + 1


HARD, EASY = ("chat", "bulgarian"), ("code", "math")
# measured teacher-forced KLD at C=51 (data/tfvar/results.txt, every 8th): hard = ifeval+multi, easy = gsm+code
MEAS = {"hard": {"exact": 0.0041, "r2": 0.1001, "m50": None, "m70": None, "m85": 0.0094},
        "easy": {"exact": None, "r2": 0.0273, "m50": 0.0114, "m70": 0.0045, "m85": 0.0021}}
RULES = {"r2": lambda ww: 2, "m50": theta(0.5), "m70": theta(0.7), "m85": theta(0.85), "exact": lambda ww: 10}
Cs = [int(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or [51, 77, 97]
print("fit at C=51: KLD = k0 + a * E^1.5 per kind of text (E = mean per-layer missed/swapped gate energy)")
law = {}
for kind, sets in (("hard", HARD), ("easy", EASY)):
    pts = []
    for pol, kl in MEAS[kind].items():
        if kl is None:
            continue
        r, m, e = replay(51, sets, nw_rule=RULES[pol])
        pts.append((pol, e, kl, r))
        print(f"  {kind:4s} {pol:5s} E {e:.5f}  mass {m:.4f}  reads {r:6.1f}  measured KLD {kl:.4f}")
    X = np.array([[1.0, p[1] ** 1.5] for p in pts]); y = np.array([p[2] for p in pts])
    (k0, a), *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(X, y, rcond=None)
    law[kind] = (k0, a)
    print(f"  {kind}: k0 {k0:.4f}  a {a:.2f}  residuals " + " ".join(f"{p[0]} {p[2] - (k0 + a * p[1] ** 1.5):+.4f}" for p in pts))
print("\nnear-tie re-routing (all chosen experts read, ranks 0-1 pinned):")
for C in Cs:
    for d in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5):
        out = []
        for kind, sets in (("hard", HARD), ("easy", EASY)):
            r, m, e = replay(C, sets, delta=d)
            k0, a = law[kind]
            out.append(f"{kind} reads {r:6.1f} swapped mass {m:.4f} E {e:.5f} -> KLD {k0 + a * e ** 1.5:.4f}")
        print(f"  C={C:3d} delta {d:4.2f}: " + " | ".join(out), flush=True)


### scripts/rg_check.py
"""Repetition-guard check for the Eval suite's B12 verdict (10-22): re-run rows that hit the token cap with the Eval suite's
own B12 + MTP setup (their 08:22 note: load_b12(frac, mtp=True) + the CLI's MTP env, B12_NORM=mass, per row
reset_generation_state, b12_prefill_state(ids, 512, 4096), spec125.generate(k_mtp 3, max_draft 3, lookup 0, prefilled=state,
stop_ids EOS)) now that load_b12 registers BigMoE's guard for spec125. The guard is exact while disarmed, so each new output
must equal the saved one token for token up to the guard's arming; past it the loop should break.
usage: rg_check.py VARIANT_DIR KEY [KEY ...]   (rows read from ~/EvalSuite/results/<VARIANT_DIR>/decode.jsonl, prompts
from ~/EvalSuite/data/items/dec_<set>.jsonl; read-only)"""
import json, os, sys, time

for _k, _v in (("BIGMOE_MTP_STREAM", "1"), ("BIGMOE_MTP_C", "64"), ("BIGMOE_MAXK", "80"), ("BIGMOE_FRSPEC", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1"), ("BIGMOE_RESET_GEN", "0"), ("BIGMOE_TIER_PF_GROUP", "64"),
               ("B12_NORM", "mass")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import bt_b12 as B
VDIR, KEYS = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2:]
rt, st = B.load_b12(float(os.environ.get("RG_FRAC", "0.10")), mtp=True)
model = rt.model
sys.path.insert(1, os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode"))
import spec125
from bigmoe_v3f.fuse import reset_generation_state
from bigmoe_v3f import repguard as RG
EOS = {248044, 248046}
E = os.path.expanduser("~/EvalSuite")
saved = {r["key"]: r for r in map(json.loads, open(os.path.join(E, "results", VDIR, "decode.jsonl")))}
items = {}
for s in ("ifeval", "math", "humaneval", "multi", "mgsm", "agent", "ruler"):
    p = os.path.join(E, "data", "items", f"dec_{s}.jsonl")
    if os.path.exists(p):
        for it in map(json.loads, open(p)):
            items[it["key"]] = it
print(f"guard importable for spec125: {'bigmoe.repguard' in sys.modules}; LOOP_GUARD={os.environ.get('BIGMOE_LOOP_GUARD', '1')}",
      flush=True)
for key in KEYS:
    if key == "synthetic":                                           # a deliberate loop: does the guard arm and steer?
        msg = os.environ.get("RG_PROMPT", "Write the sentence 'The sea is blue and the sea is wide.' over and over, "
                             "at least 100 times, with nothing else.")
        tid = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": msg}], add_generation_prompt=True,
                                               enable_thinking=False)
        it, old = {"prompt_ids": list(tid if isinstance(tid[0], int) else tid["input_ids"]), "max_tokens": 1024}, None
    else:
        it, old = items[key], saved.get(key)
    ids, cap = it["prompt_ids"], it["max_tokens"]
    reset_generation_state(model); B.STASH.clear(); B.PENDH.clear()
    for k_ in RG.STATS:
        RG.STATS[k_] = 0
    out, s = [], {}
    t0 = time.time()
    if os.environ.get("RG_EAGER") == "1":                             # B12's eager loop (b12_generate) instead of spec125
        out = B.b12_generate(model, ids, cap, stop_ids=EOS)
    else:
        state = B.b12_prefill_state(model, ids, 512, 4096)
        spec125.generate(model, ids, cap, k_mtp=3, max_draft=3, lookup=0, prefilled=state, stop_ids=EOS, stats=s,
                         on_token=lambda t: out.append(int(t)))
    st.gate.check()
    cut = next((j + 1 for j, x in enumerate(out) if x in EOS), None)
    out = out[:cut] if cut else out
    finish = "eos" if cut else "length"
    same = 0
    if old is not None:
        oc = old["completion_ids"]
        while same < min(len(out), len(oc)) and out[same] == oc[same]:
            same += 1
    text = rt.tokenizer.decode([x for x in out if x not in EOS])
    d_ = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/rg"); os.makedirs(d_, exist_ok=True)   # for guard-off vs guard-on prefixes
    json.dump(dict(key=key, tokens=out, finish=finish, guard=dict(RG.STATS), loop_guard=os.environ.get("BIGMOE_LOOP_GUARD", "1")),
              open(os.path.join(d_, f"{os.environ.get('RG_TAG', 'run')}_{key.replace('/', '_')}.json"), "w"))
    print(f"{key}: {len(out)} tokens {finish} in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s (saved: {len(old['completion_ids']) if old else '-'} "
          f"{old.get('finish') if old else '-'}); identical prefix {same} tokens; guard armed {RG.STATS['armed']}, "
          f"penalized steps {RG.STATS['penalized']} | tail {text[-140:]!r}", flush=True)


### scripts/rg_ngram.py
"""Where did the repetition guard arm, and on what text? (CPU only.) For a guard-off / guard-on pair saved by rg_check.py
(data/rg/<TAG>_<key>.json), print the first divergence, and the earliest position >= 256 at which some 12-gram of the
guard-off tokens has occurred 4 times within the last 2,048 tokens (BigMoE's guard arming rule, as its repguard.py
docstring states; MTPLX's LoopGuard may differ in details), with that 12-gram decoded.
usage: rg_ngram.py KEY_FILE_PART OFF_TAG ON_TAG   e.g. rg_ngram.py math500_test_prealgebra_1640.json off_a on_a"""
import json, os, sys
from collections import defaultdict

part, off_tag, on_tag = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
d = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/rg")
a = json.load(open(os.path.join(d, f"{off_tag}_{part}")))
b = json.load(open(os.path.join(d, f"{on_tag}_{part}")))
ta, tb = a["tokens"], b["tokens"]
div = next((i for i in range(min(len(ta), len(tb))) if ta[i] != tb[i]), min(len(ta), len(tb)))
print(f"{part}: off {len(ta)} tokens ({a['finish']}, guard {a['guard']}), on {len(tb)} tokens ({b['finish']}, guard "
      f"{b['guard']}); identical prefix {div}")
N, K, WIN, MIN = 12, 4, 2048, 256
pos = defaultdict(list)
arm, gram = None, None
for i in range(N, len(ta) + 1):
    g = tuple(ta[i - N:i])
    pos[g] = [p for p in pos[g] if p > i - WIN] + [i]
    if i >= MIN and len(pos[g]) >= K and arm is None:
        arm, gram = i, g
        break
if arm is None:
    print("no 12-gram reached 4 occurrences in the guard-off output")
    sys.exit(0)
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare"))
print(f"rule met at token {arm} (divergence at {div}): 12-gram {tok.decode(list(gram))!r} at positions "
      f"{[p - N for p in pos[gram]]}")
print(f"guard-off text around the divergence: {tok.decode(ta[max(0, div - 40):div + 20])!r}")
print(f"guard-on  text around the divergence: {tok.decode(tb[max(0, div - 40):div + 20])!r}")


### scripts/sere_sim.py
"""Predicted error energy of missing-expert estimators under frugal routing (Breakthrough D1b, 2026-10-21 night).
125B traces with router probabilities (data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz); frugal wait-top-2 with an LRU cache of C
experts per layer (only waited experts are loaded); for every missing rank r >= 2 (expert u, top-10 weight w):
  drop      : error energy w^2 N_u^2                                  (N_u = ||Y_u|| on the calibration rows)
  sere-all  : w^2 min(N_u^2, min_v D[u,v]^2) over ALL resident v not used by the token (best case of SERE)
  sere-r20  : the same over resident v among the token's router top-20 (what a routing row can carry)
  shared    : w^2 (r_sh N_u)^2 with the optimally scaled shared-expert output (P<l>.npy row 1)
  meanw     : the same with a resident mean-weights expert (row 3); meanout: the mean of all outputs (row 5, a bound)
Prints the error energy relative to 'drop' per estimator (pairwise distances are averaged over calibration tokens, so
this is a per-expert-pair prediction, not a per-token one). usage: sere_sim.py [C ...]"""
import glob, os, sys
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict

NL = 48
T = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/simtab")
sets = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
        for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz")))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in sets.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in sets.items()}
w = {k: g[:, :, :10] / g[:, :, :10].sum(-1, keepdims=True) for k, g in gp.items()}
layers = [l for l in range(NL) if os.path.exists(f"{T}/D{l}.npy") and os.path.exists(f"{T}/P{l}.npy")]
Dt = {l: np.load(f"{T}/D{l}.npy").astype(np.float32) for l in layers}
Nt = {l: np.load(f"{T}/N{l}.npy").astype(np.float32) for l in layers}
Pt = {l: np.load(f"{T}/P{l}.npy").astype(np.float32) for l in layers}
print(f"tables for {len(layers)} layers")


def run(C):
    E = {k: 0.0 for k in ("drop", "sere-all", "sere-r20", "shared", "meanw", "meanout")}
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt = ids[k], w[k]
        for l in layers:
            D, N, P = Dt[l], Nt[l], Pt[l]
            c = OrderedDict()
            for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
                row = [int(e) for e in tr[t, l]]
                used = set(row[:2])
                for e in row[:2]:
                    if e not in c: c[e] = 1
                res_now = [e for e in c if e not in used]
                for r in range(2, 10):
                    u, ww = row[r], float(wt[t, l, r])
                    if u in c:
                        used.add(u); continue
                    n2 = float(N[u]) ** 2 * ww * ww
                    E["drop"] += n2
                    cand = [v for v in res_now if v not in used]
                    best = min([float(D[u, v]) ** 2 for v in cand], default=np.inf)
                    E["sere-all"] += min(n2, best * ww * ww)
                    c20 = [v for v in row[10:20] if v in c and v not in used]
                    b20 = min([float(D[u, v]) ** 2 for v in c20], default=np.inf)
                    E["sere-r20"] += min(n2, b20 * ww * ww)
                    E["shared"] += n2 * float(P[1, u]) ** 2
                    E["meanw"] += n2 * float(P[3, u]) ** 2
                    E["meanout"] += n2 * float(P[5, u]) ** 2
                for e in row[:10]:
                    if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                while len(c) > C:
                    c.popitem(last=False)
    return {k: v / E["drop"] for k, v in E.items()}


for C in [int(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or [51, 26]:
    r = run(C)
    print(f"C={C}: error energy relative to dropping: " + "  ".join(f"{k} {v:.3f}" for k, v in r.items()), flush=True)


### scripts/sim_table.py
"""Expert output-similarity tables for the 125B (Breakthrough D1b, SERE-style substitution; 2026-10-20 22:20).
  sim_table.py capture   : exact prefill (fork125bt streamed runtime, C=128) of calibration sequences = the Eval suite's
                           own-output tf sets NOT used by tf_frugal_var.py (it scores every 4th ifeval/multi sequence;
                           here: the others, plus code and gsm), hook every routed MoE block, keep NTOK rows per layer
                           (uniform over all prefilled rows) -> data/simtab/X.npz (L<l>: [NTOK, 2560] f16)
  sim_table.py build     : per layer, read all 512 experts (9 tensors) straight from the safetensors (no model load),
                           Y_e = down(silu(gate X) * up X) for every expert on X, then per pair ||Y_u - Y_v||_F and ||Y_u||
                           -> data/simtab/D<l>.npy (512 x 512 f16 distances), N<l>.npy (512 norms). 68 GB read once.
Use: a missing expert u is replaced by the resident v minimising D[u, v] if D[u, v] < N[u] (better than skipping),
at u's own gate weight (SERE keeps router weights)."""
import json, os, sys, time
import numpy as np
os.environ["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
OUT = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/simtab"); os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
NTOK = int(os.environ.get("BT_NTOK", "512"))
MODE = sys.argv[1]

if MODE == "capture":
    os.environ["EVALSUITE_FRUGAL"] = "1"; os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = "0"; os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
    for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
        os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
    os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bt"))
    import mlx.core as mx
    from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
    from bigmoe import decode as D
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/EvalSuite"))
    from evalsuite import stages as SG
    rt, st = load_streamed(MD, 128, mtp=False)
    from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp
    orig = qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
    rows = {}

    def hook(self, x):
        bm = getattr(self.switch_mlp, "_bm", None)
        if bm is not None and x.shape[1] > 1:
            rows.setdefault(bm["L"], []).append(np.array(x[0].astype(mx.float16)))
        return orig(self, x)
    qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = hook
    items = SG.tf_items()
    pick = []
    for s in ("tf_ifeval", "tf_multi"):
        idx = [i for i, it in enumerate(items) if it["set"] == s]
        held = set(idx[::4])                                   # tf_frugal_var.py's subset: excluded
        pick += [i for i in idx if i not in held][:8]
    for s in ("tf_code", "tf_gsm"):
        pick += [i for i, it in enumerate(items) if it["set"] == s][:4]
    t0 = time.time()
    for i in pick:                                             # silent night: ~24 short prefills (SSD-bound, light GPU)
        ids = [int(t) for t in items[i]["ids"]][:512]
        D.greedy(rt.model, ids, 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    X = {}
    for L, parts in rows.items():
        a = np.concatenate(parts)
        X[f"L{L}"] = a[np.sort(rng.choice(len(a), min(NTOK, len(a)), replace=False))]
    np.savez(os.path.join(OUT, "X.npz"), **X)
    print(f"capture: {len(pick)} sequences, {len(X)} layers x {NTOK} rows in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)

elif MODE == "build":
    import mlx.core as mx
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bt"))
    from bigmoe_wt.stream import read_headers, DT
    H = read_headers(MD)
    X = np.load(os.path.join(OUT, "X.npz"))
    layers = sorted(int(k[1:]) for k in X.files)
    pre = "language_model.model.layers.{}.mlp.switch_mlp.{}.{}"

    def load(L, p, f):
        path, off, dt, shape = H[pre.format(L, p, f)]
        npdt, mxdt = DT[dt]
        n = int(np.prod(shape))
        a = np.fromfile(path, dtype=npdt, count=n, offset=off).reshape(shape)
        return mx.array(a).view(mxdt) if npdt is np.uint16 and mxdt != mx.uint16 else mx.array(a)
    for L in layers:
        if os.path.exists(os.path.join(OUT, f"D{L}.npy")):
            continue
        t0 = time.time()
        W = {(p, f): load(L, p, f) for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj") for f in ("weight", "scales", "biases")}
        x = mx.array(X[f"L{L}"]).astype(mx.bfloat16)                     # [N, 2560]
        E = W[("gate_proj", "weight")].shape[0]
        Ys = []
        for e in range(E):
            q = lambda p, h: mx.quantized_matmul(h, W[(p, "weight")][e], W[(p, "scales")][e], W[(p, "biases")][e],
                                                 transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4)
            g, u = q("gate_proj", x), q("up_proj", x)
            Ys.append(q("down_proj", (g * mx.sigmoid(g)) * u).astype(mx.float32).reshape(-1))
            if e % 64 == 63:
                mx.eval(Ys)
        Y = mx.stack(Ys)                                                   # [E, N*2560]
        G = Y @ Y.T
        n2 = mx.diag(G)
        D2 = mx.maximum(n2[:, None] + n2[None, :] - 2 * G, 0)
        mx.eval(D2, n2)
        np.save(os.path.join(OUT, f"D{L}.npy"), np.sqrt(np.array(D2)).astype(np.float16))
        np.save(os.path.join(OUT, f"N{L}.npy"), np.sqrt(np.array(n2)).astype(np.float32))
        # zero-byte proxies (SPICE-style shared-expert base; a resident mean-weights expert; the mean of all outputs as
        # the best any single common proxy could do): per expert, optimal scale alpha = <Y_u, P> / ||P||^2 and the
        # relative residual ||Y_u - alpha P|| / ||Y_u|| (1.0 = no better than dropping the expert)
        def proxy(P):
            P = P.reshape(-1); s2 = (P * P).sum(); d = Y @ P
            return np.array(d / s2), np.sqrt(np.maximum(np.array(n2 - d * d / s2), 0) / np.array(n2))
        sh = "language_model.model.layers.{}.mlp.shared_expert.{}.{}"
        def loads(p, f):
            path, off, dt, shape = H[sh.format(L, p, f)]
            npdt, mxdt = DT[dt]
            a = np.fromfile(path, dtype=npdt, count=int(np.prod(shape)), offset=off).reshape(shape)
            return mx.array(a).view(mxdt) if npdt is np.uint16 and mxdt != mx.uint16 else mx.array(a)
        SW = {(p, f): loads(p, f) for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj") for f in ("weight", "scales", "biases")}
        qs = lambda p, h: mx.quantized_matmul(h, SW[(p, "weight")], SW[(p, "scales")], SW[(p, "biases")], transpose=True,
                                              group_size=64, bits=4)
        g, u = qs("gate_proj", x), qs("up_proj", x)
        a_sh, r_sh = proxy(qs("down_proj", (g * mx.sigmoid(g)) * u).astype(mx.float32))
        Wm = {}
        for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
            acc = None
            for e0 in range(0, E, 64):
                dq = mx.dequantize(W[(p, "weight")][e0:e0 + 64], W[(p, "scales")][e0:e0 + 64], W[(p, "biases")][e0:e0 + 64],
                                   group_size=64, bits=4).astype(mx.float32).sum(axis=0)
                acc = dq if acc is None else acc + dq
            Wm[p] = (acc / E).astype(mx.bfloat16)
        gm, um = x @ Wm["gate_proj"].T, x @ Wm["up_proj"].T
        a_mw, r_mw = proxy((((gm * mx.sigmoid(gm)) * um) @ Wm["down_proj"].T).astype(mx.float32))
        a_mo, r_mo = proxy(Y.mean(axis=0))
        np.save(os.path.join(OUT, f"P{L}.npy"), np.stack([a_sh, r_sh, a_mw, r_mw, a_mo, r_mo]).astype(np.float32))
        print(f"L{L}: residual / norm with the best scaled proxy, median over experts: shared expert {np.median(r_sh):.3f}, "
              f"mean-weights expert {np.median(r_mw):.3f}, mean of all outputs {np.median(r_mo):.3f}", flush=True)
        Dn = np.sqrt(np.array(D2)); np.fill_diagonal(Dn, np.inf); nn = Dn.min(1) / np.sqrt(np.array(n2))
        print(f"L{L}: {time.time() - t0:.1f}s; nearest-other distance / own norm: median {np.median(nn):.3f}, "
              f"P10 {np.percentile(nn, 10):.3f}, P90 {np.percentile(nn, 90):.3f}", flush=True)
        del W, Y, G, D2, Ys
        mx.clear_cache()


### scripts/test_repguard_v3f.py
"""CPU unit test of bigmoe_v3f.repguard (BigMoE ea6e139 copy) R1 (LaTeX math spans masked) vs the shipped rule R0: a long answer whose formula
lines repeat must not arm R1 (R0 arms), and a plain repeated sentence must arm both.
Usage: ~/BigMoE/.venv/bin/python scripts/test_repguard.py   (CPU, ~10 s)"""
import os, sys
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/v3f"))
from bigmoe_v3f import repguard

tok = repguard._tokenizer()
intro = "We work through each case carefully and check every step of the computation along the way. " + " ".join(f"Note {i} on the setup of this problem." for i in range(40)) + "\n\n"
words = ["first", "second", "third", "fourth", "fifth", "sixth", "seventh", "eighth", "ninth", "tenth"]
math = intro + "".join(f"Check {words[i % 10]} {i}:\n$$\\text{{Area}} = \\frac{{1}}{{2}} \\times 32 \\times (\\sqrt{{2}} \\times \\sqrt{{2}}) = 32$$\n"
                       for i in range(40))
loop = intro + "I love my sneakers because they are comfortable and they look great on me every single day. " * 40
ok = True
for name, text, want in (("math", math, {"R0": True, "R1": False}), ("loop", loop, {"R0": True, "R1": True})):
    ids = tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)
    for mode in ("R0", "R1"):
        os.environ["BIGMOE_LOOP_GUARD_MATH"] = "1" if mode == "R1" else "0"
        g = repguard.make(tok)
        armed = False
        for c in range(1, len(ids) + 1):
            g.observe(ids[:c]); armed |= g.armed
        res = "armed" if armed else "never armed"
        good = armed == want[mode]; ok &= good
        print(f"{name:5s} {mode}: {res} ({len(ids)} tokens) {'ok' if good else 'UNEXPECTED'}")
print("PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)


### scripts/tf_b12.py
"""Teacher-forced step KLD of B12-family configs on the REAL v3 tier path (v3f: records3, plan 4433333333, B12's own
frugal / late / normalisation code), scored by the Eval suite's own one-token-per-call scorer (evalsuite.stages.step_forward
+ engine.var_head against its cached exact 4-bit reference; read-only import), as tf_frugal_var.py does on fork125bt
(exact 4-bit experts: its numbers do not include the 3-bit tier loss; these do).
Arms are bt_b12's env switches (set before the call), e.g.
  B12 as judged (C_m 51):                   (none)            / B12_NORM=mass
  v3 control (exact top-10 on the tiers):   B12_W=10
  dh_w4 (NewIdeas tf_defer125 'dh'):        B12_W=4 B12_LATE3=4,10   (ranks 0-3 waited, cold ranks 4-9 half a layer late)
usage: tf_b12.py NAME FRAC SETS EVERY   e.g.  tf_b12.py b12_mass 0.10 tf_ifeval,tf_multi 8
  -> a line in data/tfb12/results.txt (per-part KLD, top-1, vs the 1.10 x ar4 targets) + data/tfb12/NAME_e{EVERY}.npz"""
import os, sys, time

os.environ["EVALSUITE_FRUGAL"] = "1"                 # the suite's frugal-runtime env: no PF1 staging flags
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
NAME, frac, SETS, EVERY = sys.argv[1], float(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3].split(","), int(sys.argv[4])
from bt_b12 import load_b12, CFG
rt, st = load_b12(frac)                               # bigmoe_v3f is imported before evalsuite puts ~/BigMoE on sys.path
import numpy as np
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/EvalSuite"))
from evalsuite import stages as SG, engine as EN

TARGET = {"tf_code": 0.00253, "tf_gsm": 0.00449, "tf_ifeval": 0.0204, "tf_multi": 0.0206}   # 1.10 x ar4 (MoonShots)
items = SG.tf_items()
head = EN.var_head(SG.load_ref_tf(items), SG._targets(items))
seqs = [i for i, it in enumerate(items) if it["set"] in SETS][::EVERY]
out = {s: [] for s in SETS}
t0 = time.time()
for i in seqs:
    it = items[i]
    r = SG.step_forward(rt, it["ids"], it["pos"], lambda pos, lg, i=i: head(i, pos, lg))
    st.gate.check()                                   # a timed-out spin gate invalidates the pass
    out[it["set"]].append(r)
cat = {s: np.concatenate([r["kld"] for r in v]) for s, v in out.items() if v}
agr = {s: np.concatenate([r["agree"] for r in v]) for s, v in out.items() if v}
allk = np.concatenate(list(cat.values()))
cfg = (f"C_m={CFG['cm']} C_l={CFG['cl']} hc{CFG['hc']} w={CFG['w']} late3={CFG['l3']} norm={CFG.get('norm', 'used')} "
       f"plan={os.environ.get('BIGMOE_TIER')}")
line = (f"{NAME} [{cfg}]: KLD {allk.mean():.4f} over {len(allk)} positions ({len(seqs)} seqs, every {EVERY}) | "
        + " ".join(f"{s} {cat[s].mean():.5f} (top-1 {agr[s].mean():.3f}"
                   + (f", {'PASS' if cat[s].mean() <= TARGET[s] else 'FAIL'} vs {TARGET[s]}" if s in TARGET else "") + ")"
                   for s in cat)
        + f" | {time.time() - t0:.0f}s")
print(line, flush=True)
d = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/tfb12")
os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
with open(os.path.join(d, "results.txt"), "a") as f:
    f.write(line + "\n")
np.savez(os.path.join(d, f"{NAME}_e{EVERY}.npz"), **{f"kld_{s}": cat[s] for s in cat}, **{f"agree_{s}": agr[s] for s in agr})


### scripts/tf_frugal_var.py
"""Where does frugal routing's KLD come from? (Breakthrough, 2026-10-20 evening.) The Eval suite measured teacher-forced
step KLD 0.057 for frugal at 10 % (ref step noise 0.0018; accepted ar4 0.0096), 0.115 on ifeval and 0.092 on multilingual.
This runs the Eval suite's own one-token-per-call scorer (evalsuite.stages.step_forward + engine.var_head against its
cached reference, read-only import) on a subset of its own-output sequences, for frugal variants on fork125bt:
  VAR  nw  kc  renorm   meaning
  base  2  20  yes      today's frugal (bt_frugal.py): missing ranks 2-9 replaced by resident ranks 10-19, renormalised
  nosub 2  10  yes      missing ranks 2-9 dropped, renormalised over the used
  noren 2  10  no       dropped, NOT renormalised (weights stay p_r / sum of the top-10)
  subnr 2  20  no       substitutes at their own router weight, no renormalisation
  w4    4  20  yes      wait for ranks 0-3
  sere  2  80  no       SERE-style similar-resident substitute at the missing expert's weight (serer: renormalised)
  mNN   *  20  yes      wait for the ranks covering NN % of the top-10 gate mass (at least rank 0)
  exact 10 10  yes      every top-10 waited (harness check: should sit near the step noise)
usage: tf_frugal_var.py VAR FRAC SETS EVERY   e.g. base 0.10 tf_ifeval,tf_multi 3   -> data/tfvar/<VAR>_<FRAC>.txt line
Env as the suite's frugal runs (EVALSUITE_FRUGAL=1: plain exact streamer flags, no speed fusions)."""
import json, os, sys, time
os.environ["EVALSUITE_FRUGAL"] = "1"
os.environ["BIGMOE_SPARE"] = "0"
os.environ["BIGMOE_PF_NOPUBLISH"] = "1"
for k in ("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM",):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "off")
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/fork125bt"))
sys.path.insert(1, os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/scripts"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed            # fork125bt (imported before evalsuite puts ~/BigMoE on sys.path)
from fused_route import topk_probs

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/EvalSuite"))
from evalsuite import stages as SG, engine as EN

VAR, frac, SETS, EVERY = sys.argv[1], float(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3].split(","), int(sys.argv[4])
CFG = {"base": (2, 20, True), "nosub": (2, 10, True), "noren": (2, 10, False), "subnr": (2, 20, False),
       "w4": (4, 20, True), "w3": (3, 20, True), "exact": (10, 10, True)}
# VAR = head[_mod...]: head in CFG | mNN (wait for the ranks covering NN % of the top-10 mass) | bNN (base selection,
# partial renormalisation beta = NN/100: w = p / ((1 - beta) * P10 + beta * P_used)) | sere (SERE-style: missing rank ->
# most output-similar resident expert among the router's top-80, data/simtab D/N tables, only if D[u, v] < N[u], at u's
# own gate weight) | serer (= sere_r). Mods: nr (no renorm), r (renorm), mNN (wait by mass, for sere).
parts = VAR.split("_")
head = "sere" if parts[0] == "serer" else parts[0]
if parts[0] == "serer":
    parts.append("r")
NW, KC, RENORM, THETA, BETA, SERE = 2, 20, True, None, None, False
BILD_TAU = None
DENSE = []                # dense-diet modifiers (hc8 / hc4 / lm3), applied after load
ADA = None                # adaptive theta (head 'ada'); LAST holds the previous step's next-token entropy
LAST = {"ent": 99.0}
MODE = {"exact": False}   # set while a BiLD re-run recomputes a token with every top-10 expert
TAILBITS = None    # tK: waited rank >= 2 experts that were not resident are computed from their MSB-K planes
BAND = None        # (a, b): apply the variant only in layers a..b-1; every other layer waits for its whole top-10 (exact)
PRE = None         # pre2 (TrainLab's pre-routed frugal, 2026-10-21): in layers [a, b) (BT_PRE_BAND, default 8,40) the two
PREPICK = {}       # waited experts are layer L's router (+ MoEPrefetch's correction) on layer L-1's MoE input, top-2; the
                   # rest = the true router's best outside them, resident only (frugal substitution); weights = the TRUE
                   # router's probabilities over the chosen 10, renormalised; other layers plain frugal
LATE = None        # lAtB: ranks A..B-1 are read; those cold before the call add their output half a layer late (written
STASH = {}         # into the HC streams after layer L+1's attention, NewIdeas' dh semantics); l1t2 = frugal waiting for
                   # rank 0 only (a cold rank 1 lands late); l2t4 at 5 % = frugal + cold ranks 2-3 late, not dropped
if head in CFG:
    NW, KC, RENORM = CFG[head]
elif head.startswith("m"):
    NW, THETA = 0, int(head[1:]) / 100
elif head.startswith("b") and head[1:].isdigit():
    BETA, RENORM = int(head[1:]) / 100, False
elif head == "sere":
    SERE, NW, KC, RENORM = True, 2, 80, False
    SIM = {}
elif head == "ada":                # wait by mass with theta = lo if the previous step's entropy <= tau else hi
    NW, KC, RENORM, THETA = 0, 20, False, 0.5
    ADA = {"lo": 0.5, "hi": 0.9, "tau": 0.1}
elif head == "bild":               # BiLD-style fallback: frugal (drop, no renorm) step; if its next-token entropy > tau,
    NW, KC, RENORM = 2, 10, False  # roll the token back and recompute it with the whole top-10 waited (mod eNN: tau = NN/100)
    BILD_TAU = 0.10
else:
    raise SystemExit(f"unknown variant {VAR}")
for p in parts[1:]:
    if p == "nr": RENORM = False
    elif p == "r": RENORM = True
    elif p.startswith("m"): NW, THETA = 0, int(p[1:]) / 100
    elif p.startswith("t"): TAILBITS = int(p[1:])
    elif p.startswith("L") and "T" in p: BAND = tuple(int(v) for v in p[1:].split("T"))   # LaTb: variant only in layers a..b-1
    elif p.startswith("e") and BILD_TAU is not None: BILD_TAU = int(p[1:]) / 100
    elif ADA is not None and p.startswith("lo"): ADA["lo"] = int(p[2:]) / 100
    elif ADA is not None and p.startswith("hi"): ADA["hi"] = int(p[2:]) / 100
    elif ADA is not None and p.startswith("e"): ADA["tau"] = int(p[1:]) / 100
    elif p.startswith("S"): SETS = [{"g": "tf_gsm", "c": "tf_code", "i": "tf_ifeval", "m": "tf_multi"}[ch] for ch in p[1:]]
    elif p in ("hc8", "hc4", "lm3"): DENSE.append(p)   # dense diet (TASKS.md): HC mixers 8 / 4-bit, lm_head 3-bit
    elif p.startswith("l") and "t" in p and p[1:].replace("t", "").isdigit(): LATE = tuple(int(v) for v in p[1:].split("t"))
    elif p == "pre2": PRE = tuple(int(v) for v in os.environ.get("BT_PRE_BAND", "8,40").split(","))
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(MD, "config.json"))); cfg = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
E, K, NLAY = cfg["num_experts"], cfg["num_experts_per_tok"], cfg["num_hidden_layers"]
C = max(16, round(frac * E))
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, C, mtp=False)
MEM0 = mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9
if DENSE:
    import mlx.nn as nn
    from mlx.utils import tree_flatten
    if "hc8" in DENSE or "hc4" in DENSE:   # MoonShots' patches/hc8 (read-only import): GatedResidual mixers + PLE K/V
        sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/patches/hc8"))
        import hc8 as _hc8
        print("dense: hc", _hc8.install(rt.model, bits=8 if "hc8" in DENSE else 4), flush=True)
    if "lm3" in DENSE:                     # lm_head 4-bit -> 3-bit (affine, group 64) from its dequantised weights
        TM = getattr(rt.model, "language_model", rt.model)
        for name, mod in TM.named_modules():
            if name.endswith("lm_head") and isinstance(mod, nn.QuantizedLinear):
                W = mx.dequantize(mod.weight, mod.scales, mod.biases, mod.group_size, mod.bits)
                lin = nn.Linear(W.shape[1], W.shape[0], bias=False); lin.weight = W
                q = nn.QuantizedLinear.from_linear(lin, 64, 3)
                parent = TM
                for part in name.split(".")[:-1]:
                    parent = getattr(parent, part)
                before = sum(a.nbytes for _, a in tree_flatten(mod.parameters())) / 1e6
                setattr(parent, name.split(".")[-1], q)
                print(f"dense: {name} 4-bit -> 3-bit, {before:.0f} -> "
                      f"{sum(a.nbytes for _, a in tree_flatten(q.parameters())) / 1e6:.0f} MB", flush=True)
        mx.eval(TM.parameters())
    mx.clear_cache()
if PRE is not None:   # layer L+1's routers and MoEPrefetch's d=1 correction (indexed by the source layer)
    _TMP = rt.model.language_model.model if hasattr(rt.model, "language_model") else rt.model.model
    ROUT = [l.mlp.gate for l in _TMP.layers]
    _z = np.load(os.path.expanduser(os.environ.get("BIGMOE_PF_CORR", "~/MoEPrefetch/data/corr125_r128.npz")))
    CORR = [(mx.array(_z["A"][i].astype(np.float32)), mx.array(_z["B"][i].astype(np.float32)), mx.array(_z["mu"][i]),
             mx.array(_z["delta"][i])) for i in range(_z["A"].shape[0])]
print(f"memory after load: active {MEM0:.2f} GB, after dense changes {mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9:.2f} GB "
      f"(expert pools {st.resident_gb():.2f} GB)", flush=True)
from mtplx.models import qwen4_exp
from mtplx.attention_math import attention_gate
orig_call = qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__
STAT = {"used": 0.0, "mass": 0.0, "n": 0, "w": 0.0, "wn": 0, "calls": 0, "full": 0, "low": 0}
def _make_mask(n):
    """MSB-n of every 4-bit code in a packed uint32 word: (codes with the low 4-n bits cleared, per-word sum of them)."""
    low = (1 << (4 - n)) - 1
    keep = mx.array(int(("%X" % (0xF & ~low)) * 8, 16), dtype=mx.uint32)

    @mx.compile
    def f(w):
        lsum = None
        for j in range(8):
            part = (w >> (4 * j)) & low
            lsum = part if lsum is None else lsum + part
        return w & keep, lsum
    return f


_MASK = {n: _make_mask(n) for n in (1, 2, 3)}


class P:   # bias-corrected MSB-n tier of a 4-bit slot pool (planes125's math, here for n = 1..3)
    @staticmethod
    def _tier_proj(lin, h, sl, idx, n):
        w, s, b = lin.weight[sl], lin.scales[sl], lin.biases[sl]
        gs = lin.group_size
        wm, lsum = _MASK[n](w)
        off = lsum.reshape(*s.shape, gs // 8).sum(-1).astype(mx.float32) / gs
        bias = (b.astype(mx.float32) + s.astype(mx.float32) * off).astype(s.dtype)
        return mx.gather_qmm(h, wm, s, bias, rhs_indices=idx, transpose=True, group_size=gs, bits=4)


SYNCGATE = os.environ.get("BT_SYNCGATE", "1") == "1"   # residency on the CPU, no GPU spin gate: survives the silent-
                                                       # night thermostat's SIGSTOP (a stopped loader times out spin gates)


def sync_slots(L, idl, nw, nk):
    """frugal residency without the GPU gate: (slots before, slots after) for the ids idl (-1 = absent). Loads the first nw
    ids (blocking), LRU-touches the resident ones among the first nk, never reads the others (BIGMOE_SPARE=0 semantics)."""
    pre = np.array(ext.lookup(L, idl))
    STAT["ld"] = STAT.get("ld", 0) + int((pre[:nw] < 0).sum()); STAT["c1"] = STAT.get("c1", 0) + 1   # measured reads
    need = idl[:nw] + [e for r, e in enumerate(idl[:nk]) if r >= nw and pre[r] >= 0]
    ext.ensure(L, mx.array(np.array(need, np.int32)))
    return pre, np.array(ext.lookup(L, idl))


def route(bm, ids, nw, nk):
    if not SYNCGATE:
        return None, bm["gate"].route_and_gate(bm["L"], ids[0], nw=nw, nk=nk).reshape(1, KC).astype(mx.int32)
    pre, after = sync_slots(bm["L"], [int(v) for v in np.array(ids[0])], nw, nk)
    return pre, mx.array(after.astype(np.int32)).reshape(1, KC)


def block(self, x):
    sw = self.switch_mlp
    bm = getattr(sw, "_bm", None)
    if bm is None or x.shape[1] != 1 or bm["full"]:
        return orig_call(self, x)
    if bm["L"] == bm["first"]:
        bm["gate"].new_pass()
    if PRE is not None and not MODE["exact"]:
        L = bm["L"]
        if PRE[0] <= L + 1 < PRE[1] and L < len(CORR):              # pre-pick layer L+1's two waited experts now
            x1 = x.reshape(1, -1)
            A, Bm, mu, dl = CORR[L]
            lg = ROUT[L + 1](x1).astype(mx.float32) + ((x1.astype(mx.float32) - mu) @ A) @ Bm + dl
            PREPICK[L + 1] = [int(v) for v in np.array(mx.argpartition(-lg, kth=1, axis=-1)[0, :2])]
        if L in PREPICK:
            return pre_block(self, sw, bm, x, PREPICK.pop(L))
    ids, pk = topk_probs(self.gate(x)[0], KC)                       # [1, KC]
    nw = NW
    if MODE["exact"] or (BAND is not None and not (BAND[0] <= bm["L"] < BAND[1])):   # exact: all top-10 waited
        _, slots = route(bm, ids, K, K)
        res = slots >= 0
        sel = res & (mx.cumsum(res.astype(mx.int32), axis=-1) <= K)
        w = pk * sel; w = w / mx.maximum(w.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True), 1e-9)
        safe = mx.where(res, slots, slots[:, :1]).astype(mx.uint32)
        y = (type(sw).__mro__[1].__call__(sw, x, safe[None]) * w[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
        return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))
    if THETA is not None:                                          # CPU sync per call: a quality probe, not a speed path
        th = THETA if ADA is None else (ADA["lo"] if LAST["ent"] <= ADA["tau"] else ADA["hi"])
        p = np.array(pk[0, :K].astype(mx.float32)); c = np.cumsum(p) / p.sum()
        nw = max(1, int(np.searchsorted(c, th - 1e-9)) + 1)
        STAT["w"] += nw; STAT["wn"] += 1
    if LATE is not None:                                           # late ranks are read too (only their timing differs)
        nw = max(nw, LATE[1])
    pre0 = None
    if TAILBITS and not SYNCGATE:                                  # residency before the call (CPU, no loading)
        pre0 = np.array(ext.lookup(bm["L"], [int(v) for v in np.array(ids[0, :K])]))
    pre_s, slots = route(bm, ids, nw, K)
    pre = pre0 if pre0 is not None else (pre_s[:K] if pre_s is not None else None)
    if SERE:
        return sere_block(self, sw, bm, x, ids, pk, slots)
    res = slots >= 0
    sel = res & (mx.cumsum(res.astype(mx.int32), axis=-1) <= K)      # first K resident in rank order
    w = pk * sel
    p10 = pk[:, :K].sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True) if self.norm_topk_prob else 1.0
    if BETA is not None:
        w = w / ((1 - BETA) * p10 + BETA * w.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True))
    elif RENORM:
        w = w / mx.maximum(w.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True), 1e-9)
    else:
        w = w / p10
    safe = mx.where(res, slots, slots[:, :1]).astype(mx.uint32)      # rank 0 is always resident (waited)
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
    yk = base.__call__(sw, x, safe[None])                             # [1, 1, KC, D]
    if TAILBITS:
        loaded = np.zeros(KC, bool); loaded[:K] = pre < 0; loaded[nw:] = False   # read this call: missing waited ranks
        low = loaded.copy(); low[:2] = False                          # ranks 0-1 stay 4-bit
        STAT["calls"] += 1; STAT["full"] += int(loaded[:2].sum()); STAT["low"] += int(low.sum())
        if low.any():
            xe = mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3)); sl = safe.reshape(-1)
            idx = mx.arange(KC).astype(mx.uint32).reshape(1, 1, KC)
            h = sw.activation(P._tier_proj(sw.up_proj, xe, sl, idx, TAILBITS), P._tier_proj(sw.gate_proj, xe, sl, idx, TAILBITS))
            ylow = P._tier_proj(sw.down_proj, h, sl, idx, TAILBITS).squeeze(-2)
            yk = mx.where(mx.array(low)[None, None, :, None], ylow.astype(yk.dtype), yk)
    if LATE is not None and bm["L"] < NLAY - 1:                    # cold ranks A..B-1: out of this layer's sum, into the
        r = np.arange(KC)                                          # HC streams after layer L+1's attention (layer_call)
        lm = (r >= LATE[0]) & (r < LATE[1]) & (np.concatenate([pre, np.zeros(KC - len(pre), int)]) < 0)
        if lm.any():
            lmx = mx.array(lm)[None]
            STASH[bm["L"]] = (yk * (w * lmx)[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
            w = w * ~lmx
            STAT["late"] = STAT.get("late", 0) + int(lm.sum())
    STAT["blk"] = STAT.get("blk", 0) + 1
    y = (yk * w[None][..., None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))


def pre_block(self, sw, bm, x, pre):
    """pre2: the pre-picked two are read and waited for (in an engine: issued a layer earlier); the rest = the true router's
    best outside them, resident only, up to 10 in all; weights = the true probabilities over the chosen, renormalised."""
    L = bm["L"]
    p = np.array(mx.softmax(self.gate(x)[0].astype(mx.float32), axis=-1, precise=True)[0])   # true router, all experts
    order = np.argsort(-p)
    rest = [int(e) for e in order[:KC + 2] if int(e) not in pre][:KC - 2]
    idl = list(pre) + rest
    _, after = sync_slots(L, idl, 2, K)                            # reads the pre-picks; LRU-touches resident ranks < 10
    use = [0, 1] + [i for i in range(2, len(idl)) if after[i] >= 0][:K - 2]
    ids_u = [idl[i] for i in use]
    w = p[ids_u].astype(np.float64); w = w / w.sum()
    top2, top10 = set(int(e) for e in order[:2]), set(int(e) for e in order[:K])
    STAT["pre_n"] = STAT.get("pre_n", 0) + 2
    STAT["pre_top2"] = STAT.get("pre_top2", 0) + sum(e in top2 for e in pre)
    STAT["pre_top10"] = STAT.get("pre_top10", 0) + sum(e in top10 for e in pre)
    b = L // 8                                                     # per 8-layer band: true rank 0 / rank 1 among the picks
    PB = STAT.setdefault("pre_band", {})
    c = PB.setdefault(b, [0, 0, 0])
    c[0] += 1; c[1] += int(order[0]) in pre; c[2] += int(order[1]) in pre
    sl = [int(after[i]) for i in use] + [int(after[0])] * (K - len(use))
    w = np.concatenate([w, np.zeros(K - len(use))]).astype(np.float32)
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
    y = (base.__call__(sw, x, mx.array(sl, mx.uint32).reshape(1, 1, K)) * mx.array(w)[None, None, :, None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))


def sere_block(self, sw, bm, x, ids, pk, slots):
    L = bm["L"]
    if L not in SIM:
        d = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/simtab")
        SIM[L] = (np.load(f"{d}/D{L}.npy").astype(np.float32), np.load(f"{d}/N{L}.npy"))
    Dm, Nm = SIM[L]
    i, sl, p = np.array(ids[0]), np.array(slots[0]), np.array(pk[0].astype(mx.float32))
    w10 = p[:K] / p[:K].sum()
    res = [(int(e), int(s)) for e, s in zip(i, sl) if s >= 0]
    used, outs, ws = set(), [], []
    for r in range(K):
        if sl[r] >= 0:
            used.add(int(i[r])); outs.append(int(sl[r])); ws.append(w10[r]); continue
        u = int(i[r])
        cand = [(Dm[u, e], e, s) for e, s in res if e not in used and Dm[u, e] < Nm[u]]
        STAT["n"] += 1
        if cand:
            _, e, s = min(cand); used.add(e); outs.append(s); ws.append(w10[r]); STAT["used"] += 1
    ws = np.array(ws, np.float32)
    if RENORM:
        ws = ws / ws.sum()
    pad = K - len(outs)
    outs += [outs[0]] * pad; ws = np.concatenate([ws, np.zeros(pad, np.float32)])
    base = type(sw).__mro__[1]
    y = (base.__call__(sw, x, mx.array(outs, mx.uint32).reshape(1, 1, K)) * mx.array(ws)[None, None, :, None].astype(x.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + attention_gate(self.shared_expert(x), self.shared_expert_gate(x))


def step_forward_bild(rt, ids, pos, score, tau):
    """SG.step_forward with BiLD-style fallback: each 1-row frugal step's next-token entropy decides whether the token is
    rolled back (MTPLX rollback_after_verify, verified_tokens=1) and recomputed with the whole top-10 (MODE exact).
    Counts re-runs and SSD loads (ext misses) per decode step. The prefill's last token stays frugal."""
    from mtplx import generation as G
    from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
    from mtplx.cache_state import rollback_after_verify, snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy
    p0 = int(pos[0])
    ps = G.restore_or_prefill_prompt_state(rt, [int(x) for x in ids[:p0 + 1]], base_hidden_variant=None,
                                           mtp_history_policy="cycle", capture_hidden=False)
    cache, logits, out = ps.trunk_cache, ps.logits, []
    for t in pos:
        if t > p0:
            m0 = ext.stats()["misses"]
            snap = snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy(cache)
            x = mx.array([[int(ids[t])]])
            with attention_phase("ar_decode"):
                logits = rt.forward_ar(x, cache=cache)[:, -1, :]
            lp = logits.astype(mx.float32); lp = lp - mx.logsumexp(lp, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
            ent = float(-(mx.exp(lp) * lp).sum())
            if ent > tau:
                rollback_after_verify(cache, snap, verified_tokens=1)
                MODE["exact"] = True
                try:
                    with attention_phase("ar_decode"):
                        logits = rt.forward_ar(x, cache=cache)[:, -1, :]
                    mx.eval(logits)
                finally:
                    MODE["exact"] = False
                STAT["rerun"] = STAT.get("rerun", 0) + 1
            STAT["steps"] = STAT.get("steps", 0) + 1
            STAT["loads"] = STAT.get("loads", 0) + ext.stats()["misses"] - m0
        r = score(np.array([t]), logits)
        mx.eval(r)
        out.append({k: np.array(v) for k, v in r.items()})
    return {k: np.concatenate([o[k] for o in out]) for k in out[0]}


qwen4_exp.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = block
if LATE is not None:   # MTPLX DecoderLayer.__call__ + the half-layer-late writes (NewIdeas' tf_defer125.py dh semantics)
    assert SYNCGATE, "lAtB needs the CPU residency path (residency before the call)"
    from mtplx.models.qwen4_exp import _hyper_residual_write
    PENDH = {}

    def layer_call(self, hidden, *, input_ids, ssm_mask, cache):
        if "ple" in self:
            hidden = hidden + self.ple(hidden, input_ids, cache)
        mixed, hyper, inject = self.attn_hyper_connection(hidden)
        if self.is_linear:
            block_out = self.linear_attn(mixed, ssm_mask, cache)
        else:
            block_out = self.self_attn(mixed, cache)
        L = self._bt_L
        for lo, inj in PENDH.pop(L, []):                  # layer L-1's late ranks, before this layer's MLP read
            hyper = _hyper_residual_write(hyper, lo, inj)
        mixed, hyper, inject = self.mlp_hyper_connection(hyper, pending=(block_out, inject))
        block_out = self.mlp(mixed)
        hidden = _hyper_residual_write(hyper, block_out, inject)
        lo = STASH.pop(L, None)
        if lo is not None:
            PENDH.setdefault(L + 1, []).append((lo, inject))
        return hidden
    _TM = rt.model.language_model.model if hasattr(rt.model, "language_model") else rt.model.model
    for _i, _l in enumerate(_TM.layers):
        _l._bt_L = _i
    qwen4_exp.DecoderLayer.__call__ = layer_call
items = SG.tf_items()
head = EN.var_head(SG.load_ref_tf(items), SG._targets(items))
seqs = [i for i, it in enumerate(items) if it["set"] in SETS][::EVERY]

        def sc(pos, lg, i=i):

                LAST["ent"] = float(-(mx.exp(lp) * lp).sum())
            return head(i, pos, lg)
        LAST["ent"] = 99.0
        r = SG.step_forward(rt, it["ids"], it["pos"], sc)
    st.gate.check()
    out[it["set"]].append(r)
cat = {s: np.concatenate([r["kld"] for r in v]) for s, v in out.items() if v}
agr = {s: np.concatenate([r["agree"] for r in v]) for s, v in out.items() if v}
allk = np.concatenate(list(cat.values()))
line = (f"{VAR:6s} C={C} nw={NW} kc={KC} renorm={int(RENORM)}: KLD {allk.mean():.4f} over {len(allk)} positions "
        f"({len(seqs)} seqs, every {EVERY}) | " + " ".join(f"{s} {cat[s].mean():.4f} (top-1 {agr[s].mean():.3f})" for s in cat)
        + (f" | substituted {STAT['used'] / STAT['n']:.2f} of misses" if STAT["n"] else "")
        + (f" | mean waited ranks {STAT['w'] / STAT['wn']:.2f}" if STAT["wn"] else "")
        + (f" | per token: {48 * STAT['full'] / STAT['calls']:.1f} full + {48 * STAT['low'] / STAT['calls']:.1f} MSB-{TAILBITS} reads" if STAT["calls"] else "")
        + (f" | BiLD tau {BILD_TAU}: re-ran {STAT.get('rerun', 0) / max(1, STAT.get('steps', 0)):.1%} of steps, "

           if PRE is not None else "")
        + f" | {time.time() - t0:.0f}s")

RT = "" if abs(frac - 0.10) < 1e-9 else f"_r{round(100 * frac):02d}"   # other residencies get their own file

### scripts/tfvar_compare.py
"""Paired comparison of frugal KLD probes (data/tfvar/<VAR>_e<EVERY>.npz from tf_frugal_var.py): every variant scores the
same positions in the same order, so differences vs a baseline are paired per position. Prints mean KLD per set and
overall, top-1 agreement, and the bootstrap 95 % interval (over sequences' positions, 2,000 resamples of positions)
of the KLD difference to the baseline.   usage: tfvar_compare.py [EVERY] [BASELINE]   (default 8 base)"""
import glob, os, sys
import numpy as np

EVERY = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 8
BASE = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "base"
D = os.path.expanduser("~/Breakthrough/data/tfvar")
runs = {}
for f in sorted(glob.glob(f"{D}/*_e{EVERY}.npz")):
    v = os.path.basename(f)[: -len(f"_e{EVERY}.npz")]
    z = np.load(f)
    sets = sorted(k[4:] for k in z.files if k.startswith("kld_"))
    runs[v] = {s: (z[f"kld_{s}"], z[f"agree_{s}"]) for s in sets}
if BASE not in runs:
    sys.exit(f"no {BASE}_e{EVERY}.npz yet; have {sorted(runs)}")
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
b = np.concatenate([runs[BASE][s][0] for s in sorted(runs[BASE])])
print(f"every {EVERY}: {len(b)} positions; baseline {BASE} KLD {b.mean():.4f}")
for v, r in sorted(runs.items(), key=lambda kv: np.concatenate([kv[1][s][0] for s in kv[1]]).mean()):
    k = np.concatenate([r[s][0] for s in sorted(r)])
    a = np.concatenate([r[s][1] for s in sorted(r)])
    if len(k) != len(b):
        print(f"  {v:12s} position count differs ({len(k)} vs {len(b)}): skipped"); continue
    d = k - b
    boot = np.array([d[rng.integers(0, len(d), len(d))].mean() for _ in range(2000)])
    lo, hi = np.percentile(boot, [2.5, 97.5])
    per = " ".join(f"{s.replace('tf_', '')} {r[s][0].mean():.4f}" for s in sorted(r))
    print(f"  {v:12s} KLD {k.mean():.4f} ({per}) top-1 {a.mean():.3f} | vs {BASE} {d.mean():+.4f} [{lo:+.4f}, {hi:+.4f}]")


### scripts/theta_sim.py
"""Per-expert wait rule by gate share, with optional half-byte MSB-2 reads (Breakthrough, 2026-10-20 22:10).
125B traces with router probabilities; frugal LRU cache of whole 4-bit experts (C per layer); no substitution.
Rule per token-layer: ranks 0-1 always computed at 4 bits (read if missing). A missing rank r >= 2 with renormalised
top-10 share w_r >= theta is read (waited): 'full' = whole expert (2.77 MB, enters the cache), 'msb2' = MSB-2 planes
only (1.54 MB, used once at 2 bits, not cached). Below theta it is skipped.
Prints loads/token, MB/token, skipped mass, mass computed at 2 bits.  usage: theta_sim.py [C ...]"""
import glob, sys
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict

NL = 48; FULL, MSB2 = 2.765, 1.536
sets = {f.split("/")[-1].split("_")[0]: np.load(f)
        for f in sorted(glob.glob("/Users/kamen/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/*_800_g.npz"))}
ids = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in sets.items()}
gp = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in sets.items()}
w = {k: g[:, :, :10] / g[:, :, :10].sum(-1, keepdims=True) for k, g in gp.items()}


def run(C, theta, mode):
    mb = loads = ntok = 0.0
    skip, low = [], []
    for k in ids:
        tr, wt = ids[k], w[k]
        ntok += tr.shape[0]
        for l in range(NL):
            c = OrderedDict()
            for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
                s = lo = 0.0
                used = []
                for r in range(10):
                    e, p = int(tr[t, l, r]), float(wt[t, l, r])
                    if e in c:
                        used.append(e); continue
                    if r < 2 or (p >= theta and mode == "full"):
                        loads += 1; mb += FULL; used.append(e)
                    elif p >= theta:
                        loads += 1; mb += MSB2; lo += p
                    else:
                        s += p
                for e in used:
                    if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                    else: c[e] = 1
                while len(c) > C:
                    c.popitem(last=False)
                skip.append(s); low.append(lo)
    return loads / ntok, mb / ntok, np.mean(skip), np.mean(low)


for C in [int(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or [51, 26]:
    print(f"C={C} ({100 * C / 512:.0f} %): loads/token, MB/token | skipped mass | mass at 2 bits", flush=True)
    for mode in ("full", "msb2"):
        for theta in (1.0, 0.15, 0.10, 0.07, 0.05, 0.03):
            ld, mb, s, lo = run(C, theta, mode)
            print(f"  {mode:4s} theta {theta:4.2f}: {ld:6.1f} loads {mb:6.0f} MB | skipped {s:.3f} | 2-bit {lo:.3f}", flush=True)


### scripts/trellis.py
"""Fake-quant with a QTIP-style bitshift trellis (Breakthrough D2, 2026-10-20). Published method: QTIP (Tseng et al.,
NeurIPS 2024, arXiv 2406.11235): random Hadamard transform (incoherence) + bitshift trellis + Viterbi encoding. Here:
- RHT: random signs + mx.hadamard_transform along the input dimension, per-row RMS normalisation.
- Trellis: L-bit state; each weight shifts in k new bits (state = ((state << k) | b) & (2^L - 1)); the weight is
  reconstructed as LUT[state]. LUT = 2^L Gaussian quantiles in a fixed pseudo-random order (a "random Gaussian code",
  which QTIP shows behaves like its computed 1MAD/3INST codes). Free initial state per tile (L bits of overhead).
- Viterbi (exact MSE minimisation over all state paths) on the GPU, vectorised over tiles; predecessors of state s
  differ only in the k shifted-out bits, so the min over predecessors is shared by the 2^k states with the same s >> k.
- No Hessian-aware rounding (BlockLDLQ) here: data-free, like HIGGS. Rate = k + L/T + 16/rowlen bits/weight.
L = 12 (not QTIP's 16) keeps encoding ~16x cheaper on the M5 Pro at a small MSE cost.
usage: trellis.py test   -> NMSE on iid Gaussian data for k = 2, 3, 4 (sanity vs theory)
"""
import math, sys
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

T = 256          # weights per trellis tile


def make_lut(L, seed=0):
    from statistics import NormalDist
    nd = NormalDist()
    q = np.array([nd.inv_cdf((i + 0.5) / (1 << L)) for i in range(1 << L)], np.float32)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    return mx.array(q[rng.permutation(1 << L)])


def viterbi(x, lut, L, k):
    """x: [B, T] float32 (unit-variance-ish). Returns the reconstruction [B, T] that minimises the squared error."""
    S, K = 1 << L, 1 << k
    B, n = x.shape
    cost = (x[:, :1] - lut[None, :]) ** 2                       # [B, S], any initial state
    bps = []
    for t in range(1, n):
        prev = cost.reshape(B, K, S // K)                        # p = h * (S/K) + j, h = shifted-out bits
        bh = mx.argmin(prev, axis=1).astype(mx.uint8)            # [B, S/K]
        best = mx.min(prev, axis=1)
        cost = mx.repeat(best, K, axis=1) + (x[:, t:t + 1] - lut[None, :]) ** 2
        bps.append(bh)
        if t % 32 == 0:
            mx.eval(cost, *bps[-32:])
    s = mx.argmin(cost, axis=1)
    mx.eval(s, *bps)
    s = np.array(s).astype(np.int64)
    bp = np.stack([np.array(b) for b in bps])                    # [n-1, B, S/K]
    states = np.empty((n, B), np.int64)
    states[-1] = s
    rows = np.arange(B)
    for t in range(n - 1, 0, -1):
        j = states[t] >> k
        h = bp[t - 1, rows, j].astype(np.int64)
        states[t - 1] = (h << (L - k)) | j
    return mx.array(np.array(lut)[states.T])


_lib = None


def viterbi_cpu(x, lut, L, k):
    """C encoder (scripts/libtrellis.dylib, GCD threads): x float32 numpy [n, T] -> reconstruction [n, T]."""
    import ctypes, os
    global _lib
    if _lib is None:
        _lib = ctypes.CDLL(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "libtrellis.dylib"))
        _lib.viterbi_tiles.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p] * 3 + [ctypes.c_int] * 4 + [ctypes.c_void_p]
    x = np.ascontiguousarray(x, np.float32)
    lut = np.ascontiguousarray(lut, np.float32)
    out = np.empty_like(x)
    _lib.viterbi_tiles(x.ctypes.data, out.ctypes.data, None, x.shape[0], x.shape[1], L, k, lut.ctypes.data)
    return out


def block_hadamard(x, d):
    """Orthonormal Hadamard on blocks of the largest power of two dividing d (Sylvester: symmetric, self-inverse)."""
    b = d & -d
    y = mx.hadamard_transform(x.reshape(-1, d // b, b), scale=1.0 / math.sqrt(b))
    return y.reshape(x.shape)


def quantize(w, k, L=12, seed=0, chunk_tiles=8192, cpu=True):
    """Fake-quantize w [..., d]: random signs + block Hadamard, per-row RMS, trellis tiles of T, inverse."""
    shape, d = w.shape, w.shape[-1]
    lut = make_lut(L, seed)
    signs = mx.array(np.random.default_rng(seed + 1).choice([-1.0, 1.0], d).astype(np.float32))
    x = block_hadamard(w.reshape(-1, d).astype(mx.float32) * signs, d)
    rms = mx.sqrt(mx.mean(x * x, axis=-1, keepdims=True)) + 1e-12
    tiles = (x / rms).reshape(-1, T)
    if cpu:
        xh = mx.array(viterbi_cpu(np.array(tiles), np.array(lut), L, k))
    else:
        xh = mx.concatenate([viterbi(tiles[a:a + chunk_tiles], lut, L, k) for a in range(0, tiles.shape[0], chunk_tiles)])
    wh = block_hadamard(xh.reshape(-1, d) * rms, d) * signs
    return wh.reshape(shape)


if __name__ == "__main__" and sys.argv[1:] == ["test"]:
    import time
    g = mx.random.normal((64, 4096), key=mx.random.key(0))
    for k, cpu in ((3, False), (2, True), (3, True), (4, True)):
        t0 = time.time()
        wh = quantize(g, k, cpu=cpu)
        nmse = float(mx.mean((wh - g) ** 2).item() / mx.mean(g * g).item())
        print(f"k={k} {'cpu' if cpu else 'mlx'}: NMSE {nmse:.4f} (rate-distortion bound 2^-2k = {2 ** (-2 * k):.4f}; Lloyd-Max scalar "
              f"{ {2: 0.1175, 3: 0.03454, 4: 0.009497}[k]:.4f}), {time.time() - t0:.1f}s for {g.size} weights", flush=True)


### scripts/trellis_nmse.py
"""D2 step 1: what error does a real code reach per bit on real expert weights? (Breakthrough, 2026-10-20)
The noise sweep (data/noise35/results.txt) maps NMSE -> KLD per tensor class; this measures the NMSE side.
Codes, all on the same weights:
  affine g64 at 4 / 3 bits (MLX quantize: today's format and its 3-bit sibling),
  RHT + Lloyd-Max-like scalar (nearest of 2^k Gaussian quantiles, per-row RMS) at 3 / 4 bits,
  RHT + bitshift trellis (scripts/trellis.py, Viterbi, L = 8 / 10 / 12) at k = 2 / 3 / 4.
Sources: iid Gaussian; real 35B routed experts (8-bit unsloth pack, layers 0/20/39, experts 0-3, gate/up/down);
real 125B routed experts (4-bit bare pack, dequantized, so its NMSE is relative to the 4-bit values).
GPU, a few minutes. usage: trellis_nmse.py
"""
import glob, json, math, os, sys, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
from trellis import quantize, make_lut, T


def nmse(a, b):
    return float(mx.mean((a - b) ** 2).item() / mx.mean(b * b).item())


def affine(w, bits):
    q = mx.quantize(w.astype(mx.bfloat16), group_size=64, bits=bits)
    return mx.dequantize(*q, group_size=64, bits=bits).astype(mx.float32)


def rht_scalar(w, k, seed=0):
    d = w.shape[-1]
    signs = mx.array(np.random.default_rng(seed + 1).choice([-1.0, 1.0], d).astype(np.float32))
    x = mx.hadamard_transform(w.reshape(-1, d) * signs, scale=1.0 / math.sqrt(d))
    rms = mx.sqrt(mx.mean(x * x, axis=-1, keepdims=True)) + 1e-12
    from statistics import NormalDist
    lv = mx.array(np.array([NormalDist().inv_cdf((i + 0.5) / (1 << k)) for i in range(1 << k)], np.float32))
    z = x / rms
    idx = mx.argmin(mx.abs(z[..., None] - lv), axis=-1)
    xh = lv[idx] * rms
    return (mx.hadamard_transform(xh, scale=1.0 / math.sqrt(d)) * signs).reshape(w.shape)


def load_tensor(path, name):
    return mx.load(path)[name]


def experts35():
    d = glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-8bit/snapshots/*/"))[0]
    idx = json.load(open(d + "model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]

    for l in (0, 20, 39):

            base = f"language_model.model.layers.{l}.mlp.switch_mlp.{p}"
            f = mx.load(d + idx[base + ".weight"])
            w = mx.dequantize(f[base + ".weight"][:4], f[base + ".scales"][:4], f[base + ".biases"][:4], group_size=64, bits=8)
            out[f"35B L{l} {p}"] = w.astype(mx.float32)
    return out


def experts125():
    d = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare/")
    idx = json.load(open(d + "model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
    out = {}
    for l in (0, 24, 47):
        for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
            base = f"language_model.model.layers.{l}.mlp.switch_mlp.{p}"
            if base + ".weight" not in idx:
                continue
            f = mx.load(d + idx[base + ".weight"])

    return out


if __name__ == "__main__":

        res = {"aff4": affine(w, 4), "aff3": affine(w, 3), "rht-s4": rht_scalar(w, 4), "rht-s3": rht_scalar(w, 3),
               "tr12k4": quantize(w, 4, L=12), "tr12k3": quantize(w, 3, L=12), "tr12k2": quantize(w, 2, L=12),
               "tr10k3": quantize(w, 3, L=10), "tr8k3": quantize(w, 3, L=8)}
        vals = {c: nmse(res[c], w) for c in cols}
        for c in cols:

                agg[c].append(vals[c])
        print(f"{name:20s} " + " ".join(f"{vals[c]:8.4f}" for c in cols), flush=True)
    print("mean real experts    " + " ".join(f"{np.mean(agg[c]):8.4f}" for c in cols))
    print(f"bits/weight: aff4 4.5, aff3 3.5 (g64 bf16 scale+bias); rht-s k + ~0.01; trellis k + L/{T} + ~0.01. "
          f"{time.time() - t0:.0f}s, peak {mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB")


### scripts/waitr_sim.py
"""Wait-top-r streaming on the 125B traces (Breakthrough, 2026-10-19). MoEPrefetch's 'wait top-2' (35B: GSM8K-150
identical at C=64 and C=48): ranks < r are always exact (loaded on a miss = SSD bytes), ranks >= r use the expert if it
is resident, else a substitute: the best-ranked resident expert among the router's ranks 10-19 not already chosen,
else the rank is dropped. Used experts become MRU (LRU per layer, strict capacity C). Ids only (traces have no gates).
Prints per token: SSD loads (x 2.77 MB at 4-bit), substituted ranks, dropped ranks.
"""
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict
T = "/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/"; SETS = ["code", "chat", "bulgarian", "math"]; NL = 48
trs = {s: np.stack([np.load(T + f"{s}_800.npz")[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for s in SETS}
def run(tr, C, r):
    loads = subs = drops = 0
    for l in range(NL):
        c = OrderedDict()
        for t in range(tr.shape[0]):
            row = [int(x) for x in tr[t, l]]
            alt = [e for e in row[10:20] if e in c]
            used = []
            for rank, e in enumerate(row[:10]):
                if rank < r or e in c:
                    if e not in c: loads += 1
                    used.append(e)
                else:
                    a = next((x for x in alt if x not in used), None)
                    if a is None: drops += 1
                    else: subs += 1; used.append(a); alt.remove(a)
            for e in used:
                if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                else: c[e] = 1
            while len(c) > C: c.popitem(last=False)
    n = tr.shape[0]; return loads / n, subs / n, drops / n
print("per token, mean of 4 sets: SSD loads (MB at 2.77 MB/expert) / substituted ranks / dropped ranks (of 480 uses)")
print("C (res%)        " + "  ".join(f"r={r:<22d}" for r in (1, 2, 3, 4, 10)))
for C in (8, 13, 26, 51, 128):
    cells = []
    for r in (1, 2, 3, 4, 10):
        v = np.mean([run(trs[s], C, r) for s in SETS], 0)
        cells.append(f"{v[0]:5.1f} ({v[0]*2.77:4.0f}MB) {v[1]:5.1f} {v[2]:4.1f}")
    print(f"C={C:<3d} ({100*C/512:4.1f}%) " + "  ".join(cells))


### scripts/ws_bounds.py
"""Bounds for the budget (Breakthrough, 2026-10-19). Same 125B traces as ws_sim.py (top-10 of 20 ids per token/layer).
(A) Belady (MIN) misses per token at C slots per layer: the best any exact cache policy can do (no prefetch limits).
(B) Chunked processing: tokens handled K at a time (one pass per chunk, union of the chunk's experts per layer),
    LRU over chunk unions with C slots per layer (MRU = experts of the latest chunk). Misses per token = loads / tokens.
    K=1 is eager decode; K=4 ~ a depth-3 MTP verify pass; K>=16 needs a drafter that is accepted that far.
"""
import numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict
T = "/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/"
SETS = ["code", "chat", "bulgarian", "math"]; NL = 48
def load(s):
    d = np.load(T + f"{s}_800.npz")
    return np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32)[:, :, :10]
trs = {s: load(s) for s in SETS}

def belady(seq_steps, C):
    # seq_steps: list of sets (experts needed at each step). MIN over steps: on a miss with a full cache,
    # evict the cached expert (not needed now) whose next use is farthest.
    n = len(seq_steps); nxt = {}; next_use = [None] * n
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        next_use[i] = {e: nxt.get(e, 10**9) for e in seq_steps[i]}
        for e in seq_steps[i]: nxt[e] = i
    # next use AFTER step i for expert e: computed lazily with per-expert position lists
    pos = {}
    for i, st in enumerate(seq_steps):
        for e in st: pos.setdefault(e, []).append(i)
    ptr = {e: 0 for e in pos}
    cache = set(); miss = 0
    for i, st in enumerate(seq_steps):
        for e in st: ptr[e] += 1  # advance past current use
        for e in st:
            if e in cache: continue
            miss += 1
            if len(cache) >= C:
                cand = [x for x in cache if x not in st]
                def nu(x):
                    p = pos[x]; k = ptr[x]
                    return p[k] if k < len(p) else 10**9
                v = max(cand, key=nu); cache.remove(v)
            cache.add(e)
    return miss

def chunk_lru(tr, C, K):
    # strict capacity C per layer: a chunk's union streams through; afterwards the cache keeps the C most recently
    # used experts, where a chunk's experts count as used in order of their frequency within the chunk (most frequent last).
    loads = 0
    for l in range(NL):
        c = OrderedDict()
        for a in range(0, tr.shape[0], K):
            u, cnt = np.unique(tr[a:a + K, l], return_counts=True)
            for e in u[np.argsort(cnt, kind="stable")]:
                e = int(e)
                if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                else:
                    loads += 1; c[e] = 1
            while len(c) > C: c.popitem(last=False)
    return loads / tr.shape[0]

print("(A) misses per token (of 480 uses), mean over 4 sets: LRU vs Belady")
for C in (13, 26, 51, 128):
    lr = np.mean([chunk_lru(trs[s], C, 1) for s in SETS])
    bl = np.mean([sum(belady([set(map(int, trs[s][t, l])) for t in range(trs[s].shape[0])], C) for l in range(NL)) / trs[s].shape[0] for s in SETS])
    print(f"C={C:3d} ({100*C/512:4.1f}%): LRU {lr:6.1f}  Belady {bl:6.1f}  ({lr/bl:.2f}x)")
print("\n(B) chunked passes: expert loads per token (mean over sets); rows = chunk K, cols = C slots per layer")
Cs = (13, 26, 51, 128)
print("K      " + "  ".join(f"C={C:<5d}" for C in Cs))
for K in (1, 4, 16, 64, 256):
    print(f"{K:<6d} " + "  ".join(f"{np.mean([chunk_lru(trs[s], C, K) for s in SETS]):7.1f}" for C in Cs))


### scripts/ws_sim.py
"""Working-set and cache numbers for the bytes-per-token budget (Breakthrough, 2026-10-19).
125B decode routing traces from MoEPrefetch (data/traces125/{code,chat,bulgarian,math}_800.npz: router top-20 ids per
decode token per layer; ranks 0-9 are the active experts). Eager decode, one token per step, page cache ignored.
Prints: (1) per-layer LRU hit rate vs cache size C (slots per layer), (2) distinct experts per layer touched in windows
of W tokens, (3) LRU with a reduced k (top-k only), (4) a crude 'cache-aware routing' proxy: a missing expert at rank
>= r0 is replaced by the best-ranked *cached* expert among ranks 10-19 if one exists (quality NOT modelled).
"""
import sys, numpy as np
from collections import OrderedDict
T = "/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/"
SETS = ["code", "chat", "bulgarian", "math"]
NL = 48

def load(s):
    d = np.load(T + f"{s}_800.npz")
    return np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32)  # [tok, layer, 20]

def lru(tr, C, k=10, swap_from=None):
    ntok = tr.shape[0]; miss = 0; uses = 0; swaps = 0
    for l in range(NL):
        c = OrderedDict()
        for t in range(ntok):
            ids = list(tr[t, l, :k])
            if swap_from is not None:
                alt = [e for e in tr[t, l, 10:20] if e in c]
                out = []
                for r, e in enumerate(ids):
                    if e not in c and r >= swap_from and alt:
                        out.append(alt.pop(0)); swaps += 1
                    else:
                        out.append(e)
                ids = out
            for e in ids:
                uses += 1
                if e in c: c.move_to_end(e)
                else:
                    miss += 1; c[e] = 1
                    if len(c) > C: c.popitem(last=False)
    return 1 - miss / uses, miss / ntok, swaps / ntok

trs = {s: load(s) for s in SETS}
print("tokens per set:", {s: trs[s].shape[0] for s in SETS})
print("\n(1) LRU per layer, top-10, hit rate / misses per token (of 480 uses), by C slots per layer (%% of 512)")
Cs = [4, 8, 13, 16, 26, 32, 51, 64, 96, 128, 192, 256]
print("C      " + " ".join(f"{C:>11d}" for C in Cs))
print("% res  " + " ".join(f"{100*C/512:>10.1f}%" for C in Cs))
for s in SETS:
    row = [lru(trs[s], C) for C in Cs]
    print(f"{s:7s}" + " ".join(f"{h:5.3f}/{m:5.1f}" for h, m, _ in row))

print("\n(2) distinct experts per layer touched within W consecutive tokens (mean over layers and windows), top-10")
for s in SETS:
    tr = trs[s]; out = []
    for W in [1, 4, 16, 64, 256, 800]:
        vals = []
        for l in range(NL):
            for a in range(0, tr.shape[0] - W + 1, max(1, W)):
                vals.append(len(np.unique(tr[a:a + W, l, :10])))
        out.append(f"W={W}:{np.mean(vals):.0f}")
    print(f"{s:10s}", "  ".join(out))

print("\n(3) LRU with reduced k (only top-k routed): misses per token, C=26 / 51 / 128")
for k in [10, 8, 6, 4]:
    r = [np.mean([lru(trs[s], C, k=k)[1] for s in SETS]) for C in (26, 51, 128)]
    print(f"k={k:2d}  " + "  ".join(f"{x:6.1f}" for x in r))

print("\n(4) cache-aware swap proxy (missing rank>=r0 -> best cached among ranks 10-19): misses/token, swaps/token")
for r0 in [None, 8, 6, 4]:
    r = [[lru(trs[s], C, swap_from=r0) for s in SETS] for C in (26, 51, 128)]
    print(f"r0={str(r0):4s} " + "  ".join(f"C={C}: {np.mean([x[1] for x in rr]):6.1f} miss, {np.mean([x[2] for x in rr]):5.1f} swap" for C, rr in zip((26, 51, 128), r)))


### SpecDecode/ab_prompts.py
"""Two fixed prompts for an LM Studio vs spec-server decode comparison.
  ab_prompts.py lms|spec [FILE]   prints tok/s per prompt
"""
import json, sys, time, urllib.request

SRC = open(sys.argv[2]).read()
PROMPTS = {
    "copy-edit": f"Here is a file:\n\n```python\n{SRC}```\n\nRewrite the whole file with total_value renamed to total "
                 "everywhere. Output only the full file in one code block.",
    "fresh": "Write a Python module implementing an LRU cache class with get, put, delete, resize and "
             "a __repr__, plus a small unittest suite. Output only code.",
}


def post(url, body, headers={}):
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, json.dumps(body).encode(), {"content-type": "application/json", **headers})
    return json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=600).read())


for name, p in PROMPTS.items():
    if sys.argv[1] == "lms":
        r = post("http://localhost:1234/api/v0/chat/completions",
                 {"model": "unsloth/qwen3.6-35b-a3b-mlx", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": p}],
                  "temperature": 0, "max_tokens": 600, "chat_template_kwargs": {"enable_thinking": False}})
        st = r.get("stats", {})
        out = r["choices"][0]["message"]
        think = len(out.get("reasoning_content") or out.get("reasoning") or "")
        print(f"lms  {name:9s} {st.get('tokens_per_second', 0):6.1f} tok/s  out {r['usage']['completion_tokens']} tok"
              f"  ttft {st.get('time_to_first_token', 0):.2f}s  thinking chars {think}")
    else:
        t = time.perf_counter()
        r = post("http://127.0.0.1:1235/v1/messages", {"model": "x", "max_tokens": 600,
                 "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": p}]})
        print(f"spec {name:9s} (see server log for decode tok/s)  out {r['usage']['output_tokens']} tok  wall {time.perf_counter()-t:.1f}s")


### SpecDecode/band_probe.py
"""Which ops give batch-size-dependent results? Per-op "kernel bands" from a model's shapes.

Reads a model directory's safetensors headers (no weight load, read-only) and config, then on random tensors of
the same shapes checks, for M = 1..MMAX rows, whether row 0 of each op's output is bit-identical to the row-0
output at other M. Prints, per op, the bands of M with identical row 0. The intersection over all ops is the band
a batch-invariant decode/verify path can pin (pad passes into it). Covers: every distinct quantized Linear shape,
the routed-expert gather (top-k from config, unsorted < 64 ids and sorted >= 64), SDPA with the config's
full-attention head geometry (q = M rows at the end of a 4K-key cache), RMSNorm, and the gated-delta recurrence.
Custom kernels of other runtimes (e.g. MTPLX QSA / hyper-connections) need the model-level probe instead.
  band_probe.py MODEL_DIR [MMAX]
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import Counter

import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
import numpy as np

mdir = os.path.expanduser(sys.argv[1])
MMAX = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 17
cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(mdir, "config.json")))
tc = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
q = cfg.get("quantization") or cfg.get("quantization_config") or {}
