Before each verify pass (rows: y + MTP drafts), predict every row's top-8 routed experts at every layer, then compare with
the true routing of that pass. Predictors (router of layer L applied to a proxy residual, top-B by gate prob):
  temporal  the last committed token's own routing at L (no draft info)
  mtp       (a) the MTP head's output hidden for that row's token, as the layer-L residual
  swap      (b) last committed token's layer-L residual with its embedding swapped for the row token's embedding
  prev      (c) the previous verify pass's routing at the same position (rejected draft there), else temporal
  swap+prev blend of (b) and (c) gate probabilities
Recall = |pred top-B ∩ true top-8| / 8 per row, and per pass on the union over rows (pred union vs true union).
  pass_predict.py MODEL OUT.json [N_TOKENS] [K]
"""
import json
import os
import sys

import mlx.core as mx
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from specdec import Model  # noqa: E402
from mtp import install_capture, load_mtp  # noqa: E402

PROMPTS = {
    "code": open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
    "edit": "Here is a file:\n\n```python\n" + open(os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode/scripts/inventory_sample.py")).read()
            + "```\n\nRewrite the whole file with total_value renamed to total everywhere. Output only the full file in one code block.",
    "prose": "Explain, in plain prose for a curious 14-year-old, how a CPU cache works and why it makes programs faster. "
             "Use one everyday analogy and keep it to about 300 words.",
}
BUDGETS = (8, 12, 16)
PREDS = ("temporal", "mtp", "swap", "prev", "swap+prev")

path, out_path = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
N = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 160
K = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 3
m = Model(path)
m.cap = install_capture(m.model)
mtp = load_mtp(m.model)
lm = m.model.language_model
embed, head = lm.model.embed_tokens, lm.lm_head
layers = lm.model.layers
NL = len(layers)
TOPK = layers[0].mlp.top_k


class Rec:
    """Wraps post_attention_layernorm: keeps its input (the pre-MLP residual h) while REC is on."""

    def __init__(self, norm, i):
        self.norm, self.i = norm, i

    def __call__(self, x):
        if REC["on"]:
            REC["h"][self.i] = x
        return self.norm(x)


REC = {"on": False, "h": [None] * NL}
NORMS = [layer.post_attention_layernorm for layer in layers]
GATES = [layer.mlp.gate for layer in layers]
for i, layer in enumerate(layers):
    object.__setattr__(layer, "post_attention_layernorm", Rec(NORMS[i], i))


def probs(L, h):
    """Layer L's router probabilities for residual(s) h [..., H] -> [..., E] float32."""
    return mx.softmax(GATES[L](NORMS[L](h)).astype(mx.float32), axis=-1)


def topset(p, B):
    return np.argpartition(-p, B - 1, axis=-1)[..., :B]


def run(name, prompt):
    ids = list(m.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}], add_generation_prompt=True,
                                               enable_thinking=False))
    from mlx_lm.models import cache as cm
    m.reset()
    mcache = cm.make_prompt_cache(mtp)
    st = {"x": None}

    def hook(i, j):
        x = mtp(m.cap.post, embed(mx.array(ids[i + 1:j + 1])[None]), mcache)
        st["x"] = x[:, -1:]
        mx.eval(st["x"], [c.state for c in mcache])
    # capture the last prompt token's residuals during prefill: rerun it? cheaper: prefill all but last 1, then plain step
    m.prefill(ids[:-1], on_chunk=hook)
    y = ids[-1]
    last = None    # (token, per-layer residual [NL, H] np? kept as mx) of the last committed token
    prev = {}      # position -> per-layer router probs (np [NL, E]) from the previous verify pass
    P = len(ids) - 1
    hits = {(p, B): np.zeros(NL) for p in PREDS for B in BUDGETS}
    uhits = {(p, B): np.zeros(NL) for p in PREDS for B in BUDGETS}
    usize = {(p, B): np.zeros(NL) for p in PREDS for B in BUDGETS}
    rows_n, true_u, passes, out = 0, np.zeros(NL), 0, []
    while len(out) < N:
        # draft chain, keeping each row's MTP hidden: row 0 (y) -> st["x"], row j -> mtp(x_{j-1}, emb(d_{j-1}))
        xs, d, x = [st["x"]], [], st["x"]
        for j in range(K):
            t = mx.argmax(head(x)[0, -1])
            d.append(t)
            x = mtp(x, embed(t.reshape(1, 1)), mcache)
            xs.append(x)
        mx.eval(d, xs)
        mcache[0].trim(K)
        d = [int(t.item()) for t in d]
        toks = [y] + d
        S = len(toks)
        # predictions (only from state available before verify)
        pred = {}
        if last is not None:
            ltok, lh = last
            E_row = embed(mx.array([toks]))[0]                     # [S, H]
            E_last = embed(mx.array([ltok]))[0]                    # [H]
            Xm = mx.concatenate(xs[:S], axis=1)[0]                # [S, H]
            pm, ps, pt = [], [], []
            for L in range(NL):
                pm.append(probs(L, Xm))
                ps.append(probs(L, lh[L][None] - E_last[None] + E_row))
                pt.append(probs(L, lh[L][None]))
            pm, ps, pt = (np.array(mx.stack(a)) for a in (pm, ps, pt))  # [NL, S, E]
            pt = np.broadcast_to(pt, ps.shape)
            pp = np.stack([prev[P + r] if P + r in prev else pt[:, r] for r in range(S)], axis=1)
            pred = {"temporal": pt, "mtp": pm, "swap": ps, "prev": pp, "swap+prev": ps + pp}
        # verify
        REC["on"] = True
        g = m.forward(toks)
        REC["on"] = False
        H = mx.stack([REC["h"][L][0] for L in range(NL)])         # [NL, S, H]
        tp = np.array(mx.stack([probs(L, H[L]) for L in range(NL)]))   # [NL, S, E]
        true = topset(tp, TOPK)                                    # [NL, S, 8]
        n = 0
        while n < len(d) and d[n] == g[n]:
            n += 1
        new = d[:n] + [g[n]]
        m.keep(S, n + 1)
        o = mtp(m.last_hidden[:, :n + 1], embed(mx.array(new)[None]), mcache)
        st["x"] = o[:, -1:]
        if pred:
            for L in range(NL):
                tu = set(true[L].ravel().tolist())
                true_u[L] += len(tu)
                for p in PREDS:
                    for B in BUDGETS:
                        ps_ = topset(pred[p][L], B)
                        hits[(p, B)][L] += sum(len(set(ps_[r]) & set(true[L, r])) for r in range(S)) / TOPK
                        pu = set(ps_.ravel().tolist())
                        uhits[(p, B)][L] += len(pu & tu) / len(tu)
                        usize[(p, B)][L] += len(pu)
            rows_n += S
            passes += 1
        prev = {P + r: tp[:, r] for r in range(S)}
        last = (toks[n], H[:, n])
        P += n + 1
        y = new[-1]
        out += new
    res = {"prompt": name, "passes": passes, "rows": rows_n, "tok_per_pass": len(out) / max(1, passes + 1),
           "true_union": (true_u / passes).tolist(), "row": {}, "union": {}, "union_size": {}}
    for p in PREDS:
        for B in BUDGETS:
            res["row"][f"{p}@{B}"] = (hits[(p, B)] / rows_n).tolist()
            res["union"][f"{p}@{B}"] = (uhits[(p, B)] / passes).tolist()
            res["union_size"][f"{p}@{B}"] = (usize[(p, B)] / passes).tolist()
    return res


def show(r):
    q = [(0, NL // 4), (NL // 4, NL // 2), (NL // 2, 3 * NL // 4), (3 * NL // 4, NL)]
    print(f"== {r['prompt']}: {r['passes']} passes, {r['tok_per_pass']:.2f} tok/pass, true union/layer "
          f"{np.mean(r['true_union']):.1f}")
    print("  per-row recall of top-8      layers " + "  ".join(f"{a:2d}-{b - 1:2d}" for a, b in q) + "   all")
    for k, v in r["row"].items():
        v = np.array(v)
        print(f"  {k:14s}" + " " * 16 + "  ".join(f"{v[a:b].mean():5.2f}" for a, b in q) + f"  {v.mean():5.2f}"
              + f"   | union {np.mean(r['union'][k]):.2f} (size {np.mean(r['union_size'][k]):.0f})")


results = []
for name in os.environ.get("PROMPTS", "code,edit,prose").split(","):
    r = run(name, PROMPTS[name])
    show(r)
    results.append(r)
    json.dump(results, open(out_path, "w"))
    mx.clear_cache()


### SpecDecode/prefill_chunk.py
"""Prefill throughput vs chunk size. Bigger chunks give each expert more rows, which the NAX
gather kernel needs (fixed 64-row tiles, MLX issue #3925).
  prefill_chunk.py MODEL
"""
import json, sys, time
import mlx.core as mx
from render import render
from specdec import Model

m = Model(sys.argv[1])
s = next(json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl") if json.loads(l)["id"] == "edit02")
ids, _ = render(s, m.tokenizer)
ids = (ids * 2)[:16384]
for chunk in (1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 2048, 8192):
    m.reset()
    mx.clear_cache()
    t = m.prefill(ids, chunk=chunk)
    print(f"chunk {chunk:5d}: {len(ids)/t:6.0f} tok/s  ({t:.2f}s for {len(ids)} tokens)", flush=True)


### SpecDecode/prefill_micro.py
"""Prefill-side throughput: quantized matmul at M=2048 rows (dense) and the MoE gather path
(sorted, 2048 tokens x 8 experts), as TFLOPS. Run under different env settings to A/B the M5
neural-accelerator (NAX) kernels, e.g. MLX_METAL_GPU_ARCH=<older arch>.
  prefill_micro.py BITS
"""
import os
import sys
import time

import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx_lm.models.switch_layers import SwitchGLU

bits = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 8
M = 2048
dev = mx.metal.device_info()
print(f"arch {dev.get('architecture')}  env MLX_METAL_GPU_ARCH={os.environ.get('MLX_METAL_GPU_ARCH')}")


def bench(fn, flops, reps=10):
    for _ in range(3):
        mx.eval(fn())
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        t = time.perf_counter()
        mx.eval(fn())
        ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
    dt = sorted(ts)[reps // 2]
    return dt * 1e3, flops / dt / 1e12


lin = nn.QuantizedLinear(2048, 8192, bias=False, bits=bits)
mx.eval(lin.parameters())
x = mx.random.normal((1, M, 2048)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
ms, tf = bench(lambda: lin(x), 2 * M * 2048 * 8192)
print(f"{bits}-bit dense qmm  M={M} 2048->8192: {ms:6.2f} ms  {tf:5.1f} TFLOPS")
w = mx.random.normal((8192, 2048)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
ms, tf = bench(lambda: x @ w.T, 2 * M * 2048 * 8192)
print(f"bf16 dense matmul M={M} 2048->8192: {ms:6.2f} ms  {tf:5.1f} TFLOPS")
sw = SwitchGLU(2048, 512, 256)
nn.quantize(sw, group_size=64, bits=bits)
mx.eval(sw.parameters())
idx = mx.random.randint(0, 256, (1, M, 8)).astype(mx.uint32)
ms, tf = bench(lambda: sw(x, idx), 3 * 2 * M * 8 * 2048 * 512)
print(f"{bits}-bit MoE gather (sorted) {M} tok x 8 experts: {ms:6.2f} ms  {tf:5.1f} TFLOPS")


### SpecDecode/prefill_prof.py
"""Time one 2048-token prefill chunk (at ~8K context) of the real model, split by component.
  prefill_prof.py MODEL
"""
import json
import sys
import time

import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm.models.base import create_attention_mask, create_ssm_mask

from render import render
from specdec import Model

m = Model(sys.argv[1])
lm = m.model.language_model
tm, head = lm.model, lm.lm_head
s = next(json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl") if json.loads(l)["id"] == "edit03")
ids, _ = render(s, m.tokenizer)
pre, chunk = ids[:6000], ids[6000:8048]
m.prefill(pre)
snap = m.snapshot()
L = tm.layers
x_in = mx.array(chunk)[None]


def timeit(fn, reps=5):
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        m.restore(snap)
        mx.synchronize()
        t = time.perf_counter()
        mx.eval(fn())
        ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
    return sorted(ts)[reps // 2] * 1e3


# capture per-layer inputs once
m.restore(snap)
h = tm.embed_tokens(x_in)
xs, hs = [], []
fa_mask = create_attention_mask(h, m.cache[tm.fa_idx])
ssm_mask = create_ssm_mask(h, m.cache[tm.ssm_idx])
for layer, c in zip(L, m.cache):
    xs.append(h)
    mix = layer.linear_attn if layer.is_linear else layer.self_attn
    h = h + mix(layer.input_layernorm(h), ssm_mask if layer.is_linear else fa_mask, c)
    hs.append(layer.post_attention_layernorm(h))
    h = h + layer.mlp(hs[-1])
final = tm.norm(h)
mx.eval(xs, hs, final)


def router(i):
    g = mx.softmax(L[i].mlp.gate(hs[i]), axis=-1, precise=True)
    return mx.argpartition(g, kth=-8, axis=-1)[..., -8:]


inds = [router(i) for i in range(len(L))]
mx.eval(inds)
lin = [i for i, l in enumerate(L) if l.is_linear]
full = [i for i, l in enumerate(L) if not l.is_linear]
parts = {
    "full model (lm_head on all 2048)": lambda: m.model(x_in, cache=m.cache),
    "trunk only (no lm_head)": lambda: tm(x_in, cache=m.cache),
    "lm_head on 2048 rows": lambda: head(final),
    "linear-attn mixers (30)": lambda: [L[i].linear_attn(L[i].input_layernorm(xs[i]), ssm_mask, m.cache[i]) for i in lin],
    "full-attn mixers (10)": lambda: [L[i].self_attn(L[i].input_layernorm(xs[i]), fa_mask, m.cache[i]) for i in full],
    "routed experts (40)": lambda: [L[i].mlp.switch_mlp(hs[i], inds[i]) for i in range(len(L))],
    "shared experts (40)": lambda: [L[i].mlp.shared_expert(hs[i]) for i in range(len(L))],
}
res = {k: timeit(f) for k, f in parts.items()}
tot = res["full model (lm_head on all 2048)"]
for k, v in res.items():
    if "model" in k or "trunk" in k:
        print(f"  {k:34s} {v:7.1f} ms  ({2048 / v * 1e3:5.0f} tok/s)")
    else:
        print(f"  {k:34s} {v:7.1f} ms  {v / tot:5.0%}")


### SpecDecode/qmm_flip.py
"""Does MLX's quantized matmul give a different row 0 at M=5 vs M=6 (and other M) for the 125B's real layer-28 in_proj_qkv
weights? Many random input batches; counts mismatches per M against M=2. Reads the one tensor from safetensors."""
import glob, json, os, struct
import mlx.core as mx, numpy as np
md = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
pre = "language_model.model.layers.28.linear_attn.in_proj_qkv."
W = {}
for f in glob.glob(md + "/*.safetensors"):
    w = mx.load(f)
    for k in ("weight", "scales", "biases"):
        if pre + k in w:
            W[k] = w[pre + k]
    if len(W) == 3:
        break
mx.eval(W)
def f(x):
    return mx.quantized_matmul(x, W["weight"], W["scales"], W["biases"], transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4)
mism = {m: 0 for m in range(1, 13)}
T = 200
for t in range(T):
    x = (mx.random.normal((12, 2560)) * 0.5).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    ref = None
    outs = {}
    for m in range(1, 13):
        outs[m] = f(x[:m])[0]
    mx.eval(list(outs.values()))
    ref = np.array(outs[2].astype(mx.float32))
    for m in range(1, 13):
        if not np.array_equal(np.array(outs[m].astype(mx.float32)), ref):
            mism[m] += 1
print(f"mlx {mx.__version__}: row-0 mismatches vs M=2 over {T} random batches:", {m: c for m, c in mism.items()})


### SpecDecode/qmm_micro.py
"""Time one quantized linear layer (5120 -> 17408, like the 27B MLP) for M = 1..16 rows."""
import time
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
import sys
IN, OUT = int(sys.argv[1]), int(sys.argv[2])
for bits in (6, 8):
    lin = nn.QuantizedLinear(IN, OUT, bias=False, group_size=64, bits=bits)
    mx.eval(lin.parameters())
    res = {}
    for M in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8, 9, 12, 16, 32):
        x = mx.random.normal((1, M, IN)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
        for _ in range(5): mx.eval(lin(x))
        ts = []
        for _ in range(30):
            t = time.perf_counter(); mx.eval(lin(x)); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
        res[M] = sorted(ts)[15]
    print(f"{bits}-bit: " + " ".join(f"M{M}={res[M]/res[1]:.2f}x" for M in res) + f"  (M1 {res[1]*1e3:.2f} ms)")


### SpecDecode/reduce_band.py
"""Row-0 bands (S=1..12) of the reductions inside qwen4_exp's GatedDeltaNet: its python l2norm (f32 sum of squares + rsqrt
over the head dim) on [1,S,H,128], and mx.fast.rms_norm on [1,S,H,128]."""
import mlx.core as mx, numpy as np
H, D = 16, 128
x = mx.random.normal((1, 12, H, D)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
def l2(t):
    tf = t.astype(mx.float32)
    return (tf * mx.rsqrt((tf * tf).sum(-1, keepdims=True) + 1e-6)).astype(t.dtype)
w = mx.random.normal((D,)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
for name, f in (("l2norm (sum of squares)", l2), ("fast.rms_norm", lambda t: mx.fast.rms_norm(t, w, 1e-6)),
                ("sum over last axis f32", lambda t: t.astype(mx.float32).sum(-1))):
    rows = {}
    for S in range(1, 13):
        y = f(x[:, :S]); mx.eval(y); rows[S] = np.array(y[0, 0].astype(mx.float32))
    b, cur = [], [1]
    for S in range(2, 13):
        if np.array_equal(rows[S], rows[cur[-1]]): cur.append(S)
        else: b.append(cur); cur = [S]
    b.append(cur)
    print(f"{name}: " + " ".join(f"{{{z[0]}-{z[-1]}}}" for z in b))


### SpecDecode/render.py
"""Turn an extracted sample into token ids: prompt + forced assistant prefix.

The prompt is rendered with the model's own chat template (what LM Studio does).
For tool-call samples the assistant prefix runs up to the start of the bulk
argument (old_string / content / command), so the model then writes the part a
coding agent spends its time on. For text samples the prefix is just the empty
think block.
"""
import json

TOOLS = [
    {"type": "function", "function": {"name": n, "description": d, "parameters": {
        "type": "object", "properties": {p: {"type": "string"} for p in ps}, "required": ps[:1]}}}
    for n, d, ps in [
        ("Bash", "Run a shell command.", ["command", "description"]),
        ("Read", "Read a file from disk.", ["file_path"]),
        ("Edit", "Replace old_string with new_string in a file.", ["file_path", "old_string", "new_string"]),
        ("Write", "Write a file.", ["file_path", "content"]),
        ("Grep", "Search file contents.", ["pattern", "path"]),
    ]
]
SYSTEM = ("You are Claude Code, an interactive CLI coding agent. Use the tools to read, "
          "edit and run code in the user's project. Be concise.")
CUT = {"edit": "old_string", "write": "content", "bash": "command"}


def render(sample, tokenizer, thinking=False):
    msgs = [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM}] + sample["messages"]
    if thinking:  # plain prompt with thinking on: the model writes its reasoning first, no forced prefix
        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(msgs, tools=TOOLS, add_generation_prompt=True, tokenize=False,
                                             enable_thinking=True)
        return tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False), ""
    prompt = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(msgs, tools=TOOLS, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                           tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False)
    full = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(msgs + [sample["target"]], tools=TOOLS,
                                         tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False)
    # The template renders past assistant turns without thinking, so the target
    # renders as plain text after the generation prompt (which, with thinking
    # off, already contains the empty think block).
    target = full[len(prompt.rstrip()) :] if full.startswith(prompt.rstrip()) else None
    if target is None:
        raise ValueError("template prefix mismatch")
    kind = sample["kind"]
    if kind in CUT:
        marker = f"<parameter={CUT[kind]}>\n"
        j = target.find(marker)
        if j < 0:
            raise ValueError("marker not found")
        prefix = target[: j + len(marker)]
    else:
        prefix = ""
    text = prompt.rstrip() + prefix if prefix else prompt
    ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)
    return ids, target[len(prefix):] if prefix else target


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    from mlx_lm.tokenizer_utils import load as load_tok
    from pathlib import Path
    tok = load_tok(Path(sys.argv[1]))
    for line in open("data/samples.jsonl"):
        s = json.loads(line)
        try:
            ids, rest = render(s, tok)
        except ValueError as e:
            print(s["id"], "ERR", e)
            continue
        print(s["id"], len(ids), repr(tok.decode(ids[-40:]))[:160], "| rest:", repr(rest[:60]))


### SpecDecode/replay.py
"""Replay a long real Claude Code session through an Anthropic-compatible server, to see how
prefill per turn and decode tok/s behave as the context grows (attention + KV reads grow).

Each request = the transcript's conversation up to an assistant turn (history is the real one, not
the model's own output), streamed with max_tokens capped. Requests are sampled every `step`
assistant turns until the rendered context reaches `max_chars`. Prints one line per request.
  replay.py URL [max_chars] [step] [max_tokens]
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import sys
import time
import urllib.request

from extract import load_session

URL = sys.argv[1]
MAX_CHARS = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 330_000
STEP = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 12
MAX_TOK = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 256
TOOLS = [{"name": n, "description": d, "input_schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {p: {"type": "string"} for p in ps}}}
         for n, d, ps in [("Bash", "Run a shell command.", ["command", "description"]),
                          ("Read", "Read a file.", ["file_path"]),
                          ("Edit", "Edit a file.", ["file_path", "old_string", "new_string"]),
                          ("Write", "Write a file.", ["file_path", "content"]),
                          ("Grep", "Search files.", ["pattern", "path"])]]
SYSTEM = "You are Claude Code, an interactive CLI coding agent. Use the tools to read, edit and run code."


def to_anthropic(msgs):
    out, n = [], 0
    for m in msgs:
        if m["role"] == "assistant":
            blocks = [{"type": "text", "text": m["content"]}] if m.get("content") else []
            for c in m.get("tool_calls", []):
                n += 1
                blocks.append({"type": "tool_use", "id": f"t{n}", "name": c["function"]["name"],
                               "input": c["function"]["arguments"]})
            if not blocks:
                blocks = [{"type": "text", "text": "."}]
            out.append({"role": "assistant", "content": blocks})
        elif m["role"] == "tool":
            blk = {"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": f"t{n}", "content": m["content"] or "(empty)"}
            if out and out[-1]["role"] == "user" and isinstance(out[-1]["content"], list):
                out[-1]["content"].append(blk)
            else:
                out.append({"role": "user", "content": [blk]})
        else:
            if out and out[-1]["role"] == "user":
                c = out[-1]["content"]
                out[-1]["content"] = (c if isinstance(c, list) else [{"type": "text", "text": c}]) + \
                    [{"type": "text", "text": m["content"]}]
            else:
                out.append({"role": "user", "content": m["content"]})
    return out


def longest_session():
    best = None
    for f in glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl")):
        if "SpecDecode" in f or os.path.getsize(f) < 2_000_000:
            continue
        msgs = load_session(f)
        size = sum(len(m.get("content") or "") for m in msgs)
        if best is None or size > best[0]:
            best = (size, f, msgs)
    return best


def stream(msgs):
    body = {"model": "x", "max_tokens": MAX_TOK, "temperature": 0, "stream": True, "system": SYSTEM,
            "tools": TOOLS, "thinking": {"type": "disabled"}, "messages": msgs}
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL.rstrip("/") + "/v1/messages", json.dumps(body).encode(),
                                 {"content-type": "application/json", "x-api-key": "local"})
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    first, n = None, 0
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=1200) as r:
        for raw in r:
            line = raw.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
            if not line.startswith("data:"):
                continue
            d = json.loads(line[5:])
            if d.get("type") == "content_block_delta" and first is None:
                first = time.perf_counter()
            if d.get("type") == "message_delta":
                n = d.get("usage", {}).get("output_tokens", 0)
    t1 = time.perf_counter()
    return (first or t1) - t0, n, (n - 1) / (t1 - first) if first and t1 > first and n > 1 else 0


def main():
    size, f, msgs = longest_session()
    print(f"session {os.path.basename(f)[:8]}…: {len(msgs)} messages, {size/1e6:.1f}M chars of content", flush=True)
    a_idx = [i for i, m in enumerate(msgs) if m["role"] == "assistant" and i > 0]
    chars = 0
    for k, i in enumerate(a_idx):
        chars = sum(len(m.get("content") or "") + len(json.dumps(m.get("tool_calls", ""))) for m in msgs[:i])
        if chars > MAX_CHARS:
            break
        if k % STEP:
            continue
        hist = to_anthropic(msgs[:i])
        if not hist or hist[0]["role"] != "user":
            continue
        ttft, n, tps = stream(hist)
        print(f"turn {k:4d}  context ~{chars/3.4/1000:5.1f}K tok  ttft {ttft:6.2f}s  out {n:4d}  decode {tps:6.1f} tok/s",
              flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### SpecDecode/roofline.py
"""Roofline for one decode step of Qwen3.6-35B-A3B.

  roofline.py bytes                 bytes read per token, from safetensors headers (no load)
  roofline.py bw                    measured GPU memory bandwidth (read-only and copy)
  roofline.py profile MODEL [CTX]   time breakdown of one decode step at S=1
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import struct
import sys
import time

MODELS = {
    "Q8": "~/.lmstudio/models/unsloth/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-8bit",
    "4bit": "~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit",
}


def tensor_sizes(path):
    out = {}
    for f in glob.glob(os.path.join(os.path.expanduser(path), "*.safetensors")):
        with open(f, "rb") as fh:
            n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]
            hdr = json.loads(fh.read(n))
        for k, v in hdr.items():
            if k != "__metadata__":
                out[k] = (v["data_offsets"][1] - v["data_offsets"][0], v["shape"])
    return out


def bytes_per_token(path, ctx=10000):
    cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(os.path.expanduser(path), "config.json")))
    t = cfg.get("text_config", cfg)
    E, k, L = t["num_experts"], t["num_experts_per_tok"], t["num_hidden_layers"]
    sz = tensor_sizes(path)
    cat = {"experts (all)": 0, "shared expert": 0, "router": 0, "linear-attn": 0,
           "full-attn": 0, "embed (1 row)": 0, "lm_head": 0, "norms/other": 0, "vision (unused)": 0}
    for key, (b, shape) in sz.items():
        if "vision" in key or "visual" in key:
            cat["vision (unused)"] += b
        elif "switch_mlp" in key or ".experts." in key:
            cat["experts (all)"] += b
        elif "shared_expert" in key:
            cat["shared expert"] += b
        elif key.endswith("mlp.gate.weight") or "mlp.gate." in key:
            cat["router"] += b
        elif "linear_attn" in key:
            cat["linear-attn"] += b
        elif "self_attn" in key:
            cat["full-attn"] += b
        elif "embed_tokens" in key:
            cat["embed (1 row)"] += b / t["vocab_size"]
        elif "lm_head" in key:
            cat["lm_head"] += b
        else:
            cat["norms/other"] += b
    per_expert = cat["experts (all)"] / (E * L)
    n_full = sum(1 for x in t.get("layer_types", []) if x == "full_attention") or L // 4
    kv_dim = t["num_key_value_heads"] * t["head_dim"]
    kv = n_full * 2 * kv_dim * 2 * ctx  # bf16 K and V over the context
    n_lin = L - n_full
    lin_state = n_lin * t["linear_num_value_heads"] * t["linear_value_head_dim"] * t["linear_key_head_dim"] * 4 * 2  # f32 read+write
    weights_tok = (sum(v for c, v in cat.items() if c not in ("experts (all)", "vision (unused)"))
                   + k * L * per_expert)
    return {"per_expert_MB": per_expert / 1e6, "active_experts_GB": k * L * per_expert / 1e9,
            "dense_part_GB": (weights_tok - k * L * per_expert) / 1e9,
            "kv_GB@ctx": kv / 1e9, "linear_state_GB": lin_state / 1e9,
            "total_GB": (weights_tok + kv + lin_state) / 1e9, "cat_GB": {c: v / 1e9 for c, v in cat.items()},
            "E": E, "k": k, "L": L, "ctx": ctx}


def bandwidth():
    import mlx.core as mx
    x = mx.random.uniform(shape=(1024 * 1024 * 1024,)).astype(mx.float32)  # 4 GB
    mx.eval(x)
    res = {}
    for name, fn, nbytes in [("read (sum)", lambda: mx.sum(x), x.nbytes),
                             ("copy", lambda: x + 0, 2 * x.nbytes)]:
        ts = []
        for _ in range(7):
            t = time.perf_counter()
            mx.eval(fn())
            ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
        res[name] = nbytes / sorted(ts)[3] / 1e9
    return res


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "bytes":
        for q, p in MODELS.items():
            r = bytes_per_token(p)
            print(f"{q}: per expert {r['per_expert_MB']:.2f} MB, active experts {r['active_experts_GB']:.2f} GB, "
                  f"dense part {r['dense_part_GB']:.2f} GB, KV@{r['ctx']} {r['kv_GB@ctx']:.2f} GB, "
                  f"linear state {r['linear_state_GB']:.2f} GB  => {r['total_GB']:.2f} GB/token")
            print("    " + ", ".join(f"{c} {v:.2f}" for c, v in r["cat_GB"].items()))
    elif sys.argv[1] == "bw":
        print({k: f"{v:.0f} GB/s" for k, v in bandwidth().items()})


def profile(path, ctx=10000):
    """Time each component of one S=1 decode step separately (each group = one graph)."""
    import mlx.core as mx
    from render import render
    from specdec import Model
    m = Model(os.path.expanduser(path))
    tm = m.model.language_model.model if hasattr(m.model, "language_model") else m.model.model
    head = m.model.language_model.lm_head if hasattr(m.model, "language_model") else m.model.lm_head
    s = next(json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl") if json.loads(l)["id"] == "edit03")
    ids, _ = render(s, m.tokenizer)
    ids = ids[-ctx:]
    m.prefill(ids[:-1])
    snap = m.snapshot()
    # capture per-layer inputs for one real step
    xs, hs = [], []
    h = tm.embed_tokens(mx.array([[ids[-1]]]))
    for layer, c in zip(tm.layers, m.cache):
        xs.append(h)
        mix = layer.linear_attn if layer.is_linear else layer.self_attn
        r = mix(layer.input_layernorm(h), None, c)
        h = h + r
        hs.append(layer.post_attention_layernorm(h))
        h = h + layer.mlp(hs[-1])
    final = tm.norm(h)
    mx.eval(xs, hs, final)

    def timeit(fn, reps=15):
        ts = []
        for _ in range(reps):
            m.restore(snap)
            mx.synchronize()
            t = time.perf_counter()
            mx.eval(fn())
            ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
        return sorted(ts)[reps // 2] * 1e3

    L = tm.layers
    lin = [i for i, l in enumerate(L) if l.is_linear]
    full = [i for i, l in enumerate(L) if not l.is_linear]

    def router(i):
        mlp = L[i].mlp
        g = mx.softmax(mlp.gate(hs[i]), axis=-1, precise=True)
        return mx.argpartition(g, kth=-8, axis=-1)[..., -8:]
    inds = [router(i) for i in range(len(L))]
    mx.eval(inds)
    parts = {
        "full step (model fwd + argmax)": lambda: mx.argmax(m.model(mx.array([[ids[-1]]]), cache=m.cache), -1),
        "linear-attn mixers (30)": lambda: [L[i].linear_attn(L[i].input_layernorm(xs[i]), None, m.cache[i]) for i in lin],
        "full-attn mixers (10)": lambda: [L[i].self_attn(L[i].input_layernorm(xs[i]), None, m.cache[i]) for i in full],
        "router+topk (40)": lambda: [router(i) for i in range(len(L))],
        "routed experts gather_qmm (40)": lambda: [L[i].mlp.switch_mlp(hs[i], inds[i]) for i in range(len(L))],
        "shared expert (40)": lambda: [L[i].mlp.shared_expert(hs[i]) for i in range(len(L))],
        "lm_head + argmax": lambda: mx.argmax(head(final), -1),
    }
    res = {k: timeit(f) for k, f in parts.items()}
    tot = res.pop("full step (model fwd + argmax)")
    print(f"ctx {len(ids)}  full step {tot:.2f} ms ({1e3/tot:.0f} tok/s)")
    for k, v in res.items():
        print(f"  {k:34s} {v:6.2f} ms")
    print(f"  {'sum of parts':34s} {sum(res.values()):6.2f} ms   (rest = norms/residual/glue/launch)")
    # kernel count for one step
    import tempfile
    m.restore(snap)
    out = mx.argmax(m.model(mx.array([[ids[-1]]]), cache=m.cache), -1)
    f = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".dot", delete=False).name
    mx.export_to_dot(f, out)
    import re
    from collections import Counter
    ops = Counter(re.findall(r'label ="(\w+)", shape=rectangle', open(f).read()))
    views = {"Broadcast", "Reshape", "Squeeze", "ExpandDims", "Transpose", "Slice", "StopGradient", "Depends"}
    print(f"  primitives per step: {sum(ops.values())}, excluding views: "
          f"{sum(v for k, v in ops.items() if k not in views)}; top: {ops.most_common(8)}")


if __name__ == "__main__" and sys.argv[1] == "profile":
    profile(MODELS.get(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 10000)


### SpecDecode/routing.py
"""Record MoE routing (top-8 experts per layer) for prefill and greedy decode.

  routing.py record MODEL OUTDIR [ids...]   writes OUTDIR/<id>.npz: prompt routing, gen routing, tokens
  routing.py analyze OUTDIR                 union sizes (copied vs fresh), copy-vs-source routing match

A generated token counts as "copied" when the longest earlier match of the
current suffix (3..8-gram, most recent) predicts it; its "source" is the
position that match points at. That is exactly the token a prompt-lookup draft
would propose there.
"""
import json
import os
import sys

import numpy as np

REC = None


def install_hook():
    import mlx.core as mx
    from mlx_lm.models import qwen3_next

    orig = qwen3_next.Qwen3NextSparseMoeBlock.__call__

    def call(self, x):
        if REC is not None:
            g = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
            REC.append(mx.argpartition(g, kth=-self.top_k, axis=-1)[..., -self.top_k:])
        return orig(self, x)

    qwen3_next.Qwen3NextSparseMoeBlock.__call__ = call


def record(model_path, outdir, ids_wanted, max_new=256):
    global REC
    import mlx.core as mx
    install_hook()
    from render import render
    from specdec import Model
    m = Model(model_path)
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    samples = [json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl")]
    for s in samples:
        if ids_wanted and s["id"] not in ids_wanted:
            continue
        ids, _ = render(s, m.tokenizer)
        m.reset()
        prompt_r = []
        for i in range(0, len(ids) - 1, 2048):
            REC = []
            m.model(mx.array(ids[i : min(i + 2048, len(ids) - 1)])[None], cache=m.cache)
            mx.eval([c.state for c in m.cache], REC)
            prompt_r.append(np.stack([np.array(r[0]) for r in REC], axis=1))  # [chunk, L, 8]
            mx.clear_cache()
        y, toks, gen_r = ids[-1], [], []
        for _ in range(max_new):
            REC = []
            g = m.forward([y])
            gen_r.append(np.stack([np.array(r[0, 0]) for r in REC], axis=0))  # [L, 8]
            y = g[0]
            toks.append(y)
            if y in m.eos:
                break
        REC = None
        # routing of position j is for the token at ids[j]; gen_r[t] is for the token fed at step t
        # (ids[-1] at t=0, then toks[t-1]); so the history routing array aligns with ids + toks[:-1]
        np.savez_compressed(f"{outdir}/{s['id']}.npz", prompt=np.concatenate(prompt_r).astype(np.uint8),
                            gen=np.stack(gen_r).astype(np.uint8), ids=np.array(ids), toks=np.array(toks))
        print(s["id"], len(ids), "prompt tokens,", len(toks), "generated", flush=True)


def copy_sources(hist, start, nmax=8, nmin=3):
    """For each position t >= start in hist, the source position whose token a
    lookup draft would propose for t (or -1)."""
    idx = [dict() for _ in range(nmax + 1)]
    src = np.full(len(hist), -1)
    for t in range(1, len(hist)):
        # registration of n-grams ending at t-2 -> continuation t-1 happens before lookup at t
        for n in range(nmin, nmax + 1):
            if t - 1 >= n:
                idx[n][tuple(hist[t - 1 - n : t - 1])] = t - 1
        if t >= start:
            for n in range(nmax, nmin - 1, -1):
                if t >= n:
                    p = idx[n].get(tuple(hist[t - n : t]))
                    if p is not None:
                        if hist[p] == hist[t]:
                            src[t] = p
                        break
    return src


def analyze(outdir):
    rows = {"copied": {k: [] for k in range(1, 9)}, "fresh": {k: [] for k in range(1, 9)}}
    match_src, match_rand, match_prev = [], [], []
    srcwin = {k: [] for k in (2, 4, 8)}
    per_kind = {}
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    for f in sorted(os.listdir(outdir)):
        d = np.load(os.path.join(outdir, f))
        prompt, gen, ids, toks = d["prompt"], d["gen"], list(d["ids"]), list(d["toks"])
        P = len(ids) - 1
        # routing per history position: prompt positions 0..P-1, then gen step t routes token hist[P+t]
        route = np.concatenate([prompt, gen])            # [P + T, L, 8]
        hist = ids + toks[:-1]                             # token at each routed position
        assert len(hist) == len(route)
        src = copy_sources(hist, P + 1)
        L = route.shape[1]
        # routing sets as bitmasks per (pos, layer): 256 experts -> 4 x uint64
        onehot = np.zeros((len(route), L, 256), dtype=bool)
        np.put_along_axis(onehot, route.astype(np.int64), True, axis=2)
        gen_pos = np.arange(P, len(route))
        is_copy = src[gen_pos] >= 0
        kind = f[:-4].rstrip("0123456789")
        per_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(is_copy.mean())
        for k in range(1, 9):
            for s0 in range(0, len(gen_pos) - k + 1, 1):
                w = gen_pos[s0 : s0 + k]
                c = is_copy[s0 : s0 + k]
                lab = "copied" if c.all() else ("fresh" if not c.any() else None)
                if lab:
                    rows[lab][k].append(onehot[w].any(axis=0).sum(axis=-1).mean())
        for t in gen_pos[is_copy]:
            s = src[t]
            match_src.append((onehot[t] & onehot[s]).sum(-1).mean() / 8)
            r = rng.integers(0, P)
            match_rand.append((onehot[t] & onehot[r]).sum(-1).mean() / 8)
            match_prev.append((onehot[t] & onehot[t - 1]).sum(-1).mean() / 8)
        # does the union of the sources' routing cover the union of a copied window?
        for k in srcwin:
            for s0 in range(0, len(gen_pos) - k + 1):
                c = is_copy[s0 : s0 + k]
                if c.all():
                    w = gen_pos[s0 : s0 + k]
                    true_u = onehot[w].any(axis=0)
                    pred_u = onehot[src[w]].any(axis=0)
                    srcwin[k].append(((true_u & pred_u).sum(-1) / true_u.sum(-1)).mean())
    print("share of generated tokens that a lookup draft predicts (copied):",
          {k: f"{np.mean(v):.0%}" for k, v in per_kind.items()})
    print("\nmean distinct experts per layer for k consecutive generated tokens (8*k if no overlap)")
    print("   k   copied (n)        fresh (n)")
    for k in range(1, 9):
        c, fr = rows["copied"][k], rows["fresh"][k]
        print(f"  {k:2d}   {np.mean(c) if c else float('nan'):6.1f} ({len(c):5d})   "
              f"{np.mean(fr) if fr else float('nan'):6.1f} ({len(fr):5d})")
    print(f"\ncopied token vs its source position: {np.mean(match_src):.1%} of the 8 experts match per layer "
          f"(random prompt position: {np.mean(match_rand):.1%}, previous token: {np.mean(match_prev):.1%})")
    for k, v in srcwin.items():
        print(f"  window of {k} copied tokens: sources' routing covers {np.mean(v):.1%} of the true union")
    json.dump({"union": {lab: {k: float(np.mean(v)) if v else None for k, v in r.items()} for lab, r in rows.items()},
               "match_src": float(np.mean(match_src)), "match_rand": float(np.mean(match_rand)),
               "match_prev": float(np.mean(match_prev)),
               "src_cover": {k: float(np.mean(v)) for k, v in srcwin.items()}},
              open(os.path.join("results", "routing_" + os.path.basename(outdir.rstrip("/")) + ".json"), "w"), indent=1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "record":
        record(os.path.expanduser(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3], set(sys.argv[4:]))
    else:
        analyze(sys.argv[2])


### SpecDecode/scratch_numerics.py
import json, sys
import mlx.core as mx
from render import render
from specdec import Model
m = Model(sys.argv[1])
s = next(json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl") if json.loads(l)["id"] == sys.argv[2])
ids, _ = render(s, m.tokenizer)
m.prefill(ids[:-1]); snap = m.snapshot()
# sequential greedy with logits
y = ids[-1]; seq = []; seq_logits = []
for i in range(24):
    lg = m.model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=m.cache)[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)
    y = int(mx.argmax(lg)); seq.append(y); seq_logits.append(lg)
# batched verify of the true continuation
m.restore(snap)
lg = m.model(mx.array([[ids[-1]] + seq[:-1]]), cache=m.cache)[0].astype(mx.float32)
am = mx.argmax(lg, -1).tolist()
diffs = [float(mx.max(mx.abs(lg[i] - seq_logits[i]))) for i in range(24)]
print("batched argmax mismatches:", sum(a != b for a, b in zip(am, seq)), "max|dlogit| per pos:", [round(d, 3) for d in diffs[:12]])
# top-2 margin where mismatched
for i,(a,b) in enumerate(zip(am,seq)):
    if a!=b:
        top=mx.sort(seq_logits[i])[-2:].tolist(); print(" pos",i,"seq margin",round(top[1]-top[0],4))
# rollback test: wrong drafts, keep 1+n, then continue sequentially
for n in (0, 3, 7):
    m.restore(snap)
    drafts = seq[:n] + [0] * (8 - n)
    m.forward([ids[-1]] + drafts); m.keep(9, n + 1)
    y = seq[n]; out = seq[:n + 1]
    for i in range(24 - n - 1):
        lg2 = m.model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=m.cache)[0, -1]; y = int(mx.argmax(lg2)); out.append(y)
    print(f"rollback keep {n+1}: identical={out == seq}", "first diff", next((i for i in range(24) if out[i] != seq[i]), None))


### SpecDecode/scratch_prof.py
import json, sys, time
import mlx.core as mx
import specdec
from render import render
from specdec import Model
m = Model(sys.argv[1])
s = next(json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl") if json.loads(l)["id"] == "edit03")

m.prefill(ids[:-1]); snap = m.snapshot()
def t(fn, reps=8):
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        m.restore(snap); x = time.perf_counter(); fn(); mx.eval([c.state for c in m.cache]); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - x)
    return sorted(ts)[reps // 2] * 1e3
toks = ids[-9:]
for S in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8):
    raw = t(lambda: mx.eval(m.model(mx.array(toks[:S])[None], cache=m.cache)))
    fwd = t(lambda: m.forward(toks[:S]))

    print(f"ctx {len(ids)} S={S}: raw model {raw:6.1f} ms  +argmax/capture {fwd:6.1f}  +rollback {fk:6.1f}")


### SpecDecode/sdpa_band2.py
"""Band check for specdec._sdpa_padded vs plain SDPA (16q/2kv, D 256, 4K keys), rows 1..17."""
import os
os.environ["SPEC_SDPAPAD"] = "1"
import mlx.core as mx
import numpy as np
import specdec

q = mx.random.normal((1, 16, M, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
rows = {}
for m in range(1, M + 1):
    y = specdec._sdpa_padded(q[:, :, :m], k[:, :, :L0 + m], v[:, :, :L0 + m], None, 0.0625, "causal" if m > 1 else None)

b, cur = [], [1]
for m in range(2, M + 1):

    else: b.append(cur); cur = [m]
b.append(cur)
print("padded SDPA row-0 bands:", " ".join(f"{{{x[0]}-{x[-1]}}}" for x in b))
import time
for m in (1, 3, 8):
    ts = []
    for _ in range(9):
        t = time.perf_counter(); [mx.eval(specdec._sdpa_padded(q[:, :, :m], k[:, :, :L0 + m], v[:, :, :L0 + m], None, 0.0625, "causal")) for _ in range(10)]; ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
    ts2 = []
    for _ in range(9):
        t = time.perf_counter(); [mx.eval(mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(q[:, :, :m], k[:, :, :L0 + m], v[:, :, :L0 + m], scale=0.0625, mask="causal" if m > 1 else None)) for _ in range(10)]; ts2.append(time.perf_counter() - t)

### SpecDecode/sdpa_mask.py
"""Prefill-chunk SDPA (q=2048, head_dim 256, 16/2 heads) with mask="causal" vs an explicit boolean
array mask vs no mask, as key length grows. 10 calls = 10 layers.
"""
import time
import mlx.core as mx


def t(f, reps=3):
    for _ in range(2):
        mx.eval(f())
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        a = time.perf_counter(); mx.eval(f()); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[reps // 2] * 1e3


print("  keys   causal(str)   bool-array mask   no mask   (ms for 10 layers; TFLOPS of the best)")
for L in (16384, 49152, 98304):
    k = mx.random.normal((1, 2, L, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    v = mx.random.normal((1, 2, L, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    q = mx.random.normal((1, 16, 2048, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    off = L - 2048
    m = (mx.arange(L)[None, :] <= (mx.arange(2048)[:, None] + off))
    mx.eval(k, v, q, m)
    a = t(lambda: [mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, scale=0.0625, mask="causal") for _ in range(10)])
    b = t(lambda: [mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, scale=0.0625, mask=m) for _ in range(10)])
    c = t(lambda: [mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, scale=0.0625) for _ in range(10)])
    fl = 10 * 2 * 2 * 16 * 2048 * L * 256
    print(f"  {L:6d}   {a:9.1f}     {b:9.1f}       {c:9.1f}    best {fl / min(a, b, c) / 1e9:5.1f} TFLOPS", flush=True)


### SpecDecode/sdpa_micro.py
"""Time MLX SDPA with Qwen3.6's full-attention shape (16 q heads, 2 kv heads, head_dim 256) as the
key length grows: decode (q=1) and a prefill chunk (q=2048, causal), bf16. 10 calls = 10 layers.
"""
import time

import mlx.core as mx


def t(f, reps=5):
    for _ in range(2):
        mx.eval(f())
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        a = time.perf_counter()
        mx.eval(f())
        ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[reps // 2] * 1e3


print("  keys    decode q=1 (ms, x10 layers)   prefill q=2048 causal (ms, x10 layers)")
for L in (8192, 16384, 32768, 40960, 45056, 49152, 65536, 98304):
    k = mx.random.normal((1, 2, L, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    v = mx.random.normal((1, 2, L, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    q1 = mx.random.normal((1, 16, 1, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    qc = mx.random.normal((1, 16, 2048, 256)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    mx.eval(k, v, q1, qc)
    d = t(lambda: [mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(q1, k, v, scale=0.0625) for _ in range(10)])
    p = t(lambda: [mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(qc, k, v, scale=0.0625, mask="causal") for _ in range(10)], reps=3)
    print(f"  {L:6d}   {d:8.2f}                      {p:9.1f}", flush=True)


### SpecDecode/server.py
"""Small local server speaking the Anthropic Messages API, decoding with
prompt-lookup-else-MTP speculative decoding (greedy) on a Qwen3.6 MLX model.

  .venv/bin/python server.py [--model PATH] [--port 1235]

Point Claude Code at it:
  ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:1235 ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN=local ANTHROPIC_MODEL=qwen3.6-spec claude

- POST /v1/messages (stream or not), POST /v1/messages/count_tokens, GET /v1/models
- tools: Anthropic tools -> the model's chat template; <tool_call> output -> tool_use blocks
- prefix cache: four slots (a new session sharing only the tools block forks it, no copy;
  eviction drops the slot with the fewest tokens not held elsewhere), checkpoints of the recurrent (GatedDeltaNet) state every 4096 tokens
  and at prompt/generation ends, so a new turn only prefills what changed
- decoding is greedy (temperature is ignored); thinking if the request enables it or --thinking
- session-independent prompt prefixes (<= 32K tokens: the tools block, the auto-mode classifier
  prompt) are saved to data/prefix_cache/ and reloaded at start
- per-request numbers (no content) go to results/server_log.jsonl
"""
import argparse
import concurrent.futures
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

import mlx.core as mx

mx.set_cache_limit(4 << 30)  # don't let freed buffers pile up next to a 37 GB model
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx_lm.models import cache as cache_mod

from mtp import install_capture, load_mtp
from specdec import LookupMTP, Model, MTPDrafter, PromptLookup, generate_iter

CKPT_EVERY = 4096
CHUNK = 2048


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- engine
class Slot:
    def __init__(self, model, mtp):
        self.cache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(model)
        self.mcache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(mtp)
        self.seq = []          # tokens currently in the cache
        self.ckpts = {}        # offset -> arrays snapshot (KV is trimmed, recurrent state restored)
        self.used = 0.0


def arrays_snapshot(cache):
    return [None if isinstance(c, cache_mod.KVCache) else list(c.cache) for c in cache]


class Engine:
    def __init__(self, path, draft_vocab=100000, k_mtp=2, slots=4):
        # MLX streams are per thread: every MLX call runs on this one worker thread
        self.worker = concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
        self.worker.submit(self._init, path, draft_vocab, k_mtp, slots).result()

    def _init(self, path, draft_vocab, k_mtp, slots):
        self.m = Model(path)
        self.m.cap = install_capture(self.m.model)
        self.mtp = load_mtp(self.m.model, bits=8)  # 8-bit MTP layer: +5.5 % vs bf16 (FINDINGS §18)
        lm = self.m.model.language_model
        h = lm.lm_head
        dh = nn.QuantizedLinear.__new__(nn.QuantizedLinear)
        nn.Module.__init__(dh)
        dh.group_size, dh.bits, dh.mode = h.group_size, h.bits, getattr(h, "mode", "affine")
        dh.weight, dh.scales = mx.contiguous(h.weight[:draft_vocab]), mx.contiguous(h.scales[:draft_vocab])
        if "biases" in h:
            dh.biases = mx.contiguous(h.biases[:draft_vocab])
        dh.freeze()
        mx.eval(dh.parameters())
        self.draft_head, self.k_mtp = dh, k_mtp
        self.embed = lm.model.embed_tokens
        self.slots = [Slot(self.m.model, self.mtp) for _ in range(slots)]
        self.forks = 0
        self._warmup()
        self.load_prefix()

    def _warmup(self):
        """Compile the kernels for a prefill chunk and for 1-17-token verify passes, and touch
        every weight, before the first real request (otherwise it pays several seconds)."""
        t = time.perf_counter()
        trunk = self.m.model.language_model.model
        c = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.m.model)
        mc = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.mtp)
        ids = mx.array([[100 + (i % 5000) for i in range(CHUNK)]])
        trunk(ids, cache=c)
        x = self.mtp(self.m.cap.post, self.embed(ids), mc)
        mx.eval([s.state for s in c], x)
        self.m.cache = c
        for S in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 9, 17):
            self.m.forward([7] * S)
            self.m.keep(S, 1) if S > 1 else None
        h = self.draft_head(x[:, -1:])
        mx.eval(h)
        mx.clear_cache()
        print(f"warm-up {time.perf_counter() - t:.1f}s", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        self.tok = self.m.tokenizer

    # -- prefix cache -------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _lcp(a, b):
        n = min(len(a), len(b))
        i = 0
        while i < n and a[i] == b[i]:
            i += 1
        return i

    def _pick(self, ids):
        """Best slot by shared prefix. If that slot holds a different, longer conversation
        (e.g. a new Claude Code session sharing only the ~12K-token tools block), fork its
        checkpoint into the least recently used slot instead of overwriting it."""
        scored = sorted(((self._lcp(s.seq, ids), s) for s in self.slots), key=lambda x: -x[0])
        best_l, best = scored[0]
        if best_l < 256:
            victim = self._victim(exclude=())
            return victim, self._lcp(victim.seq, ids)
        diverged = len(best.seq) - best_l > 512  # best holds a different continuation
        if diverged:
            target = self._victim(exclude=(best,))
            cands = [o for o in best.ckpts if o <= min(best_l, len(ids) - 2)]
            if cands and max(cands) > self._lcp(target.seq, ids) + 512:
                self._fork(best, target, max(cands))
                return target, max(cands)
        return best, best_l

    def _victim(self, exclude):
        """Slot to overwrite: the one whose loss costs the least re-prefill, i.e. the fewest
        tokens not also held by another slot (ties: least recently used)."""
        def unique(s):
            others = [self._lcp(s.seq, o.seq) for o in self.slots if o is not s]
            return len(s.seq) - max(others, default=0)
        cands = [s for s in self.slots if s not in exclude]
        return min(cands, key=lambda s: (unique(s), s.used))

    def _fork(self, src, dst, off):
        """dst := src truncated to its checkpoint at off. The KV prefix is a slice of src's
        buffers (lazy, no copy up front); dst's first write reallocates, src is untouched."""
        dst.cache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.m.model)
        for d, c, arrs in zip(dst.cache, src.cache, src.ckpts[off]):
            if arrs is None:
                # own array objects (slice to off): KVCache writes via slice assignment
                d.keys, d.values, d.offset = c.keys[..., :off, :], c.values[..., :off, :], off
            else:
                d.cache = list(arrs)
        dst.mcache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.mtp)
        mc, sm = dst.mcache[0], src.mcache[0]
        mc.keys, mc.values, mc.offset = sm.keys[..., :off, :], sm.values[..., :off, :], off
        dst.seq = src.seq[:off]
        dst.ckpts = {o: a for o, a in src.ckpts.items() if o <= off}
        self.forks += 1

    PREFIX_DIR = "data/prefix_cache"
    MAX_PREFIXES = 4

    def save_prefix(self, slot):
        """Persist the slot's last 4096-aligned checkpoint <= 32768 tokens, one file per distinct
        prompt head. Claude Code's tools block (~12.7K tokens) and its auto-mode classifier prompt
        (~31K) are identical across sessions, so a restarted server skips that prefill."""
        offs = [o for o in slot.ckpts if 4096 <= o <= 32768 and o % 4096 == 0]
        if not offs:
            return
        off = max(offs)
        key = tuple(slot.seq[:off])
        saved = getattr(self, "_saved", set())
        if key in saved:
            return
        out = {"tokens": mx.array(slot.seq[:off], dtype=mx.int32)}
        for i, c in enumerate(slot.cache):
            if isinstance(c, cache_mod.KVCache):
                out[f"k{i}"], out[f"v{i}"] = c.keys[..., :off, :], c.values[..., :off, :]
        for o in offs:  # recurrent state at every aligned checkpoint, so shorter prompts can resume too
            for i, arrs in enumerate(slot.ckpts[o]):
                if arrs is not None:
                    out[f"conv{i}@{o}"], out[f"state{i}@{o}"] = arrs[0], arrs[1]
        mc = slot.mcache[0]
        out["mk"], out["mv"] = mc.keys[..., :off, :], mc.values[..., :off, :]
        os.makedirs(self.PREFIX_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        head = f"{abs(hash(tuple(slot.seq[:64]))) % 10**8:08d}"
        # a longer prefix with the same head replaces the shorter one
        mx.save_safetensors(f"{self.PREFIX_DIR}/{head}.safetensors", out, metadata={"model": self.m.path})
        saved.add(key)
        self._saved = saved
        files = sorted((os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(self.PREFIX_DIR, f)), f) for f in os.listdir(self.PREFIX_DIR))
        for _, f in files[: max(0, len(files) - self.MAX_PREFIXES)]:
            os.remove(os.path.join(self.PREFIX_DIR, f))

    def load_prefix(self):
        if not os.path.isdir(self.PREFIX_DIR):
            return
        files = sorted(os.listdir(self.PREFIX_DIR), key=lambda f: -os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(self.PREFIX_DIR, f)))
        for slot, f in zip(self.slots, files):
            w, meta = mx.load(os.path.join(self.PREFIX_DIR, f), return_metadata=True)
            if meta.get("model") != self.m.path:
                continue
            toks = w["tokens"].tolist()
            off = len(toks)
            slot.cache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.m.model)
            offs = sorted({int(k.split("@")[1]) for k in w if "@" in k})
            for i, c in enumerate(slot.cache):
                if f"k{i}" in w:
                    c.keys, c.values, c.offset = w[f"k{i}"], w[f"v{i}"], off
                else:
                    c.cache = [w[f"conv{i}@{off}"], w[f"state{i}@{off}"]]
            slot.mcache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.mtp)
            mc = slot.mcache[0]
            mc.keys, mc.values, mc.offset = w["mk"], w["mv"], off
            slot.seq = toks
            slot.ckpts = {0: [None if isinstance(c, cache_mod.KVCache) else [None, None] for c in slot.cache]}
            for o in offs:
                slot.ckpts[o] = [None if isinstance(c, cache_mod.KVCache) else [w[f"conv{i}@{o}"], w[f"state{i}@{o}"]]
                                 for i, c in enumerate(slot.cache)]
            slot.used = os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(self.PREFIX_DIR, f))
            self._saved = getattr(self, "_saved", set()) | {tuple(toks)}
            print(f"loaded {off}-token prefix cache ({f})", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)

    def _restore(self, slot, off):
        """Roll the slot back to its checkpoint at `off` (KV trim + recurrent state)."""
        for c, arrs in zip(slot.cache, slot.ckpts[off]):
            if arrs is None:
                c.trim(c.offset - off)
            else:
                c.cache = list(arrs)
        slot.mcache[0].trim(slot.mcache[0].offset - off)
        slot.seq = slot.seq[:off]
        slot.ckpts = {o: a for o, a in slot.ckpts.items() if o <= off}

    def prepare(self, ids):
        """Make a slot hold ids[:-1] (+ MTP pairs). Returns (slot, reused, prefill_s, x_last)."""
        slot, L = self._pick(ids)
        self.last_pick = {"slot": self.slots.index(slot), "lcp": L,
                          "lcps": [self._lcp(s.seq, ids) for s in self.slots], "head": hash(tuple(ids[:64])) % 10**6}
        slot.used = time.time()
        target = len(ids) - 1
        cands = [o for o in slot.ckpts if o <= min(L, target - 1)]
        if cands:
            self._restore(slot, max(cands))
        else:
            slot.cache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.m.model)
            slot.mcache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.mtp)
            slot.seq, slot.ckpts = [], {0: arrays_snapshot(slot.cache)}
        start = len(slot.seq)
        self.m.cache = slot.cache
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        x_last = None
        i = start
        while i < target:
            # chunk boundaries land on checkpoint offsets
            j = min(target, i + CHUNK, (i // CKPT_EVERY + 1) * CKPT_EVERY)
            if i < target - 1 < j:
                j = target - 1  # checkpoint one before the end, so a repeated prompt reuses everything
            self.m.model.language_model.model(mx.array(ids[i:j])[None], cache=slot.cache)  # no lm_head
            x = self.mtp(self.m.cap.post, self.embed(mx.array(ids[i + 1 : j + 1])[None]), slot.mcache)
            x_last = x[:, -1:]
            mx.eval([c.state for c in slot.cache], [c.state for c in slot.mcache], x_last)
            if (j % CKPT_EVERY == 0 and (j <= 32768 or j % (4 * CKPT_EVERY) == 0)) or j >= target - 1:
                # every 4K up to 32K (shared prompt prefixes live there), every 16K beyond:
                # each checkpoint holds the full recurrent state (~63 MB at Q8)
                slot.ckpts[j] = arrays_snapshot(slot.cache)
                # drop stale prompt-end/generation-end checkpoints that aren't 4K-aligned, keep the last 2
                odd = sorted(o for o in slot.ckpts if o % CKPT_EVERY)
                for o in odd[:-2]:
                    del slot.ckpts[o]
            mx.clear_cache()
            i = j
        slot.seq = list(ids[:target])
        return slot, start, time.perf_counter() - t0, x_last

    def generate(self, ids, max_new, on_tokens):
        return self.worker.submit(self._generate, ids, max_new, on_tokens).result()

    def _generate(self, ids, max_new, on_tokens):
        if True:
            slot, reused, t_pre, x_last = self.prepare(ids)
            mtpd = MTPDrafter(self.m, self.mtp, self.k_mtp, self.draft_head)
            mtpd.cache, mtpd.x_last = slot.mcache, x_last
            drafter = LookupMTP(PromptLookup(8, 2, 2, 16, 8), mtpd)
            drafter.xcheck = True  # lookup draft only if the MTP head agrees on token 1 (+9 % on Q8)
            st = {}
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            n = 0
            stopped = False
            if EXACT:  # keep every pass in MLX's bit-stable kernel band (S = 2..4), see FINDINGS 5.4
                inner = drafter.draft
                drafter.draft = lambda: inner()[:3]
            for new in generate_iter(self.m, ids, max_new, drafter, state=st, pad_to=2 if EXACT else 1):
                n += len(new)
                if on_tokens(new) is False:
                    stopped = True
                    break
            dt = time.perf_counter() - t0
            if not stopped:
                slot.seq = slot.seq + st["consumed"]
                slot.ckpts[len(slot.seq)] = arrays_snapshot(slot.cache)
                self.save_prefix(slot)
            else:  # generator abandoned mid-pass: fall back to the prompt-end checkpoint
                self._restore(slot, len(ids) - 1)
                self.save_prefix(slot)
            return {"prompt_tokens": len(ids), "reused": reused, "pick": self.last_pick, "prefill_s": round(t_pre, 3),
                    "gen_tokens": n, "gen_s": round(dt, 3), "tok_s": round(n / dt, 1) if dt else 0,
                    "tok_per_pass": round(n / max(1, st.get("forwards", 1)), 2)}


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- request conversion
def blocks(content):
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return [{"type": "text", "text": content}]
    return content or []


def to_template(req):
    msgs = []
    sysp = req.get("system")
    if sysp:
        msgs.append({"role": "system", "content": sysp if isinstance(sysp, str)
                     else "\n\n".join(b.get("text", "") for b in sysp if b.get("type") == "text")})
    for m in req.get("messages", []):
        if m["role"] == "user":
            text = []
            for b in blocks(m["content"]):
                if b.get("type") == "tool_result":
                    c = b.get("content")
                    if not isinstance(c, str):
                        c = "\n".join(x.get("text", "") if x.get("type") == "text" else "[image omitted]"
                                      for x in (c or []))
                    if b.get("is_error"):
                        c = "Error: " + c
                    msgs.append({"role": "tool", "content": c})
                elif b.get("type") == "text":
                    text.append(b["text"])
                elif b.get("type") == "image":
                    text.append("[image omitted]")
            if text:
                msgs.append({"role": "user", "content": "\n\n".join(text)})
        else:
            text, calls = [], []
            for b in blocks(m["content"]):
                if b.get("type") == "text":
                    text.append(b["text"])
                elif b.get("type") == "tool_use":
                    calls.append({"type": "function", "function": {"name": b["name"], "arguments": b.get("input", {})}})
            a = {"role": "assistant", "content": "".join(text)}
            if calls:
                a["tool_calls"] = calls
            msgs.append(a)
    tools = [{"type": "function", "function": {"name": t["name"], "description": t.get("description", ""),
                                               "parameters": t.get("input_schema", {})}}
             for t in req.get("tools", []) if "name" in t]
    return msgs, tools


TOOL_RE = re.compile(r"<function=([^>\n]+)>(.*?)</function>", re.S)
PARAM_RE = re.compile(r"<parameter=([^>\n]+)>\n?(.*?)\n?</parameter>", re.S)


def parse_tool(body, schemas):
    m = TOOL_RE.search(body)
    if not m:
        return None
    name = m.group(1).strip()
    props = (schemas.get(name) or {}).get("properties", {})
    args = {}
    for k, v in PARAM_RE.findall(m.group(2)):
        k = k.strip()
        typ = (props.get(k) or {}).get("type")
        if typ in ("integer", "number", "boolean", "array", "object") or (typ is None and v[:1] in "[{"):
            try:
                v = json.loads(v)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                pass
        args[k] = v
    return name, args


class OutParser:
    """Splits the decoded stream into thinking / text / tool calls."""
    TAGS = {"think": "</think>", "text": "<tool_call>", "tool": "</tool_call>"}

    def __init__(self, thinking, schemas):
        self.mode = "think" if thinking else "text"
        self.buf, self.schemas = "", schemas

    def feed(self, s, final=False):
        self.buf += s
        ev = []
        while True:
            tag = self.TAGS[self.mode]
            i = self.buf.find(tag)
            if i >= 0:
                chunk, self.buf = self.buf[:i], self.buf[i + len(tag):]
                if self.mode == "think":
                    ev.append(("think", chunk))
                    self.mode, self.buf = "text", self.buf.lstrip("\n")
                elif self.mode == "text":
                    ev.append(("text", chunk))
                    self.mode = "tool"
                else:
                    t = parse_tool(chunk, self.schemas)
                    ev.append(("tool", t) if t else ("text", "<tool_call>" + chunk + "</tool_call>"))
                    self.mode = "text"
                continue
            if self.mode == "tool" and not final:
                break
            keep = 0 if final else next((k for k in range(min(len(tag), len(self.buf)), 0, -1)
                                         if tag.startswith(self.buf[-k:])), 0)
            out, self.buf = self.buf[: len(self.buf) - keep], self.buf[len(self.buf) - keep:]
            if out:
                ev.append(("think" if self.mode == "think" else "text",
                           ("<tool_call>" + out) if self.mode == "tool" else out))
            break
        return ev


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- HTTP
ENGINE: Engine = None
MODEL_NAME = "qwen3.6-spec"
EXACT = False     # --exact: bit-identical to plain decoding in the same band (pads 1-token passes)
THINKING = False  # --thinking: always let the model think first (LM Studio's default for Qwen3.6)
LOG = "results/server_log.jsonl"


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def _json(self, code, obj):
        body = json.dumps(obj).encode()
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def do_GET(self):
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if not self._allowed():
            return
        if path in ("/v1/models", "/v1/models/"):
            return self._json(200, {"data": [{"id": MODEL_NAME, "type": "model", "display_name": MODEL_NAME}],
                                    "has_more": False})
        self._json(404, {"type": "error", "error": {"type": "not_found_error", "message": path}})

    def do_HEAD(self):
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
        self.end_headers()

    def _allowed(self):
        """Block browsers/DNS rebinding: local Host only, and JSON bodies (which force a CORS
        preflight that this server never answers)."""
        host = (self.headers.get("Host") or "").rsplit(":", 1)[0]
        if host not in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost", "[::1]"):
            self._json(403, {"type": "error", "error": {"type": "permission_error", "message": "bad host"}})
            return False
        return True

    def do_POST(self):
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if not self._allowed():
            return
        if not (self.headers.get("Content-Type") or "").startswith("application/json"):
            return self._json(415, {"type": "error", "error": {"type": "invalid_request_error", "message": "json only"}})
        n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        if n > 32 << 20:
            return self._json(413, {"type": "error", "error": {"type": "request_too_large", "message": "too large"}})
        try:
            req = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return self._json(400, {"type": "error", "error": {"type": "invalid_request_error", "message": "bad json"}})
        if path == "/v1/messages/count_tokens":
            ids = self._render(req)
            return self._json(200, {"input_tokens": len(ids)})
        if path != "/v1/messages":
            return self._json(404, {"type": "error", "error": {"type": "not_found_error", "message": path}})
        try:
            self._messages(req)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass

    def _render(self, req):
        msgs, tools = to_template(req)
        thinking = THINKING or (req.get("thinking") or {}).get("type") == "enabled"
        # preserve_thinking: render every past assistant turn the same way. Qwen's template otherwise
        # drops the (empty) think block from turns before the latest user message, which changes the
        # rendered prefix whenever the user speaks and forces a re-prefill of the whole last turn.
        text = ENGINE.tok.apply_chat_template(msgs, tools=tools or None, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                              tokenize=False, enable_thinking=thinking, preserve_thinking=True)
        return ENGINE.tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)

    def _messages(self, req):
        ids = self._render(req)
        if os.environ.get("SPEC_DUMP_DIR") and req.get("stop_sequences"):
            n = len(os.listdir(os.environ["SPEC_DUMP_DIR"]))
            with open(os.path.join(os.environ["SPEC_DUMP_DIR"], f"req{n:02d}.json"), "w") as f:
                json.dump(req, f)
        thinking = THINKING or (req.get("thinking") or {}).get("type") == "enabled"
        schemas = {t["name"]: t.get("input_schema", {}) for t in req.get("tools", []) if "name" in t}
        stream = bool(req.get("stream"))
        max_new = max(1, min(int(req.get("max_tokens", 4096)), 32768))
        stops = req.get("stop_sequences") or []
        mid = "msg_" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]
        parser = OutParser(thinking, schemas)
        detok = ENGINE.tok.detokenizer
        detok.reset()
        blocks_out = []        # final content (non-stream)
        cur = {"type": None}   # currently open streamed block
        idx = [0]
        state = {"stop": None, "text": "", "n": 0}

        def sse(event, data):
            self.wfile.write(f"event: {event}\ndata: {json.dumps(data)}\n\n".encode())

        def close_block():
            if cur["type"] is not None:
                if stream:
                    sse("content_block_stop", {"type": "content_block_stop", "index": idx[0]})
                idx[0] += 1
                cur["type"] = None

        def emit(ev):
            kind, val = ev
            if kind == "think":
                return  # thinking isn't forwarded (no signature to give it)
            if kind == "text":
                if cur["type"] != "text":
                    if not val.strip():
                        return
                    close_block()
                    val = val.lstrip("\n") if not blocks_out else val
                    cur["type"] = "text"
                    blocks_out.append({"type": "text", "text": ""})
                    if stream:
                        sse("content_block_start", {"type": "content_block_start", "index": idx[0],
                                                    "content_block": {"type": "text", "text": ""}})
                blocks_out[-1]["text"] += val
                if stream and val:
                    sse("content_block_delta", {"type": "content_block_delta", "index": idx[0],
                                                "delta": {"type": "text_delta", "text": val}})
            else:
                name, args = val
                close_block()
                tid = "toolu_" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]
                blocks_out.append({"type": "tool_use", "id": tid, "name": name, "input": args})
                state["tool"] = True
                if stream:
                    sse("content_block_start", {"type": "content_block_start", "index": idx[0],
                                                "content_block": {"type": "tool_use", "id": tid, "name": name, "input": {}}})
                    sse("content_block_delta", {"type": "content_block_delta", "index": idx[0],
                                                "delta": {"type": "input_json_delta", "partial_json": json.dumps(args)}})
                cur["type"] = "tool_use"
                close_block()

        if stream:
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
            self.send_header("Connection", "close")
            self.end_headers()
            sse("message_start", {"type": "message_start", "message": {
                "id": mid, "type": "message", "role": "assistant", "model": req.get("model", MODEL_NAME),
                "content": [], "stop_reason": None, "stop_sequence": None,
                "usage": {"input_tokens": len(ids), "output_tokens": 0}}})
            self.wfile.flush()

        def on_tokens(new):
            for t in new:
                state["n"] += 1
                if t in ENGINE.m.eos:
                    state["stop"] = state.get("stop") or "end_turn"
                    continue
                detok.add_token(t)
            seg = detok.last_segment
            if seg:
                state["text"] += seg
                for s in stops:
                    if s in state["text"]:
                        state["stop"], state["stop_seq"] = "stop_sequence", s
                for ev in parser.feed(seg):
                    emit(ev)
                if stream:
                    self.wfile.flush()
            return state["stop"] != "stop_sequence"

        stats = ENGINE.generate(ids, max_new, on_tokens)
        detok.finalize()
        tail = detok.last_segment
        for ev in parser.feed(tail, final=True):
            emit(ev)
        close_block()
        stop = state["stop"] or "max_tokens"
        if state.get("tool") and stop == "end_turn":
            stop = "tool_use"
        usage = {"input_tokens": len(ids), "output_tokens": state["n"],
                 "cache_read_input_tokens": stats["reused"]}
        stats.update(stop=stop, stream=stream)
        os.makedirs("results", exist_ok=True)
        with open(LOG, "a") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps({"t": time.time(), **stats}) + "\n")
        print(f"[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] prompt {stats['prompt_tokens']} (reused {stats['reused']}, "
              f"prefill {stats['prefill_s']}s)  out {stats['gen_tokens']} @ {stats['tok_s']} tok/s "
              f"({stats['tok_per_pass']} tok/pass)  {stop}  pick {stats['pick']}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        if stream:
            sse("message_delta", {"type": "message_delta",
                                  "delta": {"stop_reason": stop, "stop_sequence": state.get("stop_seq")},
                                  "usage": {"output_tokens": state["n"]}})
            sse("message_stop", {"type": "message_stop"})
            self.wfile.flush()
            self.close_connection = True
        else:
            self._json(200, {"id": mid, "type": "message", "role": "assistant", "model": req.get("model", MODEL_NAME),
                             "content": blocks_out, "stop_reason": stop, "stop_sequence": state.get("stop_seq"),
                             "usage": usage})


def main():
    global ENGINE
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model", default="~/.lmstudio/models/unsloth/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-8bit")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=1235)
    ap.add_argument("--thinking", action="store_true", help="always enable thinking (not forwarded to the client)")
    ap.add_argument("--exact", action="store_true", help="bit-exact mode: passes stay in MLX's S=2..4 kernel band")
    ap.add_argument("--model-only", action="store_true", help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    global THINKING, EXACT
    THINKING, EXACT = args.thinking, args.exact
    os.chdir(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    ENGINE = Engine(os.path.expanduser(args.model))
    print(f"serving {args.model} on http://127.0.0.1:{args.port}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", args.port), Handler).serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### SpecDecode/sim_moe.py
"""Cost model for speculation on the 35B-A3B MoE, driven by recorded routing.

For every verify pass the union of experts actually routed by its tokens is
taken from data/route4 (4-bit greedy outputs and their routing). Pass time:
  mlx-now   : measured t(S) curve of today's MLX (results/cost_moe35.json, Q8)
  bytes@62% : bytes actually needed (dense part + union experts) at today's 62 % of bandwidth
  bytes@90% : same at 90 % of bandwidth (fused/optimized kernels), + 0.5 ms fixed overhead
Policies (all use prompt-lookup drafts, emitted tokens are the greedy ones):
  base          one token per pass
  pl k=2 / adaptive / k=16
  union-cap U   adaptive lookup draft, truncated when the union predicted from the
                sources' prefill routing (plus the current token's routing) would exceed U
  oracle        draft exactly the tokens that will be accepted (upper bound for any
                stopping rule on these drafts)
Reported: tok/s and speedup over today's measured Q8 baseline (48 tok/s).
"""
import json
import os

import numpy as np

from routing import copy_sources
from specdec import PromptLookup

BW = 280e9
Q = {"Q8": dict(dense=2.39e9, expert=3.34e6), "4bit": dict(dense=1.43e9, expert=1.77e6)}
L = 40
cost_now = {int(k): v for k, v in json.load(open("results/cost_moe35.json"))["t"].items()}
BASE_Q8 = 48.0


def pass_time(model, S, U, quant):
    q = Q[quant]
    b = q["dense"] + U * L * q["expert"]
    if model == "mlx-now":
        return cost_now[min(S, 17)] * (1.0 if quant == "Q8" else 0.58)
    if model == "bytes@62%":
        return b / (0.62 * BW)
    return b / (0.90 * BW) + 0.5e-3


def run(policy, hist, P, onehot, src_pos, quant, model, umax=None):
    """Replay generation of hist[P+1:] (positions whose token is generated)."""
    pl = None
    if policy != "base":
        cfg = {"k2": (6, 2, 2), "adaptive": (8, 2, 2, 16, 8), "k16": (8, 2, 16)}.get(policy, (8, 2, 2, 16, 8))
        pl = PromptLookup(*cfg)
        pl.start(hist[: P + 1])
    t, i, T, ntok = 0.0, P + 1, len(hist), 0
    while i < T:
        d = pl.draft()[: T - i] if pl else []
        n = 0
        while n < len(d) and d[n] == hist[i + n]:
            n += 1
        if policy == "oracle":
            d = d[:n]
        if policy == "unioncap" and d:
            # predict union from the current token (routing known: it's being fed) + sources of drafts
            u = onehot[i - 1].copy()
            keep = 0
            for j in range(len(d)):
                s = src_pos[i + j] if src_pos[i + j] >= 0 else None
                if s is None:
                    break
                u2 = u | onehot[s]
                if u2.sum(-1).mean() > umax:
                    break
                u, keep = u2, j + 1
            d = d[:keep]
            n = min(n, keep)
        # true cost: union of routing of fed tokens (current + drafted positions)
        fed = list(range(i - 1, min(i + len(d), T)))
        U = onehot[fed].any(axis=0).sum(-1).mean()
        t += pass_time(model, len(fed), U, quant)
        adv = n + 1
        if pl:
            pl.extend(hist[i : i + adv])
        i += adv
        ntok += adv
    return ntok, t
