def main():
    data = []
    for f in sorted(os.listdir("data/route4")):
        d = np.load(os.path.join("data/route4", f))
        ids, toks = list(d["ids"]), list(d["toks"])
        route = np.concatenate([d["prompt"], d["gen"]])
        hist = ids + toks[:-1]
        P = len(ids) - 1
        onehot = np.zeros((len(route), L, 256), dtype=bool)
        np.put_along_axis(onehot, route.astype(np.int64), True, axis=2)
        src = copy_sources(hist, P + 1, nmax=8, nmin=2)
        data.append((f[:-4].rstrip("0123456789"), hist, P, onehot, src))
    policies = [("base", None), ("k2", None), ("adaptive", None), ("k16", None),
                ("unioncap", 16), ("unioncap", 24), ("unioncap", 32), ("oracle", None)]
    out = {}
    for quant in ("Q8", "4bit"):
        for model in ("mlx-now", "bytes@62%", "bytes@90%"):
            print(f"\n{quant}  cost model {model}   tok/s (x vs 48 tok/s Q8 today)")
            print("  policy        edit          bash          write         text          all")
            for pol, um in policies:
                agg = {}
                for kind, hist, P, onehot, src in data:
                    n, t = run(pol, hist, P, onehot, src, quant, model, um)
                    for k in (kind, "all"):
                        a = agg.setdefault(k, [0, 0.0])
                        a[0] += n
                        a[1] += t
                name = pol + (f"<={um}" if um else "")
                cells = []
                for k in ("edit", "bash", "write", "text", "all"):
                    tps = agg[k][0] / agg[k][1]
                    cells.append(f"{tps:6.0f} ({tps / BASE_Q8:4.1f}x)")
                    out[f"{quant}|{model}|{name}|{k}"] = tps
                print(f"  {name:12s}" + "".join(cells))
    json.dump(out, open("results/sim_moe.json", "w"), indent=1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### SpecDecode/simulate.py
"""Replay prompt-lookup drafting over the baseline greedy outputs.

Because verified speculative decoding emits exactly the greedy tokens, the
number of forward passes (and the size of each) for a prompt-lookup config is
fully determined by the baseline output. Combined with a measured cost curve
t(S) for a forward pass over S tokens, this predicts tok/s for any config
without running it. The best configs are then run for real to check.

  simulate.py RESULTS.jsonl COST.json
"""
import json
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

from render import render
from specdec import PromptLookup


def replay(prompt_ids, out, drafter):
    """Return list of (draft_len, accepted) per forward pass."""
    drafter.start(list(prompt_ids))
    steps, i = [], 0
    while i < len(out):
        d = drafter.draft()[: len(out) - i - 1]
        n = 0
        while n < len(d) and d[n] == out[i + n]:
            n += 1
        steps.append((len(d), n))
        drafter.extend(out[i : i + n + 1])
        i += n + 1
    return steps


def main():
    from mlx_lm.tokenizer_utils import load as load_tok
    from pathlib import Path
    res = [json.loads(l) for l in open(sys.argv[1])]
    cost = json.load(open(sys.argv[2]))  # {"model":..., "t": {S: seconds}}
    t = {int(k): v for k, v in cost["t"].items()}
    tok = load_tok(Path(cost["path"]))
    samples = {json.loads(l)["id"]: json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl")}
    base = [r for r in res if r["config"] == "base"]
    configs = [(nmax, nmin, k) for nmax in (2, 3, 4, 6) for nmin in (1, 2) if nmin <= nmax
               for k in (2, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16)]
    # adaptive: short drafts on short matches, long drafts on long matches
    configs += [(nmax, nmin, k, kl, nl) for nmax in (6, 8, 12) for nmin in (2, 3) for k in (0, 1, 2)
                for kl in (4, 6, 8, 12, 16) for nl in (4, 6, 8) if nl <= nmax and (k or nmin < nl)]
    agg = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(lambda: [0.0, 0.0, 0, 0]))  # cfg -> kind -> [t_base, t_spec, toks, drafted]
    for r in base:
        ids, _ = render(samples[r["id"]], tok)
        out = r["tokens"]
        tb = len(out) * t[1]
        for cfg in configs:
            steps = replay(ids, out, PromptLookup(*cfg))
            ts = sum(t[d + 1] for d, _ in steps)
            for kind in (r["kind"], "all"):
                a = agg[cfg][kind]
                a[0] += tb
                a[1] += ts
                a[2] += sum(n for _, n in steps)
                a[3] += sum(d for d, _ in steps)
    kinds = ["edit", "write", "bash", "text", "all"]
    rows = []
    for cfg in configs:
        sp = {k: agg[cfg][k][0] / agg[cfg][k][1] for k in kinds if agg[cfg][k][1]}
        acc = agg[cfg]["all"][2] / max(1, agg[cfg]["all"][3])
        rows.append((sp["all"], cfg, sp, acc))
    rows.sort(reverse=True)
    print(f"model {cost['model']}  predicted speedup over plain greedy (time-weighted)")
    print("nmax:nmin:k[:klong:nlong]  " + "  ".join(f"{k:>5s}" for k in kinds) + "  draft-acc")
    for row in rows[:15] + [None] + rows[-3:]:
        if row is None:
            print("...")
            continue
        _, cfg, sp, acc = row
        print(f"{':'.join(map(str, cfg)):>14s}  " + "  ".join(f"{sp.get(k, 0):5.2f}" for k in kinds)
              + f"  {acc:6.1%}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### SpecDecode/spec125.py
"""Thin greedy speculative loop for MTPLX runtimes (BigMoE's streamed Qwen3.8-Flash-Next / qwen4_exp).

Drafting: prompt lookup (copies from prompt AND generated text) else the model's own MTP head, with the cross-check
(lookup draft used only when the MTP head agrees on its first token). Drafts are capped (default 5) so every verify pass
stays inside the batch-invariant band of the reference kernel set (BIGMOE_DUPROW: rows 1..6 bit-identical).
Rollback: MTPLX's own capture-commit (snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy → verify_capture_scope → commit_verified_window),
falling back to rollback_after_verify + a re-forward of the kept rows (still inside the band) if a commit is refused.
Acceptance is greedy argmax prefix match, so with a batch-invariant kernel set the tokens equal plain greedy decode
(bigmoe.decode.greedy / bench.py mode ar2). No EOS stop, like decode.greedy.

    from spec125 import generate
    tokens, stats = generate(rt.model, prompt_ids, 64)
"""
import contextlib
import os
import time

import mlx.core as mx



class PromptLookup:
    """Copy of specdec.PromptLookup (no specdec import here: it patches mlx-lm globally). Drafts what followed the
    most recent earlier occurrence of the current nmax..nmin-gram; k tokens, k_long on an n_long-gram match."""

    def __init__(self, nmax=8, nmin=2, k=2, k_long=16, n_long=8):
        self.nmax, self.nmin, self.k, self.k_long, self.n_long = nmax, nmin, k, k_long, n_long

    def start(self, ids):
        self.h, self.idx = [], [dict() for _ in range(self.nmax + 1)]
        self.extend(ids)

    def extend(self, toks):
        h, idx = self.h, self.idx
        for t in toks:
            h.append(t)
            L = len(h)
            for n in range(self.nmin, self.nmax + 1):
                if L - 1 >= n:
                    idx[n][tuple(h[L - 1 - n : L - 1])] = L - 1

    def draft(self):
        h = self.h
        for n in range(self.nmax, self.nmin - 1, -1):
            if len(h) >= n:
                p = self.idx[n].get(tuple(h[-n:]))
                if p is not None:
                    self.last_n = n  # match length (for adaptive width / traces)
                    return h[p : p + (self.k_long if n >= self.n_long else self.k)]
        self.last_n = 0
        return []

try:
    from mtplx.cache_state import rollback_after_verify, snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy
    from mtplx.graphbank import ensure_eager_window_capacity
except ImportError:  # imported outside an MTPLX venv (docs, lint)
    rollback_after_verify = snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy = ensure_eager_window_capacity = None


def _prefill_call(model, ids, cache, phase_on):
    if not phase_on:
        return model(mx.array([ids]), cache=cache, return_hidden=True, emit_logits=False)
    from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
    with attention_phase("prefill"):
        return model(mx.array([ids]), cache=cache, return_hidden=True, emit_logits=False)


def _split(out):
    return (out[0], out[1]) if isinstance(out, (tuple, list)) else (out, None)


# SPEC125_FASTDRAFT=1 (drafts only, verify unchanged): append's history + last pair in one MTP layer call, and each draft
# chain step handed to the GPU as soon as it is built
FASTDRAFT = os.environ.get("SPEC125_FASTDRAFT") == "1"


class _MTPHead:
    """The model's MTP head as a drafter: history of committed (hidden_i, token_{i+1}) pairs in its own cache."""

    def __init__(self, model):
        self.model = model
        self.cache = model.make_mtp_cache()
        self.logits = self.h = None  # head output for the newest committed pair (draft source)
        self.want_probs = False      # keep each draft token's head probability (adaptive width / traces)
        self.last_probs = []

    def append(self, hidden, next_ids):
        """Commit pairs; the last one runs through mtp_forward so its draft logits are ready."""
        n = hidden.shape[1]
        if FASTDRAFT and 1 < n <= 16:  # one MTP layer call for all committed rows; the head only on the last row
            m = getattr(self.model, "language_model", self.model)
            h = m.mtp.fuse_and_run(hidden, m.model.embed_tokens(mx.array([next_ids])), self.cache)
            self.h = h[:, -1:]
            self.logits = m._mtp_draft_head_logits(m.mtp.hyper_connection_mixer(self.h))
            return
        if n > 1:
            self.model.mtp_update_cache(hidden[:, :-1], mx.array([next_ids[:-1]]), mtp_cache=self.cache)
        self.logits, self.h = self.model.mtp_forward(hidden[:, -1:], mx.array([next_ids[-1:]]),
                                                     mtp_cache=self.cache, return_hidden=True)

    def draft(self, k, p_min=0.0, st=None):
        """Up to k chained draft tokens; with p_min > 0, stop where the head's cumulative probability drops below it
        (confidence-adaptive depth: shorter drafts = fewer verify rows = fewer streamed experts)."""
        return self.draft_finish(self.draft_start(k, p_min), k, p_min, st)

    def draft_start(self, k, p_min=0.0):
        """Build the draft chain lazily and hand it to the GPU (mx.async_eval); draft_finish collects it."""
        if self.logits is None or k <= 0:
            return None
        lg = self.logits[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)
        need = p_min > 0 or self.want_probs
        toks, probs = [mx.argmax(lg)], [mx.softmax(lg)[mx.argmax(lg)]] if need else []
        if FASTDRAFT:
            mx.async_eval(toks[0])
        h = self.h
        for _ in range(k - 1):
            lgx, h = self.model.mtp_forward(h, toks[-1].reshape(1, 1), mtp_cache=self.cache, return_hidden=True)
            lgx = lgx[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)
            toks.append(mx.argmax(lgx))
            if need:
                probs.append(mx.softmax(lgx)[toks[-1]])
            if FASTDRAFT:  # the GPU runs this step while Python builds the next one
                mx.async_eval(toks[-1])
        mx.async_eval(toks, probs)
        return toks, probs

    def first(self):
        """Draft token 1 and its head probability (no cache append)."""
        lg = self.logits[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)
        t = mx.argmax(lg)
        p = mx.softmax(lg)[t]
        mx.eval(t, p)
        return int(t.item()), p.item()

    def chain(self, t1, p1, adapt, kmax):
        """Adaptive MTP chain: step j is kept while adapt.mtp_take(j, q_j) says the expected extra token pays for its
        verify row and draft step (q_j = product of head probs ** alpha); the first step that doesn't pay ends it."""
        adapt.mtp_begin()
        q = p1 ** adapt.alpha
        if kmax <= 0 or not adapt.mtp_take(1, q):
            return []
        toks, h, appended, p_prev = [t1], self.h, 0, p1
        for j in range(2, kmax + 1):
            # peek: don't pay for a chain step that wouldn't be kept even at the previous step's probability
            if adapt.peek and not adapt.mtp_would_take(j, q * p_prev ** adapt.alpha):
                break
            lgx, h = self.model.mtp_forward(h, mx.array([[toks[-1]]]), mtp_cache=self.cache, return_hidden=True)
            appended += 1
            lgx = lgx[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)
            t = mx.argmax(lgx)
            p = mx.softmax(lgx)[t]
            mx.eval(t, p)
            p_prev = p.item()
            q *= p_prev ** adapt.alpha
            if not adapt.mtp_take(j, q):
                break
            toks.append(int(t.item()))
        if appended:
            self.cache[0].trim(appended)
        return toks

    def draft_finish(self, pending, k, p_min=0.0, st=None):
        if pending is None:
            return []
        toks, probs = pending
        t = time.perf_counter()
        mx.eval(toks, probs)
        if st is not None:
            st["t_dwait"] += time.perf_counter() - t
        if k > 1:
            self.cache[0].trim(k - 1)  # chained draft entries aren't committed history
        out = [int(t.item()) for t in toks]
        self.last_probs = [p.item() for p in probs]
        if p_min > 0:
            run = 1.0
            for j, p in enumerate(probs):
                run *= p.item()
                if run < p_min:
                    return out[:j]
        return out


def _prefill_lmp4pd(model, prompt_ids, step=2048):
    """PrefillKernels' lmp4pd (layer-major, PLE precompute, prefill phase, dense-band SDPA; exact, ~4x faster at 32K),
    re-run here so every chunk's final widened stream is kept for the MTP history. Mirrors
    nsp_lmp4.prefill_qwen4_lmp4 (BigMoE bigmoe.decode.prefill_lmp4pd); needs BIGMOE_PF_PREFILL=1 BIGMOE_PF_FULLSCR=1."""
    import sys
    for p in ("~/PrefillKernels/patches/nosync_prefill", "~/MoonShots/patches/layer_major_prefill"):
        p = os.path.expanduser(p)
        if p not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, p)
    import nsp_lmp4
    import mtplx.models.qwen4_exp as Q
    from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
    os.environ["MTPLX_QSA_DENSE_BAND_SDPA"] = "1"
    cache = model.make_cache()
    tm = model.language_model.model
    ids = mx.array([list(prompt_ids)[:-1]])
    spans = [(i, min(i + step, ids.shape[1])) for i in range(0, ids.shape[1], step)]
    with attention_phase("prefill"):
        plan = nsp_lmp4.nsp4.PlePlan(model, prompt_ids, spans)
        xs = [ids[:, a:b] for a, b in spans]
        hs = [mx.tile(tm.embed_tokens(x), (1, 1, tm.args.hc_count)) for x in xs]
        ssm_masks = [Q.create_ssm_mask(h, cache[tm.ssm_idx]) for h in hs]
        scope = Q._rope_table_scope() if Q.qwen4_opdiet_enabled("rope") else None
        if scope is not None:
            scope.__enter__()
        try:
            with nsp_lmp4.sticky_staging():
                for L, (layer, c) in enumerate(zip(tm.layers, cache)):
                    for i, x in enumerate(xs):
                        if L == tm._ple_stage_idx and Q._COMPILED_VERIFY_PLE.get() is None:
                            if plan.ok:
                                plan.stage(i)
                            else:
                                layer.ple.ple_embedding.stage(x, c, layer.ple.NGRAM_IDX)
                        hs[i] = layer(hs[i], input_ids=x, ssm_mask=ssm_masks[i], cache=c)
                        mx.eval(hs[i], *[a for a in (c.state if hasattr(c, "state") else []) if isinstance(a, mx.array)])
                    mx.clear_cache()
        finally:
            plan.close()
            if scope is not None:
                scope.__exit__(None, None, None)
    tm._last_widened = hs[-1]
    return cache, hs, spans


def _prefill(model, prompt_ids, with_head, prefill_chunk=2048):
    """Prefill all but the last prompt token (as decode.greedy / MTPLX), building the MTP history alongside.
    SPEC125_PREFILL_PHASE=1 runs it under MTPLX's attention_phase("prefill") (exact, §26);
    SPEC125_PREFILL=lmp4pd uses PrefillKernels' layer-major prefill (exact per PrefillKernels)."""
    if (os.environ.get("SPEC125_PREFILL") == "lmp4pd" and len(prompt_ids) > prefill_chunk  # as BigMoE: > 1 chunk only
            and not os.environ.get("BIGMOE_TIER")):
        # (BIGMOE_TIER: prefill reads 4-bit experts from records3 in the streamed switch; layer-major not wired yet)
        cache, hs, spans = _prefill_lmp4pd(model, prompt_ids, prefill_chunk)
        head = _MTPHead(model) if with_head else None
        if head is not None:
            for (a, b), h in zip(spans, hs):
                head.append(h, prompt_ids[a + 1:b + 1])
                mx.eval(head.logits)
        return cache, head
    cache = model.make_cache()
    head = _MTPHead(model) if with_head else None
    P = len(prompt_ids) - 1
    phase_on = os.environ.get("SPEC125_PREFILL_PHASE") == "1"
    for i in range(0, P, prefill_chunk):
        j = min(i + prefill_chunk, P)
        _, hid = _split(_prefill_call(model, prompt_ids[i:j], cache, phase_on))
        if head is not None:
            head.append(hid, prompt_ids[i + 1:j + 1])
            mx.eval(head.logits)
        mx.eval(hid)
        mx.clear_cache()
    return cache, head


def prefill_state(model, prompt_ids, prefill_chunk=2048):
    """Prefill once; pass the result as generate(..., prefilled=state) / plain_from(state, ...) to run several arms
    from the same (bit-identical) cache without re-prefilling."""
    cache, head = _prefill(model, [int(t) for t in prompt_ids], True, prefill_chunk)
    return {"cache": cache, "mtp_cache": head.cache, "logits": head.logits, "h": head.h}


def restore_state(model, state, with_head=True):
    import copy
    cache = copy.deepcopy(state["cache"])
    head = None
    if with_head:
        head = _MTPHead(model)
        head.cache = copy.deepcopy(state["mtp_cache"])
        head.logits, head.h = state["logits"], state["h"]
    return cache, head


def plain_from(model, state, prompt_ids, max_tokens, on_token=None):
    """Pipelined greedy decode (bigmoe.decode.greedy's loop) from a prefill_state(): the plain reference arm."""
    if os.environ.get("BIGMOE_RESET_GEN", "1") == "1":
        try:
            from bigmoe.fuse import reset_generation_state
            reset_generation_state(model)
        except ImportError:
            pass
    cache, _ = restore_state(model, state, with_head=False)
    y = mx.array(int(prompt_ids[-1]))
    out, first = [], True
    for _ in range(max_tokens):
        nxt = model(y.reshape(1, 1), cache=cache)
        nxt = nxt[0] if isinstance(nxt, tuple) else nxt
        y2 = mx.argmax(nxt[0, -1].astype(mx.float32))
        mx.async_eval(y2)
        if not first:
            out.append(int(y.item()))
            if on_token:
                on_token(out[-1])
        first = False
        y = y2
    out.append(int(y.item()))
    if on_token:
        on_token(out[-1])
    return out[:max_tokens]


class AdaptiveWidth:
    """Per-pass verify width: keep a draft token while its expected acceptance pays for its cost.
    Cost model (ms): rest + step_ms * chain steps + V[S], with V[S] (verify time at width S) an EWMA of observed passes,
    initialised from a table. Lookup drafts: per-token acceptance by match-length bucket, learned online."""

    def __init__(self, verify_ms, step_ms=2.2, rest_ms=0.5, alpha=2.0, ewma=0.3, rates=None, lookup_full=True):
        self.V = {int(k): float(v) for k, v in verify_ms.items()}
        self.step, self.rest, self.alpha, self.a = step_ms, rest_ms, alpha, ewma
        self.lookup_full = lookup_full  # replay + live: cutting lookup drafts never paid (2-gram+ acceptance ~1.0)
        self.peek = True                # skip computing a chain step that wouldn't pay at the previous step's prob
        self.calibrate = False          # online per-depth calibration: OFF (short A/B: prose 44.8 -> 34-36 tok/s; it
                                        # shrinks drafts and then never sees deeper acceptance again; code ties anyway)
        self.qp, self.qa, self.last_q = {}, {}, []
        self.acc = {b: [r * 4, 4.0] for b, r in (rates or {0: 0.5, 1: 0.7, 2: 0.9}).items()}  # prior: 4 pseudo-obs

    @staticmethod
    def bucket(m):
        return 0 if m < 4 else 1 if m < 8 else 2

    def observe(self, S, ms):
        self.V[S] = (1 - self.a) * self.V[S] + self.a * ms

    def lookup_seen(self, match_n, w, n):
        a = self.acc[self.bucket(match_n)]
        a[0] += min(n, w)
        a[1] += min(n + 1, w)  # tokens tried until the first reject (or all w)

    def lookup_width(self, match_n, avail):
        if self.lookup_full:
            return avail
        a = self.acc[self.bucket(match_n)]
        r = a[0] / max(1e-9, a[1])
        best, e, q = 0, 1.0, 1.0
        best_v = 1.0 / (self.rest + self.step + self.V[1])
        for w in range(1, avail + 1):
            q *= r
            e += q
            v = e / (self.rest + self.step + self.V[w + 1])
            if v > best_v:
                best, best_v = w, v
        return best

    def mtp_begin(self):
        self.e = 1.0
        self.last_q = []

    def cal(self, j, q):
        """q_j scaled by depth j's realised/predicted acceptance ratio (online calibration; prior 8 pseudo-obs at 1)."""
        if not self.calibrate:
            return q
        P, A = self.qp.get(j, 0.0), self.qa.get(j, 0.0)
        return min(1.0, q * (A + 8.0) / (P + 8.0))

    def mtp_seen(self, n):
        """After an MTP pass: depth j (1-based) of the verified draft was accepted iff n >= j."""
        for j, q in enumerate(self.last_q, start=1):
            self.qp[j] = 0.98 * self.qp.get(j, 0.0) + q
            self.qa[j] = 0.98 * self.qa.get(j, 0.0) + (1.0 if n >= j else 0.0)

    def mtp_would_take(self, j, q):
        """Would step j be kept at estimated q? (its draft cost counted as not yet paid)"""
        q = self.cal(j, q)
        return (self.e + q) / (self.rest + self.step * j + self.V[j + 1]) > \
            self.e / (self.rest + self.step * (j - 1) + self.V[j])

    def mtp_take(self, j, q):
        q_raw, q = q, self.cal(j, q)
        c_new = self.rest + self.step * j + self.V[j + 1]
        c_cur = self.rest + self.step * j + self.V[j]
        if (self.e + q) / c_new > self.e / c_cur:
            self.e += q
            self.last_q.append(q_raw)
            return True
        return False


def generate(model, prompt_ids, max_tokens, k_mtp=2, max_draft=5, lookup=True, xcheck=True, on_token=None,
             prefill_chunk=2048, stats=None, p_min=0.0, early=None, width=None, adapt=None, prefilled=None, stop_ids=None):
    """early (default env SPEC125_EARLY=1): start the next pass's MTP draft on the GPU right after the accept decision,
    before the cache commit and bookkeeping (drafts only; verify and its tokens are unchanged)."""
    early = os.environ.get("SPEC125_EARLY") == "1" if early is None else early
    # width(d, src, probs, match_n, max_draft) -> how many draft tokens to verify (adaptive verify width);
    # stats["trace"] (a list) gets one record per pass; stats["pass_hook"]() may add fields (e.g. expert misses)
    tracing = isinstance((stats or {}).get("trace"), list)
    if os.environ.get("BIGMOE_RESET_GEN", "1") == "1":  # BigMoE LC1: MTPLX keeps QSA state (compiled indexer cores,
        try:                                            # lru_caches) across generations; reset it like decode.greedy
            from bigmoe.fuse import reset_generation_state
            reset_generation_state(model)
        except ImportError:
            pass
    if os.environ.get("BIGMOE_FRSPEC") == "1":  # Scout SC1b (exact): FR-Spec draft head rebuilt with the prompt's tokens
        import sys
        if os.path.expanduser("~/Scout") not in sys.path:
            sys.path.append(os.path.expanduser("~/Scout"))
        from patches.frspec4 import FRSpec
        FRSpec(model, extra_ids=[int(t) for t in prompt_ids]).on()
    st = stats if stats is not None else {}
    st.update(passes=0, drafted=0, accepted=0, commit_refused=0, lookup_used=0, t_verify=0.0, t_draft=0.0, t_build=0.0,
              t_wait=0.0, t_commit=0.0, t_head=0.0, t_dwait=0.0)
    prompt_ids = [int(t) for t in prompt_ids]
    t_pf = time.perf_counter()
    if prefilled is not None:  # reuse a prefill_state() (deep copy: this generation must not touch the shared one)
        cache, head = restore_state(model, prefilled, with_head=k_mtp > 0)
    else:
        cache, head = _prefill(model, prompt_ids, k_mtp > 0, prefill_chunk)
    if head is not None and (width is not None or tracing):
        head.want_probs = True
    st["t_prefill"] = time.perf_counter() - t_pf
    # lookup: 0/False = off, 1/True = standard (2 tokens on short matches), 2 = long matches only (>= 8-gram)
    pl = (PromptLookup(8, 8, 0, 16, 8) if lookup == 2 else PromptLookup(8, 2, 2, 16, 8)) if lookup else None
    if pl is not None:
        pl.start(prompt_ids)
    y, out = prompt_ids[-1], []
    pending = None
    try:                                # BigMoE repetition guard (exact while disarmed; armed -> 1-token steered steps)
        from bigmoe import repguard
        guard = repguard.make()
    except ImportError:
        repguard = guard = None
    # Guard timing must equal decode.greedy's: token j is steered iff the guard was armed after observing j-2 tokens
    # (hist[c] = armed after observing c tokens; one observe per token). A pass is cut right after the token that follows
    # an arming inside it, so later tokens of that pass go through the steered 1-token steps as in greedy.
    hist = [False]
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    while len(out) < max_tokens:
        c0 = len(out)
        steer_first = guard is not None and c0 >= 1 and hist[c0 - 1]   # token c0+1 steered
        armed = guard is not None and (steer_first or hist[c0])         # 1-token pass (steered or not)
        t_d = time.perf_counter()
        if adapt is not None and head is not None and head.logits is not None:  # adaptive width (AdaptiveWidth)
            t1, p1 = head.first()
            d_pl = pl.draft() if pl is not None else []
            avail = max(0, min(max_draft, max_tokens - len(out) - 1))
            if d_pl and d_pl[0] == t1:
                d, src = d_pl[:adapt.lookup_width(pl.last_n, min(len(d_pl), avail))], "lookup"
                st["lookup_used"] += 1
            else:
                d, src = head.chain(t1, p1, adapt, avail), "mtp"
            d_mtp, probs, match_n, d_full = [t1], [p1], (pl.last_n if pl is not None else 0), d
        else:
            if pending is not None:
                d_mtp, pending = head.draft_finish(pending, k_mtp, p_min, st), None
            else:
                d_mtp = head.draft(k_mtp, p_min, st) if head is not None else []
            d_pl = pl.draft() if pl is not None else []
            if d_pl and (not xcheck or not d_mtp or d_mtp[0] == d_pl[0]):
                d, src = d_pl, "lookup"
                st["lookup_used"] += 1
            else:
                d, src = d_mtp, "mtp"
            probs = head.last_probs if head is not None else []
            match_n = pl.last_n if pl is not None else 0
            d_full = d
        if width is not None and d:
            d = d[:max(0, int(width(d, src, probs, match_n, max_draft)))]
        d = d[:max_draft][: max(0, max_tokens - len(out) - 1)]
        if armed:
            d = []
        st["t_draft"] += time.perf_counter() - t_d
        toks = [y] + d
        S = len(toks)
        t_v = time.perf_counter()
        snap = None
        if S > 1:
            snap = snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy(cache)
            ensure_eager_window_capacity(cache, S)
        scope = model.verify_capture_scope() if S > 1 else contextlib.nullcontext()
        t_b = time.perf_counter()
        with scope:
            lg, hid = _split(model(mx.array([toks]), cache=cache, return_hidden=True))
        if steer_first:                 # S == 1: the next token's logits, steered on the committed tokens
            g = mx.argmax(repguard.steer(guard, out, lg[0, 0]), axis=-1).reshape(1)
        else:
            g = mx.argmax(lg[0].astype(mx.float32), axis=-1)
        t_w = time.perf_counter()
        st["t_build"] += t_w - t_b       # host graph build (GPU may already run streamed layers: BIGMOE_ASYNC_EVERY)
        mx.eval(g, hid)
        g = g.tolist()
        t_c = time.perf_counter()
        st["t_wait"] += t_c - t_w
        n = 0
        while n < len(d) and d[n] == g[n]:
            n += 1
        new = d[:n] + [g[n]]
        if guard is not None and not armed and len(new) > 2 and c0 + len(new) >= guard.config.min_tokens:
            import copy                 # would the guard arm inside this pass? then keep up to the token after that
            sim = copy.deepcopy(guard)
            for i in range(len(new) - 2):
                sim.observe(out + new[:i + 1])
                if sim.armed:
                    new, n = new[:i + 2], i + 1
                    break
        if early and head is not None and len(out) + len(new) < max_tokens:
            t_h = time.perf_counter()
            head.append(hid[:, : n + 1], new)       # verify hidden; a refused commit's repair hidden is not used here
            pending = head.draft_start(k_mtp, p_min)
            st["t_head"] += time.perf_counter() - t_h
            t_c = time.perf_counter()
        if S > 1:
            ok = model.commit_verified_window(cache, snap.states, keep_tokens=n + 1, verified_tokens=S)
            if not ok:  # repair: roll the whole window back and re-run the kept rows (inside the band)
                st["commit_refused"] += 1
                rollback_after_verify(cache, snap, verified_tokens=S)
                lg2, hid = _split(model(mx.array([toks[: n + 1]]), cache=cache, return_hidden=True))
                mx.eval(hid)
            model.clear_verify_capture(cache)
        t_h = time.perf_counter()
        st["t_commit"] += t_h - t_c
        st["t_verify"] += t_h - t_v
        if head is not None and not early:
            head.append(hid[:, : n + 1], new)
        st["t_head"] += time.perf_counter() - t_h
        if adapt is not None:
            adapt.observe(S, (t_h - t_v) * 1e3)
            if src == "lookup" and d:
                adapt.lookup_seen(match_n, len(d), n)
            elif src == "mtp":
                adapt.mtp_seen(n)
        if tracing:
            rec = dict(S=S, src=src, n=n, d=list(d_full[:max_draft]), probs=list(probs), match_n=match_n,
                       mtp=list(d_mtp), g=list(g), t_verify=time.perf_counter() - t_v)
            if "pass_hook" in st:
                rec.update(st["pass_hook"]())
            st["trace"].append(rec)
        st["passes"] += 1
        st["drafted"] += len(d)
        st["accepted"] += n
        for t in new:
            if len(out) < max_tokens:
                out.append(t)
                if on_token:
                    on_token(t)
        if pl is not None:
            pl.extend(new)
        if guard is not None:
            for c in range(c0 + 1, len(out) + 1):
                repguard.observe(guard, out[:c])
                hist.append(guard.armed)
        if stop_ids and any(t in stop_ids for t in out[c0:]):    # EOS: keep tokens through the first stop token
            j = next(i for i in range(c0, len(out)) if out[i] in stop_ids)
            del out[j + 1:]
            break
        y = new[-1]
    st["time"] = time.perf_counter() - t0
    st["tok_s"] = len(out) / st["time"]
    st["tok_per_pass"] = len(out) / max(1, st["passes"])
    return out, st


class DepthEMA:
    """Cost-model MTP depth chosen BEFORE drafting from last cycles' stats (no extra sync): maximize
    (1 + p1 + p1 p2 + ...) / t(k) with EMAs of per-position acceptance p_j and of the measured cycle time per depth t(k)
    (which prices in expert misses at that width); every `probe`-th MTP pass tries a neighbour depth (hysteresis-free
    exploration, so t(k) of unused depths stays fresh)."""

    V0 = {1: 42, 2: 59, 3: 74, 4: 87, 5: 94, 6: 107, 7: 117, 8: 123}  # verify ms by S (C=256 code calibration)

    def __init__(self, kmax=7, t0=None, p0=0.8, a=0.3, probe=8, step_ms=2.2):
        self.kmax, self.a, self.probe, self.n = kmax, a, probe, 0
        self.p = {j: p0 for j in range(1, kmax + 1)}
        # ms per pass by depth: measured verify cost at width k+1 plus k draft steps (was a generic 45 + 15k prior)
        t0 = t0 or {k: self.V0[k + 1] + step_ms * k for k in range(0, kmax + 1)}
        self.t = {k: float(t0[k]) for k in range(0, kmax + 1)}
        self.best = 3

    def choose(self):
        def value(k):
            e, q = 1.0, 1.0
            for j in range(1, k + 1):
                q *= self.p[j]
                e += q
            return e / self.t[k]
        self.best = max(range(0, self.kmax + 1), key=value)
        self.n += 1
        if self.probe and self.n % self.probe == 0:
            return max(0, min(self.kmax, self.best + (1 if (self.n // self.probe) % 2 else -1)))
        return self.best

    def seen(self, k, n_acc, ms):
        self.t[k] = (1 - self.a) * self.t[k] + self.a * ms
        for j in range(1, k + 1):  # position j reached iff all before it accepted
            if n_acc >= j - 1:
                self.p[j] = (1 - self.a) * self.p[j] + self.a * (1.0 if n_acc >= j else 0.0)


def generate_fused(model, prompt_ids, max_tokens, k=3, depth=None, lookup=True, lookup_min=4, max_draft=7,
                   stats=None, prefilled=None, prefill_chunk=2048, on_token=None):
    """One lazy graph per MTP pass: the MTP argmax chain feeds the verify forward on device, the accept length is
    cumprod(match).sum() on device, and the host reads (g, n) once. Depth: fixed k, or chosen per pass before drafting
    by `depth` (a DepthEMA). Lookup drafts with a >= lookup_min-gram match are used as is (no MTP cross-check, no MTP
    chain that pass). Exact like generate(): greedy prefix acceptance on the verify pass."""
    if os.environ.get("BIGMOE_RESET_GEN", "1") == "1":
        try:
            from bigmoe.fuse import reset_generation_state
            reset_generation_state(model)
        except ImportError:
            pass
    if os.environ.get("BIGMOE_FRSPEC") == "1":
        import sys
        if os.path.expanduser("~/Scout") not in sys.path:
            sys.path.append(os.path.expanduser("~/Scout"))
        from patches.frspec4 import FRSpec
        FRSpec(model, extra_ids=[int(t) for t in prompt_ids]).on()
    st = stats if stats is not None else {}
    st.update(passes=0, drafted=0, accepted=0, commit_refused=0, lookup_used=0, depth_hist={})
    prompt_ids = [int(t) for t in prompt_ids]
    t_pf = time.perf_counter()
    if prefilled is not None:
        cache, head = restore_state(model, prefilled, with_head=True)
    else:
        cache, head = _prefill(model, prompt_ids, True, prefill_chunk)
    st["t_prefill"] = time.perf_counter() - t_pf
    pl = PromptLookup(8, 2, 2, 16, 8) if lookup else None
    if pl is not None:
        pl.start(prompt_ids)
    y, out = prompt_ids[-1], []
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    while len(out) < max_tokens:
        t_p = time.perf_counter()
        room = max(0, min(max_draft, max_tokens - len(out) - 1))
        d_pl = pl.draft() if pl is not None else []
        use_pl = bool(d_pl) and pl.last_n >= lookup_min and room > 0
        if use_pl:
            d_host = d_pl[:room]
            kk = len(d_host)
            inp = mx.array([[y] + d_host])
            chain = 0
            st["lookup_used"] += 1
        else:
            kk = min(room, depth.choose() if depth is not None else k)
            toks = []
            if kk > 0:
                lg = head.logits[0, -1]
                toks = [mx.argmax(lg.astype(mx.float32))]
                h = head.h
                for _ in range(kk - 1):
                    lgx, h = model.mtp_forward(h, toks[-1].reshape(1, 1), mtp_cache=head.cache, return_hidden=True)
                    toks.append(mx.argmax(lgx[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)))
            chain = max(0, kk - 1)
            d_dev = mx.stack(toks).astype(mx.int32) if toks else None
            inp = mx.concatenate([mx.array([y], dtype=mx.int32), d_dev])[None] if toks else mx.array([[y]])
        S = kk + 1
        snap = None
        if S > 1:
            snap = snapshot_untrimmable_cache_lazy(cache)
            ensure_eager_window_capacity(cache, S)
        scope = model.verify_capture_scope() if S > 1 else contextlib.nullcontext()
        with scope:
            lg, hid = _split(model(inp, cache=cache, return_hidden=True))
        g = mx.argmax(lg[0].astype(mx.float32), axis=-1).astype(mx.int32)
        if S > 1:
            dd = mx.array(d_host, dtype=mx.int32) if use_pl else d_dev
            n_dev = mx.cumprod((dd == g[:kk]).astype(mx.int32)).sum()
            mx.eval(g, n_dev, dd, hid)  # the one read-back of the pass
            n = int(n_dev.item())
            d = d_host if use_pl else dd.tolist()
        else:
            mx.eval(g, hid)
            n, d = 0, []
        g = g.tolist()
        new = d[:n] + [g[n]]
        if chain:
            head.cache[0].trim(chain)  # chained draft entries aren't committed history
        if S > 1:
            ok = model.commit_verified_window(cache, snap.states, keep_tokens=n + 1, verified_tokens=S)
            if not ok:
                st["commit_refused"] += 1
                rollback_after_verify(cache, snap, verified_tokens=S)
                _, hid = _split(model(mx.array([[y] + d[:n]]), cache=cache, return_hidden=True))
                mx.eval(hid)
            model.clear_verify_capture(cache)
        head.append(hid[:, : n + 1], new)
        ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_p) * 1e3
        if depth is not None and not use_pl:
            depth.seen(kk, n, ms)
        st["depth_hist"][kk] = st["depth_hist"].get(kk, 0) + 1
        st["passes"] += 1
        st["drafted"] += kk
        st["accepted"] += n
        for t in new:
            if len(out) < max_tokens:
                out.append(t)
                if on_token:
                    on_token(t)
        if pl is not None:
            pl.extend(new)
        y = new[-1]
    st["time"] = time.perf_counter() - t0
    st["tok_s"] = len(out) / st["time"]
    st["tok_per_pass"] = len(out) / max(1, st["passes"])
    return out, st


### SpecDecode/specdec.py
"""Greedy speculative decoding for hybrid (linear + full attention) Qwen models in MLX.

Stock mlx-lm refuses speculative decoding on Qwen3.5/3.6/3.8 because the
GatedDeltaNet layers keep a recurrent state (ArraysCache) that can't be trimmed.
Here the verify pass records what each linear layer needs to rebuild its state
after any prefix of the drafted tokens:
  - conv state  = last (kernel-1) rows of [old conv state, qkv[:m]]
  - delta state = gated_delta_update over the first m tokens from the old state
The second one is just the recurrence kernel (no weight reads), so a rejected
draft costs a few tiny kernels instead of another forward pass.
Full-attention layers use the normal KVCache.trim.
"""
import time
from pathlib import Path

import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models import cache as cache_mod
from mlx_lm.models import qwen3_5
from mlx_lm.models.gated_delta import gated_delta_update

_CAPTURE = None  # list while a verify pass is recording, else None
FUSE = False     # use fused projections where kern.fuse() added them


def _gdn_call(self, inputs, mask=None, cache=None):
    """Copy of qwen3_5.GatedDeltaNet.__call__ (B=1, no sharding) that records rollback info."""
    B, S, _ = inputs.shape
    if FUSE and hasattr(self, "in_proj_all"):  # kern.py fuse: one matmul for qkv, z, b, a
        qkv, z, b, a = mx.split(self.in_proj_all(inputs), self._cuts, axis=-1)
    else:
        qkv, z = self.in_proj_qkv(inputs), self.in_proj_z(inputs)
        b, a = self.in_proj_b(inputs), self.in_proj_a(inputs)
    z = z.reshape(B, S, self.num_v_heads, self.head_v_dim)
    if cache is not None and cache[0] is not None:
        conv_state = cache[0]
    else:
        conv_state = mx.zeros((B, self.conv_kernel_size - 1, self.conv_dim), dtype=inputs.dtype)
    if mask is not None:
        qkv = mx.where(mask[..., None], qkv, 0)
    conv_input = mx.concatenate([conv_state, qkv], axis=1)
    n_keep = self.conv_kernel_size - 1
    if cache is not None:
        cache[0] = mx.contiguous(conv_input[:, -n_keep:, :])
    conv_out = nn.silu(self.conv1d(conv_input))
    q, k, v = [
        t.reshape(B, S, h, d)
        for t, h, d in zip(
            mx.split(conv_out, [self.key_dim, 2 * self.key_dim], -1),
            [self.num_k_heads, self.num_k_heads, self.num_v_heads],
            [self.head_k_dim, self.head_k_dim, self.head_v_dim],
        )
    ]
    state0 = cache[1] if cache else None
    inv_scale = k.shape[-1] ** -0.5
    q = (inv_scale**2) * mx.fast.rms_norm(q, None, 1e-6)
    k = inv_scale * mx.fast.rms_norm(k, None, 1e-6)
    out, state = gated_delta_update(q, k, v, a, b, self.A_log, self.dt_bias, state0, mask,
                                    use_kernel=not self.training)
    if cache is not None:
        cache[1] = state
        cache.advance(S)
        if _CAPTURE is not None and S > 1:
            _CAPTURE.append((self, cache, conv_input, n_keep, q, k, v, a, b, state0))
    out = self.norm(out, z)
    return self.out_proj(out.reshape(B, S, -1))


def _gdn_pure(self, inputs, conv_state, state0):
    """Pure GatedDeltaNet forward (no mask, B=1): state in, state out, so mx.compile can fuse its
    elementwise chain. Also returns what the speculative rollback needs."""
    B, S, _ = inputs.shape
    qkv, z = self.in_proj_qkv(inputs), self.in_proj_z(inputs)
    b, a = self.in_proj_b(inputs), self.in_proj_a(inputs)
    z = z.reshape(B, S, self.num_v_heads, self.head_v_dim)
    conv_input = mx.concatenate([conv_state, qkv], axis=1)
    n_keep = self.conv_kernel_size - 1
    new_conv = conv_input[:, -n_keep:, :]
    conv_out = nn.silu(self.conv1d(conv_input))
    q, k, v = [
        t.reshape(B, S, h, d)
        for t, h, d in zip(
            mx.split(conv_out, [self.key_dim, 2 * self.key_dim], -1),
            [self.num_k_heads, self.num_k_heads, self.num_v_heads],
            [self.head_k_dim, self.head_k_dim, self.head_v_dim],
        )
    ]
    inv_scale = k.shape[-1] ** -0.5
    q = (inv_scale**2) * mx.fast.rms_norm(q, None, 1e-6)
    k = inv_scale * mx.fast.rms_norm(k, None, 1e-6)
    out, state = gated_delta_update(q, k, v, a, b, self.A_log, self.dt_bias, state0, None)
    out = self.out_proj(self.norm(out, z).reshape(B, S, -1))
    return out, new_conv, state, conv_input, q, k, v, a, b


COMPILE = __import__("os").environ.get("SPEC_COMPILE") == "1"

# Batch-invariant decode, cheap version (band_probe.py): quantized matmuls give a different row-0 result for 1 row
# (qmv) than for 2+ rows (qmm); everything else on this path is row-invariant up to 4 rows (SDPA {1-4}). So for
# 1-row inputs, run each quantized matmul on the row duplicated and keep the first result. Experts aren't touched.
if __import__("os").environ.get("SPEC_DUPROW") == "1":
    _qlin_call = nn.QuantizedLinear.__call__

    def _dup_call(self, x):
        if x.ndim >= 2 and x.shape[-2] == 1:
            return _qlin_call(self, mx.concatenate([x, x], axis=-2))[..., :1, :]
        return _qlin_call(self, x)

    nn.QuantizedLinear.__call__ = _dup_call

# SDPA's kernel depends on the number of query rows ({1-4} vs {5-17}, band_probe.py). With SPEC_SDPAPAD=1, passes of
# 1..17 rows always use an explicit causal mask and at least 5 query rows (repeat the last row), so every row of a
# decode or verify pass goes through the same SDPA kernel. Lets verify passes go up to 12 rows bit-identically.
def _sdpa_padded(queries, keys, values, cache, scale, mask, sinks=None):
    L, Lk = queries.shape[2], keys.shape[2]
    if not (1 <= L <= 17) or mask is not None and not isinstance(mask, str):
        return _base_sdpa(queries, keys, values, cache, scale, mask, sinks) if sinks is not None else \
            _base_sdpa(queries, keys, values, cache, scale, mask)
    P = max(L, 5)
    rows = mx.minimum(mx.arange(P), L - 1)                      # padded rows repeat the last real row
    q = queries[:, :, rows] if P > L else queries
    m = mx.arange(Lk)[None, :] <= (Lk - L + rows)[:, None]      # causal, aligned to the end of the keys
    out = mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, keys, values, scale=scale, mask=m)
    return out[:, :, :L] if P > L else out


if __import__("os").environ.get("SPEC_SDPAPAD") == "1":
    from mlx_lm.models import qwen3_next as _q3n
    _base_sdpa = _q3n.scaled_dot_product_attention
    _q3n.scaled_dot_product_attention = _sdpa_padded


def _gdn_dispatch(self, inputs, mask=None, cache=None):
    if not (COMPILE and mask is None and cache is not None and inputs.shape[0] == 1):
        return _gdn_call(self, inputs, mask, cache)
    if "_gdn_c" not in self.__dict__:
        object.__setattr__(self, "_gdn_c", mx.compile(lambda x, c, st: _gdn_pure(self, x, c, st)))
    B, S, _ = inputs.shape
    conv_state = cache[0] if cache[0] is not None else mx.zeros((B, self.conv_kernel_size - 1, self.conv_dim), inputs.dtype)
    state0 = cache[1] if cache[1] is not None else mx.zeros((B, self.num_v_heads, self.head_v_dim, self.head_k_dim), mx.float32)
    out, new_conv, state, conv_input, q, k, v, a, b = self._gdn_c(inputs, conv_state, state0)
    cache[0], cache[1] = new_conv, state
    cache.advance(S)
    if _CAPTURE is not None and S > 1:
        _CAPTURE.append((self, cache, conv_input, self.conv_kernel_size - 1, q, k, v, a, b, state0))
    return out


qwen3_5.GatedDeltaNet.__call__ = _gdn_dispatch

if COMPILE:  # also compile each layer's MoE block (router, experts, shared expert, combine)
    _orig_layer_call = qwen3_5.DecoderLayer.__call__

    def _layer_call(self, x, mask=None, cache=None):
        if self.is_linear:
            r = self.linear_attn(self.input_layernorm(x), mask, cache)
        else:
            r = self.self_attn(self.input_layernorm(x), mask, cache)
        h = x + r
        if "_mlp_c" not in self.__dict__:
            object.__setattr__(self, "_mlp_c", mx.compile(self.mlp.__call__))
        return h + self._mlp_c(self.post_attention_layernorm(h))

    qwen3_5.DecoderLayer.__call__ = _layer_call


class Model:
    """A loaded model plus a single-sequence cache that can be rolled back."""

    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path = str(Path(path).expanduser())
        self.model, self.tokenizer = load(self.path)
        self.cache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.model)
        eos = self.tokenizer.eos_token_ids if hasattr(self.tokenizer, "eos_token_ids") else None
        self.eos = set(eos or [self.tokenizer.eos_token_id])
        self.eos.add(self.tokenizer.convert_tokens_to_ids("<|im_end|>"))

    # -- cache state --------------------------------------------------------
    def snapshot(self):
        """Cheap: MLX arrays are immutable, so holding references is enough."""
        return [(c.offset, None) if isinstance(c, cache_mod.KVCache) else (None, list(c.cache))
                for c in self.cache]

    def restore(self, snap):
        for c, (off, arrs) in zip(self.cache, snap):
            if arrs is None:
                c.trim(c.offset - off)
            else:
                c.cache = list(arrs)

    def reset(self):
        self.cache = cache_mod.make_prompt_cache(self.model)

    def prefill(self, ids, chunk=2048, on_chunk=None):
        """Feed all ids. on_chunk(i, j) runs after each chunk's forward. Returns time taken."""
        t = time.perf_counter()
        # prefill needs no logits: run the trunk only (lm_head would be ~8 % of prefill time)
        trunk = self.model.language_model.model if hasattr(self.model, "language_model") else self.model
        for i in range(0, len(ids), chunk):
            trunk(mx.array(ids[i : i + chunk])[None], cache=self.cache)
            if on_chunk is not None:
                on_chunk(i, min(i + chunk, len(ids)))
            mx.eval([c.state for c in self.cache])
            mx.clear_cache()
        return time.perf_counter() - t

    # -- forward with rollback ---------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, tokens, margins=None):
        """Run tokens through the model; return greedy argmax per position (list).
        If margins is a list, append the top1-top2 logit gap of the last position."""
        global _CAPTURE
        _CAPTURE = [] if len(tokens) > 1 else None
        logits = self.model(mx.array(tokens)[None], cache=self.cache)
        out = mx.argmax(logits[0], axis=-1)
        cap = getattr(self, "cap", None)
        self.last_hidden = cap.post if cap is not None else None
        if margins is not None:
            top2 = mx.topk(logits[0, -1].astype(mx.float32), 2)
            gap = mx.abs(top2[0] - top2[1])
            mx.eval(out, gap)
            margins.append(gap.item())
        else:
            mx.eval(out, self.last_hidden) if self.last_hidden is not None else mx.eval(out)
        self._captured, _CAPTURE = _CAPTURE, None
        return out.tolist()

    def keep(self, n_fed, m):
        """Of the last n_fed tokens forwarded, keep only the first m in the cache."""
        drop = n_fed - m
        if drop <= 0:
            return
        for c in self.cache:
            if isinstance(c, cache_mod.KVCache):
                c.trim(drop)
        for (layer, c, conv_input, n_keep, q, k, v, a, b, state0) in self._captured:
            c[0] = mx.contiguous(conv_input[:, m : m + n_keep, :])
            _, st = gated_delta_update(q[:, :m], k[:, :m], v[:, :m], a[:, :m], b[:, :m],
                                       layer.A_log, layer.dt_bias, state0)
            c[1] = st
        self._captured = None


class PromptLookup:
    """Draft by copying what followed the most recent earlier occurrence of the
    current suffix (longest n-gram first, nmax down to nmin)."""

    def __init__(self, nmax=3, nmin=1, k=8, k_long=None, n_long=None):
        # k: draft length; if k_long is set, a match of >= n_long tokens drafts k_long
        self.nmax, self.nmin, self.k = nmax, nmin, k
        self.k_long, self.n_long = k_long, n_long

    def start(self, ids):
        self.h = []
        self.idx = [dict() for _ in range(self.nmax + 1)]
        self.extend(ids)

    def extend(self, toks):
        h, idx = self.h, self.idx
        for t in toks:
            h.append(t)
            L = len(h)
            # register the n-gram that ends just before the new token -> continuation at L-1
            for n in range(self.nmin, self.nmax + 1):
                if L - 1 >= n:
                    idx[n][tuple(h[L - 1 - n : L - 1])] = L - 1

    def draft(self):
        h = self.h
        for n in range(self.nmax, self.nmin - 1, -1):
            if len(h) < n:
                continue
            p = self.idx[n].get(tuple(h[-n:]))
            if p is not None:
                k = self.k_long if self.k_long and n >= self.n_long else self.k
                return h[p : p + k]
        return []


class DraftModel:
    """A small model of the same family drafting k tokens greedily."""

    def __init__(self, m: Model, k=4):
        self.m, self.k = m, k

    def start(self, ids):
        self.m.reset()
        self.m.prefill(ids[:-1])
        self.pending = [ids[-1]]  # tokens the draft model hasn't consumed yet

    def extend(self, toks):
        pass  # handled via accept()

    def note_external(self, new):
        """Tokens were emitted from someone else's draft: consume them next time."""
        self.pending = self.pending + new

    def draft(self):
        # snapshot j is taken before feeding the j-th token, so it holds
        # pending + drafted[:j-1] (for j >= 1)
        snaps, toks, feed = [], [], self.pending
        for _ in range(self.k):
            snaps.append(self.m.snapshot())
            toks.append(self.m.forward(feed)[-1])
            self.m._captured = None  # everything the draft model consumed is kept
            feed = [toks[-1]]
        self._snaps = snaps
        return toks

    def accept(self, drafted, n_acc, new_tok):
        # Draft cache after drafting holds: pending + drafted[:-1].
        # We want it to hold pending + drafted[:n_acc]; then feed new_tok next time.
        k = len(drafted)
        if n_acc == k:
            self.pending = [drafted[-1], new_tok]
        else:
            # cache holds pending + drafted[:k-1]; we want pending + drafted[:n_acc]
            if n_acc + 1 < k:
                self.m.restore(self._snaps[n_acc + 1])
            self.pending = [new_tok]


class MTPDrafter:
    """Drafts with the model's own MTP head (see mtp.py). The head sees only real
    (hidden_i, token_{i+1}) pairs; chained draft entries are trimmed right away."""

    def __init__(self, m: Model, mtp, k=2, head=None, p_min=0.0):
        lm = m.model.language_model
        self.m, self.mtp, self.k, self.p_min = m, mtp, k, p_min
        self.embed = lm.model.embed_tokens
        self.head = head if head is not None else lm.lm_head
        self.cache = None

    def prefill_hook(self, ids):
        """Returns on_chunk for Model.prefill(ids[:-1]) that feeds the MTP its prompt pairs."""
        from mlx_lm.models import cache as cm
        self.cache = cm.make_prompt_cache(self.mtp)

        def hook(i, j):
            x = self.mtp(self.m.cap.post, self.embed(mx.array(ids[i + 1 : j + 1])[None]), self.cache)
            self.x_last = x[:, -1:]
            mx.eval(self.x_last, [c.state for c in self.cache])
        return hook

    def snapshot(self):
        return self.cache[0].offset, self.x_last

    def restore(self, snap):
        off, self.x_last = snap
        self.cache[0].trim(self.cache[0].offset - off)

    def start(self, ids):
        pass

    def extend(self, toks):
        pass

    def observe(self, H, new):
        x = self.mtp(H, self.embed(mx.array(new)[None]), self.cache)
        self.x_last = x[:, -1:]

    def draft(self):
        # chain lazily on the GPU; one sync for all k tokens
        toks, probs, x = [], [], self.x_last
        for j in range(self.k):
            logits = self.head(x)[0, -1]
            t = mx.argmax(logits)
            toks.append(t)
            if self.p_min > 0:
                probs.append(mx.softmax(logits.astype(mx.float32))[t])
            if j + 1 < self.k:
                x = self.mtp(x, self.embed(t.reshape(1, 1)), self.cache)
        mx.eval(toks, probs)
        if self.k > 1:
            self.cache[0].trim(self.k - 1)
        out = [t.item() for t in toks]
        if self.p_min > 0:  # stop the chain where the head's own confidence drops
            p_run = 1.0
            for j, p in enumerate(probs):
                p_run *= p.item()
                if p_run < self.p_min:
                    return out[:j]
        return out


class LookupMTP:
    """Prompt lookup when the context has a match, else the MTP head."""

    def __init__(self, pl: "PromptLookup", mtpd: MTPDrafter):
        self.pl, self.mtpd = pl, mtpd

    def start(self, ids):
        self.pl.start(ids)

    def extend(self, new):
        self.pl.extend(new)

    xcheck = False  # use the lookup draft only when the MTP head agrees on its first token

    def draft(self):
        d = self.pl.draft()
        alt = self.mtpd.draft() if (d and (self.xcheck or self.stats is not None)) else None
        self.last = ("pl", d, alt)
        if d and self.xcheck and alt and alt[0] != d[0]:
            d = alt
            self.last = ("mtp", d, None)
        if not d:
            d = self.mtpd.draft()
            self.last = ("mtp", d, None)
        return d

    stats = None  # set to a list to record (source, draft, alt MTP draft, accepted, true tokens)

    def observe(self, H, new):
        if self.stats is not None:
            src, d, alt = self.last
            self.stats.append((src, len(d), len(new) - 1, alt, list(new)))
        self.mtpd.observe(H, new)


class Hybrid:
    """Prompt lookup when the context has a match, otherwise the draft model."""

    def __init__(self, pl: PromptLookup, dm: DraftModel):
        self.pl, self.dm = pl, dm

    def start(self, ids):
        self.pl.start(ids)
        self.dm.start(ids)

    def draft(self):
        d = self.pl.draft()
        self.used_dm = not d
        return self.dm.draft() if self.used_dm else d

    def accept(self, drafted, n_acc, new_tok, new):
        if self.used_dm:
            self.dm.accept(drafted, n_acc, new_tok)
        else:
            self.dm.note_external(new)

    def extend(self, new):
        self.pl.extend(new)


def generate_iter(m: Model, prompt_ids, max_new, drafter=None, margins=None, pad_to=1, state=None):
    """Greedy decode from a cache that already holds prompt_ids[:-1], yielding the new
    tokens of every forward pass. state (dict, optional) gets "consumed": the tokens now in
    the cache after prompt_ids[:-1], and "forwards"/"accepted" stats."""
    y = prompt_ids[-1]
    n_out = 0
    st = state if state is not None else {}
    tracing = "trace" in st
    st.update(consumed=[], forwards=0, accepted=[], t_draft=0.0, t_verify=0.0, drafted=0)
    if tracing:
        st["trace"] = []
    if drafter is not None:
        drafter.start(list(prompt_ids))
    while n_out < max_new:
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        d = drafter.draft() if drafter is not None else []
        d = d[: max_new - n_out - 1] if d else d
        if pad_to > 1:
            d = d[:3]  # exact mode: stay inside the S=2..4 kernel band
        t1 = time.perf_counter()
        st["t_draft"] += t1 - t0
        st["drafted"] += len(d)
        # batch-invariant mode: MLX picks kernels by pass size, and results are
        # bitwise stable within a band (S=2..4), so pad short passes into the band
        pad = max(0, pad_to - 1 - len(d))
        g = m.forward([y] + d + [y] * pad, margins if not d else None)
        st["t_verify"] += time.perf_counter() - t1
        st["forwards"] += 1
        n = 0
        while n < len(d) and d[n] == g[n]:
            n += 1
        new = d[:n] + [g[n]]
        if d or pad:
            m.keep(len(d) + 1 + pad, n + 1)
            if isinstance(drafter, DraftModel):
                drafter.accept(d, n, g[n])
            elif isinstance(drafter, Hybrid):
                drafter.accept(d, n, g[n], d[:n] + [g[n]])
        elif isinstance(drafter, Hybrid):
            drafter.dm.note_external([g[0]])
        if hasattr(drafter, "observe"):
            drafter.observe(m.last_hidden[:, : n + 1], new)
        st["consumed"] += [y] + d[:n]
        st["accepted"].append(n)
        if "trace" in st:  # per-pass record: source, drafted, accepted, draft/verify seconds
            src = getattr(drafter, "last", ("?",))[0] if drafter is not None else "plain"
            st["trace"].append((src, len(d), n, t1 - t0, st["t_verify"] - st.get("_tv_prev", 0.0)))
            st["_tv_prev"] = st["t_verify"]
        stop = False
        for i, t in enumerate(new):
            if t in m.eos:
                new = new[: i + 1]
                stop = True
                break
        new = new[: max_new - n_out]
        n_out += len(new)
        if drafter is not None and not isinstance(drafter, DraftModel):
            drafter.extend(new)
        y = new[-1]
        yield new
        if stop:
            break


def generate(m: Model, prompt_ids, max_new, drafter=None, margins=None, pad_to=1):
    """Greedy decode from a cache that already holds prompt_ids[:-1].

    Returns (tokens, stats). With drafter=None it's plain one-token-per-forward."""
    st = {}
    out = []
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for new in generate_iter(m, prompt_ids, max_new, drafter, margins, pad_to, st):
        out += new
    dt = time.perf_counter() - t0
    return out, {"time": dt, "forwards": st["forwards"], "accepted": st["accepted"],
                 "tok_s": len(out) / dt, "t_draft": st["t_draft"], "t_verify": st["t_verify"],
                 "drafted": st["drafted"]}


### SpecDecode/suffix_sim.py
"""Offline: would a SuffixDecoding-style global index help where per-request lookup has nothing?
Global index = assistant turns from Kamen's other sessions (other projects than the sample's), rendered in
Qwen's tool-call format and tokenized; n-gram (4..12) -> continuation counts.
Replays the 4-bit greedy outputs (results/mtp4b.jsonl, base). On steps where PromptLookup gives no draft,
query the global index (longest n-gram, most frequent continuation, up to 8 tokens while its share > 0.5).
Compare accepted tokens there with the MTP head's 1.32 accepted/step on such steps (tree_stats.py).
  suffix_sim.py [max_sessions]
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

from mlx_lm.tokenizer_utils import load as load_tok

from extract import load_session
from render import TOOLS, render
from specdec import PromptLookup

NMIN, NMAX, KMAX = 4, 12, 8
tok = load_tok(Path("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit").expanduser())
samples = {json.loads(l)["id"]: json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl")}
base = [json.loads(l) for l in open("results/mtp4b.jsonl") if json.loads(l)["config"] == "base"]


def render_assistant(m):
    """Assistant turn as Qwen would emit it (tool calls in its XML format)."""
    msgs = [{"role": "user", "content": "x"}, m]
    full = tok.apply_chat_template(msgs, tools=TOOLS, tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False)
    pre = tok.apply_chat_template(msgs[:1], tools=TOOLS, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    return full[len(pre):]


# build one index per project-exclusion: index[proj] excludes sessions of that project
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl")), key=os.path.getsize, reverse=True)
files = [f for f in files if "SpecDecode" not in f][: int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 60]
seqs = []  # (project, token ids)
for f in files:
    proj = f.split("/")[-2]
    for m in load_session(f):
        if m["role"] == "assistant":
            try:
                seqs.append((proj, tok.encode(render_assistant(m), add_special_tokens=False)))
            except Exception:
                pass
print(f"global corpus: {len(seqs)} assistant turns from {len(files)} sessions, "
      f"{sum(len(s) for _, s in seqs)/1e6:.2f}M tokens", flush=True)


def build(exclude):
    idx = defaultdict(Counter)
    for proj, s in seqs:
        if proj == exclude:
            continue
        for n in range(NMIN, NMAX + 1):
            for i in range(n, len(s)):
                idx[tuple(s[i - n:i])][s[i]] += 1  # next-token counts; multi-token drafts chain lookups
    return idx


def gdraft(idx, hist):
    out, h = [], list(hist)
    while len(out) < KMAX:
        nxt = None
        for n in range(NMAX, NMIN - 1, -1):
            c = idx.get(tuple(h[-n:]))
            if c:
                t, k = c.most_common(1)[0]
                if k / sum(c.values()) > 0.5:
                    nxt = t
                break
        if nxt is None:
            break
        out.append(nxt)
        h.append(nxt)
    return out


cache = {}
tot = Counter()
for r in base:
    s = samples[r["id"]]
    proj = s["project"]
    if proj not in cache:
        cache[proj] = build(proj)
    idx = cache[proj]
    ids, _ = render(s, tok)
    out = r["tokens"]
    pl = PromptLookup(8, 2, 2, 16, 8)
    pl.start(list(ids))
    i = 0
    while i < len(out):
        d = pl.draft()[: len(out) - i - 1]
        if d:
            n = 0
            while n < len(d) and d[n] == out[i + n]:
                n += 1
            tot["pl_steps"] += 1
        else:
            g = gdraft(idx, list(ids) + out[:i])[: len(out) - i - 1]
            n = 0
            while n < len(g) and g[n] == out[i + n]:
                n += 1
            tot["empty_steps"] += 1
            tot["g_drafted"] += bool(g)
            tot["g_acc"] += n
            tot["g_len"] += len(g)
        pl.extend(out[i:i + n + 1])
        i += n + 1
e = tot["empty_steps"]
print(f"lookup-empty steps: {e}; global index drafted on {tot['g_drafted']} ({tot['g_drafted']/max(1,e):.0%}), "
      f"accepted {tot['g_acc']/max(1,e):.2f} tokens/step overall ({tot['g_acc']}/{tot['g_len']} drafted), "
      f"vs MTP head ~1.32 accepted/step on such steps")


### SpecDecode/summarize.py
"""Compact per-kind summary of bench results; also writes a CSV next to the input.

  summarize.py results/X.jsonl [more.jsonl ...]
"""
import csv
import json
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

KINDS = ["edit", "write", "bash", "text", "all"]


def main():
    rows = [json.loads(l) for f in sys.argv[1:] for l in open(f)]
    base_time = {(r["model"], r["id"]): r for r in rows if r["config"] == "base"}
    agg = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(float)))
    for r in rows:
        b = base_time.get((r["model"], r["id"]))
        if not b:
            continue
        for k in (r["kind"], "all"):
            a = agg[(r["model"], r["config"])][k]
            a["tok"] += r["n_tokens"]
            a["time"] += r["time"]
            a["btok"] += b["n_tokens"]
            a["btime"] += b["time"]
            a["fwd"] += r["forwards"]
            a["n"] += 1
            a["same"] += bool(r["identical"])
            a["prefill"] += r["prefill_s"]
            a["ptok"] += r["prompt_tokens"]
            a["t_draft"] += r.get("t_draft") or 0
            a["t_verify"] += r.get("t_verify") or 0
            if r["identical"] is False and r.get("diff_margin") is not None:
                a["maxgap"] = max(a["maxgap"], r["diff_margin"])
    out_csv = sys.argv[1].rsplit(".", 1)[0] + "_summary.csv"
    w = csv.writer(open(out_csv, "w"))
    w.writerow(["model", "config", "kind", "n", "tok_s", "speedup", "tok_per_fwd", "identical",
                "max_margin_at_divergence"])
    for (model, cfg), d in sorted(agg.items()):
        print(f"\n{model}  {cfg}   (prefill {d['all']['ptok']/d['all']['prefill']:.0f} tok/s)")
        A = d["all"]
        if A["t_draft"]:
            print(f"  time split: verify {A['t_verify']/A['time']:.0%}, draft+observe {A['t_draft']/A['time']:.0%}, "
                  f"other {1-(A['t_verify']+A['t_draft'])/A['time']:.0%}; verify ms/pass {1e3*A['t_verify']/A['fwd']:.1f}")
        print("  kind    n   tok/s  speedup  tok/fwd  identical")
        for k in KINDS:
            a = d.get(k)
            if not a or not a["n"]:
                continue
            tps = a["tok"] / a["time"]
            sp = tps / (a["btok"] / a["btime"])
            tpf = a["tok"] / a["fwd"]
            print(f"  {k:6s} {int(a['n']):3d} {tps:7.1f}  {sp:6.2f}x  {tpf:6.2f}   "
                  f"{int(a['same'])}/{int(a['n'])}" + (f" (div. at gap<={a['maxgap']:.2f})" if a["maxgap"] else ""))
            w.writerow([model, cfg, k, int(a["n"]), round(tps, 1), round(sp, 3), round(tpf, 2),
                        int(a["same"]), round(a["maxgap"], 3)])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### SpecDecode/trace_pass.py
"""Per-pass trace of lookup+MTP (cross-check) on real long-session turns. Where does a pass's time go,
and which passes waste it? Output: per context a summary by draft source and draft length.
  trace_pass.py MODEL ctx [ctx ...]
"""
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

import mlx.core as mx

mx.set_cache_limit(4 << 30)
model_path, ctxs = sys.argv[1], [int(x) for x in sys.argv[2:]]
sys.argv = [sys.argv[0], "http://x"]
import replay  # noqa: E402
import server  # noqa: E402
from mtp import install_capture, load_mtp  # noqa: E402
from specdec import LookupMTP, Model, MTPDrafter, PromptLookup, generate_iter  # noqa: E402

m = Model(model_path)
m.cap = install_capture(m.model)
mtp = load_mtp(m.model)
size, f, msgs = replay.longest_session()
a_idx = [i for i, x in enumerate(msgs) if x["role"] == "assistant" and i > 0]


def prompt_ids(k):
    mm, t = server.to_template({"system": replay.SYSTEM, "tools": replay.TOOLS, "messages": replay.to_anthropic(msgs[: a_idx[k]])})
    return m.tokenizer.encode(m.tokenizer.apply_chat_template(mm, tools=t, add_generation_prompt=True, tokenize=False,
                              enable_thinking=False, preserve_thinking=True), add_special_tokens=False)


for target in ctxs:
    lo, hi = 0, len(a_idx) - 1
    while lo < hi:
        mid = (lo + hi + 1) // 2
        lo, hi = (mid, hi) if len(prompt_ids(mid)) <= target else (lo, mid - 1)
    ids = prompt_ids(lo)
    m.reset()
    mtpd = MTPDrafter(m, mtp, 2)
    m.prefill(ids[:-1], on_chunk=mtpd.prefill_hook(ids))
    # plain step time at this context, for reference
    snap, msnap = m.snapshot(), mtpd.snapshot()
    st = {}
    for _ in generate_iter(m, ids, 16, None, state=st):
        pass
    t1 = st["t_verify"] / st["forwards"]
    m.restore(snap); mtpd.restore(msnap)
    dr = LookupMTP(PromptLookup(8, 2, 2, 16, 8), MTPDrafter(m, mtp, 2))
    dr.mtpd.cache, dr.mtpd.x_last = mtpd.cache, mtpd.x_last
    dr.xcheck = True
    st = {"trace": []}
    out = []
    for new in generate_iter(m, ids, 192, dr, state=st):
        out += new
    tr = st["trace"]
    tot_t = sum(a + b for _, _, _, a, b in tr)
    print(f"\nctx {len(ids)}: {len(out)} tokens in {len(tr)} passes, {len(out)/tot_t:.1f} tok/s; plain step {t1*1e3:.1f} ms")
    agg = defaultdict(lambda: [0, 0, 0, 0.0, 0.0])  # passes, drafted, accepted, t_draft, t_verify
    for src, d, n, td, tv in tr:
        key = (src, "0" if d == 0 else "1-3" if d <= 3 else "4-8" if d <= 8 else "9-16")
        a = agg[key]; a[0] += 1; a[1] += d; a[2] += n; a[3] += td; a[4] += tv
    print("  source draftlen  passes  accepted/drafted  verify ms/pass (x plain)  draft ms/pass  share of time  tokens/s in these passes")
    for key in sorted(agg):
        p, d, n, td, tv = agg[key]
        print(f"  {key[0]:6s} {key[1]:>5s}   {p:5d}   {n:4d}/{d:<4d}          {tv/p*1e3:6.1f} ({tv/p/t1:4.2f})            "
              f"{td/p*1e3:5.1f}       {(td+tv)/tot_t:5.0%}          {(n+p)/(td+tv):6.1f}")


### SpecDecode/tree_stats.py
"""Upper bound for a 2-branch tree (lookup draft + MTP draft verified together).

Runs lookup-else-MTP and, on every step where the lookup draft was used, also asks the MTP
head for its draft. Scores both against the actual greedy output. A tree would emit
max(lookup accepted, MTP accepted) on those steps (at the price of a bigger verify pass).
  tree_stats.py MODEL [n_samples]
"""
import json
import sys

import mlx.core as mx

from mtp import install_capture, load_mtp
from render import render
from specdec import LookupMTP, Model, MTPDrafter, PromptLookup, generate


def main():
    m = Model(sys.argv[1])
    m.cap = install_capture(m.model)
    mtp = load_mtp(m.model)
    samples = [json.loads(l) for l in open("data/samples.jsonl")]
    if len(sys.argv) > 2:
        samples = samples[: int(sys.argv[2])]
    tot = {"pl_steps": 0, "pl_acc": 0, "tree_acc": 0, "mtp_steps": 0, "mtp_acc": 0, "tokens": 0, "passes": 0,
           "pl_zero": 0, "alt_hit_on_zero": 0, "xcheck_acc": 0, "xcheck_switch": 0}
    for s in samples:
        ids, _ = render(s, m.tokenizer)
        m.reset()
        mtpd = MTPDrafter(m, mtp, 2)
        m.prefill(ids[:-1], on_chunk=mtpd.prefill_hook(ids))
        dr = LookupMTP(PromptLookup(8, 2, 2, 16, 8), mtpd)
        dr.stats = []
        toks, st = generate(m, ids, 256, dr)
        pos = 0
        for src, dlen, n, alt, new in dr.stats:
            if src == "pl":
                tot["pl_steps"] += 1
                tot["pl_acc"] += n
                a = 0
                while alt and a < len(alt) and pos + a < len(toks) and alt[a] == toks[pos + a]:
                    a += 1
                tot["tree_acc"] += max(n, a)
                # cross-check: keep the lookup draft only if MTP's first token agrees with it
                alt_right = a > 0
                if n >= 1:  # lookup's first token is the true one
                    tot["xcheck_acc"] += n if alt_right else 0
                    tot["xcheck_switch"] += not alt_right
                else:
                    tot["xcheck_acc"] += a  # disagree when MTP is right: switch to its draft
                    tot["xcheck_switch"] += alt_right
                if n == 0:
                    tot["pl_zero"] += 1
                    tot["alt_hit_on_zero"] += a > 0
            else:
                tot["mtp_steps"] += 1
                tot["mtp_acc"] += n
            pos += len(new)
        tot["tokens"] += len(toks)
        tot["passes"] += st["forwards"]
        print(s["id"], len(toks), "tok", st["forwards"], "passes", flush=True)
    t = tot
    base = t["tokens"] / t["passes"]
    gain = t["tree_acc"] - t["pl_acc"]
    print(f"\ntokens/pass now {base:.2f}; lookup steps {t['pl_steps']} (accepting {t['pl_acc']/max(1,t['pl_steps']):.2f}/step), "
          f"MTP steps {t['mtp_steps']} ({t['mtp_acc']/max(1,t['mtp_steps']):.2f}/step)")
    print(f"lookup rejected at token 0 on {t['pl_zero']} steps; MTP's draft was right there on {t['alt_hit_on_zero']}")
    print(f"2-branch tree upper bound: +{gain} tokens => tokens/pass {(t['tokens']+gain)/t['passes']:.2f} "
          f"(+{gain/t['tokens']:.1%}), before paying for the bigger verify pass")
    xg = t["xcheck_acc"] - t["pl_acc"]
    print(f"cross-check policy (lookup only if MTP agrees on token 1): {xg:+d} tokens => tokens/pass "
          f"{(t['tokens']+xg)/t['passes']:.2f} ({xg/t['tokens']:+.1%}), switched {t['xcheck_switch']} of {t['pl_steps']} lookup steps "
          f"to the 2-token MTP draft (smaller verify)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### SpecDecode/turn_split.py
"""Prefill vs decode share of real Claude Code API requests (from Kamen's transcripts).

Per request: new tokens to prefill = input_tokens + cache_creation_input_tokens (the part a
prefix cache can't reuse), decode = output_tokens, context = all input. Converted to time with
measured local rates. Prints a summary only.
  turn_split.py [prefill_tok_s] [decode_tok_s]
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import sys

PRE = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 1200.0
DEC = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 70.0
reqs = {}
for f in glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl")):
    if "SpecDecode" in f:
        continue
    for line in open(f):
        try:
            d = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        m = d.get("message")
        if d.get("type") != "assistant" or not isinstance(m, dict) or d.get("isSidechain"):
            continue
        u = m.get("usage")
        if not u or not m.get("id"):
            continue
        reqs[m["id"]] = u  # last entry per request id has the final output count
new = [u.get("input_tokens", 0) + u.get("cache_creation_input_tokens", 0) for u in reqs.values()]
out = [u.get("output_tokens", 0) for u in reqs.values()]
ctx = [u.get("input_tokens", 0) + u.get("cache_creation_input_tokens", 0) + u.get("cache_read_input_tokens", 0)
       for u in reqs.values()]
n = len(new)
tp, td = sum(new) / PRE, sum(out) / DEC
srt = sorted(new)
print(f"{n} requests; new (uncached) input tokens per request: median {srt[n//2]}, mean {sum(new)/n:.0f}, "
      f"p90 {srt[int(n*.9)]}; output tokens: median {sorted(out)[n//2]}, mean {sum(out)/n:.0f}; "
      f"context: median {sorted(ctx)[n//2]}")
print(f"at prefill {PRE:.0f} tok/s and decode {DEC:.0f} tok/s: prefill {tp/(tp+td):.0%} of model time, decode {td/(tp+td):.0%}")
big = [(a, b) for a, b in zip(new, out) if a > 20000]
print(f"requests with >20K new tokens (session starts/compactions): {len(big)} ({len(big)/n:.1%}), "
      f"holding {sum(a for a,_ in big)/max(1,sum(new)):.0%} of all prefill tokens")
small = [(a, b) for a, b in zip(new, out) if a <= 20000]
tps, tds = sum(a for a, _ in small) / PRE, sum(b for _, b in small) / DEC
print(f"ordinary turns only (<=20K new): prefill {tps/(tps+tds):.0%}, decode {tds/(tps+tds):.0%}")


### scripts/actrefit35.py
"""Activation-aware refit of the truncated 2-bit tier on REAL routed inputs (35B proxy, NewIdeas' capture data/cap35/L<l>.npz):
per expert, the 2-bit codes stay m2 = q >> 2 (nested layout); only the per-group scale/bias change. Methods:
  a trunc     stored truncation: 4s, b + s * mean(q & 3)                       (today's tier 2)
  b ls        per-group least squares in weight space                          (scripts/lsrefit2.py)
  c diag      per-group least squares weighted by the layer's channel second moments E[x_k^2]
  d act       per-row joint least squares of the outputs on the expert's own routed inputs (ridge toward a)
Fit on the even segments, scored on the odd ones: relative output error ||(W_hat - W) x|| / ||W x|| per projection
(gate/up on x; down on h = silu(gate x) * up x from the 4-bit weights). Experts: the NE most-routed of the layer.
Weights: the 8-bit unsloth 35B requantized to 4-bit g64 exactly as capture35.py did (on the CPU stream here).
usage: actrefit35.py [layer=3] [NE=24] [block]   (CPU only; ~minutes; "block": the whole MoE block, all 256
experts, plan 42222222 with truncated vs refit 2-bit ranks against the 4-bit block on held-out tokens)"""
import glob, os, sys, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

L = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 3
NE = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 24
mx.set_default_device(mx.cpu)
MODEL = glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-8bit/snapshots/*/"))[0]
t0 = time.time()
cap = np.load(os.path.expanduser(f"~/NewIdeas/data/cap35/L{L}.npz"))
X, I, seg = cap["X"].astype(np.float32), cap["I"].astype(np.int64), cap["seg"]
SPLIT = os.environ.get("REFIT_SPLIT", "parity")                         # parity | xdomain:<prose|gsm|code> (held-out source)
SRC = {"prose": 0, "gsm": 1, "code": 2}                                   # capture35: 4 segments per source, in this order
train = (seg % 2) == 0 if SPLIT == "parity" else (seg // 4) != SRC[SPLIT.split(":")[1]]
sig2 = (X[train] ** 2).mean(0)                                            # channel second moments (layer, all tokens)
pre = f"language_model.model.layers.{L}.mlp.switch_mlp."
import json
W = mx.load(MODEL + json.load(open(MODEL + "model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"][pre + "gate_proj.weight"])
counts = np.bincount(I.ravel(), minlength=256)
experts = np.argsort(-counts)[:NE]
print(f"layer {L}: {len(X)} tokens ({train.sum()} train), experts {NE} most routed ({counts[experts].min()}-{counts[experts].max()} "
      f"rows each)", flush=True)


def q4(name, e):
    """4-bit g64 codes q [out, K], scale s, bias b [out, K/64] (float32) of expert e, requantized from the 8-bit weights"""
    w = mx.dequantize(W[pre + name + ".weight"][e], W[pre + name + ".scales"][e], W[pre + name + ".biases"][e], group_size=64, bits=8)
    wq, s, b = mx.quantize(w, group_size=64, bits=4)
    mx.eval(wq, s, b)
    wq = np.array(wq)
    q = ((wq[..., None] >> (4 * np.arange(8, dtype=np.uint32))) & 15).reshape(wq.shape[0], -1).astype(np.float32)
    return q, np.array(s.astype(mx.float32)), np.array(b.astype(mx.float32))


def bf16(a):
    return np.array(mx.array(a.astype(np.float32)).astype(mx.bfloat16).astype(mx.float32))


def fits(q, s, b, Xtr):
    """per-method (alpha, beta) [out, G] for the 2-bit tier of one projection"""
    out, K = q.shape
    G = K // 64
    m = np.floor(q / 4).reshape(out, G, 64)
    w = (s[..., None] * q.reshape(out, G, 64) + b[..., None])             # 4-bit weights [out, G, 64]
    res = {"a trunc": (s * 4, b + s * (q.reshape(out, G, 64) - 4 * m).mean(-1))}
    for name, wt in (("b ls", np.ones(K, np.float32)), ("c diag", sig2 if K == X.shape[1] else None)):
        if wt is None:
            continue
        wt = wt.reshape(1, G, 64)
        sw = wt.sum(-1)
        mm, wm = (wt * m).sum(-1) / sw, (wt * w).sum(-1) / sw
        var = (wt * (m - mm[..., None]) ** 2).sum(-1)
        cov = (wt * (m - mm[..., None]) * (w - wm[..., None])).sum(-1)
