        alpha = np.where(var > 0, cov / np.maximum(var, 1e-30), 4 * s)
        res[name] = (alpha, wm - alpha * mm)
    # d act: per row, outputs y = sum_g alpha_g (x_g . m_g) + beta_g sum(x_g) fitted to x . w on the routed inputs
    if Xtr is None:
        res["d act"] = res["a trunc"]
        return {k: (bf16(a), bf16(bb)) for k, (a, bb) in res.items()}, m, w
    S = Xtr.shape[0]
    Xg = Xtr.reshape(S, G, 64)
    phi_a = np.einsum("sgk,ngk->nsg", Xg, m, optimize=True)               # [out, S, G]
    phi_b = np.broadcast_to(Xg.sum(-1)[None], (out, S, G))
    Phi = np.concatenate([phi_a, phi_b], -1)                              # [out, S, 2G]
    t = np.einsum("sgk,ngk->ns", Xg, w, optimize=True)                    # [out, S]
    th0 = np.concatenate(res["a trunc"], -1)                              # [out, 2G]
    A = np.einsum("nsi,nsj->nij", Phi, Phi, optimize=True)
    lam = float(os.environ.get("REFIT_LAMBDA", "1e-3")) * np.trace(A, axis1=1, axis2=2)[:, None, None] / (2 * G) + 1e-6
    rhs = np.einsum("nsi,ns->ni", Phi, t) + lam[..., 0] * th0
    th = np.linalg.solve(A + lam * np.eye(2 * G)[None], rhs[..., None])[..., 0]
    res["d act"] = (th[:, :G], th[:, G:])
    return {k: (bf16(a), bf16(bb)) for k, (a, bb) in res.items()}, m, w


def err(alpha, beta, m, w, Xte):
    out, G, _ = m.shape
    wh = alpha[..., None] * m + beta[..., None]
    y = Xte @ w.reshape(out, -1).T
    return np.linalg.norm(Xte @ (wh - w).reshape(out, -1).T) / np.linalg.norm(y)


if len(sys.argv) > 3 and sys.argv[3] == "block":
    S_ = cap["S"].astype(np.float32)
    te_rows = np.nonzero(~train)[0]
    pos = {t: i for i, t in enumerate(te_rows)}
    rank = np.argsort(np.argsort(-S_, axis=1), axis=1)                    # 0 = highest score in the row
    Yv = {k: np.zeros((len(te_rows), X.shape[1]), np.float32) for k in ("4bit", "a trunc", "d act")}
    silu = lambda g: g / (1 + np.exp(-g))
    for e in range(256):
        rows = np.nonzero((I == e).any(1))[0]
        tr, te = rows[train[rows]], rows[~train[rows]]
        if len(te) == 0:
            continue
        j = np.argmax(I[te] == e, axis=1)                                 # column of e in each test row
        sc, r1 = S_[te, j], rank[te, j] == 0
        W4, F = {}, {}
        for name in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
            q, s4, b4 = q4(name, e)
            if name == "down_proj":
                hh = lambda r: silu(X[r] @ W4["gate_proj"].T) * (X[r] @ W4["up_proj"].T)
                Xtr = hh(tr) if len(tr) >= 32 else np.zeros((0, q.shape[1]), np.float32)
            else:
                Xtr = X[tr] if len(tr) >= 32 else np.zeros((0, q.shape[1]), np.float32)
            fz, m, w = fits(q, s4, b4, Xtr if len(Xtr) else None)        # no calibration rows: truncation only
            W4[name] = w.reshape(w.shape[0], -1)
            F[name] = {k: (fz[k][0][..., None] * m + fz[k][1][..., None]).reshape(w.shape[0], -1) for k in ("a trunc", "d act")}
        xt = X[te]
        y4 = (silu(xt @ W4["gate_proj"].T) * (xt @ W4["up_proj"].T)) @ W4["down_proj"].T
        ii = np.array([pos[t] for t in te])
        Yv["4bit"][ii] += sc[:, None] * y4
        for k in ("a trunc", "d act"):
            y2 = (silu(xt @ F["gate_proj"][k].T) * (xt @ F["up_proj"][k].T)) @ F["down_proj"][k].T
            Yv[k][ii] += sc[:, None] * np.where(r1[:, None], y4, y2)
        if e % 32 == 31:
            print(f"  experts 0-{e} done ({time.time() - t0:.0f} s)", flush=True)
    ycap = cap["Y"][te_rows].astype(np.float32)
    nrm = np.linalg.norm(Yv["4bit"])
    print(f"layer {L} MoE block, plan 42222222 (rank 1 4-bit, ranks 2-8 2-bit), {len(te_rows)} held-out tokens: 4-bit block vs "
          f"captured Y rel {np.linalg.norm(Yv['4bit'] - ycap) / np.linalg.norm(ycap):.4f}", flush=True)
    for k in ("a trunc", "d act"):
        d = Yv[k] - Yv["4bit"]
        print(f"  {k}: block rel error {np.linalg.norm(d) / nrm:.4f} (MSE x{(np.linalg.norm(d) / np.linalg.norm(Yv['a trunc'] - Yv['4bit'])) ** 2:.3f})", flush=True)
    sys.exit(0)

tot = {}
for e in experts:
    rows = np.nonzero((I == e).any(1))[0]
    tr, te = rows[train[rows]], rows[~train[rows]]
    if len(tr) < 200 or len(te) < 100:
        continue
    line = f"e{e:3d} ({len(tr)}/{len(te)}):"
    acts = {}
    for name in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
        q, s, b = q4(name, e)
        if name == "down_proj":
            gq, uq = acts["gate_proj"], acts["up_proj"]
            h = lambda r: (lambda g, u: g / (1 + np.exp(-g)) * u)(X[r] @ gq.T, X[r] @ uq.T)
            Xtr, Xte = h(tr), h(te)
        else:
            Xtr, Xte = X[tr], X[te]
        F, m, w = fits(q, s, b, Xtr)
        acts[name] = w.reshape(w.shape[0], -1)
        es = {k: err(a, bb, m, w, Xte) for k, (a, bb) in F.items()}
        for k, v in es.items():
            tot.setdefault((name, k), []).append(v)
        line += f"  {name[:4]} " + " ".join(f"{k.split()[0]}{v:.4f}" for k, v in es.items())
    print(line, flush=True)
print(f"--- mean relative output error over experts (test segments), {time.time() - t0:.0f} s", flush=True)
for name in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
    base = np.mean(tot[(name, "a trunc")])
    print(f"{name:9s} " + "  ".join(f"{k}: {np.mean(tot[(name, k)]):.4f} (MSE x{(np.mean(tot[(name, k)]) / base) ** 2:.3f})"
                                    for k in ("a trunc", "b ls", "c diag", "d act") if (name, k) in tot), flush=True)


### scripts/admission_sim.py
"""Cache ADMISSION (not eviction) for SSD-streamed experts, on the real 125B traces (MoEPrefetch traces125/*_800.npz, verify
windows = 4-token unions, stride 3). A missed expert must be read and computed either way. With admission control it may
go to a scratch slot instead of the cache, so the hot set is not displaced by one-off experts.
  lru       : always admit, evict LRU
  tinylfu   : W-TinyLFU-style (Einziger et al. 2015): admit only if est_freq(new) > est_freq(LRU victim); frequencies
              decay (halved every W accesses per layer); a small window cache (1 %) always admits to catch new hot experts
  lfu-evict : always admit, evict the least frequently used (decayed counts), for reference
Reports misses per pass (the SSD reads) and stalled layers per pass, after a 200-token warm-up.   CPU only.
usage: admission_sim.py [FRAC]"""
import glob, os, sys
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
FRAC = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.50
E, K = 512, 10
C = int(FRAC * E)
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))


def run(policy, W=None):
    tot_m = tot_s = P = 0
    for f in files:
        z = np.load(f); NL = len(z.files)
        tr = [z[f"L{L}"][:, :K].astype(np.int64) for L in range(NL)]
        T = tr[0].shape[0]
        caches = [OrderedDict() for _ in range(NL)]
        freq = [np.zeros(E) for _ in range(NL)]
        win = [OrderedDict() for _ in range(NL)]
        Wn = max(1, int(0.01 * C)) if policy == "tinylfu" else 0
        acc = [0] * NL
        for start in range(0, T - 4, 3):
            st = 0; ms = 0
            for L in range(NL):
                u = [int(e) for e in np.unique(tr[L][start:start + 4])]
                cache, fr, w = caches[L], freq[L], win[L]
                miss = [e for e in u if e not in cache and e not in w]
                for e in u:
                    fr[e] += 1; acc[L] += 1
                    if e in cache: cache.move_to_end(e)
                    if e in w: w.move_to_end(e)
                if W and acc[L] >= W: fr *= 0.5; acc[L] = 0
                for e in miss:
                    if policy == "lru":
                        cache[e] = 1
                        if len(cache) > C: cache.popitem(last=False)
                    elif policy == "lfu-evict":
                        cache[e] = 1
                        if len(cache) > C:
                            v = min(cache, key=lambda x: fr[x]); del cache[v]
                    else:                                       # tinylfu: window first, then admission to the main cache
                        w[e] = 1
                        if len(w) > Wn:
                            cand, _ = w.popitem(last=False)
                            if len(cache) < C - Wn:
                                cache[cand] = 1
                            else:
                                victim = next(iter(cache))
                                if fr[cand] > fr[victim]:
                                    del cache[victim]; cache[cand] = 1
                if start >= 200:
                    ms += len(miss); st += bool(miss)
            if start >= 200:
                tot_m += ms; tot_s += st; P += 1
    return tot_m / P, tot_s / P


print(f"125B traces, {len(files)} prompts, C={C}/512 ({FRAC:.0%}), verify windows of 4 tokens")
for pol, W in (("lru", None), ("lfu-evict", 20 * C), ("tinylfu", 10 * C), ("tinylfu", 20 * C), ("tinylfu", 50 * C)):
    m, s = run(pol, W)
    print(f"  {pol:9s} {'' if W is None else f'decay/{W // C}C':9s}: misses/pass {m:6.1f}  stalled layers/pass {s:5.1f}", flush=True)


### scripts/budget24.py
"""QUEUE B9 + B7 (NewIdeas Sun ~06:00): SSD bytes per token at a 24 GB pool budget, all 512 experts, exact top-10 routing.
125B traces (Breakthrough's expA traces125: bulgarian / chat / code / math, 800 tokens, rank-ordered top-20 per layer).
Per layer, LRU pools under the byte budget of today's 24 GB split (C_m 147 MSB-3 + C_l 46 LSB-1 = 353.3 MB per layer):
  A0  today's split, plan 4433333333 (ranks 0-1 need MSB + LSB, ranks 2-9 MSB)
  A   the same plan, split rebalanced (C_l swept, C_m from the rest of the budget)
  B   + a 2-bit WARM tier (HOBBIT-style multi-precision residency): ranks 4-9 use the MSB-3 copy if resident, else the
      2-bit copy if resident, else read the 2-bit copy (Q2 bytes); ranks 0-3 as in A (2-bit never serves them);
      C_l and C_2 swept, C_m from the rest of the budget
  C   reference plans: 3333333333 (one MSB pool) and 4444444444 (one full-record pool)
Each set: tokens 0-399 warm the pools (not counted), tokens 400-799 measured. Per-token replay (misses are drafting-
invariant; NewIdeas) plus 16-row checks accepting 10 tokens (union per check) for the best configs.
usage: budget24.py [Q2_bytes]   (CPU only)"""
import glob, os, sys, time
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np

NL, E = 48, 512
MSB, LSB = 2162688, 770048                      # records3 parts (index.json): msb_sz, lsb_sz
Q2 = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 1540096   # planar MSB-2 + bf16 g64 scales / biases, 16 KB aligned
BUD = 147 * MSB + 46 * LSB                      # today's 24 GB split per layer (353.3 MB)
T = "/Users/kamen/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/"
SETS = {os.path.basename(f).split("_")[0]: np.load(f) for f in sorted(glob.glob(T + "*_800_g.npz"))}
IDS = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1)[:, :, :10].astype(np.int32) for k, d in SETS.items()}


class LRU:
    __slots__ = ("C", "od")

    def __init__(self, C):
        self.C, self.od = C, OrderedDict()

    def has(self, e):
        return e in self.od

    def touch(self, e):                         # hit: refresh; miss: insert (evict LRU); returns 1 on a miss
        od = self.od
        if e in od:
            od.move_to_end(e)
            return 0
        if self.C <= 0:
            return 1
        od[e] = True
        if len(od) > self.C:
            od.popitem(last=False)
        return 1


def replay(ids, cfg, R=1, A=1):
    """cfg: dict(plan, Cm, Cl, C2, Cf). R rows per check, A tokens accepted per check. Returns (bytes per token, stats)."""
    n = ids.shape[0]
    plan = cfg["plan"]
    pm = [LRU(cfg.get("Cm", 0)) for _ in range(NL)]
    pl = [LRU(cfg.get("Cl", 0)) for _ in range(NL)]
    p2 = [LRU(cfg.get("C2", 0)) for _ in range(NL)]
    pf = [LRU(cfg.get("Cf", 0)) for _ in range(NL)]
    st = dict(bytes=0, msb=0, lsb=0, q2=0, full=0, low_uses=0, low_2bit=0, tok=0)
    pos = 0
    while pos + R <= n:
        meas = pos >= n // 2
        for l in range(NL):
            need = {}                                # expert -> best (lowest) rank among the check's rows
            for r in range(R):
                for k, e in enumerate(ids[pos + r, l]):
                    e = int(e)
                    if e not in need or k < need[e]:
                        need[e] = k
            for e, k in need.items():
                b = 0
                if plan == "4444444444":
                    b += pf[l].touch(e) * (MSB + LSB); st["full"] += b > 0
                elif plan == "3333333333":
                    m = pm[l].touch(e); b += m * MSB; st["msb"] += m
                elif plan == "3B" and k < 4:         # 3B: plan 3333333333, ranks 4-9 cold -> 2-bit
                    m = pm[l].touch(e); b += m * MSB; st["msb"] += m
                elif k < 2 and plan != "3B":         # 4-bit ranks: MSB + LSB
                    m = pm[l].touch(e); s = pl[l].touch(e); b += m * MSB + s * LSB; st["msb"] += m; st["lsb"] += s
                elif k < 4 or plan == "4433333333":  # 3-bit ranks (plans 4433333333, B, Bnp)
                    m = pm[l].touch(e); b += m * MSB; st["msb"] += m
                else:                                # plan "B" / "Bnp" / "3B": ranks 4-9 take MSB-3 if resident, else 2-bit
                    st["low_uses"] += meas
                    if pm[l].has(e):
                        if plan != "Bnp":                # Bnp: a low-rank hit does not refresh the MSB LRU
                            pm[l].touch(e)
                    else:
                        q = p2[l].touch(e); b += q * Q2; st["q2"] += q; st["low_2bit"] += meas
                if meas:
                    st["bytes"] += b
        if meas:
            st["tok"] += A
        pos += A
    return st["bytes"] / max(1, st["tok"]), st


def mean_bpt(cfg, R=1, A=1):
    out = {k: replay(v, cfg, R, A) for k, v in IDS.items()}
    return float(np.mean([b for b, _ in out.values()])), out


def fit(Cl, C2=0, q2=Q2):
    return int((BUD - Cl * LSB - C2 * q2) // MSB)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    t0 = time.time()
    lines = []

    def say(s):
        print(s, flush=True); lines.append(s)
    say(f"budget per layer {BUD / 1e6:.1f} MB (C_m 147 + C_l 46); parts MSB {MSB}, LSB {LSB}, Q2 {Q2}")
    a0, o0 = mean_bpt(dict(plan="4433333333", Cm=147, Cl=46))
    say(f"A0 today 147 / 46: {a0 / 1e6:.1f} MB/token  (" + ", ".join(f"{k} {b / 1e6:.1f}" for k, (b, _) in o0.items()) + ")")
    best_a = (a0, 46)
    for Cl in range(0, 161, 8):
        b, _ = mean_bpt(dict(plan="4433333333", Cm=fit(Cl), Cl=Cl))
        say(f"A  C_m {fit(Cl)} C_l {Cl}: {b / 1e6:.1f} MB/token ({100 * (b / a0 - 1):+.1f} %)")
        best_a = min(best_a, (b, Cl))
    say(f"A best: C_l {best_a[1]} (C_m {fit(best_a[1])}): {best_a[0] / 1e6:.1f} MB/token ({100 * (best_a[0] / a0 - 1):+.1f} %)")
    best_b = None
    for Cl in (16, 32, 48, 64, 96):
        for C2 in (0, 32, 64, 96, 128, 160, 192, 224):
            Cm = fit(Cl, C2)
            if Cm < 16:
                continue
            b, o = mean_bpt(dict(plan="B", Cm=Cm, Cl=Cl, C2=C2))
            lu = sum(s["low_uses"] for _, s in o.values()); l2 = sum(s["low_2bit"] for _, s in o.values())
            say(f"B  C_m {Cm} C_l {Cl} C_2 {C2}: {b / 1e6:.1f} MB/token ({100 * (b / a0 - 1):+.1f} %), rank 4-9 uses at 2-bit "
                f"{100 * l2 / max(1, lu):.0f} %")
            if best_b is None or b < best_b[0]:
                best_b = (b, Cm, Cl, C2, l2 / max(1, lu))
    say(f"B best: C_m {best_b[1]} C_l {best_b[2]} C_2 {best_b[3]}: {best_b[0] / 1e6:.1f} MB/token "
        f"({100 * (best_b[0] / a0 - 1):+.1f} %), rank 4-9 uses at 2-bit {100 * best_b[4]:.0f} %")
    c3, _ = mean_bpt(dict(plan="3333333333", Cm=int(BUD // MSB)))
    c4, _ = mean_bpt(dict(plan="4444444444", Cf=int(BUD // (MSB + LSB))))
    say(f"C  3333333333 C {int(BUD // MSB)}: {c3 / 1e6:.1f} MB/token ({100 * (c3 / a0 - 1):+.1f} %); 4444444444 C "
        f"{int(BUD // (MSB + LSB))}: {c4 / 1e6:.1f} MB/token ({100 * (c4 / a0 - 1):+.1f} %)")
    for lab, cfg in (("A0", dict(plan="4433333333", Cm=147, Cl=46)), ("A best", dict(plan="4433333333", Cm=fit(best_a[1]), Cl=best_a[1])),
                     ("B best", dict(plan="B", Cm=best_b[1], Cl=best_b[2], C2=best_b[3]))):
        b16, _ = mean_bpt(cfg, R=16, A=10)
        say(f"16-row checks (10 accepted): {lab}: {b16 / 1e6:.1f} MB/token")
    say(f"({time.time() - t0:.0f} s)")
    open(os.path.expanduser(f"~/MoonShots/data/budget24_q{Q2}.txt"), "w").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")


### scripts/code_entropy.py
"""Higher-order entropy of MLX 4-bit weight codes. SpecDecode measured order-0 at 3.6–3.7 of 4 bits. MLX issue #3043
claims 2.17 bits. If neighbouring codes (same row, next column; same column, next row) predict each other,
a fused rANS/table decoder could cut *all* weight bytes losslessly. CPU, mmap, one tensor at a time.
Usage: code_entropy.py [model_dir] [max_tensors]"""
import glob, json, os, struct, sys
import numpy as np

D = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
MAXT = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 12


def H(counts):
    p = counts[counts > 0] / counts.sum()
    return float(-(p * np.log2(p)).sum())


def cond_H(a, b, k=16):
    """H(b | a) for code arrays a, b of equal shape."""
    joint = np.bincount((a.astype(np.int64) * k + b).ravel(), minlength=k * k).reshape(k, k)
    return H(joint.ravel()) - H(joint.sum(1))


done = 0
for f in sorted(glob.glob(f"{D}/*.safetensors")):
    with open(f, "rb") as fh:
        n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]
        hdr = json.loads(fh.read(n))
    for name, m in hdr.items():
        if not name.endswith(".weight") or m["dtype"] != "U32" or len(m["shape"]) < 2:
            continue
        if done >= MAXT or (done % 3 and "experts" not in name and "switch" not in name):
            pass
        a, b = m["data_offsets"]
        shp = m["shape"]
        rows = shp[-2] if len(shp) == 2 else shp[-2]
        words = np.memmap(f, np.uint32, "r", offset=8 + n + a, shape=((b - a) // 4,))
        take = min(len(words), 4_000_000)                     # ≤ 16 MB per tensor
        w = np.asarray(words[:take])
        codes = np.stack([(w >> (4 * i)) & 0xF for i in range(8)], 1).reshape(-1).astype(np.uint8)
        cols = shp[-1] * 8
        nrow = len(codes) // cols
        c = codes[: nrow * cols].reshape(nrow, cols)
        h0 = H(np.bincount(c.ravel(), minlength=16))
        h_row = cond_H(c[:, :-1], c[:, 1:])                   # next column, same row
        h_col = cond_H(c[:-1, :], c[1:, :]) if nrow > 1 else float("nan")   # next row, same column
        print(f"{name[-60:]:60s} H0 {h0:.3f}  H(next col|prev) {h_row:.3f}  H(next row|prev) {h_col:.3f} bits", flush=True)
        done += 1
        if done >= MAXT:
            sys.exit()


### scripts/code_entropy125.py
"""How compressible (losslessly) are the 125B's routed-expert 4-bit codes? For layers 0,12,24,36,47 x gate/up/down, a sample of
experts: order-0 entropy H0 of the nibbles, a static Huffman code's mean length (one table per tensor), and the same with one
table per (tensor, nibble position in its 64-value group) and per expert, plus the bytes saved per expert (scales/biases
kept raw). Lossless => a decoder reproduces the exact codes. CPU only.   usage: code_entropy125.py [experts_per_tensor]"""
import heapq, json, os, struct, sys
import numpy as np
NE = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 48
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
idx = json.load(open(f"{MD}/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)


def raw(name, sel):
    f = f"{MD}/{idx[name]}"
    with open(f, "rb") as fh:
        n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]; m = json.loads(fh.read(n))[name]
    a, b = m["data_offsets"]
    mm = np.memmap(f, np.uint32, "r", offset=8 + n + a, shape=((b - a) // 4,)).reshape(m["shape"])
    return np.array(mm[sel])                                     # [ne, rows, words]


def nibbles(w):
    w = w.astype(np.uint32)
    return np.stack([(w >> (4 * j)) & 15 for j in range(8)], axis=-1).reshape(w.shape[0], -1).astype(np.uint8)   # [ne, rows*K]


def huff_len(counts):
    """mean code length of a Huffman code for these symbol counts."""
    c = [int(x) for x in counts if x > 0]
    if len(c) == 1: return 1.0
    h = [(x, i, (i,)) for i, x in enumerate(c)]; heapq.heapify(h); depth = [0] * len(c); k = len(c)
    while len(h) > 1:
        a, _, sa = heapq.heappop(h); b, _, sb = heapq.heappop(h)
        for s in sa + sb: depth[s] += 1
        heapq.heappush(h, (a + b, k, sa + sb)); k += 1
    return sum(d * x for d, x in zip(depth, c)) / sum(c)


def H(counts):
    p = counts[counts > 0] / counts.sum()
    return float(-(p * np.log2(p)).sum())


tot_bits, tot_raw = 0.0, 0.0
for L in (0, 12, 24, 36, 47):
    sel = np.sort(rng.choice(512, NE, replace=False))
    for proj in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
        w = raw(f"language_model.model.layers.{L}.mlp.switch_mlp.{proj}.weight", sel)
        q = nibbles(w)                                            # [NE, rows*K]
        cnt = np.bincount(q.ravel(), minlength=16).astype(np.float64)
        h0, hl = H(cnt), huff_len(cnt)
        pe = np.mean([huff_len(np.bincount(q[i], minlength=16)) for i in range(NE)])        # one table per expert
        pos = q.reshape(NE, -1, 64)
        pp = np.mean([huff_len(np.bincount(pos[:, :, j].ravel(), minlength=16)) for j in range(64)])   # per group position
        print(f"L{L:2d} {proj:9s}: H0 {h0:.3f} bits | Huffman per tensor {hl:.3f}, per expert {pe:.3f}, per group position {pp:.3f} "
              f"| top code share {cnt.max()/cnt.sum():.3f}", flush=True)
        tot_bits += min(hl, pe) * q.size; tot_raw += 4 * q.size
print(f"routed-expert codes: Huffman {tot_bits / tot_raw * 4:.3f} bits per 4-bit code -> codes {100 * (1 - tot_bits / tot_raw):.1f} % "
      f"smaller; per expert (codes are {2.6/2.77:.0%} of 2.77 MB, scales/biases raw): {100 * (1 - tot_bits / tot_raw) * 0.94:.1f} % fewer bytes")


### scripts/critical_chain125.py
"""Round 32k: the dependent-kernel chain of a 125B verify pass, offline from the exported MLX graphs (data/pass_graph125.dot,
data/_layer.dot; round 26-29 exports of an unevaluated 4-row forward). DAG over primitives (prim -> prim when an output
array of one is an input of the other); weight 1 per kernel, 0 per free view op (Reshape, Broadcast, ExpandDims, Squeeze,
Flatten, Unflatten, Slice, Split, Transpose, AsStrided, StopGradient: round 29's classification). Prints the number of
kernel nodes, the longest weighted path (= dependent kernels on the critical chain), per layer (/48), and the label
histogram along the critical chain (what a chain-shortening fusion would have to merge).
usage: critical_chain125.py [dot ...]   (CPU, seconds)"""
import collections, re, sys

FREE = {"Reshape", "Broadcast", "ExpandDims", "Squeeze", "Flatten", "Unflatten", "Slice", "Split", "Transpose",
        "AsStrided", "StopGradient", "Depends"}
files = sys.argv[1:] or ["/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/pass_graph125.dot", "/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/_layer.dot"]
for f in files:
    # a primitive object can be shared by several ops (compiled / custom kernels reused across layers): each definition
    # line is its own node, and the edges that follow it (until the id's next definition) belong to that occurrence
    label, a2p_out, a2p_in = {}, {}, collections.defaultdict(list)   # node labels; array -> producing node; array -> consumers
    occ, cur = collections.Counter(), {}
    for line in open(f):
        m = re.match(r'\{ (\d+) \[label ="([^"]+)"', line.strip())
        if m:
            occ[m.group(1)] += 1
            cur[m.group(1)] = f"{m.group(1)}#{occ[m.group(1)]}"
            label[cur[m.group(1)]] = m.group(2)
            continue
        m = re.match(r'"?([^"\s]+)"? -> "?([^"\s]+)"?$', line.strip())
        if m:
            a, b = m.group(1), m.group(2)
            if a.isdigit():                                  # prim -> array (output)
                a2p_out[b] = cur[a]
            else:                                            # array -> prim (input)
                a2p_in[a].append(cur[b])
    succ = collections.defaultdict(set)
    indeg = collections.Counter()
    for arr, prod in a2p_out.items():
        for cons in a2p_in.get(arr, []):
            if cons != prod and cons not in succ[prod]:
                succ[prod].add(cons)
                indeg[cons] += 1
    nodes = list(label)
    w = {n: (0 if label[n] in FREE else 1) for n in nodes}
    order, q = [], [n for n in nodes if indeg[n] == 0]
    indeg2 = dict(indeg)
    while q:
        n = q.pop()
        order.append(n)
        for s in succ[n]:
            indeg2[s] -= 1
            if indeg2[s] == 0:
                q.append(s)
    dist, prev = {}, {}
    for n in order:
        d0 = dist.get(n, 0) + w[n]
        dist[n] = d0
        for s in succ[n]:
            if d0 > dist.get(s, -1) - 0:                     # longest path: keep the max predecessor
                if d0 >= dist.get(s, 0):
                    dist[s] = d0
                    prev[s] = n
    # dist[s] currently holds max over preds of (pred's dist incl. pred weight); add own weight when visited above
    end = max(order, key=lambda n: dist[n])
    path, n = [], end
    while n is not None:
        path.append(n)
        n = prev.get(n)
    path.reverse()
    chain = [label[n] for n in path if w[n]]
    kern = sum(w.values())
    print(f"{f.split('/')[-1]}: {len(nodes)} primitives, {kern} kernels; critical chain {len(chain)} kernels "
          f"({len(chain) / 48:.1f} per layer if this is a full pass); unordered nodes {len(nodes) - len(order)}", flush=True)
    print("   chain kernels by type:", dict(collections.Counter(chain).most_common(14)), flush=True)
    seg = chain[: min(60, len(chain))]
    print("   chain start:", " > ".join(seg[:40]), flush=True)

# per-layer chain segments: split the critical chain at the router's ArgPartition (one per MoE layer)
if len(sys.argv) <= 1:
    segs, cur_seg = [], []
    for k in chain:
        cur_seg.append(k)
        if k == "ArgPartition":
            segs.append(cur_seg); cur_seg = []
    lens = [len(s) for s in segs]
    print(f"\nlayer segments on the chain (ending at each router ArgPartition): {len(segs)}, kernels each: {lens}", flush=True)
    for i in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        if i < len(segs):
            print(f"segment {i} ({len(segs[i])}): " + " > ".join(segs[i]), flush=True)


### scripts/depth_model125.py
"""Round 32i: optimal MTP draft depth in today's engine vs a levered one (pass model, scripts/pass_model125.py).
Per-depth conditional acceptance from the trace's MTP stats (verify_trace125.json meta: accepted/drafted by depth, 5
prompts): pooled d1 0.854, d2 0.796, d3 0.825 (3.09 tokens/pass); beyond depth 3 extrapolated at a(d) = 0.80 - 0.02 (d - 3).
Pass cost at depth D: rows = D + 1, expert union and misses x ((D + 1) / 4)^0.67, plus D draft steps x c_d ms (unknown
until the DRAFT_SYNC profile: c_d = 1.5 and 3 ms).   usage: depth_model125.py   (CPU, instant)"""
import json, os, sys
sys.argv = [sys.argv[0]]
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
import importlib.util
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("pm", os.path.join(HERE, "pass_model125.py"))
src = open(os.path.join(HERE, "pass_model125.py")).read().split("STACKS = [")[0]
pm = {}; exec(src, pm)
meta = json.load(open("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/verify_trace125.json"))["meta"]
acc = [sum(m["accepted_by_depth"][d] for m in meta) for d in range(3)]
drf = [sum(m["drafted_by_depth"][d] for m in meta) for d in range(3)]
a = [acc[0] / drf[0], acc[1] / acc[0], acc[2] / acc[1]]
print("pooled conditional acceptance d1..d3:", [round(x, 3) for x in a])
def cond(d):
    return a[d - 1] if d <= 3 else max(0.4, 0.80 - 0.02 * (d - 3))
def tokens(D):
    t, p = 1.0, 1.0
    for d in range(1, D + 1):
        p *= cond(d); t += p
    return t
REG = {"today 17 GB": (17.0, set()), "today 34 GB": (34.0, set()),
       "levered 17 GB": (17.0, {"3c", "t3", "eff", "thin", "hide", "opt"}),
       "levered 34 GB": (34.0, {"3c", "t3", "eff", "thin", "hide", "opt"})}
for cd in (1.5, 3.0):
    print(f"\ndraft step cost {cd} ms (the base pass model already includes 3 steps in 'between'; extra steps beyond 3 cost {cd} ms each)")
    for name, (gb, lv) in REG.items():
        row = []
        for D in range(1, 9):
            sc = ((D + 1) / 4) ** 0.67
            _, T = pm["tps"](gb, lv, tok=1.0, scale=sc)
            T += (D - 3) * cd
            row.append((1000 * tokens(D) / T, D))
        best = max(row)
        print(f"  {name:14s} " + " ".join(f"D{D}:{v:5.1f}" for v, D in row) + f"   best D={best[1]} ({best[0]:.1f} tok/s; tokens/pass {tokens(best[1]):.2f})")


### scripts/exact_stack125.py
"""Projection: MoonShots' exact pieces stacked on the 24 GB setting (C=128, 17 GB of experts), per verify pass of 4 rows
and ~3.1 tokens. Base 131 ms/pass (pass model at 17 GB: compute 46 + between 17 + stalls ~68 at 0.30 ms x 226 misses;
measured 23.7 tok/s depth 2). Savings (ms/pass), measured where noted:
  3c+combine      routed GLU -25 %: 48 x (0.421 - 0.315) ms at M=4 (bench, measured)              -5.1
  3-bit tiers     SSD bytes per miss -22 % (MSB part + LSB for 2 of 10 ranks); stalls latency-bound, take -15 %  -10.2
  out-of-order    post-spin path at M=4: 0.314 -> 0.122 ms/layer at 30 % misses (measured); applies to layers that stall (~all at 17 GB)  -9.2
  exact GDN       prework measured 0.035 ms/layer x 36 + gated norm est. 0.012 x 36                    -1.7
The pieces touch different time (GLU compute, stall bytes, stall overlap, dense chain); OoO and 3c overlap a little
(OoO hides the 3c hit compute), so the stack is applied with 3c's saving counted on the miss pairs only under OoO.
usage: exact_stack125.py   (instant)"""
base, tok = 131.0, 3.1
s3c, st3, sooo, sgdn = 5.1, 10.2, 12.5, 1.7   # OoO at 24 GB: stalls ~1.5 ms/layer >> hit compute (round 32o)
rows = [("today (pass model, profile config)", 0.0), ("+ 3c+combine", s3c), ("+ 3-bit tiers (4433333333)", s3c + st3),
        ("+ out-of-order experts", s3c * 0.3 + st3 + sooo), ("+ exact GDN prework + gated norm", s3c * 0.3 + st3 + sooo + sgdn)]
for name, sav in rows:
    T = base - sav
    print(f"{name:40s} {T:6.1f} ms/pass  {1000 * tok / T:5.1f} tok/s  ({base / T - 1:+.0%})")


### scripts/expert_align125.py
"""Are the 125B's routed experts the same up to a permutation of their 640 intermediate neurons? (Follow-up to
expert_similarity.py, which found raw-coordinate correlation 0; Git Re-Basin-style weight matching.) A SwiGLU expert is
invariant to permuting its intermediate neurons (rows of gate/up, columns of down) and to flipping the sign of one neuron's
(up row, down column) pair. Per neuron j the features are g_j, u_j, d_j (each length 2560, standardized).
  joint match : score(j, k) = corr(g_aj, g_bk) + |corr(u_aj, u_bk) + corr(d_aj, d_bk)|, greedy max-weight matching (no
                scipy here), then the aligned correlation of gate / up / down (sign-corrected per neuron).
  held-out    : match on gate rows only, then score up/down under that permutation. A true permutation aligns them too;
                a spurious one leaves them at the ~0.02 noise level.
Null: the same matching between experts of different layers (no shared origin); greedy matching of 640 neurons in 2560
dims still finds spurious maxima, so the same-layer number must be read against it. Delta coding an expert against an
aligned partner saves ~0.5*log2(1/(1-rho^2)) bits/weight (Gaussian rate-distortion), so rho must reach ~0.87 to save 1 bit.
CPU only, < 1 GB.   usage: expert_align125.py [experts_per_layer]"""
import json, os, struct, sys, time
import numpy as np
NE = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 6
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
idx = json.load(open(f"{MD}/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
rng = np.random.default_rng(3)


def raw(name, sel, dt):
    f = f"{MD}/{idx[name]}"
    with open(f, "rb") as fh:
        n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]; m = json.loads(fh.read(n))[name]
    a, b = m["data_offsets"]
    mm = np.memmap(f, dt, "r", offset=8 + n + a, shape=((b - a) // np.dtype(dt).itemsize,)).reshape(m["shape"])
    return np.array(mm[sel])


def std(x):                                     # standardize each row
    x = x - x.mean(-1, keepdims=True)
    return x / x.std(-1, keepdims=True)


def experts(L, sel):
    """[{g, u, d}] per expert, each [640, 2560] with standardized rows (d = down columns)."""
    f32 = lambda u: (u.astype(np.uint32) << 16).view(np.float32)
    m = {}
    for proj in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
        pre = f"language_model.model.layers.{L}.mlp.switch_mlp.{proj}."
        w = raw(pre + "weight", sel, np.uint32); s = f32(raw(pre + "scales", sel, np.uint16)); b = f32(raw(pre + "biases", sel, np.uint16))
        q = np.stack([(w >> (4 * j)) & 15 for j in range(8)], -1).reshape(len(sel), w.shape[1], -1).astype(np.float32)
        m[proj] = (q.reshape(len(sel), q.shape[1], -1, 64) * s[..., None] + b[..., None]).reshape(len(sel), q.shape[1], -1)
    return [{"g": std(m["gate_proj"][i]), "u": std(m["up_proj"][i]), "d": std(m["down_proj"][i].T)} for i in range(len(sel))]


def greedy_match(S):
    n = S.shape[0]
    used_r, used_c, perm, k = np.zeros(n, bool), np.zeros(n, bool), np.full(n, -1), 0
    for flat in np.argsort(-S, axis=None):
        i, j = divmod(int(flat), n)
        if used_r[i] or used_c[j]:
            continue
        perm[i] = j; used_r[i] = used_c[j] = True; k += 1
        if k == n:
            break
    return perm


def compare(A, B):
    D = A["g"].shape[1]
    Cg, Cu, Cd = (A[x] @ B[x].T / D for x in "gud")
    j = np.arange(Cg.shape[0])
    su = Cu + Cd
    p = greedy_match(Cg + np.abs(su))                      # joint match
    sg = np.sign(su[j, p])
    joint = (Cg[j, p].mean(), (sg * Cu[j, p]).mean(), (sg * Cd[j, p]).mean())
    pg = greedy_match(Cg)                                  # gate-only match, up/down held out
    held = (Cg[j, pg].mean(), np.abs(Cu[j, pg]).mean(), np.abs(Cd[j, pg]).mean())
    ident = (np.diag(Cg).mean(), np.diag(Cu).mean(), np.diag(Cd).mean())
    return np.array(joint + held + ident)


def bits(rho):
    return 0.5 * np.log2(1 / max(1e-9, 1 - rho ** 2))


t0 = time.time()
LAYERS = (0, 24, 47)
E = {L: experts(L, np.sort(rng.choice(512, NE, replace=False))) for L in LAYERS}
print(f"125B, {NE} experts per layer, layers {LAYERS}; loaded in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
hdr = "joint match g/u/d | gate-only match: g, held-out |u|/|d| | identity g/u/d"
for L in LAYERS:
    same = np.mean([compare(E[L][a], E[L][b]) for a in range(NE) for b in range(a + 1, NE)], 0)
    L2 = LAYERS[(LAYERS.index(L) + 1) % len(LAYERS)]
    null = np.mean([compare(E[L][a], E[L2][a]) for a in range(NE)], 0)
    for tag, v in (("same layer", same), (f"null L{L}-L{L2}", null)):
        print(f"L{L:2d} {tag:14s}: joint {v[0]:.3f}/{v[1]:.3f}/{v[2]:.3f} | gate-only {v[3]:.3f}, held-out {v[4]:.3f}/{v[5]:.3f} "
              f"| identity {v[6]:+.3f}/{v[7]:+.3f}/{v[8]:+.3f}", flush=True)
    rho = same[:3].mean()
    print(f"L{L:2d} delta coding vs the aligned partner would save ~{bits(rho):.3f} bits/weight (rho {rho:.3f}; null rho "
          f"{null[:3].mean():.3f})", flush=True)
print(f"({hdr}; {time.time() - t0:.0f}s)")


### scripts/expert_bits35.py
"""How few bits do fine-grained MoE experts need? (memory-halving hunt; NOT bit-exact: quality is the measure)
Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 4-bit (256 experts, top-8), fully resident; ~2K tokens of real text, teacher-forced. Routed experts only are
requantized (dense layers, routers and shared experts stay 4-bit). Per variant: perplexity, mean NLL delta, and greedy top-1
agreement with the 4-bit model.
  b3        : all routed experts RTN 3-bit g64 (from the dequantized 4-bit weights)
  b2 / b2g32: RTN 2-bit g64 / g32
  mixR      : gate-rank-aware precision: each token's top-R experts (by gate) at 4-bit, the rest at 2-bit g32 (errors of
              low-gate experts are scaled by their small gate weight). Memory model: hot experts kept at 4-bit, the rest 2-bit.
usage: expert_bits35.py   (GPU, ~30 GB: exclusive window)"""
import math, os, sys, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx_lm import load

MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
text = ""
for p in ("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md", "~/BigMoE/FINDINGS.md", "~/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md"):
    text += open(os.path.expanduser(p)).read() + "\n\n"
ids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:2049])[None]
inp, tgt = ids[:, :-1], ids[:, 1:]


def logits():
    out = model(inp)
    return out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out


def score(lg, ref_top=None):
    lp = lg[0].astype(mx.float32) - mx.logsumexp(lg[0].astype(mx.float32), axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lp, tgt[0][:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0]
    top = mx.argmax(lg[0], axis=-1)
    mx.eval(nll, top)
    agree = float((top == ref_top).astype(mx.float32).mean().item()) if ref_top is not None else 1.0
    return float(nll.mean().item()), agree, top


base_nll, _, base_top = score(logits())
print(f"4-bit reference: ppl {math.exp(base_nll):.4f} over {inp.shape[1]} tokens", flush=True)

sws = [l.mlp.switch_mlp for l in tm.layers]
orig = [{p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases, getattr(sw, p).bits, getattr(sw, p).group_size)
         for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")} for sw in sws]


def requant(bits, gs):
    out = []
    for sw, o in zip(sws, orig):
        d = {}
        for p, (w, s, b, ob, og) in o.items():
            deq = mx.dequantize(w, s, b, group_size=og, bits=ob)
            d[p] = tuple(mx.quantize(deq, group_size=gs, bits=bits)) + (bits, gs)   # quantize returns a list
        mx.eval([v[:3] for v in d.values()])
        out.append(d)
    return out


def install(qs):
    for sw, d in zip(sws, qs):
        for p, (w, s, b, bits, gs) in d.items():
            m = getattr(sw, p); m.weight, m.scales, m.biases, m.bits, m.group_size = w, s, b, bits, gs


def restore():
    install([{p: v for p, v in o.items()} for o in orig])


for name, bits, gs in (("b3", 3, 64), ("b2", 2, 64), ("b2g32", 2, 32)):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    install(requant(bits, gs))
    nll, ag, _ = score(logits(), base_top)
    restore(); mx.clear_cache()
    print(f"{name:6s}: ppl {math.exp(nll):.4f} (dNLL {nll - base_nll:+.4f}), greedy top-1 agreement {ag:.4f}  ({time.perf_counter()-t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)

# gate-rank-aware mix: run each MoE block with two SwitchGLU weight sets, top-R ranks on 4-bit, the rest on 2-bit g32
q2 = requant(2, 32)
MoE = type(tm.layers[0].mlp)
orig_call = MoE.__call__
R = [None]


def mixed_call(self, x):
    gates = self.gate(x)
    gates = mx.softmax(gates, axis=-1, precise=True)
    k = self.top_k
    inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
    scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    if getattr(self, "norm_topk_prob", False):
        scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    order = mx.argsort(-scores, axis=-1)                        # rank by gate
    inds = mx.take_along_axis(inds, order, axis=-1); scores = mx.take_along_axis(scores, order, axis=-1)
    sw = self.switch_mlp
    y = sw(x, inds[..., :R[0]])                                 # MLX is lazy: each call captures the arrays installed now
    if R[0] < k:
        keep = {p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases, getattr(sw, p).bits, getattr(sw, p).group_size)
                for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")}
        for p, (w, s, b, bits, gs) in q2[self._li].items():
            m = getattr(sw, p); m.weight, m.scales, m.biases, m.bits, m.group_size = w, s, b, bits, gs
        lo = sw(x, inds[..., R[0]:])
        for p, (w, s, b, bits, gs) in keep.items():
            m = getattr(sw, p); m.weight, m.scales, m.biases, m.bits, m.group_size = w, s, b, bits, gs
        y = mx.concatenate([y, lo], axis=-2)
    y = (y * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    sh = self.shared_expert(x)
    if hasattr(self, "shared_expert_gate"):
        sh = mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * sh
    return y + sh


for li, l in enumerate(tm.layers):
    object.__setattr__(l.mlp, "_li", li)
MoE.__call__ = mixed_call
for r in (8, 1, 2, 4, 6):                                       # mix8 = all ranks 4-bit: must match the reference
    R[0] = r
    nll, ag, _ = score(logits(), base_top)
    print(f"mix{r}  : top-{r} ranks 4-bit, rest 2-bit g32: ppl {math.exp(nll):.4f} (dNLL {nll - base_nll:+.4f}), agreement {ag:.4f}", flush=True)
MoE.__call__ = orig_call


### scripts/expert_planes35.py
"""Memory-halving hunt, round 2 (NOT bit-exact: quality is the measure). Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 4-bit, routed experts only, 2048
teacher-forced tokens of real text; per variant: perplexity, mean NLL delta and greedy top-1 agreement vs the 4-bit model.

1. Nested bit-planes, training-free: slice each existing 4-bit code q = 4m + l into an MSB plane m (2 bits, with its own
   scale 4s and bias b + off*s) and an LSB plane l (2 bits, scale s, bias 0). MSB + LSB reproduces the 4-bit weights exactly,
   so a runtime can keep the MSB plane of every expert resident and stream the LSB plane only for each token's top-R gate
   ranks: those ranks stay exactly 4-bit, the rest run on 2.5 bits/weight (vs 4.5). Offsets: msb2 = bin midpoint 1.5,
   msb2m = per-group mean of the LSB codes (same storage).
   mixR: each token's top-R of 8 ranks on the full 4-bit weights, the other ranks on msb2m (or another 2-bit set).
   Prior art: HOBBIT (2024) loads separate low-precision copies of less important experts on cache misses; MatQuant (2025)
   nests int2 in int4/int8 by MSB slicing but co-trains for it. Not found: training-free MSB/LSB planes of an existing 4-bit
   MoE with gate-rank-aware LSB streaming.
2. Cheap training-free fixes for plain RTN at 2-3 bits: clip (per-group MSE-optimal symmetric range shrink, a grid search
   around the group midpoint), rot (randomized Hadamard rotation of each projection's input dim, QuIP#-style incoherence,
   then RTN; the runtime rotates the activations: x @ Q for gate/up, h @ Q for down).
Checks: a packing unit test (MSB + LSB planes == the 4-bit dequant) and `path4`, the 4-bit weights through this script's own
MoE path (must match the reference up to summation order).
usage: expert_planes35.py   (GPU, ~35 GB peak: exclusive window)"""
import math, os, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models.switch_layers import _gather_sort, _scatter_unsort

MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
text = ""
for p in ("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md", "~/BigMoE/FINDINGS.md", "~/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md"):
    text += open(os.path.expanduser(p)).read() + "\n\n"
ids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:2049])[None]
inp, tgt = ids[:, :-1], ids[:, 1:]
P3 = ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")


def score(ref_top=None):
    out = model(inp)
    lg = (out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out)[0].astype(mx.float32)
    nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True), tgt[0][:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0]
    top = mx.argmax(lg, axis=-1)
    mx.eval(nll, top)
    agree = float((top == ref_top).astype(mx.float32).mean().item()) if ref_top is not None else 1.0
    return float(nll.mean().item()), agree, top


base_nll, _, base_top = score()
print(f"4-bit reference: ppl {math.exp(base_nll):.4f} over {inp.shape[1]} tokens", flush=True)

sws = [l.mlp.switch_mlp for l in tm.layers]
orig = [{p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases, getattr(sw, p).bits, getattr(sw, p).group_size)
         for p in P3} for sw in sws]
assert all(v[3] == 4 and v[4] == 64 for o in orig for v in o.values())
SH4 = mx.arange(0, 32, 4).astype(mx.uint32)
SH2 = mx.arange(0, 32, 2).astype(mx.uint32)


def unpack4(w):                       # uint32 [..., K/8] -> codes [..., K] (value j of a word sits at bits 4j)
    return ((w[..., None] >> SH4) & 15).reshape(*w.shape[:-1], -1)


def pack2(q):                         # codes 0..3 [..., K] -> uint32 [..., K/16]
    return (q.reshape(*q.shape[:-1], -1, 16) << SH2).sum(-1).astype(mx.uint32)


def msb_term(w, s, b, off):           # off: None = per-group mean of the LSB codes, else a constant
    q = unpack4(w)
    if off is None:
        o = (q & 3).reshape(*s.shape, 64).astype(mx.float32).mean(-1)
        bias = (b.astype(mx.float32) + s.astype(mx.float32) * o).astype(s.dtype)
    else:
        bias = (b.astype(mx.float32) + off * s.astype(mx.float32)).astype(s.dtype)
    return (pack2(q >> 2), 4 * s, bias, 2, 64)


# packing unit test on random data: MSB plane (bias b) + LSB plane (bias 0) must give the 4-bit dequant back
t = mx.random.normal((4, 64, 256), key=mx.random.key(0)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
w_, s_, b_ = mx.quantize(t, group_size=64, bits=4)
d4 = mx.dequantize(w_, s_, b_, group_size=64, bits=4).astype(mx.float32)
q_ = unpack4(w_)
e_unpack = mx.abs(s_.astype(mx.float32)[..., None] * q_.reshape(*s_.shape, 64).astype(mx.float32)
                  + b_.astype(mx.float32)[..., None] - d4.reshape(*s_.shape, 64)).max().item()
dm = mx.dequantize(pack2(q_ >> 2), 4 * s_, b_, group_size=64, bits=2).astype(mx.float32)
dl = mx.dequantize(pack2(q_ & 3), s_, mx.zeros_like(b_), group_size=64, bits=2).astype(mx.float32)
e_planes = mx.abs(dm + dl - d4).max().item()
print(f"packing test: unpack4 max err {e_unpack:.2e}, MSB+LSB planes max err {e_planes:.2e} (values ~N(0,1))", flush=True)
assert e_unpack < 0.1 and e_planes < 0.1                        # bf16 rounding ~0.01; a packing bug gives errors ~1


def hadamard_q(n, seed):
    H = np.array([[1.0]])
    while H.shape[0] < n:
        H = np.block([[H, H], [H, -H]])
    sg = np.random.default_rng(seed).choice([-1.0, 1.0], n)
    return mx.array(sg[:, None] * H / math.sqrt(n), dtype=mx.float32)   # Q = diag(s) Had / sqrt(n), orthogonal


QF = {2048: hadamard_q(2048, 1), 512: hadamard_q(512, 2)}
QB = {n: q.astype(mx.bfloat16) for n, q in QF.items()}


def deq32(w, s, b, bits, gs):
    return mx.dequantize(w, s, b, group_size=gs, bits=bits).astype(mx.float32)


def clip_quant(x, dt, bits, gs, grid):
    """per-group MSE-optimal symmetric shrink of [min, max] around the midpoint, then MLX affine quantization."""
    sh = x.shape
    xg = x.reshape(*sh[:-1], -1, gs)
    lo, hi = xg.min(-1, keepdims=True), xg.max(-1, keepdims=True)
    c, r = (lo + hi) / 2, (hi - lo) / 2
    best = best_a = None
    for a in grid:
        xc = mx.clip(xg, c - a * r, c + a * r).reshape(sh).astype(dt)
        qw, qs, qb = mx.quantize(xc, group_size=gs, bits=bits)
        e = ((mx.dequantize(qw, qs, qb, group_size=gs, bits=bits).astype(mx.float32).reshape(xg.shape) - xg) ** 2).sum(-1, keepdims=True)
        if best is None:
            best, best_a = e, mx.full(e.shape, a)
        else:
            m = e < best
            best, best_a = mx.where(m, e, best), mx.where(m, a, best_a)
        mx.eval(best, best_a)
    xc = mx.clip(xg, c - best_a * r, c + best_a * r).reshape(sh).astype(dt)
    return tuple(mx.quantize(xc, group_size=gs, bits=bits)) + (bits, gs)


GRID = {2: (1.0, 0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.6, 0.5), 3: (1.0, 0.95, 0.9, 0.85, 0.8, 0.75, 0.7)}


def build(kind):
    """per layer: {proj: [terms]}; the gather_qmm outputs of a projection's terms are summed."""
    out = []
    for o in orig:
        d = {}
        for p, (w, s, b, bits, gs) in o.items():
            if kind == "4bit":
                d[p] = [(w, s, b, bits, gs)]
            elif kind == "planes":
                d[p] = [msb_term(w, s, b, 0.0), (pack2(unpack4(w) & 3), s, mx.zeros_like(b), 2, 64)]
            elif kind in ("msb2", "msb2m"):
                d[p] = [msb_term(w, s, b, 1.5 if kind == "msb2" else None)]
            else:                                   # clip2 / clip2g32 / clip3 / rot2 / rotc2 / rot3
                x = deq32(w, s, b, bits, gs)
                nb, g = int(kind[-1]) if kind[-1].isdigit() else 2, 64
                if kind == "clip2g32":
                    nb, g = 2, 32
                if kind.startswith("rot"):
                    x = x @ QF[x.shape[-1]]
                grid = GRID[nb] if (kind.startswith("clip") or kind.startswith("rotc")) else (1.0,)
                d[p] = [clip_quant(x, s.dtype, nb, g, grid)]
            mx.eval([t[:3] for t in d[p]])
        out.append(d)
    return out


def glu(sw, x, idx, terms, rot):
    if rot:
        x = x @ QB[x.shape[-1]]
    x, sidx, inv = _gather_sort(mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3)), idx)

    def proj(p, h):
        y = None
        for (w, s, b, bits, gs) in terms[p]:
            o = mx.gather_qmm(h, w, s, b, rhs_indices=sidx, transpose=True, group_size=gs, bits=bits, sorted_indices=True)
            y = o if y is None else y + o
        return y

    h = sw.activation(proj("up_proj", x), proj("gate_proj", x))
    if rot:
        h = h @ QB[h.shape[-1]]
    return _scatter_unsort(proj("down_proj", h), inv, idx.shape).squeeze(-2)


CUR = {}


def moe_call(self, x):
    gates = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
    k = self.top_k
    inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
    scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    if self.norm_topk_prob:
        scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    order = mx.argsort(-scores, axis=-1)                           # gate rank 0 = largest gate
    inds, scores = mx.take_along_axis(inds, order, axis=-1), mx.take_along_axis(scores, order, axis=-1)
    R = CUR["R"]
    y = glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds[..., :R], CUR["hi"][self._li], CUR["hi_rot"])
    if R < k:
        y = mx.concatenate([y, glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds[..., R:], CUR["lo"][self._li], CUR["lo_rot"])], axis=-2)
    y = (y * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * self.shared_expert(x)


for li, l in enumerate(tm.layers):
    object.__setattr__(l.mlp, "_li", li)
type(tm.layers[0].mlp).__call__ = moe_call


def run(name, hi, R=8, lo=None, hi_rot=False, lo_rot=False, note=""):
    CUR.update(hi=hi, lo=lo, R=R, hi_rot=hi_rot, lo_rot=lo_rot)
    nll, ag, _ = score(base_top)
    print(f"{name:10s}: ppl {math.exp(nll):.4f} ({100 * (math.exp(nll - base_nll) - 1):+.2f} %, dNLL {nll - base_nll:+.4f}), "
          f"agreement {ag:.4f}  {note}", flush=True)


W4 = build("4bit")
run("path4", W4, note="(harness check: 4-bit through this MoE path)")
for kind, bpw in (("msb2", 2.5), ("msb2m", 2.5)):
    t0 = time.perf_counter(); S = build(kind)
    run(kind, S, note=f"(MSB plane only, {bpw} bits/weight; build {time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s)")
    for R in ((2, 4, 6) if kind == "msb2m" else (4,)):
        run(f"mix{R}-{kind}", W4, R=R, lo=S, note=f"(top-{R} ranks exact 4-bit = MSB+LSB, rest {kind})")
    del S; CUR.clear(); mx.clear_cache()
for kind, bpw in (("clip2", 2.5), ("clip2g32", 3.0), ("clip3", 3.5), ("rot2", 2.5), ("rotc2", 2.5), ("rot3", 3.5)):
    t0 = time.perf_counter(); S = build(kind); rot = kind.startswith("rot")
    run(kind, S, hi_rot=rot, note=f"({bpw} bits/weight; build {time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s)")
    if kind in ("clip2", "rotc2"):
        run(f"mix4-{kind}", W4, R=4, lo=S, lo_rot=rot, note=f"(top-4 ranks 4-bit, rest {kind})")
    del S; CUR.clear(); mx.clear_cache()


### scripts/expert_similarity.py
"""Are the 125B's experts upcycled / correlated? If experts share structure, a missing expert could be streamed as a lossless
delta against a resident base. For layers 0/24/47, gate/up/down, a sample of experts: Pearson correlation of dequantized
weights between expert pairs and with the sample mean, and the order-0 entropy of 4-bit code differences (code_e - code_r)
mod 16 vs the codes themselves (~3.70 bits). CPU only.   usage: expert_similarity.py [experts]"""
import json, os, struct, sys
import numpy as np
NE = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 16
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
idx = json.load(open(f"{MD}/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
rng = np.random.default_rng(1)


def raw(name, sel, dt):
    f = f"{MD}/{idx[name]}"
    with open(f, "rb") as fh:
        n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]; m = json.loads(fh.read(n))[name]
    a, b = m["data_offsets"]
    mm = np.memmap(f, dt, "r", offset=8 + n + a, shape=((b - a) // np.dtype(dt).itemsize,)).reshape(m["shape"])
    return np.array(mm[sel])


def H(x):
    c = np.bincount(x.ravel(), minlength=16).astype(np.float64); p = c[c > 0] / c.sum()
    return float(-(p * np.log2(p)).sum())


for L in (0, 24, 47):
    sel = np.sort(rng.choice(512, NE, replace=False))
    for proj in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
        pre = f"language_model.model.layers.{L}.mlp.switch_mlp.{proj}."
        w = raw(pre + "weight", sel, np.uint32)                                   # [NE, rows, K/8]
        s = raw(pre + "scales", sel, np.uint16); b = raw(pre + "biases", sel, np.uint16)
        q = np.stack([(w >> (4 * j)) & 15 for j in range(8)], -1).reshape(NE, w.shape[1], -1).astype(np.int16)   # [NE, rows, K]
        f32 = lambda u: (u.astype(np.uint32) << 16).view(np.float32)
        sc, bi = f32(s), f32(b)                                                    # [NE, rows, K/64]
        deq = q.reshape(NE, q.shape[1], -1, 64) * sc[..., None] + bi[..., None]   # [NE, rows, groups, 64]
        x = deq.reshape(NE, -1)
        x = (x - x.mean(1, keepdims=True)) / x.std(1, keepdims=True)
        c = (x @ x.T) / x.shape[1]
        off = c[~np.eye(NE, dtype=bool)]
        mean = x.mean(0); cm = np.array([np.corrcoef(x[i], mean)[0, 1] for i in range(NE)])
        dq = ((q[1:] - q[:1]) % 16).astype(np.uint8)
        print(f"L{L:2d} {proj:9s}: pair corr mean {off.mean():+.3f} max {np.abs(off).max():.3f} | corr with mean {cm.mean():.3f} "
              f"| H(code) {H(q.astype(np.uint8)):.3f}  H(code diff vs expert 0) {H(dq):.3f} bits", flush=True)


### scripts/expert_tiers35.py
"""Round 18 PoC-1 (35B quality, NOT bit-exact): deeper nested bit-planes and gate-rank tiers. Every MSB-n tier is a prefix of
the existing 4-bit codes q: MSB-3 = q>>1 (3.5 bits/weight), MSB-2 = q>>2 (2.5), MSB-1 = q>>3 (1.5), each with scale
2^(4-n)*s and bias b + s*mean(low bits) per group of 64. Ranks are gate ranks (0 = largest gate) of a token's top-8.
  T(a,b)      : ranks [0,a) exact 4-bit, [a,b) MSB-2, [b,8) MSB-1. Memory-halving route: MSB-1 planes of every expert are
                1.5 bits/weight (125B: ~23 GB), upper planes stream for the higher ranks
  mixR-msb3   : ranks [0,R) exact, the rest MSB-3
  fallback p  : mix4-msb2m, but each top-4 use runs MSB-2 with probability p (its LSB plane arrived late; never waited for)
  dense-msb3  : every non-expert 4-bit QuantizedLinear in the layers (attention, GDN, shared expert; not the routers, not
                lm_head) at MSB-3 (4-bit codes with the low bit cleared = exactly the MSB-3 values), alone and with mix4-msb2m
Each tier's weights run all 8 ranks through gather_qmm; per (token, rank) the tier map picks one. MSB-2/MSB-1 are packed
2-bit (layout validated in expert_planes35.py); MSB-3 is 4-bit codes with bit 0 cleared.
usage: expert_tiers35.py   (GPU, ~40 GB peak: exclusive window)"""
import math, os, time
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models.switch_layers import _gather_sort, _scatter_unsort

MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
text = ""
for p in ("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md", "~/BigMoE/FINDINGS.md", "~/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md"):
    text += open(os.path.expanduser(p)).read() + "\n\n"
ids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:2049])[None]
inp, tgt = ids[:, :-1], ids[:, 1:]
P3 = ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")


def score(ref_top=None):
    out = model(inp)
    lg = (out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out)[0].astype(mx.float32)
    nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True), tgt[0][:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0]
    top = mx.argmax(lg, axis=-1)
    mx.eval(nll, top)
    agree = float((top == ref_top).astype(mx.float32).mean().item()) if ref_top is not None else 1.0
    return float(nll.mean().item()), agree, top


base_nll, _, base_top = score()
print(f"4-bit reference: ppl {math.exp(base_nll):.4f} over {inp.shape[1]} tokens", flush=True)
sws = [l.mlp.switch_mlp for l in tm.layers]
orig = [{p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases, getattr(sw, p).bits, getattr(sw, p).group_size)
         for p in P3} for sw in sws]
assert all(v[3] == 4 and v[4] == 64 for o in orig for v in o.values())
SH4 = mx.arange(0, 32, 4).astype(mx.uint32)
SH2 = mx.arange(0, 32, 2).astype(mx.uint32)
CLR0 = mx.array(0xEEEEEEEE, dtype=mx.uint32)                       # clears bit 0 of every nibble


def unpack4(w):
    return ((w[..., None] >> SH4) & 15).reshape(*w.shape[:-1], -1)


def pack2(q):
    return (q.reshape(*q.shape[:-1], -1, 16) << SH2).sum(-1).astype(mx.uint32)


def msb_term(w, s, b, n):
    """the MSB-n prefix of 4-bit codes w (scales s, biases b, groups of 64) as a gather_qmm term."""
    q = unpack4(w)
    off = (q & ((1 << (4 - n)) - 1)).reshape(*s.shape, 64).astype(mx.float32).mean(-1)
    bias = (b.astype(mx.float32) + s.astype(mx.float32) * off).astype(s.dtype)
    if n == 3:
        return (w & CLR0, s, bias, 4, 64)                            # s*(q & ~1) + bias = 2s*(q>>1) + b + s*off
    return (pack2(q >> (4 - n)), (1 << (4 - n)) * s, bias, 2, 64)


def build(n):
    out = []
    for o in orig:
        d = {p: (v if n is None else msb_term(v[0], v[1], v[2], n)) for p, v in o.items()}
        mx.eval([t[:3] for t in d.values()])
        out.append(d)
    return out


def glu(sw, x, idx, T):
    x, sidx, inv = _gather_sort(mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3)), idx)

    def proj(p, h):
        w, s, b, bits, gs = T[p]
        return mx.gather_qmm(h, w, s, b, rhs_indices=sidx, transpose=True, group_size=gs, bits=bits, sorted_indices=True)

    h = sw.activation(proj("up_proj", x), proj("gate_proj", x))
    return _scatter_unsort(proj("down_proj", h), inv, idx.shape).squeeze(-2)


CUR = {}


def moe_call(self, x):
    gates = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
    k = self.top_k
    inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
    scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    if self.norm_topk_prob:
        scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    order = mx.argsort(-scores, axis=-1)
    inds, scores = mx.take_along_axis(inds, order, axis=-1), mx.take_along_axis(scores, order, axis=-1)
    tiers = CUR["tiers"]
    if len(tiers) == 1:
        y = glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds, tiers[0][self._li])
    else:
        tmap = CUR["tmap"](self._li, inds.shape)
        y = None
        for t, T in enumerate(tiers):
            Yt = mx.where((tmap == t)[..., None], glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds, T[self._li]), 0)
            y = Yt if y is None else y + Yt
    y = (y * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * self.shared_expert(x)


for li, l in enumerate(tm.layers):
    object.__setattr__(l.mlp, "_li", li)
type(tm.layers[0].mlp).__call__ = moe_call


def by_rank(*t):
    a = mx.array(t, dtype=mx.int32)
    return lambda li, shape: mx.broadcast_to(a, shape)


def fallback(p):
    return lambda li, shape: mx.where((mx.arange(shape[-1]) < 4) & (mx.random.uniform(shape=shape, key=mx.random.key(1000 + li)) >= p), 0, 1).astype(mx.int32)


def run(name, tiers, tmap=None, note=""):
    CUR.update(tiers=tiers, tmap=tmap)
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    nll, ag, _ = score(base_top)
    print(f"{name:22s}: ppl {math.exp(nll):.4f} ({100 * (math.exp(nll - base_nll) - 1):+.2f} %, dNLL {nll - base_nll:+.4f}), "
          f"agreement {ag:.4f}  {note} ({time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)


W4 = build(None)
run("path4", [W4], note="(harness check)")
M3 = build(3)                                                     # MSB-3 first (4-bit-sized copy), freed before the rest
run("msb3 all", [M3], note="(3.5 bits/weight)")
run("mix4-msb3", [W4, M3], by_rank(0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1))
run("mix2-msb3", [W4, M3], by_rank(0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1))
del M3; CUR.clear(); mx.clear_cache()
M2 = build(2)
run("mix4-msb2m", [W4, M2], by_rank(0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1), "(reference from expert_planes35: +1.02 %)")
for p in (0.10, 0.25):
    run(f"mix4 fallback p={p}", [W4, M2], fallback(p), f"({int(p * 100)} % of top-4 uses MSB-2)")
M1 = build(1)
run("msb1 all", [M1], note="(1.5 bits/weight)")
run("T(4,6)", [W4, M2, M1], by_rank(0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2))
run("T(4,4) mix4-msb1", [W4, M2, M1], by_rank(0, 0, 0, 0, 2, 2, 2, 2))
run("T(3,6)", [W4, M2, M1], by_rank(0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2))
run("T(2,4)", [W4, M2, M1], by_rank(0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2, 2, 2))
run("T(2,6)", [W4, M2, M1], by_rank(0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2))
del M1; CUR.clear(); mx.clear_cache()
# dense part (non-expert 4-bit linears in the layers, not the routers) at MSB-3, in place
dense = [(n, m) for n, m in tm.named_modules() if isinstance(m, nn.QuantizedLinear) and "switch_mlp" not in n
         and not n.endswith("mlp.gate") and not n.endswith("shared_expert_gate") and m.bits == 4 and m.group_size == 64]
keep = [(m, m.weight, m.biases) for _, m in dense]
nb = 0
for _, m in dense:
    w, s, b, _, _ = msb_term(m.weight, m.scales, m.biases, 3)
    m.weight, m.biases = w, b
    nb += m.weight.size * 32 // 4
mx.eval([m.weight for _, m in dense] + [m.biases for _, m in dense])
print(f"dense-msb3: {len(dense)} linears, {nb / 1e9:.2f} G weights at 3.5 instead of 4.5 bits", flush=True)
run("dense-msb3", [W4], note="(experts exact)")
run("dense-msb3 + mix4-msb2m", [W4, M2], by_rank(0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1))
for m, w, b in keep:
    m.weight, m.biases = w, b


### scripts/expert_tiers35b.py
"""Round 18 follow-up (35B quality, NOT bit-exact): the 2+1+1 bit-plane frontier. expert_tiers35 found a 3-bit low-rank tier
free (top-2 exact + ranks 2-7 MSB-3: ppl -0.13 %, noise) and MSB-1 dead. Here: three tiers by gate rank, exact 4-bit (E),
MSB-3 (q>>1, 3.5 bits/weight incl. scales), MSB-2 (q>>2, 2.5 bits), mapped per rank. Tiers are made on the fly per layer by
clearing the low bits of the 4-bit codes (identical values to the packed planes; bias += s * mean(cleared bits) per group),
so only the 4-bit weights stay resident. Relative expert bytes per token: E 4.5, MSB-3 3.5, MSB-2 2.5 per rank.
usage: expert_tiers35b.py   (GPU, ~25 GB: exclusive window)"""
import math, os, time
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models.switch_layers import _gather_sort, _scatter_unsort

MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
text = ""
for p in ("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md", "~/BigMoE/FINDINGS.md", "~/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md"):
    text += open(os.path.expanduser(p)).read() + "\n\n"
ids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:2049])[None]
inp, tgt = ids[:, :-1], ids[:, 1:]
P3 = ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")


def score(ref_top=None):
    out = model(inp)
    lg = (out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out)[0].astype(mx.float32)
    nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True), tgt[0][:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0]
    top = mx.argmax(lg, axis=-1)
    mx.eval(nll, top)
    agree = float((top == ref_top).astype(mx.float32).mean().item()) if ref_top is not None else 1.0
    return float(nll.mean().item()), agree, top


base_nll, _, base_top = score()
print(f"4-bit reference: ppl {math.exp(base_nll):.4f} over {inp.shape[1]} tokens", flush=True)
sws = [l.mlp.switch_mlp for l in tm.layers]
orig = [{p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases) for p in P3} for sw in sws]
assert all(getattr(sw, p).bits == 4 and getattr(sw, p).group_size == 64 for sw in sws for p in P3)
KEEP = {3: mx.array(0xEEEEEEEE, dtype=mx.uint32), 2: mx.array(0xCCCCCCCC, dtype=mx.uint32)}


def tier(li, p, n):
    w, s, b = orig[li][p]
    if n == 4:
        return w, s, b
    low = (1 << (4 - n)) - 1
    lsum = None
    for j in range(8):
        part = (w >> (4 * j)) & low
        lsum = part if lsum is None else lsum + part
    off = lsum.reshape(*s.shape, 8).sum(-1).astype(mx.float32) / 64
    return w & KEEP[n], s, (b.astype(mx.float32) + s.astype(mx.float32) * off).astype(s.dtype)


def glu(sw, x, idx, li, n):
    x, sidx, inv = _gather_sort(mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3)), idx)

    def proj(p, h):
        w, s, b = tier(li, p, n)
        return mx.gather_qmm(h, w, s, b, rhs_indices=sidx, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4, sorted_indices=True)

    h = sw.activation(proj("up_proj", x), proj("gate_proj", x))
    return _scatter_unsort(proj("down_proj", h), inv, idx.shape).squeeze(-2)


CUR = {}


def moe_call(self, x):
    gates = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
    k = self.top_k
    inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
    scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    if self.norm_topk_prob:
        scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    order = mx.argsort(-scores, axis=-1)
    inds, scores = mx.take_along_axis(inds, order, axis=-1), mx.take_along_axis(scores, order, axis=-1)
    plan = CUR["plan"]                                            # bits per rank, e.g. (4, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2, 2)
    y = None
    for n in sorted(set(plan), reverse=True):
        sel = mx.array([b == n for b in plan])[:, None]
        Y = mx.where(sel, glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds, self._li, n), 0)
        y = Y if y is None else y + Y
    y = (y * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * self.shared_expert(x)


for li, l in enumerate(tm.layers):
    object.__setattr__(l.mlp, "_li", li)
type(tm.layers[0].mlp).__call__ = moe_call
BYTES = {4: 4.5, 3: 3.5, 2: 2.5}
for plan in ((4,) * 8, (4, 4, 3, 3, 3, 3, 3, 3), (4, 4, 4, 4, 2, 2, 2, 2), (4, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2, 2), (4, 4, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2),
             (4, 3, 3, 3, 3, 3, 3, 3), (4, 4, 3, 3, 3, 3, 2, 2), (4, 4, 4, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2), (4, 3, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2, 2),
             (3, 3, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2, 2)):
    CUR["plan"] = plan
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    nll, ag, _ = score(base_top)
    rel = sum(BYTES[b] for b in plan) / (4.5 * len(plan))
    print(f"{''.join(map(str, plan))}: ppl {math.exp(nll):.4f} ({100 * (math.exp(nll - base_nll) - 1):+.2f} %, dNLL "
          f"{nll - base_nll:+.4f}), agreement {ag:.4f}, expert bytes {100 * (rel - 1):+.0f} % ({time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s)",
          flush=True)


### scripts/expert_tiers35c.py
"""Round 19 PoC C (35B quality, NOT bit-exact): gate-mass-adaptive bit plans vs fixed rank plans. Per token, the gate
mass before a rank decides its tier: exact 4-bit while mass-before < t4, MSB-3 while < t3, else MSB-2 (peaked gates spend
fewer bits, flat ones more). Compared with fixed rank plans at the same average expert bytes. Tiers are masked on the fly
from the 4-bit codes (as expert_tiers35b.py). Relative bytes: 4.5 / 3.5 / 2.5 per rank-use.
usage: expert_tiers35c.py   (GPU, ~25 GB: exclusive window)"""
import math, os, time
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models.switch_layers import _gather_sort, _scatter_unsort

MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
text = ""
for p in ("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md", "~/BigMoE/FINDINGS.md", "~/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md"):
    text += open(os.path.expanduser(p)).read() + "\n\n"
ids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:2049])[None]
inp, tgt = ids[:, :-1], ids[:, 1:]
P3 = ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")


def score(ref_top=None):
    out = model(inp)
    lg = (out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out)[0].astype(mx.float32)
    nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True), tgt[0][:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0]
    top = mx.argmax(lg, axis=-1)
    mx.eval(nll, top)
    agree = float((top == ref_top).astype(mx.float32).mean().item()) if ref_top is not None else 1.0
    return float(nll.mean().item()), agree, top


base_nll, _, base_top = score()
print(f"4-bit reference: ppl {math.exp(base_nll):.4f} over {inp.shape[1]} tokens", flush=True)
sws = [l.mlp.switch_mlp for l in tm.layers]
orig = [{p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases) for p in P3} for sw in sws]
assert all(getattr(sw, p).bits == 4 and getattr(sw, p).group_size == 64 for sw in sws for p in P3)
KEEP = {3: mx.array(0xEEEEEEEE, dtype=mx.uint32), 2: mx.array(0xCCCCCCCC, dtype=mx.uint32)}


def tier(li, p, n):
    w, s, b = orig[li][p]
    if n == 4:
        return w, s, b
    low = (1 << (4 - n)) - 1
    lsum = None
    for j in range(8):
        part = (w >> (4 * j)) & low
        lsum = part if lsum is None else lsum + part
    off = lsum.reshape(*s.shape, 8).sum(-1).astype(mx.float32) / 64
    return w & KEEP[n], s, (b.astype(mx.float32) + s.astype(mx.float32) * off).astype(s.dtype)


def glu(sw, x, idx, li, n):
    x, sidx, inv = _gather_sort(mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3)), idx)

    def proj(p, h):
        w, s, b = tier(li, p, n)
        return mx.gather_qmm(h, w, s, b, rhs_indices=sidx, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4, sorted_indices=True)

    h = sw.activation(proj("up_proj", x), proj("gate_proj", x))
    return _scatter_unsort(proj("down_proj", h), inv, idx.shape).squeeze(-2)


CUR = {"bytes": [], "plan": None, "t": None}
BYTES = {4: 4.5, 3: 3.5, 2: 2.5}


def moe_call(self, x):
    gates = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
    k = self.top_k
    inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
    scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    if self.norm_topk_prob:
        scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    order = mx.argsort(-scores, axis=-1)
    inds, scores = mx.take_along_axis(inds, order, axis=-1), mx.take_along_axis(scores, order, axis=-1)
    if CUR["plan"] is not None:                                  # fixed bits per rank
        bits = mx.broadcast_to(mx.array(CUR["plan"]), scores.shape)
    else:                                                         # gate-mass-adaptive
        t4, t3 = CUR["t"]
        frac = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        before = mx.cumsum(frac, axis=-1) - frac
        bits = mx.where(before < t4, 4, mx.where(before < t3, 3, 2))
    CUR["bytes"].append(mx.where(bits == 4, 4.5, mx.where(bits == 3, 3.5, 2.5)).mean())
    y = None
    for n in (4, 3, 2):
        Y = mx.where((bits == n)[..., None], glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds, self._li, n), 0)
        y = Y if y is None else y + Y
    y = (y * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * self.shared_expert(x)


for li, l in enumerate(tm.layers):
    object.__setattr__(l.mlp, "_li", li)
type(tm.layers[0].mlp).__call__ = moe_call


def run(name, plan=None, t=None):
    CUR.update(plan=plan, t=t, bytes=[])
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    nll, ag, _ = score(base_top)
    rel = float(mx.stack(CUR["bytes"]).mean().item()) / 4.5
    print(f"{name:24s}: ppl {math.exp(nll):.4f} ({100 * (math.exp(nll - base_nll) - 1):+.2f} %, dNLL {nll - base_nll:+.4f}), "
          f"agreement {ag:.4f}, expert bytes {100 * (rel - 1):+.1f} % ({time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)


run("fixed 44444444 (check)", plan=(4,) * 8)
for plan in ((4, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2, 2), (4, 3, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2, 2), (4, 4, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2), (4, 4, 4, 4, 2, 2, 2, 2)):
    run("fixed " + "".join(map(str, plan)), plan=plan)
for t in ((0.25, 0.6), (0.3, 0.7), (0.35, 0.75), (0.4, 0.8), (0.5, 0.85), (0.3, 0.85), (0.5, 0.7), (0.2, 0.5)):
    run(f"adaptive t4={t[0]} t3={t[1]}", t=t)


### scripts/export_pass_graph125.py
"""Round 32k follow-up: export one 4-row verify forward's graph (unevaluated) under the CURRENT environment flags (pass the
reference's BIGMOE_* / MTPLX_* variables in the environment) and measure its dependent-kernel chain with
critical_chain125.py. As kcount125.py (BigMoE) but 4 rows (the MTP verify shape) inside attention_phase("decode_verify").
usage: [ENV...] export_pass_graph125.py [FRAC] [OUT.dot]   (GPU-light: builds a graph, one prefill; model-size memory)"""
import os, subprocess, sys

from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed

                     capture_output=True, text=True).stdout, flush=True)


### scripts/fetch_mtp_bf16.py
"""Fetch only the MTP module (31 bf16 tensors, ~8 GB) of the official Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next checkpoint with HTTP range
requests, for the MTP-precision test (diagnosis (c), orchestrator OK 13:25: official repo, MTP module only, keep >= ~100 GB
free). The tensors are spread over 28 of the 131 shards (360 GB total), so whole-shard downloads (~77 GB) are avoided:
each shard's safetensors header is read (8 + n bytes), then only the MTP tensors' byte ranges are fetched and streamed into
one local safetensors file with the official names.   usage: fetch_mtp_bf16.py OUT.safetensors"""
import json, os, struct, subprocess, sys, time, urllib.request
REPO = "https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next"
OUT = sys.argv[1]
idx = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(f"{REPO}/raw/main/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
mtp = {k: f for k, f in idx.items() if k.startswith("mtp.")}
print(f"{len(mtp)} mtp tensors in {len(set(mtp.values()))} shards", flush=True)


def rng(fname, a, b):
    """bytes [a, b) of a shard (curl keeps Range across the CDN redirect)."""
    r = subprocess.run(["curl", "-sfL", "-r", f"{a}-{b - 1}", f"{REPO}/resolve/main/{fname}"], capture_output=True, check=True)
    assert len(r.stdout) == b - a, (fname, a, b, len(r.stdout))
    return r.stdout


plan = []                                            # (name, dtype, shape, shard, abs_start, abs_end)
for fname in sorted(set(mtp.values())):
    n = struct.unpack("<Q", rng(fname, 0, 8))[0]
    hdr = json.loads(rng(fname, 8, 8 + n))
    for name, m in hdr.items():
        if name.startswith("mtp."):
            a, b = m["data_offsets"]
            plan.append((name, m["dtype"], m["shape"], fname, 8 + n + a, 8 + n + b))
plan.sort()
total = sum(e - s for *_, s, e in plan)
st = os.statvfs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(OUT)))
free = st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize / 1e9
print(f"to fetch: {total / 1e9:.2f} GB in {len(plan)} tensors; free {free:.0f} GB", flush=True)
assert free - total / 1e9 >= 110, "would go below the 110 GB margin"
header, off = {}, 0
for name, dt, shape, _, s, e in plan:
    header[name] = {"dtype": dt, "shape": shape, "data_offsets": [off, off + (e - s)]}; off += e - s
header["__metadata__"] = {"source": "Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (official), MTP tensors only, fetched by range"}
hb = json.dumps(header).encode(); hb += b" " * ((8 - len(hb) % 8) % 8)
t0 = time.time()
with open(OUT + ".part", "wb") as fh:
    fh.write(struct.pack("<Q", len(hb))); fh.write(hb)
    for i, (name, dt, shape, fname, s, e) in enumerate(plan):
        CH = 256 << 20                               # 256 MB range pieces for the big expert tensors
        for a in range(s, e, CH):
            fh.write(rng(fname, a, min(e, a + CH)))
        print(f"  {i + 1}/{len(plan)} {name} {dt} {shape} ({(e - s) / 1e6:.0f} MB, {time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)
os.replace(OUT + ".part", OUT)
print(f"done: {OUT} ({os.path.getsize(OUT) / 1e9:.2f} GB) in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s")


### scripts/flag_ab125.py
"""Round 29: per-flag A/B of MTPLX's own kernel-cutting switches on BigMoE's streamed 125B (BigMoE ledger A2 tested four
together: slower, output changed from token 1; R2 asks for a per-flag A/B). The flag under test is set in the environment
by the runner (MTPLX reads them at import). One process per configuration:
  kernels   kernel-launching graph nodes of a 4-token forward under attention_phase("decode_verify"), per layer type
  exact     sha1 of that forward's logits (bitwise) and of each prompt's greedy tokens (committed MTP depth 3)
  speed     decode tok/s, 3 prompts x MAXTOK tokens
Prints one JSON line.   usage: flag_ab125.py LABEL [FRAC] [MAXTOK]   (GPU, BigMoE-size memory: quiet lock)"""
import collections, hashlib, json, os, re, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe import fuse
LABEL = sys.argv[1]
frac = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0.5
MAXTOK = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 128
rt, st = load_streamed(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare"), round(frac * 512))
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx import generation as G
from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
model = rt.model
TM = model.language_model
FREE = {"Reshape", "Broadcast", "ExpandDims", "Squeeze", "Flatten", "Unflatten", "Slice", "Split", "Transpose", "AsStrided", "View"}
# 1) kernels + bitwise logits of a fixed 4-row forward
ids = rt.tokenizer.encode("The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog. " * 4)[:32]
fuse.reset_generation_state(model)
cache = model.make_cache()
o = model(mx.array([ids[:28]]), cache=cache)
mx.eval(o if not isinstance(o, tuple) else o[0])
with attention_phase("decode_verify"):
    y = model(mx.array([ids[28:32]]), cache=cache)
y = y[0] if isinstance(y, tuple) else y
path = os.path.expanduser(f"~/MoonShots/data/_flag_{LABEL}.dot")
mx.export_to_dot(path, y)
mx.eval(y)
txt = open(path).read()
os.remove(path)
labels = [re.sub(r"[\s(].*$", "", l) for l in re.findall(r'\{\s*\d+\s*\[label\s*=\s*"([^"]*)"', txt)]
kern = collections.Counter(l for l in labels if l not in FREE)
logits_sha = hashlib.sha1(np.array(y.astype(mx.float32)).tobytes()).hexdigest()[:12]
# 2) greedy decode, committed MTP depth 3
g8 = json.loads(open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl")).readline())["question"]
PROMPTS = {"code": open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
           "math": "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + g8,
           "prose": "Explain how a refrigerator works to a curious 10 year old, in about 150 words."}
greedy = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)
shas, tps = {}, {}
for name, text in PROMPTS.items():
    t = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    t = list(t if isinstance(t[0], int) else t["input_ids"])
    stamps = []
    cb = lambda tk: stamps.append((time.perf_counter(), len(tk) if isinstance(tk, list) else 1))
    fuse.reset_generation_state(model)
    res = G.generate_mtpk(rt, t, max_tokens=MAXTOK, sampler=greedy, speculative_depth=3, seed=0,
                          mtp_history_policy="committed", token_callback=cb)
    shas[name] = hashlib.sha1(str(list(res.tokens)).encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
    n = sum(k for _, k in stamps) - stamps[0][1]
    tps[name] = round(n / (stamps[-1][0] - stamps[0][0]), 2)
st.gate.check()
env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if (k.startswith("MTPLX_") and ("FUSED" in k or "OPDIET" in k or "GLUE" in k))
       or k.startswith("MLX_")}
print(json.dumps(dict(label=LABEL, env=env, kernels_total=sum(kern.values()), kernels_per_layer=round(sum(kern.values()) / 48, 1),
