                      top=dict(kern.most_common(12)), logits_sha=logits_sha, shas=shas, tok_s=tps,
                      tok_s_mean=round(sum(tps.values()) / len(tps), 2), peak_gb=round(mx.get_peak_memory() / 1e9, 1))), flush=True)


### scripts/fused_moe_125b.py
"""MTPLX's fused routed-expert kernels (moe_glu_decode / moe_glu_verify: 2 dispatches per layer, batch-invariant) vs MLX's
gather_qmm chain, on the 125B MoE shape (512 experts, top-10, 4-bit g64, gate/up 640x2560, down 2560x640), dependent chain over
4 layers' distinct weights. Usage: fused_moe_125b.py   (GPU, quiet lock; ~6 GB of weights)"""
import importlib.util, os, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("mgd", os.path.expanduser(
    "~/.cache/uv/archive-v0/dfadjselx9BIIozk/mtplx/kernels/moe_glu_decode.py"))
mgd = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(mgd)

NE, K, D, F, G, NLAY = 512, 10, 2560, 640, 64, 4
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
layers = []
for _ in range(NLAY):
    gate = (mx.random.normal((NE, F, D)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    up = (mx.random.normal((NE, F, D)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    down = (mx.random.normal((NE, D, F)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    gq, uq, dq = (mx.quantize(w, group_size=G, bits=4) for w in (gate, up, down))
    gu = mx.quantize(mx.concatenate([gate, up], axis=1), group_size=G, bits=4)     # fused [E, 2F, D/8] pack
    mx.eval(gq, uq, dq, gu)
    del gate, up, down
    layers.append(dict(gq=gq, uq=uq, dq=dq, gu=gu))


def routing(S):
    base = rng.choice(NE, K, replace=False); rows = [base]
    for _ in range(S - 1):
        keep = rng.choice(base, int(0.45 * K), replace=False)
        rows.append(np.concatenate([keep, rng.choice(np.setdiff1d(np.arange(NE), keep), K - len(keep), replace=False)]))
        base = rows[-1]
    return np.stack(rows)


def mlx_moe(x, idx, wts, L):
    flat = idx.reshape(-1); order = mx.argsort(flat); xs = x[order // K][:, None, :]; ids = flat[order]
    kw = dict(transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=4, sorted_indices=True)
    g = mx.gather_qmm(xs, *L["gq"], rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    u = mx.gather_qmm(xs, *L["uq"], rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    h = mx.gather_qmm((g * mx.sigmoid(g)) * u, *L["dq"], rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    inv = mx.argsort(order)
    h = h[inv].reshape(x.shape[0], K, D)
    return (h * wts[..., None]).sum(1).astype(mx.bfloat16)


def fused_moe(x, idx, wts, L):
    S = x.shape[0]
    ex = idx.reshape(-1).astype(mx.uint32); rw = wts.reshape(-1).astype(mx.float32)
    if S == 1:
        return mgd.moe_glu_decode(x[0], *L["gu"], *L["dq"], ex, rw, gu_group_size=G, dn_group_size=G)[None]
    return mgd.moe_glu_verify(x, *L["gu"], *L["dq"], ex, rw, gu_group_size=G, dn_group_size=G)


for S in (1, 2, 3, 4):
    idxs = [mx.array(routing(S).astype(np.int32)) for _ in range(NLAY)]
    wts = [mx.softmax(mx.random.normal((S, K)), axis=-1) for _ in range(NLAY)]
    x0 = (mx.random.normal((S, D)) * 0.5).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    res = {}
    for name, f in (("MLX gather chain", mlx_moe), ("MTPLX fused", fused_moe)):
        def run():
            y = x0
            for L, i, w in zip(layers, idxs, wts):
                y = f(y, i, w, L)
            mx.eval(y)
            return y
        for _ in range(3):
            run()
        ts = []
        for _ in range(15):
            a = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
        res[name] = sorted(ts)[7] / NLAY * 1e3
    a = mlx_moe(x0, idxs[0], wts[0], layers[0]).astype(mx.float32); b = fused_moe(x0, idxs[0], wts[0], layers[0]).astype(mx.float32)
    rel = (mx.abs(a - b).max() / mx.abs(a).max()).item()
    print(f"S={S}: MLX gather chain {res['MLX gather chain']:.3f} ms/layer | MTPLX fused {res['MTPLX fused']:.3f} ms/layer "
          f"({res['MLX gather chain']/res['MTPLX fused']:.2f}x) | rel diff {rel:.1e} -> x48 layers: "
          f"{(res['MLX gather chain']-res['MTPLX fused'])*48:+.1f} ms per pass", flush=True)


### scripts/gapclock.py
"""Does the GPU (or the memory fabric) slow down after short idle gaps?
A fake decode step = ~1.9 GB of 4-bit matvecs in one graph. We time the step itself and vary the
CPU-side idle gap before it (0 .. 20 ms), as happens between speculative passes or in Python glue.
If step time grows with the gap, clocks are ramping down between tokens."""
import time, random
import mlx.core as mx

N, IN, OUT = 200, 4096, 4096
mats = []
for _ in range(N):
    q, s, b = mx.quantize((mx.random.normal((OUT, IN)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16), group_size=64, bits=4)
    mats.append((q, s, b))
mx.eval(mats)
gb = sum(a.nbytes for p in mats for a in p) / 1e9
x = mx.random.normal((1, IN)).astype(mx.bfloat16)


def step():
    mx.eval([mx.quantized_matmul(x, *p, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4) for p in mats])


def spin(ms):                      # busy-wait, so the CPU side stays awake like a real decode loop
    t = time.perf_counter() + ms / 1e3
    while time.perf_counter() < t:
        pass


for _ in range(10):
    step()
gaps = [0, 0.5, 2, 5, 10, 20]
res = {g: [] for g in gaps}
order = gaps * 40
random.Random(0).shuffle(order)
for g in order:
    spin(g) if g else None
    t = time.perf_counter(); step(); res[g].append(time.perf_counter() - t)
base = sorted(res[0])[20]
print(f"step = {gb:.2f} GB of 4-bit matvecs; back-to-back {base*1e3:.2f} ms = {gb/base:.0f} GB/s")
for g in gaps:
    v = sorted(res[g]); med = v[len(v) // 2]
    print(f"gap {g:>4} ms: step {med*1e3:6.2f} ms ({med/base:5.3f}x), p10 {v[4]*1e3:6.2f} p90 {v[36]*1e3:6.2f}")


### scripts/gdn_exact_model125.py
"""Model-level check of the exact GDN prework (patches/planes_engine/gdn_exact_patch.py) on the 125B: the same prompt,
prefill + a 1-row decode step + a 4-row verify forward (attention_phase decode_verify), logits compared bitwise between
  ref    the eager chain (MTPLX fusion flags off)
  exact  MTPLX_FUSED_GDN_CONVNORM=1 + MTPLX_FUSED_CONVNORM_VERIFY=1 routed to the exact kernel
  mtplx  the same flags with MTPLX's own kernel (expected to differ: fp32 throughout)
Each arm runs in its own process (flags are read at import). usage: gdn_exact_model125.py [FRAC]   (GPU, model memory)"""
import hashlib, json, os, subprocess, sys
ARM = os.environ.get("GDN_ARM")
FRAC = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.25
if ARM is None:                                             # driver: one process per arm
    res = {}
    for arm in ("ref", "exact", "mtplx"):
        env = dict(os.environ, GDN_ARM=arm)
        if arm != "ref":
            env.update(MTPLX_FUSED_GDN_CONVNORM="1", MTPLX_FUSED_CONVNORM_VERIFY="1")
        out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, __file__, str(FRAC)], env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
        line = [l for l in out.stdout.splitlines() if l.startswith("RESULT ")]
        res[arm] = json.loads(line[-1][7:]) if line else {"error": out.stderr[-600:]}
        print(arm, res[arm], flush=True)
    same = lambda a, b: all(res[a].get(k) == res[b].get(k) for k in ("decode", "verify"))
    print(f"exact == ref: {same('exact', 'ref')} | mtplx == ref: {same('mtplx', 'ref')}", flush=True)
    sys.exit(0 if same("exact", "ref") else 1)
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/patches/planes_engine"))
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
if ARM == "exact":
    import gdn_exact_patch
    gdn_exact_patch.install()
rt, st = load_streamed(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare"), round(FRAC * 512))
m = rt.model
ids = rt.tokenizer.encode("def parse(path):\n    rows = []\n    with open(path) as f:\n        for line in f:")
L0 = lambda o: o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o
cache = m.make_cache()
mx.eval(L0(m(mx.array([ids[:-5]]), cache=cache)))           # prefill
d = L0(m(mx.array([[ids[-5]]]), cache=cache))                # 1-row decode step
mx.eval(d)
from mtplx.attention_context import attention_phase
with attention_phase("decode_verify"):
    v = L0(m(mx.array([ids[-4:]]), cache=cache))             # 4-row verify forward
mx.eval(v)
import numpy as np
h = lambda a: hashlib.sha256(np.array(a.astype(mx.float32)).tobytes()).hexdigest()[:16]
print("RESULT " + json.dumps({"decode": h(d), "verify": h(v)}), flush=True)


### scripts/glu_kernels_bench.py
"""Round 26 baseline: routed-expert GLU throughput on this Mac for the 125B's shapes (gate_up fused [1280, 2560] and down
[2560, 640], 4-bit g64, 128-slot pools as BigMoE's C=128), M = 1..4 rows x top-10, measured the way a forward pass runs
it: 48 GLUs per command buffer over 8 distinct layer pools (2.8 GB, far beyond the SLC), each layer's input depending on
the previous one ("chain", like the model) or all independent ("free", the scheduler may overlap). Variants:
  stock   MLX's chain as SwitchGLU runs it: gather_qmm(gu) -> split -> silu*mul -> gather_qmm(down) -> weighted sum
  mtplx   MTPLX's fused kernels (moe_glu_decode M=1 / moe_glu_verify M=2..4): two dispatches per layer
  stream  a pure read of the same expert bytes (uint4 loads, one dispatch per layer): the bandwidth ceiling for this
          access pattern, plus one big contiguous read for the machine's roofline
GB/s counts unique expert bytes per layer (2.77 MB each).   usage: glu_kernels_bench.py   (GPU ~3 GB; quiet lock)"""
import time
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mtplx.kernels.moe_glu_decode import moe_glu_decode, moe_glu_verify
SLOTS, K, N, G, NLP, REPS = 128, 2560, 640, 64, 8, 6
mx.random.seed(0)


def qpool(rows, cols):
    w = (mx.random.normal((SLOTS, rows, cols)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    q = mx.quantize(w, group_size=G, bits=4)
    mx.eval(q)
    del w
    return q


POOLS = [(qpool(2 * N, K), qpool(K, N)) for _ in range(NLP)]
BYTES = {}
for (gw, gs, gb), (dw, ds, db) in POOLS[:1]:
    per = (gw.nbytes + gs.nbytes + gb.nbytes + dw.nbytes + ds.nbytes + db.nbytes) / SLOTS
print(f"expert bytes {per / 1e6:.3f} MB; pools {NLP} x {SLOTS} slots = {NLP * SLOTS * per / 1e9:.1f} GB", flush=True)


def stock(x, li, idx, w, u=None):
    (gw, gs, gb), (dw, ds, db) = POOLS[li]
    xg = mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3))                                           # [M, 1, 1, K]
    gu = mx.gather_qmm(xg, gw, gs, gb, rhs_indices=idx, transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=4)   # [M, k, 1, 2N]
    g, u = mx.split(gu, 2, axis=-1)
    h = nn.silu(g) * u
    y = mx.gather_qmm(h, dw, ds, db, rhs_indices=idx, transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=4)    # [M, k, 1, K]
    return (y.squeeze(-2) * w[..., None].astype(y.dtype)).sum(axis=-2)


def mtplx(x, li, idx, w, u=None):
    (gw, gs, gb), (dw, ds, db) = POOLS[li]
    if x.shape[0] == 1:
        return moe_glu_decode(x[0], gw, gs, gb, dw, ds, db, idx[0].astype(mx.uint32), w[0].astype(mx.float32),
                              gu_group_size=G, dn_group_size=G)[None]
    return moe_glu_verify(x, gw, gs, gb, dw, ds, db, idx.reshape(-1).astype(mx.uint32), w.reshape(-1).astype(mx.float32),
                          gu_group_size=G, dn_group_size=G)


_rd = mx.fast.metal_kernel(
    name="stream_read",
    input_names=["a", "b", "ids", "n_ids"],
    output_names=["out"],
    source="""
    // each threadgroup reads one expert's gate_up (a) and down (b) slices with uint4 loads; xor-reduce, one word out
    const uint e = ids[threadgroup_position_in_grid.x];
    const uint t = thread_position_in_threadgroup.x;
    const uint nt = threads_per_threadgroup.x;
    const device uint4* pa = (const device uint4*)(a) + (size_t)e * A4;
    const device uint4* pb = (const device uint4*)(b) + (size_t)e * B4;
    uint4 acc = uint4(0);
    for (uint i = t; i < A4; i += nt) acc ^= pa[i];
    for (uint i = t; i < B4; i += nt) acc ^= pb[i];
    uint r = acc.x ^ acc.y ^ acc.z ^ acc.w;
    r = simd_xor(r) ;
    if ((t & 31) == 0) atomic_fetch_xor_explicit((device atomic_uint*)&out[0], r, memory_order_relaxed);
    """,
)


def stream(x, li, idx, w, u=None):
    (gw, gs, gb), (dw, ds, db) = POOLS[li]
    (o,) = _rd(inputs=[gw, dw, u, u.size], template=[("A4", gw.shape[1] * gw.shape[2] // 4), ("B4", dw.shape[1] * dw.shape[2] // 4)],
               grid=(u.size * 1024, 1, 1), threadgroup=(1024, 1, 1), output_shapes=[(1,)], output_dtypes=[mx.uint32],
               init_value=0)
    return x + o.astype(x.dtype) * 0


def run(fn, M, chain=True, iters=5):
    xs = mx.random.normal((M, K)).astype(mx.bfloat16) * 0.1
    idxs = [mx.stack([mx.random.permutation(SLOTS)[:10] for _ in range(M)]).astype(mx.uint32) for _ in range(NLP * REPS)]
    ws = [mx.softmax(mx.random.normal((M, 10)), axis=-1) for _ in range(NLP * REPS)]
    mx.eval(xs, *idxs, *ws)
    us = [mx.array(sorted(set(i.reshape(-1).tolist())), dtype=mx.uint32) for i in idxs]
    mx.eval(*us)
    uniq = sum(u.size for u in us)

    def once():
        x, acc = xs, []
        for l in range(NLP * REPS):
            y = fn(x if chain else xs, l % NLP, idxs[l], ws[l], us[l])
            if chain:
                x = (x + y * 0.01).astype(mx.bfloat16)
            else:
                acc.append(y)
        mx.eval(x if chain else mx.stack(acc).sum(axis=0))
    once()
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(iters):
        once()
    dt = (time.perf_counter() - t0) / iters / (NLP * REPS)
    return dt * 1e3, uniq / (NLP * REPS) * per / dt / 1e9


if __name__ == "__main__":
    big = mx.zeros((1 << 28,), mx.uint32)                          # 1 GiB contiguous
    mx.eval(big)
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(10):
        mx.eval(big.sum())
    roof = 10 * big.nbytes / (time.perf_counter() - t0) / 1e9
    print(f"roofline (1 GiB contiguous sum): {roof:.0f} GB/s", flush=True)
    print("ms per layer GLU (GB/s of unique expert bytes):")
    for M in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        parts = []
        for name, fn in (("stock", stock), ("mtplx", mtplx), ("stream", stream)):
            for chain in (True, False):
                if name == "stream" and chain:
                    continue
                try:
                    ms, gbs = run(fn, M, chain)
                    parts.append(f"{name}{'/chain' if chain else '/free'} {ms:.3f} ({gbs:.0f})")
                except Exception as e:
                    parts.append(f"{name} ERR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:60]}")
        print(f"M={M}: " + " | ".join(parts), flush=True)


### scripts/gridbarrier.py
"""Megakernel feasibility on M5: cost of a grid-wide barrier inside one dispatch vs a dependent kernel hop.
(a) hop: N tiny dependent kernels in one MLX graph (each reads the previous output), time / N.
(b) barrier: one dispatch of G threadgroups doing N phases; between phases every threadgroup bumps a device atomic
    counter and spins until it reaches phase*G. A bounded spin sets a fail flag instead of hanging (no co-residency
    guarantee on Metal), so too many threadgroups shows up as FAIL, not a GPU hang.
Prints µs per hop vs µs per barrier for several G. Tiny GPU job (< 1 s per config); needs a quiet lock for timing."""
import time
import mlx.core as mx

N = 400

HOP = """
    uint i = thread_position_in_grid.x;
    y[i] = x[i] + 1.0f;
"""
hop = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="hop", input_names=["x"], output_names=["y"], source=HOP)

BAR = """
    uint tg = threadgroup_position_in_grid.x;
    uint t = thread_position_in_threadgroup.x;
    threadgroup uint stop;
    if (t == 0) stop = 0;
    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
    for (uint p = 1; p <= NPH; ++p) {
        // phase work: a little device traffic per threadgroup
        atomic_fetch_add_explicit(&work[tg * 256 + t], 1.0f, memory_order_relaxed);
        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_device);
        if (t == 0) {
            atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst);
            atomic_fetch_add_explicit(&cnt[0], 1u, memory_order_relaxed);
            uint target = p * G;
            uint spins = 0;
            while (atomic_load_explicit(&cnt[0], memory_order_relaxed) < target) {
                if (++spins > 4000000u) { atomic_store_explicit(&fail[0], 1u, memory_order_relaxed); stop = 1; break; }
            }
            atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst);
        }
        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
        if (stop) return;
    }
"""
bar = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="gridbar", input_names=["work_in"], output_names=["cnt", "fail", "work"],
                           source=BAR, atomic_outputs=True)


def t_hops():
    x = mx.zeros((4096,), mx.float32)
    def run():
        y = x
        for _ in range(N):
            y = hop(inputs=[y], grid=(4096, 1, 1), threadgroup=(256, 1, 1),
                    output_shapes=[(4096,)], output_dtypes=[mx.float32])[0]
        mx.eval(y)
    run()
    ts = []
    for _ in range(7):
        a = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[3] / N * 1e6


def t_bar(G):
    w = mx.zeros((G * 256,), mx.float32)
    def run():
        cnt, fail, work = bar(inputs=[w], template=[("G", G), ("NPH", N)], grid=(G * 256, 1, 1),
                              threadgroup=(256, 1, 1), output_shapes=[(1,), (1,), (G * 256,)],
                              output_dtypes=[mx.uint32, mx.uint32, mx.float32], init_value=0)
        mx.eval(cnt, fail, work)
        return cnt.item(), fail.item()
    c, f = run()
    if f:
        return None, c
    ts = []
    for _ in range(7):
        a = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[3] / N * 1e6, c


print(f"dependent kernel hop (MLX graph, {N} chained tiny kernels): {t_hops():.2f} µs/hop")
for G in (1, 8, 16, 20, 32, 40, 64, 128):
    us, c = t_bar(G)
    if us is None:
        print(f"grid barrier G={G:>3}: FAIL (counter reached {c} of {G*N}: threadgroups not co-resident)")
        break
    print(f"grid barrier G={G:>3} threadgroups x 256: {us:.2f} µs/barrier (counter {c} = {G*N} expected)")


### scripts/gsm8k_planes35.py
"""GSM8K screen for nested bit-plane experts (NOT bit-exact; Kamen OK'd testing non-exact ideas 07:4x). Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 4-bit.
Arm A: plain 4-bit (stock mlx-lm MoE call). Arm B: VARIANT, e.g. mix4-msb2m = each token's top-4 of 8 gate ranks on the
exact 4-bit experts (MSB + LSB planes), the other ranks on the MSB plane only (2.5 bits/weight, per-group mean-LSB offset).
Same prompt, greedy CoT, max 900 tokens and answer extraction as ~/MoEPrefetch/scripts/gsm8k.py (data read from there).
Prints both accuracies, per-problem strings, how many final answers and whole outputs match arm A, and the teacher-forced
ppl of both arms on the expert_planes35 text.   usage: gsm8k_planes35.py VARIANT N   (GPU, ~30 GB: exclusive window)"""
import json, math, os, re, sys, time
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.generate import generate_step
from mlx_lm.sample_utils import make_sampler
from mlx_lm.models.switch_layers import _gather_sort, _scatter_unsort

VARIANT, N = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2])
MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
OFF = int(os.environ.get("OFFSET", "0"))                         # REUSE_A=<json>: take arm A from an earlier run; SKIP_C=1
probs = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))][OFF:OFF + N]
INSTR = "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n"
MAXT = 900
eos = set(getattr(tok, "eos_token_ids", None) or [tok.eos_token_id])


def extract(text):
    m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\**\s*\$?\s*(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", text)
    if not m:
        m = re.findall(r"(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", text)
    return m[-1].replace(",", "").rstrip(".") if m else None


def gold(a):
    return a.split("####")[-1].strip().replace(",", "")


def correct(pred, p):
    try:
        return int(pred is not None and abs(float(pred) - float(gold(p["answer"]))) < 1e-6)
    except ValueError:
        return 0


text = ""
for f in ("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md", "~/BigMoE/FINDINGS.md", "~/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md"):
    text += open(os.path.expanduser(f)).read() + "\n\n"
tids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:2049])[None]


def ppl():
    out = model(tids[:, :-1])
    lg = (out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out)[0].astype(mx.float32)
    nll = -mx.take_along_axis(lg - mx.logsumexp(lg, axis=-1, keepdims=True), tids[0, 1:][:, None], axis=-1)[:, 0].mean()
    return math.exp(nll.item())


def arm(name):
    res, t0, ntok = [], time.perf_counter(), 0
    for p in probs:
        ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": INSTR + p["question"]}], add_generation_prompt=True,
                                      enable_thinking=False)
        out = []
        for t, _ in generate_step(mx.array(ids), model, max_tokens=MAXT, sampler=make_sampler(temp=0.0)):
            t = int(t)
            if t in eos:
                break
            out.append(t)
        ntok += len(out)
        txt = tok.decode(out)
        pred = extract(txt)
        res.append({"pred": pred, "ok": correct(pred, p), "text": txt})
    dt = time.perf_counter() - t0
    acc = sum(r["ok"] for r in res)
    print(f"{name:12s}: GSM8K {acc}/{len(res)} = {100 * acc / len(res):.1f}%  ({dt:.0f}s, {ntok / dt:.1f} tok/s)  "
          f"per-problem {''.join(str(r['ok']) for r in res)}", flush=True)
    return res


# ---- the nested bit-plane variant (same construction as expert_planes35.py)
P3 = ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")
sws = [l.mlp.switch_mlp for l in tm.layers]
orig = [{p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases, getattr(sw, p).bits, getattr(sw, p).group_size)
         for p in P3} for sw in sws]
assert all(v[3] == 4 and v[4] == 64 for o in orig for v in o.values())
SH4 = mx.arange(0, 32, 4).astype(mx.uint32)
SH2 = mx.arange(0, 32, 2).astype(mx.uint32)


def unpack4(w):
    return ((w[..., None] >> SH4) & 15).reshape(*w.shape[:-1], -1)


def pack2(q):
    return (q.reshape(*q.shape[:-1], -1, 16) << SH2).sum(-1).astype(mx.uint32)


def msb_term(w, s, b, off):
    q = unpack4(w)
    if off is None:
        o = (q & 3).reshape(*s.shape, 64).astype(mx.float32).mean(-1)
        bias = (b.astype(mx.float32) + s.astype(mx.float32) * o).astype(s.dtype)
    else:
        bias = (b.astype(mx.float32) + off * s.astype(mx.float32)).astype(s.dtype)
    return (pack2(q >> 2), 4 * s, bias, 2, 64)


def glu(sw, x, idx, terms):
    x, sidx, inv = _gather_sort(mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3)), idx)

    def proj(p, h):
        y = None
        for (w, s, b, bits, gs) in terms[p]:
            o = mx.gather_qmm(h, w, s, b, rhs_indices=sidx, transpose=True, group_size=gs, bits=bits, sorted_indices=True)
            y = o if y is None else y + o
        return y

    h = sw.activation(proj("up_proj", x), proj("gate_proj", x))
    return _scatter_unsort(proj("down_proj", h), inv, idx.shape).squeeze(-2)


CUR = {}


def moe_call(self, x):
    gates = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
    k = self.top_k
    inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
    scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    if self.norm_topk_prob:
        scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    order = mx.argsort(-scores, axis=-1)
    inds, scores = mx.take_along_axis(inds, order, axis=-1), mx.take_along_axis(scores, order, axis=-1)
    R = CUR["R"]
    y = glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds[..., :R], CUR["hi"][self._li]) if R > 0 else None
    if R < k:
        lo = glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds[..., R:], CUR["lo"][self._li])
        y = lo if y is None else mx.concatenate([y, lo], axis=-2)
    y = (y * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * self.shared_expert(x)


m = re.fullmatch(r"mix(\d)-(msb2m?)|(msb2m?)", VARIANT)
assert m, VARIANT
R, kind = (int(m.group(1)), m.group(2)) if m.group(1) else (0, m.group(3))

ppl_a = ppl()
print(f"arm A plain 4-bit: teacher-forced ppl {ppl_a:.4f}", flush=True)
if os.environ.get("REUSE_A"):
    A = json.load(open(os.environ["REUSE_A"]))["A"]
    print(f"4-bit (reused from {os.environ['REUSE_A']}): GSM8K {sum(r['ok'] for r in A)}/{len(A)}  per-problem {''.join(str(r['ok']) for r in A)}", flush=True)
else:
    A = arm("4-bit")
W4 = [{p: [v] for p, v in o.items()} for o in orig]
LO = []
for o in orig:
    d = {p: [msb_term(w, s, b, 1.5 if kind == "msb2" else None)] for p, (w, s, b, _, _) in o.items()}
    mx.eval([t[:3] for v in d.values() for t in v])
    LO.append(d)
for li, l in enumerate(tm.layers):
    object.__setattr__(l.mlp, "_li", li)
type(tm.layers[0].mlp).__call__ = moe_call
CUR.update(R=R, hi=W4, lo=LO)
ppl_b = ppl()
print(f"arm B {VARIANT}: teacher-forced ppl {ppl_b:.4f} ({100 * (ppl_b / ppl_a - 1):+.2f} %)", flush=True)
B = arm(VARIANT)


def agree(X, name):
    print(f"{name} vs arm A: final answers {sum(a['pred'] == x['pred'] for a, x in zip(A, X))}/{N}, whole outputs "
          f"{sum(a['text'] == x['text'] for a, x in zip(A, X))}/{N}; both correct {sum(a['ok'] and x['ok'] for a, x in zip(A, X))}, "
          f"A-only {sum(a['ok'] and not x['ok'] for a, x in zip(A, X))}, {name}-only {sum(x['ok'] and not a['ok'] for a, x in zip(A, X))}",
          flush=True)


agree(B, "B")
# arm C (noise floor): the same 4-bit weights through this script's MoE path; only summation order and row tiling differ
C = None
if not os.environ.get("SKIP_C"):
    CUR.update(R=8, hi=W4, lo=None)
    C = arm("path4")
    agree(C, "C")
json.dump({"variant": VARIANT, "A": A, "B": B, "C": C, "ppl": [ppl_a, ppl_b]},
          open(os.path.expanduser(f"~/MoonShots/data/gsm8k_planes35_{VARIANT}{'_off%d' % OFF if OFF else ''}.json"), "w"))


### scripts/insert_policy125.py
"""Scan-resistant insertion policies for the expert cache (CPU caches: LIP/BIP, Qureshi et al. ISCA 2007; SRRIP, Jaleel
et al. ISCA 2010; 2Q, Johnson & Shasha 1994). OPT's advantage over LRU here is keeping frequently reused experts and
dropping one-off experts at once; these policies try to get that online. Exact (a policy never changes outputs).
Trace data/verify_trace125.npz, per layer C slots, 4-bit MB per pass.
  lru        baseline
  lip-k      a newly inserted expert starts k passes "old" (evicted first unless reused soon); a hit makes it MRU
  srrip-n    n-bit re-reference prediction values: insert at 2^n - 2, hit -> 0, evict the max (aging all until one is max)
  2q-f       admit to a probation FIFO of f*C slots; promote to the protected LRU on a reuse
usage: insert_policy125.py   (CPU, ~1 min, 1 core)"""
import numpy as np

z = np.load("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/verify_trace125.npz")
ids = z["ids"].astype(np.int32)
P, L = ids.shape[:2]
seqs = [[set(np.unique(ids[p, l]).tolist()) for p in range(P)] for l in range(L)]
MB = 2.765


def lip(seq, C, k):
    age, miss = {}, 0                                    # e -> effective last-use time
    for t, need in enumerate(seq):
        for e in need:
            if e in age:
                age[e] = t
            else:
                miss += 1
                age[e] = t - k
        if len(age) > C:
            cand = sorted((e for e in age if e not in need), key=lambda e: age[e])
            # newly inserted experts of this pass are protected for this pass only if C allows
            for e in cand[:len(age) - C]:
                del age[e]
            if len(age) > C:                             # all candidates gone: drop the oldest of this pass's new ones
                for e in sorted(age, key=lambda e: age[e])[:len(age) - C]:
                    del age[e]
    return miss


def srrip(seq, C, n):
    M = (1 << n) - 1
    rr, miss = {}, 0
    for t, need in enumerate(seq):
        for e in need:
            if e in rr:
                rr[e] = 0
            else:
                miss += 1
                rr[e] = M - 1
        while len(rr) > C:
            cand = [e for e in rr if e not in need]
            if not cand:
                break
            mx_ = max(rr[e] for e in cand)
            if mx_ < M:
                for e in rr:
                    if e not in need:
                        rr[e] += M - mx_
            victim = next(e for e in cand if rr[e] >= M)
            del rr[victim]
    return miss


def twoq(seq, C, f):
    A = max(1, int(f * C))                               # probation FIFO size
    prob, prot, miss = {}, {}, 0                         # e -> insertion time / last use
    for t, need in enumerate(seq):
        for e in need:
            if e in prot:
                prot[e] = t
            elif e in prob:
                del prob[e]
                prot[e] = t
            else:
                miss += 1
                prob[e] = t
        while len(prob) + len(prot) > C:
            pc = [e for e in prob if e not in need]
            if len(prob) > A and pc:
                del prob[min(pc, key=lambda e: prob[e])]
                continue
            qc = [e for e in prot if e not in need]
            if qc:
                del prot[min(qc, key=lambda e: prot[e])]
            elif pc:
                del prob[min(pc, key=lambda e: prob[e])]
            else:
                break
    return miss


for C in (64, 96, 128, 192):
    out = [f"lru {sum(lip(s, C, 0) for s in seqs) / P * MB:6.1f}"]
    for k in (1, 2, 4):
        out.append(f"lip-{k} {sum(lip(s, C, k) for s in seqs) / P * MB:6.1f}")
    for n in (2, 3):
        out.append(f"srrip-{n} {sum(srrip(s, C, n) for s in seqs) / P * MB:6.1f}")
    for f in (0.25, 0.5):
        out.append(f"2q-{f} {sum(twoq(s, C, f) for s in seqs) / P * MB:6.1f}")
    print(f"C={C:3d} ({48 * C * MB / 1e3:4.1f} GB) MB/pass  " + " | ".join(out), flush=True)


### scripts/insert_policy125_sweep.py
"""LIP-k for larger k and SRRIP-4 (insert_policy125.py functions), plus per-prompt-half robustness: first half vs second
half of the passes. usage: insert_policy125_sweep.py   (CPU, 1 core)"""
import sys
sys.argv = [sys.argv[0]]
import numpy as np
import insert_policy125 as I                               # runs its own table first (kept for the record)
MB, P = I.MB, I.P
half = P // 2
for C in (64, 96, 128):
    out = []
    for name, fn in [("lru", lambda s: I.lip(s, C, 0))] + [(f"lip-{k}", (lambda k: lambda s: I.lip(s, C, k))(k)) for k in (4, 8, 16, 32)] + \
                    [("srrip-4", lambda s: I.srrip(s, C, 4))]:
        a = sum(fn(s[:half]) for s in I.seqs) / half * MB
        b = sum(fn(s[half:]) for s in I.seqs) / (P - half) * MB
        out.append(f"{name} {a:6.1f}/{b:6.1f}")
    print(f"C={C:3d} MB/pass (1st half / 2nd half)  " + " | ".join(out), flush=True)


### scripts/insert_policy800.py
"""Robustness of the LIP/SRRIP insertion result on a second trace set: MoEPrefetch's 125B traces (*_800.npz, 4 prompts x
800 tokens), verify windows of 4 tokens with stride 3 (as planes3_sim.py), per layer C slots, 4-bit MB per pass, LRU vs
LIP-4/8 vs SRRIP-3 vs Belady OPT. Uses insert_policy125.py's and opt_bound125.py's policy functions (imported lazily).
usage: insert_policy800.py   (CPU, 1 core; run with taskpolicy -b)"""
import glob, os, sys, types
import numpy as np
sys.argv = [sys.argv[0]]
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
src = open(os.path.join(HERE, "insert_policy125.py")).read().split("\nfor C in (64, 96, 128, 192):")[0]
src = src.replace('z = np.load("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/verify_trace125.npz")', "z = None").replace(
    "ids = z[\"ids\"].astype(np.int32)\nP, L = ids.shape[:2]\nseqs = [[set(np.unique(ids[p, l]).tolist()) for p in range(P)] for l in range(L)]", "")
I = types.ModuleType("I"); exec(src, I.__dict__)
osrc = open(os.path.join(HERE, "opt_bound125.py")).read()
opt_src = osrc[osrc.index("def opt(seq, C):"):osrc.index("MB = 2.765")]
exec(opt_src, I.__dict__)
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))
per_layer = None
P = 0
for f in files:
    z = np.load(f)
    tr = [z[f"L{L}"][:, :10].astype(np.int64) for L in range(len(z.files))]
    if per_layer is None:
        per_layer = [[] for _ in tr]
    T = tr[0].shape[0]
    starts = list(range(0, T - 4, 3))
    P += len(starts)
    for L in range(len(tr)):
        per_layer[L] += [np.unique(tr[L][s:s + 4]) for s in starts]   # prompts concatenated (cold start counted once each)
MB = 2.765
print(f"{len(files)} prompts, {P} windows of 4, {len(per_layer)} layers", flush=True)
sets = [[set(x.tolist()) for x in s] for s in per_layer]
for C in (64, 96, 128, 192):
    lru = sum(I.lip(s, C, 0) for s in sets) / P * MB
    l4 = sum(I.lip(s, C, 4) for s in sets) / P * MB
    l8 = sum(I.lip(s, C, 8) for s in sets) / P * MB
    sr = sum(I.srrip(s, C, 3) for s in sets) / P * MB
    op = sum(I.opt(s, C) for s in per_layer) / P * MB
    print(f"C={C:3d} ({48 * C * MB / 1e3:4.1f} GB) MB/pass: LRU {lru:6.1f} | LIP-4 {l4:6.1f} ({l4 / lru - 1:+.1%}) | LIP-8 {l8:6.1f} "
          f"({l8 / lru - 1:+.1%}) | SRRIP-3 {sr:6.1f} ({sr / lru - 1:+.1%}) | OPT {op:6.1f} ({op / lru - 1:+.1%})", flush=True)


### scripts/interpass125.py
"""Where the ~17 ms between verify passes goes (data/pass_profile125.json, frac 0.5; clock shared by the loader trace and
Python): per pass, t_rel[47] (last layer's experts released) -> the MTP draft steps (Python-side timestamps of
mtp.fuse_and_run, seq-tagged) -> the next pass's t_seen[0] (GPU reached layer 0's gate). Splits:
  a  t_rel[47] -> first draft step: layer 47's MoE, final norm + lm_head, sampling/acceptance, MTP history append
  b  first -> last draft step (draft steps 1..2 incl. their syncs)
  c  last draft step -> next t_seen[0]: the last draft step, verify launch, layer 0 attention + router
usage: interpass125.py   (CPU, instant)"""
import json, collections
import numpy as np
d = json.load(open("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/pass_profile125.json"))
for run in d["EV"]:
    by = collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for L, seq, kind, ts, tr in run["events"]:
        by[int(seq)][int(L)] = (ts, tr)
    dr = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for seq, t0 in run["drafts"]:
        dr[int(seq)].append(t0)
    seqs = sorted(s for s in by if len(by[s]) == 48)
    rows = []
    for s, s2 in zip(seqs, seqs[1:]):
        t47, t0n = by[s][47][1], by[s2][0][0]
        ds = sorted(t for k in (s, s + 1) for t in dr.get(k, []) if t47 <= t <= t0n)
        if not ds:
            continue
        rows.append((ds[0] - t47, ds[-1] - ds[0], t0n - ds[-1], t0n - t47, len(ds)))
    a = np.array(rows) * 1e3
    print(f"{run['prompt']}: {len(rows)} passes, draft steps/pass {np.mean(a[:, 4] / 1e3):.1f}; inter-pass {a[:, 3].mean():.1f} ms = "
          f"a {a[:, 0].mean():.1f} (verify tail: L47 MoE, lm_head, sampling, acceptance, history) + b {a[:, 1].mean():.1f} "
          f"(between draft steps) + c {a[:, 2].mean():.1f} (last draft, launch, L0 attention); p90 total {np.percentile(a[:, 3], 90):.1f}")


### scripts/kl125_screen.py
"""Per-part prefill-KL screen for 2-bit-tail tier plans on the 125B (NewIdeas 2026-10-22, lever 1's quality gate), using the
Eval suite's own machinery READ-ONLY (~/EvalSuite: tf items, the cached 4-bit reference cache/ref/tf_ref.npz, var_head, the
vendored planes125 tier math) on the own-output tf sets only (the 4 x 64K documents are skipped: ~4 min per plan instead of
~15). Prints per set (gsm / ifeval / code / multi) mean KLD vs the reference and top-1 agreement, next to the same per-set
numbers of the suite's existing runs (floor = same weights, ar4 = accepted build, plan4433333333, plan4433332222) read from
~/EvalSuite/results/*/tf.npz. Writes ~/MoonShots/data/kl125/screen_<plan>.json (nothing is written into ~/EvalSuite).
usage: kl125_screen.py plan4322222222 [plan4222222222 ...]   (GPU, ~24 GB at C=128; quiet lock; no BIGMOE_* env flags)"""
import json, os, sys, time
import numpy as np
for k in [k for k in os.environ if k.startswith("BIGMOE_")]:
    os.environ.pop(k)                                         # evalsuite/env.py refuses BigMoE speed flags
EV = os.path.expanduser("~/EvalSuite")
sys.path.insert(0, EV)
from evalsuite import stages as SG, model as M, engine as E
OUT = os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/kl125")
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)


def per_set(sets_by_item, seq, kld, agree):
    out = {}
    for s in sorted(set(sets_by_item)):
        if "doc" in s:
            continue
        m = np.isin(seq, [i for i, x in enumerate(sets_by_item) if x == s])
        out[s] = (float(kld[m].mean()), float(agree[m].mean()), int(m.sum()))
    return out


existing = {}
for name in ("floor", "ar4", "plan4433333333", "plan4433332222"):
    p = os.path.join(EV, "results", name, "tf.npz")
    if os.path.exists(p):
        z = np.load(p)
        existing[name] = per_set(list(z["sets"]), z["seq"], z["kld"], z["agree"])
names = sys.argv[1:]
vs = [M.variant(n) for n in names]
t0 = time.time()
rt, st = M.load(vs[0], C=128)
items_all = SG.tf_items()
ref_all = SG.load_ref_tf(items_all)
keep = [i for i, it in enumerate(items_all) if "doc" not in it["set"]]
items = [items_all[i] for i in keep]
ref = {j: ref_all[i] for j, i in enumerate(keep)}
tg = SG._targets(items)
print(f"loaded in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s; {len(items)} own-output sequences, {sum(len(it['pos']) for it in items)} positions", flush=True)
new = {}
for v in vs:
    M.configure(v)
    t = time.time()
    res = SG.run_batches(rt, items, E.var_head(ref, tg), capture=True, label=f"{v['name']} tf screen")
    seq = np.concatenate([np.full(len(res[i]["kld"]), i, np.int32) for i in range(len(items))])
    kld = np.concatenate([res[i]["kld"] for i in range(len(items))])
    agree = np.concatenate([res[i]["agree"] for i in range(len(items))])
    new[v["name"]] = per_set([it["set"] for it in items], seq, kld, agree)
    json.dump(dict(plan=v["name"], per_set=new[v["name"]], pooled=float(kld.mean()), seconds=time.time() - t),
              open(os.path.join(OUT, f"screen_{v['name']}.json"), "w"), indent=1)
    print(f"{v['name']}: pooled KLD {kld.mean():.5f}, agree {agree.mean():.4f} ({time.time() - t:.0f}s)", flush=True)
st.gate.check()
rows = {**existing, **new}
sets = sorted({s for r in rows.values() for s in r})
print("\nper-set mean KLD vs the 4-bit reference (top-1 agreement); targets = 1.10 x ar4 (gate_table.py)")
print("variant".ljust(16) + "".join(s.ljust(22) for s in sets))
for name, r in rows.items():
    print(name.ljust(16) + "".join((f"{r[s][0]:.5f} ({r[s][1]:.3f})" if s in r else "-").ljust(22) for s in sets))
if "ar4" in rows:
    print("target 1.10x ar4".ljust(16) + "".join((f"{1.10 * rows['ar4'][s][0]:.5f}" if s in rows["ar4"] else "-").ljust(22) for s in sets))


### scripts/layer_law125.py
"""Round 32j: where does the 46 ms of per-pass compute exceed the matvec law? (data/pass_profile125.json, frac 0.5)
between_L = t_seen[L+1] - t_rel[L] = L's MoE (routed + shared) + L+1's attention side + router. Bytes per interval:
moe_b[L] (L's non-routed MoE weights) + att_b[L+1] (L+1's attention-side weights) + L's unique routed experts x 2.765 MB
(uni "seq:L") + GDN recurrent state (fp32 read + write) if L+1 is GDN. Law: bytes / 300 GB/s + k x 20 us, k = dependent
big kernels; reported: measured ms, bytes-only ms, and the implied dependent-kernel count (measured - bytes) / 20 us,
by the kind of L+1. usage: layer_law125.py   (CPU, instant)"""
import collections, json
import numpy as np
d = json.load(open("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/pass_profile125.json"))
att_b, moe_b, kinds, gst = d["att_b"], d["moe_b"], d["kinds"], d["gdn_state"]
acc = collections.defaultdict(list)
for run in d["EV"]:
    by = collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for L, seq, kind, ts, tr in run["events"]:
        by[int(seq)][int(L)] = (ts, tr)
    uni = run["uni"]
    for seq, lay in by.items():
        if len(lay) < 48:
            continue
        for L in range(47):
            ms = (lay[L + 1][0] - lay[L][1]) * 1e3
            u = uni.get(f"{seq}:{L}")
            if u is None:
                continue
            b = moe_b[L] + att_b[L + 1] + u * 2.765e6 + (2 * gst if kinds[L + 1] == "gdn" else 0)
            acc[kinds[L + 1]].append((ms, b / 300e6, u))           # bytes / 300 GB/s in ms
for k, v in acc.items():
    a = np.array(v)
    med, bo = np.median(a[:, 0]), np.median(a[:, 1])
    print(f"next layer {k:4s}: n {len(a):5d} | measured p50 {med:.3f} ms (mean {a[:, 0].mean():.3f}, p90 {np.percentile(a[:, 0], 90):.3f}) | "
          f"bytes-only p50 {bo:.3f} ms ({bo / med:.0%}) | implied dependent kernels (p50) {(med - bo) / 0.020:.1f} x 20 us | "
          f"unique experts {a[:, 2].mean():.1f}")
allv = np.concatenate([np.array(v) for v in acc.values()])
print(f"per pass (47 intervals): measured p50-sum {np.median(allv[:, 0]) * 47:.1f} ms (mean-sum {allv[:, 0].mean() * 47:.1f}), "
      f"bytes-only {allv[:, 1].mean() * 47:.1f} ms")


### scripts/layer_ops125.py
"""Round 29 map: which primitives does each layer of a 4-row 125B forward launch? Captures every DecoderLayer's output
array during one (unevaluated) 4-token forward on BigMoE's streamed runtime, exports each to DOT and diffs the node sets
of consecutive layers, so each layer's own nodes are counted by primitive type (GDN vs QSA vs PLE layer). Free view ops
(Reshape, Broadcast, ExpandDims, Squeeze, Flatten, Unflatten, Slice, Split, Transpose*) are listed apart from the rest.
usage: layer_ops125.py [FRAC]   (GPU, BigMoE-size memory: quiet lock)"""
import collections, os, re, sys
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
frac = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.25
rt, st = load_streamed(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare"), round(frac * 512))
model = rt.model
TM = model.language_model
LAYERS = TM.model.layers
OUT = []
for L, layer in enumerate(LAYERS):
    cls = type(layer)
    if not getattr(cls, "_l29_wrapped", False):
        orig = cls.__call__

        def wrapped(self, *a, __orig=orig, **kw):
            y = __orig(self, *a, **kw)
            OUT.append((self, y))
            return y
        cls.__call__ = wrapped
        cls._l29_wrapped = True
ids = rt.tokenizer.encode("The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog. " * 4)[:32]
cache = model.make_cache()
out = model(mx.array([ids[:28]]), cache=cache)
mx.eval(out if not isinstance(out, tuple) else out[0])
OUT.clear()
y = model(mx.array([ids[28:32]]), cache=cache)
y = y[0] if isinstance(y, tuple) else y
lay = {id(l): i for i, l in enumerate(LAYERS)}
arrs = [(lay.get(id(s)), (o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o)) for s, o in OUT]


def nodes(a, path):
    mx.export_to_dot(path, a)
    txt = open(path).read()
    return dict(re.findall(r'\{\s*(\d+)\s*\[label\s*=\s*"([^"]*)"', txt))


FREE = {"Reshape", "Broadcast", "ExpandDims", "Squeeze", "Flatten", "Unflatten", "Slice", "Split", "Transpose", "AsStrided"}
tmp = os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/_layer.dot")
prev = set()
per = {}
for L, a in arrs:
    n = nodes(a, tmp)
    mine = {k: v for k, v in n.items() if k not in prev and not re.match(r"^[\d\s,()x]*$", v)}
    prev |= set(n)
    per[L] = collections.Counter(re.sub(r"[\s(].*$", "", v) for v in mine.values())
mx.eval(y)
st.gate.check()
types = ["ple" if getattr(LAYERS[L], "ple", None) is not None else ("gdn" if LAYERS[L].is_linear else "qsa") for L in range(len(LAYERS))]
for kind in ("gdn", "qsa", "ple"):
    Ls = [L for L in per if L is not None and types[L] == kind and L > 0]
    if not Ls:
        continue
    tot = collections.Counter()
    for L in Ls:
        tot.update(per[L])
    k_all = {k: v / len(Ls) for k, v in tot.items()}
    kern = sum(v for k, v in k_all.items() if k not in FREE)
    print(f"\n{kind.upper()} layers ({len(Ls)}): {sum(k_all.values()):.0f} nodes per layer, ~{kern:.0f} non-view (kernel) nodes:")
    print("  " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v:.1f}" for k, v in sorted(k_all.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1]) if k not in FREE))
    print("  views: " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v:.1f}" for k, v in sorted(k_all.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1]) if k in FREE))


### scripts/layered_prefill.py
"""#18 layer-major MoE prefill: does one MoE layer get cheaper per token when it sees more tokens at once?
Today a 2048-token chunk runs every layer, so each of 256 experts gets ~64 rows. Layer-major prefill would
run a layer's MoE once over the whole prompt (8K-32K tokens -> 256-1024 rows per expert).
One Qwen3.6-35B-A3B MoE layer (256 experts, top-8, hidden 2048, expert intermediate 512) at Q8 or 4-bit,
sorted gather_qmm as mlx-lm's SwitchGLU does it. Prints µs per token and TFLOPS vs tokens per call.
Usage: layered_prefill.py [bits]   (GPU, needs a quiet lock; ~1 GB of weights)"""
import sys, time
import mlx.core as mx

BITS = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 8
SHAPE = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "35b"
NE, K, D, F = {"35b": (256, 8, 2048, 512), "125b": (512, 10, 2560, 640)}[SHAPE]
G = 64


def qexperts(o, i):
    w = (mx.random.normal((NE, o, i)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    return mx.quantize(w, group_size=G, bits=BITS)


gate, up, down = qexperts(F, D), qexperts(F, D), qexperts(D, F)
mx.eval(gate, up, down)


def moe(x, idx):
    """x: (T, D); idx: (T, K) expert ids. Sorted path, like SwitchGLU with sort."""
    T = x.shape[0]
    flat = idx.reshape(-1)
    order = mx.argsort(flat)
    xs = x[order // K][:, None, :]                       # (T*K, 1, D)
    ids = flat[order]
    kw = dict(transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=BITS, sorted_indices=True)
    g = mx.gather_qmm(xs, *gate, rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    u = mx.gather_qmm(xs, *up, rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    h = mx.gather_qmm(nn_silu(g) * u, *down, rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    return h


def nn_silu(a):
    return a * mx.sigmoid(a)


print(f"{SHAPE}: {BITS}-bit MoE layer, {NE} experts top-{K}, D={D}, F={F}")
base = None
for T in (512, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384, 32768):
    x = mx.random.normal((T, D)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    logits = mx.random.normal((T, NE))
    idx = mx.argpartition(-logits, K, axis=-1)[:, :K]
    for _ in range(2):
        mx.eval(moe(x, idx))
    ts = []
    for _ in range(7):
        t = time.perf_counter(); mx.eval(moe(x, idx)); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
    t = sorted(ts)[3]
    us = t / T * 1e6
    base = base or us
    tflops = 2 * T * K * 3 * D * F / t / 1e12
    print(f"T={T:>6} ({T*K//NE:>5} rows/expert): {us:6.2f} µs/token ({base/us:4.2f}x vs T=512), {tflops:5.1f} TFLOPS")


### scripts/lookahead_bound125.py
"""How much of Belady's OPT gain needs only short-horizon knowledge? (125B, data/verify_trace125.npz, per layer C slots)
Policy LA(W): on eviction, protect every expert the next W passes will use (known exactly: the bound of a perfect
W-pass route predictor), evict the least recently used of the rest (LRU fallback; if all are protected, evict the one
used farthest in the future). W = 0 is plain LRU; W = inf is OPT. Misses per pass in 4-bit MB.
usage: lookahead_bound125.py   (CPU, ~1 min, 1 core)"""
import numpy as np

z = np.load("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/verify_trace125.npz")
ids = z["ids"].astype(np.int32)
P, L = ids.shape[:2]
seqs = [[set(np.unique(ids[p, l]).tolist()) for p in range(P)] for l in range(L)]
MB = 2.765


def next_use(seq):
    """nu[t][e]: the next pass index > t that uses e (for e used or cached at t), as a dict per t, built backwards."""
    nxt_after = [None] * len(seq)
    last = {}
    for t in range(len(seq) - 1, -1, -1):
        nxt_after[t] = dict(last)                       # next use strictly after t
        for e in seq[t]:
            last[e] = t
    return nxt_after


def la(seq, C, W, nu):
    cache, miss = {}, 0                                 # e -> last use
    for t, need in enumerate(seq):
        for e in need:
            miss += e not in cache
            cache[e] = t
        if len(cache) > C:
            after = nu[t]
            def key(e):
                n = after.get(e, 10 ** 9)
                prot = (n - t) <= W                     # used within the next W passes
                # unprotected first (evict LRU among them); protected last (evict farthest next use among them)
                return (prot, cache[e] if not prot else -n)
            cand = sorted((e for e in cache if e not in need), key=key)
            for e in cand[:len(cache) - C]:
                del cache[e]
    return miss


NU = [next_use(s) for s in seqs]
for C in (64, 96, 128, 192):
    row = []
    for W in (0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 10 ** 6):
        m = sum(la(s, C, W, nu) for s, nu in zip(seqs, NU)) / P
        row.append(f"W={'inf' if W > 1000 else W}: {m * MB:6.1f}")
    print(f"C={C:3d} ({48 * C * MB / 1e3:4.1f} GB) MB/pass  " + " | ".join(row), flush=True)


### scripts/lsrefit2.py
"""Does a per-group least-squares refit of the truncated tiers' scale and bias beat the stored truncation (scale 2^shift * s,
bias b + s * mean(q & low)) on the 125B's real experts? Weight-space squared error against the 4-bit expert w = s q + b,
per tier (3: m = q >> 1, 2: m = q >> 2), refit alpha, beta rounded to bf16 as they would be stored. Also the records4
cost: a refit P2 scale is no longer 4 s exactly, so tiers 3/4 would need their own stored scales.
usage: lsrefit2.py [layers, default 0,24,47] [experts per layer, default 32]   (CPU only, reads records3)"""
import os, sys
import numpy as np
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "patches", "planes_engine"))
import make_records3 as M

layers = [int(v) for v in (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "0,24,47").split(",")]
NE = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 32
lay = M.layout()
R3 = lay["recsz"]
mo, ms, lo, ls = lay["msb_piece_off"], lay["msb_piece_size"], lay["lsb_piece_off"], lay["lsb_piece_size"]


def bf16(a):
    return M.bf16_to_f32(M.f32_to_bf16(a.astype(np.float32)))


tot = {n: np.zeros(4) for n in (3, 2)}                            # [trunc err, refit err, signal, groups with var 0]
for L in layers:
    f = M.open_nocache(os.path.expanduser(f"~/BigMoE/models/bare/records3/L{L:02d}.bin"), os.O_RDONLY)
    buf = M.aligned(NE * R3)
    M.pread_into(f, buf, 0)
    os.close(f)
    for i, (out, K) in enumerate(M.SHAPES):
        words = M.merge_codes(M.view(buf, NE, R3, mo[3 * i], ms[3 * i], np.uint8, inner=3), M.view(buf, NE, R3, lo[2 * i], ls[2 * i], np.uint8))
        q = ((words.reshape(NE, out, K // 8)[..., None] >> (4 * np.arange(8, dtype=np.uint32))) & 15).reshape(NE, out, K // 64, 64).astype(np.float32)
        s = (M.bf16_to_f32(M.view(buf, NE, R3, mo[3 * i + 1], ms[3 * i + 1], np.uint16)) * 0.5).reshape(NE, out, K // 64, 1)
        b = M.bf16_to_f32(M.view(buf, NE, R3, lo[2 * i + 1], ls[2 * i + 1], np.uint16)).reshape(NE, out, K // 64, 1)
        w = s * q + b
        sig = ((w - w.mean(-1, keepdims=True)) ** 2).sum()
        for n in (3, 2):
            sh = 4 - n
            m = np.floor(q / 2 ** sh)
            low = q - m * 2 ** sh
            wt = bf16(s * 2 ** sh) * m + bf16(b + s * low.mean(-1, keepdims=True))
            mm, wm = m.mean(-1, keepdims=True), w.mean(-1, keepdims=True)
            var = ((m - mm) ** 2).sum(-1, keepdims=True)
            alpha = np.where(var > 0, ((m - mm) * (w - wm)).sum(-1, keepdims=True) / np.maximum(var, 1e-30), s * 2 ** sh)
            alpha = bf16(alpha)
            beta = bf16(wm - alpha * mm)
            wl = alpha * m + beta
            et, el = ((wt - w) ** 2).sum(), ((wl - w) ** 2).sum()
            tot[n] += [et, el, sig, (var == 0).sum()]
            r = (alpha / (s * 2 ** sh))[var > 0]
            print(f"L{L:02d} {('gate', 'up', 'down')[i]:4s} tier {n}: rel err trunc {np.sqrt(et / sig):.4f} refit {np.sqrt(el / sig):.4f} "
                  f"(MSE x{el / et:.3f}); refit scale / stored scale median {np.median(r):.3f} [p10 {np.percentile(r, 10):.3f}, "
                  f"p90 {np.percentile(r, 90):.3f}]", flush=True)
for n in (3, 2):
    et, el, sig, z = tot[n]
    print(f"ALL tier {n}: rel err trunc {np.sqrt(et / sig):.4f} -> refit {np.sqrt(el / sig):.4f}: MSE x{el / et:.3f} "
          f"({100 * (1 - el / et):.1f} % less); constant-code groups {int(z)}", flush=True)


### scripts/mega_skeleton.py
"""Payoff test for a between-gates megakernel on a Qwen3.8-Flash-Next GDN layer (timing skeleton, not the real math).
Phases with the real shapes and bytes (125B, hidden 2560, HC 4x2560, 4-bit projections, bf16 HC/router):
  P1 HC read down  bf16  320 x 10240     P2 HC read up   bf16 10240 x 320
  P3 in_proj qkv+z  4-bit 16384 x 2560   P4 gated-delta state r/w (48 x 128 x 128 fp32)
  P5 out_proj       4-bit 2560 x 6144    P6/P7 second HC read (down, up)
  P8 router         bf16  512 x 2560     P9 shared expert gate+up 4-bit 1280 x 2560   P10 shared down 4-bit 2560 x 640
(a) mega : one persistent dispatch per layer, 128 threadgroups x 256, grid barriers between phases
(b) eager: the same 9 matvecs + state update as separate MLX ops (mx.matmul / quantized_matmul), dependent chain
Weights for 8 distinct layers (~0.6 GB) so nothing is served from cache across layers.
Prints ms per layer for both and the bytes/280 GB/s floor. Needs a quiet GPU lock."""
import time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

NL, G, NSG = 8, 128, 128 * 8
D, HCD, R = 2560, 10240, 320
QZ, VO, NH, HD = 16384, 6144, 48, 128
SH = 640


def bf(shape):
    return (mx.random.normal(shape) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)


def q4(o, i):
    return mx.quantize(bf((o, i)), group_size=64, bits=4)


layers = []
for _ in range(NL):
    L = dict(hcd=bf((R, HCD)), hcu=bf((HCD, R)), qz=q4(QZ, D), out=q4(D, VO), hcd2=bf((R, HCD)), hcu2=bf((HCD, R)),
             rt=bf((512, D)), shgu=q4(2 * SH, D), shd=q4(D, SH),
             st=mx.random.normal((NH * HD * HD,)) * 0.01)
    mx.eval(L)
    layers.append(L)
nbytes = sum(a.nbytes for k, v in layers[0].items() for a in (v if isinstance(v, (tuple, list)) else (v,)))
nbytes += layers[0]["st"].nbytes                      # state is written back as well
print(f"one layer skeleton: {nbytes/1e6:.1f} MB read+written -> floor {nbytes/280e9*1e3:.3f} ms at 280 GB/s")

HDR = """
inline float bf2f(ushort b) { return as_type<float>((uint)b << 16); }
"""
SRC = """
    const uint tg = threadgroup_position_in_grid.x, t = thread_position_in_threadgroup.x;
    const uint lane = t % 32, gsg = tg * 8 + t / 32;
    uint phase = 0;
    // scratch layout in `sc`: x(10240) | hc(320) | mix(10240) | qz(16384) | o(2560) | hc2(320) | mix2(10240) | rt(512) | sh(1280) | y(2560)
    device float* S = sc;
    device float* X = S; device float* HC = S + 10240; device float* MIX = HC + 320; device float* QZv = MIX + 10240;
    device float* O = QZv + 16384; device float* HC2 = O + 2560; device float* MIX2 = HC2 + 320; device float* RT = MIX2 + 10240;
    device float* SHv = RT + 512; device float* Y = SHv + 1280;

#define BARRIER() { phase++; threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_device); \\
    if (t == 0) { atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst, thread_scope_device); \\
      atomic_fetch_add_explicit((device atomic_uint*)cnt, 1u, memory_order_relaxed); uint s = 0; \\
      while (atomic_load_explicit((device atomic_uint*)cnt, memory_order_relaxed) < phase * G && ++s < 20000000u) {} \\
      atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst, thread_scope_device); } \\
    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_device); }

    // bf16 matvec: y[r] = sum_k W[r,k] * f(x[k]); lanes read 4 bf16 (8 bytes) at a time
#define MV_BF16(W, ROWS, K, XIN, YOUT, ACT) \\
    for (uint r = gsg; r < ROWS; r += NSG) { float a = 0.0f; device const ushort4* w4 = (device const ushort4*)(W + (size_t)r * K); \\
      for (uint k4 = lane; k4 < K / 4; k4 += 32) { ushort4 w = w4[k4]; uint k = k4 * 4; \\
        a += bf2f(w.x) * ACT(XIN[k]) + bf2f(w.y) * ACT(XIN[k+1]) + bf2f(w.z) * ACT(XIN[k+2]) + bf2f(w.w) * ACT(XIN[k+3]); } \\
      a = simd_sum(a); if (lane == 0) YOUT[r] = a; }
    // 4-bit affine matvec (MLX layout: 8 nibbles per uint32, bf16 scale/bias per 64)
#define MV_Q4(W, SC, BI, ROWS, K, XIN, YOUT) \\
    for (uint r = gsg; r < ROWS; r += NSG) { float a = 0.0f; device const uint* wr = W + (size_t)r * (K / 8); \\
      for (uint w = lane; w < K / 8; w += 32) { uint q = wr[w]; uint k = w * 8; uint g = (size_t)r * (K / 64) + k / 64; \\
        float s = bf2f(SC[g]), b = bf2f(BI[g]); float d = 0.0f, xs = 0.0f; \\
        for (uint j = 0; j < 8; ++j) { float xv = XIN[k + j]; d += (float)((q >> (4 * j)) & 15u) * xv; xs += xv; } \\
        a += s * d + b * xs; } \\
      a = simd_sum(a); if (lane == 0) YOUT[r] = a; }
#define ID(v) (v)
#define SILU(v) ((v) / (1.0f + metal::exp(-(v))))

    if (tg == 0 && t < 32) { for (uint i = t; i < 10240; i += 32) X[i] = x0[i]; }
    BARRIER();
    MV_BF16(hcd, 320, 10240, X, HC, ID);  BARRIER();          // P1
    MV_BF16(hcu, 10240, 320, HC, MIX, SILU);  BARRIER();      // P2 (mean over HC folded into next reads)
    MV_Q4(qzw, qzs, qzb, 16384, 2560, MIX, QZv);  BARRIER();  // P3
    for (uint i = tg * 256 + t; i < 48 * 128 * 128; i += G * 256) {   // P4 gated-delta state update (r/w 3.1 MB)
        stout[i] = st[i] * 0.99f + QZv[i % 16384] * 0.001f; }
    BARRIER();
    MV_Q4(ow, osc, obi, 2560, 6144, QZv, O);  BARRIER();      // P5
    MV_BF16(hcd2, 320, 10240, MIX, HC2, ID);  BARRIER();      // P6 (stand-in input: the residual streams)
    MV_BF16(hcu2, 10240, 320, HC2, MIX2, SILU);  BARRIER();   // P7
    MV_BF16(rtw, 512, 2560, MIX2, RT, ID);
    MV_Q4(shw, shs, shb, 1280, 2560, MIX2, SHv);  BARRIER();  // P8 + P9 (independent: same phase)
    MV_Q4(sdw, sds, sdb, 2560, 640, SHv, Y);                  // P10
"""
names = ["x0", "hcd", "hcu", "qzw", "qzs", "qzb", "st", "ow", "osc", "obi", "hcd2", "hcu2", "rtw", "shw", "shs", "shb",
         "sdw", "sds", "sdb"]
mega = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="mega_gdn_skeleton", input_names=names, output_names=["cnt", "sc", "stout"],
                            header=HDR, source=SRC, atomic_outputs=False)
SCN = 10240 + 320 + 10240 + 16384 + 2560 + 320 + 10240 + 512 + 1280 + 2560


def mega_layer(L, x):
    ins = [x, L["hcd"].view(mx.uint16), L["hcu"].view(mx.uint16), L["qz"][0], L["qz"][1].view(mx.uint16),
           L["qz"][2].view(mx.uint16), L["st"], L["out"][0], L["out"][1].view(mx.uint16), L["out"][2].view(mx.uint16),
           L["hcd2"].view(mx.uint16), L["hcu2"].view(mx.uint16), L["rt"].view(mx.uint16), L["shgu"][0],
           L["shgu"][1].view(mx.uint16), L["shgu"][2].view(mx.uint16), L["shd"][0], L["shd"][1].view(mx.uint16),
           L["shd"][2].view(mx.uint16)]
    cnt, sc, st = mega(inputs=ins, template=[("G", G), ("NSG", NSG)], grid=(G * 256, 1, 1), threadgroup=(256, 1, 1),
                       output_shapes=[(4,), (SCN,), (NH * HD * HD,)], output_dtypes=[mx.uint32, mx.float32, mx.float32],
                       init_value=0)
    # next layer's input: the 2560-float output broadcast to the 10240 streams (dependency between layers)
    return mx.tile(sc[SCN - 2560:], 4)


def eager_layer(L, x):
    q = lambda v, p: mx.quantized_matmul(v, *p, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4)
    xb = x.astype(mx.bfloat16)[None]
    hc = xb @ L["hcd"].T
    mix = (hc * mx.sigmoid(hc)) @ L["hcu"].T
    qz = q(mix[:, :D], L["qz"])
    st = L["st"] * 0.99 + mx.tile(qz[0].astype(mx.float32), NH * HD * HD // QZ) * 0.001
    o = q(qz[:, :VO], L["out"])
    hc2 = mix @ L["hcd2"].T
    mix2 = (hc2 * mx.sigmoid(hc2)) @ L["hcu2"].T
    rt = mix2[:, :D] @ L["rt"].T
    sh = q(mix2[:, :D], L["shgu"])
    y = q(sh[:, :SH], L["shd"]) + o + rt.sum() * 0 + st[:1].astype(mx.bfloat16) * 0
    return mx.tile(y[0].astype(mx.float32), 4)


def bench(fn, reps=15):
    x = mx.random.normal((HCD,)).astype(mx.float32)
    def run():
        v = x
        for L in layers:
            v = fn(L, v)
        mx.eval(v)
    for _ in range(3):
        run()
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        a = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[reps // 2] / NL * 1e3


m = bench(mega_layer)
e = bench(eager_layer)
print(f"megakernel (1 dispatch/layer, 9 grid barriers): {m:.3f} ms/layer = {nbytes/(m*1e-3)/1e9:.0f} GB/s")
print(f"eager MLX chain (~14 ops/layer):               {e:.3f} ms/layer = {nbytes/(e*1e-3)/1e9:.0f} GB/s")
print(f"floor at 280 GB/s:                              {nbytes/280e9*1e3:.3f} ms/layer")


### scripts/miss_pred.py
"""Does the online next-token predictor (online_pred.py's logistic model) cut cache misses when used
as a whole-token-ahead prefetch source? Per-layer LRU cache holding FRAC of 256 experts (BigMoE's
exact design: every miss stalls). After token t, prefetch the predictor's top-P experts for token t+1
that aren't resident (evicting LRU), then serve token t+1. Counts demand misses and prefetch loads per token.
Usage: miss_pred.py set [FRAC] [P] [MAXTOK]"""
import sys
import numpy as np

E, NL = 256, 40
name = sys.argv[1]
FRAC = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0.6
P = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 16
MAXT = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 6000
CAP = int(round(FRAC * E))
z = np.load(f"/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/traces/{name}.npz")
doc = z["doc"][:MAXT]
act = np.stack([z[f"L{l}_act"][:MAXT, :8] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int64)
T = len(doc)


class LRU:
    def __init__(s):
        s.last = np.full((NL, E), -1e9)
        s.res = np.zeros((NL, E), bool)
        s.res[:, :CAP] = True                      # arbitrary warm start
        s.last[:, :CAP] = -1e8

    def touch(s, L, ids, now):
        """returns how many of ids were not resident; makes them resident."""
        miss = 0
        for e in ids:
            if not s.res[L, e]:
                miss += 1
                v = np.where(s.res[L], s.last[L], np.inf).argmin()
                s.res[L, v] = False
                s.res[L, e] = True
            s.last[L, e] = now
        return miss


base, pf = LRU(), LRU()
W = np.zeros((NL, 3 * E, E), np.float32); bias = np.zeros((NL, E), np.float32); lr = 0.05
m_base = m_pf = loads = useful = n = 0
for t in range(T - 1):
    same = doc[t] == doc[t + 1]
    warm = t > 300
    for L in range(NL):                          # serve token t
        mb = base.touch(L, act[t, L], t)
        mp = pf.touch(L, act[t, L], t)
        if warm:
            m_base += mb; m_pf += mp
    if warm:
        n += 1
    if not same:
        continue
    for L in range(NL):                          # predict token t+1, prefetch, learn
        feats = np.concatenate([k * E + act[t, LL] for k, LL in enumerate((L - 1, L, L + 1)) if 0 <= LL < NL])
        sl = W[L, feats].sum(0) + bias[L]
        pred = np.argpartition(-sl, P)[:P]
        new = [e for e in pred if not pf.res[L, e]]
        pf.touch(L, new, t + 0.5)                  # prefetched: more recent than token t's own uses
        if warm:
            loads += len(new)
            useful += np.isin(act[t + 1, L], new).sum()
        y = np.zeros(E, np.float32); y[act[t + 1, L]] = 1
        g = (y - 1 / (1 + np.exp(-sl))) * lr
        W[L, feats] += g; bias[L] += g
print(f"{name:9s} {FRAC:.0%} resident, P={P}: misses/token LRU {m_base/n:5.1f} -> with prefetch {m_pf/n:5.1f} "
      f"({1 - m_pf/m_base:+.0%} fewer); prefetch loads/token {loads/n:5.1f}, of which used next token {useful/max(loads,1):.0%}",
      flush=True)


### scripts/module_bench125.py
"""Round 26c: which weight-bearing ops of a 125B verify pass are slow? Every Linear / QuantizedLinear / GatedResidual of
the real model's 48 layers (real weights, BigMoE's load; routed experts excluded: round 26 measured them), timed the way
a pass runs them: all 48 (or 36 / 12) layers' instances of one op in one command buffer, each call depending on the
previous one (x_next = x + 0 * y.sum()), M = 1 and 4 rows (decode, 4-row verify). Bytes = the module's parameters, read
once per call. Plus the non-layer ops (lm_head, embedding gather, final mixer).
usage: module_bench125.py [FRAC]   (GPU; BigMoE-size memory at FRAC 0.125 = 9 GB of expert pools; quiet lock)"""
import os, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx.utils import tree_flatten
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
frac = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.125
rt, st = load_streamed(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare"), round(frac * 512))
import mtplx.models.qwen4_exp as Q
TM = rt.model.language_model
LAYERS = TM.model.layers
CEIL = 318.0


def nbytes(mod):
    return sum(a.nbytes for _, a in tree_flatten(mod.parameters()))


# collect op instances by name across layers
ops = {}
for L, layer in enumerate(LAYERS):
    for name, mod in layer.named_modules():
        if ".switch_mlp" in name or name.endswith("switch_mlp") or name == "":
            continue
        if isinstance(mod, Q.GatedResidual):
            ops.setdefault(("hc", name), []).append(mod)
        elif isinstance(mod, (nn.Linear, nn.QuantizedLinear)):
            if any(name.startswith(p + ".") for p in ("attn_hyper_connection", "mlp_hyper_connection")):
                continue                                             # timed as part of the GatedResidual read
            ops.setdefault(("lin", name), []).append(mod)
        elif name.endswith("shared_expert") and hasattr(mod, "gate_proj"):
            ops.setdefault(("mlp", name), []).append(mod)


def in_dim(mod):
    if isinstance(mod, nn.QuantizedLinear):
        return mod.weight.shape[1] * 32 // mod.bits
    if isinstance(mod, nn.Linear):
        return mod.weight.shape[1]
    if hasattr(mod, "gate_proj"):
        return in_dim(mod.gate_proj)
    return mod.hc_count * mod.hidden_size


def call(kind, mod, x):
    if kind == "hc":
        out = mod(x)
        return out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
    return mod(x)


def bench(kind, mods, M, iters=5):
    d = in_dim(mods[0])
    x0 = (mx.random.normal((1, M, d)) * 0.5).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    mx.eval(x0)

    def once():
        x = x0
        for m in mods:
            y = call(kind, m, x)
            x = x0 + (y.astype(mx.float32).sum() * 0).astype(x0.dtype)
        mx.eval(x)
    once()
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(iters):
        once()
    return (time.perf_counter() - t0) / iters / len(mods) * 1e3


rows = []
for (kind, name), mods in sorted(ops.items(), key=lambda kv: -nbytes(kv[1][0])):
    b = nbytes(mods[0])
    dt = {M: bench(kind, mods, M) for M in (1, 4)}
    dtype = str(getattr(mods[0], "weight", mx.zeros(1)).dtype).replace("mlx.core.", "") if kind == "lin" else kind
    bits = getattr(mods[0], "bits", None)
    rows.append((name, kind, len(mods), b, dt, bits, dtype))
    print(f"{name:52s} x{len(mods):2d} {b / 1e6:7.2f} MB {('q%d' % bits) if bits else dtype:6s}  M=1 {dt[1]:.4f} ms "
          f"({b / 1e6 / dt[1]:5.0f} GB/s)  M=4 {dt[4]:.4f} ms ({b / 1e6 / dt[4]:5.0f} GB/s, {b / 1e6 / dt[4] / CEIL:4.0%})", flush=True)
tot = {M: sum(n * dt[M] for _, _, n, _, dt, _, _ in rows) for M in (1, 4)}
byt = sum(n * b for _, _, n, b, _, _, _ in rows)
print(f"\nall per-layer weight-bearing ops (excl. routed experts): {byt / 1e9:.2f} GB per pass; M=1 {tot[1]:.1f} ms "
      f"({byt / 1e6 / tot[1]:.0f} GB/s), M=4 {tot[4]:.1f} ms ({byt / 1e6 / tot[4]:.0f} GB/s)", flush=True)
# non-layer: lm_head over 1 and 4 rows
lm = TM.lm_head if hasattr(TM, "lm_head") else None
if lm is not None:
    b = nbytes(lm)
    for M in (1, 4):
        x = (mx.random.normal((1, M, in_dim(lm))) * 0.5).astype(mx.bfloat16)
        mx.eval(x)
        for _ in range(3):
            mx.eval(lm(x))
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        for _ in range(10):
            mx.eval(lm(x))
        dt = (time.perf_counter() - t0) / 10 * 1e3
        print(f"lm_head ({b / 1e6:.0f} MB) M={M}: {dt:.3f} ms ({b / 1e6 / dt:.0f} GB/s)", flush=True)


### scripts/msb3_cache_sim.py
"""MSB-3 cache + LSB-1 sidecar vs whole-expert LRU at a fixed expert-cache budget (CPU; real 125B traces, MoEPrefetch
traces125: ranked router top-20 per layer, 4 prompts x 800 tokens). Eager decode: one token per pass, so the result can be
calibrated against BigMoE's measured eager bench (17 GB: 77.6 misses/token, 33.3 stalled layers/token, 16.1 tok/s).
Formats (bytes per expert): whole 4-bit 2.77 MB; MSB-3 = codes q>>1 (3 bits) + g64 scale/bias (3.5 bits/weight, 2.15 MB);
LSB-1 = the last bit + a bf16 bias delta per group (1.25 bits/weight, 0.77 MB); a record [MSB-3 | LSB-1] is contiguous,
so a top-rank use that misses both parts is one read.
Plan 4433333333 (equal accuracy on the 125B GSM8K-150): ranks 0-1 need MSB-3 + LSB-1, ranks 2-9 need MSB-3.
Policies (per layer, byte budget = GB * 1024 / 48):
  whole        : LRU of whole experts (today)
  msb3-sep     : LRU of MSB-3 parts + a separate LRU of LSB-1 parts (fraction f of the budget)
  msb3-unified : one LRU over both unit kinds, LSB-1 inserted at the cold end (SliceMoE's LSB-first eviction)
Per pass and layer: needed units, misses, a stall event if any needed unit misses, and an estimated stall from this Mac's
measured cold-read curve (scripts/ssd_qd.py: one read 0.40 ms for 2.77 MB at QD 1; reads in parallel share ~14 GB/s).
usage: msb3_cache_sim.py"""
import glob, os
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
E, K, WARM = 512, 10, 50
W, M3, L1 = 2.77, 2.15, 0.77
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))
TR = []
for f in files:
    z = np.load(f)
    TR.append([z[f"L{L}"][:, :K].astype(np.int64) for L in range(len(z.files))])
NL = len(TR[0])
BW = {1: 6.95, 2: 12.7, 3: 13.4, 4: 14.0}                       # GB/s by in-flight reads (measured, whole reads)


def stall_ms(sizes):
    """sizes: MB of the reads a layer waits for, all issued together."""
    if not sizes:
        return 0.0
    n = len(sizes)
    bw = BW.get(n, 14.5)
    return 0.05 + sum(sizes) / bw                               # ~0.05 ms command latency + transfer at QD n


def run(policy, budget_mb, frac_l=0.15):
    miss = events = st = byt = 0.0
    P = 0
    for tr in TR:
        T = tr[0].shape[0]
        for L in range(NL):
            if policy == "msb3-sep":
                caches = {"M": (OrderedDict(), budget_mb * (1 - frac_l)), "L": (OrderedDict(), budget_mb * frac_l)}
            else:
                caches = {"*": (OrderedDict(), budget_mb)}
            used = {k: 0.0 for k in caches}
            for t in range(T):
                ids = [int(e) for e in tr[L][t]]
                if policy == "whole":
                    need = [(e, "W") for e in ids]
                else:
                    need = [(e, "M") for e in ids] + [(e, "L") for e in ids[:2]]
                late = []
                for u in need:
                    key = "*" if "*" in caches else ("M" if u[1] in ("M", "W") else "L")
                    c = caches[key][0]
                    if u in c:
                        c.move_to_end(u)
                    else:
                        late.append(u)
                # a top-rank use missing both parts is one contiguous read of the record
                reads, merged = [], set()
                for u in late:
                    if u[1] == "M" and (u[0], "L") in late:
                        reads.append(M3 + L1); merged.add(u[0])
                    elif u[1] == "L" and u[0] in merged:
                        continue
                    else:
                        reads.append({"W": W, "M": M3, "L": L1}[u[1]])
                for u in late:
                    key = "*" if "*" in caches else ("M" if u[1] in ("M", "W") else "L")
                    c, cap = caches[key]
                    size = {"W": W, "M": M3, "L": L1}[u[1]]
                    c[u] = size; used[key] += size
                    if policy == "msb3-unified" and u[1] == "L":
                        c.move_to_end(u, last=False)                  # LSB-first eviction
                    while used[key] > cap and c:
                        _, sz = c.popitem(last=False); used[key] -= sz
                if t >= WARM:
                    miss += len(late); events += bool(late); st += stall_ms(reads); byt += sum(reads)
            if L == 0:
                P += max(0, T - WARM)
    return miss / P, events / P, st / P, byt / P


print(f"125B traces: {len(TR)} prompts, {NL} layers, eager (1 token per pass), after {WARM} warm-up tokens")
for gb in (17, 20, 24, 34):
    b = gb * 1024 / NL
    m, e, s, by = run("whole", b)
    print(f"{gb} GB  whole-expert LRU : misses {m:5.1f}/tok, stalled layers {e:4.1f}/tok, est. stall {s:5.1f} ms/tok, "
          f"read {by:5.0f} MB/tok", flush=True)
    for pol, fl in (("msb3-unified", None), ("msb3-sep", 0.10), ("msb3-sep", 0.20)):
        m, e, s, by = run(pol, b, fl or 0.15)
        print(f"   {pol:12s}{'' if fl is None else f' f={fl:.2f}':7s}: units missed {m:5.1f}/tok, stalled layers {e:4.1f}/tok, "
              f"est. stall {s:5.1f} ms/tok, read {by:5.0f} MB/tok", flush=True)


### scripts/msb3_mtp_sim.py
"""Round 25: the MSB-3 expert cache (round 20) revisited under MTP verify passes. Under eager decode it scored only ~+7 %
because stall events, not bytes, dominated (33 stalled layers/token); with committed MTP each pass commits ~2.8 tokens,
stalled layers drop to ~14/token while misses per token stay ~80, so bytes per miss now carry most of the stall time.
Trace-driven (data/verify_trace125.npz/json: 125B, committed history, depth 3, 401 verify passes of 4 rows; routing
never depends on the cache). Plan 4433333333 (GSM8K 427/450 vs 431/450 for 4-bit, n.s.): every routed expert needs its
MSB-3 part (2.15 MB: codes q>>1 + g64 scale/bias), the top-2 ranks also need LSB-1 (0.77 MB). The trace has no gate
scores (argpartition order), so 2 of each row's 10 ids, chosen by a fixed hash, stand in for the top-2 ranks.
Policies at a byte budget (per layer, GB * 1024 / 48 MB):
  whole      LRU of whole 4-bit experts (2.77 MB, BigMoE today)
  msb3-sep   LRU of MSB-3 parts + a separate LRU of LSB-1 parts (fraction f of the budget)
  msb3-uni   one LRU over both kinds, LSB-1 parts inserted at the cold end
A top-rank use that misses both parts reads one contiguous record [MSB-3 | LSB-1] (2.92 MB).
Stall model (round 20, BigMoE eager benches): 0.40 ms per stalled layer + 0.083 ms per MB read.
usage: msb3_mtp_sim.py"""
import json, os
from collections import OrderedDict, defaultdict
import numpy as np
D = os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data")
z = np.load(f"{D}/verify_trace125.npz")
meta = json.load(open(f"{D}/verify_trace125.json"))
ids, rows, seqs, prompt = z["ids"], z["rows"], z["seq"], z["prompt"]
P, NL, RM, K = ids.shape
committed = defaultdict(int)
for (p, s, n) in meta["callbacks"]:
    committed[(p, s)] += n
ntok_pass = np.array([max(1, min(committed.get((int(prompt[i]), int(seqs[i])), 1), int(rows[i]))) for i in range(P)])
W, M3, L1 = 2.77, 2.15, 0.77
WARM = 60


def top2(row):
    """stand-in for the two highest-weight experts of a row (no gate scores in the trace): a fixed hash pick."""
    return set(sorted(row, key=lambda e: (e * 2654435761) % 1000003)[:2])


def run(policy, budget_mb, frac_l=0.10):
    miss_units = stalled = mb = 0.0
    ntok = 0
    caches = []
    for L in range(NL):
        if policy == "msb3-sep":
            caches.append({"M": [OrderedDict(), budget_mb * (1 - frac_l), 0.0], "L": [OrderedDict(), budget_mb * frac_l, 0.0]})
        else:
            caches.append({"*": [OrderedDict(), budget_mb, 0.0]})
    for i in range(P):
        R = int(rows[i])
        for L in range(NL):
            a = ids[i, L]
            if a[0, 0] < 0:
                continue
            rowsets = [[int(e) for e in a[r] if e >= 0] for r in range(R)]
            if policy == "whole":
                need = {(e, "W") for rs in rowsets for e in rs}
            else:
                need = {(e, "M") for rs in rowsets for e in rs} | {(e, "L") for rs in rowsets for e in top2(rs)}
            cs = caches[L]
            key = lambda u: "*" if "*" in cs else ("M" if u[1] in ("M", "W") else "L")
            late = [u for u in need if u not in cs[key(u)][0]]
            reads = []
            lset = {u[0] for u in late if u[1] == "L"}
            for u in late:
                if u[1] == "M" and u[0] in lset:
                    reads.append(M3 + L1)
                elif u[1] == "L" and (u[0], "M") in late:
                    continue
                else:
                    reads.append({"W": W, "M": M3, "L": L1}[u[1]])
            for u in need:                                  # recency: every use moves to the MRU end
                c = cs[key(u)][0]
                if u in c:
                    c.move_to_end(u)
            for u in late:
                ent = cs[key(u)]
                size = {"W": W, "M": M3, "L": L1}[u[1]]
                ent[0][u] = size; ent[2] += size
                if policy == "msb3-uni" and u[1] == "L":
                    ent[0].move_to_end(u, last=False)
                while ent[2] > ent[1] and ent[0]:
                    _, sz = ent[0].popitem(last=False); ent[2] -= sz
            if i >= WARM:
                miss_units += len(late); stalled += bool(late); mb += sum(reads)
        if i >= WARM:
            ntok += int(ntok_pass[i])
    ms = stalled * 0.40 + mb * 0.083
    return miss_units / ntok, stalled / ntok, mb / ntok, ms / ntok


print(f"verify trace: {P} passes, {int(ntok_pass[WARM:].sum())} tokens after {WARM} warm-up passes; per token:")
print("budget          policy          misses   stalled layers   MB read   est. stall ms   vs whole")
for gb in (13, 17, 21, 26):
    b = gb * 1024 / NL
    base = None
    for pol, f in (("whole", None), ("msb3-sep", 0.10), ("msb3-sep", 0.20), ("msb3-uni", None)):
        m, s, mbr, ms = run(pol, b, f or 0.10)
        base = base or ms
        lab = pol + (f" f={f:.2f}" if f else "")
        print(f"{gb:2d} GB         {lab:15s} {m:6.1f}    {s:6.2f}          {mbr:6.1f}    {ms:6.2f}          {ms / base - 1:+.0%}",
              flush=True)


### scripts/msb_entropy.py
"""24 GB wall, lossless lever: empirical entropy of the MSB-3 part of real experts (records3), to bound how many SSD bytes
an exact (lossless) code could save. Per expert, per projection (gate / up / down: w3 = MLX 3-bit packing of m, 8 values in
3 bytes little-endian; s3, b3 bf16 per group of 64). Checks the unpacking (every group of 64 has m min 0 and max 7, since
the 4-bit code spans 0..15). Reports bits per weight: order-0 entropy of m (per projection type, pooled), the same per
group-position-free context (previous weight's m in the row), and order-0 byte entropy of s3 / b3; vs today's 3.5.
usage: msb_entropy.py [n_experts=48]   (CPU only)"""
import json, sys
import numpy as np

D = "/Users/kamen/BigMoE/models/bare/records3/"
IX = json.load(open(D + "index.json"))["layers"]
N = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 48
REC = 2932736
rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
picks = [(int(L), int(e)) for L, e in zip(rng.integers(0, 48, N), rng.integers(0, 512, N))]
SH = {0: (640, 2560), 1: (640, 2560), 2: (2560, 640)}      # gate, up [640, 2560]; down [2560, 640] (out, K)


def unpack3(b, n):
    t = b.reshape(-1, 3).astype(np.uint32)
    v = t[:, 0] | (t[:, 1] << 8) | (t[:, 2] << 16)
    return ((v[:, None] >> (3 * np.arange(8, dtype=np.uint32))) & 7).reshape(-1)[:n].astype(np.uint8)


def H(c):
    p = c[c > 0] / c.sum()
    return float(-(p * np.log2(p)).sum())


hist = np.zeros((3, 8)); cond = np.zeros((3, 8, 8)); sb = [np.zeros(65536), np.zeros(65536)]
bad = 0
for L, e in picks:
    ly = IX[str(L)]
    with open(D + ly["file"], "rb") as f:
        f.seek(e * REC); rec = np.frombuffer(f.read(ly["msb_sz"]), np.uint8)
    off, sz = ly["msb_piece_off"], ly["msb_piece_size"]
    for p in range(3):
        o, K = SH[p]
        m = unpack3(rec[off[3 * p]:off[3 * p] + sz[3 * p]], o * K).reshape(o, K)
