        g = m.reshape(o, K // 64, 64)
        bad += int((g.min(-1) != 0).sum() + (g.max(-1) != 7).sum())
        hist[p] += np.bincount(m.reshape(-1), minlength=8)
        np.add.at(cond[p], (m[:, :-1].reshape(-1), m[:, 1:].reshape(-1)), 1)
        for j, q in ((0, 1), (1, 2)):
            v = np.frombuffer(rec[off[3 * p + q]:off[3 * p + q] + sz[3 * p + q]].tobytes(), np.uint16)
            sb[j] += np.bincount(v, minlength=65536)
print(f"{N} experts; groups whose m does not span 0..7 (unpacking check, want 0): {bad}")
names = ("gate", "up", "down")
h0 = [H(hist[p]) for p in range(3)]
h1 = [sum(cond[p][a].sum() / cond[p].sum() * H(cond[p][a]) for a in range(8)) for p in range(3)]
for p in range(3):
    print(f"{names[p]:4s} m histogram {np.round(hist[p] / hist[p].sum(), 4).tolist()}  H0 {h0[p]:.3f}  H(m | previous m) {h1[p]:.3f} bits")
hs, hb = H(sb[0]), H(sb[1])
print(f"s3 bf16 order-0 entropy {hs:.2f} of 16 bits, b3 {hb:.2f} of 16 bits (one pair per 64 weights)")
cur = 3 + 32 / 64
best = np.mean(h1) + (hs + hb) / 64
print(f"bits per weight: today {cur:.3f}; lossless floor (order-1 codes + order-0 scales) ~{best:.3f} -> "
      f"{100 * (1 - best / cur):.1f} % fewer MSB bytes per miss")


### scripts/mtp_ab125.py
"""MTP acceptance A/B on the 125B, one load, draft head and MTP-history policy varied in place.
Draft heads:
  A  the head as BigMoE loads it (bare sidecar: 4-bit g32 experts, 4-bit g64 attention, 8-bit router/indexer)
  R  control: the same sidecar re-attached by attach() below (must equal A token for token and draft for draft)
  B  the official bf16 head (Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, fetched by fetch_mtp_bf16.py, mapped as mtp_bf16_check.py)
  D  control for B: the sidecar dequantized to bf16 in dense modules (A's values, B's kernels)
MTP-history policy (generate_mtpk's mtp_history_policy): "cycle" is the Python API default that BigMoE's scripts get
(the head never sees the prompt), "committed" is MTPLX's product default (server, CLI): the head's own KV cache is
primed with the prompt and extended with every committed token.
Prompts and samplers as mtp_accept125.py (code, math, prose; greedy depth 3 and 1, temperature 1.0 depth 3; 128 tokens).
Greedy outputs must be identical across all runs (the verify keeps outputs exact for any draft head and history).
usage: mtp_ab125.py OFFICIAL.safetensors [FRAC] [RUNS]   RUNS like "A:cycle,A:committed,R:committed,B:committed,D:committed"
(GPU, BigMoE-size memory: exclusive window)"""
import gc, hashlib, json, os, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)                 # as BigMoE's mtp_diag.py (MTPLX server defaults)
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx.utils import tree_flatten
from mtp_bf16_check import official_to_mtplx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe import fuse
OFF = sys.argv[1]
frac = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0.25
RUNS = [r.split(":") for r in (sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else
                                "A:cycle,A:committed,R:committed,B:committed,D:committed,B:cycle").split(",")]
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
C = round(frac * 512)
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, C)
import mtplx.models.qwen4_exp as Q
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx import generation as G
from bigmoe.gated import ext
TM = rt.model.language_model
ARGS = TM.args


def stats(v):
    v = v.astype(mx.float32)
    return f"mean {v.mean().item():+.4f} min {v.min().item():+.4f} max {v.max().item():+.4f}"


print("runtime MTP norms as loaded (pre_fc norms should read raw + 1):", flush=True)
for n, p in tree_flatten(TM.mtp.parameters()):
    if p.ndim == 1:
        print(f"  {n:58s} {stats(p)}", flush=True)


def stripped_of(d):
    """{mtp.*} -> attach_mtp's stripped dict (prefix removed, +1 on the raw pre_fc norms, as attach_mtp does)."""
    s = {k[4:]: v for k, v in d.items() if k.startswith("mtp.")}
    for n in ("pre_fc_norm_embedding.weight", "pre_fc_norm_hidden.weight"):
        s[n] = (s[n].astype(mx.float32) + 1.0).astype(s[n].dtype)
    return s


def module_inputs():
    mtp = Q.Qwen4ExpMTP(ARGS)
    return {p[:-7]: int(a.shape[-1]) for p, a in tree_flatten(mtp.parameters()) if p.endswith(".weight") and a.ndim >= 2}


def attach(s):
    """attach_mtp's build, from a dict: quantize the modules the dict ships packed, load strict, publish."""
    mtp = Q.Qwen4ExpMTP(ARGS)
    qmap = {}
    for mod, in_f in module_inputs().items():
        w, sc = s.get(f"{mod}.weight"), s.get(f"{mod}.scales")
        if w is None or sc is None or w.dtype != mx.uint32:
            continue
        qmap[mod] = {"bits": int(w.shape[-1]) * 32 // in_f, "group_size": in_f // int(sc.shape[-1])}
    if qmap:
        nn.quantize(mtp, group_size=64, bits=8, class_predicate=lambda p, m: qmap.get(p) or False)
    mtp.load_weights(list(s.items()), strict=True)
    mtp.eval()
    mx.eval(mtp.parameters())
    TM.mtp = mtp
    return len(qmap)


def dequantized(s):
    """the dict with every packed module dequantized to bf16 (bits/group inferred as attach_mtp does)."""
    out = dict(s)
    for mod, in_f in module_inputs().items():
        w, sc = s.get(f"{mod}.weight"), s.get(f"{mod}.scales")
        if w is None or sc is None or w.dtype != mx.uint32:
            continue
        bits, gs = int(w.shape[-1]) * 32 // in_f, in_f // int(sc.shape[-1])
        out[f"{mod}.weight"] = mx.dequantize(w, sc, s[f"{mod}.biases"], group_size=gs, bits=bits).astype(mx.bfloat16)
        del out[f"{mod}.scales"], out[f"{mod}.biases"]
    return out


g8 = json.loads(open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl")).readline())["question"]
PROMPTS = {
    "code": open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
    "math": "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + g8,
    "prose": "Explain how a refrigerator works to a curious 10 year old, in about 150 words.",
}
IDS = {}
for name, text in PROMPTS.items():
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    IDS[name] = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
SAMPLERS = {"greedy": SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0), "t1.0": SamplerConfig(temperature=1.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)}
CONFIGS = [("greedy", 3), ("greedy", 1), ("t1.0", 3)]
rows = []


def measure(head, policy):
    for name, ids in IDS.items():
        for sname, depth in CONFIGS:
            stamps = []

            def cb(toks):
                if not stamps:
                    ext.reset_stats()               # decode only: the prefill is done by the first callback
                stamps.append((time.perf_counter(), len(toks) if isinstance(toks, list) else 1))
            fuse.reset_generation_state(rt.model)
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            res = G.generate_mtpk(rt, ids, max_tokens=128, sampler=SAMPLERS[sname], speculative_depth=depth, seed=0,
                                  mtp_history_policy=policy, token_callback=cb)
            dt = time.perf_counter() - t0
            x = ext.stats()
            toks, S = list(res.tokens), res.stats
            acc, dr = list(S.accepted_by_depth), list(S.drafted_by_depth)
            rounds = dr[0] if dr else 0
            cond = [round(acc[0] / dr[0], 3) if dr[0] else None] + [round(acc[i] / acc[i - 1], 3) if acc[i - 1] else None
                                                                    for i in range(1, len(acc))]
            n_dec = max(1, sum(k for _, k in stamps) - stamps[0][1]) if stamps else 1
            dec = stamps[-1][0] - stamps[0][0] if len(stamps) > 1 else float("nan")
            row = dict(head=head, policy=policy, prompt=name, sampler=sname, depth=depth, tokens=len(toks),
                       verify_calls=S.verify_calls, mtp_rounds=rounds, accepted_by_depth=acc, drafted_by_depth=dr,
                       conditional_accept=cond, overall_accept=round(sum(acc) / max(1, sum(dr)), 3),
                       mtp_tokens_per_round=round((sum(acc) + rounds) / max(1, rounds), 2),
                       tokens_per_verify=round(len(toks) / max(1, S.verify_calls), 2),
                       decode_tok_s=round(n_dec / dec, 2) if dec == dec and dec > 0 else None,
                       stalled_per_tok=round(x["stalled"] / n_dec, 2), misses_per_tok=round(x["misses"] / n_dec, 2),
                       io_ms_per_tok=round(1e3 * x.get("io_s", 0.0) / n_dec, 2),
                       sha=hashlib.sha1(str(toks).encode()).hexdigest()[:10], seconds=round(dt, 1))
            rows.append(row)
            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)


cur = "A"
for head, policy in RUNS:
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    if head != cur:
        if head in ("R", "D"):
            bare = stripped_of(mx.load(os.path.join(MD, "mtp.safetensors")))
            nq = attach(dequantized(bare) if head == "D" else bare)
            del bare
        elif head == "B":
            off = stripped_of(official_to_mtplx(mx.load(OFF)))
            nq = attach(off)
            del off
        else:
            raise SystemExit(f"head {head}: A is only the head as loaded (run it first)")
        cur = head
        gc.collect(); mx.clear_cache()
        print(f"[{head}] attached ({nq} quantized modules) in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.0f}s, "
              f"active {mx.get_active_memory() / 1e9:.1f} GB", flush=True)
    try:
        measure(head, policy)
    except Exception:
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        print(f"[{head}:{policy}] FAILED, continuing", flush=True)
    json.dump(rows, open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/mtp_ab125.json"), "w"), indent=1)
st.gate.check()
print(f"\nC={C} slots/layer ({C * 2.77 * 48 / 1e3:.0f} GB experts). Pooled over the 3 prompts:")
print("head policy     config    cond. accept by position   overall  MTP tok/round  tok/verify  decode tok/s  greedy sha")
for head, policy in RUNS:
    for sname, depth in CONFIGS:
        rs = [r for r in rows if r["head"] == head and r["policy"] == policy and r["sampler"] == sname and r["depth"] == depth]
        if not rs:
            continue
        acc = [sum(r["accepted_by_depth"][i] for r in rs) for i in range(depth)]
        dr = [sum(r["drafted_by_depth"][i] for r in rs) for i in range(depth)]
        cond = [acc[0] / max(1, dr[0])] + [acc[i] / acc[i - 1] if acc[i - 1] else 0 for i in range(1, depth)]
        tpr = (sum(acc) + dr[0]) / max(1, dr[0])
        tpv = sum(r["tokens"] for r in rs) / sum(r["verify_calls"] for r in rs)
        tps = sum(r["decode_tok_s"] or 0 for r in rs) / len(rs)
        sha = "/".join(r["sha"][:4] for r in rs) if sname == "greedy" else ""
        print(f"{head:4s} {policy:10s} {sname:6s} d{depth}  " + " ".join(f"{c:.3f}" for c in cond).ljust(27)
              + f"{sum(acc) / max(1, sum(dr)):.3f}    {tpr:.2f}           {tpv:.2f}        {tps:5.1f}        {sha}", flush=True)


### scripts/mtp_accept125.py
"""MTP acceptance per draft position on the 125B (Qwen3.8-Flash-Next) through MTPLX on BigMoE's exact streamed runtime

def chat_ids(text):
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    return list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])

                       tokens_per_verify=round(tpv, 2), accepted_by_depth=acc, drafted_by_depth=dr, accept_rate_by_depth=rate,
                       mean_accept_prob_by_depth=mp, bonus=S.bonus_tokens, correction=S.correction_tokens,
                       skipped=S.skipped_drafts, seconds=round(dt, 1))
            rows.append(row)
            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

### scripts/mtp_bf16_check.py
"""Validate the official bf16 MTP tensors (fetch_mtp_bf16.py) against BigMoE's bare sidecar (mtp.safetensors) before
swapping them in: map the official names/layout to MTPLX's (experts.gate_up_proj [E, 2*inter, hidden] split on axis 1 into
switch_mlp.gate_proj / up_proj, experts.down_proj -> switch_mlp.down_proj, the converter's +1 on hc/q/k/qk-layernorm norms;
pre_fc norms stay raw, attach_mtp adds +1), then for every sidecar tensor:
  bf16 in the sidecar  -> must be bit-identical to the mapped official tensor;
  packed in the sidecar -> re-quantize the official tensor with the sidecar's own bits/group (inferred as attach_mtp does)
                           and report identical words, and the error of the sidecar's dequantized values vs bf16.
CPU only.   usage: mtp_bf16_check.py OFFICIAL.safetensors"""
import os, sys
import mlx.core as mx
SIDE = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare/mtp.safetensors")
SHIFT = (".q_norm.weight", ".k_norm.weight", ".q_layernorm.weight", ".k_layernorm.weight", ".hc_norm.weight")


def official_to_mtplx(off):
    """official {mtp.*: bf16} -> {mtp.*: bf16} in the sidecar's names and layout (pre_fc norms raw)."""
    out = {}
    for k, v in off.items():
        if k.endswith("mlp.experts.gate_up_proj"):
            p = k[: -len("experts.gate_up_proj")]
            assert v.shape[1] == 2 * 640 and v.shape[2] == 2560, v.shape
            g, u = mx.split(v, 2, axis=1)
            out[p + "switch_mlp.gate_proj.weight"], out[p + "switch_mlp.up_proj.weight"] = g, u
        elif k.endswith("mlp.experts.down_proj"):
            assert v.shape[1] == 2560 and v.shape[2] == 640, v.shape
            out[k[: -len("experts.down_proj")] + "switch_mlp.down_proj.weight"] = v
        elif v.ndim == 1 and k.endswith(SHIFT):
            out[k] = (v.astype(mx.float32) + 1.0).astype(v.dtype)
        else:
            out[k] = v
    return out


if __name__ == "__main__":
    mx.set_default_device(mx.cpu)      # only for the standalone check: importers keep the GPU
    off = official_to_mtplx(mx.load(sys.argv[1]))
    side = mx.load(SIDE)
    mods = sorted({k.rsplit(".", 1)[0] for k in side})
    worst = 0.0
    for m in mods:
        w = side.get(m + ".weight")
        ref = off.get(m + ".weight")
        if ref is None:
            print(f"MISSING in official: {m}.weight"); continue
        if w.dtype != mx.uint32:
            same = bool(mx.array_equal(w, ref).item())
            print(f"{m:62s} bf16 {tuple(w.shape)}  bit-identical: {same}")
            if not same:
                d = mx.abs(w.astype(mx.float32) - ref.astype(mx.float32))
                print(f"    max |diff| {d.max().item():.4g}, mean {d.mean().item():.4g}")
            continue
        s, b = side[m + ".scales"], side[m + ".biases"]
        in_f = ref.shape[-1]
        bits = w.shape[-1] * 32 // in_f
        gs = in_f // s.shape[-1]
        # big expert tensors: compare a slice of experts to keep the CPU check short
        sl = (slice(0, 8),) if ref.ndim == 3 else (slice(None),)
        r = ref[sl]
        qw, qs, qb = mx.quantize(r, group_size=gs, bits=bits)
        eq_w = mx.mean((qw == w[sl]).astype(mx.float32)).item()
        eq_s = mx.mean((qs == s[sl]).astype(mx.float32)).item()
        deq = mx.dequantize(w[sl], s[sl], b[sl], group_size=gs, bits=bits).astype(mx.float32)
        rf = r.astype(mx.float32)
        rel = (mx.sqrt(mx.mean((deq - rf) ** 2)) / mx.sqrt(mx.mean(rf ** 2))).item()
        own = mx.dequantize(qw, qs, qb, group_size=gs, bits=bits).astype(mx.float32)
        rel_own = (mx.sqrt(mx.mean((own - rf) ** 2)) / mx.sqrt(mx.mean(rf ** 2))).item()
        worst = max(worst, rel)
        print(f"{m:62s} {bits}-bit g{gs} {tuple(r.shape)}  words equal {eq_w:.4f} scales equal {eq_s:.4f}  "
              f"rel.err sidecar {rel:.4f} (own RTN {rel_own:.4f})")
    print(f"worst sidecar rel.err vs official bf16: {worst:.4f}  (a layout or mapping error would be ~1.4)")

def head_tokens(text, n):
    ids = tok.encode(text)

    "prose4k": "Here is a research note:\n\n" + head_tokens(doc, CTX) + "\n\nSummarize the note in about 200 words for an "

### scripts/mtp_resident_draft125.py
"""Round 32o: resident-only routing for the MTP drafter (24 GB mode, MTP_STREAM=1). The MTP head's experts stream through
gate layer 48 and its 3 draft steps stall on misses (~1.4-1.8 ms each, ~4.5-5.1 ms/pass: data/mtp_draft_stalls125.txt).
Drafts only affect acceptance (verification stays exact), so the MTP router may pick among RESIDENT experts only: mask
the router's probabilities with the layer-48 residency snapshot (ext.lookup, CPU table; the MTP pool only evicts on its
own misses, which then no longer happen), top-k among them, renormalize as the block does. Arms (one process each):
  A  normal drafts                B  resident-only drafts
Same greedy prompts; reports tokens, verify passes (tokens/pass = acceptance), wall tok/s, and checks that B's output
tokens == A's (they must: verification is exact). usage: [REF ENV] mtp_resident_draft125.py [FRAC] [MAXTOK]
(GPU, model memory; 24 GB mode needs BIGMOE_MTP_STREAM=1 BIGMOE_RECORDS=3 in the environment)"""
import hashlib, json, os, subprocess, sys, time
ARM = os.environ.get("MRD_ARM")
FRAC = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.25
MAXTOK = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 192
if ARM is None:
    res = {}
    for arm in ("A", "B"):
        out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, __file__, str(FRAC), str(MAXTOK)], env=dict(os.environ, MRD_ARM=arm),
                             capture_output=True, text=True)
        lines = [l for l in out.stdout.splitlines() if l.startswith("RESULT ")]
        res[arm] = json.loads(lines[-1][7:]) if lines else {"error": out.stderr[-800:]}
        print(arm, json.dumps(res[arm]), flush=True)
    if all("prompts" in r for r in res.values()):
        for p in res["A"]["prompts"]:
            a, b = res["A"]["prompts"][p], res["B"]["prompts"][p]
            print(f"{p}: tokens/pass A {a['tpp']:.2f} B {b['tpp']:.2f} | tok/s A {a['tps']:.1f} B {b['tps']:.1f} ({b['tps'] / a['tps'] - 1:+.1%}) | "
                  f"outputs identical: {a['sha'] == b['sha']}", flush=True)
    sys.exit(0)
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe.gated import ext
import mtplx.models.qwen4_exp as Q
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx import generation as Gen
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, round(FRAC * 512))
TM = rt.model.language_model
mtp = getattr(TM, "mtp", None) or getattr(rt.model, "mtp", None)
MTP_BLOCK = mtp.layers[0].mlp
MTP_L = getattr(MTP_BLOCK.switch_mlp, "_bm", {}).get("L")
print("MTP gate layer:", MTP_L, flush=True)
if ARM == "B" and MTP_L is not None:
    _orig = type(MTP_BLOCK).__call__
    ALL = list(range(int(MTP_BLOCK.gate.weight.shape[0])))

    def call(self, x):
        if self is not MTP_BLOCK:
            return _orig(self, x)
        slots = np.array(ext.lookup(MTP_L, ALL))            # CPU residency snapshot of the MTP pool
        res = mx.array(slots >= 0)
        if int(res.sum().item()) < self.top_k:               # pool not warm yet: normal routing
            return _orig(self, x)
        gates = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
        gates = mx.where(res, gates, mx.zeros_like(gates))
        k = self.top_k
        inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
        scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
        if self.norm_topk_prob:
            scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        y = (self.switch_mlp(x, inds) * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
        return y + mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * self.shared_expert(x)

    type(MTP_BLOCK).__call__ = call
probs = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))]
PROMPTS = {"code": open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
           "math": "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + probs[0]["question"],
           "chat": "Explain to a curious teenager why the sky is blue, in a few short paragraphs."}
smp = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)
out = {}
for name, text in PROMPTS.items():
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
    first = []
    cb = lambda toks: first.append(time.perf_counter()) if not first else None
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    r = Gen.generate_mtpk(rt, ids, max_tokens=MAXTOK, sampler=smp, speculative_depth=3, seed=0,
                          mtp_history_policy="committed", token_callback=cb)
    t1 = time.perf_counter()
    toks = list(r.tokens)
    dec = t1 - (first[0] if first else t0)
    out[name] = dict(tokens=len(toks), passes=int(r.stats.verify_calls), tpp=len(toks) / max(1, r.stats.verify_calls),
                     tps=len(toks) / max(1e-9, dec), sha=hashlib.sha256(str(toks).encode()).hexdigest()[:12])
    print(name, out[name], flush=True)
print("RESULT " + json.dumps({"prompts": out}), flush=True)


### scripts/mtp_route_pred125.py
"""Round 23: can the MTP draft head predict the trunk's experts for the next verify pass (cross-pass prefetch lead time)?
SP-MoE (arXiv 2510.10302) feeds a separate, layer-aligned draft model's attention outputs to the target's gates
(top-1 ~88 %) and prefetches while drafting. Here the drafter is the 125B's own 1-layer MTP head: its output state
(the widened [hc*d] stream it chains on) for draft step j sits at the position of verify row j-1. During drafting we
apply every trunk layer's MoE read (mlp hyper-connection mix of the hc-normed stream) and router to it and keep the top
PRED experts per layer; the verify pass then reveals the actual top-10 of every row (gate hook, as verify_trace125.py).
Baselines from the same trace: the previous position's experts at the same layer, and the previous pass's union.
Recall@10/20 by layer group is the measure; if deep layers are predictable a whole pass ahead, their misses can load
during drafting and the first layers of the verify pass.
Output: data/mtp_route_pred125.npz/json.   usage: mtp_route_pred125.py [FRAC] [MAXTOK]   (GPU, exclusive window)"""
import json, os, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_CONTEXT_COPY", "0")           # keep every round an MTP verify pass of <= 4 rows
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe import fuse
frac = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.5
MAXTOK = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 300
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, round(frac * 512))
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx import generation as G
TM = rt.model.language_model
LAYERS = TM.model.layers
NL, K, PRED = len(LAYERS), 10, 20
HC, D = TM.args.hc_count, TM.args.hidden_size
gate = st.gate
REC, MTPP = [], []                       # gate calls (seq, L, ids); MTP predictions (seq, step, [NL, PRED] ids)
_orig = gate.route_and_gate


def hook(L, ids, seq=None, preds=None, counts=None):
    REC.append((gate.seq if seq is None else seq, L, ids))
    return _orig(L, ids, seq=seq, preds=preds, counts=counts)


gate.route_and_gate = hook


def predict(h):
    """top-PRED experts of every trunk layer's router on the stream h [.., hc*d] (each layer's own MoE read)."""
    out = []
    for layer in LAYERS:
        hcm = layer.mlp_hyper_connection
        normed = hcm.hc_norm(h)
        w = nn.silu(hcm.input_mix_weight_down(normed) / HC)
        w = mx.sigmoid(hcm.input_mix_weight_up(w))
        mixed = (w.reshape(*w.shape[:-1], HC, D) * normed.reshape(*normed.shape[:-1], HC, D)).mean(axis=-2)
        lg = layer.mlp.gate(mixed).reshape(-1, 512)[-1]
        out.append(mx.argsort(-lg.astype(mx.float32))[:PRED])
    return mx.stack(out)


mtp = TM.mtp
_fr = mtp.fuse_and_run
STEP = [0, -1]                                          # draft step within the current round, seq it belongs to


def fr_hook(widened, tok_emb, cache):
    h = _fr(widened, tok_emb, cache)
    s = int(gate.seq)
    if STEP[1] != s:
        STEP[0], STEP[1] = 0, s
    STEP[0] += 1
    p = predict(h)
    mx.eval(p)
    MTPP.append((s, STEP[0], np.array(p, dtype=np.int16)))
    return h


object.__setattr__(mtp, "fuse_and_run", fr_hook)
lines = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))]
PROMPTS = [
    open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
    "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + lines[0]["question"],
    "Explain how a refrigerator works to a curious 10 year old, in about 150 words.",
    "Explain in detail how TCP congestion control works: slow start, congestion avoidance, fast retransmit and fast "
    "recovery, and how CUBIC and BBR differ. Use headings and a short example.",
    "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + lines[1]["question"],
]
smp = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)
CB, META, passes, preds = [], [], [], []
for pi, text in enumerate(PROMPTS):
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
    fuse.reset_generation_state(rt.model)
    REC.clear(); MTPP.clear()

    def cb(toks, pi=pi):
        CB.append((pi, int(gate.seq), len(toks) if isinstance(toks, list) else 1))
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    res = G.generate_mtpk(rt, ids, max_tokens=MAXTOK, sampler=smp, speculative_depth=3, seed=0,
                          mtp_history_policy="committed", token_callback=cb)
    S = res.stats
    byseq = {}
    for seq, L, a in REC:
        byseq.setdefault(int(seq), {})[int(L)] = np.array(a.reshape(-1, K), dtype=np.int16)
    for seq in sorted(byseq):
        passes.append((pi, seq, byseq[seq]))
    for s, j, p in MTPP:
        preds.append((pi, s, j, p))
    META.append(dict(prompt=pi, tokens=len(res.tokens), verify_calls=S.verify_calls, accepted_by_depth=list(S.accepted_by_depth),
                     gated_passes=len(byseq), mtp_steps=len(MTPP), seconds=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 1)))
    print(json.dumps(META[-1]), flush=True)
st.gate.check()
P = len(passes)
R = max(max(a.shape[0] for a in d.values()) for _, _, d in passes)
arr = np.full((P, NL, R, K), -1, np.int16)
rows = np.zeros(P, np.int16); seqs = np.zeros(P, np.int64); pidx = np.zeros(P, np.int16)
for i, (pi, seq, d) in enumerate(passes):
    pidx[i], seqs[i] = pi, seq
    for L, a in d.items():
        arr[i, L, :a.shape[0]] = a
        rows[i] = max(rows[i], a.shape[0])
pp = np.stack([p for (_, _, _, p) in preds]) if preds else np.zeros((0, NL, PRED), np.int16)
np.savez_compressed(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/mtp_route_pred125.npz"), ids=arr, rows=rows, seq=seqs, prompt=pidx,
                    pred=pp, pred_prompt=np.array([a for (a, _, _, _) in preds], np.int16),
                    pred_seq=np.array([b for (_, b, _, _) in preds], np.int64), pred_step=np.array([c for (_, _, c, _) in preds], np.int16))
json.dump(dict(callbacks=CB, meta=META, K=K, PRED=PRED), open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/mtp_route_pred125.json"), "w"))
print(f"{P} gated passes, {len(preds)} MTP draft steps with predictions", flush=True)


### scripts/mtp_sweep125.py
"""MTP sweeps on the 125B over BigMoE's streamed runtime with MTPLX's product history setting ("committed", round 21):
decode tok/s, acceptance by draft position and the loader's work per token, for given draft depths at one expert-cache
size (C = FRAC x 512 slots per layer; C=128 is 17 GB of experts, the 24 GB-total target). Depth 0 = eager decode (MTP
off, generate_ar). Prompts: BigMoE's code prompt, GSM8K #0, a prose request; greedy; MAXTOK tokens each.
Night jobs (round 22): the depth sweep at 17 GB (depths 0-6), and the speed-vs-memory curve (depth 3 and eager at
C = 96 .. 256). Utilization per Kamen's rule: SSD GB/s (misses x 2.77 MB / time) and the loader-read share of wall time.
usage: mtp_sweep125.py FRAC DEPTHS [MAXTOK]   e.g. mtp_sweep125.py 0.25 0,1,2,3,4,5,6 192
(GPU, BigMoE-size memory: exclusive window)"""
import hashlib, json, os, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import mlx.core as mx

from bigmoe import fuse

DEPTHS = [int(x) for x in sys.argv[2].split(",")]

MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
C = round(frac * 512)

rt, st = load_streamed(MD, C)

from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig

g8 = json.loads(open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl")).readline())["question"]

if os.environ.get("PROMPTS") == "6":                     # night runs: three more kinds (explainer, 2nd math, code edit)

        "edit": "Add type hints and one-line docstrings to every function in this Python code, and return the full "
                "edited code only:\n\n```python\n" + _src + "```",
    })
IDS = {}
for name, text in PROMPTS.items():
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    IDS[name] = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
greedy = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)
rows = []
for depth in DEPTHS:
    for name, ids in IDS.items():
        stamps = []

        def cb(toks):
            if not stamps:
                ext.reset_stats()                   # decode only: the prefill is done by the first callback
            stamps.append((time.perf_counter(), len(toks) if isinstance(toks, list) else 1))
        fuse.reset_generation_state(rt.model)
        if depth == 0:
            res = G.generate_ar(rt, ids, max_tokens=MAXTOK, sampler=greedy, token_callback=cb)
        else:
            res = G.generate_mtpk(rt, ids, max_tokens=MAXTOK, sampler=greedy, speculative_depth=depth, seed=0,
                                  mtp_history_policy="committed", token_callback=cb)
        x = ext.stats()
        toks = list(getattr(res, "tokens", None) or [])
        S = getattr(res, "stats", None)
        acc = list(S.accepted_by_depth) if depth and S else []
        dr = list(S.drafted_by_depth) if depth and S else []
        n_dec = max(1, sum(k for _, k in stamps) - stamps[0][1]) if stamps else 1
        dec = stamps[-1][0] - stamps[0][0] if len(stamps) > 1 else float("nan")
        tps = n_dec / dec if dec == dec and dec > 0 else float("nan")
        row = dict(C=C, depth=depth, prompt=name, tokens=len(toks), decode_tok_s=round(tps, 2),
                   verify_calls=getattr(S, "verify_calls", None) if depth else None,

for depth in DEPTHS:
    rs = [r for r in rows if r["depth"] == depth]
    tps = sum(r["decode_tok_s"] for r in rs) / len(rs)
    ms = 1e3 / tps
    io = sum(r["io_ms_per_tok"] for r in rs) / len(rs)
    miss = sum(r["misses_per_tok"] for r in rs) / len(rs)
    stl = sum(r["stalled_per_tok"] for r in rs) / len(rs)
    if depth:
        acc = [sum(r["accepted_by_depth"][i] for r in rs) for i in range(depth)]
        dr = [sum(r["drafted_by_depth"][i] for r in rs) for i in range(depth)]
        cond = [acc[0] / max(1, dr[0])] + [acc[i] / acc[i - 1] if acc[i - 1] else 0 for i in range(1, depth)]
        tpr = (sum(acc) + dr[0]) / max(1, dr[0])
        cs = " ".join(f"{c:.2f}" for c in cond)
    else:
        cs, tpr = "(eager)", 1.0
    print(f"{depth:5d}  {cs:40s} {tpr:5.2f}         {tps:5.1f}   {io / ms:5.0%}              {miss * 2.77 / ms:5.2f}     {stl:5.1f}",
          flush=True)


### scripts/nax_gemm.py
"""Prefill-width 4-bit GEMM throughput on the M5 Pro (applegpu_g17s; MLX 0.32.x has NAX kernels incl. gather_qmm_rhs_nax and
qmm_t_nax, dispatched automatically): real 125B layer-0 expert weights, sorted gather_qmm (SwitchGLU's prefill path) at
rows/expert 40..320 for gate (K=2560 -> 640) and down (K=640 -> 2560), and a dense qmm of a GDN in_proj shape
(K=2560 -> 16480) at M=2048/8192. TFLOPS (2*M*K*N / t) and determinism.   usage: nax_gemm.py   (GPU, quiet lock, ~1 min)"""
import json, os, struct, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
idx = json.load(open(f"{MD}/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]


def tensor(name):
    f = f"{MD}/{idx[name]}"
    with open(f, "rb") as fh:
        n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]; m = json.loads(fh.read(n))[name]
    a, b = m["data_offsets"]; dt = {"U32": np.uint32, "BF16": np.uint16}[m["dtype"]]
    arr = mx.array(np.array(np.memmap(f, dt, "r", offset=8 + n + a, shape=((b - a) // np.dtype(dt).itemsize,))).reshape(m["shape"]))
    return arr.view(mx.bfloat16) if m["dtype"] == "BF16" else arr


pre = "language_model.model.layers.0."
W = {p: tuple(tensor(pre + f"mlp.switch_mlp.{p}.{f}") for f in ("weight", "scales", "biases")) for p in ("gate_proj", "down_proj")}
QKV = tuple(tensor(pre + f"linear_attn.in_proj_qkv.{f}") for f in ("weight", "scales", "biases"))
mx.eval(W, QKV)
E = 512


def t(f, n=7):
    for _ in range(2): mx.eval(f())
    ts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        a = time.perf_counter(); mx.eval(f()); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[n // 2]


print("device", mx.device_info()["architecture"], flush=True)
for rpe in (40, 80, 160, 320):
    ids = mx.array(np.repeat(np.arange(E), rpe).astype(np.uint32)); P = E * rpe
    for name, K, N in (("gate", 2560, 640), ("down", 640, 2560)):
        x = (mx.random.normal((P, 1, K)) * 0.5).astype(mx.bfloat16)
        f = lambda: mx.gather_qmm(x, *W[name + "_proj"], rhs_indices=ids, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4, sorted_indices=True)
        dt = t(f)
        det = mx.array_equal(f(), f()).item()
        print(f"gather_qmm sorted {name} K={K} N={N}: {rpe:3d} rows/expert, {P} rows: {dt*1e3:7.2f} ms -> "
              f"{2 * P * K * N / dt / 1e12:5.1f} TFLOPS, deterministic {det}", flush=True)
for M in (2048, 8192):
    x = (mx.random.normal((M, 2560)) * 0.5).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    f = lambda: mx.quantized_matmul(x, *QKV, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4)
    dt = t(f); N = QKV[0].shape[0]
    print(f"dense qmm in_proj_qkv K=2560 N={N} M={M}: {dt*1e3:6.2f} ms -> {2 * M * 2560 * N / dt / 1e12:5.1f} TFLOPS", flush=True)


### scripts/official_peek.py
"""Fetch small 1-D (norm) tensors of the official Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next checkpoint by HTTP range and print the same
stats as st_peek.py, to compare the raw (HF) norm convention with BigMoE's converted bare model.
usage: official_peek.py REGEX   (only 1-D tensors under 1 MB are fetched)"""
import json, re, struct, subprocess, sys, urllib.request
import numpy as np
REPO = "https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next"
pat = re.compile(sys.argv[1])
idx = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(f"{REPO}/raw/main/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]


def rng(fname, a, b):
    r = subprocess.run(["curl", "-sfL", "-r", f"{a}-{b - 1}", f"{REPO}/resolve/main/{fname}"], capture_output=True, check=True)
    assert len(r.stdout) == b - a
    return r.stdout


want = {}
for k, f in idx.items():
    if pat.search(k):
        want.setdefault(f, []).append(k)
for f, keys in sorted(want.items()):
    n = struct.unpack("<Q", rng(f, 0, 8))[0]
    hdr = json.loads(rng(f, 8, 8 + n))
    for k in sorted(keys):
        m = hdr[k]
        a, b = m["data_offsets"]
        if len(m["shape"]) != 1 or b - a > 1 << 20 or m["dtype"] != "BF16":
            continue
        v = (np.frombuffer(rng(f, 8 + n + a, 8 + n + b), np.uint16).astype(np.uint32) << 16).view(np.float32)
        print(f"{k:70s} BF16 n={v.size:6d} mean {v.mean():+.4f} min {v.min():+.4f} max {v.max():+.4f}  {np.round(v[:6], 3).tolist()}", flush=True)


### scripts/online_pred.py
"""Branch-predictor-style next-token expert prediction, trained online (no offline training).
For token t+1 at layer L, predict the top-8 experts from token t's routing at layers L-1, L, L+1
(available a whole token ahead). Models learn as they go, like a CPU's perceptron prefetcher.
Baseline (MoEPrefetch step 6): token t's own top-16 at layer L, recall ~0.51 at a 16-expert budget.
Uses ~/MoEPrefetch/traces/*.npz (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, 40 layers x 256 experts, top-8)."""
import sys
import numpy as np

E, NL, B = 256, 40, 16
sets = sys.argv[1:] or ["chat", "code", "bulgarian", "realcode"]


def run(name):
    z = np.load(f"/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/traces/{name}.npz")
    doc = z["doc"]
    act = np.stack([z[f"L{l}_act"][:, :8] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int64)   # (T, 40, 8)
    act16 = np.stack([z[f"L{l}_act"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int64)        # router top-16
    T = len(doc)
    # features: experts of token t at layers L-1, L, L+1 -> index (k*E + e), k in 0..2
    R = int(__import__("os").environ.get("R", "1")); C = np.zeros((NL, (2 * R + 1) * E, E), np.float32)      # co-occurrence counts f(t) -> e(t+1)
    W = np.zeros((NL, (2 * R + 1) * E, E), np.float32)      # online logistic weights
    bias = np.zeros((NL, E), np.float32)
    hits = {"temporal16": 0, "markov": 0, "logistic": 0, "markov+temporal": 0}
    n = 0
    lr = float(__import__("os").environ.get("LR", "0.05"))
    for t in range(T - 1):
        if doc[t] != doc[t + 1]:
            continue
        warm = t > 200
        for L in range(NL):
            feats = []
            for k, LL in enumerate(range(L - R, L + R + 1)):
                if 0 <= LL < NL:
                    feats.extend(k * E + act[t, LL])
            feats = np.array(feats)
            truth = act[t + 1, L]
            sm = C[L, feats].sum(0)
            sl = W[L, feats].sum(0) + bias[L]
            if warm:
                n += 8
                hits["temporal16"] += np.isin(truth, act16[t, L]).sum()
                hits["markov"] += np.isin(truth, np.argpartition(-sm, B)[:B]).sum()
                hits["logistic"] += np.isin(truth, np.argpartition(-sl, B)[:B]).sum()
                mix = np.concatenate([act[t, L], [e for e in np.argsort(-sm) if e not in act[t, L]][:B - 8]])
                hits["markov+temporal"] += np.isin(truth, mix).sum()
            # online updates
            C[L, feats[:, None], truth[None, :]] += 1
            y = np.zeros(E, np.float32); y[truth] = 1
            p = 1 / (1 + np.exp(-sl))
            g = (y - p) * lr
            W[L, feats] += g
            bias[L] += g
    print(f"{name:10s} tokens {T}: recall@{B} " + "  ".join(f"{k} {v/n:.3f}" for k, v in hits.items()), flush=True)


for s in sets:
    run(s)


### scripts/opt_bound125.py
"""First-principles bound on SSD traffic for the 125B (data/verify_trace125.npz: 401 committed MTP verify passes x 48
layers x 4 rows x top-10). Per layer, a cache of C expert slots; compare LRU (today's policy) with Belady's OPT (evict
the expert whose next use is farthest: the floor for ANY replacement / perfect-prediction prefetch policy) at the same C.
Also: distinct experts per pass vs rows (how verify width amortizes expert loads).
usage: opt_bound125.py   (CPU, seconds)"""
import numpy as np

z = np.load("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/verify_trace125.npz")
ids = z["ids"].astype(np.int32)
P, L = ids.shape[:2]
print("distinct experts per layer per pass by rows used:",
      {r: round(float(np.mean([len(np.unique(ids[p, l, :r])) for p in range(P) for l in range(0, L, 4)])), 2) for r in (1, 2, 3, 4)})
seqs = [[np.unique(ids[p, l]) for p in range(P)] for l in range(L)]   # per layer: the set each pass needs


def lru(seq, C):
    cache, miss = {}, 0
    for t, need in enumerate(seq):
        for e in need:
            miss += e not in cache
            cache[e] = t
        if len(cache) > C:
            for e, _ in sorted(cache.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])[:len(cache) - C]:
                del cache[e]
    return miss


def opt(seq, C):
    nxt, last = [], {}
    for t in range(len(seq) - 1, -1, -1):
        d = {}
        for e in seq[t]:
            d[e] = last.get(e, 10 ** 9)
            last[e] = t
        nxt.append(d)
    nxt = nxt[::-1]
    cache, miss = {}, 0                                  # e -> its next use
    for t, need in enumerate(seq):
        for e in need:
            miss += e not in cache
            cache[e] = nxt[t][e]
        if len(cache) > C:
            prot = set(need.tolist())
            cand = sorted(((v, e) for e, v in cache.items() if e not in prot), reverse=True)
            for v, e in cand[:len(cache) - C]:
                del cache[e]
    return miss


MB = 2.765                                               # one 4-bit expert
for C in (16, 32, 64, 96, 128, 192, 256):
    ml = sum(lru(s, C) for s in seqs) / P
    mo = sum(opt(s, C) for s in seqs) / P
    print(f"C={C:3d} ({48 * C * MB / 1e3:4.1f} GB): misses/pass LRU {ml:6.1f} ({ml * MB:6.1f} MB) | OPT {mo:6.1f} "
          f"({mo * MB:6.1f} MB) | OPT/LRU {mo / ml:.2f}", flush=True)


### scripts/overlap2q.py
"""Decoupled runahead, measurement 1: do two MLX GPU streams (two Metal command queues) overlap on the M5 Pro?
A = main stream, B = second stream (mx.new_stream(mx.gpu)). Three cases, medians of 9:
  1. spin: A runs a busy-loop kernel on a few threadgroups (the BigMoE gate's spin while the CPU loads experts, ~1 ms);
     B runs a slice-sized chain of 4-bit matvecs (a GDN in_proj-sized matrix, ~24 MB/step). Does B finish while A spins?
  2. busy: A runs a bandwidth-heavy qmv chain (a layer's worth); B the same slice chain. Total vs A alone + B alone.
  3. B alone, A alone: references.
usage: overlap2q.py   (GPU, quiet lock, <1 min)"""
import time
import mlx.core as mx

SA, SB = mx.default_stream(mx.gpu), mx.new_stream(mx.gpu)
SPIN = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="spin", input_names=["n"], output_names=["o"], source="""
    uint acc = thread_position_in_grid.x;
    for (uint i = 0; i < (uint)n[0]; i++) acc = acc * 1664525u + 1013904223u;   // ALU-only busy loop
    o[thread_position_in_grid.x] = acc;
""")


def spin(iters, tgs=2):
    return SPIN(inputs=[mx.array([iters], mx.int32)], grid=(32 * tgs, 1, 1), threadgroup=(32, 1, 1),
                output_shapes=[(32 * tgs,)], output_dtypes=[mx.uint32], stream=SA)[0]


def qmat(rows, cols=2560):
    w = mx.random.randint(0, 2**30, (rows, cols // 8), dtype=mx.int32).view(mx.uint32)
    s = (mx.random.normal((rows, cols // 64)) * 0.01).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    b = (mx.random.normal((rows, cols // 64)) * 0.01).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    mx.eval(w, s, b)
    return w, s, b


WB = qmat(16384)          # ~22 MB: a GDN in_proj (qkv+z) of the 125B -> the slice's main cost per layer
WA = qmat(40000)          # ~55 MB: a main layer's worth of weight reads
x0 = (mx.random.normal((1, 2560)) * 0.1).astype(mx.bfloat16)


def chain(W, n, stream):
    y = x0
    with mx.stream(stream):
        for _ in range(n):
            o = mx.quantized_matmul(y, *W, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4)
            y = (o[:, :2560] * 0.001).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    return y


def t(f, reps=9):
    for _ in range(3): f()
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        a = time.perf_counter(); f(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[reps // 2] * 1e3


# calibrate the spin to ~1 ms
it = 200000
for _ in range(6):
    ms = t(lambda: mx.eval(spin(it)), 5)
    it = int(it * 1.0 / max(ms, 0.05))
ms_spin = t(lambda: mx.eval(spin(it)))
ms_b = t(lambda: mx.eval(chain(WB, 4, SB)))
ms_a = t(lambda: mx.eval(chain(WA, 8, SA)))
print(f"alone: spin {ms_spin:.3f} ms (2 threadgroups) | B slice chain 4 x 22 MB {ms_b:.3f} ms | A main chain 8 x 55 MB {ms_a:.3f} ms", flush=True)


def both_spin():
    a = spin(it); b = chain(WB, 4, SB)
    t0 = time.perf_counter(); mx.async_eval(a); mx.eval(b); tb = time.perf_counter() - t0; mx.eval(a)
    return tb


def both_busy():
    a = chain(WA, 8, SA); b = chain(WB, 4, SB)
    t0 = time.perf_counter(); mx.async_eval(a); mx.eval(b); tb = time.perf_counter() - t0; mx.eval(a)
    return tb, time.perf_counter() - t0


for _ in range(3): both_spin()
tbs = sorted(both_spin() for _ in range(9))
print(f"spin on A + slice on B: B done after {tbs[4]*1e3:.3f} ms (alone {ms_b:.3f}; serialized would be ~{ms_spin + ms_b:.3f})", flush=True)
for _ in range(3): both_busy()
r = sorted(both_busy() for _ in range(9))
print(f"main chain on A + slice on B: B done after {r[4][0]*1e3:.3f} ms, both after {r[4][1]*1e3:.3f} ms "
      f"(A alone {ms_a:.3f}, A+B serial {ms_a + ms_b:.3f})", flush=True)


### scripts/par45.py
"""Parallel-answer fast mode at 45 GB (NewIdeas Mon 03:35, design only): price k independent streams of one answer x
16-row checks per pass on the all-resident non-exact 45 GB stack, from L1-32's measured expert unions (no runs).
Bytes per check = routed experts (NewIdeas' '! 350 at 45 GB' 1x16 figure 4.9 GB, scaled by L1-32's union ratio)
+ dense diet 2.1 GB read once per pass (shared by all rows) + each stream's GDN state read and written once (36 layers x
48 heads x 128 x 128 fp32 = 113 MB). Time = bytes / 280 GB/s + non-byte time: 'lean' 15 ms flat (the '! 350' row's
assumption) or 'rows' 15 -> 37.5 ms at 64 rows (GDN mixers, attention, drafter and glue grow with rows; L1-13: the 35B's
GDN mixers already cost 12.2 ms vs a 2.04 ms floor at 16 rows). Each stream accepts 9.5 tokens per check (L1-32's
code-like acceptance). Answer model: router 5 + skeleton 40 tokens serial, then k = 4 parts with lengths mean x
[0.75, 0.9, 1.1, 1.25]; streams retire as parts finish. usage: python par45.py  -> stdout"""
U8 = {1: 54.6, 2: 87.9, 3: 109.7, 4: 131.5}       # top-8 union per layer, L1-32 mean of 4 sets (3 streams interpolated)
U10 = {1: 66.1, 2: 105.4, 3: 130.5, 4: 155.5}     # top-10
E1, DENSE, GDN, BW, A = 4.9, 2.1, 0.113, 280.0, 9.5


def t_check(k, nb, U=U8):
    gb = E1 * U[k] / U[1] + DENSE + 2 * GDN * k
    return gb, gb / BW * 1e3 + nb(k)


NB = {"lean": lambda k: 15.0, "rows": lambda k: 15.0 + 22.5 * (k - 1) / 3}


def answer(parts, nb, serial=45):
    t = serial / A * t_check(1, nb)[1]
    left, done = sorted(parts), 0.0
    while left:
        k = len(left)
        step = left[0] - done                          # tokens until the shortest active part finishes
        t += step / A * t_check(k, nb)[1]
        done = left[0]
        left = [p for p in left if p > done]
    return t


for name, nb in NB.items():
    print(f"[{name} non-byte time]")
    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        gb, ms = t_check(k, nb)
        gb10, ms10 = t_check(k, nb, U10)
        print(f"  {k} stream(s) x 16 rows: {gb:5.2f} GB, {ms:5.1f} ms per check, {k * A:4.1f} tokens -> {1e3 * k * A / ms:4.0f} tok/s"
              f"   (top-10: {gb10:5.2f} GB, {1e3 * k * A / ms10:4.0f} tok/s)")
    one = 1e3 * A / t_check(1, nb)[1]
    for normal, longer in ((600, 1.0), (600, 1.3)):
        body = normal * longer - 45
        parts = [body / 4 * f for f in (0.75, 0.9, 1.1, 1.25)]
        t = answer(parts, nb)
        print(f"  SoT answer {normal * longer:.0f} tokens (normal {normal}, x{longer}): {t:5.0f} ms -> {normal * longer / t * 1e3:4.0f} "
              f"tok/s visible ({normal * longer / t * 1e3 / one:.2f}x); time to answer {normal / one * 1e3:5.0f} -> {t:5.0f} ms "
              f"({normal / one * 1e3 / t:.2f}x)")
    g = (600 * 1.3 / answer([(600 * 1.3 - 45) / 4 * f for f in (0.75, 0.9, 1.1, 1.25)], nb) * 1e3) / one
    for f in (0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9):
        print(f"  mix: {100 * f:3.0f} % of output tokens in parallel answers -> {one / ((1 - f) + f / g):4.0f} tok/s")
    print(f"  350 needs {100 * (1 - one / 350) / (1 - 1 / g):.0f} % of output tokens in parallel answers (gain {g:.2f}x on them)")


### scripts/pass_graph125.py
"""Round 27b: how many kernels does one 4-row 125B forward launch? Builds the graph of a 4-token forward (BigMoE's
streamed runtime, eager MTPLX as BigMoE runs it) without evaluating it, writes it with mx.export_to_dot and counts the
primitive nodes by type; per layer = total / 48. With ~3-7 us per dependent kernel (measured: 6.3 us/op at 1000 dependent
elementwise ops, 13.7 at 200), the count says how much of the ~1 ms per layer is dependency latency.
usage: pass_graph125.py [FRAC]   (GPU, BigMoE-size memory: quiet lock)"""
import collections, os, re, sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))

y = y[0] if isinstance(y, tuple) else y
path = os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/pass_graph125.dot")
mx.export_to_dot(path, y)

st.gate.check()

### scripts/pass_model125.py
"""First-principles time model of a 125B verify pass on the M5 Pro (64 GB, SSD 14.6 GB/s), calibrated to measurements,
to see which stack of levers could reach the goals (100-150 tok/s at 6-12 GB, 50-75 tok/s on a 16 GB / 3 GB/s mini).
Per pass (MTP depth 3: 4 rows, 3.1 committed tokens/pass on the trace prompts):
  T = compute + between + stall_visible
  compute   46 ms at 34 GB (pass_profile125: 48 % of 96 ms, ~130 GB/s); of it routed GLU ~20 ms (48 x 0.42 ms, stock M=4)
  between   17 ms (inter-pass: final mixer, lm_head, sampling, acceptance, MTP drafts, CPU syncs)
  stall     misses/pass x 0.30 ms (31-34 ms at 107 LRU misses/pass, frac 0.5); misses from the trace sim (LRU / OPT)
Calibration check: C=128 (17 GB) predicts 141 ms/pass (22 tok/s) vs 23.7 measured (depth 2); C=96 (12.7 GB) 168 ms
(18.5) vs 19.8 at 13 GB. Levers (multiplicative on their term):
  3c      routed GLU -25 %             (bench_tierglu, bitwise)          -> compute -5 ms
  t3      3-bit tiers: miss bytes x0.78 (MSB part 2.15 + LSB 0.77 for 2 of ~10 ranks vs 2.765 MB)
  lip     LIP-4 insertion: misses x0.96 (insert_policy125)
  ooo     out-of-order experts: hides min(stall/layer, 0.3 ms) per layer (ooo_bound125)
  opt     Belady-class caching: misses x OPT/LRU (opt_bound125; the unreachable floor, ~x0.6)
  eff     compute at the read ceiling + dependent-kernel floor: 17.7 ms bytes + 48 x 12 x 20 us = 29.2 ms
  thin    inter-pass work 17 -> 4 ms (everything on the GPU, no CPU syncs)
  hide    all SSD traffic overlapped: stall_visible = max(0, SSD time - compute - between)
usage: pass_model125.py   (CPU, instant)"""
MISS = {  # misses per pass, 4-bit experts, from data/opt_bound125.txt (LRU, OPT)
    4.2: (698.0, 611.7), 8.5: (483.2, 309.2), 12.7: (351.3, 202.4), 17.0: (261.2, 145.9), 25.5: (158.7, 91.6), 34.0: (107.2, 67.5)}
TOK = 3.1
SSD_MS_PER_MB = 1 / 14.6


def tps(gb, levers, tok=TOK, ssd_gbs=14.6, scale=1.0):
    """scale: expert-union growth vs 4 rows (misses and expert compute both scale with it)."""
    lru, opt = MISS[gb]
    miss = (opt if "opt" in levers else lru) * scale
    if "lip" in levers and "opt" not in levers:
        miss *= 0.96
    mb = 2.765 * (0.78 if "t3" in levers else 1.0)
    routed = 20.0 * scale * (0.75 if "3c" in levers else 1.0)          # routed GLU ms (stock 4-bit ~20 ms at 4 rows)
    # eff: bytes at the ~300 GB/s read ceiling + the dependent-kernel floor (matvec law: ~20 us per dependent big kernel,
    # ~12 per layer -> 11.5 ms/pass; round 29: cutting overlapped small kernels buys nothing)
    compute = (2500 + 2780 * scale) / 300 + 11.5 if "eff" in levers else 26.0 + routed
    between = 4.0 if "thin" in levers else 17.0
    stall_per_miss = 0.30 * (0.85 if "t3" in levers else 1.0)          # latency-dominated: bytes help less than x0.78
    stall = miss * stall_per_miss
    if "ooo" in levers:
        stall -= min(stall, 48 * 0.3 * min(1.0, stall / (48 * 0.65)))   # per layer min(stall_L, 0.3 ms), stall_L ~ p50
    if "hide" in levers:
        ssd = miss * mb / ssd_gbs                                        # ms (MB / GB/s)
        stall = max(0.0, ssd - compute - between)
    T = compute + between + stall
    return 1000 * tok / T, T


STACKS = [("today", set()), ("+3c+t3", {"3c", "t3"}), ("+3c+t3+ooo+lip", {"3c", "t3", "ooo", "lip"}),
          ("+...+eff+thin", {"3c", "t3", "ooo", "lip", "eff", "thin"}),
          ("+...+eff+thin+hide", {"3c", "t3", "lip", "eff", "thin", "hide"}),
          ("+...+eff+thin+hide+opt", {"3c", "t3", "eff", "thin", "hide", "opt"})]
print("tok/s (ms/pass) at 3.1 tokens/pass, M5 Pro SSD 14.6 GB/s; cache budget = experts only (+~2.5 GB dense +KV)")
print("stack".ljust(28) + "".join(f"{g:>14.1f} GB" for g in MISS))
for name, lv in STACKS:
    print(name.ljust(28) + "".join(f"{tps(g, lv)[0]:9.1f} ({tps(g, lv)[1]:5.0f})" for g in MISS))
print("\n16 GB Mac mini (SSD 3 GB/s), full stack incl. hide (+opt in the 2nd row):")
for lv in ({"3c", "t3", "lip", "eff", "thin", "hide"}, {"3c", "t3", "eff", "thin", "hide", "opt"}):
    print("   " + "".join(f"{g:>5.1f} GB: {tps(g, lv, ssd_gbs=3.0)[0]:5.1f} tok/s   " for g in (4.2, 8.5)))
print("\nTokens/pass needed for 100 tok/s with the full stack + opt at 12.7 GB and 34 GB (union grows ~k^0.67, so bytes/pass x(k/4)^0.67):")
for tok in (3.1, 4, 6, 8, 12):
    k = tok * 4 / 3.1
    sc = (k / 4) ** 0.67
    print(f"   {tok:4.1f} tok/pass (~{k:4.1f} rows): {tps(12.7, {'3c', 't3', 'eff', 'thin', 'hide', 'opt'}, tok=tok, scale=sc)[0]:6.1f} tok/s"
          f" | 34 GB {tps(34.0, {'3c', 't3', 'eff', 'thin', 'hide', 'opt'}, tok=tok, scale=sc)[0]:6.1f}")


### scripts/pass_profile125.py
"""Round 26b: where a 125B verify pass spends its time (orchestrator 20:12: per component ms, GB/s, % of the 310-326 GB/s
read ceiling, CPU-GPU sync points, and why). BigMoE's spin gate leaves no command-buffer boundary at the gates; with
BIGMOE_TRACE=1 its loader logs, per (layer L, pass), when it saw the GPU's routing row (t_seen: the GPU reached L's gate)
and when it released the slots (t_rel). On that clock (CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW, shared with Python's) a pass decomposes into:
  stall_L     t_rel[L] - t_seen[L]           loader read of L's misses (the GPU spins)
  between_L   t_seen[L+1] - t_rel[L]         L's MoE (routed + shared + HC write) and L+1's attention + HC read + router
  inter-pass  t_seen[0] of the next pass - t_rel[47]: layer 47's MoE, final mixer + lm_head, sampling and acceptance
              (CPU syncs), MTP history append, the draft steps, layer 0's attention
Bytes per interval come from the modules' resident weights (attention side of L+1, MoE side of L) plus L's unique
routed experts (2.765 MB each, counted by a route_and_gate hook) and the GDN recurrent state (fp32, read + write).
Python-side timestamps of the MTP draft steps and token callbacks are taken on the same clock.
usage: pass_profile125.py [FRAC] [MAXTOK]   (GPU, BigMoE-size memory: exclusive window)"""
import json, os, sys, time
os.environ["BIGMOE_TRACE"] = "1"
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx.utils import tree_flatten
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe import fuse
from bigmoe.gated import ext
frac = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.5
MAXTOK = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 160
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, round(frac * 512))
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx import generation as G
TM = rt.model.language_model
LAYERS = TM.model.layers
NL = len(LAYERS)
EXPERT_MB = 2.765


def clk():
    return time.clock_gettime(time.CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW)


# resident bytes per layer, split into the attention side and the MoE side (routed pools excluded)
def nbytes(mod):
    return sum(a.nbytes for _, a in tree_flatten(mod.parameters()))


att_b, moe_b, kinds = [], [], []
for L, layer in enumerate(LAYERS):
    names = dict(layer.children()) if hasattr(layer, "children") else {}
    a = m = 0
    for n, mod in names.items():
        if not hasattr(mod, "parameters"):
            continue
        b = nbytes(mod)
        if n == "mlp":
            b -= sum(a_.nbytes for k_, a_ in tree_flatten(mod.switch_mlp.parameters()))
            m += b
        elif n in ("mlp_hyper_connection",):
            m += b
        else:
            a += b
    att_b.append(a); moe_b.append(m)
    kinds.append("gdn" if getattr(layer, "is_linear", False) else "qsa")
print("resident bytes per layer (MB): attention side gdn %.1f / qsa %.1f, MoE side %.1f" % (
    np.mean([a for a, k in zip(att_b, kinds) if k == "gdn"]) / 1e6, np.mean([a for a, k in zip(att_b, kinds) if k == "qsa"]) / 1e6,
    np.mean(moe_b) / 1e6), flush=True)
print("non-layer resident MB: " + ", ".join(f"{n} {nbytes(mod) / 1e6:.0f}" for n, mod in dict(TM.model.children()).items()
                                            if n != "layers" and hasattr(mod, "parameters"))
      + f", lm_head {nbytes(TM.lm_head) / 1e6:.0f}" if hasattr(TM, "lm_head") else "", flush=True)
# GDN recurrent state per layer (fp32) from the args
A = TM.args
gdn_state = getattr(A, "linear_num_value_heads", 0) * getattr(A, "linear_key_head_dim", 0) * getattr(A, "linear_value_head_dim", 0) * 4
print(f"GDN recurrent state per layer: {gdn_state / 1e6:.2f} MB (fp32)", flush=True)

# hooks: unique routed experts per (pass, layer); MTP draft steps; token callbacks
gate = st.gate
UNI = {}
_orig = gate.route_and_gate


def hook(L, ids, seq=None, preds=None, counts=None):
    UNI[(int(gate.seq if seq is None else seq), int(L))] = ids
    return _orig(L, ids, seq=seq, preds=preds, counts=counts)


gate.route_and_gate = hook
DRAFT = []
_fr = TM.mtp.fuse_and_run


DRAFT_SYNC = os.environ.get("DRAFT_SYNC") == "1"           # round 32f: time each draft step's GPU work (serializes)
DRAFT_DONE = []


def fr_hook(widened, tok_emb, cache):
    t0 = clk()
    h = _fr(widened, tok_emb, cache)
    DRAFT.append((int(gate.seq), t0))
    if DRAFT_SYNC:
        mx.eval(h)
        DRAFT_DONE.append((int(gate.seq), t0, clk()))
    return h


object.__setattr__(TM.mtp, "fuse_and_run", fr_hook)
VERIFY_TIMES = os.environ.get("VERIFY_TIMES") == "1"       # round 32n: Python-side time to build each trunk forward graph
VT = []
if VERIFY_TIMES:
    _tm_cls = type(TM)
    _tm_call = _tm_cls.__call__

    def _tm_hook(self, *a, **k):
        t0 = clk()
        out = _tm_call(self, *a, **k)
        VT.append((int(gate.seq), t0, clk(), int(a[0].shape[-1]) if a and hasattr(a[0], "shape") else -1))
        return out

    _tm_cls.__call__ = _tm_hook
lines = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))]
PROMPTS = {"code": open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
           "math": "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + lines[0]["question"]}
smp = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)
EV, CB = [], []
for name, text in PROMPTS.items():
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
    fuse.reset_generation_state(rt.model)
    first = []

    def cb(toks):
        if not first:
            ext.trace(); ext.reset_stats(); UNI.clear(); DRAFT.clear(); DRAFT_DONE.clear(); VT.clear(); first.append(clk())
        CB.append((name, int(gate.seq), clk(), len(toks) if isinstance(toks, list) else 1))
    res = G.generate_mtpk(rt, ids, max_tokens=MAXTOK, sampler=smp, speculative_depth=3, seed=0,
                          mtp_history_policy="committed", token_callback=cb)
    ev = [e for e in ext.trace() if int(e[2]) == 1]
    uni = {k: int(len(set(np.array(v).reshape(-1).tolist()))) for k, v in UNI.items()}

                   tokens=len(res.tokens), verify_calls=res.stats.verify_calls))
    print(f"{name}: {len(ev)} loader events, {len(uni)} gated (pass, layer) calls, {len(DRAFT)} draft steps", flush=True)
st.gate.check()

          open(os.path.expanduser(os.environ.get("PROFILE_OUT", "~/MoonShots/data/pass_profile125.json")), "w"))

# ---- analysis
rows = []
for run in EV:
    by = {}
    for L, seq, kind, ts, tr in run["events"]:
        by.setdefault(int(seq), {})[int(L)] = (ts, tr)
    seqs = sorted(s for s in by if len(by[s]) == NL)
    for a, b in zip(seqs, seqs[1:]):
        if b != a + 1:
            continue
        p, q = by[a], by[b]
        stall = sum(p[L][1] - p[L][0] for L in range(NL))
        betw = {L: p[L + 1][0] - p[L][1] for L in range(NL - 1)}

      f"{tot_inter:.1f} ms ({tot_inter / tot:.0%})")

### scripts/path80_125.py
"""Main-track arithmetic: what does 80 tok/s exact at the 24 GB setting (C=128) need? Measured today (true reference,
24 GB mode): pass ~110 ms = loader stalls ~70 ms + compute/other ~40 ms; LRU misses ~261/pass (opt_bound125, 4 rows) x
2.93 MB (records3 record, exact mode) = 765 MB/pass -> the loader delivers ~11 GB/s effective. 3.1 tokens/pass.
Scenarios: SSD bytes per pass (relative), overlap (none = stalls add; full = max(SSD, compute)), compute ms.
usage: path80_125.py   (instant)"""
TOK, COMP, BW, MB0 = 3.1, 40.0, 11.0, 765.0
S = [("today (exact 4-bit records3, no overlap)", 1.00, False, COMP),
     ("3-bit tiers 4433333333 (bytes -22 %)", 0.78, False, COMP),
     ("+ out-of-order experts (-12.5 ms)", 0.78, False, COMP - 12.5),
     ("3-bit tiers + full loader/compute overlap", 0.78, True, COMP),
     ("+ caching at the online limit (-6 % misses)", 0.78 * 0.94, True, COMP),
     ("2-bit-heavy tiers 4222222222 on records4 (-45 %) + full overlap", 0.55, True, COMP),
     ("+ compute at the chain/byte floor (~29 ms)", 0.55, True, 29.0),
     ("Belady-class caching (x0.6) + 3-bit tiers + full overlap + 29 ms compute", 0.78 * 0.6, True, 29.0)]
for name, rel, ov, comp in S:
    ssd = MB0 * rel / BW
    T = max(ssd, comp) if ov else ssd * (70.0 / (MB0 / BW)) + comp      # no overlap: stalls scale with bytes from today's 70 ms
    print(f"{name:72s} SSD {ssd:5.1f} ms  T {T:6.1f} ms  {1000 * TOK / T:5.1f} tok/s")


### scripts/planes3_sim.py
"""Round 19 PoC A (CPU): three nested bit-planes per expert as separate cache units, on the real 125B traces.
Units: P2 = MSB-2 plane (2-bit codes + scale + bias, 2.5 bits/weight = 1.54 MB), B3 = third bit (1 bit + uint8 offset per
group, 0.69 MB), B4 = last bit (0.69 MB); whole 4-bit expert = 2.77 MB. A plan gives the bits each gate rank needs, e.g.
4433322222 = ranks 0-1 exact (P2+B3+B4), 2-4 MSB-3 (P2+B3), 5-9 MSB-2 (P2). Verify windows of 4 tokens, stride 3; per layer
an LRU over units within a byte budget; demand MB/pass = bytes of units missing when a window starts (no prefetch, no
fallback: deterministic). Quality of plans: scripts/expert_tiers35b.py (35B).   usage: planes3_sim.py"""
import glob, os
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
E, K, WARM = 512, 10, 200
SIZE = {"W": 2.77, "P2": 1.54, "B3": 0.69, "B4": 0.69}
NEED = {4: ("P2", "B3", "B4"), 3: ("P2", "B3"), 2: ("P2",)}
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))
TR = []
for f in files:
    z = np.load(f)
    TR.append([z[f"L{L}"][:, :K].astype(np.int64) for L in range(len(z.files))])
NL = len(TR[0])


def run(plan, budget_mb):
    dem = miss = P = 0
    for tr in TR:
        T = tr[0].shape[0]
        starts = list(range(0, T - 4, 3))
        P += sum(1 for s in starts if s >= WARM)
        for L in range(NL):
            cache, used = OrderedDict(), 0.0
            for s in starts:
                w = tr[L][s:s + 4]
                need = set()
                for r in range(K):
                    for e in np.unique(w[:, r]):
                        if plan == "whole":
                            need.add((int(e), "W"))
                        else:
                            for u in NEED[int(plan[r])]:
                                need.add((int(e), u))
                late = [u for u in sorted(need) if u not in cache]
                for u in sorted(need):
                    if u in cache:
                        cache.move_to_end(u)
                for u in late:
                    if s >= WARM:
                        dem += SIZE[u[1]]; miss += 1
                    cache[u] = SIZE[u[1]]; used += SIZE[u[1]]
                    while used > budget_mb and cache:
                        _, sz = cache.popitem(last=False); used -= sz
    return dem / P, miss / P


print(f"125B traces: {len(TR)} prompts, {NL} layers, verify windows of 4 (stride 3); demand MB/pass (all layers)")
BYTES = {4: 4.75, 3: 3.625, 2: 2.5}
for gb in (20, 24, 28, 34):
    b = gb * 1024 / NL
    d, m = run("whole", b)
    print(f"budget {gb} GB: whole-expert LRU {d:6.1f} MB/pass ({m:5.1f} misses)", flush=True)
    for plan in ("4444222222", "4433322222", "4433333333", "4443322222", "4333322222", "4433222222"):
        d, m = run(plan, b)
        rel = sum(BYTES[int(c)] for c in plan) / (4.5 * K)
        print(f"   plan {plan} (read {100 * (rel - 1):+.0f} % bytes per use set): demand {d:6.1f} MB/pass ({m:5.1f} unit misses)",
              flush=True)


### scripts/planes4_sim.py
"""Is the 2+1+1 record format (records4: P2 + B3 + B4 parts) worth it over records3 ([MSB-3 | LSB-1]) for plan 4433332222?
Same model as planes3_sim.py (MoEPrefetch's 125B traces *_800.npz, verify windows of 4 tokens with stride 3, warm-up 200
tokens, per layer one LRU over cache units within a byte budget, demand MB/pass = bytes of the units missing when a window
starts; deterministic, no prefetch), with the unit sizes of the concrete formats:
  whole     4-bit expert 2.765 MB (records/)
  records3  M3 = MSB-3 part 2.150 MB (w3 + s3 + b3), L1 = LSB part 0.768 MB (l1 + b)
  records4  P2 = 1.536 MB (2-bit codes + s2 + b2), B3 = 0.768 MB (bit + b3), B4 = 0.768 MB (bit + b)
usage: planes4_sim.py   (CPU, 1 core, a few minutes)"""
import glob, os
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np

E, K, WARM = 512, 10, 200
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))
TR = []
for f in files:
    z = np.load(f)
    TR.append([z[f"L{L}"][:, :K].astype(np.int64) for L in range(len(z.files))])
NL = len(TR[0])
FMT = {
    "whole": ({"W": 2.765}, {4: ("W",), 3: ("W",), 2: ("W",)}),
    "records3": ({"M3": 2.150, "L1": 0.768}, {4: ("M3", "L1"), 3: ("M3",), 2: ("M3",)}),
    "records4": ({"P2": 1.536, "B3": 0.768, "B4": 0.768}, {4: ("P2", "B3", "B4"), 3: ("P2", "B3"), 2: ("P2",)}),
}


def run(fmt, plan, budget_mb):
    size, need_of = FMT[fmt]
    dem = miss = P = 0
    for tr in TR:
        T = tr[0].shape[0]
        starts = list(range(0, T - 4, 3))
        P += sum(1 for s in starts if s >= WARM)
        for L in range(NL):
            cache, used = OrderedDict(), 0.0
            for s in starts:
                w = tr[L][s:s + 4]
                need = set()
                for r in range(K):
                    for e in np.unique(w[:, r]):
                        for u in need_of[int(plan[r])]:
                            need.add((int(e), u))
                for u in sorted(need):
                    if u in cache:
                        cache.move_to_end(u)
                for u in sorted(need):
                    if u not in cache:
                        if s >= WARM:
                            dem += size[u[1]]; miss += 1
                        cache[u] = size[u[1]]; used += size[u[1]]
                        while used > budget_mb and cache:
                            _, sz = cache.popitem(last=False); used -= sz
    return dem / P, miss / P


import sys
EXTRA = len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == "extra"
MENU = sys.argv[2].split(",") if len(sys.argv) > 2 and sys.argv[1] == "menu" else None
print(f"125B traces: {len(TR)} prompts x {TR[0][0].shape[0]} tokens, {NL} layers; demand MB/pass (misses/pass)", flush=True)
for gb in (10, 13, 17, 24):
    b = gb * 1024 / NL
    if MENU:
        cells = [("records3 4433333333", run("records3", "4433333333", b))] + [(f"records4 {pl}", run("records4", pl, b)) for pl in MENU]
        print(f"expert budget {gb:2d} GB: " + " | ".join(f"{n} {d:6.1f} ({m:5.1f})" for n, (d, m) in cells), flush=True)
        continue
    cells = [("whole 4444444444", run("whole", "4444444444", b)),
             ("records3 4433333333", run("records3", "4433333333", b)),
             ("records3 4433332222", run("records3", "4433332222", b)),
             ("records4 4433333333", run("records4", "4433333333", b)),
             ("records4 4433332222", run("records4", "4433332222", b)),
             ("records4 4444222222", run("records4", "4444222222", b))] if not EXTRA else \
            [("records4 4422222222", run("records4", "4422222222", b)), ("records4 4222222222", run("records4", "4222222222", b)),
             ("records4 2222222222", run("records4", "2222222222", b))]
    print(f"expert budget {gb:2d} GB: " + " | ".join(f"{n} {d:6.1f} ({m:5.1f})" for n, (d, m) in cells), flush=True)


### scripts/planes_cache_sim.py
"""Plane-granular expert caching on the real 125B traces (round 18; CPU only). An expert's 4-bit codes split into an MSB
plane (2 bits + scale + both biases, 2.625 bits/weight, 1.61 MB) and an LSB plane (2 bits/weight, 1.23 MB); whole 4-bit
expert 2.77 MB. Verify windows of 4 tokens, stride 3 (as admission_sim / stall_alloc_sim); traces are ranked router top-20
(MoEPrefetch traces125, from waittop125 trace mode). Per layer, an LRU over cache units within a byte budget.
  whole      : the exact model, LRU of whole experts; every use needs all of it (BigMoE today)
  planes-R   : mix-R (GSM8K-equal on the 35B at R=4 of 8): uses at gate rank < R need MSB + LSB, the others MSB only;
               MSB and LSB planes are separate LRU units, so cold LSB planes leave first (a compression cache, Douglis 1993)
  pinMSB-R   : every MSB plane pinned (when they fit), LSB planes LRU in the remaining bytes
  fallback-R : planes-R, but a missing LSB is never waited for (game-engine mip fallback): that use runs MSB-only and the
               LSB streams in the background; demand bytes = MSB misses only; cost = the fallback rate of top-R uses
Reports demand (stall) MB/pass, background MB/pass, misses/pass and the fallback rate.   usage: planes_cache_sim.py"""
import glob, os
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
E, K, WARM, NL = 512, 10, 200, 48
WHOLE, MSB, LSB = 2.77, 1.61, 1.23                       # MB per unit
LSBFIRST = False                                         # SliceMoE-style: a fetched LSB plane goes to the LRU end
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))
TR = []
for f in files:
    z = np.load(f)
    TR.append([z[f"L{L}"][:, :K].astype(np.int64) for L in range(len(z.files))])
NL = len(TR[0])


def windows(tr):
    T = tr.shape[0]
    for s in range(0, T - 4, 3):
        yield s, tr[s:s + 4]                              # [4 tokens, K ranks]


def run(policy, budget_mb, R):
    dem = bg = miss = fb = top = P = 0
    for tr_layers in TR:
        for L in range(NL):
            cache, used = OrderedDict(), 0.0
            pin = policy == "pinMSB" and E * MSB <= budget_mb
            cap = budget_mb - (E * MSB if pin else 0.0)
            for s, w in windows(tr_layers[L]):
                if policy == "whole":
                    need = {(int(e), "W") for e in np.unique(w)}
                elif policy == "msbfull":               # MSB plane files + the original 4-bit files (no LSB files):
                    topw = {int(e) for e in np.unique(w[:, :R])}         # top-rank uses need the whole expert
                    need = {(e, "W") for e in topw}
                    for e in {int(e) for e in np.unique(w)} - topw:      # low-rank uses take the whole one if resident
                        need.add((e, "W") if (e, "W") in cache else (e, "M"))
                    for e in topw:                                         # a whole expert makes its MSB unit redundant
                        if (e, "M") in cache:
                            used -= cache.pop((e, "M"))
                else:
                    need = {(int(e), "M") for e in np.unique(w)} if not pin else set()
                    need |= {(int(e), "L") for e in np.unique(w[:, :R])}
                warm = s >= WARM
                late = []
                for u in sorted(need):
                    if u in cache:
                        cache.move_to_end(u)
                    else:
                        late.append(u)
                for u in late:
                    size = WHOLE if u[1] == "W" else MSB if u[1] == "M" else LSB
                    if LSBFIRST and u[1] == "L":           # SliceMoE: LSB slices lowest priority -> insert at the LRU end
                        if warm:
                            miss += 1
                            if policy == "fallback":
                                bg += size; fb += int((w[:, :R] == u[0]).sum())
                            else:
                                dem += size
                        cache[u] = size; cache.move_to_end(u, last=False); used += size
                        while used > cap and cache:
                            _, sz = cache.popitem(last=False); used -= sz
                        continue
                    if warm:
                        miss += 1
                        if policy == "fallback" and u[1] == "L":
                            bg += size
                            fb += int((w[:, :R] == u[0]).sum())    # top-R uses of this expert in the window fall back
                        else:
                            dem += size
                    cache[u] = size; used += size
                    while used > cap and cache:
                        _, sz = cache.popitem(last=False); used -= sz
                if warm:
                    top += w[:, :R].size if policy != "whole" else 0
            P += sum(1 for s, _ in windows(tr_layers[0]) if s >= WARM) if L == 0 else 0
    return dem / P, bg / P, miss / P, (fb / top if top else 0.0)


print(f"125B traces: {len(TR)} prompts x 800 tokens, {NL} layers, verify windows of 4 (stride 3); MB per pass, all layers")
import sys
if sys.argv[1:] == ["small"]:                    # Kamen's target: today's speed in 24 GB total -> ~17 GB expert cache
    for gb in (17, 20, 34):
        b = gb * 1024 / NL
        d, _, m, _ = run("whole", b, K)
        print(f"budget {gb} GB: whole-expert LRU: demand {d:6.1f} MB/pass ({m:5.1f} misses)", flush=True)
        for lf in (False, True):
            LSBFIRST = lf
            for pol in ("planes", "fallback"):
                d, g, m, f = run(pol, b, 4)
                print(f"   {pol:8s} R=4{' LSB-first' if lf else '          '}: demand {d:6.1f} MB/pass, background {g:6.1f}"
                      + (f", fallback {100 * f:.1f} % of top-4 uses" if pol == "fallback" else ""), flush=True)
        LSBFIRST = False
    sys.exit()
if sys.argv[1:] == ["msbfull"]:                  # disk-light variant: only MSB plane files are written (~40 GB for the 125B)
    for gb in (24, 34, 45):
        b = gb * 1024 / NL
        d, _, m, _ = run("whole", b, K)
        print(f"budget {gb} GB: whole-expert LRU: demand {d:6.1f} MB/pass, misses {m:5.1f}/pass", flush=True)
        for R in (4, 5):
            d, _, m, _ = run("msbfull", b, R)
            print(f"   msbfull R={R}: demand {d:6.1f} MB/pass, unit misses {m:5.1f}/pass", flush=True)
    sys.exit()
for gb in (24, 34, 45):
    b = gb * 1024 / NL
    d, _, m, _ = run("whole", b, K)
    print(f"budget {gb} GB ({gb / 68 * 100:.0f} % of 4-bit experts): whole-expert LRU: demand {d:6.1f} MB/pass, "
          f"misses {m:5.1f}/pass", flush=True)
    for R in (4, 5):
        for pol in ("planes", "pinMSB", "fallback"):
            d, g, m, f = run(pol, b, R)
            print(f"   {pol:8s} R={R}: demand {d:6.1f} MB/pass, background {g:6.1f}, unit misses {m:5.1f}/pass"
                  + (f", fallback {100 * f:.1f} % of top-{R} uses" if pol == "fallback" else ""), flush=True)


### scripts/pmatch_policy.py
"""Round 32h: can context matching supply the long-horizon foresight Belady needs? (exact: a cache policy only)
Per layer, before evicting at window t: find the earlier window t' (t' < t - 1) whose expert set overlaps the current
one most (ties -> most recent), predict F = union of the expert sets of windows t'+1 .. t'+W (what followed the similar
context last time), and evict first the cached experts NOT in F (LRU among them), then LRU among the rest.
Variants: match on the layer's own set ("own"), or on the summed overlap over all layers ("all", one match per window).
Trace sets: data/verify_trace125.npz (401 committed verify passes) and MoEPrefetch's traces125 *_800.npz (windows of 4,
stride 3). MB/pass of 4-bit experts vs LRU and OPT (insert_policy800.py / opt_bound125.py numbers).
usage: pmatch_policy.py   (CPU, 1 core; taskpolicy -b)"""
import glob, os
import numpy as np

E, MB = 512, 2.765


def load_sets():
    out = {}
    z = np.load("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/verify_trace125.npz")
    ids = z["ids"].astype(np.int64)
    out["verify401"] = [[np.unique(ids[p, l]) for p in range(ids.shape[0])] for l in range(ids.shape[1])]
    per = None
    for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz"))):
        zz = np.load(f)
        tr = [zz[f"L{L}"][:, :10].astype(np.int64) for L in range(len(zz.files))]
        per = per or [[] for _ in tr]
        for L in range(len(tr)):
            per[L] += [np.unique(tr[L][s:s + 4]) for s in range(0, tr[0].shape[0] - 4, 3)]
    out["traces800"] = per
    return out


def run(seqs, C, W, mode):
    L, T = len(seqs), len(seqs[0])
    Mx = np.zeros((L, T, E), np.float32)
    for l in range(L):
        for t, s in enumerate(seqs[l]):
            Mx[l, t, s] = 1
    best_all = {}
    if mode == "all" and W > 0:                          # one match per window over all layers (same for every layer)
        G = Mx.transpose(1, 0, 2).reshape(T, L * E)
        for t in range(3, T):
            ov = G[:t - 1] @ G[t]
            best_all[t] = int(len(ov) - 1 - np.argmax(ov[::-1]))
    miss = 0
    for l in range(L):
        cache = {}                                       # e -> last use
        for t in range(T):
            need = seqs[l][t]
            for e in need.tolist():
                miss += e not in cache
                cache[e] = t
            if len(cache) <= C:
                continue
            F = set()
            if t >= 3 and W > 0:
                if mode == "own":
                    ov = Mx[l, :t - 1] @ Mx[l, t]
                    tp = int(len(ov) - 1 - np.argmax(ov[::-1]))  # most overlapping, most recent on ties
                else:
                    tp = best_all[t]
                for j in range(tp + 1, min(t, tp + 1 + W)):          # only windows already seen (no lookahead)
                    F.update(seqs[l][j].tolist())
