            nd = set(need.tolist())
            cand = sorted((e for e in cache if e not in nd), key=lambda e: (e in F, cache[e]))
            for e in cand[:len(cache) - C]:
                del cache[e]
    return miss / T * MB


for name, seqs in load_sets().items():
    print(f"{name}: {len(seqs[0])} windows x {len(seqs)} layers", flush=True)
    for C in (64, 96, 128):
        row = [f"LRU {run(seqs, C, 0, 'own'):6.1f}"]
        for mode in ("own", "all"):
            for W in (8, 16, 32):
                row.append(f"{mode}-W{W} {run(seqs, C, W, mode):6.1f}")
        print(f"  C={C:3d} ({48 * C * MB / 1e3:4.1f} GB) MB/pass: " + " | ".join(row), flush=True)


### scripts/publish.py
"""Can a running Metal kernel publish a value to a spinning CPU thread mid-kernel, fast, if the GPU keeps streaming memory
(as a real layer does with its weights)? BigMoE found GPU writes reach the CPU only at command-buffer end or after a random
0.5–1000 ms *while the kernel only spins*, and that a 64 MB one-thread sweep published it (but slowly).
Here: thread 0 of threadgroup 0 stores req[0]=1 at kernel start, then ALL threadgroups stream X MB of a big buffer
(coalesced, like a matvec's weight reads) or, in the 'idle' variant, thread 0 just spins ~2 ms with no traffic.
The CPU spins on req[0] (zero-copy numpy view of the MLX buffer) and records when it sees it vs when the kernel completes.
publish delay ≈ seen(X) − seen(X=0). Fast mid-kernel publish ⇒ a persistent per-token kernel can hand routing ids to the
CPU loader without splitting command buffers. Small GPU job; needs a quiet lock."""
import time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

BIG = mx.random.randint(0, 2**31, ((512 << 20) // 4,)).astype(mx.uint32)   # 512 MB, random (no zero-page shortcut)
mx.eval(BIG)
G = 160                                                                       # threadgroups x 256 threads

SRC = """
    uint tg = threadgroup_position_in_grid.x, t = thread_position_in_threadgroup.x;
    device int* r = const_cast<device int*>(req);
    if (tg == 0 && t == 0) {
        *(volatile device int*)r = 1;
    }
    if (EXIT && tg == 0) return;                               // publisher threadgroup: store, then finish
    if (PUB && tg == 1) {                                      // late publisher: wait for a GPU-side phase flag, store, exit
        if (t == 0) {
            device atomic_int* ph = (device atomic_int*)(r + 4096);
            uint s = 0;
            while (atomic_load_explicit(ph, memory_order_relaxed) == 0 && s < 40000000u) s++;
            *(volatile device int*)(r + 8) = 1;
        }
        return;
    }
    uint acc = 0;
    if (IDLE) {
        if (tg == 0 && t == 0) { for (uint i = 0; i < 3000000u; ++i) acc += i * 2654435761u; }
    } else {
        const uint per = (uint)(XW / G);                       // words per threadgroup
        const device uint* p = big + (size_t)tg * per;
        for (uint i = t; i < per; i += 256) {
            acc ^= p[i];
            if (PUB && tg == 2 && i == t + 256u * 64u && t == 0)   // a worker raises the phase flag early in its stream
                atomic_store_explicit((device atomic_int*)(r + 4096), 1, memory_order_relaxed);
        }
    }
    out[tg * 256 + t] = acc;
"""
k = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="publish", input_names=["req", "big"], output_names=["out"], source=SRC)


def run(xmb, idle=0, reps=7, exit_=0, pub=0):
    req = mx.zeros((16384,), mx.int32); mx.eval(req)
    Q = np.frombuffer(memoryview(req), np.int32)
    seen_l, done_l = [], []
    for _ in range(reps):
        Q[:] = 0
        o = k(inputs=[req, BIG], template=[("XW", (xmb << 20) // 4), ("G", G), ("IDLE", idle), ("EXIT", exit_), ("PUB", pub)],
              grid=(G * 256, 1, 1), threadgroup=(256, 1, 1), output_shapes=[(G * 256,)], output_dtypes=[mx.uint32])[0]
        t0 = time.perf_counter(); mx.async_eval(o); seen = None
        while time.perf_counter() - t0 < 2.0:
            if (Q[8] if pub else Q[0]):
                seen = time.perf_counter() - t0
                break
        mx.eval(o); done = time.perf_counter() - t0
        seen_l.append(seen if seen is not None else float("nan")); done_l.append(done)
    s = np.nanmedian(seen_l) * 1e3; d = np.median(done_l) * 1e3
    return s, d, sum(np.isnan(seen_l))


run(16)                                                      # warm-up / compile
print(f"{G} threadgroups x 256 threads; CPU-seen time of a store made at kernel start vs kernel completion (ms, medians)")
base = None
for xmb in (0, 1, 4, 16, 64, 256, 512):
    s, d, miss = run(xmb)
    base = s if base is None else base
    print(f"stream {xmb:>3} MB after the store: seen {s:7.3f}  done {d:7.3f}  -> publish delay ~{(s-base)*1e3:7.1f} µs "
          f"(store seen {d - s:6.3f} ms before kernel end){'  [not seen before end: ' + str(miss) + ']' if miss else ''}")
s, d, miss = run(0, idle=1)
print(f"idle spin (no memory traffic)  : seen {s:7.3f}  done {d:7.3f}  (store seen {d - s:6.3f} ms before kernel end)")

print("-- publisher threadgroups (does a finished threadgroup flush its stores?)")
for xmb in (64, 256, 512):
    s, d, miss = run(xmb, exit_=1)
    print(f"TG0 stores then exits, others stream {xmb:>3} MB: seen {s:7.3f}  done {d:7.3f}  (seen {d - s:6.3f} ms before end)")
for xmb in (64, 256, 512):
    s, d, miss = run(xmb, pub=1)
    print(f"TG1 waits for a GPU phase flag, stores, exits; stream {xmb:>3} MB: seen {s:7.3f}  done {d:7.3f}  (seen {d - s:6.3f} ms before end){'  MISS ' + str(miss) if miss else ''}")


### scripts/qmm3_bench.py
"""Round 25 check before a 3-bit tier prototype: MLX gather_qmm speed at 3 bits vs 4 bits for the 125B's routed-expert
shapes (gate/up [640, 2560], down [2560, 640], group 64) over a 128-slot pool, 1-4 rows x top-10 (decode and MTP verify),
and the plan-4433333333 split (top-2 of each row at 4 bits, 8 at 3 bits: two calls per projection) vs all 4-bit.
GPU-light (~1 GB), ~1 min; timing: quiet lock.   usage: qmm3_bench.py"""
import time
import mlx.core as mx
SLOTS, H, I, G = 128, 2560, 640, 64
mx.random.seed(0)


def qpool(bits, n_out, n_in):
    w = mx.random.normal((SLOTS, n_out, n_in)).astype(mx.bfloat16) * 0.02
    q = mx.quantize(w, group_size=G, bits=bits)
    mx.eval(q)
    return q


P = {b: {"gate": qpool(b, I, H), "up": qpool(b, I, H), "down": qpool(b, H, I)} for b in (3, 4)}


def glu(bits, x, idx):
    """x [rows, 1, H] (one token per row), idx [rows, k] slot ids -> [rows, k, H]"""
    p = P[bits]
    xg = mx.expand_dims(x, -2)                                           # [rows, 1, 1, H]
    g = mx.gather_qmm(xg, *p["gate"], rhs_indices=idx, transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=bits)
    u = mx.gather_qmm(xg, *p["up"], rhs_indices=idx, transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=bits)
    a = (g * mx.sigmoid(g)) * u                                          # [rows, k, 1, I]
    return mx.gather_qmm(a, *p["down"], rhs_indices=idx, transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=bits)


def bench(fn, iters=200):
    for _ in range(10):
        mx.eval(fn())
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(iters):
        mx.eval(fn())
    return (time.perf_counter() - t0) / iters * 1e3


print("ms per layer's routed-expert GLU (gate, up, down), 128-slot pools, top-10 per row:")
for rows in (1, 2, 4):
    x = mx.random.normal((rows, 1, H)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    idx = mx.stack([mx.random.permutation(SLOTS)[:10] for _ in range(rows)]).astype(mx.uint32)
    mx.eval(x, idx)
    t4 = bench(lambda: glu(4, x, idx))
    t3 = bench(lambda: glu(3, x, idx))
    t_split = bench(lambda: (glu(4, x, idx[:, :2]), glu(3, x, idx[:, 2:])))
    mb4 = rows * 10 * 2.77
    print(f"rows {rows}: 4-bit {t4:.3f} ms ({mb4 / t4:.0f} GB/s of expert bytes), 3-bit {t3:.3f} ms, "
          f"plan split (2 @ 4-bit + 8 @ 3-bit) {t_split:.3f} ms ({t_split / t4 - 1:+.0%} vs 4-bit)", flush=True)


### scripts/refit35.py
"""Model-level test of the activation-aware refit of truncated expert tiers (IDEAS 33c) on the 35B proxy (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B,
lmstudio 4-bit g64, 40 layers, 256 experts, top-8). NOT bit-exact; quality study.
1. Calibration: the 4-bit reference runs on texts disjoint from the eval (gsm8k test 0-399 Q+A, scripts/*.py, MoEPrefetch +
   SpecDecode findings); every layer's MoE input x and score-sorted top-8 ids are kept.
2. Refit, per layer / expert / projection / tier n in {3, 2}: codes stay m = q >> (4 - n) (nested planes); the 2 G group
   scales and biases of each output row are a ridge least-squares fit of the row's outputs on the expert's routed calibration
   inputs (gate/up: x; down: h = silu(gate x) * up x from the 4-bit weights), ridge toward the stored truncation; experts with
   too few routed rows keep the truncation. Applied as gather_qmm(bits=4) on the masked codes with scale alpha / 2^(4-n).
3. Eval: prefill KL(4-bit ref || plan) per token, per corpus part (gsm solutions 1000-1149, MoonShots patches code, NewIdeas
   docs), plus dNLL and top-1 agreement, for 44333333 / 44222222 / 42222222 / 43222222, truncated vs refit.
usage: refit35.py   (GPU, ~25 GB, quiet lock; ~10 min)"""
import glob, json, math, os, sys, time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models.switch_layers import _gather_sort, _scatter_unsort

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from refit_lib import fit_proj, codes, solve

T0 = time.time()
MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
NL = len(tm.layers)
P3 = ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj")
SEG = 2048
rd = lambda p: open(os.path.expanduser(p)).read()
gsm = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))]
qa = lambda a, b: "\n\n".join("Question: " + p["question"] + "\nAnswer: " + p["answer"] for p in gsm[a:b])
cat = lambda fs: "\n\n".join(rd(f) for f in fs)
CAL = [qa(0, 400), cat(sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/scripts/*.py")))),
       rd("~/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md") + "\n\n" + rd("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md")]
EVAL = {"gsm": qa(1000, 1150), "code": cat(sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/patches/*/*.py")))),
        "general": rd("~/NewIdeas/GAPS.md") + "\n\n" + rd("~/NewIdeas/IDEAS.md") + "\n\n" + rd("~/NewIdeas/CHARTER.md")}
segs = lambda text, n: [mx.array(tok.encode(text)[i * SEG:(i + 1) * SEG + 1])[None] for i in range(n)]
cal_ids = [s for t in CAL for s in segs(t, 2)]                       # 6 segments x 2048 = 12,288 calibration tokens
eval_ids = {k: segs(t, 1)[0] for k, t in EVAL.items()}
sws = [l.mlp.switch_mlp for l in tm.layers]
orig = [{p: (getattr(sw, p).weight, getattr(sw, p).scales, getattr(sw, p).biases) for p in P3} for sw in sws]
assert all(getattr(sw, p).bits == 4 and getattr(sw, p).group_size == 64 for sw in sws for p in P3)
KEEP = {3: mx.array(0xEEEEEEEE, dtype=mx.uint32), 2: mx.array(0xCCCCCCCC, dtype=mx.uint32)}
TIERS = {}                                                           # (li, p, n) -> refit (scale, bias) [E, out, G]


def trunc_sb(li, p, n):
    w, s, b = orig[li][p]
    low = (1 << (4 - n)) - 1
    lsum = None
    for j in range(8):
        part = (w >> (4 * j)) & low
        lsum = part if lsum is None else lsum + part
    off = lsum.reshape(*s.shape, 8).sum(-1).astype(mx.float32) / 64
    return s, (b.astype(mx.float32) + s.astype(mx.float32) * off).astype(s.dtype)


def tier(li, p, n, mode):
    """(codes, scale, bias) for gather_qmm(bits=4): tier n keeps the top n bits of every code"""
    w, s, b = orig[li][p]
    if n == 4:
        return w, s, b
    s2, b2 = TIERS[(li, p, n)] if mode == "refit" else trunc_sb(li, p, n)
    return w & KEEP[n], s2, b2


CUR = {"plan": (4,) * 8, "mode": "trunc", "cap": None}


def glu(sw, x, idx, li, n, mode):
    x, sidx, inv = _gather_sort(mx.expand_dims(x, (-2, -3)), idx)

    def proj(p, h):
        w, s, b = tier(li, p, n, mode)
        return mx.gather_qmm(h, w, s, b, rhs_indices=sidx, transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=4, sorted_indices=True)

    h = sw.activation(proj("up_proj", x), proj("gate_proj", x))
    return _scatter_unsort(proj("down_proj", h), inv, idx.shape).squeeze(-2)


def moe_call(self, x):
    gates = mx.softmax(self.gate(x), axis=-1, precise=True)
    k = self.top_k
    inds = mx.argpartition(gates, kth=-k, axis=-1)[..., -k:]
    scores = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    if self.norm_topk_prob:
        scores = scores / scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    order = mx.argsort(-scores, axis=-1)
    inds, scores = mx.take_along_axis(inds, order, axis=-1), mx.take_along_axis(scores, order, axis=-1)
    if CUR["cap"] is not None:
        CUR["cap"][self._li].append((x[0].astype(mx.float16), inds[0].astype(mx.int32), scores[0].astype(mx.float32)))
    plan, y = CUR["plan"], None
    for n in sorted(set(plan), reverse=True):
        sel = mx.array([b == n for b in plan])[:, None]
        Y = mx.where(sel, glu(self.switch_mlp, x, inds, self._li, n, CUR["mode"]), 0)
        y = Y if y is None else y + Y
    y = (y * scores[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + mx.sigmoid(self.shared_expert_gate(x)) * self.shared_expert(x)


for li, l in enumerate(tm.layers):
    object.__setattr__(l.mlp, "_li", li)
type(tm.layers[0].mlp).__call__ = moe_call

# ---- 1. calibration capture (4-bit reference)
CUR["cap"] = [[] for _ in range(NL)]
for s in cal_ids:
    out = model(s[:, :-1])
    mx.eval(out, [c[-1] for c in CUR["cap"]])
cap = [tuple(mx.concatenate([t[j] for t in c]) for j in range(3)) for c in CUR["cap"]]
CUR["cap"] = None
mx.eval(cap)
print(f"calibration: {cap[0][0].shape[0]} tokens x {NL} layers captured ({time.time() - T0:.0f} s)", flush=True)


# ---- 2. refit
POOL = ThreadPoolExecutor(8)
LAM0 = float(os.environ.get("REFIT_LAMBDA", "1e-3"))
WEIGHT = os.environ.get("REFIT_WEIGHT", "none")                      # none | score2 (rows weighted by gate score^2)
MINRANK = int(os.environ.get("REFIT_MINRANK", "0"))                  # fit only on rows where the expert is at rank >= this (0-based)
print(f"refit options: lambda {LAM0}, weight {WEIGHT}, min rank {MINRANK}", flush=True)
MINROWS = 3                                                          # x 2G params (gate/up 192 rows, down 48)
nfit = {3: [0, 0], 2: [0, 0]}
for li in range(NL):
    X, I, SC = cap[li][0].astype(mx.float32), np.array(cap[li][1]), np.array(cap[li][2])
    S = {p: orig[li][p][1].astype(mx.float32) for p in P3}
    B = {p: orig[li][p][2].astype(mx.float32) for p in P3}
    new = {(p, n): ([], []) for p in P3 for n in (3, 2)}
    pending = []
    for e in range(256):
        hit = I == e
        hit[:, :MINRANK] = False
        rows = np.nonzero(hit.any(1))[0]
        Xe = X[mx.array(rows)] if len(rows) else None
        we = mx.array((SC[rows][hit[rows]] ** 2).astype(np.float32)) if (WEIGHT == "score2" and len(rows)) else None
        Qe = {p: codes(orig[li][p][0][e][None])[0] for p in P3}            # [out, K] codes 0..15 of expert e
        wdq = {p: (S[p][e][..., None] * Qe[p].reshape(*S[p][e].shape, 64).astype(mx.float32) + B[p][e][..., None]) for p in P3}
        if Xe is not None:
            g_, u_ = Xe @ wdq["gate_proj"].reshape(wdq["gate_proj"].shape[0], -1).T, Xe @ wdq["up_proj"].reshape(wdq["up_proj"].shape[0], -1).T
            He = g_ * mx.sigmoid(g_) * u_
        jobs = []
        for p in P3:
            out, G = S[p][e].shape
            inp = None if Xe is None else (He if p == "down_proj" else Xe)
            for n in (3, 2):
                r = fit_proj(inp, Qe[p], S[p][e], B[p][e], n, MINROWS, we, LAM0)
                if r[2] is None:
                    jobs.append((p, n, None, (r[0], r[1])))
                    nfit[n][1] += 1
                    continue
                mx.eval(r[0], r[1])
                jobs.append((p, n, POOL.submit(solve, np.array(r[0]), np.array(r[1])), r[2]))
                nfit[n][0] += 1
        pending.append(jobs)
        while len(pending) > 4 or (e == 255 and pending):              # collect with a lag: GPU and CPU solves overlap
            for p, n, fut, extra in pending.pop(0):
                if fut is None:
                    a, b = extra
                else:
                    th = mx.array(fut.result())
                    a, b = th[:, :extra], th[:, extra:]
                new[(p, n)][0].append(a)
                new[(p, n)][1].append(b)
    for p in P3:
        s0 = orig[li][p][1]
        for n in (3, 2):
            a = mx.stack(new[(p, n)][0]).astype(s0.dtype)                 # alpha in bf16 (the stored value)
            b = mx.stack(new[(p, n)][1]).astype(s0.dtype)
            TIERS[(li, p, n)] = ((a.astype(mx.float32) / (1 << (4 - n))).astype(s0.dtype), b)
            mx.eval(TIERS[(li, p, n)])
    if li % 8 == 7:
        print(f"  refit layers 0-{li} ({time.time() - T0:.0f} s; fits tier3 {nfit[3]}, tier2 {nfit[2]} [fitted, kept])", flush=True)
del cap
print(f"refit done ({time.time() - T0:.0f} s): tier 3 {nfit[3][0]} fitted / {nfit[3][1]} kept truncated, tier 2 {nfit[2][0]} / "
      f"{nfit[2][1]}", flush=True)

# ---- 3. eval: prefill KL vs the 4-bit reference per corpus part
PLANS = [("44333333", "trunc"), ("44333333", "refit"), ("44222222", "trunc"), ("44222222", "refit"),
         ("42222222", "trunc"), ("42222222", "refit"), ("43222222", "trunc"), ("43222222", "refit")]
if os.environ.get("REFIT_PLANS"):
    keep = os.environ["REFIT_PLANS"].split(",")
    PLANS = [(p, m) for p, m in PLANS if p in keep and (m == "refit" or os.environ.get("REFIT_TRUNC", "1") == "1")]
res = {}
logits = lambda inp: (lambda o: (o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o)[0])(model(inp))
CH = 256
for part, ids in eval_ids.items():
    inp, tgt = ids[:, :-1], ids[0, 1:]
    T = inp.shape[1]
    CUR["plan"], CUR["mode"] = (4,) * 8, "trunc"
    lo = logits(inp)
    lr, nll_r, top_r = [], 0.0, []
    for c in range(0, T, CH):
        l = lo[c:c + CH].astype(mx.float32)
        l = l - mx.logsumexp(l, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        nll_r += -mx.take_along_axis(l, tgt[c:c + CH, None], axis=-1).sum().item()
        top_r.append(mx.argmax(l, axis=-1))
        lr.append(l.astype(mx.bfloat16))
        mx.eval(lr[-1], top_r[-1])
    del lo
    for plan, mode in PLANS:
        CUR["plan"], CUR["mode"] = tuple(int(c) for c in plan), mode
        lo = logits(inp)
        kls, nll, ag = [], 0.0, 0.0
        for i, c in enumerate(range(0, T, CH)):
            l = lo[c:c + CH].astype(mx.float32)
            l = l - mx.logsumexp(l, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
            r = lr[i].astype(mx.float32)
            kl = (mx.exp(r) * (r - l)).sum(-1)
            nll += -mx.take_along_axis(l, tgt[c:c + CH, None], axis=-1).sum().item()
            ag += (mx.argmax(l, axis=-1) == top_r[i]).astype(mx.float32).sum().item()
            mx.eval(kl)
            kls.append(np.array(kl))
        del lo
        kl = np.concatenate(kls)
        res[(part, plan, mode)] = (kl.mean(), (nll - nll_r) / T, ag / T)
        print(f"{part:8s} {plan} {mode:5s}: KL {kl.mean():.5f} (p99 {np.percentile(kl, 99):.4f}), dNLL {(nll - nll_r) / T:+.4f}, "
              f"top-1 agreement {ag / T:.4f}", flush=True)
    del lr
print("--- refit / trunc KL ratio per plan and part:", flush=True)
for plan in sorted({p for p, _ in PLANS}):
    print(f"{plan}: " + "  ".join(f"{part} {res[(part, plan, 'refit')][0] / res[(part, plan, 'trunc')][0]:.3f}" for part in eval_ids),
          flush=True)
print(f"done ({time.time() - T0:.0f} s)", flush=True)


### scripts/refit_lib.py
"""Helpers for the activation-aware refit of truncated expert tiers (scripts/refit35.py, scripts/test_refit_lib.py)."""
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx


def fit_proj(inp, qe, se, be, n, minrows=3, wrow=None, lam0=1e-3):
    """Activation-aware refit of tier n for one projection of one expert. inp [S, K] f32 routed inputs (or None), qe [out, K]
    codes 0..15, se/be [out, G] f32 4-bit scales/biases. Returns (a0, b0, None) to keep the truncation, or
    (A [out, 2G, 2G], rhs [out, 2G], G) for the ridge system whose solution is (alpha | beta). wrow [S]: per-row weights
    (e.g. gate score^2: the expert's share of the block output); lam0: ridge strength relative to the mean diagonal."""
    out, G = se.shape
    sh = 4 - n
    q = qe.reshape(out, G, 64)
    m = (q >> sh).astype(mx.float32)
    low = (q & ((1 << sh) - 1)).astype(mx.float32)
    a0 = se * (1 << sh)
    b0 = be + se * low.mean(-1)
    if inp is None or inp.shape[0] < minrows * 2 * G:
        return a0, b0, None
    ns = inp.shape[0]
    if wrow is not None:                                                   # weighted LS: scale rows by sqrt(weight)
        inp = inp * mx.sqrt(wrow / wrow.mean())[:, None]
    w = se[..., None] * q.astype(mx.float32) + be[..., None]
    xg = inp.reshape(ns, G, 64)
    pa = mx.einsum("sgk,ngk->nsg", xg, m)                                 # [out, S, G]
    pb = xg.sum(-1)                                                        # [S, G]
    t = inp @ w.reshape(out, -1).T                                         # [S, out]
    Aaa = mx.einsum("nsg,nsh->ngh", pa, pa)
    Aab = mx.einsum("nsg,sh->ngh", pa, pb)
    Abb = mx.broadcast_to((pb.T @ pb)[None], (out, G, G))
    A = mx.concatenate([mx.concatenate([Aaa, Aab], -1), mx.concatenate([Aab.transpose(0, 2, 1), Abb], -1)], 1)
    ra, rb = mx.einsum("nsg,sn->ng", pa, t), (pb.T @ t).T
    th0 = mx.concatenate([a0, b0], -1)
    lam = (lam0 * max(1.0, 512.0 / ns)) * mx.trace(A, axis1=1, axis2=2) / (2 * G) + 1e-6
    rhs = mx.concatenate([ra, rb], -1) + lam[:, None] * th0
    return A + lam[:, None, None] * mx.eye(2 * G)[None], rhs, G


def codes(w):
    return ((w[..., None] >> mx.arange(0, 32, 4, dtype=mx.uint32)) & 15).reshape(w.shape[0], w.shape[1], -1)


def solve(A, rhs):
    return np.linalg.solve(A, rhs[..., None])[..., 0]


### scripts/reuse_policy125.py
"""Learned-reuse replacement for the expert cache (CPU-cache idea: Mockingjay, Shah et al. HPCA 2022, predicts each line's
reuse distance and evicts the one whose predicted next use is farthest; Hawkeye, Jain & Lin ISCA 2016, learns OPT's
decisions from the past). Exactness-preserving (a cache policy never changes outputs). Trace: data/verify_trace125.npz,
per layer C slots, misses per pass in 4-bit MB, vs LRU and Belady OPT (scripts/opt_bound125.py).
Policies (all online, using only the past):
  rd-ema    predicted next use = last use + EMA of the expert's observed reuse distances (passes); evict the farthest
  rd-last   the same with the last observed reuse distance
  rd-mix    max(predicted next use, LRU age guard): rd-ema but an expert unseen for > 2x its predicted distance is stale
usage: reuse_policy125.py   (CPU, ~1 min, 1 core)"""
import numpy as np

z = np.load("/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/verify_trace125.npz")
ids = z["ids"].astype(np.int32)
P, L = ids.shape[:2]
seqs = [[set(np.unique(ids[p, l]).tolist()) for p in range(P)] for l in range(L)]
MB = 2.765


def run(seq, C, mode, a=0.5):
    cache, last, rd, miss = set(), {}, {}, 0
    gm = 8.0                                             # prior reuse distance for never-reused experts (passes)
    for t, need in enumerate(seq):
        for e in need:
            if e in last:
                d = t - last[e]
                rd[e] = d if (mode == "rd-last" or e not in rd) else a * d + (1 - a) * rd[e]
            miss += e not in cache
            cache.add(e)
            last[e] = t
        if len(cache) > C:
            def pred(e):
                if mode == "lru":
                    return -last[e]
                nxt = last[e] + rd.get(e, gm)
                if mode == "rd-mix" and t - last[e] > 2 * rd.get(e, gm):
                    nxt = 10 ** 9                        # stale: overdue by 2x its usual gap
                return nxt
            cand = sorted((e for e in cache if e not in need), key=pred, reverse=True)
            for e in cand[:len(cache) - C]:
                cache.discard(e)
    return miss


for C in (64, 96, 128, 192):
    out = []
    for mode in ("lru", "rd-ema", "rd-last", "rd-mix"):
        m = sum(run(s, C, mode) for s in seqs) / P
        out.append(f"{mode} {m * MB:6.1f}")
    print(f"C={C:3d} ({48 * C * MB / 1e3:4.1f} GB) MB/pass  " + " | ".join(out), flush=True)


### scripts/route_pred_eval.py
"""Round 23 evaluation: how well does the MTP head predict the next verify pass's experts (data/mtp_route_pred125.*)?
Per verify row r (positions t..t+2 of a depth-3 pass), the MTP draft step r+1 gave a top-20 per trunk layer. Measures:
  recall@B   |predicted top-B ∩ actual top-10| / 10, by layer group, vs the previous position's own top-10 (row r-1, or
             the previous pass's last valid row) as a baseline;
  misses     a per-layer LRU of C slots replays the passes (all rows' experts loaded, MRU, as BigMoE today); a miss is
             "covered" if it is in the union of the pass's MTP predictions at that layer (top-B per predicted row), i.e.
             it could have been read during drafting; "wasted" = predicted, not cached, not used by the pass.
usage: route_pred_eval.py [C] [B ...]   (default C=128 = 17 GB, B = 10 20)"""
import json, os, sys
from collections import OrderedDict, defaultdict
import numpy as np
D = os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data")
z = np.load(f"{D}/mtp_route_pred125.npz")
meta = json.load(open(f"{D}/mtp_route_pred125.json"))
ids, rows, seqs, prompt = z["ids"], z["rows"], z["seq"], z["prompt"]
pred, pp, ps, pj = z["pred"], z["pred_prompt"], z["pred_seq"], z["pred_step"]
P, NL, RM, K = ids.shape
C = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 128
Bs = [int(x) for x in sys.argv[2:]] or [10, 20]
# map each MTP draft step to (pass index, row): the next gated pass of the prompt after the seq it was drafted under
by_prompt = defaultdict(list)
for i in range(P):
    by_prompt[int(prompt[i])].append(i)
predmap = {}                                           # (pass, row) -> [NL, PRED]
for k in range(len(pred)):
    cand = [i for i in by_prompt[int(pp[k])] if seqs[i] > ps[k]]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = cand[0]
    r = int(pj[k]) - 1
    if 0 <= r < rows[i]:
        predmap[(i, r)] = pred[k]
# valid rows (accepted path) from the callbacks, as wrongpath_sim.py
committed = defaultdict(int)
for (p, s, n) in meta["callbacks"]:
    committed[(p, s)] += n
valid = np.ones((P, RM), bool)
for i in range(P):
    n = committed.get((int(prompt[i]), int(seqs[i])), 0)
    if n:
        valid[i, min(n, rows[i]):] = False
groups = [(0, 12), (12, 24), (24, 36), (36, 48)]
print(f"{P} passes, {len(predmap)} (pass, row) predictions, rows/pass {np.bincount(rows).tolist()}")
# recall
for B in Bs:
    rec = np.zeros((NL, 3)); base = np.zeros((NL, 3)); cnt = np.zeros((NL, 3))
    for (i, r), pr in predmap.items():
        if r > 2:
            continue
        for L in range(NL):
            act = set(ids[i, L, r].tolist())
            if -1 in act:
                continue
            rec[L, r] += len(act & set(pr[L, :B].tolist())) / K
            if r >= 1:
                prev = set(ids[i, L, r - 1].tolist())
            else:
                j = i - 1
                if j < 0 or prompt[j] != prompt[i]:
                    continue
                last = int(valid[j, :rows[j]].sum()) - 1
                prev = set(ids[j, L, max(0, last)].tolist())
            base[L, r] += len(act & prev) / K
            cnt[L, r] += 1
    print(f"\nrecall@{B} of the actual top-10 (MTP head | previous position's top-10 as baseline), by layer group:")
    for r in range(3):
        parts = []
        for a, b in groups:
            n = cnt[a:b, r].sum()
            parts.append(f"L{a:2d}-{b - 1:2d} {rec[a:b, r].sum() / max(n, 1):.2f} | {base[a:b, r].sum() / max(n, 1):.2f}")
        print(f"  row {r}: " + "   ".join(parts))
# miss coverage under a per-layer LRU (all rows loaded, MRU)
for B in Bs:
    caches = [OrderedDict() for _ in range(NL)]
    miss = np.zeros(NL); cov = np.zeros(NL); waste = np.zeros(NL); npass = 0
    for i in range(P):
        R = int(rows[i])
        rowsp = [r for r in range(R) if (i, r) in predmap]
        for L in range(NL):
            c = caches[L]
            need = {int(e) for r in range(R) for e in ids[i, L, r] if e >= 0}
            if not need:
                continue
            ps_ = {int(e) for r in rowsp for e in predmap[(i, r)][L, :B]}
            m = [e for e in need if e not in c]
            if i >= 60:
                miss[L] += len(m)
                cov[L] += sum(1 for e in m if e in ps_)
                waste[L] += len(ps_ - need - set(c.keys()))
            for e in need:
                if e in c:
                    c.move_to_end(e)
                else:
                    c[e] = 1
            while len(c) > C:
                c.popitem(last=False)
        if i >= 60:
            npass += 1
    print(f"\nC={C} ({C * 2.77 * NL / 1e3:.0f} GB), MTP top-{B} per predicted row: misses per pass, share covered by the "
          f"prediction (loadable during drafting), wasted prefetch reads per pass:")
    for a, b in groups:
        print(f"  L{a:2d}-{b - 1:2d}: misses {miss[a:b].sum() / npass:6.1f}, covered {cov[a:b].sum() / max(1, miss[a:b].sum()):.0%}, "
              f"wasted {waste[a:b].sum() / npass:6.1f}")
    print(f"  all   : misses {miss.sum() / npass:6.1f}, covered {cov.sum() / max(1, miss.sum()):.0%}, wasted {waste.sum() / npass:6.1f}")


### scripts/runahead_recall.py
"""Runahead execution for SSD-streamed MoE: how well does it predict the next layers' experts?
Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 4-bit (40 layers x 256 experts, top-8), real weights, ~900 tokens of real text (teacher-forced prefill;
routing matches token-by-token decode on ~98 % of top-8 sets, MoEPrefetch). Per-layer LRU residency at 55 % (141 of 256).
For layer L and each token, three predictors of layer L+1's true top-8:
  look-ahead  : L+1's router on L's pre-MLP residual (BigMoE prefetch v1 / MoEPrefetch pmid1)
  ra-noattn   : L's MoE computed from resident experts only (missing ones substituted by the router's next-best resident
                experts, as MoEPrefetch does), then L+1's router on that residual (cheap runahead: no attention)
  runahead    : as ra-noattn, then layer L+1's attention/GDN on that scratch state, then L+1's router (CPU runahead
                execution, Mutlu et al. 2003, applied to expert streaming). Pessimistic: the scratch run also perturbs
                earlier tokens' K/V, whereas real runahead would only perturb the current token.
Depth 2 (every 4th L): L+2's true experts vs look-ahead-2 (L+2's router on L's pre-MLP residual) and 2-layer runahead.
Recall@B = share of the true top-8 found in the predictor's top-B, averaged over tokens that miss at layer L (runahead only
runs during stalls) and over all tokens. Usage: runahead_recall.py   (GPU, ~20 GB: needs an exclusive window)"""
import os, sys
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models.qwen3_5 import create_attention_mask, create_ssm_mask

MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
layers = tm.layers
NL = len(layers)
E = layers[0].mlp.num_experts
K = layers[0].mlp.top_k
C = int(0.55 * E)
WARM = 300

text = open(os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md")).read()[:6000] + "\n\n" + \
       open(os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode/mtp.py")).read()[:4000]
ids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:900])[None]
T = ids.shape[1]
h = tm.embed_tokens(ids)
fa = create_attention_mask(h, None)
ssm = create_ssm_mask(h, None)


def mixer(layer, x):
    m = ssm if layer.is_linear else fa
    f = layer.linear_attn if layer.is_linear else layer.self_attn
    return x + f(layer.input_layernorm(x), m, None)


def gates_of(layer, hmid):
    return mx.softmax(layer.mlp.gate(layer.post_attention_layernorm(hmid)), axis=-1, precise=True)


def moe_with(layer, hmid, inds_np, gates):
    """layer's MoE output with the given expert ids per token (scores = router probs, renormalised over the chosen ids)."""
    mlp = layer.mlp
    xn = layer.post_attention_layernorm(hmid)
    inds = mx.array(inds_np[None].astype(np.int32))
    sc = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    sc = sc / sc.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    y = (mlp.switch_mlp(xn, inds) * sc[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)
    return y + mx.sigmoid(mlp.shared_expert_gate(xn)) * mlp.shared_expert(xn)


# exact pass: per-layer pre-MLP residual and true routing
x = h
hmid, top16, gts = [], [], []
for L, layer in enumerate(layers):
    hm = mixer(layer, x)
    g = gates_of(layer, hm)
    x = hm + layer.mlp(layer.post_attention_layernorm(hm))
    mx.eval(hm, g, x)
    hmid.append(hm); gts.append(g)
    top16.append(np.argsort(-np.array(g[0].astype(mx.float32)), axis=-1)[:, :16])

# LRU residency per layer (resident set before each token's access)
resident = []
for L in range(NL):
    lru, res = OrderedDict(), []
    for t in range(T):
        res.append(set(lru))
        for e in top16[L][t, :K]:
            lru[int(e)] = 1; lru.move_to_end(int(e))
        while len(lru) > C:
            lru.popitem(last=False)
    resident.append(res)


def substitute(L):
    """per token: the true top-8 with missing experts replaced by the next-best resident ones from the top-16."""
    out = np.zeros((T, K), np.int64); miss = np.zeros(T, bool)
    for t in range(T):
        r = resident[L][t]
        tr = [int(e) for e in top16[L][t, :K]]
        miss[t] = any(e not in r for e in tr)
        ch = [e for e in tr if e in r] + [int(e) for e in top16[L][t, K:] if int(e) in r]
        ch = (ch + [e for e in tr if e not in r])[:K]          # pad (rare) with true ids if too few are resident
        out[t] = ch
    return out, miss


def recall(pred_gates, true_top8, rows):
    p = np.argsort(-np.array(pred_gates[0].astype(mx.float32)), axis=-1)
    r = {}
    for B in (8, 12, 16):
        hit = [len(set(p[t, :B]) & set(true_top8[t])) / K for t in rows]
        r[B] = float(np.mean(hit))
    return r


acc = {k: {B: [] for B in (8, 12, 16)} for k in ("look-ahead", "ra-noattn", "runahead", "ra-attn-only", "ra-attn+shared", "look-ahead-2", "runahead-2")}
acc_all = {k: {B: [] for B in (8, 12, 16)} for k in ("look-ahead", "runahead")}
stall_share = []
for L in range(NL - 1):
    sub, miss = substitute(L)
    rows = [t for t in range(WARM, T) if miss[t]]
    allr = list(range(WARM, T))
    stall_share.append(len(rows) / len(allr))
    nxt = layers[L + 1]
    x1 = hmid[L] + moe_with(layers[L], hmid[L], sub, gts[L])          # layer L output from resident experts
    hm1 = mixer(nxt, x1)                                               # runahead through L+1's attention/GDN
    g_la, g_na, g_ra = gates_of(nxt, hmid[L]), gates_of(nxt, x1), gates_of(nxt, hm1)
    # cheaper slices: skip L's routed experts entirely (attention only), or add only the always-resident shared expert
    xn = layers[L].post_attention_layernorm(hmid[L])
    shared = mx.sigmoid(layers[L].mlp.shared_expert_gate(xn)) * layers[L].mlp.shared_expert(xn)
    g_ao = gates_of(nxt, mixer(nxt, hmid[L]))
    g_as = gates_of(nxt, mixer(nxt, hmid[L] + shared))
    mx.eval(g_la, g_na, g_ra, g_ao, g_as)
    true1 = top16[L + 1][:, :K]
    for name, g in (("look-ahead", g_la), ("ra-noattn", g_na), ("runahead", g_ra), ("ra-attn-only", g_ao),
                    ("ra-attn+shared", g_as)):
        for B, v in recall(g, true1, rows).items():
            acc[name][B].append(v)
    for name, g in (("look-ahead", g_la), ("runahead", g_ra)):
        for B, v in recall(g, true1, allr).items():
            acc_all[name][B].append(v)
    if L % 4 == 0 and L + 2 < NL:                                      # depth 2
        sub1, _ = substitute(L + 1)
        # runahead continues: L+1's MoE from resident experts, routed by the runahead's own L+1 prediction
        pred1 = np.argsort(-np.array(g_ra[0].astype(mx.float32)), axis=-1)
        sub1r = np.zeros((T, K), np.int64)
        for t in range(T):
            r = resident[L + 1][t]
            ch = [int(e) for e in pred1[t, :16] if int(e) in r][:K]
            sub1r[t] = (ch + [int(e) for e in pred1[t, :K] if int(e) not in ch])[:K]
        x2 = hm1 + moe_with(nxt, hm1, sub1r, g_ra)
        hm2 = mixer(layers[L + 2], x2)
        g_la2, g_ra2 = gates_of(layers[L + 2], hmid[L]), gates_of(layers[L + 2], hm2)
        mx.eval(g_la2, g_ra2)
        for name, g in (("look-ahead-2", g_la2), ("runahead-2", g_ra2)):
            for B, v in recall(g, top16[L + 2][:, :K], rows).items():
                acc[name][B].append(v)
    print(f"L={L:>2} stall share {stall_share[-1]:.0%}  recall@16 look-ahead {acc['look-ahead'][16][-1]:.3f} "
          f"ra-noattn {acc['ra-noattn'][16][-1]:.3f} runahead {acc['runahead'][16][-1]:.3f}", flush=True)

print(f"\n{T} tokens, 55 % resident (LRU, C={C}); tokens that miss at layer L: {np.mean(stall_share):.0%} on average")
print("recall of the next layer's true top-8 (mean over layers), on tokens that stall at L:")
for name in ("look-ahead", "ra-noattn", "runahead", "ra-attn-only", "ra-attn+shared", "look-ahead-2", "runahead-2"):
    print(f"  {name:13s} " + "  ".join(f"@{B}: {np.mean(v):.3f}" for B, v in acc[name].items()))
print("on all tokens:")
for name in ("look-ahead", "runahead"):
    print(f"  {name:13s} " + "  ".join(f"@{B}: {np.mean(v):.3f}" for B, v in acc_all[name].items()))


### scripts/sb_audit.py
"""W24-sp step (1), NewIdeas GO Mon 03:32: CPU audit of the exact scale / bias pack over ALL 24,576 experts (48 x 512).
Reads ONLY the scale / bias pieces of BigMoE's records3 (MSB part: s3 + b3 per projection, LSB part: b per projection = 9
pieces of 25,600 bf16 per expert, 450 KB of the 2.93 MB record; F_NOCACHE preads, one thread). Per piece: the bf16 exponent
span over its nonzero values, zeros / subnormals / inf-nan, sign use, and a pack + unpack round trip of the planar formats
  12-bit: plane A = sign + 7 mantissa bits (one byte), plane B = 4-bit exponent offset from a per-piece base (2 per byte)
  11-bit: the same with a 3-bit offset (MLX 3-bit packing, 8 values in 3 bytes)
offset 0 = (signed) zero, offsets 1..W = exponents base..base + W - 1 (W = 15 / 7), compared bitwise with the original
bytes. A piece whose nonzero exponents don't fit (span > 14 / > 6) or that holds a subnormal / inf / nan is an ESCAPE (kept
raw); for those the best window's value-level escape count is reported too. Quiet research mode: waits out
~/.local/share/quiet/PAUSE between layers; stops if pmset reports a thermal / performance warning.
usage: taskpolicy -b python sb_audit.py [first_layer=0] [last_layer=47] [experts=512]  -> stdout, data/sb_audit.npz"""
import fcntl, json, os, re, subprocess, sys, time
import numpy as np

D = "/Users/kamen/BigMoE/models/bare/records3/"
IX = json.load(open(D + "index.json"))["layers"]
L0 = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0
L1 = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 47
NE = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 512
REC, G, CH = 2932736, 25600, 32                       # record bytes, values per piece, experts per chunk
TYPES = ("s3", "b3", "b")                             # piece index = type * 3 + projection
PAUSE = os.path.expanduser("~/.local/share/quiet/PAUSE")
F_NOCACHE = getattr(fcntl, "F_NOCACHE", 48)


def hot():
    t = subprocess.run(["pmset", "-g", "therm"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    return any(int(x) > 0 for x in re.findall(r"level set to (\d+)", t)) or \
        any(int(x) < 100 for x in re.findall(r"CPU_Speed_Limit\s*=\s*(\d+)", t))


def pack(b, k):                                       # b: (P, G) uint8 offsets < 2^k -> (P, G * k / 8) uint8
    if k == 4:
        b = b.reshape(len(b), -1, 2)
        return b[:, :, 0] | (b[:, :, 1] << 4)
    w = (b.reshape(len(b), -1, 8).astype(np.uint32) << (3 * np.arange(8, dtype=np.uint32))).sum(2, dtype=np.uint32)
    return np.stack([w & 0xFF, (w >> 8) & 0xFF, w >> 16], 2).astype(np.uint8).reshape(len(b), -1)


def unpack(p, k):
    if k == 4:
        return np.stack([p & 0xF, p >> 4], 2).reshape(len(p), -1)
    t = p.reshape(len(p), -1, 3).astype(np.uint32)
    w = t[:, :, 0] | (t[:, :, 1] << 8) | (t[:, :, 2] << 16)
    return ((w[:, :, None] >> (3 * np.arange(8, dtype=np.uint32))) & 7).astype(np.uint8).reshape(len(p), -1)


def best_window(e, W):                                # value-level: most nonzero normal exponents inside W consecutive
    h = np.bincount(e, minlength=256)
    c = np.convolve(h, np.ones(W, np.int64))[W - 1:]
    return len(e) - int(c.max())


def audit(v):                                         # v: (P, G) uint16 -> per-piece stats + round-trip mismatches
    e = ((v >> 7) & 0xFF).astype(np.int16)
    z = (v & 0x7FFF) == 0
    sub = (e == 0) & ~z
    spc = e == 255
    ok = ~z & ~sub & ~spc
    emin = np.where(ok, e, 999).min(1)
    emax = np.where(ok, e, -1).max(1)
    has = emax >= 0
    span = np.where(has, emax - emin, 0)
    neg = ((v >> 15) == 1) & ~z
    pos = ((v >> 15) == 0) & ~z
    r = dict(emin=np.where(has, emin, 0), emax=np.where(has, emax, 0), span=span, zeros=z.sum(1), sub=sub.sum(1),
             spc=spc.sum(1), both=neg.any(1) & pos.any(1))
    a = (((v >> 8) & 0x80) | (v & 0x7F)).astype(np.uint8)
    base = np.where(has, emin, 1)[:, None]
    for k in (4, 3):
        W = 2 ** k - 1
        fits = (span <= W - 1) & (r["sub"] == 0) & (r["spc"] == 0)
        off = np.where(z, 0, np.clip(e - base + 1, 0, W)).astype(np.uint8)
        b2 = unpack(pack(off, k), k).astype(np.int16)
        v2 = ((a.astype(np.uint16) & 0x80) << 8) | (a.astype(np.uint16) & 0x7F) | \
            np.where(b2 == 0, 0, (b2 + base - 1) << 7).astype(np.uint16)
        r[f"esc{k}"] = ~fits
        r[f"mism{k}"] = ((v2 != v) & fits[:, None]).sum(1)
        r[f"vesc{k}"] = np.array([0 if f else best_window(e[i][ok[i]], W) + int(sub[i].sum() + spc[i].sum())
                                  for i, f in enumerate(fits)])
    return r


def main():
    t_all = time.time()
    keys = ("emin", "emax", "span", "zeros", "sub", "spc", "both", "esc4", "mism4", "vesc4", "esc3", "mism3", "vesc3")
    out = {k: np.zeros((48, NE, 9), np.int32) for k in keys}
    print(f"W24-sp step (1) audit: layers {L0}-{L1}, {NE} experts each, 9 scale / bias pieces of {G} bf16 per expert; "
          f"start {time.strftime('%a %H:%M:%S')}", flush=True)
    last = L0 - 1
    for L in range(L0, L1 + 1):
        waited = 0.0
        while os.path.exists(PAUSE):
            time.sleep(5); waited += 5
        if hot():
            print(f"STOP: pmset reports a thermal / performance warning before layer {L}", flush=True); break
        t0 = time.time()
        ly = IX[str(L)]
        mo, ms, lo, ls = ly["msb_piece_off"], ly["msb_piece_size"], ly["lsb_piece_off"], ly["lsb_piece_size"]
        assert all(mo[3 * p + 2] == mo[3 * p + 1] + ms[3 * p + 1] and ms[3 * p + 1] == ms[3 * p + 2] == 2 * G
                   and ls[2 * p + 1] == 2 * G for p in range(3))
        fd = os.open(D + ly["file"], os.O_RDONLY)
        fcntl.fcntl(fd, F_NOCACHE, 1)
        for c0 in range(0, NE, CH):
            n = min(CH, NE - c0)
            buf = np.empty((n, 9, G), np.uint16)
            for i in range(n):
                o = (c0 + i) * REC
                for p in range(3):
                    sb = np.frombuffer(os.pread(fd, 4 * G, o + mo[3 * p + 1]), np.uint16)
                    buf[i, p], buf[i, 3 + p] = sb[:G], sb[G:]
                    buf[i, 6 + p] = np.frombuffer(os.pread(fd, 2 * G, o + lo[2 * p + 1]), np.uint16)
            r = audit(buf.reshape(n * 9, G))
            for k in keys:
                out[k][L, c0:c0 + n] = r[k].reshape(n, 9)
        os.close(fd)
        sp = out["span"][L]
        print(f"L{L:02d} span max s3 {sp[:, 0:3].max()} b3 {sp[:, 3:6].max()} b {sp[:, 6:9].max()} | escapes 12-bit "
              f"{out['esc4'][L].sum()} 11-bit {out['esc3'][L].sum()} | zeros {out['zeros'][L].sum()} subnormal "
              f"{out['sub'][L].sum()} inf/nan {out['spc'][L].sum()} | round-trip mismatches 12-bit {out['mism4'][L].sum()} "
              f"11-bit {out['mism3'][L].sum()} | {time.time() - t0:.0f} s" + (f" (waited {waited:.0f} s on PAUSE)" if waited
                                                                            else ""), flush=True)
        last = L
    if last < L0:
        return
    L, done = last, slice(L0, last + 1)
    np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "data", "sb_audit.npz"),
                        **{k: out[k][done].astype(np.int16 if k in ("emin", "emax", "span") else np.int32) for k in keys},
                        layers=np.arange(L0, L + 1))
    npc = (L + 1 - L0) * NE * 3
    print(f"\nSUMMARY over layers {L0}-{L}: {npc * 3} pieces ({npc} per type), {npc * 3 * G} values; "
          f"{time.time() - t_all:.0f} s; end {time.strftime('%a %H:%M:%S')}")
    for t, nm in enumerate(TYPES):
        s = slice(3 * t, 3 * t + 3)
        sp = out["span"][done, :, s].ravel()
        hist = np.bincount(sp, minlength=8)
        print(f"{nm:2s}: span histogram " + " ".join(f"{i}:{c}" for i, c in enumerate(hist) if c) +
              f" | both signs in {100 * out['both'][done, :, s].mean():.2f} % of pieces | zeros {out['zeros'][done, :, s].sum()}"
              f" (pieces with any {(out['zeros'][done, :, s] > 0).sum()}) | subnormal {out['sub'][done, :, s].sum()} inf/nan "
              f"{out['spc'][done, :, s].sum()} | exponent range {out['emin'][done, :, s].min()}-{out['emax'][done, :, s].max()}")
    for k, bits in ((4, 12), (3, 11)):
        esc = out[f"esc{k}"][done]
        print(f"{bits}-bit (offset {k} bits, window {2 ** k - 1} exponents + zero code): escaped pieces {esc.sum()} of "
              f"{esc.size} ({100 * esc.mean():.4f} %), layers with any {int((esc.reshape(esc.shape[0], -1).sum(1) > 0).sum())};"
              f" value-level escapes {out[f'vesc{k}'][done].sum()} (max per piece {out[f'vesc{k}'][done].max()} of {G}); "
              f"round-trip mismatches on packed pieces {out[f'mism{k}'][done].sum()}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


### scripts/scale_exp.py
"""24 GB wall, exact scale/bias packing: per projection of real experts (records3 MSB part s3, b3; LSB part b), the bf16
exponent span (max - min over the projection's 25,600 groups), sign use, and the bits a lossless fixed-width packing needs:
sign (if used) + 7 mantissa + ceil(log2(span + 1)) exponent offset from a per-projection base (+ zero handling).
usage: scale_exp.py [n_experts=48]   (CPU only)"""
import json, math, sys
import numpy as np

D = "/Users/kamen/BigMoE/models/bare/records3/"
IX = json.load(open(D + "index.json"))["layers"]
N = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 48
REC = 2932736
rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
picks = [(int(L), int(e)) for L, e in zip(rng.integers(0, 48, N), rng.integers(0, 512, N))]
stats = {"s3": [], "b3": [], "b": []}
for L, e in picks:
    ly = IX[str(L)]
    with open(D + ly["file"], "rb") as f:
        f.seek(e * REC); rec = np.frombuffer(f.read(REC), np.uint8)
    mo, ms, lo, ls = ly["msb_piece_off"], ly["msb_piece_size"], ly["lsb_piece_off"], ly["lsb_piece_size"]
    for p in range(3):
        for name, o, sz in (("s3", mo[3 * p + 1], ms[3 * p + 1]), ("b3", mo[3 * p + 2], ms[3 * p + 2]), ("b", lo[2 * p + 1], ls[2 * p + 1])):
            v = np.frombuffer(rec[o:o + sz].tobytes(), np.uint16)
            ex = (v >> 7) & 0xFF; sg = v >> 15; z = (v & 0x7FFF) == 0
            exn = ex[~z]
            span = int(exn.max() - exn.min()) if exn.size else 0
            stats[name].append((span, int(sg.any()), int(z.sum()), int(exn.min()) if exn.size else 0))
for name, st in stats.items():
    sp = np.array([s[0] for s in st]); sg = np.array([s[1] for s in st]); zz = np.array([s[2] for s in st])
    bits = [(1 if s[1] else 0) + 7 + math.ceil(math.log2(s[0] + 2)) for s in st]   # +2: one code reserved for zero
    print(f"{name:2s}: exponent span per projection median {int(np.median(sp))} max {sp.max()}; sign used in "
          f"{100 * sg.mean():.0f} % of projections; zeros per projection median {int(np.median(zz))}; lossless fixed-width "
          f"bits median {int(np.median(bits))} max {max(bits)} (vs 16)")


### scripts/screen_head.py
"""Screened lm_head: how many vocab rows must be read exactly so the argmax (greedy) or the
Gumbel-max sample (temperature T) is provably the same as with the full head?
Proxy: Qwen3.5-2B (same 248320 x 2048 head shape as Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, tied embedding).
Screen = a low-bit requantization of the head + a per-row error bound. Rows whose upper bound
falls below the best lower bound can't win; the rest are computed exactly.
Step 1 (GPU, small): hidden states -> data/screen_h.npz. Step 2 (CPU, numpy): the analysis."""
import os, sys
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

MODEL = "/Users/kamen/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.5-2B-MLX-4bit"
NTOK = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 900
CACHE = "/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data/screen_h.npz"

if not os.path.exists(CACHE):
    from mlx_lm import load
    m, tok = load(MODEL)
    tm = m.language_model.model
    texts = [open(p).read() for p in ["/Users/kamen/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md", "/Users/kamen/SpecDecode/mtp.py",
                                      "/Users/kamen/MoEPrefetch/FINDINGS.md"]]
    H = []
    for t in texts:
        ids = tok.encode(t)[: NTOK // len(texts)]
        h = tm(mx.array(ids)[None])[0]                  # after the final norm
        mx.eval(h); H.append(np.array(h.astype(mx.float32)))
    emb = tm.embed_tokens
    W = np.array(mx.dequantize(emb.weight, emb.scales, emb.biases, group_size=emb.group_size,
                               bits=emb.bits).astype(mx.float32))
    np.savez(CACHE, H=np.concatenate(H), W=W)
    print("saved hidden states", flush=True)
    sys.exit()

z = np.load(CACHE)
H, W = z["H"], z["W"]
V, D = W.shape
N = len(H)
Z = H @ W.T
top2 = np.partition(Z, -2, axis=1)[:, -2:]
gap = np.abs(top2[:, 1] - top2[:, 0])
print(f"N={N} hidden states, head {V}x{D}; top1-top2 logit gap median {np.median(gap):.2f}, "
      f"p10 {np.percentile(gap, 10):.2f}", flush=True)
HEAD_BYTES = V * D * 8.5 / 8                           # 35B's head at Q8 (g64): 0.54 GB
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
hn = np.linalg.norm(H, axis=1)


def requant(Wf, bits, g=64):
    q, s, b = mx.quantize(mx.array(Wf), group_size=g, bits=bits)
    return np.array(mx.dequantize(q, s, b, group_size=g, bits=bits))


def evaluate(name, Zs, Bd, screen_bytes):
    """Zs: screened logits (N, V); Bd: bound on |exact - screened| (N, V)."""
    lo = (Zs - Bd).max(1, keepdims=True)
    cands = (Zs + Bd >= lo).sum(1)
    tot = screen_bytes + cands.mean() * D * 8.5 / 8
    print(f"{name:44s} candidates median {int(np.median(cands)):>6} p90 {int(np.percentile(cands, 90)):>6} "
          f"max {cands.max():>6}  bytes {tot/1e6:5.0f} MB = {tot/HEAD_BYTES:6.1%} of the Q8 head", flush=True)


def gumbel(T):
    u = rng.random((N, V), dtype=np.float32)
    return -np.log(-np.log(np.clip(u, 1e-12, 1 - 1e-7))) * T   # argmax(z + T*g) == sample at temperature T


for bits in (2, 3, 4):
    Ws = requant(W, bits)
    E = W - Ws
    Zs = H @ Ws.T
    rown = np.linalg.norm(E, axis=1)
    Bcs = hn[:, None] * rown[None, :]                    # Cauchy-Schwarz
    Eg = np.abs(E).reshape(V, D // 64, 64).max(-1)       # per-group max |err|
    Bg = np.abs(H).reshape(N, D // 64, 64).sum(-1) @ Eg.T
    sb = V * D * (bits + 0.5) / 8
    evaluate(f"{bits}-bit screen, Cauchy-Schwarz row bound", Zs, Bcs, sb + V * 4)
    evaluate(f"{bits}-bit screen, group-L1 bound", Zs, Bg, sb + V * D // 64)
    if bits >= 3:
        for T in (0.7, 1.0):
            evaluate(f"{bits}-bit screen, group-L1, sample T={T}", Zs + gumbel(T), Bg, sb + V * D // 64)
    del Zs, Bcs, Bg

P = np.linalg.svd(W[rng.choice(V, 20000, replace=False)], full_matrices=False)[2]
for r in (256, 512):
    Pr = P[:r]
    Wr = (W @ Pr.T) @ Pr
    rn = np.linalg.norm(W - Wr, axis=1)
    hperp = np.linalg.norm(H - (H @ Pr.T) @ Pr, axis=1)
    evaluate(f"rank-{r} screen (bf16), projected CS bound", H @ Wr.T, hperp[:, None] * rn[None, :], V * r * 2)


### scripts/sibling_union125.py
"""Round 32g: what does tree WIDTH cost in experts? (125B on BigMoE's streamed runtime, greedy.)
For a few prompts, generate G tokens greedily; at branch points t, take the model's top-4 next-token candidates at
position t (siblings: same context, different token) and record each sibling's top-10 routing per layer (a hook on the MoE
block computes the router on the last row itself, so any expert path works). Compare per layer:
  siblings  union of the 4 candidates' experts at one position (what 4-wide tree nodes cost)
  chain     union of 4 consecutive committed tokens ending at t (what a depth-4 chain costs; trace value ~26.9)
  single    10
If siblings' union is far below the chain's, wide trees are byte-cheap and tokens/pass can grow at little SSD cost.
Each probe is a cache-free forward of prompt + prefix (+ candidate), ~150-300 tokens.
usage: sibling_union125.py [FRAC] [G] [NPOS]   (GPU, BigMoE-size memory: exclusive daytime window, ~3-5 min)"""
import json, os, sys
for k, v in (("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off"), ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "0"), ("MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "0"),
             ("MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "0"), ("MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX", "0")):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, v)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
import mtplx.models.qwen4_exp as Q

frac = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.25
G = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 48
NPOS = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 6
rt, st = load_streamed(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare"), round(frac * 512))
model, tok = rt.model, rt.tokenizer
L0 = lambda o: o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o
layers = model.language_model.model.layers if hasattr(model, "language_model") else model.model.layers
LIDX = {id(l.mlp): i for i, l in enumerate(layers) if hasattr(l, "mlp")}
REC = {}                                                   # layer -> ids [rows, 10] of the last NR rows of the call
NR = 4
_orig = Q.SparseMoeBlock.__call__


def hook(self, x):
    L = LIDX.get(id(self))
    if L is not None and x.ndim >= 2:
        xs = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1])[-NR:]
        g = self.gate(xs)
        REC[L] = np.array(mx.argpartition(g, kth=-10, axis=-1)[..., -10:])
    return _orig(self, x)


Q.SparseMoeBlock.__call__ = hook
probs = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))]
PROMPTS = {"code": open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
           "math": "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + probs[0]["question"],
           "chat": "Explain to a curious teenager why the sky is blue, in a few short paragraphs."}


def fwd(ids):
    REC.clear()
    lg = L0(model(mx.array([ids]), cache=model.make_cache()))[0, -1].astype(mx.float32)   # fresh cache per probe
    mx.eval(lg)
    return lg, {L: v.copy() for L, v in REC.items()}


out = {}
for name, text in PROMPTS.items():
    ids = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
    gen = []
    cache = model.make_cache()
    lg = L0(model(mx.array([ids]), cache=cache))[0, -1]
    for _ in range(G):                                     # greedy continuation
        y = int(mx.argmax(lg).item())
        gen.append(y)
        lg = L0(model(mx.array([[y]]), cache=cache))[0, -1]
    del cache
    rows = []
    for t in np.linspace(8, G - 1, NPOS).astype(int):
        base = ids + gen[:t]
        lg, rec_chain = fwd(base)                          # last 4 rows = 4 consecutive committed tokens ending at t-1
        cands = np.argsort(-np.array(lg))[:4].tolist()
        sib = []
        for c in cands:
            _, r = fwd(base + [c])
            sib.append(r)
        su = [len(np.unique(np.concatenate([s[L][-1] for s in sib]))) for L in sorted(sib[0])]
        cu = [len(np.unique(rec_chain[L].reshape(-1))) for L in sorted(rec_chain)]
        p = np.exp(np.array(lg) - np.array(lg).max()); p /= p.sum()
        rows.append(dict(t=int(t), sib_union=float(np.mean(su)), chain_union=float(np.mean(cu)),
                         cand_p=[float(p[c]) for c in cands], greedy_is_top=bool(cands[0] == gen[t])))
        print(f"{name} t={t}: siblings union {np.mean(su):5.1f} | chain-4 union {np.mean(cu):5.1f} | cand p "
              f"{' '.join(f'{p[c]:.2f}' for c in cands)}", flush=True)
    out[name] = rows
allr = [r for v in out.values() for r in v]
print(f"ALL: siblings union {np.mean([r['sib_union'] for r in allr]):.1f} vs chain-4 union "
      f"{np.mean([r['chain_union'] for r in allr]):.1f} experts/layer (single token 10)", flush=True)
json.dump(out, open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/sibling_union125.json"), "w"), indent=1)


### scripts/sim125.py
"""Expert-streaming policies on REAL 125B verify passes (BigMoE's oracle recording: per (pass, layer) the union of routed experts;
48 layers x 512 experts, spec125 k=3), with the 35B proxy traces (S=3 unions) as a cross-check. 50 % resident per layer.
Timing per layer: ATTN, router, stall until the needed experts land (+ gate), MoE. SSD: demand reads first (no queueing), prefetch
reads FIFO; n reads issued together land at t + 0.2 + 0.25 n ms (measured). Look-ahead (BigMoE v1) is emulated from the true next
layer: each true expert kept with probability R_LA (125B: 0.75 @12), plus false positives up to B per pass. Runahead: when a stall
lasts > RA ms, L+1's true needs are prefetched RA ms into the stall. Policies on top:
  insert : where prefetched experts enter the cache: 'lru_end' (BigMoE victim prefetch) or 'srrip' (RRIP: re-reference interval 2 of 3)
  repl   : cache replacement: 'lru' or 'srrip' (scan-resistant, demand inserted at RRPV 2, promoted to 0 on hit)
  fdp    : feedback-directed throttling: per-layer prefetch cap adapted from measured accuracy (used/issued) over a window
  crit   : criticality order: prefetch reads for the nearest layer first (vs issue order)
Usage: sim125.py   (CPU only)"""
import os, struct
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np

LAT, PER, GATE = 0.2, 0.25, 0.07
LAYER = 38.0 / 48
ATTN = 0.5 * LAYER
RA = 0.6


def load125(path=os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/oracle125.bin")):
    rows = {}
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        while True:
            h = f.read(16)
            if len(h) < 16:
                break
            k, n = struct.unpack("<qq", h)
            rows[k] = list(struct.unpack(f"<{n}i", f.read(4 * n)))
    seqs = sorted({k // 1024 for k in rows})
    NL = max(k % 1024 for k in rows) + 1
    passes = [[rows.get(s * 1024 + L, []) for L in range(NL)] for s in seqs]
    return passes, NL, 512


def load35(S=3, maxpass=600):
    z = dict(np.load(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/traces/code.npz")))
    NL = 40
    T = len(z["doc"])
    acts = [z[f"L{L}_act"][:, :8] for L in range(NL)]
    passes = [[list(dict.fromkeys(acts[L][t:t + S].reshape(-1).tolist())) for L in range(NL)]
              for t in range(0, min(T - S, S * maxpass), S)]
    return passes, NL, 256


class Cache:
    """per-layer cache: LRU or SRRIP (2-bit RRPV)."""

    def __init__(self, C, repl):
        self.C, self.repl = C, repl
        self.d = OrderedDict()          # expert -> rrpv (srrip) ; order = recency (lru)

    def __contains__(self, e):
        return e in self.d

    def hit(self, e):
        if self.repl == "lru":
            self.d.move_to_end(e)
        else:
            self.d[e] = 0

    def victim(self):
        if self.repl == "lru":
            return next(iter(self.d))
        while True:
            for e, v in self.d.items():
                if v >= 3:
                    return e
            for e in self.d:
                self.d[e] += 1

    def insert(self, e, how):
        while len(self.d) >= self.C:
            del self.d[self.victim()]
        if self.repl == "lru":
            self.d[e] = 0
            if how == "low":
                self.d.move_to_end(e, last=False)
        else:
            self.d[e] = {"high": 0, "mid": 2, "low": 3}[how]


def run(passes, NL, E, *, frac=0.5, lookahead=True, B=12, R_LA=0.75, runahead=False, insert="lru_end", repl="lru",
        fdp=False, crit=False, warm=None, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    C = int(frac * E)
    caches = [Cache(C, repl) for _ in range(NL)]
    cap = [B] * NL
    used_w, iss_w = [1.0] * NL, [1.0] * NL
    warm = len(passes) // 4 if warm is None else warm
    stl = ms = 0.0
    n = 0
    for p, need in enumerate(passes):
        now, ssd = 0.0, 0.0
        land = [dict() for _ in range(NL)]
        issued = [set() for _ in range(NL)]
        pend = []                                     # (layer, expert) prefetches not yet started (crit ordering)

        def prefetch(L, es, t):
            nonlocal ssd
            es = [e for e in es if e not in caches[L] and e not in land[L]][:cap[L]]
            if crit:                                  # nearer layers first: start this batch before any queued farther-layer reads
                ssd = max(t, min(ssd, t + 0.0))
            start = max(t, ssd)
            for i, e in enumerate(es):
                land[L][e] = start + LAT + PER * (i + 1)
                caches[L].insert(e, "low" if insert == "lru_end" else "mid")
                issued[L].add(e)
            if es:
                ssd = start + PER * len(es)

        for L in range(NL):
            now += ATTN
            if lookahead and L + 1 < NL:
                tru = need[L + 1]
                pred = [e for e in tru if rng.random() < R_LA]
                while len(pred) < min(B * 2, len(tru) + B):    # false positives (union of per-row top-B over ~3 rows)
                    pred.append(int(rng.integers(E)))
                prefetch(L + 1, list(dict.fromkeys(pred)), now)
            miss = [e for e in need[L] if e not in caches[L] and e not in land[L]]
            for i, e in enumerate(miss):              # demand reads: first in line
                land[L][e] = now + LAT + PER * (i + 1)
            ready = max([land[L].get(e, 0.0) for e in need[L]] + [now])
            stall = ready - now
            if stall > 0:
                stall += GATE
                if p >= warm:
                    stl += 1
                    ms += stall
                if runahead and L + 1 < NL and stall > RA:
                    prefetch(L + 1, need[L + 1], now + RA)
            now += max(stall, 0.0)
            for e in need[L]:
                if e in caches[L]:
                    caches[L].hit(e)
                else:
                    caches[L].insert(e, "high")
            if fdp and issued[L]:                     # feedback: accuracy of this layer's prefetches
                acc = len(issued[L] & set(need[L])) / len(issued[L])
                used_w[L] = 0.8 * used_w[L] + 0.2 * acc
                cap[L] = int(np.clip(round(B * 1.5 * used_w[L]), 2, 2 * B))
            now += LAYER - ATTN
        if p >= warm:
            n += 1
    sc = 48 / NL
    return stl / n * sc, ms / n * sc


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for name, (passes, NL, E) in (("125B real verify passes", load125()), ("35B proxy (code, S=3)", load35())):
        print(f"{name}: {len(passes)} passes, {NL} layers x {E} experts, 50 % resident (stall per pass, x48/{NL})")
        base = dict(passes=passes, NL=NL, E=E)
        cases = [("no prefetch", dict(lookahead=False)),
                 ("look-ahead (v1, LRU-end)", dict()),
                 ("+ runahead", dict(runahead=True)),
                 ("+ runahead + FDP throttle", dict(runahead=True, fdp=True)),
                 ("+ runahead + criticality order", dict(runahead=True, crit=True)),
                 ("+ runahead, SRRIP insert (RRPV 2)", dict(runahead=True, insert="srrip")),
                 ("+ runahead, SRRIP cache", dict(runahead=True, insert="srrip", repl="srrip")),
                 ("runahead only (no look-ahead)", dict(lookahead=False, runahead=True))]
        for tag, kw in cases:
            r = [run(**base, seed=s, **kw) for s in range(3)]
            sl = np.mean([x[0] for x in r]); msv = np.mean([x[1] for x in r])
            print(f"  {tag:36s}: stalled layers {sl:5.1f}, stall {msv:5.1f} ms/pass", flush=True)


### scripts/sim_runahead_depth.py
"""Can runahead make SSD streaming nearly free? Runahead only runs while a layer stalls, so as it removes stalls it also removes
its own trigger. This models the equilibrium on the MoEPrefetch proxy traces (35B: 40 layers x 256 experts, top-8; S=3 verify
unions; per-layer LRU at 55 %; prefetched experts inserted at the LRU end), with the measured SSD curve and per-layer timing.
Per layer L (in order): attention, router, stall if a needed expert is neither resident nor landed, MoE.
  look-ahead (always on, BigMoE v1): at L's router, issue L+1's top-B prediction (pmid1 from the traces, union over rows).
  runahead depth d (only when L stalls): RA ms into the stall, issue the *exact* needs of L+1..L+d (measured recall 0.96-1.0).
SSD: n reads issued together land at t + 0.2 + 0.25 n ms (measured: 1/2/3/4 experts -> 0.45/0.71/0.99/1.17 ms), FIFO across batches.
Stall time = until the last needed expert lands, + 0.07 ms gate. Prints stalled layers and stall ms per pass (x48/40 to the 125B).
Usage: sim_runahead_depth.py   (CPU only)"""
import os
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np

TR = os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/traces")
NL, K, E, S = 40, 8, 256, 3
zs = [dict(np.load(os.path.join(TR, f"{s}.npz"))) for s in ("chat", "code", "realcode")]
C = int(0.55 * E)
LAYER = 38.0 / 48            # ms per layer of compute in an S=3 verify pass (BigMoE: 38 ms)
ATTN = 0.5 * LAYER           # share before the router
RA = 0.6                     # ms into a stall until runahead knows the next layer's routing (+0.8 per further layer)
GATE, LAT, PER = 0.07, 0.2, 0.25
PCAP = 6                     # at most this many prefetch reads per layer (BigMoE: bounded by slots)


def run(depth, B=12, lookahead=True, passes=300):
    stl, ms, n = 0, 0.0, 0
    for z in zs:
        T = len(z["doc"])
        acts = [z[f"L{L}_act"][:, :K] for L in range(NL)]
        p1 = [z[f"L{L}_pmid1"][:, :B] for L in range(NL - 1)]
        lru = [OrderedDict() for _ in range(NL)]
        for t0 in range(200, min(T - S, 200 + S * passes), S):
            n += 1
            now, ssd = 0.0, 0.0
            land = [dict() for _ in range(NL)]        # expert -> landing time, this pass

            def issue(L, es, t, demand=False, cap=None):
                nonlocal ssd
                es = [e for e in es if e not in lru[L] and e not in land[L]]
                if cap is not None:
                    es = es[:cap]
                if demand:                            # demand reads go first (software priority): no queueing
                    for i, e in enumerate(es):
                        land[L][e] = t + LAT + PER * (i + 1)
                    return
                start = max(t, ssd)                   # prefetches: FIFO among themselves
                for i, e in enumerate(es):
                    land[L][e] = start + LAT + PER * (i + 1)
                if es:
                    ssd = start + PER * len(es)
                for e in es:                          # low-priority insert: evict the current LRU victim, take its place
                    while len(lru[L]) >= C:
                        lru[L].popitem(last=False)
                    lru[L][e] = 1
                    lru[L].move_to_end(e, last=False)
            need = [list(dict.fromkeys(acts[L][t0:t0 + S].reshape(-1).tolist())) for L in range(NL)]
            for L in range(NL):
                now += ATTN
                if lookahead and L + 1 < NL:
                    issue(L + 1, list(dict.fromkeys(p1[L][t0:t0 + S].reshape(-1).tolist())), now, cap=PCAP)
                miss = [e for e in need[L] if e not in lru[L] and e not in land[L]]
                issue(L, miss, now, demand=True)
                ready = max([land[L].get(e, 0.0) for e in need[L]] + [now])
                stall = ready - now
                if stall > 0:
                    stall += GATE
                    stl += 1
                    ms += stall
                    for d in range(1, depth + 1):     # runahead during the stall, if it lasts long enough
                        t_known = now + RA + 0.8 * (d - 1)
                        if L + d < NL and t_known < now + stall:
                            issue(L + d, need[L + d], t_known, cap=PCAP)
                now += max(stall, 0.0)
                for e in need[L]:                     # true use: promote to MRU
                    lru[L][e] = 1
                    lru[L].move_to_end(e, last=True)
                while len(lru[L]) > C:
                    lru[L].popitem(last=False)
                now += LAYER - ATTN
    sc = 48 / NL
    return stl / n * sc, ms / n * sc


print("S=3 verify pass on proxy traces, 55 % resident; per pass, scaled to 48 layers (BigMoE measured: none 36.5 stalled / "
      "40.5 ms, look-ahead B=12 27 stalled / 26 ms)")
for name, kw in (("no prefetch", dict(depth=0, lookahead=False)), ("look-ahead B=12", dict(depth=0)),
                 ("+ runahead d=1", dict(depth=1)), ("+ runahead d=2", dict(depth=2)), ("+ runahead d=3", dict(depth=3)),
                 ("runahead d=2 only", dict(depth=2, lookahead=False))):
    sl, ms = run(**kw)
    print(f"  {name:18s}: stalled layers {sl:5.1f}, stall {ms:5.1f} ms/pass", flush=True)


### scripts/sim_verify.py
"""Event-driven model of SSD streaming in one S=3 verify pass (BigMoE 125B), on the MoEPrefetch proxy traces (35B routing:
40 layers x 256 experts, top-8; results scaled x48/40 layers). Per-layer LRU at 55 % resident plus a spare pool for prefetches.
Timeline per layer L: attention/GDN, router (needed experts known), wait for missing experts (stall), MoE compute.
SSD: FIFO, each read = LAT + bytes/BW, at most QD reads in flight. Policies for *when* the CPU learns which experts to read:
  none      : at L's router only (demand)
  v1(B)     : + layer L+1's look-ahead prediction (pmid1, top-B per row, union) issued at L's router (BigMoE prefetch v1)
  v1+d2(B)  : + layer L+2's prediction (pmid2) issued at L's router too
  runahead  : v1, plus during a stall at L the GPU runs ahead through L+1's attention with substitute experts and learns
              L+1's *exact* routing RA ms after the stall starts (if the stall lasts that long); then reads the rest
  oracle    : every layer's misses known at pass start (upper bound for any early-routing scheme)
Calibrate LAT/BW on BigMoE's measured points (none: 40.5 ms streaming, 36.5 stalled layers; v1 B=12: 26 ms, 27 stalled)."""
import os, sys
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np

TR = os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/traces")
NL, K, E, S = 40, 8, 256, 3
SETS = ["chat", "code", "realcode"]
zs = [dict(np.load(os.path.join(TR, f"{s}.npz"))) for s in SETS]
C = int(0.55 * E)
COMPUTE = 38.0 / 48          # ms of GPU work per layer in an S=3 verify pass (BigMoE: 38 ms compute)
ATTN = 0.5 * COMPUTE         # share before the router (attention/GDN + HC)
GATE = 0.07                  # ms gate round trip on a stalled layer
SZ = 2.77                    # MB per 125B expert


def run(policy, B=12, LAT=0.35, BW=13.0, RA=None, spare=12, maxpass=400):
    RA = ATTN if RA is None else RA
    st_ms, st_layers, passes, misses = 0.0, 0, 0, 0
    for z in zs:
        T = len(z["doc"])
        acts = [z[f"L{L}_act"][:, :K] for L in range(NL)]
        p1 = [z[f"L{L}_pmid1"][:, :B] for L in range(NL - 1)]
        p2 = [z[f"L{L}_pmid2"][:, :B] for L in range(NL - 2)]
        main = [OrderedDict() for _ in range(NL)]
        for t0 in range(200, min(T - S, 200 + S * maxpass), S):     # skip the cold start
            passes += 1
            now, ssd_free = 0.0, 0.0
            arrive = [dict() for _ in range(NL)]                   # expert -> arrival time (in flight or landed this pass)

            def issue(L, experts, t):
                nonlocal ssd_free
                for e in experts:
                    if e in main[L] or e in arrive[L]:
                        continue
                    start = max(t, ssd_free - LAT)                  # FIFO: bandwidth shared, latency overlaps
                    done = max(t + LAT, ssd_free) + SZ / BW
                    ssd_free = done
                    arrive[L][e] = done
            need = [list(dict.fromkeys(acts[L][t0:t0 + S].reshape(-1).tolist())) for L in range(NL)]
            if policy == "oracle":
                for L in range(NL):
                    issue(L, need[L], 0.0)
            for L in range(NL):
                now += ATTN
                if policy in ("v1", "v1+d2", "runahead") and L + 1 < NL:     # at most `spare` prefetch reads per layer
                    pred = list(dict.fromkeys(p1[L][t0:t0 + S].reshape(-1).tolist()))
                    issue(L + 1, [e for e in pred if e not in main[L + 1] and e not in arrive[L + 1]][:spare], now)
                if policy == "v1+d2" and L + 2 < NL:
                    pred = list(dict.fromkeys(p2[L][t0:t0 + S].reshape(-1).tolist()))
                    issue(L + 2, [e for e in pred if e not in main[L + 2] and e not in arrive[L + 2]][:spare], now)
                miss = [e for e in need[L] if e not in main[L]]
                issue(L, miss, now)
                wait = max([arrive[L][e] for e in miss] + [now]) - now
                if miss and wait > 0:
                    wait += GATE
                    st_layers += 1
                    if policy == "runahead" and L + 1 < NL and wait > RA:
                        issue(L + 1, need[L + 1], now + RA)          # exact L+1 routing learned during the stall
                now += wait
                st_ms += wait
                misses += len(miss)
                for e in need[L]:                                    # LRU update / insert
                    main[L][e] = 1
                    main[L].move_to_end(e)
                while len(main[L]) > C:
                    main[L].popitem(last=False)
                now += COMPUTE - ATTN
    sc = 48 / NL
    return st_ms / passes * sc, st_layers / passes * sc, misses / passes * sc * (10 / 8)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    LAT = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.35
    BW = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 13.0
    print(f"S=3 verify pass, 55 % resident, LAT={LAT} ms, BW={BW} GB/s, compute {COMPUTE*48:.0f} ms/pass "
          f"(BigMoE measured: none 40.5 ms / 36.5 stalled; v1 B=12: 26 ms / 27 stalled)")
    for pol, B in (("none", 12), ("v1", 12), ("v1", 16), ("v1+d2", 12), ("v1+d2", 16), ("runahead", 12),
                   ("runahead", 16), ("oracle", 12)):
        ms, sl, mi = run(pol, B, LAT, BW)
        print(f"  {pol:9s} B={B:>2}: streaming {ms:5.1f} ms/pass, stalled layers {sl:4.1f}, demand misses {mi:5.1f}", flush=True)


### scripts/skinny.py
"""Skinny-M quantized matmul on Metal: read each weight once for M = 1..16 rows.
Compares MLX's quantized_matmul with a custom kernel that dequantizes once and
multiplies against all M rows. Usage: skinny.py [check|bench] [bits] [IN] [OUT] [NMAT]"""
import sys, time
import mlx.core as mx

mode = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "check"
BITS = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 8
IN = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 5120
OUT = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 17408
NMAT = int(sys.argv[5]) if len(sys.argv) > 5 else 24
G = 64
ROWS_PER_SG, SGS = 4, 8          # 4 output rows per simdgroup, 8 simdgroups -> 32 rows per threadgroup

SRC = """
    uint tid = thread_position_in_threadgroup.x;
    uint sg = tid / 32, lane = tid % 32;
    uint row0 = threadgroup_position_in_grid.x * (R * SG) + sg * R;
    const uint KW = IN * BITS / 32;
    const uint NG = IN / 64;
    float acc[R][M];
    for (int r = 0; r < R; r++) for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) acc[r][m] = 0.0f;
    for (uint k = lane * 8; k < IN; k += 256) {
        uint g = k / 64;
        bool first = (k % 64) == 0;
        float4 xa[M], xb[M];
        for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) {
            const device float4* xp = (const device float4*)(x + m * IN + k);
            xa[m] = xp[0]; xb[m] = xp[1];
        }
        for (int r = 0; r < R; r++) {
            uint row = row0 + r;
            float4 qa, qb;
            if (BITS == 8) {
                const device uint* wp = w + row * KW + k / 4;
                uint w0 = wp[0], w1 = wp[1];
                qa = float4(w0 & 0xff, (w0 >> 8) & 0xff, (w0 >> 16) & 0xff, w0 >> 24);
                qb = float4(w1 & 0xff, (w1 >> 8) & 0xff, (w1 >> 16) & 0xff, w1 >> 24);
            } else {
                uint w0 = w[row * KW + k / 8];
                qa = float4(w0 & 0xf, (w0 >> 4) & 0xf, (w0 >> 8) & 0xf, (w0 >> 12) & 0xf);
                qb = float4((w0 >> 16) & 0xf, (w0 >> 20) & 0xf, (w0 >> 24) & 0xf, w0 >> 28);
            }
            float sc = float(s[row * NG + g]);
            float bi = float(b[row * NG + g]);
            for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) {
                float d = dot(qa, xa[m]) + dot(qb, xb[m]);
                acc[r][m] += sc * d + (first ? bi * xs[m * NG + g] : 0.0f);
            }
        }
    }
    for (int r = 0; r < R; r++) for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) {
        float v = simd_sum(acc[r][m]);
        if (lane == 0) y[m * OUT + row0 + r] = v;
    }
"""
kern = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="skinny_qmv", input_names=["w", "s", "b", "x", "xs"],
                            output_names=["y"], source=SRC)


def skinny(w, s, b, x):
    M = x.shape[0]
    xf = x.astype(mx.float32)
    xs = xf.reshape(M, IN // G, G).sum(-1)
    return kern(inputs=[w, s, b, xf, xs],
                template=[("M", M), ("IN", IN), ("OUT", OUT), ("BITS", BITS), ("R", ROWS_PER_SG), ("SG", SGS)],
                grid=(OUT // (ROWS_PER_SG * SGS) * 256, 1, 1), threadgroup=(256, 1, 1),
                output_shapes=[(M, OUT)], output_dtypes=[mx.float32])[0]


mats = []
for i in range(NMAT if mode == "bench" else 1):
    W = (mx.random.normal((OUT, IN)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    wq, sc, bi = mx.quantize(W, group_size=G, bits=BITS)
    mx.eval(wq, sc, bi)
    mats.append((wq, sc, bi))


def mlx_mm(p, x):
    return mx.quantized_matmul(x, *p, transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=BITS)


if mode == "check":
    for M in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 16):
        x = mx.random.normal((M, IN)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
        ref = mlx_mm(mats[0], x).astype(mx.float32)
        got = skinny(*mats[0], x)
        err = (mx.abs(ref - got).max() / mx.abs(ref).max()).item()
        print(f"M={M} max rel err {err:.2e}")
    sys.exit()

Ms = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8, 12, 16)


def bench(fn, M, reps=15):
    x = mx.random.normal((M, IN)).astype(mx.bfloat16)

    def run():
        mx.eval([fn(p, x) for p in mats])
    for _ in range(3):
        run()
    ts = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        t = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
    return sorted(ts)[reps // 2]


gb = sum(p[0].nbytes + p[1].nbytes + p[2].nbytes for p in mats) / 1e9
res = {M: (bench(mlx_mm, M), bench(lambda p, x: skinny(*p, x), M)) for M in Ms}
a1, c1 = res[1]
print(f"{BITS}-bit {IN}->{OUT} x{NMAT} ({gb:.2f} GB/pass)  MLX M1 {a1*1e3:.1f} ms = {gb/a1:.0f} GB/s, "
      f"skinny M1 {c1*1e3:.1f} ms = {gb/c1:.0f} GB/s")
print("M    " + " ".join(f"{M:>6}" for M in Ms))
print("MLX  " + " ".join(f"{res[M][0]/a1:5.2f}x" for M in Ms))
print("skin " + " ".join(f"{res[M][1]/a1:5.2f}x" for M in Ms) + "   (both relative to MLX M=1)")


### scripts/slice_recall35.py
"""Cheap continuous runahead slice: recall of layer T's true top-8 at distance d = 2, 3 (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 4-bit, real weights,
