~900 tokens of real text, teacher-forced; same harness as runahead_recall.py). Seed = layer s = T-d's pre-MoE residual (what
router reuse at distance d uses). The slice runs, for j = s .. T-1: x = hm + MoEapprox_j(hm), hm = attn_{j+1}(x); then T's router.
Variants (attention weights x MoE approximation):
  attention: exact (the model's 4-bit) | 2bit (projections requantized to 2-bit g64) | lr<r> (rank-r SVD, factors 4-bit g64)
  MoE approx: shared (shared expert only) | dflt (shared + per-layer mean of the routed output, calibrated on tokens < WARM)
              | resid (routed MoE from the experts resident under LRU at 55 %, the 0.999 variant)
Baseline: router reuse (T's router on hmid[s]). Pessimistic like round 8: the slice perturbs all tokens' GDN/KV history.
Prints recall@8/12/16 averaged over targets and tokens >= WARM, and the slice's bytes per predicted layer.
usage: slice_recall35.py [variants]   (GPU, ~22 GB: exclusive window)"""
import os, sys, time
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
import mlx.nn as nn
from mlx_lm import load
from mlx_lm.models.qwen3_5 import create_attention_mask, create_ssm_mask

MODEL = os.path.expanduser("~/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit")
model, tok = load(MODEL)
tm = model.language_model.model
layers = tm.layers
NL = len(layers)
E = layers[0].mlp.num_experts
K = layers[0].mlp.top_k
C = int(0.55 * E)
WARM = 300
text = open(os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode/FINDINGS.md")).read()[:6000] + "\n\n" + \
       open(os.path.expanduser("~/SpecDecode/mtp.py")).read()[:4000]
ids = mx.array(tok.encode(text)[:900])[None]
T = ids.shape[1]
h = tm.embed_tokens(ids)
fa = create_attention_mask(h, None)
ssm = create_ssm_mask(h, None)


def attn_mod(layer):
    return layer.linear_attn if layer.is_linear else layer.self_attn


def mixer(layer, x, mod=None):
    m = ssm if layer.is_linear else fa
    f = mod if mod is not None else attn_mod(layer)
    return x + f(layer.input_layernorm(x), m, None)


def gates_of(layer, hmid):
    return mx.softmax(layer.mlp.gate(layer.post_attention_layernorm(hmid)), axis=-1, precise=True)


def shared_of(layer, hm):
    xn = layer.post_attention_layernorm(hm)
    return mx.sigmoid(layer.mlp.shared_expert_gate(xn)) * layer.mlp.shared_expert(xn)


def routed_with(layer, hm, inds_np, gates):
    xn = layer.post_attention_layernorm(hm)
    inds = mx.array(inds_np[None].astype(np.int32))
    sc = mx.take_along_axis(gates, inds, axis=-1)
    sc = sc / sc.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    return (layer.mlp.switch_mlp(xn, inds) * sc[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)


HEADW = {}                                         # layer -> per value-head contribution weight (calibrated, tokens < WARM)
# exact pass
x = h
hmid, top16, gts, dflt = [], [], [], []
for L, layer in enumerate(layers):
    hm = mixer(layer, x)
    if layer.is_linear:
        mod = layer.linear_attn
        cap = {}
        orig_out = mod.out_proj
        def _cap(u, _o=orig_out, _c=cap): _c["u"] = u; return _o(u)
        mod.out_proj = _cap
        mod(layer.input_layernorm(x[:, :WARM]), ssm[..., :WARM] if ssm is not None else None, None)
        mod.out_proj = orig_out
        u = cap["u"][0].astype(mx.float32)                                            # [WARM, Hv*Dv]
        Hv = mod.num_v_heads; Dv = u.shape[-1] // Hv
        wo = mx.dequantize(orig_out.weight, orig_out.scales, orig_out.biases, group_size=orig_out.group_size, bits=orig_out.bits).astype(mx.float32)
        HEADW[L] = np.array([float(mx.abs(u[:, i * Dv:(i + 1) * Dv] @ wo[:, i * Dv:(i + 1) * Dv].T).mean()) for i in range(Hv)])
    g = gates_of(layer, hm)
    sh = shared_of(layer, hm)
    out = layer.mlp(layer.post_attention_layernorm(hm))
    dflt.append((out - sh)[:, :WARM].astype(mx.float32).mean(axis=1, keepdims=True).astype(hm.dtype))   # mean routed part
    x = hm + out
    mx.eval(hm, g, x, dflt[-1])
    hmid.append(hm); gts.append(g)
    top16.append(np.argsort(-np.array(g[0].astype(mx.float32)), axis=-1)[:, :16])
resident = []
for L in range(NL):
    lru, res = OrderedDict(), []
    for t in range(T):
        res.append(set(lru))
        for e in top16[L][t, :K]:
            lru[int(e)] = 1; lru.move_to_end(int(e))
        while len(lru) > C:
            lru.popitem(last=False)
    resident.append(res)


def topres(L, hm, pred_gates, m):
    """routed output from only the m best resident experts by predicted gate weight (not renormalised: their own weights)."""
    p = np.argsort(-np.array(pred_gates[0].astype(mx.float32)), axis=-1)
    inds = np.zeros((T, m), np.int64)
    for t in range(T):
        r = resident[L][t]
        ch = [int(e) for e in p[t, :24] if int(e) in r][:m]
        inds[t] = (ch + [int(e) for e in p[t, :m] if int(e) not in ch])[:m]
    xn = layers[L].post_attention_layernorm(hm)
    ii = mx.array(inds[None].astype(np.int32))
    sc = mx.take_along_axis(pred_gates, ii, axis=-1)
    return (layers[L].mlp.switch_mlp(xn, ii) * sc[..., None]).sum(axis=-2)


def resident_ids(L, pred_gates):
    p = np.argsort(-np.array(pred_gates[0].astype(mx.float32)), axis=-1)
    out = np.zeros((T, K), np.int64)
    for t in range(T):
        r = resident[L][t]
        ch = [int(e) for e in p[t, :24] if int(e) in r][:K]
        out[t] = (ch + [int(e) for e in p[t, :K] if int(e) not in ch])[:K]
    return out


# ---- shadow attention modules
def requant(ql, bits):
    w = mx.dequantize(ql.weight, ql.scales, ql.biases, group_size=ql.group_size, bits=ql.bits)
    q = nn.QuantizedLinear(w.shape[1], w.shape[0], bias=False, group_size=64, bits=bits)
    q.weight, q.scales, q.biases = mx.quantize(w, group_size=64, bits=bits)
    return q, q.weight.nbytes + q.scales.nbytes + q.biases.nbytes


class LowRank(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, a, b):
        super().__init__()
        self.a, self.b = a, b                              # x @ A^T -> [r], then @ B^T -> [out]

    def __call__(self, x):
        return self.b(self.a(x))


def lowrank(ql, r):
    w = np.array(mx.dequantize(ql.weight, ql.scales, ql.biases, group_size=ql.group_size, bits=ql.bits).astype(mx.float32))
    u, s, vt = np.linalg.svd(w, full_matrices=False)
    A = mx.array((vt[:r] * np.sqrt(s[:r])[:, None]).astype(np.float32)).astype(mx.bfloat16)        # [r, in]
    B = mx.array((u[:, :r] * np.sqrt(s[:r])[None]).astype(np.float32)).astype(mx.bfloat16)        # [out, r]
    qa = nn.QuantizedLinear(A.shape[1], r, bias=False, group_size=64, bits=4); qa.weight, qa.scales, qa.biases = mx.quantize(A, 64, 4)
    qb = nn.QuantizedLinear(r, B.shape[0], bias=False, group_size=64, bits=4); qb.weight, qb.scales, qb.biases = mx.quantize(B, 64, 4)
    nb = sum(a.nbytes for a in (qa.weight, qa.scales, qa.biases, qb.weight, qb.scales, qb.biases))
    return LowRank(qa, qb), nb


PROJ = ("in_proj_qkvz", "in_proj_ba", "out_proj", "in_proj_qkv", "in_proj_z", "in_proj_b", "in_proj_a",
        "q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj")




def prune_heads(mod, L, frac):
    """zero the out_proj input columns of the weakest value heads (by mean |contribution| on calibration tokens)."""
    ql = mod.out_proj
    w = mx.dequantize(ql.weight, ql.scales, ql.biases, group_size=ql.group_size, bits=ql.bits)     # [d, Hv*Dv]
    Hv = mod.num_v_heads; Dv = w.shape[1] // Hv
    score = HEADW[L]
    drop = np.argsort(score)[: int(frac * Hv)]
    mask = np.ones(w.shape[1], np.float32)
    for hh in drop: mask[hh * Dv:(hh + 1) * Dv] = 0
    return w * mx.array(mask)[None].astype(w.dtype)


def shadow(mod, kind, L=None):
    """a shallow clone of an attention module with its quantized projections replaced; returns (module, bytes of projections)."""
    import copy
    m2 = copy.copy(mod)
    nb = 0
    for name in PROJ:
        ql = getattr(mod, name, None)
        if not isinstance(ql, nn.QuantizedLinear):
            continue
        if kind == "exact":
            nb += ql.weight.nbytes + ql.scales.nbytes + ql.biases.nbytes
            continue
        if kind.startswith("gdn2bit") and not hasattr(mod, "num_v_heads"):
            nb += ql.weight.nbytes + ql.scales.nbytes + ql.biases.nbytes      # full-attention layer: exact
            continue
        if kind == "gdn2bit_h50" and name == "out_proj":
            w = prune_heads(mod, L, 0.5)
            q = nn.QuantizedLinear(w.shape[1], w.shape[0], bias=False, group_size=64, bits=2)
            q.weight, q.scales, q.biases = mx.quantize(w, group_size=64, bits=2)
            b = (q.weight.nbytes + q.scales.nbytes + q.biases.nbytes) // 2           # pruned heads' columns skipped
        elif kind == "gdn2bit_h50" and name in ("in_proj_qkvz", "in_proj_qkv", "in_proj_z"):
            q, b = requant(ql, 2); b = int(b * 0.67)                                  # v and z rows of pruned heads skipped
        elif kind in ("2bit", "gdn2bit", "gdn2bit_h50"):
            q, b = requant(ql, 2)
        else:
            r = int(kind[2:])
            q, b = (lowrank(ql, r) if min(ql.weight.shape[0], ql.weight.shape[1] * 32 // ql.bits) > 2 * r else requant(ql, 4))
        object.__setattr__(m2, name, q) if False else setattr(m2, name, q)
        nb += b
    return m2, nb


variants = sys.argv[1].split(",") if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ["exact:shared", "exact:dflt", "exact:resid", "2bit:dflt", "lr256:dflt", "lr128:dflt"]
kinds = sorted({v.split(":")[0] for v in variants})
shadows, sbytes = {}, {}
for kd in kinds:
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    shadows[kd] = []; tot = 0
    for Li, layer in enumerate(layers):
        m2, nb = shadow(attn_mod(layer), kd, Li)
        mx.eval(m2.parameters())
        shadows[kd].append(m2); tot += nb
    sbytes[kd] = tot / NL
    print(f"shadow {kd}: attention projections {sbytes[kd]/1e6:.2f} MB/layer ({time.perf_counter()-t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)


def recall(pg, true8, rows):
    p = np.argsort(-np.array(pg[0].astype(mx.float32)), axis=-1)
    return {B: float(np.mean([len(set(p[t, :B]) & set(true8[t])) / K for t in rows])) for B in (8, 12, 16)}


rows = list(range(WARM, T))
acc = {}
for d in (2, 3):
    for Tg in range(d, NL):
        s = Tg - d
        true8 = top16[Tg][:, :K]
        g_rr = gates_of(layers[Tg], hmid[s]); mx.eval(g_rr)
        for B, v in recall(g_rr, true8, rows).items(): acc.setdefault(("router-reuse", d), {}).setdefault(B, []).append(v)
        for var in variants:
            kd, ma = var.split(":")
            hm, g_prev = hmid[s], gts[s]
            for j in range(s, Tg):
                if ma == "shared":
                    xo = hm + shared_of(layers[j], hm)
                elif ma == "dflt":
                    xo = hm + shared_of(layers[j], hm) + dflt[j]
                elif ma in ("res1", "res2"):
                    xo = hm + shared_of(layers[j], hm) + topres(j, hm, g_prev, int(ma[3]))
                else:
                    xo = hm + shared_of(layers[j], hm) + routed_with(layers[j], hm, resident_ids(j, g_prev), g_prev)
                hm = mixer(layers[j + 1], xo, shadows[kd][j + 1])
                g_prev = gates_of(layers[j + 1], hm)
                mx.eval(hm, g_prev)
            for B, v in recall(g_prev, true8, rows).items(): acc.setdefault((var, d), {}).setdefault(B, []).append(v)
        if Tg % 8 == 0:
            print(f"d={d} T={Tg}: router-reuse@12 {acc[('router-reuse', d)][12][-1]:.3f} " +
                  " ".join(f"{v}@12 {acc[(v, d)][12][-1]:.3f}" for v in variants), flush=True)
print(f"\n{T} tokens (recall over tokens >= {WARM}), mean over target layers; slice attention bytes per predicted layer:")
for d in (2, 3):
    for name in ["router-reuse"] + variants:
        r = acc[(name, d)]
        kd = name.split(":")[0]
        ma = name.split(":")[1] if ":" in name else ""
        ew = sum(a.nbytes for a in (layers[0].mlp.switch_mlp.gate_proj.weight, layers[0].mlp.switch_mlp.gate_proj.scales,
                                    layers[0].mlp.switch_mlp.gate_proj.biases)) * 3 / E
        moe_b = {"shared": 0, "dflt": 0, "res1": ew, "res2": 2 * ew, "resid": K * ew}.get(ma, 0)
        full = sbytes.get("exact", 18e6) + K * ew
        extra = (f"  per step: attn {sbytes[kd]/1e6:5.2f} + routed {moe_b/1e6:5.2f} MB = {(sbytes[kd] + moe_b)/full:.2f} of a layer (x {d} steps)"
                 if name != "router-reuse" else "")
        print(f"  d={d} {name:14s} " + "  ".join(f"@{B}: {np.mean(v):.3f}" for B, v in r.items()) + extra)


### scripts/splitk.py
"""Split-K decode matvec vs MLX on Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 125B shapes (M = 1 and 4 input rows).
Kernel: grid = row tiles x SPLITS threadgroups; each simdgroup does RSG rows over one K-chunk and writes a partial;
the last threadgroup to finish a row tile (device atomic counter, reset by itself) sums the partials in fixed order
s = 0..SPLITS-1. Deterministic, and batch-invariant by construction (each input row uses the same order at any M).
Measured as a dependent chain of NC distinct matrices (output feeds the next input, same glue for both variants),
so per-op ramp/tail costs are included as in real decode. Prints µs per op, effective GB/s, and best SPLITS.
Usage: splitk.py [M]   (GPU, quiet lock)"""
import sys, time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

M = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 1
NC = 16

SRC = """
    const uint tg = threadgroup_position_in_grid.x, t = thread_position_in_threadgroup.x;
    const uint sg = t / 32, lane = t % 32;
    const uint RT = 8 * RSG;
    const uint ntile = (R + RT - 1) / RT;
    const uint tile = tg % ntile, split = tg / ntile;
    const uint KC = K / SPLITS, k0 = split * KC;
    const uint NG = K / 64;
    for (uint rr = 0; rr < RSG; ++rr) {
        const uint row = tile * RT + sg * RSG + rr;
        if (row >= R) break;
        float acc[M];
        for (uint m = 0; m < M; ++m) acc[m] = 0.0f;
if (BITS == 16) {
        device const ushort4* wr = (device const ushort4*)(w + (size_t)row * K);
        for (uint k = k0 + lane * 4; k < k0 + KC; k += 128) {
            ushort4 q = wr[k / 4];
            float4 wf = float4(as_type<float>((uint)q.x << 16), as_type<float>((uint)q.y << 16),
                               as_type<float>((uint)q.z << 16), as_type<float>((uint)q.w << 16));
            for (uint m = 0; m < M; ++m) acc[m] += dot(wf, *(device const float4*)(x + m * K + k));
        }
        } else {
        constexpr uint PER = 32 / BITS;                 // values per uint32
        constexpr uint MASK = (1u << BITS) - 1u;
        device const uint2* wr = (device const uint2*)(w + (size_t)row * (K / PER));
        for (uint k = k0 + lane * 2 * PER; k < k0 + KC; k += 64 * PER) {
            uint2 q = wr[k / (2 * PER)];
            const uint g = k / 64;
            const float s = as_type<float>((uint)sc[row * NG + g] << 16), b = as_type<float>((uint)bi[row * NG + g] << 16);
            for (uint m = 0; m < M; ++m) {
                device const float* xm = x + m * K + k;
                float d = 0.0f, xs = 0.0f;
                for (uint j = 0; j < PER; ++j) { float xv = xm[j]; d += (float)((q.x >> (BITS * j)) & MASK) * xv; xs += xv; }
                for (uint j = 0; j < PER; ++j) { float xv = xm[PER + j]; d += (float)((q.y >> (BITS * j)) & MASK) * xv; xs += xv; }
                acc[m] += s * d + b * xs;
            }
        }
        }
        for (uint m = 0; m < M; ++m) {
            float a = simd_sum(acc[m]);
            if (lane == 0) part[((size_t)split * M + m) * R + row] = a;
        }
    }
    threadgroup bool last;
    atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst, thread_scope_device);   // every writer publishes
    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_device);
    if (t == 0) {
        atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst, thread_scope_device);
        device atomic_uint* c = (device atomic_uint*)(const_cast<device uint*>(cnt)) + tile;
        uint prev = atomic_fetch_add_explicit(c, 1u, memory_order_relaxed);
        last = (prev == SPLITS - 1);
        if (last) atomic_store_explicit(c, 0u, memory_order_relaxed);   // ready for the next call
        atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst, thread_scope_device);
    }
    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_device);
    atomic_thread_fence(mem_flags::mem_device, memory_order_seq_cst, thread_scope_device);   // every reader acquires
    if (last) {
        for (uint i = t; i < RT * M; i += 256) {
            const uint row = tile * RT + i % RT, m = i / RT;
            if (row < R) {
                float a = 0.0f;
                for (uint s = 0; s < SPLITS; ++s) a += part[((size_t)s * M + m) * R + row];
                y[m * R + row] = a;
            }
        }
    }
"""
kern = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="splitk_mv", input_names=["w", "sc", "bi", "x", "cnt"], output_names=["y", "part"],
                            source=SRC)


def make(R, K, bits):
    if bits == 16:
        W = (mx.random.normal((R, K)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16)
        return dict(w=W, wv=W.view(mx.uint16), sc=mx.zeros((1,), mx.uint16), bi=mx.zeros((1,), mx.uint16), bits=16)
    q, s, b = mx.quantize((mx.random.normal((R, K)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16), group_size=64, bits=bits)
    return dict(q=q, s=s, b=b, wv=q, sc=s.view(mx.uint16), bi=b.view(mx.uint16), bits=bits)


def mlx_op(P, x):                       # x: (M, K) bf16
    if P["bits"] == 16:
        return x @ P["w"].T
    return mx.quantized_matmul(x, P["q"], P["s"], P["b"], transpose=True, group_size=64, bits=P["bits"])


def sk_op(P, x, R, K, splits, rsg, cnt):
    ntile = -(-R // (8 * rsg))
    y, _ = kern(inputs=[P["wv"], P["sc"], P["bi"], x, cnt],
                template=[("R", R), ("K", K), ("SPLITS", splits), ("RSG", rsg), ("BITS", P["bits"]), ("M", M)],
                grid=(ntile * splits * 256, 1, 1), threadgroup=(256, 1, 1),
                output_shapes=[(M, R), (splits * M * R,)], output_dtypes=[mx.float32, mx.float32])
    return y


def glue(y, K):
    R = y.shape[1]
    return y[:, :K] if R >= K else mx.tile(y, (1, -(-K // R)))[:, :K]


def chain(ops, x0, K):
    def run():
        x = x0
        for op in ops:
            x = glue(op(x), K)
        mx.eval(x)
        return x
    for _ in range(3):
        run()
    ts = []
    for _ in range(9):
        a = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    return sorted(ts)[4] / len(ops) * 1e6


SHAPES = [  # name, R, K, bits, calls per token on the 125B
    ("HC down 320x10240 bf16", 320, 10240, 16, 97),
    ("HC up 10240x320 bf16", 10240, 320, 16, 97),
    ("HC inject 4x10240 bf16", 4, 10240, 16, 96),
    ("router 512x2560 q8", 512, 2560, 8, 48),
    ("GDN in_proj_qkv 10240x2560 q4", 10240, 2560, 4, 36),
    ("GDN in_proj_z 6144x2560 q4", 6144, 2560, 4, 36),
    ("out/o_proj 2560x6144 q4", 2560, 6144, 4, 48),
    ("attn q_proj 12288x2560 q4", 12288, 2560, 4, 12),
    ("shared gate+up 1280x2560 q4", 1280, 2560, 4, 48),
    ("shared down 2560x640 q4", 2560, 640, 4, 48),
    ("PLE key_proj 10240x2560 bf16", 10240, 2560, 16, 1),
]
print(f"M = {M} input rows; dependent chain of {NC} distinct matrices per shape")
tot_save = 0.0
for name, R, K, bits, calls in SHAPES:
    Ps = [make(R, K, bits) for _ in range(NC)]
    mx.eval([v for P in Ps for v in P.values() if isinstance(v, mx.array)])
    wbytes = sum(a.nbytes for a in ((Ps[0]["w"],) if bits == 16 else (Ps[0]["q"], Ps[0]["s"], Ps[0]["b"])))
    xb = mx.random.normal((M, K)).astype(mx.bfloat16)
    g_b = chain([lambda x: x.astype(mx.bfloat16)[:, :R] if R <= K else mx.tile(x, (1, -(-R // K)))[:, :R]] * NC, xb, K)
    t_mlx = chain([lambda x, P=P: mlx_op(P, x) for P in Ps], xb, K) - g_b
    xf = xb.astype(mx.float32)
    best = None
    for rsg in (1, 2, 4):
        for splits in (1, 2, 4, 8, 16):
            kc = K // splits
            if K % splits or kc < 128 or (bits == 16 and kc % 4) or (bits != 16 and kc % 64):
                continue
            ntile = -(-R // (8 * rsg))
            cnt = mx.zeros((max(ntile, 4096),), mx.uint32); mx.eval(cnt)
            try:
                t = chain([lambda x, P=P: sk_op(P, x, R, K, splits, rsg, cnt) for P in Ps], xf, K) - g_b
            except Exception as e:
                s_ = str(e); print("  skip", name, splits, rsg, s_[s_.find("error:"):s_.find("error:")+160]); continue
            if best is None or t < best[0]:
                best = (t, splits, rsg)
    # correctness + determinism of the best config
    t_sk, splits, rsg = best
    ntile = -(-R // (8 * rsg)); cnt = mx.zeros((max(ntile, 4096),), mx.uint32); mx.eval(cnt)
    a1 = sk_op(Ps[0], xf, R, K, splits, rsg, cnt); a2 = sk_op(Ps[0], xf, R, K, splits, rsg, cnt)
    ref = mlx_op(Ps[0], xb).astype(mx.float32)
    err = (mx.abs(a1 - ref).max() / (mx.abs(ref).max() + 1e-9)).item()
    det = mx.array_equal(a1, a2).item()
    save = (t_mlx - t_sk) * calls
    tot_save += save
    print(f"{name:32s} MLX {t_mlx:6.1f} µs ({wbytes/t_mlx/1e3:5.0f} GB/s) | split-K {t_sk:6.1f} µs "
          f"({wbytes/t_sk/1e3:5.0f} GB/s, SPLITS={splits}, RSG={rsg}) | rel.err {err:.1e} det {det} | "
          f"x{calls}/token: {save/1e3:+.2f} ms", flush=True)
print(f"projected saving per token (all shapes, M={M}): {tot_save/1e3:+.2f} ms")


### scripts/ssd_qd.py
"""SSD read bandwidth vs queue depth on this Mac (research-first build step 1 for the 24 GB goal; PRIOR_ART.md lever 1).
Cold random preads with F_NOCACHE from the 125B's expert tensors (~/BigMoE/models/bare, read-only); every (layer, expert)
is used once across the whole run, so neither the page cache nor a previous read helps. Two patterns:
  whole : one contiguous 2.77 MB read per expert-sized request (what an expert-major file layout allows)
  pieces: BigMoE's layout today: 9 preads per expert (gate/up/down codes ~0.82 MB each + 6 scale/bias reads ~51 KB),
          issued together, as its loader does with dispatch_apply
QD = concurrent preads (a thread pool; os.pread releases the GIL). Reports GB/s and experts/s per QD.
SlotStream measured an M5 Pro 2 TB SSD at 9.5 / 17.3 / 17.3 GB/s for cold 2.76 MB F_NOCACHE preads at QD 1 / 8 / 32.
usage: ssd_qd.py   (I/O heavy for ~1 min: run under a quiet lock)"""
import fcntl, json, os, random, struct, time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
idx = json.load(open(f"{MD}/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
fds, hdr = {}, {}


def meta(name):
    f = f"{MD}/{idx[name]}"
    if f not in hdr:
        with open(f, "rb") as fh:
            n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]; hdr[f] = (n, json.loads(fh.read(n)))
        fd = os.open(f, os.O_RDONLY); fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_NOCACHE, 1); fds[f] = fd
    n, h = hdr[f]
    a, b = h[name]["data_offsets"]
    E = h[name]["shape"][0]
    return fds[f], 8 + n + a, (b - a) // E


PIECES = [(p, fl) for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj") for fl in ("weight", "scales", "biases")]
LAYERS = 48
pieces = {L: [meta(f"language_model.model.layers.{L}.mlp.switch_mlp.{p}.{fl}") for p, fl in PIECES] for L in range(LAYERS)}
EXPERT = sum(per for _, _, per in pieces[0])
rng = random.Random(7)
pool = [(L, e) for L in range(LAYERS) for e in range(512)]
rng.shuffle(pool)
cursor = [0]


def take(n):
    out = pool[cursor[0]:cursor[0] + n]; cursor[0] += n
    assert len(out) == n, "ran out of cold experts"
    return out


def read(fd, off, size):
    got = 0
    while got < size:
        r = os.pread(fd, size - got, off + got)
        if not r:
            break
        got += len(r)
    return got


def run(pattern, qd, n_exp):
    exps = take(n_exp)
    if pattern == "whole":        # one EXPERT-sized contiguous read inside the expert's gate_proj codes region
        jobs = []
        for L, e in exps:
            fd, off, per = pieces[L][0]
            e0 = min(e, 512 - 4)  # 2.77 MB spans ~3.4 experts of gate codes; stays inside the tensor
            jobs.append((fd, off + e0 * per, EXPERT))
    else:
        jobs = [(fd, off + e * per, per) for L, e in exps for fd, off, per in pieces[L]]
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=qd) as ex:
        total = sum(ex.map(lambda j: read(*j), jobs))
    dt = time.perf_counter() - t0
    return total / dt / 1e9, n_exp / dt


print(f"expert = {EXPERT / 1e6:.2f} MB in 9 pieces ({', '.join(f'{per / 1e3:.0f}K' for _, _, per in pieces[0])})", flush=True)
for pattern in ("whole", "pieces"):
    for qd in (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64):
        gbs, eps = run(pattern, qd, 240 if qd >= 4 else 120)
        print(f"{pattern:6s} QD {qd:2d}: {gbs:5.2f} GB/s, {eps:6.0f} experts/s ({1e3 / eps * 10:.2f} ms per 10 experts)", flush=True)


### scripts/st_peek.py
"""Print summary stats of 1-D (norm) tensors in safetensors files without loading them (header + byte ranges).
usage: st_peek.py FILE [REGEX]   (bf16/f32/f16; prints mean, min, max, first 6 values)"""
import json, re, struct, sys
import numpy as np
f, pat = sys.argv[1], re.compile(sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else r".")
with open(f, "rb") as fh:
    n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]
    hdr = json.loads(fh.read(n))
    for k in sorted(hdr):
        m = hdr[k]
        if k == "__metadata__" or len(m["shape"]) != 1 or not pat.search(k):
            continue
        a, b = m["data_offsets"]
        fh.seek(8 + n + a)
        raw = fh.read(b - a)
        dt = m["dtype"]
        if dt == "BF16":
            v = (np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint16).astype(np.uint32) << 16).view(np.float32)
        elif dt == "F16":
            v = np.frombuffer(raw, np.float16).astype(np.float32)
        elif dt == "F32":
            v = np.frombuffer(raw, np.float32)
        else:
            continue
        print(f"{k:70s} {dt} n={v.size:6d} mean {v.mean():+.4f} min {v.min():+.4f} max {v.max():+.4f}  {np.round(v[:6], 3).tolist()}")


### scripts/stall_alloc_sim.py
"""Two caching ideas on the real 125B traces (verify windows of 4 tokens, stride 3; per-layer LRU slot pools; CPU only).
1. Stall-time-aware slot allocation (cost-aware caching, Cao & Irani 1997, with the measured concave SSD curve: a layer with m
   misses stalls t(m) = 0.45/0.71/0.99/1.17 ms for m = 1..4, +0.25 ms per extra). The same total slots, split per layer by greedy
   marginal allocation, either for fewest misses (MoEPrefetch's DP objective) or for least stall time.
2. Draft-informed eviction (Belady's MIN 1966 x speculative decoding): LRU, but a victim that the NEXT verify window needs at
   this layer is skipped. Oracle next window = upper bound for predictors (MTP drafts, prev-pass routing).
Reports misses and stall ms per pass.   usage: stall_alloc_sim.py [FRAC]"""
import glob, os, sys
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
FRAC = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.50
E, K, WARM = 512, 10, 200
tcost = lambda m: 0.0 if m == 0 else [0.45, 0.71, 0.99, 1.17][m - 1] if m <= 4 else 1.17 + 0.25 * (m - 4)
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))
TR = []
for f in files:
    z = np.load(f); NL = len(z.files)
    tr = [z[f"L{L}"][:, :K].astype(np.int64) for L in range(NL)]
    wins = [[sorted(set(int(e) for e in tr[L][s:s + 4].ravel())) for s in range(0, tr[0].shape[0] - 4, 3)] for L in range(NL)]
    TR.append(wins)
NL = len(TR[0])


def layer_run(wins_by_prompt, C, belady=False):
    """one layer's LRU over all prompts: (misses list per window, stall ms list) after warm-up."""
    ms, st = [], []
    for wins in wins_by_prompt:
        cache = OrderedDict()
        for i, u in enumerate(wins):
            nxt = set(wins[i + 1]) if (belady and i + 1 < len(wins)) else set()
            miss = [e for e in u if e not in cache]
            for e in u:
                if e in cache: cache.move_to_end(e)
            for e in miss:
                cache[e] = 1
                while len(cache) > C:
                    victim = next((v for v in cache if v not in nxt and v not in u), None) if belady else None
                    if victim is None: cache.popitem(last=False)
                    else: del cache[victim]
            if i * 3 >= WARM:
                ms.append(len(miss)); st.append(tcost(len(miss)))
    return np.mean(ms), np.mean(st)


C0 = int(FRAC * E)
grid = sorted({max(16, int(C0 * f)) for f in (0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, 1.2, 1.35, 1.5)} | {C0})
curves = {}
for L in range(NL):
    for C in grid:
        curves[(L, C)] = layer_run([TR[p][L] for p in range(len(TR))], C)
uni_m = sum(curves[(L, C0)][0] for L in range(NL)); uni_s = sum(curves[(L, C0)][1] for L in range(NL))


def allocate(obj):
    """greedy: start every layer at the smallest grid budget, then repeatedly buy the step with the best gain per slot."""
    cur = {L: grid[0] for L in range(NL)}; left = C0 * NL - grid[0] * NL
    while True:
        best = None
        for L in range(NL):
            i = grid.index(cur[L])
            if i + 1 < len(grid):
                d = grid[i + 1] - cur[L]
                if d <= left:
                    gain = (curves[(L, cur[L])][obj] - curves[(L, grid[i + 1])][obj]) / d
                    if best is None or gain > best[0]: best = (gain, L, grid[i + 1], d)
        if best is None: break
        _, L, c, d = best; cur[L] = c; left -= d
    return cur


print(f"125B traces: {len(TR)} prompts, {NL} layers, C={C0}/512 per layer ({FRAC:.0%}), budget grid {grid}")
print(f"  uniform LRU           : misses/pass {uni_m:6.1f}  stall {uni_s:6.2f} ms/pass")
for name, obj in (("alloc for misses", 0), ("alloc for stall ms", 1)):
    a = allocate(obj)
    m = sum(curves[(L, a[L])][0] for L in range(NL)); s = sum(curves[(L, a[L])][1] for L in range(NL))
    print(f"  {name:22s}: misses/pass {m:6.1f}  stall {s:6.2f} ms/pass  (slots {sum(a.values())} vs {C0 * NL})", flush=True)
bm = bs = 0
for L in range(NL):
    m, s = layer_run([TR[p][L] for p in range(len(TR))], C0, belady=True); bm += m; bs += s
print(f"  LRU + next-window oracle eviction (Belady-informed): misses/pass {bm:6.1f}  stall {bs:6.2f} ms/pass", flush=True)


### scripts/stall_clusters.py
"""Upper bound for stall-funded runahead slices on the real 125B routing traces (MoEPrefetch data/traces125/*_800.npz).
Verify passes = unions of 4 consecutive tokens' top-10 (stride 3, ~ spec k=3 at 3.4 tok/pass); per-layer LRU at FRAC of 512.
A stall at layer X funds a slice that perfectly protects X+1..X+D (D = 2: predictions 2 and 3 layers past the slice seed);
a protected layer does not stall and funds nothing (no idle time). Reports stalls per pass without and with slices, the share of
stalled layers that sit within D layers after a previous stall, and the same for a continuous slice (every layer protected
except the first D after a pass starts) for scale. CPU only."""
import glob, os, sys
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
FRAC = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.50
D = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 2
E, K, C = 512, 10, int(FRAC * 512)
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/traces125/*_800.npz")))
tot = dict(passes=0, stalls=0, clustered=0, left_slice=0)
for f in files:
    z = np.load(f)
    NL = len(z.files)
    tr = [z[f"L{L}"][:, :K].astype(np.int64) for L in range(NL)]
    T = tr[0].shape[0]
    lru = [OrderedDict() for _ in range(NL)]
    warm = 200
    for start in range(0, T - 4, 3):
        stalled = []
        for L in range(NL):
            u = np.unique(tr[L][start:start + 4])
            miss = sum(1 for e in u if e not in lru[L])
            for e in u:
                lru[L].pop(int(e), None); lru[L][int(e)] = True
            while len(lru[L]) > C: lru[L].popitem(last=False)
            stalled.append(miss > 0)
        if start < warm: continue
        tot["passes"] += 1
        tot["stalls"] += sum(stalled)
        tot["clustered"] += sum(1 for L in range(NL) if stalled[L] and any(stalled[L - j] for j in range(1, D + 1) if L - j >= 0))
        protect = -1                                  # stall-funded slices: a stall protects the next D layers
        for L in range(NL):
            if stalled[L] and L > protect:
                tot["left_slice"] += 1
                protect = L + D
    print(f"{os.path.basename(f)}: done", flush=True)
P = tot["passes"]
print(f"FRAC {FRAC}, D {D}, {P} verify passes (4-token unions, LRU, no prefetch):")
print(f"  stalled layers/pass {tot['stalls']/P:.1f}; of them within {D} layers after a previous stall: {tot['clustered']/tot['stalls']:.0%}")
print(f"  with stall-funded slices (perfect, protect next {D}): stalls/pass {tot['left_slice']/P:.1f} "
      f"({1 - tot['left_slice']/tot['stalls']:.0%} removed)")


### scripts/stall_io.py
"""Is BigMoE's ~1.0 ms per stalled layer SSD-intrinsic? Replays its loader's read pattern on the real 125B files:
for n missing experts of one layer (n = 1..4), all (expert x piece) preads in flight at once (F_NOCACHE), as
bigmoe_ext.cpp's load_jobs does with dispatch_apply; compared with the same bytes as n contiguous reads, and with
reads issued serially. Random cold experts from random layers. Prints wall ms per stall (median, p90).
Usage: stall_io.py   (CPU + SSD only; quiet lock for timing)"""
import fcntl, json, os, struct, time, random, glob
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import numpy as np

D = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
idx = json.load(open(f"{D}/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
hdrs = {}


def meta(name):
    f = idx[name]
    if f not in hdrs:
        with open(f"{D}/{f}", "rb") as fh:
            n = struct.unpack("<Q", fh.read(8))[0]
            hdrs[f] = (json.loads(fh.read(n)), 8 + n)
    h, base = hdrs[f]
    m = h[name]
    return f, base + m["data_offsets"][0], m["data_offsets"][1] - m["data_offsets"][0], m["shape"][0]


fds = {}


def fd(f):
    if f not in fds:
        fds[f] = os.open(f"{D}/{f}", os.O_RDONLY)
        fcntl.fcntl(fds[f], fcntl.F_NOCACHE, 1)
    return fds[f]


PIECES = [f"mlp.switch_mlp.{p}.{q}" for p in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj") for q in ("weight", "scales", "biases")]


def expert_reads(L, e):
    out = []
    for p in PIECES:
        f, off, size, E = meta(f"language_model.model.layers.{L}.{p}")
        per = size // E
        out.append((fd(f), off + e * per, per))
    return out


pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(64)
bufs = [bytearray(4 << 20) for _ in range(64)]


def rd(job):
    f, off, n = job[0]
    b = bufs[job[1]]
    mv = memoryview(b)[:n]
    done = 0
    while done < n:
        done += os.preadv(f, [mv[done:]], off + done)


def stall_parallel(jobs):
    list(pool.map(rd, [(j, i % 64) for i, j in enumerate(jobs)]))


def stall_serial(jobs):
    for i, j in enumerate(jobs):
        rd((j, i % 64))


rng = random.Random(0)
per_exp = sum(n for _, _, n in expert_reads(0, 0))
print(f"one expert = {len(PIECES)} pieces, {per_exp/1e6:.2f} MB")
for n in (1, 2, 3, 4):
    res = {"parallel pieces": [], "parallel contiguous": [], "serial pieces": []}
    for trial in range(40):
        L = rng.randrange(48)
        es = rng.sample(range(512), n)
        jobs = [r for e in es for r in expert_reads(L, e)]
        # contiguous stand-in: the same byte count as n single reads at random offsets of the down_proj tensor region
        f, off, size, E = meta(f"language_model.model.layers.{L}.mlp.switch_mlp.gate_proj.weight")
        cont = [(fd(f), off + (rng.randrange(size - per_exp) & ~16383), per_exp) for _ in range(n)]
        order = list(res)
        rng.shuffle(order)
        for k in order:
            L2 = rng.randrange(48); es2 = rng.sample(range(512), n)
            j2 = [r for e in es2 for r in expert_reads(L2, e)]
            t = time.perf_counter()
            if k == "parallel pieces":
                stall_parallel(j2)
            elif k == "parallel contiguous":
                f2, off2, size2, _ = meta(f"language_model.model.layers.{L2}.mlp.switch_mlp.up_proj.weight")
                stall_parallel([(fd(f2), off2 + (rng.randrange(size2 - per_exp) & ~16383), per_exp) for _ in range(n)])
            else:
                stall_serial(j2)
            res[k].append(time.perf_counter() - t)
    line = "  ".join(f"{k}: {np.median(v)*1e3:.2f} ms (p90 {np.percentile(v,90)*1e3:.2f})" for k, v in res.items())
    print(f"{n} missing experts ({n*per_exp/1e6:.1f} MB): {line}  -> {n*per_exp/np.median(res['parallel pieces'])/1e9:.1f} GB/s", flush=True)


### scripts/suffix_global.py
"""#17: does a global index over *earlier sessions* add accepted lookup drafts beyond the current context?
Replays Kamen's Claude Code transcripts (~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl) in time order as one token stream
(Qwen tokenizer). At each assistant-output position: find the most recent earlier occurrence of the last
NG tokens; draft the K tokens that followed it; accepted = common prefix with the truth. Context-only lookup may
use matches inside the current session; global lookup may also use any earlier session.
Decode walk: a step emits accepted+1 tokens. Reports tokens per step (lookup only, no MTP) on the
evaluated (most recent) sessions. Usage: suffix_global.py [MAX_MB] [EVAL_FRAC]"""
import glob, json, os, sys
import numpy as np
from tokenizers import Tokenizer

MAX_MB = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 120
EVAL_FRAC = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0.2
NG, K, CAP = 4, 16, 20000
tk = Tokenizer.from_file("/Users/kamen/.lmstudio/models/lmstudio-community/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-4bit/tokenizer.json")


def render(rec):
    """-> list of (text, is_output) for one transcript line."""
    m = rec.get("message") or {}
    c = m.get("content")
    out = rec.get("type") == "assistant"
    if isinstance(c, str):
        return [(c[:CAP], out)]
    res = []
    for b in c or []:
        t = b.get("type")
        if t == "text":
            res.append((b.get("text", "")[:CAP], out))
        elif t == "tool_use":
            res.append((json.dumps(b.get("input", {}), ensure_ascii=False)[:CAP], out))
        elif t == "tool_result":
            cc = b.get("content")
            if isinstance(cc, list):
                cc = "\n".join(x.get("text", "") for x in cc if isinstance(x, dict))
            res.append((str(cc)[:CAP], False))
    return res


files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl")), key=os.path.getmtime)
picked, tot = [], 0
for f in reversed(files):                         # most recent files up to MAX_MB
    s = os.path.getsize(f) / 1e6
    if tot + s > MAX_MB and picked:
        continue
    picked.append(f); tot += s
picked = picked[::-1]
toks, isout, sess = [], [], []
for si, f in enumerate(picked):
    pieces = []
    for line in open(f, errors="ignore"):
        try:
            pieces += render(json.loads(line))
        except Exception:
            pass
    pieces = [p for p in pieces if p[0]]
    if not pieces:
        continue
    enc = tk.encode_batch([p[0] for p in pieces])
    for (txt, o), e in zip(pieces, enc):
        toks += e.ids; isout += [o] * len(e.ids); sess += [si] * len(e.ids)
S = np.array(toks, np.int64); O = np.array(isout); SE = np.array(sess)
N = len(S)
sess_start = np.zeros(SE.max() + 1, np.int64)
first = np.r_[0, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(SE)) + 1]
sess_start[SE[first]] = first
print(f"{len(picked)} sessions, {tot:.0f} MB jsonl, {N/1e6:.1f}M tokens, outputs {O.mean():.0%}", flush=True)

# hash of the NG-gram ending at each position; sorted (hash, pos) for "most recent earlier occurrence"
h = np.zeros(N, np.uint64)
for j in range(NG):
    h = h * np.uint64(1000003) + np.roll(S, j).astype(np.uint64)
h[:NG] = 0
order = np.lexsort((np.arange(N), h))
hs, ps = h[order], order


def prev_occ(i):
    """most recent position p < i with the same NG-gram hash ending at p, or -1."""
    lo = np.searchsorted(hs, h[i], "left")
    hi = np.searchsorted(hs, h[i], "right")
    k = lo + np.searchsorted(ps[lo:hi], i, "left") - 1
    return ps[k] if k >= lo else -1


eval_from = sess_start[int(SE.max() * (1 - EVAL_FRAC))]
stats = {"none": 0, "ctx": 0, "glob": 0}
steps = {"none": 0, "ctx": 0, "glob": 0}
ntok = 0
i = eval_from
outpos = np.flatnonzero(O[eval_from:]) + eval_from
# walk each policy over the output tokens
for pol in ("none", "ctx", "glob"):
    i_idx = 0
    st = 0
    while i_idx < len(outpos):
        i = outpos[i_idx]
        acc = 0
        if pol != "none" and i >= NG:
            p = prev_occ(i)
            if p >= 0 and (pol == "glob" or p >= sess_start[SE[i]]):
                d = S[p + 1:p + 1 + K]; t = S[i + 1:i + 1 + K]
                m = min(len(d), len(t))
                neq = np.flatnonzero(d[:m] != t[:m])
                acc = int(neq[0]) if len(neq) else m
                acc = min(acc, int(np.argmin(O[i + 1:i + 1 + K])) if not O[i + 1:i + 1 + K].all() else acc)
        st += 1
        i_idx += acc + 1
    steps[pol] = st
n = len(outpos)
print(f"evaluated {n/1e3:.0f}K output tokens in the latest {EVAL_FRAC:.0%} of sessions (lookup only, K={K}, {NG}-gram)")
for pol in ("none", "ctx", "glob"):
    print(f"  {pol:5s}: {n/steps[pol]:.3f} tokens/step")
print(f"global over context-only: {steps['ctx']/steps['glob']:.3f}x fewer steps")


### scripts/test_refit_lib.py
"""CPU check of refit_lib.fit_proj (MLX, used by refit35.py on the GPU) against the numpy fit validated in actrefit35.py (held-out
error), on real 35B routed inputs (NewIdeas' cap35/L3) for the most-routed expert, tiers 3 and 2, gate and down.
usage: test_refit_lib.py   (CPU only)"""
import glob, json, os, sys
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from refit_lib import fit_proj, codes, solve

mx.set_default_device(mx.cpu)
MODEL = glob.glob(os.path.expanduser("~/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-MLX-8bit/snapshots/*/"))[0]
cap = np.load(os.path.expanduser("~/NewIdeas/data/cap35/L3.npz"))
X, I, seg = cap["X"].astype(np.float32), cap["I"].astype(np.int64), cap["seg"]
pre = "language_model.model.layers.3.mlp.switch_mlp."
W = mx.load(MODEL + json.load(open(MODEL + "model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"][pre + "gate_proj.weight"])
e = int(np.argmax(np.bincount(I.ravel(), minlength=256)))
rows = np.nonzero((I == e).any(1))[0]
tr, te = rows[seg[rows] % 2 == 0], rows[seg[rows] % 2 == 1]


def q4(name):
    w = mx.dequantize(W[pre + name + ".weight"][e], W[pre + name + ".scales"][e], W[pre + name + ".biases"][e], group_size=64, bits=8)
    wq, s, b = mx.quantize(w, group_size=64, bits=4)
    return wq, s.astype(mx.float32), b.astype(mx.float32)


def numpy_fit(q, s, b, Xtr, n):
    """actrefit35.fits 'd act' for tier n (ridge 1e-3 * tr / 2G + 1e-6 toward the truncation)"""
    out, K = q.shape
    G, sh = K // 64, 4 - n
    m = np.floor(q / 2 ** sh).reshape(out, G, 64)
    w = s[..., None] * q.reshape(out, G, 64) + b[..., None]
    th0 = np.concatenate([s * 2 ** sh, b + s * (q.reshape(out, G, 64) - 2 ** sh * m).mean(-1)], -1)
    Xg = Xtr.reshape(len(Xtr), G, 64)
    Phi = np.concatenate([np.einsum("sgk,ngk->nsg", Xg, m, optimize=True), np.broadcast_to(Xg.sum(-1)[None], (out, len(Xtr), G))], -1)
    t = np.einsum("sgk,ngk->ns", Xg, w, optimize=True)
    A = np.einsum("nsi,nsj->nij", Phi, Phi, optimize=True)
    lam = 1e-3 * np.trace(A, axis1=1, axis2=2)[:, None, None] / (2 * G) + 1e-6
    th = np.linalg.solve(A + lam * np.eye(2 * G)[None], (np.einsum("nsi,ns->ni", Phi, t) + lam[..., 0] * th0)[..., None])[..., 0]
    return th[:, :G], th[:, G:], m, w


ok = True
wg = {}
for name in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
    wq, s, b = q4(name)
    qe = codes(wq[None])[0]
    q, sn, bn = np.array(qe).astype(np.float32), np.array(s), np.array(b)
    wg[name] = (sn[..., None] * q.reshape(*sn.shape, 64) + bn[..., None]).reshape(q.shape)
    if name == "up_proj":
        continue
    if name == "down_proj":
        h = lambda r: (lambda g, u: g / (1 + np.exp(-g)) * u)(X[r] @ wg["gate_proj"].T, X[r] @ wg["up_proj"].T)
        Xtr, Xte = h(tr), h(te)
    else:
        Xtr, Xte = X[tr], X[te]
    for n in (3, 2):
        A, rhs, G = fit_proj(mx.array(Xtr), qe, s, b, n)
        th = solve(np.array(A), np.array(rhs))
        a_m, b_m = th[:, :G], th[:, G:]
        a_n, b_n, m, w = numpy_fit(q, sn, bn, Xtr, n)
        ra = np.abs(a_m - a_n).max() / np.abs(a_n).max()
        rb = np.abs(b_m - b_n).max() / np.abs(b_n).max()
        err = lambda a, bb: np.linalg.norm(Xte @ ((a[..., None] * m + bb[..., None]) - w).reshape(len(w), -1).T) / np.linalg.norm(Xte @ w.reshape(len(w), -1).T)
        e_m, e_n = err(a_m, b_m), err(a_n, b_n)
        good = ra < 1e-2 and rb < 1e-2 and abs(e_m - e_n) < 1e-3
        ok &= good
        print(f"expert {e} {name} tier {n}: max rel diff alpha {ra:.2e} beta {rb:.2e}; held-out rel error MLX {e_m:.4f} numpy {e_n:.4f} "
              f"-> {'ok' if good else 'MISMATCH'}", flush=True)
print("PASS" if ok else "FAIL", flush=True)


### scripts/tlb.py
"""Does the Apple GPU lose bandwidth to address translation when a decode step reads many
scattered chunks (experts) instead of one contiguous region?
One kernel reads the same total bytes from a 6 GB buffer in chunks of C bytes, either in order
or at random chunk offsets. Random 3 MB chunks = what an MoE expert gather does."""
import time, sys
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

TOTAL_GB = 6.0
READ_MB = 1024                                  # bytes read per pass
buf = mx.random.randint(0, 2**31, (int(TOTAL_GB * 1e9) // 16, 4)).astype(mx.uint32)   # random, so no zero-page shortcut
mx.eval(buf)
NE = buf.shape[0]

SRC = """
    // each threadgroup reads one chunk starting at offs[tg]. buf is bound as uint32, so a chunk
    // is CH*4 bytes (not CH*16); the printed GB/s divide by 4 for that.
    uint tg = threadgroup_position_in_grid.x, t = thread_position_in_threadgroup.x;
    uint base = offs[tg];
    uint4 acc = 0;
    for (uint i = t; i < CH; i += 256) acc ^= buf[base + i];
    acc.x ^= acc.y ^ acc.z ^ acc.w;
    acc.x = simd_sum(acc.x);
    if (t == 0) out[tg] = acc.x;
"""
kern = mx.fast.metal_kernel(name="chunkread", input_names=["buf", "offs"], output_names=["out"], source=SRC,
                            ensure_row_contiguous=False)
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
print(f"{READ_MB} MB per pass from a {TOTAL_GB:.0f} GB buffer, one kernel")
for ch_kb in (16, 64, 256, 1024, 3072):
    ch = ch_kb * 1024 // 16
    n = READ_MB * 1024 // ch_kb
    res = {}
    for pat in ("seq", "rand"):
        if pat == "seq":
            start = rng.integers(0, NE - n * ch)
            offs = start + np.arange(n) * ch
        else:
            offs = rng.choice(NE // ch, n, replace=False) * ch
        o = mx.array(offs.astype(np.uint32))

        def run():
            mx.eval(kern(inputs=[buf, o], template=[("CH", ch)], grid=(n * 256, 1, 1), threadgroup=(256, 1, 1),
                         output_shapes=[(n,)], output_dtypes=[mx.uint32])[0])
        for _ in range(3):
            run()
        ts = []
        for _ in range(15):
            t = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
        res[pat] = READ_MB / 1024 / sorted(ts)[7]
    print(f"chunk {ch_kb:>5} KB: sequential {res["seq"]*1.074/4:6.0f} GB/s, random {res["rand"]*1.074/4:6.0f} GB/s "
          f"({res['rand']/res['seq']:.3f}x)")


### scripts/union_growth125.py
"""Union growth of routed experts over blocks of k consecutive committed tokens (125B, data/verify_trace125.*): each
pass's rows 0..n-1 are the inputs that became committed tokens (n = tokens committed by that pass), so concatenating them
per prompt gives the true routing of the committed sequence. For k = 1..32: mean distinct experts per layer in a block of
k consecutive tokens, and cold 4-bit MB per token if every block had to stream its union (no cache): the amortization a
k-token exact verify would give with perfect drafts. usage: union_growth125.py   (CPU, seconds)"""
import collections, json
import numpy as np
D = "/Users/kamen/MoonShots/data"
z = np.load(f"{D}/verify_trace125.npz")
cb = json.load(open(f"{D}/verify_trace125.json"))["callbacks"]
ids, seq, pr = z["ids"].astype(np.int32), z["seq"], z["prompt"]
tot = collections.Counter()
for p, s, n in cb:
    tot[(p, s)] += n
streams = collections.defaultdict(list)                  # prompt -> [token][layer][10]
for i in range(len(seq)):
    n = tot.get((int(pr[i]), int(seq[i])), 0)
    for r in range(n):
        streams[int(pr[i])].append(ids[i, :, r])
L = ids.shape[1]
print("committed tokens per prompt:", {p: len(v) for p, v in streams.items()})
MB = 2.765
for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32):
    us = []
    for v in streams.values():
        a = np.stack(v)                                  # [T, L, 10]
        for t0 in range(0, len(a) - k + 1, k):
            blk = a[t0:t0 + k]
            us.append(np.mean([len(np.unique(blk[:, l])) for l in range(L)]))
    u = float(np.mean(us))
    print(f"k={k:2d}: distinct experts/layer {u:6.1f} (x{u / 10:4.2f} of one token) | cold MB/token {u * L * MB / k:7.1f}", flush=True)


### scripts/verify_gather.py
"""Verify-pass expert compute on the 125B: how efficient is MLX's gather_qmm at verify sizes?
One MoE layer (512 experts, 4-bit, gate/up 640x2560, down 2560x640), S rows (1..4) each routed to top-10 with a realistic overlap
(union ~10 / 16 / 21 / 26 experts for S = 1..4, as on the proxy traces). Times the three gathers (sorted path, as SwitchGLU does)
over 16 layers' distinct weights and reports effective GB/s of the *unique* expert bytes. Ideal: every union expert read once at ~250 GB/s.
Usage: verify_gather.py   (GPU, quiet lock; ~1.4 GB x 4 of weights)"""
import time
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx

NE, K, D, F, G = 512, 10, 2560, 640, 64
NLAY = 4                                   # 4 x 1.4 GB of expert weights
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)


def q(o, i):
    return mx.quantize((mx.random.normal((NE, o, i)) * 0.02).astype(mx.bfloat16), group_size=G, bits=4)


layers = [(q(F, D), q(F, D), q(D, F)) for _ in range(NLAY)]
mx.eval(layers)
per_expert = sum(a.nbytes for t in layers[0] for a in t) / NE


def routing(S):
    """S rows, top-10 each; consecutive tokens share ~45 % of experts (proxy-trace statistic)."""
    base = rng.choice(NE, K, replace=False)
    rows = [base]
    for _ in range(S - 1):
        keep = rng.choice(base, int(0.45 * K), replace=False)
        new = rng.choice(np.setdiff1d(np.arange(NE), keep), K - len(keep), replace=False)
        rows.append(np.concatenate([keep, new]))
        base = rows[-1]
    return np.stack(rows)


def moe(x, idx, w):
    gate, up, down = w
    flat = idx.reshape(-1)
    order = mx.argsort(flat)
    xs = x[order // K][:, None, :]
    ids = flat[order]
    kw = dict(transpose=True, group_size=G, bits=4, sorted_indices=True)
    g = mx.gather_qmm(xs, *gate, rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    u = mx.gather_qmm(xs, *up, rhs_indices=ids, **kw)
    return mx.gather_qmm((g * mx.sigmoid(g)) * u, *down, rhs_indices=ids, **kw)


for S in (1, 2, 3, 4):
    idxs = [mx.array(routing(S).astype(np.int32)) for _ in range(NLAY)]
    uniq = np.mean([len(np.unique(np.array(i))) for i in idxs])
    x = mx.random.normal((S, D)).astype(mx.bfloat16)

    def run():
        y = x
        for w, i in zip(layers, idxs):
            y = moe(y, i, w).reshape(S, K, D).sum(1).astype(mx.bfloat16)   # dependent chain like real layers
        mx.eval(y)
    for _ in range(3):
        run()
    ts = []
    for _ in range(15):
        a = time.perf_counter(); run(); ts.append(time.perf_counter() - a)
    t = sorted(ts)[7] / NLAY
    print(f"S={S}: union {uniq:4.1f} experts/layer, {t*1e3:6.3f} ms/layer -> {uniq*per_expert/t/1e9:5.0f} GB/s of unique "
          f"expert bytes; {S*K} (row, expert) pairs -> {S*K*per_expert/t/1e9:5.0f} GB/s if each pair re-reads", flush=True)


### scripts/verify_trace125.py
"""Verify-pass routing trace for the wrong-path expert study (round 22). The 125B on BigMoE's streamed runtime, MTPLX
generate_mtpk with committed MTP history (the product setting, round 21), depth 3, greedy, BigMoE's MTPLX flags
(capture-commit: one gated trunk pass per round, no repair pass). Records every decode/verify trunk pass through the
gate (Gate.route_and_gate, wrapped): its seq and, per layer, the routing ids of every row (row 0 = the committed token,
rows 1.. = drafts), and every token_callback (seq at the time, tokens committed). Offline, each round's accepted drafts =
committed tokens - 1, so rows after the first rejected draft are labelled wrong-path. The routing does not depend on the
cache, so cache policies can be compared exactly on this trace (scripts/wrongpath_sim.py).
Output: data/verify_trace125.npz (ids [passes, layers, rows, k] int16, -1 padded; rows per pass; seq; prompt index)
        data/verify_trace125.json (callbacks, per-prompt token counts, MTP stats)
usage: verify_trace125.py [FRAC] [MAXTOK]   (GPU, BigMoE-size memory: exclusive window)"""
import json, os, sys, time
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_NGRAM_PREWARM", "off")
for _k, _v in (("MTPLX_FAMILY_CAPTURE_COMMIT", "1"), ("MTPLX_DROP_EVENTS", "1"), ("MTPLX_LAZY_MTP_HISTORY_APPEND", "1"),
               ("MTPLX_DEFER_REPAIR_EVAL", "1")):
    os.environ.setdefault(_k, _v)
os.environ.setdefault("MTPLX_CONTEXT_COPY", "0")    # prompt-lookup rounds can exceed the 40-id gate row (MAXK) and
                                                   # take the unrecorded sync path; this study is about MTP verify rows
for k in ("MTPLX_COMPILED_VERIFY", "MTPLX_QWEN4EXP_COMPILE", "MTPLX_COMPILE_AR_FORWARD", "MTPLX_COMPILED_TARGET_PREFIX"):
    os.environ.setdefault(k, "0")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE"))
import numpy as np
import mlx.core as mx
from bigmoe.loader import load_streamed
from bigmoe import fuse

MAXTOK = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 400
MD = os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/models/bare")
rt, st = load_streamed(MD, round(frac * 512))
from mtplx.sampling import SamplerConfig
from mtplx import generation as G
K = 10
REC = []                                    # (seq, L, ids) per gated call
gate = st.gate
_orig = gate.route_and_gate


def hook(L, ids, seq=None, preds=None, counts=None):
    REC.append((gate.seq if seq is None else seq, L, ids))
    return _orig(L, ids, seq=seq, preds=preds, counts=counts)


gate.route_and_gate = hook
lines = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoEPrefetch/data/gsm8k/test.jsonl"))]
PROMPTS = [
    open(os.path.expanduser("~/BigMoE/data/prompt_code.txt")).read(),
    "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + lines[0]["question"],
    "Explain how a refrigerator works to a curious 10 year old, in about 150 words.",
    "Explain in detail how TCP congestion control works: slow start, congestion avoidance, fast retransmit and fast "
    "recovery, and how CUBIC and BBR differ. Use headings and a short example.",
    "Solve the problem step by step, briefly. End with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.\n\n" + lines[1]["question"],
]
smp = SamplerConfig(temperature=0.0, top_p=1.0, top_k=0)
CB, META, passes = [], [], []
for pi, text in enumerate(PROMPTS):
    ids = rt.tokenizer.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": text}], add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    ids = list(ids if isinstance(ids[0], int) else ids["input_ids"])
    fuse.reset_generation_state(rt.model)
    REC.clear()

    def cb(toks, pi=pi):
        CB.append((pi, int(gate.seq), len(toks) if isinstance(toks, list) else 1))
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    res = G.generate_mtpk(rt, ids, max_tokens=MAXTOK, sampler=smp, speculative_depth=3, seed=0,

                     accepted_by_depth=list(S.accepted_by_depth), drafted_by_depth=list(S.drafted_by_depth),

st.gate.check()

P = len(passes)

arr = np.full((P, NL, R, K), -1, np.int16)
rows = np.zeros(P, np.int16); seqs = np.zeros(P, np.int64); pidx = np.zeros(P, np.int16); full = np.zeros(P, np.int16)

### scripts/wall24.py
"""24 GB SSD wall (NewIdeas Sun ~13:20): the ceiling for ANY residency policy (eviction, admission, prefetch-into-pool) at
v3's 24 GB split, on the expA traces (bulgarian / chat / code / math, 800 tokens, top-10 of the rank-ordered top-20).
v3 plan 4433333333: every needed expert needs its MSB-3 part (pool C_m 147), ranks 0-1 also the LSB-1 part (pool C_l 46;
LSB needed when the expert's best rank among the check's rows is < 2). 16-row checks accepting 10 (one gated call per
layer per check with the union of the rows' needs), tokens 0-399 warm the pools, checks starting at 400+ are measured.
Policies per pool (independent per layer):
  lru     the old default
  lfu     the engine's BIGMOE_EVICT_LFU=d rule (~/BigMoE/ext/bigmoe_ext.cpp ensure / make_room / lfu_victim): per call
          w /= d, every needed expert += w; a miss evicts the lowest-score resident not needed by this call
  belady  forced admission like the engine (every needed expert is resident after its call), the victim is the
          resident not needed now whose next use is farthest (perfect foresight)
  min     Belady's MIN with bypass: after each call keep the C experts with the nearest next use among residents and
          this call's needs (a needed expert may be read into scratch and dropped). Optimal for equal-size objects: no
          policy of any kind can read fewer bytes at this pool size
usage: wall24.py   (CPU only, one core)"""
import glob, os, time
import numpy as np

NL = 48
MSB, LSB = 2162688, 770048
T = "/Users/kamen/Breakthrough/data/expA/traces125/"
SETS = {os.path.basename(f).split("_")[0]: np.load(f) for f in sorted(glob.glob(T + "*_800_g.npz"))}
IDS = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1)[:, :, :10].astype(np.int32) for k, d in SETS.items()}
INF = 1 << 30


MEAS = (400, 800)                                  # measured check starts [a, b); the trace (Belady's future) runs to 800


def needs(ids, R, A):
    """per check: (measured?, per layer (msb need set, lsb need set))"""
    n, out, pos = ids.shape[0], [], 0
    while pos + R <= n:
        lay = []
        for l in range(NL):
            best = {}
            for r in range(R):
                for k, e in enumerate(ids[pos + r, l]):
                    e = int(e)
                    if e not in best or k < best[e]:
                        best[e] = k
            lay.append((frozenset(best), frozenset(e for e, k in best.items() if k < 2)))
        out.append((MEAS[0] <= pos < MEAS[1], lay))
        pos += A
    return out


def run_pool(seq, C, policy, d=0.99):
    """seq: list of need sets (one per call). Returns misses per call."""
    miss = []
    H = None
    if policy.startswith("min") and policy != "min" or policy.startswith("ws"):
        H = int(policy[3:] if policy.startswith("min") else policy[2:])
    if policy.startswith("min") or policy == "belady" or policy.startswith("ws"):
        uses = {}
        for t, N in enumerate(seq):
            for e in N:
                uses.setdefault(e, []).append(t)
        ptr = {e: 0 for e in uses}

        def nxt(e, t):                               # first use strictly after t
            u, i = uses[e], ptr[e]
            while i < len(u) and u[i] <= t:
                i += 1
            ptr[e] = i
            return u[i] if i < len(u) else INF
    R = set()
    if policy == "lfub" or policy.startswith("gap"):
        return run_online(seq, C, policy, d)
    if policy == "lru":
        from collections import OrderedDict
        od = OrderedDict()
    score, w = {}, 1.0
    for t, N in enumerate(seq):
        if policy == "lru":
            m = [e for e in N if e not in od]
            miss.append(len(m))
            for e in N:
                if e in od:
                    od.move_to_end(e)
            for e in m:
                while len(od) >= C:
                    for v in od:                        # oldest not needed now
                        if v not in N:
                            del od[v]
                            break
                    else:
                        break
                od[e] = True
            continue
        if policy == "lfu" or H is not None:
            w /= d
            for e in N:
                score[e] = score.get(e, 0.0) + w
        m = [e for e in N if e not in R]
        miss.append(len(m))
        if policy.startswith("min") or policy.startswith("ws"):
            cand = R | N
            if len(cand) > C:
                if H is None:
                    key = lambda e: nxt(e, t)
                elif policy.startswith("min"):
                    key = lambda e: (nxt(e, t) if nxt(e, t) - t <= H else INF, -score[e])
                else:
                    key = lambda e: (0 if nxt(e, t) - t <= H else 1, -score[e])
                R = set(sorted(cand, key=key)[:C])
            else:
                R = cand
            continue
        for e in m:                                  # lfu / belady: forced admission, one victim per miss when full
            if len(R) >= C:
                vic = [v for v in R if v not in N]
                if vic:
                    if policy == "lfu":
                        v = min(vic, key=lambda v: score[v])
                    else:
                        v = max(vic, key=lambda v: nxt(v, t))
                    R.discard(v)
            R.add(e)
    return miss


_NEEDS = {}


def run_online(seq, C, policy, d=0.99):
    miss, R, score, w = [], set(), {}, 1.0
    forced = policy.endswith("_f")
    if policy.startswith("gap"):
        a = int(policy[3:].split("_")[0]) / 10
    last, g, G0 = {}, {}, 40.0
    for t, N in enumerate(seq):
        w /= d
        for e in N:
            score[e] = score.get(e, 0.0) + w
        m = [e for e in N if e not in R]
        miss.append(len(m))
        if policy == "lfub":
            cand = R | N
            R = set(sorted(cand, key=lambda e: -score[e])[:C]) if len(cand) > C else cand
            continue
        for e in N:
            if e in last:
                g[e] = a * (t - last[e]) + (1 - a) * g[e]
            else:
                g[e] = G0
            last[e] = t

        def pred(e):
            p = last[e] + g[e]
            return p if p > t else t + g[e]
        if forced:
            for e in m:
                if len(R) >= C:
                    vic = [v for v in R if v not in N]
                    if vic:
                        R.discard(max(vic, key=pred))
                R.add(e)
        else:
            cand = R | N
            R = set(sorted(cand, key=pred)[:C]) if len(cand) > C else cand
    return miss


def bytes_per_token(policy, Cm=147, Cl=46, R=16, A=10, d=0.99):
    res = {}
    for k, ids in IDS.items():
        if (k, R, A) not in _NEEDS:
            _NEEDS[(k, R, A)] = needs(ids, R, A)
        ch = _NEEDS[(k, R, A)]
        meas = [m for m, _ in ch]
        tot = 0
        for l in range(NL):
            mm = run_pool([lay[l][0] for _, lay in ch], Cm, policy, d)
            ml = run_pool([lay[l][1] for _, lay in ch], Cl, policy, d)
            tot += sum(a * MSB + b * LSB for a, b, f in zip(mm, ml, meas) if f)
        res[k] = tot / (A * sum(meas))
    return float(np.mean(list(res.values()))), res


PROB = {k: np.stack([d[f"G{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.float32) for k, d in SETS.items()}
IDS20 = {k: np.stack([d[f"L{l}"] for l in range(NL)], 1).astype(np.int32) for k, d in SETS.items()}


def hint_seq(k, l, R=16, A=10, mode="r", beta=0.5):
    """per check at layer l: dict expert -> LFU increment factor from the check's rows' top-20 (the router's near misses).
    mode r: 1 for an expert in some row's top-10, beta for one only in ranks 10-19; mode p: sum over rows of its gate prob
    (normalised by the call's mean over its needed experts)"""
    ids, pr, out, pos, n = IDS20[k], PROB[k], [], 0, IDS20[k].shape[0]
    while pos + R <= n:
        h = {}
        for r in range(R):
            for j in range(20):
                e = int(ids[pos + r, l, j])
                if mode == "r":
                    h[e] = max(h.get(e, 0.0), 1.0 if j < 10 else beta)
                else:
                    h[e] = h.get(e, 0.0) + float(pr[pos + r, l, j])
        if mode == "p":
            nd = [v for e, v in h.items() if any(int(x) == e for x in ids[pos:pos + R, l, :10].reshape(-1))]
            mu = sum(nd) / max(1, len(nd))
            h = {e: v / mu for e, v in h.items()}
        out.append(h)
        pos += A
    return out


def run_lfu_hint(seq, hints, C, d=0.99):
    """the engine's LFU (forced admission, victim = lowest score not needed now), scores fed by the hint dicts"""
    miss, R, score, w = [], set(), {}, 1.0
    for N, h in zip(seq, hints):
        w /= d
        for e, f in h.items():
            score[e] = score.get(e, 0.0) + w * f
        for e in N:
            if e not in h:
                score[e] = score.get(e, 0.0) + w
        m = [e for e in N if e not in R]
        miss.append(len(m))
        for e in m:
            if len(R) >= C:
                vic = [v for v in R if v not in N]
                if vic:
                    R.discard(min(vic, key=lambda v: score[v]))
            R.add(e)
    return miss


def hints():
    t0 = time.time()
    lines = []

    def say(s):
        print(s, flush=True); lines.append(s)
    say("LFU fed by the router's near misses (top-20), MSB pool only (LSB pool: plain LFU), 16-row checks accepting 10,"
        " measured tokens [400, 800); MB/token")
    lfu, _ = bytes_per_token("lfu")
    say(f"  lfu (engine) {lfu / 1e6:6.1f}")
    for mode, beta in (("r", 0.0), ("r", 0.25), ("r", 0.5), ("r", 1.0), ("p", 0.0)):
        res = {}
        for k, ids in IDS.items():
            if (k, 16, 10) not in _NEEDS:
                _NEEDS[(k, 16, 10)] = needs(ids, 16, 10)
            ch = _NEEDS[(k, 16, 10)]
            meas = [m for m, _ in ch]
            tot = 0
            for l in range(NL):
                hs = hint_seq(k, l, mode=mode, beta=beta)[:len(ch)]
                mm = run_lfu_hint([lay[l][0] for _, lay in ch], hs, 147)
                ml = run_pool([lay[l][1] for _, lay in ch], 46, "lfu")
                tot += sum(a * MSB + b * LSB for a, b, f in zip(mm, ml, meas) if f)
            res[k] = tot / (10 * sum(meas))
        b = float(np.mean(list(res.values())))
        say(f"  mode {mode} beta {beta:.2f}: {b / 1e6:6.1f}  {100 * (b / lfu - 1):+5.1f} % vs lfu  (" + ", ".join(f"{k} {v / 1e6:.1f}" for k, v in res.items()) + ")")
    say(f"({time.time() - t0:.0f} s)")
    open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/wall24_hints.txt"), "w").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")


def run_global(chs, Cm, Cl, policy="lfu", d=0.99, protect=False, prev=False):
    """one MSB pool and one LSB pool shared by all 48 layers (records are the same size in every layer): calls in pass
    order (check t: layers 0..47). lfu: per (layer, expert) decayed counts (each layer's w advances once per check, so
    scores compare across layers); a miss evicts the globally lowest score not needed by this call (lazy min-heap).
    Returns bytes over measured checks."""
    import heapq
    tot = 0
    for part, C, sz in ((0, Cm, MSB), (1, Cl, LSB)):
        R, score, w, hp = set(), {}, 1.0, []
        last = [set() for _ in range(NL)]                 # prev: the previous check's needs per layer (online proxy)
        for meas, lay in chs:
            w /= d
            P = {(l, e) for l in range(NL) for e in lay[l][part]} if protect else None   # the whole check's needs
            for l in range(NL):
                if prev:                                  # upcoming layers of this check, as last check needed them
                    P = {(j, e) for j in range(l + 1, NL) for e in last[j]}
                    last[l] = set(lay[l][part])
                N = {(l, e) for e in lay[l][part]}
                for x in N:
                    score[x] = score.get(x, 0.0) + w
                m = [x for x in N if x not in R]
                if meas:
                    tot += len(m) * sz
                for x in m:
                    if len(R) >= C:
                        aside = []
                        while hp:
                            sc, v = heapq.heappop(hp)
                            if v not in R or sc != score[v]:
                                continue                     # stale entry
                            if v in N or (P is not None and v in P):
                                aside.append((sc, v)); continue
                            R.discard(v); break
                        for a in aside:
                            heapq.heappush(hp, a)
                    R.add(x)
                for x in N:                                  # needed experts' scores changed: fresh heap entries
                    heapq.heappush(hp, (score[x], x))
    return tot


def shared():
    t0 = time.time()
    lines = []

    def say(s):
        print(s, flush=True); lines.append(s)
    lfu, per = bytes_per_token("lfu")
    say(f"per-layer pools (engine) lfu: {lfu / 1e6:6.1f} MB/token")
    res = {}
    for k, ids in IDS.items():
        if (k, 16, 10) not in _NEEDS:
            _NEEDS[(k, 16, 10)] = needs(ids, 16, 10)
        ch = _NEEDS[(k, 16, 10)]
        res[k] = run_global(ch, 147 * NL, 46 * NL) / (10 * sum(m for m, _ in ch))
    b = float(np.mean(list(res.values())))
    say(f"one shared pool (C_m {147 * NL}, C_l {46 * NL}) lfu: {b / 1e6:6.1f}  {100 * (b / lfu - 1):+5.1f} %  (" +
        ", ".join(f"{k} {v / 1e6:.1f}" for k, v in res.items()) + ")")
    res = {}
    for k, ids in IDS.items():
        ch = _NEEDS[(k, 16, 10)]
        res[k] = run_global(ch, 147 * NL, 46 * NL, protect=True) / (10 * sum(m for m, _ in ch))
    b = float(np.mean(list(res.values())))
    res2 = {}
    for k, ids in IDS.items():
        ch = _NEEDS[(k, 16, 10)]
        res2[k] = run_global(ch, 147 * NL, 46 * NL, prev=True) / (10 * sum(m for m, _ in ch))
    b2 = float(np.mean(list(res2.values())))
    say(f"shared pool, victims exclude upcoming layers' needs of the PREVIOUS check (online): {b2 / 1e6:6.1f}  "
        f"{100 * (b2 / lfu - 1):+5.1f} %  (" + ", ".join(f"{k} {v / 1e6:.1f}" for k, v in res2.items()) + ")")
    say(f"shared pool, victims exclude the whole check's needs (oracle within the check): {b / 1e6:6.1f}  "
        f"{100 * (b / lfu - 1):+5.1f} %  (" + ", ".join(f"{k} {v / 1e6:.1f}" for k, v in res.items()) + ")")
    say(f"({time.time() - t0:.0f} s)")
    open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/wall24_shared.txt"), "w").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

        print(s, flush=True); lines.append(s)
    say("v3 24 GB split C_m 147 / C_l 46, plan 4433333333, MB per token (mean of 4 sets: bulgarian chat code math)")
    base = {}
    for R, A in ((1, 1), (16, 10)):
        for pol in ("lru", "lfu", "belady", "min"):
            b, per = bytes_per_token(pol, R=R, A=A)

            say(f"R {R:2d} A {A:2d} {pol:6s}: {b / 1e6:6.1f}  (" + ", ".join(f"{k} {v / 1e6:.1f}" for k, v in per.items()) + ")"
                + ("" if pol == "lru" else f"  {100 * (b / base[('lru', R)] - 1):+.1f} % vs lru"))
    lfu16, min16 = base[("lfu", 16)], base[("min", 16)]
    say(f"MIN (any policy's floor) vs the engine's LFU at 16-row checks: {100 * (min16 / lfu16 - 1):+.1f} %")
    # RAM worth of the policy headroom: LFU pool scale f (C_m and C_l x f) that reads as little as MIN at 147 / 46
    for f in (1.1, 1.2, 1.3, 1.4, 1.6):
        b, _ = bytes_per_token("lfu", Cm=int(147 * f), Cl=int(46 * f))
        say(f"lfu with pools x{f:.1f} (C_m {int(147 * f)}, C_l {int(46 * f)}, +{(f - 1) * 16.96:.1f} GB): {b / 1e6:6.1f}")
        if b <= min16:
            break
    say(f"({time.time() - t0:.0f} s)")
    open(os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data/wall24.txt"), "w").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")


### scripts/wrongpath_sim.py
"""Wrong-path expert study (round 22): do experts loaded only for rejected draft rows pollute the expert cache?
Trace-driven and exact for miss counts: routing depends only on the tokens, never on the cache, so every policy below sees
the same passes (data/verify_trace125.npz/json from verify_trace125.py: 125B, MTPLX committed history, depth 3, greedy).
Each round's accepted drafts = committed tokens - 1; rows after the first rejected draft are wrong-path. All rows of a
pass are verified, so their experts are loaded in every policy; the policies differ only in what the cache keeps after
the pass (per-layer LRU of C slots, persistent across prompts):
  mru        every expert the pass used goes to the MRU end (BigMoE today, LRU-like)
  demote     as mru, then experts used only by wrong-path rows move to the LRU end (evicted first)
  validonly  recency updated only by valid rows: a wrong-path-only expert that was cached keeps its old position,
             one that was loaded for the pass is inserted at the LRU end
  nowrong    upper bound: wrong-path rows never existed (no loads for them at all; not achievable)
CPU analogue: wrong-path memory references are often useful prefetches (Mutlu et al. 2005), so demotion can also hurt;
this measures which way it goes for MoE drafts.
Cost model (round 20, fitted to BigMoE's eager benches): 0.40 ms per stalled layer + 0.083 ms per MB, 2.77 MB per expert.
usage: wrongpath_sim.py [C ...]   (default 96 128 160 192 = 13, 17, 21, 26 GB of experts)"""
import json, os, sys
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
D = os.path.expanduser("~/MoonShots/data")
z = np.load(f"{D}/verify_trace125.npz")
meta = json.load(open(f"{D}/verify_trace125.json"))
ids, rows, seqs, prompt = z["ids"], z["rows"], z["seq"], z["prompt"]
P, NL, RM, K = ids.shape
# label rows: a pass's committed tokens = the sum over token_callbacks made while it was the latest gated pass (MTPLX may
# call back more than once per round); accepted drafts = committed - 1. Checked against MTPLX's accepted_by_depth.
from collections import defaultdict
valid = np.ones((P, RM), bool)
cbs = meta["callbacks"]
committed = defaultdict(int)
for (p, s, n) in cbs:
    committed[(p, s)] += n
bad = 0
acc_lab = defaultdict(lambda: [0] * 8)
for i in range(P):
    n = committed.get((int(prompt[i]), int(seqs[i])), 0)
    if n == 0:
        bad += 1; continue
    a = n - 1
    if a > rows[i] - 1:
        bad += 1; a = rows[i] - 1
    valid[i, a + 1:] = False
    for k in range(a):
        acc_lab[int(prompt[i])][k] += 1
for m in meta["meta"]:
    print(f"  prompt {m['prompt']}: accepted by depth from labels {acc_lab[m['prompt']][:3]} vs MTPLX {m['accepted_by_depth']}")
tokens = sum(n for (_, _, n) in cbs)
wrong_rows = int(sum((~valid[i, 1:rows[i]]).sum() for i in range(P)))
print(f"trace: {P} passes, {tokens} committed tokens, rows/pass {np.bincount(rows).tolist()}, wrong-path rows {wrong_rows} "
      f"({wrong_rows / max(1, int(rows.sum())):.1%} of rows), unmatched {bad}")


def run(C, policy, warm=60):
    miss = stalled = 0
    n_tok = 0
    caches = [OrderedDict() for _ in range(NL)]
    for i in range(P):
        R = int(rows[i])
        use_rows = [r for r in range(R) if valid[i, r] or policy != "nowrong"]
        for L in range(NL):
            c = caches[L]
            a = ids[i, L]
            if a[0, 0] < 0:
                continue
            vset = {int(e) for r in range(R) if valid[i, r] for e in a[r]}
            need = {int(e) for r in use_rows for e in a[r]}
            m = [e for e in need if e not in c]
            if i >= warm:
                miss += len(m); stalled += bool(m)
            # the pass touches its experts in row order; then the policy decides what stays where
            for e in need:
                if e in c:
                    if policy in ("mru", "demote", "nowrong") or e in vset:
                        c.move_to_end(e)
                else:
                    c[e] = 1
                    if policy == "validonly" and e not in vset:
                        c.move_to_end(e, last=False)
            if policy == "demote":
                for e in need - vset:
                    c.move_to_end(e, last=False)
            while len(c) > C:
                c.popitem(last=False)
        if i >= warm:
            n_tok += int(valid[i, :R].sum())        # tokens committed by this pass = valid rows (accepted + 1)
    ms = stalled * 0.40 + miss * 2.77 * 0.083
    return miss / n_tok, stalled / n_tok, ms / n_tok


Cs = [int(x) for x in sys.argv[1:]] or [96, 128, 160, 192]
print("C (GB)        policy     misses/token  stalled layers/token  est. stall ms/token")
for C in Cs:
    for pol in ("mru", "demote", "validonly", "nowrong"):
        m, s, ms = run(C, pol)
        print(f"{C:3d} ({C * 2.77 * NL / 1e3:4.1f})  {pol:10s} {m:8.1f}      {s:8.2f}              {ms:6.2f}", flush=True)
